\section*{考点 6 函数模型}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\dfrac{mH}{ht}$

【解析】设第二次拍照飞船的实际上升了 $x$ ,则 $\dfrac{H}{h} = \dfrac{x}{m}$ ,解得 $x = \dfrac{Hm}{h}$ ,所以拍照时飞船的瞬时速度为: $v = \dfrac{x}{t} = \dfrac{Hm}{ht}$ .

2. 4.96

【解析】依题意,汽车限速区间 $v \in  \left\lbrack  {{30}{{km}}/{h},{70}{{km}}/{h}}\right\rbrack   =  \left\lbrack  {{30} \times  \dfrac{1000}{3600}{m}/{s},{70} \times  \dfrac{1000}{3600}{m}/{s}}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  {8\dfrac{1}{3}{m}/{s},{19}\dfrac{4}{9}{m}/{s}}\right\rbrack  ,$ 反应距离 ${s}_{1} = {0.7584v}$ ,刹车距离 ${s}_{2} = {0.072}{v}^{2}$ ,时间 $t =  \dfrac{{s}_{1} + {s}_{2} + {30}}{v} = \dfrac{{0.7584v} + {0.072}{v}^{2} + {30}}{v} = {0.7584} + {0.072v} +  \dfrac{30}{v}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {8\dfrac{1}{3}{m}/{s},{19}\dfrac{4}{9}{m}/{s}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减，故在左端点取得最大值 4.96 秒，即该路口黄灯亮的时间最多，为 4.96 秒.

\section*{二、选择题}

3. A

【解析】 \ding{172}  若 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是周期函数,设 $T$ 是它的正周期, 即 $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x + T}\right)$ ,假设函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不是常值函数,设 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,且 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的减函数, 得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \geq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 恒成立,因此 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,取 $n =  \left\lbrack  \dfrac{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}{T}\right\rbrack   + 1\left( n\right.$ 取无穷大整数更直观 $)$ ,其中 $\left\lbrack  \dfrac{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}{T}\right\rbrack$ 是不大于 $\dfrac{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}{T}$ 的最大整数,则 ${x}_{1} + {nT} > {x}_{2}$ ,而 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  =  f\left( {{x}_{1} + {nT}}\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + {nT}}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,这与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是减函数矛盾,所以 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 不成立,所以 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ , 即 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是常值函数，故 \ding{172} 是真命题；

 \ding{173}  取 $f\left( x\right)  = x,g\left( x\right)  = {2x}$ ，则对任意的 $a$ 、 $b$ ， $\left| {f\left( a\right)  - f\left( b\right) }\right|  =  \left| {a - b}\right| ,\left| {g\left( a\right)  - g\left( b\right) }\right|  = 2\left| {a - b}\right|$ ,满足 $\left| {f\left( a\right)  - f\left( b\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {g\left( a\right)  - g\left( b\right) }\right|$ ,但 $f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right)  =  - x$ 是减函数,故 \ding{173} 是假命题. 综上, 选 A.

4. A

【解析】对于 \ding{172} ,取 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\left( {1,4}\right)$ ,故 $y = {2}^{f\left( x\right) } \in  \left( {2,{16}}\right) ,y = {\log }_{2}f\left( x\right)  \in  \left( {0,2}\right)$ ,令 $S = \left\lbrack  {0,{16}}\right\rbrack  ,A \in  \left( {2,{16}}\right)$ , $B \in  \left( {0,2}\right)$ ,满足 $A \cup  B \subseteq  S$ 和 ${\complement }_{S}\left( {A \cup  B}\right)$ 是有限集,从而 $y = {2}^{f\left( x\right) }$ 和 $y = {\log }_{2}f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left\lbrack  {0,{16}}\right\rbrack$ 一互补函数, \ding{172}  正确; 对于  \ding{173} ，取 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是增函数， $t = g\left( x\right)$ ，由复合函数性质，只需考虑 $y = \sin t$ 和 $y = \tan t$ 即可,先让 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的值域包含 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ,则 $y = \tan t > 1,y = \sin t \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},1}\right)$ ,那么接下来考虑让 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)$ 的部分被 $y = \sin t$ 和 $y = \tan t$ 取得,因为 $y = \tan t$ 的值域没有 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},1}\right)$ ,所以 $t$ 的值域中没有 $\left\lbrack  {\arctan \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ , 所以 $y = \sin t$ 的值域没有 $\left\lbrack  {\sin \left( {\arctan \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right) ,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)$ ,所以考虑让 $y = \tan t$ 的值域中有 $\left\lbrack  {\sin \left( {\arctan \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right) ,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)$ ,则 $t$ 的值域有 $\left\lbrack  {\arctan \left\lbrack  {\sin \left( {\arctan \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack  ,\arctan \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack  ,\cdots \cdots$ ,依次类推,按照这样的方式构造下去,可以得到满足题意的 $y = g\left( x\right)$ , \ding{173}  正确. 故选: A.

\section*{三、解答题}

5. ( 1 ) $S = \dfrac{{F}_{0}}{{V}_{0}} = \dfrac{\pi {R}^{2} + {2\pi R} \cdot  H}{\pi {R}^{2} \cdot  H} = \dfrac{R + {2H}}{RH}$ ；

(2)由题意，宿舍高 ${3n}$ 米，底面面积 $A = \dfrac{T}{n}$ ，所以体积 ${V}_{0} =  {3n} \cdot  A = {3T}$ ,由 $f = \dfrac{{L}^{2}}{A} = {18}$ ,所以底面周长 $L = \sqrt{\dfrac{18T}{n}}$ ,所以 ${F}_{0} = L \cdot  {3n} + A = \sqrt{\dfrac{18T}{n}} \cdot  {3n} + \dfrac{T}{n}$ ,所以 “体形系数” $S =  \dfrac{{F}_{0}}{{V}_{0}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{18n}{T}} + \dfrac{1}{3n} = \sqrt{\dfrac{18n}{10000}} + \dfrac{1}{3n} = \dfrac{3\sqrt{2n}}{100} + \dfrac{1}{3n},n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,由计算器 TABLE 功能,可知 $n = 6$ 时,“体形系数” $S$ 最小.

6.(1)当燃气用量 $x \in  (0,{310}\rbrack$ 时，燃气费用 $f\left( x\right)  = {ax}$ ；当燃气用量 $x \in  ({310},{520}\rbrack$ 时，燃气费用 $f\left( x\right)  = {310a} + \left( {x - {310}}\right) b$ ；

当燃气用量 $x \in  \left( {{520}, + \infty }\right)$ 时,燃气费用 $f\left( x\right)  = {310a} +  {210b} + \left( {x - {520}}\right) c$ ; 综上所述普通居民的年度燃气费用

$f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {ax},x \in  (0,{310}\rbrack , \\  {310a} + \left( {x - {310}}\right) b,x \in  ({310},{520}\rbrack , \\  {310a} + {210b} + \left( {x - {520}}\right) c,x \in  ({520}, + \infty \rbrack ; \end{array}\right.$

(2) $f\left( {56}\right)  = {56a} = {168} \Longrightarrow  a = 3$ ，第 1-5 月共用 ${56} + {80} + {66} +  {58} + {60} = {320}$ ,可得第 5 月有 10 立方米燃气单价为 $b$ 元/立方米; ${50a} + {10b} = {183},a = 3 \Longrightarrow  b = {3.3}$ ,第 12 个月,燃气单价为 $c$ 元/立方米, ${63c} = {264.6} \Longrightarrow  c = {4.2}$ . 综上所述: $a = 3,b =$ 3.3, $c = 4.2$ .

7. ( 1 )记 $f\left( x\right)  = {3}^{x}$ ，对任意的 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ，有 $f\left( x\right)  = {3}^{x} \geq  1$ ， $f\left( 1\right)  = {3}^{1} = 3$ ; 对于任意的 $x$ 、 $y \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,若 $x + y \leq  1$ ,则 $f\left( {x + y}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  - f\left( y\right)  + 1 = {3}^{x + y} - {3}^{x} - {3}^{y} + 1 = \left( {{3}^{x} - 1}\right)  \left( {{3}^{y} - 1}\right)  \geq  0$ ,即 $f\left( {x + y}\right)  \geq  f\left( x\right)  + f\left( y\right)  - 1$ . 故函数 $y = {3}^{x}$ 是 $U$ 型函数;

(2)设 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ，且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ，则 ${x}_{2} - {x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ . 因此 $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left\lbrack  {{x}_{1} + \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right) }\right\rbrack   - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \geq  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  +  f\left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right)  - 1 - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right)  - 1 \geq  0$ ,可知 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上为增函数;

(3)因为 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n - 1}}\right)  = f\left( {\dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}} + \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}} + \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}}\right)  \geq  f\left( {\dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}} + \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}}\right)  +  f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}\right)  - 1 \geq  {3f}\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}\right)  - 2$ ,所以 $f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}\right)  \leq  \dfrac{1}{3}f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n - 1}}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{3} \leq  \dfrac{1}{{3}^{2}}f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n - 2}}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{{3}^{2}} + \dfrac{2}{3} \leq  \cdots  \leq  \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}}f\left( \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n - n}}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{{3}^{n}} + \cdots  + \dfrac{2}{{3}^{2}} +  \dfrac{2}{3} = \dfrac{1}{{3}^{n}} \times  3 + 2 \times  \dfrac{\dfrac{1}{3}\left( {1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}}\right) }{1 - \dfrac{1}{3}} = \dfrac{2}{{3}^{n}} + 1.$

8. ( 1 )设 $a$ 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的 $S$ 值，则方程 ${x}^{2} - {2x} = a$ 有唯一实数解,即 ${x}^{2} - {2x} - a = 0$ 有唯一解,法 1: 由 $\Delta  = 4 +  {4a} = 0$ ,得 $a =  - 1$ ,法 2: 通过作图知,当 $x = 1$ 时,方程有唯一解,此时 $a = 1 - 2 =  - 1$ ,故 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $S$ 值”为 -1 ;

(2)由题意， ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {12}{x}^{3} - \left( {{12k} + {12}}\right) {x}^{2} + {12kx} = {12x}(x -$ 1) $\left( {x - k}\right)$ , \ding{172}  当 $0 < k < 1$ 时,函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,0\rbrack$ 上严格减，在 $\left\lbrack  {0,k}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增，在 $\left\lbrack  {k,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减，在 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ 上严格增,若 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的函数值中不存在 $S$ 值,则 $g\left( 0\right)  = g\left( 1\right)  \Longrightarrow  1 = 3 - {4k} -  4 + {6k} + 1 \Longrightarrow  k = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $k = 1$ 时,函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,0\rbrack$ 上严格减，在 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上严格增,显然 $g\left( 0\right)$ 是 $S$ 值, 舍;综上,实数 $k$ 的取值集合为 $\left\{  \dfrac{1}{2}\right\}$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/22_636_1486_235_247_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)法一:由函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的图像是一条连续曲线，且函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的所有函数值均为“ $S$ 值”可知， $h\left( x\right)  = a$ 的任意解均唯一,即 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 是单调函数. 证明: 必要性: 因为 $m <  n,y = h\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left\lbrack  {m,n}\right\rbrack$ 上的严格增函数,所以 $h\left( m\right)  < h\left( n\right)$ ; 充分性: 若 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {m,n}\right\rbrack$ 上为严格增函数,则对任意 $m \leq  {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} \leq  n$ ,有 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - h\left( {x}_{2}\right)  < 0$ ,即 $h\left( m\right)  - h\left( n\right)  < 0$ 成立,即 $h\left( m\right)  < h\left( n\right)$ ;

法二: 必要性: 因为 $m < n,y = h\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left\lbrack  {m,n}\right\rbrack$ 上的严格增函数,所以 $h\left( m\right)  < h\left( n\right)$ ; 充分性: 假设 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 不是区间 $\lbrack m$ , $n\rbrack$ 上的严格增函数,则存在 $m \leq  {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} \leq  n$ ,使得 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \geq  h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,因为 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的所有函数值均为 “ $S$ 值”,显然 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,所以 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ;  \ding{172}  若 $m = {x}_{1}$ ,因为 $h\left( m\right)  <  h\left( n\right)$ ,所以 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < h\left( n\right)$ ,又 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,构造函数 $H\left( x\right)  = h\left( x\right)  - h\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,则 $H\left( {x}_{2}\right)  < 0,H\left( n\right)  > 0$ ,且函数 $y = H\left( x\right)$ 的图像是一连续曲线,由零点存在性定理,知存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {{x}_{2},n}\right)$ ,使得 $H\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即 $h\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = h\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,这与 $y =  h\left( x\right)$ 所有函数值都是 “ $S$ 值” 矛盾;  \ding{173}  若 $m < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} \leq  n$ , (i) 若 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = h\left( m\right)$ ,这与 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 所有函数值都是 “ $S$ 值” 矛盾; (ii) 若 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > h\left( m\right)$ , a. 若 $h\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > h\left( m\right)$ ,构造函数 $G\left( x\right)  = h\left( x\right)  - h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,则 $G\left( m\right)  < 0,G\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > 0$ ,且函数 $y =  G\left( x\right)$ 的图像是一条连续曲线,由零点存在性定理得: 存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {m,{x}_{1}}\right)$ ,使得 $G\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即 $h\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = h\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,这与 $y =  h\left( x\right)$ 所有函数值都是 “ $S$ 值” 矛盾; b. 若 $h\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = h\left( m\right)$ ,这与 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 所有函数值都是 “ $S$ 值” 矛盾; c. 若 $h\left( {x}_{2}\right)  <  h\left( m\right)$ ,同理可证矛盾; (iii) 若 $h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < h\left( m\right)$ ,同理可证矛盾; 综上,假设不成立,故 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left\lbrack  {m,n}\right\rbrack$ 上的严格增函数.

点睛: 若集合 $M$ 中有且仅有一个元素,等价于 $f\left( x\right)  = a$ 有唯一唯一解，研究函数的单调性以及图像，判断 $f\left( x\right)  = a$ 有唯一解的情况即可.

9. ( 1 )因为函数 $y = \dfrac{2}{x - 1}$ 在区间 $\left( {2,6}\right)$ 上严格递减，所以函数 $y = \dfrac{2}{x - 1}\left( {2 < x < 6}\right)$ 的值域为 $\left( {\dfrac{2}{5},2}\right)$ ,所以函数 $y =  \dfrac{2}{x - 1}\left( {2 < x < 6}\right)$ 的上确界为 2 ;

(2) $y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{x} = {x}^{2} - {hx} + {2\ln }x,{y}^{\prime } = {2x} - h + \dfrac{2}{x},x > 0$ ，因为集合 ${M}_{n} = \left\{  {f\left( x\right)  \mid  y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{{x}^{n}}}\right.$ 在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数\},所以 ${y}^{\prime } \geq  0$ 恒成立,因为 ${2x} - h + \dfrac{2}{x} \geq  2\sqrt{{2x} \times  \dfrac{2}{x}} - h =  4 - h$ ,当且仅当 $x = 1$ 时取等号,所以 $h \leq  4$ ,所以 $h$ 的最大值为 4 ;

(3)证明:因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 有上界，设 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  k$ ，假设存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ,设 ${x}_{1} > {x}_{0}$ ,因为 $y =  f\left( x\right)  \in  {M}_{2}$ ,所以 $y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{{x}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,进而 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{1}^{2}} >  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{{x}_{0}^{2}} > 0$ ,得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > 0,k > 0$ ,取 ${x}_{2} > {x}_{1}$ ,且 ${x}_{2} > \sqrt{\dfrac{k{x}_{1}^{2}}{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }}$ , 由于 ${x}_{2} > {x}_{1}$ ,得到 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{2}^{2}} > \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{1}^{2}}$  \ding{172} ,由 ${x}_{2} > \sqrt{\dfrac{k{x}_{1}^{2}}{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }}$ ,得 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{1}^{2}} > \dfrac{k}{{x}_{2}^{2}} \geq  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{2}^{2}}$  \ding{173} ,显然 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 两式矛盾,所以假设不成立,即对任意 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,均有 $f\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,令 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{x}$ , $x > 0$ ,则 $y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{{x}^{2}} =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{3}}$ ,因为当 $x > 0$ 时, ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{3}{{x}^{4}} > 0$ ,所以 $y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{{x}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增, $y = f\left( x\right)  \in  {M}_{2}$ ,因为 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{x},x > 0$ 的值域为 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{x}$ 的上确界为 0 .

点睛: 关键点点睛:

(1)第二问的关键是导函数大于等于零恒成立, 用基本不等式求解;

(2)第三问关键是根据不等式的结构能够想到取 ${x}_{2} > \sqrt{\dfrac{k{x}_{1}^{2}}{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }}$ ,再得到 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{1}^{2}} > \dfrac{k}{{x}_{2}^{2}} \geq  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{2}^{2}}$ 与当 ${x}_{2} > {x}_{1}$ 时得到 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{2}^{2}} > \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{1}^{2}}$ 矛盾.

10. (1) $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不具有性质 $P$ ,理由: 取 ${x}_{1} =  - 2,{x}_{2} = 1$ , 有 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{{e}}^{-2} + {e}}{-1} < 0.y = g\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ . 理由: 对任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R},{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} \neq  0$ ,有 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}} =  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{3} + {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}^{3} + {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}} = {x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + 1 = {\left( {x}_{1} - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{2}\right) }^{2} +  \dfrac{3}{4}{x}_{2}^{2} + 1 > 0$

( 2 )函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ ，故对 ${x}_{1}$ 、 $- {x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R},{x}_{1} -  {x}_{2} \neq  0$ ,都有 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{2}}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} > 0$ ,而 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,故 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} > 0$ ,即 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 +  {2a}\cos {2x} \geq  0$ 恒成立. 若 $a > 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = 1 - {2a} \geq  0$ ,解得 $0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ; 若 $a = 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 \geq  0$ 恒成立; 若 $a < 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = 1 + {2a} \geq  0$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{1}{2} \leq  a < 0$ . 综合上述,实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$ ;

(3)证明:因函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ，要证明 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数，只要证明:对任意实数 ${x}_{0}$ ， $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = 0$ 即可. 对任意实数 ${x}_{0}$ ,设 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = c$ ,则由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ 知: 当 $x + {x}_{0} \neq  0$ 时, $\dfrac{f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{x + {x}_{0}} > 0$  \ding{172} ,设 $h\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + c$ ,当 $x + {x}_{0} > 0$ ,即 $x >  - {x}_{0}$ 时,由 \ding{172}  得 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > 0$ ,即当 $x \in  \left( {-{x}_{0}, + \infty }\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  > 0$  \ding{173} , 当 $x + {x}_{0} < 0$ ,即 $x <  - {x}_{0}$ 时,由 \ding{172} 得 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < 0$ ,即 $x \in  \left( {-\infty , - {x}_{0}}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  < 0$  \ding{174} ,于是由曲线 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的连续性,函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上存在零点 $\bar{x}$ ,即 $h\left( \bar{x}\right)  = f\left( \bar{x}\right)  +  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$  \ding{175} ，由函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增，知:函数 $y =  h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增; 所以由 \ding{173} 知 $\bar{x} \leq   - {x}_{0}$ ，由 \ding{174} 知 $\bar{x} \geq  - {x}_{0}$ ,故 $\bar{x} =  - {x}_{0}$ . 故由 \ding{175} 得: $h\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即对任意对任意实数 ${x}_{0}$ ,均有 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = 0$ ; 因此,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数.

另证: (3) 由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ ,知: 当 $x > 0$ 时 $f\left( x\right)  +  f\left( 0\right)  > 0$ ,当 $x < 0$ 时 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( 0\right)  < 0$ ,由零点存在定理知 $f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,即 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ . 下面用反证法证明 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数. 假设存在 ${x}_{0}$ 使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  \neq  0$ ,不妨设 ${x}_{0} > 0$ ,则由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,知 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > f\left( 0\right)  >  f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)$ . 若 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  > 0$ ,则构造函数 $F\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2},F\left( 0\right)  = f\left( 0\right)  - \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2} =  \dfrac{f\left( 0\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{2} + \dfrac{f\left( 0\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2} < 0,F\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  -  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2} > 0$ ,由零点存在定理知, 存在 $t \in  \left( {0,{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,使得 $F\left( t\right)  = 0$ ,即 $f\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{2} \neq  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ; 而 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,同样由单调性知 $\dfrac{f\left( t\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{t - {x}_{0}} =  - \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{t - {x}_{0}} > 0$ ,从而有 $\dfrac{f\left( t\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{t + \left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) } = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right) }{t - {x}_{0}} < 0$ ,与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ 矛盾. 若 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  < 0$ ,构造函数 $G\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{2}$ ,同理也可推出与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P$ 矛盾. 综合上述,存在 ${x}_{0}$ 使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  \neq  0$ 的假设不能成立,即对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-x}\right)  = 0$ ,故 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数.

11. (1) 若 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 不为相关函数对,则 $f\left( a\right)  +  g\left( b\right)  \leq  0$ 且 $f\left( b\right)  + g\left( a\right)  \leq  0$ ,则 $f\left( a\right)  + g\left( b\right)  + f\left( b\right)  + g\left( a\right)  \leq$ 0,所以只要 $f\left( a\right)  + g\left( b\right)  + f\left( b\right)  + g\left( a\right)  > 0$ 即可,当 $f\left( x\right)  = x + 1,g\left( x\right)  =  - x + 1$ 时, $f\left( a\right)  + g\left( b\right)  + f\left( b\right)  +  g\left( a\right)  = a + 1 - b + 1 + b + 1 - a + 1 = 4 > 0$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  x + 1$ 与 $g\left( x\right)  =  - x + 1$ 是相关函数对;

(2)因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}$ 与 $g\left( x\right)  =  - x + k$ 为相关函数对，所以 ${{e}}^{a} -  a + {{e}}^{b} - b + {2k} > 0$ ,令 $t\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - x,{t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时， ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ；当 $x < 0$ 时， ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，所以 $x = 0$ 是极小值点， $t\left( x\right)  \geq  1$ ,所以 ${{e}}^{a} - a + {{e}}^{b} - b + {2k} > 2 + {2k} \geq  0$ ,所以 $k \geq   - 1$ ;

(3)假设对任意 $m$ 、 $n\left( {m < n}\right)$ 均存在 $x \in  \left( {m,n}\right)$ ，均有 $f\left( x\right)  +  g\left( x\right)  <  - \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ,则取 $m \in  \mathbf{Z},n = m + 1,{x}_{m} \in  \left( {m,m + 1}\right)$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{m}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{m}\right)  <  - \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ,对任意 $i < j,{x}_{i} < {x}_{j}$ ,有 $f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{i}\right)  <  - \dfrac{1}{2024},f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{j}\right)  <  - \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ,又函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为相关函数对,则 \ding{172} 若 $f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{j}\right)  >$ 0,则 $f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{j}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  \Longrightarrow  f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  >  - g\left( {x}_{j}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  > \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ;  \ding{173}  若 $f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{i}\right)  > 0$ ,则 $f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{i}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  \Longrightarrow  f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{j}\right)  < g\left( {x}_{i}\right)  +  f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  <  - \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ,由  \ding{172}  \ding{173} 知: $\left| {f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{j}\right) }\right|  > \dfrac{1}{2024}$ ,由 $f\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-M,M}\right\rbrack$ ,将其分为很多个子区间,如 $\lbrack  - M, - M +  \left. \dfrac{1}{2024}\right) ,\left\lbrack  {-M + \dfrac{1}{2024}, - M + \dfrac{2}{2024}}\right. ),\left\lbrack  {-M + \dfrac{2}{2024}, - M + }\right.  \left. \dfrac{3}{2024}\right) ,\cdots$ ,则以上每个区间至多包含一个 $f\left( {x}_{n}\right)$ ,矛盾,假设不成立,故存在实数 $m,n\left( {m < n}\right)$ ,使得对任意 $x \in  (m$ , $n)$ ,均有 $f\left( x\right)  + g\left( x\right)  \geq   - \dfrac{1}{2024}$ .

\section*{考点 7 函数综合问题}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\left\lbrack  {-7,8}\right\rbrack$

【解析】 ${x}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  \left( {2{x}_{0} + \dfrac{\pi }{3}}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\pi }{3},\dfrac{5\pi }{6}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack  ;f\left( x\right)  =  - {\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + m + 9,x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,3}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  f\left( x\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {m - 7,m + 9}\right\rbrack$ . 对任意 ${x}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right\rbrack$ ,存在 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,3}\right\rbrack$ , 使得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} m - 7 \leq  1 \\  m + 9 \geq  2 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  m \in  \left\lbrack  {-7,8}\right\rbrack  }\right.$ . 2. $\dfrac{1}{{2}^{a + 2} - 1}$

【解析】因为 $a > 0$ ,则 $y = {\log }_{2}x,y = {ax} + b$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {t,t + 1}\right\rbrack  \left( {t > 0}\right)$ 上严格增,则 $y = {\log }_{2}x + {ax} + b$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {t,t + 1}\right\rbrack  \left( {t > 0}\right)$ 上严格增,又因为 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {{\log }_{2}x + {ax} + b}\right|$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {t,t + 1}\right\rbrack  (t  > 0)$ 上的最大值为 ${M}_{t}\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,可得 ${M}_{t}\left( {a,b}\right)  = f\left( t\right)$ 或 ${M}_{t}(a$ , $b) = f\left( {t + 1}\right)$ ,由题意可知: $f\left( t\right)  \geq  a + 1$ 或 $f\left( {t + 1}\right)  \geq  a + 1$ , 则 $- \left( {{\log }_{2}t + {at} + b}\right)  \geq  a + 1$ 或 ${\log }_{2}\left( {t + 1}\right)  + a\left( {t + 1}\right)  + b \geq  a +$ 1,整理得 $b \leq   - {\log }_{2}t - {at} - a - 1$ 或 $b \geq   - {\log }_{2}\left( {t + 1}\right)  - {at} + 1$ , 即关于 $b$ 的不等式 $b \leq   - {\log }_{2}t - {at} - a - 1$ 或 $b \geq   - {\log }_{2}\left( {t + 1}\right)  - {at} + 1$ 的解集为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,可知 $- {\log }_{2}\left( {t + 1}\right)  - {at} + 1 \leq   - {\log }_{2}t - {at}  - a - 1$ ,整理得 ${\log }_{2}\left( {t + 1}\right)  - {\log }_{2}t = {\log }_{2}\left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{t}}\right)  \geq  a + 2$ ,则 $1 + \dfrac{1}{t} > {2}^{a + 2}$ ,又因为 $t > 0,a > 0$ ,则 ${2}^{a + 2} > 1$ ,解得 $0 < t \leq  \dfrac{1}{{2}^{a + 2} - 1}$ ,所以 $t$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{1}{{2}^{a + 2} - 1}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{{2}^{a + 2} - 1}$ .

点睛: 恒成立问题解题方法指导:

方法 1: 分离参数法求最值.

(1)分离变量. 构造函数，直接把问题转化为函数的最值问题；

(2) $a \geq  f\left( x\right)$ 恒成立 $\Leftrightarrow  a \geq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\max };a \leq  f\left( x\right)$ 恒成立 $\Leftrightarrow  a \leq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\min };a \geq  f\left( x\right)$ 能成立 $\Leftrightarrow  a \geq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\min };a \leq  f\left( x\right)$ 能成立 $\Leftrightarrow  a \leq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\max }.$

方法 2:根据不等式恒成立构造函数转化成求函数的最值问题, 一般需讨论参数范围, 借助函数单调性求解.

3. $\left( {-\dfrac{4}{3}, - \dfrac{9}{7}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{9}{7},\dfrac{4}{3}}\right)$

【解析】令 $t = \dfrac{{2}^{x + 1}}{{2}^{x} + {2}^{-x}} - 1 = \dfrac{2}{1 + {2}^{-{2x}}} - 1 \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ,则 $y =  f\left( x\right)  = g\left( t\right)  = \left| {t - a}\right| ,t \in  \left( {-1,1}\right) .$

 \ding{172}  若 $a \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ，则函数值可趋近于 0，此时 $n$ 可取趋近于无穷大的值，这与 $n$ 的最大值为 8 矛盾；

 \ding{173}  若 $a > 1$ ，则 $y = g\left( t\right)  = a - t \in  \left( {a - 1,a + 1}\right)$ . 由 $\left( {n - 1}\right) (a -  1) < \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{n - 1}}f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{n}\right)  < a + 1$ ,故 $n < \dfrac{a + 1}{a - 1} + 1 = \dfrac{2a}{a - 1}$ ,又 $n$ 的最大值为 8,因此 $8 < \dfrac{2a}{a - 1} \leq  9$ ,解得 $\dfrac{9}{7} \leq  a < \dfrac{4}{3}$ ;

 \ding{174}  若 $a <  - 1$ ，则 $y = g\left( t\right)  = t - a \in  \left( {-a - 1, - a + 1}\right)$ . 由 ( $n -$ 1) $\left( {-a - 1}\right)  < \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{n - 1}}f\left( {x}_{i}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{n}\right)  <  - a + 1$ ,故 $n < \dfrac{a - 1}{a + 1} + 1 =  \dfrac{2a}{a + 1}$ ,又 $n$ 的最大值为 8,因此 $8 < \dfrac{2a}{a + 1} \leq  9$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{4}{3} < a \leq  - \dfrac{9}{7}$ . 综上所述,实数 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {-\dfrac{4}{3}, - \dfrac{9}{7}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{9}{7},\dfrac{4}{3}}\right)$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

4. C

【解析】由任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2}}\right)  = {4f}\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ . ${f}^{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,令 ${x}_{1} = {x}_{2} = 0$ ,可得 $f\left( 0\right)  = 4{f}^{3}\left( 0\right)$ ,因为 $f\left( 0\right)  > 0$ , 解得 $f\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,故 \ding{172}  正确; 令 ${x}_{1} =  - x,{x}_{2} = x$ ,可得 $f( - x +  {2x}) = {4f}\left( {-x}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{2}\left( x\right)$ ,整理得 $f\left( x\right)  = {4f}\left( {-x}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{2}\left( x\right)$ , 又 $f\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,得 $f\left( x\right)  \cdot  f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,故  \ding{173}  正确；对于 \ding{174} 举反例，如 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x - 1}$ ，满足条件 (1)，又 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2}}\right)  = {2}^{{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2} - 1}$ ， ${4f}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 4 \times  {2}^{{x}_{1} - 1} \times  {\left( {2}^{{x}_{2} - 1}\right) }^{2}$ ,则 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{2}}\right)  =  {4f}\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{2}\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,满足条件 (2),而 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x - 1} > 0$ 没有最小值, 故  \ding{174}  错误. 所以正确的有 2 个. 故选: C.

5. B

【解析】依题意可得,若函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为“ $R$ 函数”,则直线 $y = x + t\left( {t \in  \mathbf{R}}\right)$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像至多只有一个交点,对于命题 \ding{172} :令 $x + t = \left( {m - 1}\right) x + \dfrac{1}{x}$ 得 $\left( {m - 2}\right) {x}^{2} - {tx} + 1 = 0$ ， 故 $m = 2$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m \neq  2, \\  \Delta  = {t}^{2} - 4\left( {m - 2}\right)  = 0. \end{array}\right.$ 若 $m = 2$ ,则 $m > 1$ ,若 $m \neq  2$ ,则 $m = 2 + \dfrac{{t}^{2}}{4}$ 不可能对任意 $t \in  \mathbf{R}$ 成立,因此 $m > 1$ 成立,命题 \ding{172} 为真命题; 对于命题 \ding{173} : 令 $x + t = \dfrac{k\left( {x + 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,得 $t = \dfrac{k\left( {x + 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}} - x$ ,令 $p\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{k\left( {x + 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}} - x$ ,则 $p\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上单调,若 $k = 0$ ,则 $p\left( x\right)  =  - x$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上单调递减,满足要求; 若 $k > 0$ ,则 $x \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $p\left( x\right)  \rightarrow   - \infty$ ,当 $x \rightarrow   - \infty$ 时, $p\left( x\right)  \rightarrow  - \infty$ ,而 $p\left( 0\right)  = k > 0$ ,故 $p\left( x\right)$ 不单调,矛盾; 若 $k < 0$ ,则 ${p}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{kx}{{{e}}^{x}} - 1$ ,由 ${p}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  =  - 1 < 0$ 知此时需 ${p}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ 恒成立,因为 ${p}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{k\left( {x - 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,所以当 $x < 1$ 时, ${p}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,{p}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 单调递增,当 $x > 1$ 时, ${p}^{\prime \prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,{p}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 单调递减,所以 ${p}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  =  - \dfrac{k}{{e}} - 1 \leq  0$ ,解得 $k \geq   - {e}$ ,所以整数 $k$ 可以取 $- 2, - 1$ , \ding{173}  为假命题.

6. A

【解析】对于  \ding{172}  由题得 $f\left( 1\right)  = f\left( a\right)$ ,若函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的严格增函数,因为 $a > 0,a \neq  1$ ,则当 $a > 1$ 时, $f\left( 1\right)  < f\left( a\right)$ ,当 $0 < a < 1$ 时, $f\left( 1\right)  > f\left( a\right)$ ,均与 $f\left( 1\right)  =  f\left( a\right)$ 矛盾,所以无论 $a$ 取何值,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的严格增函数,故  \ding{172}  正确; 对于 \ding{173} 因为对于任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( {a}^{x}\right)$ ,令 ${I}_{1} = \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,当 $x \in  {I}_{1} = \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, ${a}^{x} \in  \left( {a,1}\right)  = {I}_{2} \subset  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,且 $\left\{  {y\left| {y = f\left( x\right) }\right. ,x \in  {I}_{1}}\right\}   = \{ y \mid  y =  \left. {f\left( x\right) ,x \in  {I}_{2}}\right\}$ ,当 $x \in  {I}_{2} = \left( {a,1}\right)$ 时, ${a}^{x} \in  \left( {a,{a}^{a}}\right)  = {I}_{3} \subset  {I}_{2}$ , 且 $\left\{  {y\left| {y = f\left( x\right) }\right. ,x \in  {I}_{2}}\right\}   = \left\{  {y\left| {y = f\left( x\right) }\right. ,x \in  {I}_{3}}\right\}$ ,当 $x \in  {I}_{3} = \left( {a,{a}^{a}}\right)$ 时, ${a}^{x} \in  \left( {{a}^{{a}^{a}},{a}^{a}}\right)  = {I}_{4} \subset  {I}_{3}$ ,且 $\left\{  {y \mid  y = f\left( x\right) }\right.$ , $\left. {x \in  {I}_{3}}\right\}   = \left\{  {y \mid  y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  {I}_{4}}\right\}$ ,以此类推,故当 $0 < a < 1$ 时，存在无穷多个开区间 ${I}_{1},{I}_{2},\cdots ,{I}_{n},\cdots$ ，使得 ${I}_{1} \sqsupset  {I}_{2} \supset  \cdots  \supset  {I}_{n} \supset  \cdots$ ,且集合 $\left\{  {y \mid  y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  {I}_{n}}\right\}   = \{ y \mid  y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left. {I}_{n + 1}\right\}$ 对任意正整数 $n$ 都成立,故 \ding{173} 正确,故选: A.

\section*{三、解答题}

7. ( 1 )由 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$ ，且 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ ，得 $2 < {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} < 4$ ，即 $\left| {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right|  < 4$ ,则 $\left| {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  < 4\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|$ ,即 $\mid  {x}_{1}^{2} -  {x}_{2}^{2}\left| { < 2}\right| 2{x}_{1} - 2{x}_{2} \mid$ ,即 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \leq  2\left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|$ ,则函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2},x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$ 与 $g\left( x\right)  = {2x}$ “具有性质 $H\left( 2\right)$ ”;

( 2 )由函数 $f\left( x\right)  = 2 + {x}^{2},x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 与 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x}$ “具有性质 $H\left( t\right)$ ",得 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \leq  t\left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right| ,{x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  (0$ , 1],且 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ ,即 $\left| {2 + {x}_{1}^{2} - 2 - {x}_{2}^{2}}\right|  \leq  t\left| {\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}} - \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}}}\right|$ ,整理得

$\left| {\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) \left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right) }\right|  \leq  t\left| \dfrac{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}\right|$ ,则 $t \geq  {x}_{1}{x}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)$ 对 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 恒成立,又 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  (0,1\rbrack ,{x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ ,则 $0 <  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} < 2,0 < {x}_{1}{x}_{2} < 1$ ,即 $0 < {x}_{1}{x}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  < 2$ ,则 $t \geq  2$ , 即所求的 $t$ 的取值范围为 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ ;

(3)由函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 有两个零点 ${x}_{1}$ 、 ${x}_{2}$ ，得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  =  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 0$ ,又函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} + 2\ln x - 3$ 与 $y = g\left( x\right)$ “具有性质 $H\left( 1\right)$ ”,则 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  = 0$ , 即 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,即 $\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}^{2}} + 2\ln {x}_{1} - 3 = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}^{2}} + 2\ln {x}_{2} - 3$ ,令 ${x}_{1}^{2} =  {t}_{1},{x}_{2}^{2} = {t}_{2}$ ,即 $\dfrac{1}{{t}_{1}} + \ln {t}_{1} - 3 = \dfrac{1}{{t}_{2}} + \ln {t}_{2} - 3$ ,记 $h\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} +  \ln x - 3$ ,即 $h\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = h\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ ,因为 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{x} = \dfrac{x - 1}{{x}^{2}}$ , 当 $0 < x < 1$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ; 当 $x > 1$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,所以函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 是严格减函数,在 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数. 要证 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} > 2$ ,即证 ${t}_{1} + {t}_{2} > 2$ ,不妨设 $0 < {t}_{1} <  1 < {t}_{2}$ ,即证 ${t}_{2} > 2 - {t}_{1} > 1$ ,只需证 $h\left( {t}_{2}\right)  > h\left( {2 - {t}_{1}}\right)$ ,即证 $h\left( {t}_{1}\right)  > h\left( {2 - {t}_{1}}\right)$ ,设 $H\left( x\right)  = h\left( x\right)  - h\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,即 $H\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{x} + \ln x - \dfrac{1}{2 - x} - \ln \left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,因为 ${H}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{x} -  \dfrac{1}{{\left( 2 - x\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{2 - x} =  - \dfrac{4{\left( 1 - x\right) }^{2}}{{x}^{2}{\left( 2 - x\right) }^{2}} \leq  0$ ,所以函数 $y = H\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 是减函数,且 $H\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,又 $0 < {t}_{1} < 1$ ,则 $H\left( {t}_{1}\right)  >  H\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,即 $h\left( {t}_{1}\right)  - h\left( {2 - {t}_{1}}\right)  > 0$ ,则 $h\left( {t}_{1}\right)  > h\left( {2 - {t}_{1}}\right)$ 得证,故 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} > 2$ .

8. (1) 当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  > \dfrac{1}{2} > 0$ ,当 ${x}_{1} > 0,{x}_{2} > 0$ , ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} < 1$ 时, $g\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  = {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2},g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  +  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = {x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + 1$ ,则 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - g\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  =  {x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + 1 - {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{1}{2} - 2{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{1 - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}{2}$ ,因为 $1 > {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} \geq  2\sqrt{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}$ ,所以 $1 - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2} > 0$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  +  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > g\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}$ 为 $L$ 型函数;

(2)当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时，由 $p\left( x\right)  > \ln \left( {0 + b}\right)  \geq  0$ 得 $b \geq  1$ ，当 ${x}_{1} > 0,{x}_{2} > 0,{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} < 1$ 时, $p\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  = \ln \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + b}\right)$ , $p\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + p\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = \ln \left( {{x}_{1} + b}\right)  + \ln \left( {{x}_{2} + b}\right)$ ,由 $p\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  <  p\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + p\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,得 $\ln \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + b}\right)  < \ln \left( {{x}_{1} + b}\right)  + \ln \left( {{x}_{2} + }\right.  b)$ ,即 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + b < \left( {{x}_{1} + b}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + b}\right)$ ,即 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + b < {x}_{1}{x}_{2} +  b\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + {b}^{2}$ ,即 ${b}^{2} + b\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} - 1}\right)  + {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  >$ 0,令 $h\left( b\right)  = {b}^{2} + b\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} - 1}\right)  + {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,则对称轴 $b = \dfrac{1 - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) }{2} \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $h\left( b\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ 上的最小值为 $h\left( 1\right)$ ，只要 $h\left( 1\right)  > 0$ ，则 $h\left( b\right)  > 0$ ，因为 $h\left( 1\right)  = {1}^{2} + \left( {{x}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {{x}_{2} - 1}\right)  + {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} > 0$ ,所以 $b \in  \lbrack 1, + \infty )$ ;

(3)存在，举例 $1 : r\left( x\right)  = 4\sqrt{x}$ . 理由如下:当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时， $r\left( x\right)  \in  \left( {0,4}\right)$ 符合 $r\left( x\right)  > 0$ ; 当 ${x}_{1} > 0,{x}_{2} > 0,{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} < 1$ 时, $r\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  = 4\sqrt{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}},r\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + r\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 4\sqrt{{x}_{1}} + 4\sqrt{{x}_{2}}.$ 因为 ${\left( \sqrt{{x}_{1}} + \sqrt{{x}_{2}}\right) }^{2} = {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + 2\sqrt{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}},\left( {\sqrt{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}}^{2}\right)  =  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}$ ,所以 ${\left( \sqrt{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) }^{2} < {\left( \sqrt{{x}_{1}} + \sqrt{{x}_{2}}\right) }^{2}$ ,

所以 $4\sqrt{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}} < 4\sqrt{{x}_{1}} + 4\sqrt{{x}_{2}}$ ,

即 $r\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  < r\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + r\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,即 $y = r\left( x\right)$ 是 $L$ 型函数.

9. (1) 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2}$ ,故对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  -  f\left( {-x}\right)  = 0$ . 又因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的“约束函数”,则对任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \geq  \left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|$ ,取 ${x}_{1} = x \in  \mathbf{R},{x}_{2} =  - x$ ,可得 $0 =  \mid  f\left( x\right)  -  f\left( {-x}\right) \left|  \geq  \right| g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {-x}\right)  \mid$ 恒成立,即 $g\left( x\right)  = g\left( {-x}\right)$ 对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 成立，故 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数；

(2)因为 $y = {ax}\left( {a > 0}\right) ,y = {x}^{3}$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数，则 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数,设 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,则 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ , 进而 $\left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,可得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  -  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) ,g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) ,$ 所以 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{2}\right) ,f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,设 $u\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + g\left( x\right) ,v\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  -  g\left( x\right)$ ,则 $y = u\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = v\left( x\right)$ 均为 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数,因为 ${u}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a + 3{x}^{2} + \cos x \geq  0,{v}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a + 3{x}^{2} - \cos x \geq  0$ 恒成立,对于 ${v}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a + 3{x}^{2} - \cos x \geq  0$ 恒成立,因为 $3{x}^{2} \geq  0$ , $- \cos x \geq   - 1$ ,当且仅当 $x = 0$ 时,等号成立,所以 $a + 3{x}^{2} -  \cos x \geq  a - 1 \geq  0$ ,解得 $a \geq  1$ ,当 $a \geq  1$ 时, ${u}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a + 3{x}^{2} +  \cos x \geq  a + \cos x \geq  0$ 恒成立,所以实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $a \geq  1$ ; (3)设 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ，因为 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是严格减函数，所以 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  >  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,即 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > 0$ ,而 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right|  \geq  \left| {g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - }\right|  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \mid$ ,所以 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  > 0$ ,所以对任意 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,都有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ， \ding{172}  首先证明:当 $0 < x < 1$ 时， $f\left( 0\right)  <  f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 1\right)$ ,假设存在 $0 < {x}_{0} < 1$ ,且 $f\left( 1\right)  < f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,设 $h\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - f\left( 1\right)$ ,则 $h\left( 0\right)  < 0,h\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > 0$ ,所以存在 ${x}_{3} \in  \left( {0,{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,使得 $h\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = 0$ ,得 $f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = f\left( 1\right)$ ,与结论对任意 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2},f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 矛盾,所以不存在 $0 < {x}_{0} < 1$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  <  f\left( 1\right)  < f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,同理可得: 也不存在 $0 < {x}_{0} < 1$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  <  f\left( 0\right)$ ,所以当 $0 < x < 1$ 时, $f\left( 0\right)  < f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 1\right)$ .  \ding{173}  再证明: 当 $0 < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < 1$ 时, $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,假设存在 $0 < {x}_{1} <  {x}_{2} < 1$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,则 $f\left( 0\right)  < f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  < f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  <  f\left( 1\right)$ ,设 $h\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,则 $h\left( 0\right)  < 0,h\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > 0$ ,所以存在 ${x}_{3} \in  \left( {0,{x}_{1}}\right)$ ,使得 $h\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = 0$ ,得 $f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,与结论对任意 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2},f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 矛盾,所以假设不成立, 即对任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,都有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,所以 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上的严格增函数.

\section*{第 4 章 幂函数、指数函数与对数函数}

\section*{考点 1 幂函数及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. ${a}^{\dfrac{5}{4}}$

【解析】 $\sqrt{{a}^{2}\sqrt{a}} = \sqrt{{a}^{2} \cdot  {a}^{\dfrac{1}{2}}} = \sqrt{{a}^{\dfrac{5}{2}}} = {a}^{\dfrac{5}{4}}$ . 故答案为: ${a}^{\dfrac{5}{4}}$ . 2. $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$

【解析】设幂函数 $y = {x}^{a},a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,代入点 $\left( {3,\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ 可得 ${3}^{a} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3} = {3}^{-\dfrac{1}{2}}$ ,即 $a =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,可得 $y = {x}^{-\dfrac{1}{2}} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}$ ,因为 $\sqrt{x} > 0$ ,可得 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}} > 0$ ,所以该幂函数的值域是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ . 故答案为: $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ .

3. 9

【解析】 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{a},4 = {2}^{a},a = 2,f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2},f\left( 3\right)  = 9$ .

4. $- \dfrac{4}{25}$

【解析】 $f\left( \dfrac{8}{125}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{8}{125}\right) }^{\dfrac{2}{3}} = \dfrac{4}{25} \Longrightarrow  f\left( {-\dfrac{8}{125}}\right)  =  - f\left( \dfrac{8}{125}\right)  =  - \dfrac{4}{25}$ .

5.  \ding{173} 

【解析】因为幂函数 $y = {x}^{a}$ 在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数，所以 $a > 0$ ，故 \ding{175} 不满足题意，因为该幂函数图像关于原点成中心对称，所以 $y = {x}^{a}$ 为奇函数，根据奇函数的性质 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,因为 $y = {x}^{\dfrac{1}{2}} = \sqrt{x}$ 的定义域为 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ , 所以图像不关于原点成中心对称, 故  \ding{172}  不满足题意; 因为 $y = {x}^{\dfrac{1}{3}} = \sqrt[3]{x}$ 的定义域为 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ ,且 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \sqrt[3]{-x} =  - \sqrt[3]{x} =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,故 \ding{173}  满足题意; 因为 $y = {x}^{\dfrac{2}{3}} = \sqrt[3]{{x}^{2}}$ 的定义域为 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ ,且 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \sqrt[3]{{\left( -x\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt[3]{{x}^{2}} = f\left( x\right)$ , 故 \ding{174} 不满足题意. 故答案为: \ding{173} .

6. $- \dfrac{2}{3}$

【解析】因为图像关于 $y$ 轴对称,所以函数是偶函数; 又因为图像与坐标轴无交点,所以指数 $a$ 为负数. 综上所述, $a =  - \dfrac{2}{3}$ . 故答案为: $- \dfrac{2}{3}$ .

7. $\left( {-1,0}\right)$

【解析】 $g\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{3},x \geq  0, \\  {\left( -x\right) }^{3} =  - {x}^{3},x < 0, \end{array}\right.$ 由 $g\left( x\right)  <  - x$ ,得  \ding{172}  $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x \geq  0 \\  {x}^{3} <  - x \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x > 0 \\  {x}^{2} <  - 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  x \in  \varnothing }\right. }\right.$ ;  \ding{173}  $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x < 0 \\   - {x}^{3} <  - x \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x < 0 \\  {x}^{2} < 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right. }\right.  - 1 < x < 0$ ; 综上,不等式 $g\left( x\right)  <  - x$ 的解集为 $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

8. C

9. B

\section*{三、解答题}

10. (1) 由题意, $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {2 - x}\right)  = {2}^{x} - {2}^{2 - x} = {2}^{x} - \dfrac{4}{{2}^{x}} = 3$ , 令 $t = {2}^{x},t > 0$ ,则有 $t - \dfrac{4}{t} = 3$ ,即 ${t}^{2} - {3t} - 4 = 0$ ,得 $\left( {t - 4}\right) (t +  1) = 0$ ,解得 $t = 4$ 或 $t =  - 1$ (舍去),所以 $t = {2}^{x} = 4$ ,则 $x = 2$ ;

(2)假设存在实数 $a$ ，使函数 $y = f\left( x\right)  + {af}\left( {-x}\right)  = {2}^{x} +  a \cdot  {2}^{-x}$ 是奇函数,则 $x = 0$ 时, $y = 1 + a = 0$ ,解得 $a =  - 1$ . $a =  - 1$ 时,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)  + {af}\left( {-x}\right)  = {2}^{x} - {2}^{-x}$ ,定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 设函数 $g\left( x\right)  = y = {2}^{x} - {2}^{-x},x \in  \mathbf{R}$ . 对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R},g\left( {-x}\right)  =  {2}^{-x} - {2}^{x} =  - g\left( x\right)$ ,故函数 $g\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} - {2}^{-x}$ 为奇函数. 综上, 存在实数 $a =  - 1$ ,使函数 $y = f\left( x\right)  + {af}\left( {-x}\right)$ 是奇函数.

11. (1) 由已知代入可得不等式: ${\log }_{2}\left( {{2}^{x} - 1}\right)  + {\log }_{2}{2}^{x} \leq  1  \Leftrightarrow  {\log }_{2}\left\lbrack  {\left( {{2}^{x} - 1}\right) {2}^{x}}\right\rbrack   \leq  {\log }_{2}2$ ,根据对数函数的单调性可得: $\left( {{2}^{x} - 1}\right) {2}^{x} \leq  2$ 且 ${2}^{x} - 1 > 0$ ,则 ${\left( {2}^{x}\right) }^{2} - {2}^{x} - 2 \leq  0 \Leftrightarrow  \left( {{2}^{x} - 2}\right)  \left( {{2}^{x} + 1}\right)  \leq  0$ 且 $x > 0$ ,解得: $\{ x \mid  0 < x \leq  1\}$ ;

(2)由已知可得:

${2}^{m\sin {x}_{0}} \cdot  {2}^{m\cos {x}_{0}} = {\left( {2}^{2 + \sin 2{x}_{0}}\right) }^{2} \Leftrightarrow  {2}^{m\sin {x}_{0} + m\cos {x}_{0}}  = {2}^{4 + 2\sin 2{x}_{0}} \Leftrightarrow  m\sin {x}_{0} + m\cos {x}_{0}  = 4 + 2\sin 2{x}_{0}$ ,

则 $m = \dfrac{4 + 2\sin 2{x}_{0}}{\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}} = 2\dfrac{1 + \sin 2{x}_{0} + 1}{\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}}$

$= 2\dfrac{{\left( \sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}\right) }^{2} + 1}{\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}},$

$m = 2\left( {\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0} + \dfrac{1}{\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}}}\right) ,$

令 $t = \sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0} = \sqrt{2}\sin \left( {{x}_{0} + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ ,因为 ${x}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ , 所以 ${x}_{0} + \dfrac{\pi }{4} \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{3\pi }{4}}\right\rbrack$ ,即 $\sin \left( {{x}_{0} + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},1}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $t = \sqrt{2}\sin \left( {{x}_{0} + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,此时 $y = t + \dfrac{1}{t}$ 在 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,

则 $y \in  \left\lbrack  {2,\dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack  ,m = 2\left( {\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0} + \dfrac{1}{\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}}}\right)$ ,

则 $m \in  \left\lbrack  {4,3\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,故 $m$ 的最小值为 4 .

\section*{考点 2 指数函数及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 1

【解析】当 $x > 0$ 时, $\ln {x}_{0} + 1 = 1 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{0} = 1$ ,符合题意; 当 $x \leq  0$ 时, ${2}^{{x}_{0}} + 1 = 1 \Longrightarrow$ 无解. 故 ${x}_{0} = 1$ .

2. 二

【解析】已知 $a \geq  1,b <  - 1$ ,则指数函数 $y = {a}^{x}$ 单调递增,过定点 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,且 $\left| b\right|  > 1$ ,函数 $y = {a}^{x} + b$ 的图像是由函数 $y = {a}^{x}$ 向下平移 $\left| b\right|$ 个单位,作出函数 $y = {a}^{x} + b$ 的图像,可知图像必定不经过第二象限. 故答案为:二.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/26_1365_1905_214_151_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

3. -1

【解析】由题意知, $f\left( a\right)  = a$ ; 当 $a \geq  0$ 时,有 $\dfrac{1}{2}a - 1 = a$ ,解得 $a =  - 2$ (舍去)；当 $a < 0$ 时，有 $\dfrac{1}{a} = a$ ，解得 $a = 1$ (舍去)或 $a =  - 1$ . 所以实数 $a$ 的值是: $a =  - 1$ . 故答案为:-1.

4. ${\log }_{2}3$

【解析】 $f\left( x\right)  = 2$ 等价于 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2}^{x} - 1 = 2 \\  x > 1 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x - 1 = 2 \\  x \leq  1 \end{array}\right.$ ,故 $x =  {\log }_{2}3$ ,故答案为: ${\log }_{2}3$ .

5. -1

【解析】函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的奇函数, $f\left( 0\right)  = 1 +  b = 0,b =  - 1,f\left( x\right)  = \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {{e}}^{x} - 1,x \geq  0 \\  1 - {{e}}^{-x},x < 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  f\left( {-\ln 2}\right)  = 1 - {{e}}^{\ln 2} = }\right.  1 - 2 =  - 1$ .

6. $\left( {0,1}\right)$

【解析】 $x \leq  0,g\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} - 1 \in  ( - 1,0\rbrack ,x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right) ,y =  f\left( x\right)  \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ .

7. -1

【解析】当 $x > 0$ 时,有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{3}^{-x}} = {3}^{x} = f\left( x\right)$ ,又 $f\left( x\right)$ 定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,故 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数,又当 $x > 0$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,故对 $f\left( m\right)  \geq  f\left( {m + 2}\right)$ ,有 $\left| m\right|  \geq  \left| {m + 2}\right|$ ,即 ${m}^{2} \geq  {\left( m + 2\right) }^{2}$ ,即有 ${4m} + 4 \leq  0$ ,解得 $m \leq   - 1$ ,故 $m$ 的最大值为 -1 .

8. $x = 3$

【解析】 $x < 0,g\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} + 1 = 2$ ,无解, $x \geq  0,{\log }_{2}\left( {x + 1}\right)  =  2 \Longrightarrow  x = 3$ .

9. $- \dfrac{19}{9}$

【解析】因为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数,所以 $f\left( 2\right)  =  - f\left( {-2}\right)  =  - \left\lbrack  {{3}^{-2} - \left( {-2}\right) }\right\rbrack   =  - \dfrac{19}{9}.$

10. $\left( {1,2}\right)$

【解析】 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} + x$ ,在 $\mathbf{R}$ 严格增且 $f\left( 1\right)  = 3,f\left( \left| {{2x} - 3}\right| \right)  <  3 \Longrightarrow  \left| {{2x} - 3}\right|  < 1 \Longrightarrow  x \in  \left( {1,2}\right)$ .

11. $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \{ 1\}$

【解析】因为 $0 < a < 2$ ,所以当 $x \in  ( - \infty ,2\rbrack$ 时, $y = (a -$ 2) $x + {4a} + 1$ 严格减，其 ${y}_{\min } = {6a} - 3$ ; 当 $a = 1$ 时，因为 $y =  \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - x + 5,x \leq  2 \\  2,x > 2 \end{array}\right.$ ,显然有 ${y}_{\min } = 2$ ,当 $0 < a < 1$ 时, $y = 2{a}^{x - 1}$ 在 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减,且 $y = 2{a}^{x - 1} \in  \left( {0,{2a}}\right)$ ,要想有最小值, 则 ${6a} - 3 \leq  0 \Longrightarrow  0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,当 $1 < a < 2$ 时, $y = 2{a}^{x - 1}$ 在 (2, $+ \infty )$ 上严格增,且 $y = 2{a}^{x - 1} \in  \left( {{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ ,要想有最小值, 则 ${6a} - 3 \leq  {2a} \Longrightarrow  a \leq  \dfrac{3}{4}$ (舍); 综上, $a \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \{ 1\}$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

12. B

【解析】ACD 三个选项中函数定义域是 $\mathbf{R}$ ,函数 $y = \lg \left| x\right|$ 的定义域是 $\{ x \mid  x \neq  0\} ,\lg \left| {-x}\right|  = \lg \left| x\right| ,y = \lg \left| x\right|$ 为偶函数,由对数函数性质知其值域为 $\mathbf{R}$ , B 符合; ${\left( -x\right) }^{\dfrac{1}{3}} =  - {x}^{\dfrac{1}{3}}$ , 因此 $y = {x}^{\dfrac{1}{3}}$ 是奇函数, ${A}$ 不符; ${{e}}^{-x} + {{e}}^{-\left( {-x}\right) } = {{e}}^{-x} + {{e}}^{x} =  {{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x}$ ,因此 $y = {{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x}$ 是偶函数,但 $y = {{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x} \geq  2\sqrt{{{e}}^{x} \cdot  {{e}}^{-x}} = 2$ ,当且仅当 $x = 0$ 时取等号,因此函数值域不是 $\mathbf{R},{C}$ 不符; ${\left( -x\right) }^{3}\cos \left( {-x}\right)  =  - {x}^{3}\cos x,y = {x}^{3}\cos x$ 是奇函数, D 不符. 故选: B.

13. D

【解析】因为 $a > 0,{a}^{s} > a$ ,所以 ${a}^{s - 1} > 1 = {a}^{0}$ ,当 $a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $y = {a}^{x}$ 定义域上严格减，此时若 $s - 1 < 0$ ，则一定有 ${a}^{s - 1} >  1 = {a}^{0}$ 成立，故 D 正确，C 错误；当 $a \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时， $y = {a}^{x}$ 定义域上严格增,要满足 ${a}^{s - 1} > 1 = {a}^{0}$ ,需 $s > 1$ ,即 ${A},{B}$ 错误. 故选: D.

14. A

【解析】 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{x} - 1}{{2}^{x} + 1} = \dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1 - 2}{{2}^{x} + 1} = 1 + \dfrac{-2}{{2}^{x} + 1},x \uparrow  ,f\left( x\right)  \uparrow$ , $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{x} - 1}{{2}^{x} + 1} + \dfrac{{2}^{-x} - 1}{{2}^{-x} + 1} = 0$ ,所以函数 $y = \dfrac{{2}^{x} - 1}{{2}^{x} + 1}$ 在定义域 $\left( {-\infty , + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,又是奇函数,故选 A.

15. B

【解析】对于 \ding{172} : $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - 3$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递减,且 $f\left( {-1}\right)  = 2$ , $f\left( 1\right)  =  - 2$ ,则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像与圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 有且只有两个公共点，故 \ding{172} 正确；对于 \ding{173} :根据对称性，假设正方形的中心在原点,设 ${OA}$ 直线方程为 $y = {kx}$ ,直线 ${OB}$ 的方程 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}x$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} \\  y = 3{x}^{2} - 3 \end{array}\right.$ ,

则 ${x}^{2} = k + 3$ ,同理可得: ${x}_{2}^{2} = 3 - \dfrac{1}{k}$ ,由 $\left| {OA}\right|  = \left| {OB}\right|$ 得, $\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\sqrt{k + 3} = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}^{2}}}\sqrt{3 - \dfrac{1}{k}}$ ,即 ${k}^{4} + 3{k}^{3} - {3k} + 1 =$ 0,所以 $\left( {{k}^{2} + {2k} - 1}\right) \left( {{k}^{2} + k - 1}\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $k = \dfrac{-1 \pm  \sqrt{5}}{2}$ 或 $k =  - 1 \pm  \sqrt{2}$ ,所以不止一个正方形 ${ABCD}$ ,其四个顶点都在函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上,故 \ding{173} 不正确. 故选: B.

\section*{三、解答题}

16.(1)由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数，可知 $f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - f\left( 1\right)$ ，即 $- \left( {1 + {2a}}\right)  =  - \left( {2 + a}\right)$ ,解得 $a = 1$ ,当 $a = 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{{2}^{x} + 1}{{2}^{x} - 1},f\left( {-x}\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{-x} + 1}{{2}^{-x} - 1} = \dfrac{1 + {2}^{x}}{1 - {2}^{x}} =  - f\left( x\right)$ 对一切非零实数 $x$ 恒成立,故 $a = 1$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数;

(2)由 $f\left( 2\right)  = a$ ，可得 $\dfrac{4 + a}{3} = a$ ，解得 $a = 2$ ，所以 $f\left( x\right)  > a \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{{2}^{x} + 2}{{2}^{x} - 1} > 2 \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{{2}^{x} - 4}{{2}^{x} - 1} < 0 \Leftrightarrow  1 < {2}^{x} < 4$ ,解得: $0 < x < 2$ ,所以满足 $f\left( x\right)  > a$ 的实数 $x$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {0,2}\right)$ .

17. ( 1 )方法一:由 $f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{4 + k}{2},f\left( {-1}\right)  = \dfrac{{4k} + 1}{2}$ ，且 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数，满足 $\dfrac{4 + k}{2} =  - \dfrac{{4k} + 1}{2}$ ，解方程得 $k =  - 1$ . (利用 $f\left( 0\right)  = 1 + k = 0$ 解得 $k =  - 1$ 的同样得分

当 $k =  - 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{4}^{x} - 1}{{2}^{x}} = {2}^{x} - {2}^{-x}$ ,其定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,对任意给定的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,并且 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {2}^{-x} - {2}^{x} =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,因此当 $k =  - 1$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数.

方法二: 根据题意,假设存在实数 $k$ ,使函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,对任意给定的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,且 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ . 即 $\dfrac{{4}^{x} + k}{{2}^{x}} + \dfrac{{4}^{-x} + k}{{2}^{-x}} = 0$ ,变形可得: $(k +$ 1) ${4}^{x} + k + 1 = 0$ 对于任意实数恒成立,解得 $k =  - 1$ ,因此当 $k =  - 1$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数;

( 2 ) $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} + \dfrac{1}{{2}^{x}}$ ，若关于 $x$ 的不等式 $f\left( x\right)  \geq  a$ 恒成立， 则 $a \leq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\min }$ . 令 $t = {2}^{x} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = h\left( t\right)  =  t + \dfrac{1}{t} \geq  2$ ,等号当且仅当 $t = 1$ ,即 $x = 0$ 时成立. 故 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } =  f\left( 0\right)  = 2$ ，所以 $a \leq  2$ ，因此，实数 $a$ 的取值范围是( - ∞,2 ).

18.(1)将点 $\left( {2,9}\right)$ 代入函数解析式，得: $9 = {a}^{2}$ ，因为 $a > 0$ 且 $a \neq  1$ ，所以 $a = 3$ . 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {3}^{x}$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上是严格增函数， $f\left( \left| {{2x} - 1}\right| \right)  > 3$ 所以 ${3}^{\left| 2x - 1\right| } > 3,\left| {{2x} - 1}\right|  > 1$ ,解得 $x < 0$ 或 $x > 1$ ,所以原不等式的解集为 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ;

(2)由题意，数列 $f\left( 1\right) ,f\left( {tx}\right) ,f\left( {{x}^{2} + 2}\right)$ 是等比数列，得: ${\left\lbrack  f\left( tx\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} = f\left( 1\right)  \cdot  f\left( {{x}^{2} + 2}\right)$ ,即: ${\left( {a}^{tx}\right) }^{2} = {a}^{1} \cdot  {a}^{{x}^{2} + 2}$ ,化简得 ${2tx} = {x}^{2} + 3$ ,因为 $x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ ,所以 ${2t} = x + \dfrac{3}{x}$ ,因为 $y =  x + \dfrac{3}{x}$ 在 $(0,1\rbrack$ 上是严格减函数,所以 $x + \dfrac{3}{x} \in  \lbrack 4, + \infty )$ ,所以 $t$ 的取值范围是 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ . (若 ${\left( {3}^{tx}\right) }^{2} = {3}^{1} \cdot  {3}^{{x}^{2} + 2}$ ,化简得 ${2tx} = {x}^{2} + 3$ 的解法,最多扣一分).

\section*{考点 3 对数函数及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$

【解析】 $y = {\log }_{2}x \Longrightarrow  x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ .

2. $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .

【解析】 ${x}^{2} - 1 > 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .

3. 2

【解析】将点 $\left( {4,2}\right)$ 代入 $y = {\log }_{a}x$ 得 $2 = {\log }_{a}4$ ，解得 $a = 2$ ， 故答案为: 2 .

4. $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$

【解析】 $\dfrac{x}{x - 1} > 0 \Longrightarrow  x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .

5. 0

【解析】由解析式知 $f\left( 1\right)  = \ln 1 = 0$ . 故答案为:0.

6. $\left( {1,2}\right)$

【解析】令 $x = 1$ ,则 $y = 2 \Longrightarrow$ 定点坐标为 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ .

7. $4 + \dfrac{1}{a}$

【解析】由题意得, ${\log }_{2}{48} = \dfrac{{\log }_{3}{48}}{{\log }_{3}2} = \dfrac{{\log }_{3}{16} + {\log }_{3}3}{{\log }_{3}2} =  \dfrac{4{\log }_{3}2 + 1}{{\log }_{3}2} = \dfrac{{4a} + 1}{a} = 4 + \dfrac{1}{a}$ . 故答案为: $4 + \dfrac{1}{a}$ .

8. 1

【解析】由 ${2}^{a} = {3}^{b} = 6$ 可知 $a = {\log }_{2}6,b = {\log }_{3}6$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{a} +  \dfrac{1}{b} = {\log }_{6}2 + {\log }_{6}3 = {\log }_{6}6 = 1$ . 故答案为: 1 .

9. $\left( {-1,2}\right)$

【解析】令 $x =  - 1$ ,可得 $y = {a}^{-1 + 1} - {\log }_{a}\left( {-1 + 2}\right)  + 1 =  {a}^{0} - {\log }_{a}1 + 1 = 2$ . 所以定点 $A$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-1,2}\right)$ . 故答案为: $\left( {-1,2}\right)$ .

10. $\left\{  {a\left| { - \dfrac{1}{2} < a < 0\text{ 或 }a > 2}\right. }\right\}$

【解析】因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的奇函数，所以 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ ,当 $x < 0$ 时, $- x > 0$ ,所以 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  =  - {\log }_{2}\left( {-x}\right)$ ,若 $f\left( a\right)  > 1$ ,当 $a > 0$ 时,可得 ${\log }_{2}a > 1$ ,解得 $a > 2$ ,当 $a < 0$ 时, 可得 $- {\log }_{2}\left( {-a}\right)  > 1$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{1}{2} < a < 0$ ,当 $a = 0$ 时,可得 $0 > 1$ , 显然不成立,故 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left\{  {a\left| { - \dfrac{1}{2} < a < 0\text{ 或 }a > 2}\right. }\right\}$ . 故答案为: $\left\{  {a\left| { - \dfrac{1}{2} < a < 0\text{ 或 }a > 2}\right. }\right\}$ .

11. 6

【解析】因为 ${9}^{a} = {4}^{b} = m > 0$ ,所以 $a = {\log }_{9}m = \dfrac{\lg m}{\lg 9},b =  {\log }_{4}m = \dfrac{\lg m}{\lg 4}$ ,因为 $\dfrac{1}{a} + \dfrac{1}{b} = 2$ ,所以 $\dfrac{\lg 9}{\lg m} + \dfrac{\lg 4}{\lg m} = \dfrac{\lg 9 + \lg 4}{\lg m} =  \dfrac{\lg {36}}{\lg m} = {\log }_{m}{36} = 2$ ,所以 ${m}^{2} = {36}$ ,因为 $m > 0$ ,所以 $m = 6$ . 故答案为: 6 .

12. $(0,{10}\rbrack  \cup  \lbrack {1000}, + \infty )$

【解析】因为 $\left| {f\left( x\right) }\right|  + \left| {g\left( x\right) }\right|  = \left| {f\left( x\right)  + g\left( x\right) }\right|$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right) g\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ ,即 $\left( {\lg x - 1}\right) \left( {\lg x - 3}\right)  \geq  0$ ,解得 $\lg x \leq  1$ 或 $\lg x \geq$ 3,所以 $x$ 的取值范围是 $(0,{10}\rbrack  \cup  \lbrack {1000}, + \infty )$ ,故答案为: $(0,{10}\rbrack  \cup  \lbrack {1000}, + \infty )$ .

13. 130

【解析】因为 $I = {10}{W}/{{m}}^{2},{I}_{0} = {10}^{-{12}}{W}/{{m}}^{2}$ ,所以其相应的声强级为 $y = {10}\lg \dfrac{10}{{10}^{-{12}}} = {10}\lg {10}^{13} = {130}{{dB}}$ . 故答案为: 130 . 14. 4

【解析】不妨设 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,由 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \Longrightarrow  \left| {{\log }_{2}{x}_{1}}\right|  =  \left| {{\log }_{2}{x}_{2}}\right|  \Longrightarrow   - {\log }_{2}{x}_{1} = {\log }_{2}{x}_{2} \Longrightarrow  {\log }_{2}\left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} = 1$ , ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,所以 $4{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 4{x}_{1} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}} \geq  4$ ,当且仅当 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, 等号成立.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/28_1115_1499_256_216_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

15. $\left( {-\infty ,{e} - 1}\right)$ .

【解析】数形结合,交点的横坐标为 ${e} - 1$ ,所以解集为 $\left( {-\infty ,{e} - 1}\right)$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/28_1098_1847_290_191_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

16. $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{4},2}\right\rbrack$

【解析】函数 $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( {a{x}^{2} - {8x} + {15}}\right)$ ,令 $t = a{x}^{2} - {8x} +$ 15，则 $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}t$ ，函数 $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( {a{x}^{2} - {8x} + {15}}\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上严格增,由函数 $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}t$ 在定义域上严格减,则函数 $t =  a{x}^{2} - {8x} + {15}$ 在区间 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上严格减,有 ${t}^{\prime } = {2ax} - 8 \leq  0$ 在区间 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上恒成立,即 $a \leq  \dfrac{4}{x}$ 在 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上恒成立,得 $a \leq  2$ , 又 $a{x}^{2} - {8x} + {15} > 0$ 在区间 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上恒成立,则 $a > \dfrac{8}{x} - \dfrac{15}{{x}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ 上恒成立,令 $s = \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,则 $a > {8s} - {15}{s}^{2}$ 在 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},1}\right)$ 上恒成立,由二次函数的性质可得 $a \geq  \dfrac{8}{2} - \dfrac{15}{4} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,所以实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{4},2}\right\rbrack$ . 故答案为: $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{4},2}\right\rbrack$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

17. B

【解析】设处理池中的残留物初始时为 $a$ ,则 $t$ 小时后,处理池中的残留物为 $a{\left( 1 - {12}\% \right) }^{t}$ ,根据题意可得 $a(1 -  {0.12}{)}^{t} = {0.1a}$ ,即 ${0.88}{}^{t} = {0.1}$ ,解得 $t = {\log }_{0.88}{0.1} = \dfrac{\lg {0.1}}{\lg {0.88}} \approx$ 18. 因此,要使处理池中的污染物水平降到最初的 10\% ,大约需要的时间为 18 小时。故选:B.

\section*{三、解答题}

18.(1)因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的奇函数，所以 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right) ,f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $f\left( {-2}\right)  + f\left( 0\right)  =  - f\left( 2\right)  +  f\left( 0\right)  =  - 1 + 0 =  - 1;$

(2) $g\left( x\right)  = \left( {{\log }_{2}x}\right)  \cdot  \left( {{\log }_{2}\dfrac{x}{4}}\right)  = \left( {{\log }_{2}x}\right)  \cdot  \left( {{\log }_{2}x - 2}\right)  =  {\left( {\log }_{2}x\right) }^{2} - 2{\log }_{2}x,x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack$ ,令 ${\log }_{2}x = t,t \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ ,问题等价于求 $h\left( t\right)  = {t}^{2} - {2t},t \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ 的值域,因为函数 $h\left( t\right)  = {t}^{2} - {2t}$ 图像开口向上,对称轴为直线 $t = 1$ ,所以 $h{\left( t\right) }_{\min } = h\left( 1\right)  =  - 1,h{\left( t\right) }_{\max } = h\left( 3\right)  = 3$ ,所以函数 $g\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\left\lbrack  {-1,3}\right\rbrack$ .

19.(1)由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + 2 > 0 \\  2 - x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $- 2 < x < 2$ ，所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\left( {-2,2}\right)$ ,关于原点对称, $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {\log }_{a}\left( {-x + 2}\right)  -  {\log }_{a}\left( {2 + x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数;

(2) $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}\left( {x + 2}\right)  - {\log }_{a}\left( {2 - x}\right)  = {\log }_{a}\dfrac{x + 2}{2 - x}$ ,令 $u =  - \dfrac{x - 2 + 4}{x - 2} =  - 1 - \dfrac{4}{x - 2}$ ,则 $u =  - 1 - \dfrac{4}{x - 2}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,又 $y = {\log }_{a}x\left( {a > 1}\right)$ 为严格增函数,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{a}(x +$ 2) $- {\log }_{a}\left( {2 - x}\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,其最大值为 $f\left( 1\right)  = {\log }_{a}3  = 2$ ,解得 $a = \sqrt{3}$ .

20. ( 1 )已知函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像过点 $\left( {4,2}\right)$ ，所以 ${\log }_{a}4 =$ 2,即 ${a}^{2} = 4$ ,因为 $a > 0,a \neq  1$ ,所以 $a = 2$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ . 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ 的定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,且在定义域上严格增. 由 $f\left( {{2x} - 2}\right)  < f\left( x\right)$ 可得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {2x} - 2 > 0 \\  x > 0 \\  {2x} - 2 < x \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $1 < x < 2$ , 所以不等式的解集为 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ ;

(2)当 $a = \sqrt{2}$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}x$ ， $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ， $f\left( {ax}\right)  = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {\sqrt{2}x}\right)  = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\sqrt{2} + {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}x = 1 + {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}x,f(x +  2) = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + 2}\right)$ . 若 $f\left( {x + 1}\right) ,f\left( {ax}\right) ,f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 成等差数列,则 ${2f}\left( {ax}\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  + f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ,即 $2\left( {1 + {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}x}\right)  =  {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + 1}\right)  + {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ,所以 $2 + 2{\log }_{\sqrt{2}}x = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\lbrack (x +$ 1) $\left( {x + 2}\right) \rbrack$ ,即 ${\log }_{\sqrt{2}}{\left( \sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} + {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}{x}^{2} = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {{x}^{2} + {3x} + 2}\right)$ ,即 ${\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {2{x}^{2}}\right)  = {\log }_{\sqrt{2}}\left( {{x}^{2} + {3x} + 2}\right)$ ,则 $2{x}^{2} = {x}^{2} + {3x} + 2$ ,移项可得 ${x}^{2} - {3x} - 2 = 0$ . 对于一元二次方程 ${x}^{2} - {3x} - 2 = 0,\Delta  =  {\left( -3\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  \left( {-2}\right)  = 9 + 8 = {17} > 0$ ，所以方程有实数解，即存在 $x$ 使得 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 、 $f\left( {ax}\right)$ 、 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 成等差数列.

21. (1) 函数 $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\dfrac{2 + x}{x - 2}$ 的定义域为 $D = \left( {-\infty , - 2}\right)  \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ ,在 $D$ 中任取一个实数 $x$ ,都有 $- x \in  D$ ,并 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\dfrac{2 - x}{-x - 2} = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\dfrac{x - 2}{x + 2} = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}{\left( \dfrac{x + 2}{x - 2}\right) }^{-1} =  - f\left( x\right)$ . 因此, $y = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\dfrac{2 + x}{x - 2}$ 是奇函数;

(2) $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( {x + k}\right)$ 等价于 $x + k = \dfrac{x + 2}{x - 2}$ 即 $k = \dfrac{x + 2}{x - 2} -  x = \dfrac{4}{x - 2} - x + 1$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ 上有解. 记 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{4}{x - 2} - x + 1$ , 因为 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ 上为严格减函数,所以, $g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = g\left( 3\right)  =$ 2， $g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = g\left( 4\right)  =  - 1$ ，故 $g\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\left\lbrack  {-1,2}\right\rbrack$ ，因此，实数 $k$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {-1,2}\right\rbrack$ .

22. (1)因为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像过 $\left( {4,2}\right)$ ，故 ${\log }_{a}4 = 2$ ，故 ${a}^{2} =  4 \Longrightarrow  a =  \pm  2$ (负舍)，而 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上为严格增函数,由 $f\left( {{2x} - 2}\right)  < f\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow  0 < {2x} - 2 < x \Longrightarrow  1 < x < 2$ ,故 $f\left( {{2x} - 2}\right)  < f\left( x\right)$ 的解集为 $\{ x \mid  1 < x < 2\}$ ；

(2)因为存在 $x$ 使得 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)$ 、 $f\left( {ax}\right)$ 、 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 成等差数列， 所以 ${2f}\left( {ax}\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  + f\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 有解,即 $2{\log }_{a}\left( {ax}\right)  =  {\log }_{a}\left( {x + 1}\right)  + {\log }_{a}\left( {x + 2}\right)$ 有解,因为 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {ax} > 0 \\  x + 2 > x + 1 > 0 \\  a > 0,a \neq  1 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $x > 0 \Longrightarrow  {a}^{2}{x}^{2} = \left( {x + 1}\right) \left( {x + 2}\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上有解,即 ${a}^{2} =  \dfrac{{x}^{2} + {3x} + 2}{{x}^{2}} = 1 + \dfrac{3}{x} + \dfrac{2}{{x}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上有解,令 $t = \dfrac{1}{x} \in  (0$ , $+ \infty )$ ,则 $y = 2{t}^{2} + {3t} + 1$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的值域为 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)  \Longrightarrow  {a}^{2} \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)  \Longrightarrow  a \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ .

\section*{考点 4 幂指对函数值大小比较}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 100000

【解析】设 $\lg {x}_{3} = k,k \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,依题意, $\lg {x}_{1} = k + 2,\lg {x}_{4} =  k - 1,\lg {x}_{5} = k - 2,k \geq  3$ ,由 ${\left( \lg {x}_{4}\right) }^{2} < \lg {x}_{1} \cdot  \lg {x}_{5}$ ,得 ${\left( k - 1\right) }^{2} <  \left( {k + 2}\right) \left( {k - 2}\right)$ ,解得 $k > \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,因此 $k \geq  3$ ,则 $\lg {x}_{1} \geq  5,{x}_{1} \geq$ 100000,所以 ${x}_{1}$ 的最小值为 100000 . 故答案为: 100000 .

\section*{二、选择题}

2. A

【解析】 $a > b > 0$ ,则 ${a}^{2} > {b}^{2}$ ,故 A 正确; ${2}^{a} > {2}^{b}$ ,故 B 错误; ${a}^{\dfrac{1}{2}} > {b}^{\dfrac{1}{2}}$ ,故 C 错误; ${\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}a < {\log }_{\dfrac{1}{2}}b$ ,故 D 错误. 故选: A. 备注:解析中,确定 ${A}$ 正确后, ${B},{C},{D}$ 可不再研判. 同理,第 3 题中, ${C},{D}$ 可不再研判. 另解,可用特殊值法,取 $a = 2,b = 1$ , 可知选 A.

3. B

【解析】因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2},f\left( x\right)  = \left| x\right|$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的偶函数,所以当实数 $a,b$ 满足 $a > b$ 时, ${a}^{2} > {b}^{2},\left| a\right|  > \left| b\right|$ 不一定成立，故 A、C 不符合题意；因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3}$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增的奇函数，所以当实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 满足 $a > b$ 时，则 ${a}^{3} > {b}^{3}$ ，故 B 符合题意; 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{-1}$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right) ,\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减，所以当实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 满足 $a > b$ 时， ${a}^{-1} > {b}^{-1}$ 不一定成立，不符合题意. 故选: B.

4. D

【解析】对 ${A}$ ,取 $x =  - 1,y =  - 2$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{{x}^{2} + 1} > \dfrac{1}{{y}^{2} + 1}$ , A 错误; 对 ${B}$ ,取 $x =  - 1,y =  - 2$ ,则 $\ln 2 < \ln 5$ ,即 $\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  <  \ln \left( {{y}^{2} + 1}\right) ,{B}$ 错误; 对 ${C}$ ,取 $x = \dfrac{5\pi }{6},y = \dfrac{2\pi }{3}$ ,满足 $x > y$ ,但 $\sin \dfrac{5\pi }{6} < \sin \dfrac{2\pi }{3},{C}$ 错误; 对 ${D}$ ,因为幂函数 $y = {x}^{3}$ 在定义域 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,且 $x > y$ ,所以 ${x}^{3} > {y}^{3}$ , D 正确; 故选: D.

\section*{三、解答题}

5.(1)因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是定义在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的偶函数，即 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 时, $- x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {-x + 1}\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {-x + 1}\right)$ ;

(2)当 $x \in  \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + {2025}^{x}$ ，由幂函数和指数函数的单调性可得 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为严格增函数，又函数为偶函数,所以 $f\left( {{3t} - 2}\right)  < f\left( {t - 1}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  \left| {{3t} - 2}\right|  < \left| {t - 1}\right|$ ,两边平方后展开可得 $9{t}^{2} - {12t} + 4 < {t}^{2} - {2t} + 1$ ,即 $8{t}^{2} - {10t} + 3 < 0$ , 解得 $\dfrac{1}{2} < t < \dfrac{3}{4}$ .

6.(1) $f\left( 2\right)  = 4$ 即 ${a}^{2} = 4$ ，解得 $a = 2$ ，于是 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x}$ ，方程 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {-x}\right)  = 2$ 即为 ${2}^{x} - {2}^{-x} = 2$ ,令 $t = {2}^{x} > 0$ ,则有 $t -  \dfrac{1}{t} = 2$ 即 ${t}^{2} - {2t} - 1 = 0$ ,求得 $t = 1 \pm  \sqrt{2}$ (舍负),所以方程的解为 $x = {\log }_{2}\left( {1 + \sqrt{2}}\right)$ ;

(2)由已知 ${\left\lbrack  f\left( mx\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} \geq  f\left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  \cdot  f\left( {x + 3}\right)$ 得 ${\left( {a}^{mx}\right) }^{2} \geq  {a}^{{x}^{2} + 1} \cdot  {a}^{x + 3}$ ,整理得 ${a}^{2mx} \geq  {a}^{{x}^{2} + x + 4}$ ,因为 $0 < a < 1$ ,所以 ${2mx} \leq  {x}^{2} + x + 4$ ,从而 ${2m} \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{2} + x + 4}{x} = x + \dfrac{4}{x} + 1$ 对任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,2}\right\rbrack$ 恒成立,因为 $x + \dfrac{4}{x} + 1 \geq  2\sqrt{4} + 1 = 5$ (当且仅当 $x = 2$ 取等号),所以 ${2m} \leq  5$ ,即实数 $m$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{5}{2}$ .

7. ( 1 )存在实数 $a = 6$ ，使得函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数. 要使函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{3}\left( {x + a}\right)  + {\log }_{3}\left( {6 - x}\right)$ 有意义,须满足 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + a > 0 \\  6 - x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x >  - a \\  x < 6 \end{array}\text{ ,显然 } - a < 6}\right.$ ,即 $a >  - 6$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域 $D = \left( {-a,6}\right)$ . 当 $a \neq  6$ 时,函数定义域不关于原点对称,此时必然存在 $x \in  D$ 且 $- x \notin  D$ ,此时函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不是偶函数. 当 $a = 6$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{3}\left( {x + 6}\right)  + {\log }_{3}\left( {6 - x}\right)$ ,函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\left( {-6,6}\right)$ ,对于任意的 $x \in  \left( {-6,6}\right)$ ,都有 $- x \in  \left( {-6,6}\right)$ ,并且 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = {\log }_{3}\left( {-x + 6}\right)  + {\log }_{3}\left( {6 + x}\right)  =  f\left( x\right)$ ,因此函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是一个偶函数,综上所述,存在实数 $a = 6$ ,使得函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数;

(2)由 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( {6 - x}\right)$ ，得 ${\log }_{3}\left( {x + a}\right)  + {\log }_{3}\left( {6 - x}\right)  \leq  {\log }_{3}\left( {6 - x + a}\right)  + {\log }_{3}x$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + a > 0, \\  6 - x > 0, \\  6 - x + a > 0, \\  x > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 且 $\left( {x + a}\right) (6 -  x) \leq  \left( {6 - x + a}\right) x$  \ding{172} . 由  \ding{172} 得， ${ax} \geq  {3a}$ . 因为 $a >  - 3$ 且 $a \neq  0$ , 所以当 $- 3 < a < 0$ 时， $- a < x \leq  3$ ，当 $a > 0$ 时， $3 \leq  x < 6$ . 综上可得: 当 $- 3 < a < 0$ 时，不等式 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( {6 - x}\right)$ 的解集为 $\left( {-a,3}\right\rbrack$ ; 当 $a > 0$ 时,不等式 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( {6 - x}\right)$ 的解集为 $\lbrack 3,6)$ . 8.(1)由已知，函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ ，因为函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数,所以对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ , 所以 ${{e}}^{-\dfrac{{\left( x - \mu \right) }^{2}}{2}} = {{e}}^{-\dfrac{{\left( -x - \mu \right) }^{2}}{2}}$ ,所以 ${\left( x + u\right) }^{2} = {\left( x - u\right) }^{2}$ ,所以 $u = 0$ ,所以 $y = f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2}}$ ,因为 $f\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{e}}}$ ,所以 ${{e}}^{-\dfrac{{t}^{2}}{2}} =  {{e}}^{-\dfrac{1}{2}}$ ,因为 $y = {{e}}^{x}$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数,所以 $- \dfrac{{t}^{2}}{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ , 所以 ${t}^{2} = 1$ ,所以 $t =  \pm  1$ ;

(2)因为 $- \dfrac{{\left( x - u\right) }^{2}}{2} \leq  0$ 且 $y = {{e}}^{x}$ 是 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数，所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-\dfrac{{\left( x - u\right) }^{2}}{2}} \in  (0,1\rbrack$ ,因为 $k > 0$ ,所以 $k + \dfrac{1}{k} \geq  2$ ,当且仅当 $k = 1$ 时等号成立, $k + \dfrac{1}{k}$ 的最小值为 2,因为 ${2f}\left( x\right)  \leq  2$ ,所以对任意的正实数 $k$ 和实数 $x,k + \dfrac{1}{k} \geq  {2f}\left( x\right)$ 恒成立.

9. ( 1 )由函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\log }_{2}x$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 严格增，所以 0< ${3x} - 2 < {2x} + 1 \Longrightarrow  x \in  \left( {\dfrac{2}{3},3}\right)$ ;

(2)原问题等价于关于 $x$ 的方程 ${\log }_{2}x + 1 = 2{\log }_{2}\left( {x - a}\right)$ 恰有一个实数解,求实数 $a$ 的取值范围. 即 $\dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{2}\left( {2x}\right)  = {\log }_{2}\left( {x - a}\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恰有一个实数解,等价于 $\sqrt{2x} = x - a$ 在 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恰有一个实数解,即 $a = x - \sqrt{2x}$ 在 $x \in  (0$ , $+ \infty )$ 上恰有一个实数解. 令 $\sqrt{2x} = t\left( {t > 0}\right)$ ,则 $a = \dfrac{1}{2}{t}^{2} - t$ 在 $t \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恰有一个实数解. 画出关于 $t$ 的二次函数在 $t \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的图像可知, $a \in  \left\{  {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\}   \cup  \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 时只有一个交点; 所以 $a \in  \left\{  {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\}   \cup  \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ .

10. ( 1 )证明:因为 ${f}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = a\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  - b\sin \left( {x + t}\right)$ ，

${f}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - a\sin \left( {x + t}\right)  - b\cos \left( {x + t}\right) ,$

${f}^{\left( 3\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - a\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  + b\sin \left( {x + t}\right) ,$

${f}^{\left( 4\right) }\left( x\right)  = a\sin \left( {x + t}\right)  + b\cos \left( {x + t}\right)  = f\left( x\right) ,$

所以,对任意实数 $a,b,t$ ,都有 ${f}^{\left( 4\right) }\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ;

(2) $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{mx} + n\cos x$ ,

${g}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = m{{e}}^{mx} - n\sin x,$

${g}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  = {m}^{2}{{e}}^{mx} - n\cos x,$

由题意知, ${{e}}^{mx} + n\cos x = {m}^{2}{{e}}^{mx} - n\cos x$ 对任意实数 $x$ 恒成立, 令 $x = 0$ ,则 $1 + n = {m}^{2} - n$ ,即 ${m}^{2} = 1 + {2n}$ ,令 $x = \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,

则 ${{e}}^{\dfrac{m\pi }{2}} = {m}^{2}{{e}}^{\dfrac{m\pi }{2}}$ ,则 ${m}^{2} = 1$ ,所以 $m = 1,n = 0$ 或 $m =  - 1,n = 0$ . 若 $m = 1,n = 0$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x},{g}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} = g\left( x\right)$ ,最小导周期不是 2,矛盾; 若 $m =  - 1,n = 0$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}$ ,

${g}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - {{e}}^{-x},{g}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x} = g\left( x\right)$ ,最小导周期为 2,

符合要求,所以 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}$ .

$M\left( {a,b}\right)  = \sqrt{{\left( a - b\right) }^{2} + {\left( a + 1 + g\left( b\right) \right) }^{2}}  = \sqrt{{\left( a - b\right) }^{2} + {\left( -a - 1 - {{e}}^{-b}\right) }^{2}},$

可视为点 $P\left( {a, - a - 1}\right)$ 与点 $Q\left( {b,{{e}}^{-b}}\right)$ 之间的距离,当实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 变化时,点 $P\left( {a, - a - 1}\right)$ 在直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 上运动,点 $Q  \left( {b,{{e}}^{-b}}\right)$ 在曲线 $y = {{e}}^{-x}$ 上运动，因此所求最小值可转化为曲线 $y = {{e}}^{-x}$ 上的点到直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 距离的最小值，而曲线 $y  = {{e}}^{-x}$ 在直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 上方,平移直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 使其与曲线 $y = {{e}}^{-x}$ 相切,则切点到直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 的距离即为所求. 设切点 $M\left( {{x}_{0},{{e}}^{-{x}_{0}}}\right) ,{y}^{\prime } =  - {{e}}^{-x}$ ,切线斜率 $- {{e}}^{-{x}_{0}} =  - 1$ , 得 ${x}_{0} = 0$ ,切点为 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,点 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 到直线 $y =  - x - 1$ 距离 $d =  \dfrac{\left| 1 + 0 + 1\right| }{\sqrt{{1}^{2} + {1}^{2}}} = \sqrt{2}$ . 即 $M\left( {a,b}\right)$ 的最小值为 $\sqrt{2}$ ;

(3) ${h}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - \omega \sin \left( {\omega x}\right) ,{h}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  =  - {\omega }^{2}\cos \left( {\omega x}\right)$ ,

记 $\varphi \left( x\right)  = {h}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  - x$ ,即 $\varphi \left( x\right)  =  - {\omega }^{2}\cos {\omega x} - x$ .

由 $\varphi \left( x\right)  = {h}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( x\right)  - x \leq  0$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恒成立及存在 ${x}_{0} >$ 0 使 $\varphi \left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {h}^{\left( 2\right) }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - {x}_{0} = 0$ ,可知 $x = {x}_{0}$ 是函数 $y =  \varphi \left( x\right)$ 的极大值点,于是 ${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {\omega }^{3}\sin \omega {x}_{0} - 1 = 0$ ,

则 $\sin \omega {x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{{\omega }^{3}}$  \ding{172} ，又 $\varphi \left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  - {\omega }^{2}\cos \omega {x}_{0} - {x}_{0} = 0$ ，

则 $\cos \omega {x}_{0} = \dfrac{-{x}_{0}}{{\omega }^{2}}$  \ding{173} ，由 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 得 $\dfrac{1}{{\omega }^{6}} + \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{\omega }^{4}} = 1$ ，

则 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{\omega }^{6} - 1}}{\omega }$ . 又因为 $\sin \omega {x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{{\omega }^{3}} > 0$ ,

$\cos \omega {x}_{0} = \dfrac{-{x}_{0}}{{\omega }^{2}} < 0$ ,所以 ${2k\pi } + \dfrac{\pi }{2} < \omega {x}_{0} < {2k\pi } + \pi \left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ,

由 $\omega {x}_{0} > 0$ 得 $k \geq  0$ ,又因为 $\dfrac{\pi }{2\omega } < {x}_{0} - \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega } < \dfrac{\pi }{\omega }\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ,

所以 $\varphi \left( {{x}_{0} - \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega }}\right)  =  - {\omega }^{2}\cos \left\lbrack  {\omega \left( {{x}_{0} - \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega }}\right) }\right\rbrack   - \left( {{x}_{0} - \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega }}\right)  =  - {\omega }^{2}\cos \omega {x}_{0} - {x}_{0} + \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega } = \dfrac{2k\pi }{\omega } \leq  0,$

有 $k \leq  0$ ，于是 $k = 0$ ，所以 $\dfrac{\pi }{2\omega } < {x}_{0} < \dfrac{\pi }{\omega }$ .

11. (1) 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + 1$ ，求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}$ ，

则 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + 1 > {{e}}^{x} = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 是 “超导函数”;

(2)函数 $F\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right) h\left( x\right)$ ，求导得 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) h\left( x\right)  +  g\left( x\right) {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,则 $F\left( x\right)  - {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right) h\left( x\right)  - {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) h\left( x\right)  -  g\left( x\right) {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \left\lbrack  {g\left( x\right)  - {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) }\right\rbrack  \left\lbrack  {h\left( x\right)  - {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right) }\right\rbrack   - {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,$ 由函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 都是 “超导函数”,得 $g\left( x\right)  -  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,h\left( x\right)  - {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,由对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  >  0,{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) {h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,因此 $F\left( x\right)  - {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,即 $F\left( x\right)  > {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以函数 $y = F\left( x\right)$ 是“超导函数”;

(3)由函数 $y = \varphi \left( x\right)$ 是“超导函数”,得对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R},\varphi \left( x\right)  >  {\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,令 $u\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\varphi \left( x\right) }{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,求导得 ${u}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\varphi \left( x\right)  - {\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right) }{{{e}}^{x}} > 0$ , 函数 $u\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,且 $u\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,由 $\varphi \left( {\ln t + 1 - {at}}\right)  =  {{e}}^{\ln t + 1 - {at}}$ ,得 $\dfrac{\varphi \left( {\ln t + 1 - {at}}\right) }{{{e}}^{\ln t + 1 - {at}}} = 1$ ,即 $u\left( {\ln t + 1 - {at}}\right)  = u\left( 1\right)$ ,因此 $\ln t + 1 - {at} = 1$ ,即 $a = \dfrac{\ln t}{t}$ ,令 $G\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{\ln t}{t}$ ,由有且仅有一个实数 $t$ 满足 $\varphi \left( {\ln t + 1 - {at}}\right)  = {{e}}^{\ln t + 1 - {at}}$ ,得直线 $y = a$ 与函数 $y = G\left( t\right)$ 的图像有且只有 1 个交点, ${G}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{1 - \ln t}{{t}^{2}}$ ,当 $0 <  t < {e}$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  > 0$ ; 当 $t > {e}$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $G\left( t\right)$ 在 $(0,{e}\rbrack$ 上严格增,函数值的集合为 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right\rbrack$ ,在 $\lbrack {e}, + \infty )$ 上严格减，函数值的集合为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right\rbrack$ ，因此当 $a \leq  0$ 或 $a = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ 时，直线 $y = a$ 与函数 $y = G\left( t\right)$ 的图像有且只有 1 个交点,所以 $a$ 的取值范围 $a \leq  0$ 或 $a = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ .

\section*{考点 2 数列的递推公式}
