\section*{二、选择题}

8. B

【解析】由题意 ${a}_{n}$ 、 ${b}_{n}$ 、 ${c}_{n} > 0$ ，不妨设 $A\left( {n,{a}_{n}}\right)$ ， $B\left( {n,{b}_{n}}\right)$ ， $C\left( {n,{c}_{n}}\right)$ ,三点均在第一象限内,由 ${c}_{n} = \lambda {a}_{n} + \left( {1 - \lambda }\right) {b}_{n}$ 可知, $\vv{BC} = \lambda \vv{BA},\lambda  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,故点 $C$ 恒在线段 ${AB}$ 上,则有 $\min \left\{  {{a}_{n}\text{ , }}\right.  \left. {b}_{n}\right\}   \leq  {c}_{n} \leq  \max \left\{  {{a}_{n},{b}_{n}}\right\}   < {a}_{n} + {b}_{n}$ . 即对任意的 $\lambda  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ , ${c}_{n} < {a}_{n} + {b}_{n}$ 恒成立,令 ${10x} - 9 = {2}^{x}$ ,构造函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x} -  {10x} + 9,x > 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x}\ln 2 - {10}$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 单调递增,又 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 3\right)  < 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( 4\right)  > 0$ ,存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {3,4}\right)$ ,使 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即当 $0 < x < {x}_{0}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递减; 当 $x > {x}_{0}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 单调递增; 故 $f\left( x\right)$ 至多 2 个零点,又由 $f\left( 1\right)  > 0,f\left( 2\right)  < 0,f\left( 5\right)  < 0,f\left( 6\right)  > 0$ ,可知 $f\left( x\right)$ 存在 2 个零点,不妨设 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1} < {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,且 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {1,2}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \left( {5,6}\right)$ .

 \ding{172}  若 ${a}_{n} \leq  {b}_{n}$ ，即 ${10n} - 9 \leq  {2}^{n}$ 时，此时 $n = 1$ 或 $n \geq  6$ . 则 ${a}_{n} \leq  {c}_{n} \leq  {b}_{n}$ ,可知 ${b}_{n} + {c}_{n} > {a}_{n}$ 成立,要使 ${a}_{n},{b}_{n},{c}_{n}$ 的值均能构成三角形,所以 ${a}_{n} + {c}_{n} > {b}_{n}$ 恒成立,故 ${b}_{n} < 2{a}_{n}$ ,所以有 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {10n} - 9 \leq  {2}^{n} \\  {2}^{n} < 2\left( {{10n} - 9}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $n = 6$ ;

 \ding{173}  若 ${a}_{n} \geq  {b}_{n}$ ，即 ${10n} - 9 \geq  {2}^{n}$ 时，此时 $n = 2,3,4,5$ . 则 ${a}_{n} \geq  {c}_{n} \geq  {b}_{n}$ ,可知 ${a}_{n} + {c}_{n} > {b}_{n}$ 成立,要使 ${a}_{n},{b}_{n},{c}_{n}$ 的值均能构成三角形,所以 ${b}_{n} + {c}_{n} > {a}_{n}$ 恒成立,故 ${a}_{n} < 2{b}_{n}$ ,所以有 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {10n} - 9 \geq  {2}^{n} \\  {10n} - 9 < {2}^{n + 1} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $n = 4$ 或 5 ; 综上可知,正整数 $n$ 的个数有 3 个. 故选: B.

9. B

【解析】令 ${b}_{n} = \sin \left( \dfrac{n\pi }{3}\right)$ ,则数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的一个周期为 6,又 ${b}_{1} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},{b}_{2} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},{b}_{3} = 0,{b}_{4} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},{b}_{5} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},{b}_{6} = 0$ ,则 ${b}_{1} + {b}_{2} + {b}_{3} + {b}_{4} + {b}_{5} + {b}_{6} = 0$ ,令 ${c}_{n} = {\left( -1\right) }^{n}\cos \left( \dfrac{n\pi }{4}\right)$ ,则数列 $\left\{  {c}_{n}\right\}$ 的一个周期为 8,又 ${c}_{1} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{c}_{2} = 0,{c}_{3} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{c}_{4} =  - 1,{c}_{5} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{c}_{6} = 0,{c}_{7} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{c}_{8} = 1$ ,则 ${c}_{1} + {c}_{2} + {c}_{3} + {c}_{4} +  {c}_{5} + {c}_{6} + {c}_{7} + {c}_{8} = 0$ ,所以数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的一个周期为 24,且 ${S}_{24} = 0$ ,所以 ${S}_{n} = {S}_{n + {24}}$ ,则 $\left\{  {S}_{n}\right\}$ 的一个周期为 24,又 ${a}_{1} =  {b}_{1} + {c}_{1} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} \neq  0,{a}_{6} = 0$ ,所以 ${T}_{1} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{T}_{6} = 0$ ,故 ${T}_{n} = 0\left( {n \geq  6,n \in  \mathbf{N}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left\{  {T}_{n}\right\}$ 不是周期数列. 故选: B.

方法二: 数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的周期性: $\sin \left( \dfrac{n\pi }{3}\right)$ 的周期为 6 ; ${\left( -1\right) }^{n}\cos \left( \dfrac{n\pi }{4}\right)$ 的周期为 8,因此,24 为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的一个周期. 数列 $\left\{  {S}_{n}\right\}$ 的周期性: 计算前 24 项和 ${S}_{24} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{24}}\sin \left( \dfrac{k\pi }{3}\right)  +  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{24}}{\left( -1\right) }^{k}\cos \left( \dfrac{k\pi }{4}\right)$ . 正弦部分: 周期为 6,24 项包含 4 个完整周期，每个周期和为 0 ，故总和为 0 ；余弦部分:符号交替项每 8 项和为 0 (验证如下), 24 项包含 3 个完整周期, 总和为 0 ; $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{8}{\left( -1\right) }^{k}\cos \left( \dfrac{k\pi }{4}\right)  =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 0 + \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} - 1 + \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 0 - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 1 =$ 0 . 因此, ${S}_{24} = 0$ ,且对于任意自然数 $m$ ,有 ${S}_{24m}$ ,表明数列 $\left\{  {S}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 24 的数列. 前 $n$ 项积 ${T}_{n}$ 的周期性,因为 ${T}_{6} = 0$ ,所以数列 $\left\{  {T}_{n}\right\}$ 不是周期数列. 故选 B.

10. C

【解析】对于 \ding{172} ,由等比数列 $\left\{  {2}^{n}\right\}$ 可得 ${S}_{n} = {2}^{n + 1} - 2$ ,若对任意正整数 $n$ ,总存在正整数 $m$ ,使得 ${S}_{n} = {a}_{m}$ ,则 ${2}^{n + 1} - 2 = {2}^{m}$ , 即 ${2}^{n + 1} = {2}^{m} + 2$ ,显然不成立,故 \ding{172} 为假命题; 对于 \ding{173} ,设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ ,则 ${a}_{n} = {a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) d = n{a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) (d  \left. {-{a}_{1}}\right) \left( {n\text{ 为正整数 }}\right)$ . 令 ${b}_{n} = n{a}_{1},{c}_{n} = \left( {n - 1}\right) \left( {d - {a}_{1}}\right)$ ,则 ${a}_{n} =  {b}_{n} + {c}_{n}\left( {n\text{ 为正整数 }}\right)$ . 下面证 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 “某数列”. 设 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 $n$ 项和为 ${T}_{n}$ ,则 ${T}_{n} = \dfrac{n\left( {n + 1}\right) }{2}{a}_{1}\left( n\right.$ 为正整数). 于是对任意的正整数 $n$ ,总存在正整数 $m = \dfrac{n\left( {n + 1}\right) }{2}$ ,使得 ${T}_{n} = {b}_{n}$ ,所以 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 “某数列”. 同理,可证 $\left\{  {c}_{n}\right\}$ 也是“某数列”. 所以对任意的等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,总存在两个 “某数列” $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 和 $\left\{  {c}_{n}\right\}$ ,使得 ${a}_{n} =  {b}_{n} + {c}_{n}$ ( $n$ 为正整数)成立,故 \ding{173} 为真命题. 故选:C.

11. A

【解析】对于 \ding{172} ,设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ ,则 ${t}_{n} = \dfrac{{S}_{n}}{n} =  \dfrac{n{a}_{1} + \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2}d}{n} = {a}_{1} + \dfrac{n - 1}{2}d$ ,代入 $\left( {i - j}\right) {t}_{k} + \left( {j - k}\right) {t}_{i} +  \left( {k - i}\right) {t}_{j} = c$ 可得 $\left( {i - j}\right) {a}_{1} + \dfrac{\left( {i - j}\right) \left( {k - 1}\right) }{2}d + \left( {j - k}\right) {a}_{1} +  \dfrac{\left( {j - k}\right) \left( {i - 1}\right) }{2}d + \left( {k - i}\right) {a}_{1} + \dfrac{\left( {k - i}\right) \left( {j - 1}\right) }{2}d = \left( {i - j}\right) \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {\dfrac{\left( k - 1\right) }{2}d}\right\rbrack   + \left( {j - k}\right) \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} + \dfrac{\left( i - 1\right) }{2}d}\right\rbrack   + \left( {k - i}\right) \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} + \dfrac{\left( j - 1\right) }{2}d}\right\rbrack  = {a}_{1}\left\lbrack  {\left( {i - j}\right)  + \left( {j - k}\right)  + \left( {k - i}\right) }\right\rbrack   + \dfrac{d}{2}\left\lbrack  {\left( {i - j}\right) \left( {k - 1}\right)  + }\right.  \left( {j - k}\right) \left( {i - 1}\right)  + \left( {k - i}\right) \left( {j - 1}\right) \rbrack  = {a}_{1} \times  0 + \dfrac{d}{2}\lbrack {ik} - i - {jk} +  j + {ji} - j - {ki} + k + {kj} - k - {ij} + i\rbrack  = \dfrac{d}{2} \times  0 = 0$ ,即可得到 $c =$ 0, \ding{172} 正确；对于 \ding{173} ，设等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q$ ,则 ${t}_{n} = \dfrac{{S}_{n}}{n} =  \dfrac{{b}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{n}}\right) }{n\left( {1 - q}\right) }$ ,代入 $\left( {i - j}\right) {t}_{k} + \left( {j - k}\right) {t}_{i} + \left( {k - i}\right) {t}_{j} = c$ ,可得 $\left( {i - j}\right)  \times  \dfrac{{b}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{k}}\right) }{k\left( {1 - q}\right) } + \left( {j - k}\right)  \times  \dfrac{{b}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{i}}\right) }{i\left( {1 - q}\right) } + \left( {k - i}\right)  \times  \dfrac{{b}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{j}}\right) }{j\left( {1 - q}\right) } = \dfrac{{b}_{1}}{1 - q}\left\lbrack  {\left( {i - j}\right)  \times  \dfrac{\left( 1 - {q}^{k}\right) }{k} + \left( {j - k}\right)  \times  \dfrac{\left( 1 - {q}^{i}\right) }{i} + }\right.  \left. {\left( {k - i}\right)  \times  \dfrac{\left( 1 - {q}^{j}\right) }{j}}\right\rbrack$ ,要使其为常数,化简后会发现很难对于任意的 $i$ 、 $j$ 、 $k$ 都满足为常数 (因为公比 $q \neq  1$ )，故不存在公比不为 1 的等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 “轮换均值数列, \ding{173} 不正确. 故选: A.

12. A

【解析】当 $n = 1$ 时, ${a}_{2} = \dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{a}_{1}} = 1$ . 当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${S}_{n} = {a}_{n}{a}_{n + 1}$ , 所以 ${S}_{n - 1} = {a}_{n - 1}{a}_{n}$ ,两式相减得: ${a}_{n} = {a}_{n}\left( {{a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n - 1}}\right)$ ,因为 ${a}_{n} \neq  0$ ,所以 ${a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n - 1} = 1$ . 所以数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的奇数项是以 ${a}_{1}$ 为首项,1 为公差的等差数列,且 ${a}_{1} \neq  0$ . 所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{6}{a}_{{2i} - 1} =  6{a}_{1} + \dfrac{6 \times  5}{2} \times  1 = 6{a}_{1} + {15}$ . 同理,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的偶数项是以 1 为首项，1 为公差的等差数列，所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{5}{a}_{2i} = 5 \times  1 + \dfrac{5 \times  4}{2} \times  1 = {15}$ . 所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{5}{a}_{2i} - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{6}{a}_{{2i} - 1} = {15} - 6{a}_{1} - {15} =  - 6{a}_{1}$ . 若 $- 6{a}_{1} =  - 6 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = 1$ ,则数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 各项均不为 0,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是无穷数列,故 ${A}$ 正确; 若 $- 6{a}_{1} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = 0$ ,这与 ${a}_{1} \neq  0$ 矛盾,故 B 错误; 若 $- 6{a}_{1} = 6 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} =  - 1$ ,根据奇数项成公差为 1 的等差数列,则 ${a}_{3} = 0$ ,则 ${a}_{4}$ 无法求出,这与数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是无穷数列矛盾,故 ${C}$ 错误; 若 $- 6{a}_{1} = {12} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} =  - 2$ ,根据奇数项成公差为 1 的等差数列,则 ${a}_{5} = 0$ ,则 ${a}_{6}$ 无法求出,这与数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是无穷数列矛盾,故 ${D}$ 错误. 故选: A.

13. B

【解析】设等差数列的首项和公差分别为 ${a}_{1},d$ ,若等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 满足 “性质 ${\Omega }^{n * }$ ; 由 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{n + 1} \geq  \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{n}$ 可得 $\dfrac{{a}_{n} + d}{n + 1} \geq  \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{n}$ ,故 $n{a}_{n} +  {nd} \geq  n{a}_{n} + {a}_{n}$ ,即 ${nd} \geq  {a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) d \Longrightarrow  d \geq  {a}_{1}$ ,故只需要 $d \geq  {a}_{1}$ 即可满足 “性质 $\Omega$ ”,故 \ding{172} 是真命题；设等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的首项和公比分别为 ${a}_{1},q$ ,若 $q = 1,{a}_{1} > 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{n + 1} \geq  \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{n} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{a}_{1}}{n + 1} \geq  \dfrac{{a}_{1}}{n} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{1}{n + 1} \geq  \dfrac{1}{n}$ 显然不成立,因此存在等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不满足 “性质 $\Omega$ ”,故 \ding{173} 是假命题；故选:B.

14. B

【解析】一方面,对 ${a}_{n} = {2n} - 1$ ,知 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列,而 ${S}_{n} =  \dfrac{1}{2}n \cdot  \left( {1 + {2n} - 1}\right)  = {n}^{2}$ ,令 $c = 1$ 就有 $2\sqrt{{S}_{n}} = {2n} = {2n} -  1 + 1 = {a}_{n} + c$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 $p$ ,这表明存在等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 $p$ ; 另一方面,对 ${a}_{n} = {\left( -1\right) }^{n - 1}$ ,知 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等比数列,当 $n$ 为奇数时, ${a}_{n} = 1;n$ 为偶数时, ${a}_{n} =  - 1$ . 故当 $n$ 为奇数时, ${S}_{n} = 1;n$ 为偶数时, ${S}_{n} = 0$ . 故当 $n$ 为奇数时, $2\sqrt{{S}_{n}} = 2 = 1 + 1 = {a}_{n} + 1;n$ 为偶数时, $2\sqrt{{S}_{n}} = 0 =  - 1 + 1 =  {a}_{n} + 1$ ,这表明 $2\sqrt{{S}_{n}} = {a}_{n} + 1$ 恒成立,再令 $c = 1$ 就有 $2\sqrt{{S}_{n}} =  {a}_{n} + c$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 $p$ ,这表明存在等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 $p$ . 综上, \ding{172}  真、 \ding{173} 假. 故选: B.

15. C

【解析】对于 \ding{172} ,若 ${a}_{1} = 1,\left| {{S}_{n} - {a}_{m}}\right|  < {a}_{1} \Longrightarrow  {S}_{n} - {a}_{m} = 0 \Longrightarrow  n + \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2} \times  3 = 1 + 3\left( {m - 1}\right)$ ,当 $n = 2$ 时, $m = \dfrac{7}{3}$ ,不符合题意,则不是 “可控数列”,则 \ding{172}  错误; 对于 \ding{173} ,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不是常数列且 ${a}_{n} > 0$ ,所以 $q \neq  1,q > 0,\left| {{S}_{n} - {a}_{m}}\right|  < {a}_{1} \Leftrightarrow  \left| {\dfrac{{a}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{n}}\right) }{1 - q} - {a}_{1}{q}^{m - 1}}\right|  < {a}_{1} \Leftrightarrow  \left| {\dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} - {q}^{m - 1}}\right|  < 1$ ,若 $m =  1,\left| {{S}_{n} - {a}_{m}}\right|  < {a}_{1} \Leftrightarrow  q + {q}^{2} + \cdots  + {q}^{n - 1} < 1$ 对任意 $n \geq  2$ 成立, 故得 ${\left( q + {q}^{2} + \cdots  + {q}^{n - 1}\right) }_{\min } = q < 1$ ,故 $q \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ; 若 $m \in  \{ 2,\cdots ,n\}$ ,则 $\left| {\dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} - {q}^{m - 1}}\right|  = 1 + q + {q}^{2} + \cdots  + {q}^{n - 1} - {q}^{m - 1} \geq$ 1,若 $m \geq  n + 1$ ,设 $m = n + k,k \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,则 $\left| {\dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} - {q}^{m - 1}}\right|  =  \left| {\dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} - {q}^{n + k - 1}}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} - {q}^{n + k - 1}}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{{q}^{k - 1}\left( {q - 1}\right)  - 1}{q - 1}{q}^{n} + }\right.  \dfrac{1}{q - 1}$ ,若 ${q}^{k - 1}\left( {q - 1}\right)  - 1 = 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{q - 1} < 1 \Longrightarrow  q > 2$ ,但此时 ${q}^{k - 1}\left( {q - 1}\right)  > {q}^{k - 1} > 1$ 矛盾,故 ${q}^{k - 1}\left( {q - 1}\right)  - 1 \neq  0$ ,故当 $q > 1$ 时,当 $n \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $\left| {\dfrac{{q}^{k - 1}\left( {q - 1}\right)  - 1}{q - 1}{q}^{n} + \dfrac{1}{q - 1}}\right|  \rightarrow   + \infty$ ,不符题意；综上所述，若等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是“可控数列”，则其公比为 $q \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ , \ding{173} 为真命题；故选 C.

16. A

【解析】对于命题 \ding{172} ,对于数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,令 ${a}_{n} = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1,n = 1 \\  {2}^{n - 2},n \geq  2 \end{array}\right.$ , 则 ${S}_{n} = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1,n = 1 \\  {2}^{n - 1},n \geq  2 \end{array}\right.$ ,数列 $\left\{  {S}_{n}\right\}$ 为公比不为 1 的等比数列,当 $n = 1$ 时, ${S}_{1} = 1$ 是数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${S}_{n} = {2}^{n - 1}$ 是数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项,所以对任意的正整数 $n,{S}_{n}$ 都是数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项，故命题 \ding{172} 正确；对于命题 \ding{173} ，等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ，令 ${a}_{1} =  - d$ ,则 ${a}_{n} = {a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) d = \left( {n - 2}\right) d$ ,则 ${S}_{n} = \dfrac{n\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{n}}\right) }{2} =  \dfrac{n\left\lbrack  {-d + \left( {n - 2}\right) d}\right\rbrack  }{2} = \dfrac{n\left( {n - 3}\right) }{2}d$ ,因为 $n - 2 \geq   - 1$ 且 $n - 2 \in  \mathbf{Z}$ , $\dfrac{n\left( {n - 3}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}{\left( n - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{9}{8} \geq   - 1$ ,且 $n$ 为正整数, $\dfrac{n\left( {n - 3}\right) }{2}  \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,所以对任意的正整数 $n,{S}_{n}$ 都是数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项,所以对于任意的实数 ${a}_{1}$ ,都存在实数 $d$ ,使得以 ${a}_{1}$ 为首项、 $d$ 为公差的等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为“ $T$ 数列”,故命题 \ding{173} 正确；故选:A.

\section*{考点 3 等差数列及其前 $n$ 项和}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 13

【解析】设首项为 1,公差是 3 的等差数列为 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,则 ${a}_{n} =  {a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) d = {3n} - 2$ ,故 ${a}_{5} = 3 \times  5 - 2 = {13}$ . 故答案为: 13 . 2. 12

【解析】根据等差数列的求和公式, ${S}_{6} = 6{a}_{1} + \dfrac{6 \times  5}{2}d = {12}$ . 故答案为: 12 .

3. 5

【解析】 ${a}_{1} + {a}_{6} = {a}_{4} + {a}_{3} = {12},{a}_{4} = 7 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{3} = 5$ .

4. 8

【解析】由等差数列的性质可得: ${a}_{1} + {a}_{16} = {a}_{4} + {a}_{13} = 1$ ,所以 ${S}_{16} = \dfrac{\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{16}}\right)  \times  {16}}{2} = \dfrac{1 \times  {16}}{2} = 8$ ,故答案为: 8 .

5. $\dfrac{3}{2}$

【解析】设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的首项为 ${a}_{1}$ ,公差为 $d,{a}_{1} + \left( {{3n} - }\right.$ 1) $d = 3\left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) d}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} + \left( {{3n} - 1}\right) d = 3{a}_{1} + \left( {{3n} - 3}\right) d \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = d$ . 所以 ${S}_{5} = \dfrac{5\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{5}}\right) }{2} = 5{a}_{3} = 5\left( {{a}_{1} + {2d}}\right)  = {15d}$ , ${a}_{10} = {a}_{1} + {9d} = {10d}$ ,所以: $\dfrac{{S}_{5}}{{a}_{10}} = \dfrac{15d}{10d} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{3}{2}$ .

6. 36

【解析】在等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中, ${a}_{1} =  - 6,{a}_{3} = 0$ ,则公差 $d =  \dfrac{{a}_{3} - {a}_{1}}{3 - 1} = 3$ ,所以 ${S}_{8} = 8{a}_{1} + \dfrac{8 \times  7}{2}d = {36}$ . 故答案为: 36 .

7. 9

【解析】 ${S}_{5} = {25} = 5{a}_{3} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{3} = 5,{a}_{1} = 1 \Longrightarrow  d = 2,{a}_{5} = {a}_{3} +  {2d} = 5 + 4 = 9$ .

8. 56

【解析】等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的首项 ${a}_{1} = 0$ ,公差 $d \neq  0$ ,若 ${a}_{m} =  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{11}}{a}_{i}$ ,则 $0 + \left( {m - 1}\right) d = {11} \times  0 + \dfrac{{11} \times  {10}}{2}d$ ,因为 $d \neq  0$ ,所以 $m - 1 = {55}$ ,即 $m = {56}$ . 故答案为: 56 .

9. 17

【解析】因为 ${a}_{3} + {a}_{15} = 2$ ,所以 ${S}_{17} = \dfrac{{17}\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{17}}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{{17}\left( {{a}_{3} + {a}_{15}}\right) }{2}  = {17}$ . 故答案为: 17 .

10. $\left( {-\infty , - 4}\right)$

【解析】 ${a}_{n} = n + c$ ,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为等差数列. ${S}_{7} = 7{a}_{4} < 0 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{4} <  0,{a}_{4} = 4 + c < 0 \Longrightarrow  c <  - 4$ . 故 $c$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\infty , - 4}\right)$ .

11. $\dfrac{133}{66}$

【解析】因为 ${a}_{1} + 2{a}_{2} + 3{a}_{3} + \cdots  + n{a}_{n} = n\left( {n + 2}\right)$  \ding{172} ,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${a}_{1} + 2{a}_{2} + 3{a}_{3} + \cdots  + \left( {n - 1}\right) {a}_{n - 1} = \left( {n - 1}\right) \left( {n + 1}\right)$  \ding{173} ,  \ding{172} - \ding{173} 得 $n{a}_{n} = {2n} + 1$ ，所以 ${a}_{n} = \dfrac{{2n} + 1}{n}\left( {n \geq  2}\right)$ ，所以 ${a}_{66} =  \dfrac{133}{66}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{133}{66}$ .

12. 5

【解析】 ${a}_{1} + {2d} = 5{a}_{1} + \dfrac{5 \times  4}{2} \times  d \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} =  - 4,{S}_{n} = {n}^{2} - {5n}$ , ${a}_{n} = {2n} - 6$ ,由 ${S}_{n} < {a}_{n}$ ,得 ${n}^{2} - {7n} + 6 < 0$ ,解得 $1 < n < 6$ ,故 $n$ 的最大值为 5 .

13. -7

【解析】因为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为等差数列,则 ${a}_{7} + {a}_{8} + {a}_{9} = 3{a}_{8} =$ 0,即 ${a}_{8} = 0$ ,且 ${a}_{7} + {a}_{10} = {a}_{8} + {a}_{9} = 1$ ,可得 ${a}_{9} = 1$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{8} = {a}_{1} + {7d} = 0 \\  {a}_{9} = {a}_{1} + {8d} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{1} =  - 7 \\  d = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,故答案为: -7 .

14. 2

【解析】由题意得 ${a}_{3},{a}_{7}$ 是 ${x}^{2} - {4x} + 2 = 0$ 的两个根,由韦达定理得 ${a}_{3} + {a}_{7} = 4$ ,因为 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列,所以 ${a}_{5} = \dfrac{{a}_{3} + {a}_{7}}{2} =$ 2. 故答案为:2.

15. 52

【解析】设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ ,则由题得 $2{a}_{1} + 3{a}_{11} =  5{a}_{1} + {30d} = {20}$ 即 ${a}_{1} + {6d} = 4$ ,所以 ${S}_{13} = {13}{a}_{1} + \dfrac{{13} \times  {12}}{2}d =  {13}\left( {{a}_{1} + {6d}}\right)  = {13} \times  4 = {52}$ . 故答案为: 52 .

16. 21

【解析】法一: 不妨设数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差大于零,由于 ${a}_{9}{a}_{10} < 0$ , 得 ${a}_{9} < 0,{a}_{10} > 0$ ,且 $n \leq  9$ 时, ${a}_{n} < 0,n \geq  {10}$ 时, ${a}_{n} > 0$ ,不妨取 $m > n$ ,则 ${S}_{m} - {S}_{n} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = n + 1}}^{m}{a}_{i}$ ,设 $k = \left| {{S}_{30} - {S}_{9}}\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = {10}}}^{{30}}{a}_{i}$ , 若 $n > 9,m = {30}$ ,则 $\left| {{S}_{30} - {S}_{n}}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = {n}_{0} + 1}}^{{30}}{a}_{i} < k$ ,此时式子取不了最大值; 若 $n < 9,m = {30}$ ,则 $\left| {{S}_{30} - {S}_{n}}\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = {n}_{0} + 1}}^{9}{a}_{i} + k}\right|$ ,又 $i \leq  9$ 时, ${a}_{i} < 0$ ,因为 $\left| {{S}_{30} - {S}_{n}}\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = {n}_{0} + 1}}^{9}{a}_{i} + k}\right|  < k$ ,此时式子取不了最大值; 因此这就说明 $n = {n}_{0} = 9$ 必成立. 若 $m <$ 30,则 $\left| {{S}_{m} - {S}_{9}}\right|  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = {10}}}^{{m}_{0}}{a}_{i} < k$ ,这也就说明 ${m}_{0} < {30}$ 不成立, 因此 ${m}_{0} = {30}$ ,所以 $\left| {{m}_{0} - {n}_{0}}\right|  = {21}$ . 故答案为: 21 .

法二: 设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ . 当 $d > 0$ 时,由于 ${a}_{9}{a}_{10} <$ 0,所以 ${a}_{9} < 0 < {a}_{10}$ ,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为严格增数列,故 ${S}_{n}$ 在区间 $\lbrack 1$ , 30]上， ${S}_{9}$ 为最小值， ${S}_{30}$ 为最大值，若 $\left| {{S}_{m} - {S}_{n}}\right|$ 取得最大值， 则 $m = {30},n = 9$ ,故 $\left| {{m}_{0} - {n}_{0}}\right|  = \left| {{30} - 9}\right|  = {21}$ ; 当 $d < 0$ 时, ${a}_{9} > 0 > {a}_{10}$ ,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为严格减数列, ${S}_{n}$ 在 $n \in  \left\lbrack  {1,{30}}\right\rbrack  ,{S}_{9}$ 为最大值, ${S}_{30}$ 为最小值,若 $\left| {{S}_{m} - {S}_{n}}\right|$ 取得最大值,则 $m = 9,n =$ 30，故 $\left| {{m}_{0} - {n}_{0}}\right|  = \left| {9 - {30}}\right|  = {21}$ ，综上， $\left| {{m}_{0} - {n}_{0}}\right|  = {21}$ .

17. 15

【解析】由 $\left( {{S}_{8} - {S}_{7}}\right) \left( {{S}_{9} - {S}_{7}}\right)  < 0$ ,可得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {S}_{8} - {S}_{7} < 0 \\  {S}_{9} - {S}_{7} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {S}_{8} - {S}_{7} > 0 \\  {S}_{9} - {S}_{7} < 0 \end{array},\text{ 当 }\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {S}_{8} - {S}_{7} < 0 \\  {S}_{9} - {S}_{7} > 0 \end{array},\text{ 可得 }\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {a}_{8} < 0 \\  {a}_{8} + {a}_{9} > 0 \end{array},\text{ 所以 }}\right. }\right. }\right.  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{8} < 0 \\  {a}_{9} >  - {a}_{8} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为严格增数列,且前 8 项为负,从第 9 项开始为正,又 ${S}_{15} = \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{15}}\right) }{2} = {15}{a}_{8} < 0,{S}_{16} =  \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{16}}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{8} + {a}_{9}}\right) }{2} > 0$ ,所以 ${S}_{15}{S}_{16} < 0$ ,所以 $m =$ 15; 当 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {S}_{8} - {S}_{7} > 0 \\  {S}_{9} - {S}_{7} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,可得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{8} > 0 \\  {a}_{8} + {a}_{9} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{8} > 0 \\  {a}_{9} <  - {a}_{8} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为严格增数列,且前 8 项为正,从第 9 项开始为负,又 ${S}_{15} = \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{15}}\right) }{2} = {15}{a}_{8} > 0,{S}_{16} = \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{16}}\right) }{2} =  \dfrac{{15}\left( {{a}_{8} + {a}_{9}}\right) }{2} < 0$ ,所以 ${S}_{15}{S}_{16} < 0$ ,所以 $m = {15}$ ; 综上所述: $m = {15}$ . 故答案为: 15 .

18. $\left( {\arccos \dfrac{1}{8},\pi }\right)$

【解析】设公差为 $m$ ,由题意: $2\cos a = \cos b + \cos c \Longrightarrow  2\cos (b -  m) = \cos b + \cos \left( {b + m}\right)  \Longrightarrow  \cos \left( {b - m}\right)  - \cos b = \cos \left( {b + m}\right)  -  \cos \left( {b - m}\right)  \Longrightarrow   - 2\sin \dfrac{{2b} - m}{2}\sin \dfrac{-m}{2} =  - 2\sin b\sin m \Longrightarrow  \sin \left( {\dfrac{m}{2} - }\right.$ b) $\sin \dfrac{m}{2} = \sin b\sin m = 2\sin b\sin \dfrac{m}{2}\cos \dfrac{m}{2} \Longrightarrow  \sin \dfrac{m}{2}\cos b -  \cos \dfrac{m}{2}\sin b = 2\sin b\cos \dfrac{m}{2} \Longrightarrow  \tan \dfrac{m}{2} = 3\tan b$ ,故 $m$ 随 $b$ 增大而增大,由图像分析知: $a \in  \left( {0,\pi }\right) ,b \in  \left( {\pi ,\dfrac{3}{2}\pi }\right)$ ,所以当 $c$ 到达 ${2\pi }$ 时是临界值,此时 $b = \dfrac{a}{2} + \pi$ ,代入 $2\cos a = \cos \left( {\dfrac{a}{2} + \pi }\right)  +  \cos {2\pi } \Longrightarrow  2\cos a =  - \cos \dfrac{a}{2} + 1 \Longrightarrow  \cos a = 1$ (舍) 或 $\cos a = \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,则 $a =  \arccos \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,综上所述实数 $a$ 的取值范围是 $a \in  \left( {\arccos \dfrac{1}{8},\pi }\right)$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

19. C

【解析】设 1965 年 5 月出生的男职工退休年龄为 ${a}_{1} = {60} +  \dfrac{2}{12} = {60}\dfrac{1}{6}$ 岁,则 1966 年 5 月出生的男职工退休年龄为 ${a}_{2} = {60}\dfrac{1}{6} + \dfrac{3}{12} = {60}\dfrac{1}{6} + \dfrac{1}{4}$ 岁, $\cdots$ ,设 5 月出生的男职工退休年龄为 ${a}_{n}$ ,得 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是首项为 ${60}\dfrac{1}{6}$ 、公差为 $\dfrac{1}{4}$ 的等差数列, 则 1974 年 5 月出生的男职工退休年龄为 ${a}_{10} = {60}\dfrac{1}{6} + 9 \times  \dfrac{1}{4} = {62}\dfrac{5}{12}$ ,故答案为 62 岁 5 个月,选 C.

20. B

【解析】命题 $\alpha  : {a}_{n} = 4,{S}_{n} = {4n},{S}_{n + 1} = {4n} + 4$ ,

$\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {{S}_{n} - {2022}}\right) \left( {{S}_{n + 1} - {2022}}\right)  < 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {{4n} - {2022}}\right) \left( {{4n} - {2018}}\right)  < 0 \\  \left( {{S}_{n} - {2023}}\right) \left( {{S}_{n + 1} - {2023}}\right)  < 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {{4n} - {2023}}\right) \left( {{4n} - {2019}}\right)  < 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,

$n \in  \mathbf{N} \Longrightarrow  n = {505}$ ,解集均为 $M = \{ {505}\}$ ,唯一,故命题 $\alpha$ 为假命题; 命题 $\beta$ : 若数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差 $d > 0$ ,

$\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \left( {{S}_{{n}_{0}} - {2022}}\right) \left( {{S}_{{n}_{0} + 1} - {2022}}\right)  < 0 \\  \left( {{S}_{{n}_{0}} - {2023}}\right) \left( {{S}_{{n}_{0} + 1} - {2023}}\right)  < 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {S}_{{n}_{0}} < {2022} \\  {S}_{{n}_{0} + 1} > {2023} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{{n}_{0} + 1} = }\right. }\right.  {S}_{{n}_{0} + 1} - {S}_{{n}_{0}} > 1$ ,命题 $\beta$ 是真命题; 综上所述: 命题 $\alpha$ 是假命题,命题 $\beta$ 是真命题; 故选 B.

21. B

【解析】设等差数列的首项为 $a$ ,公差为 $d$ ,则第 $i$ 项满足 ${a}_{i} =  a + \left( {i - 1}\right) d$ ,每个二次函数 ${f}_{i}\left( x\right)  = {a}_{i}{x}^{2} + {b}_{i}x + {c}_{i}$ 满足 ${f}_{i}\left( i\right)  = {a}_{i}{i}^{2} + {b}_{i}i + {c}_{i} = {a}_{1} + \left( {i - 1}\right) d$ ,变形为 ${a}_{i}{i}^{2} + \left( {{b}_{i} - d}\right) i +  \left( {{c}_{i} - {a}_{1} + d}\right)  = 0$ ,对于固定的 ${a}_{i},{b}_{i},{c}_{i},d$ ,这是一个关于 $i$ 的二次方程，最多有两个整数解，因此，每个二次函数最多能覆盖两个不同的 $i$ 值. 所以总共 2025 个 $i$ 值需要覆盖，因此需要 2025 $= {1012.5}$ 个，但 $i$ 为整数，所以需要 1013 个. 故选:B.

22. A

【解析】对于 \ding{172} ,数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 $n$ 项和 ${T}_{n} = {\left( n - t\right) }^{2}(t$ 为正整数),当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${b}_{n} = {T}_{n} - {T}_{n - 1} = {\left( n - t\right) }^{2} - {\left( n - 1 - t\right) }^{2} =  {2n} - {2t} - 1$ ,当 $n = 1$ 时, ${b}_{1} = {T}_{1} = {\left( 1 - t\right) }^{2}$ 不满足上式,所以 $\left| {b}_{n}\right|  = \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\left( 1 - t\right) }^{2},n = 1 \\  {2n} - {2t} - 1,n \geq  2 \end{array}\text{ ,当 }t = 1,n \geq  2}\right.$ 时, ${2n} - {2t} - 1 =  {2n} - 3 > 0$ ,所以数列 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 与原数列相同,所以 ${T}_{n} = (n -  1{)}^{2}$ ,所以当 $t = 1$ 时,数列 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 为完全平方数列,当 $t \geq  2$ 时, $\left| {b}_{1}\right|  + \left| {b}_{2}\right|  = {\left( t - 1\right) }^{2} + \left| {3 - {2t}}\right|  = {t}^{2} - {2t} + 1 + {2t} - 3 =  {t}^{2} - 2$ 不是 “完全平方数，所以当 $t \geq  2$ 时，数列 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 不是完全平方数列,综上所述: 只有当 $t = 1$ 时,数列 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 为 “完全平方数列”,故 \ding{172} 是真命题；对于 \ding{173} ，因为 ${S}_{n}$ 为完全平方数，故 $d \geq  0,{a}_{1} \geq  0,d,{a}_{1} \in  \mathbf{N}$ ,若 $d = 0$ ,则 ${S}_{n} = n{a}_{1}$ ,若对任意的正整数 $n,n{a}_{1}$ 均为完全平方数,则 ${a}_{1} = 0$ ,否则假设 $p$ 为 ${a}_{1}$ 的素因数,且 ${p}^{a}$ 恰好整除 ${a}_{1},a$ 为正整数,若 $a$ 为奇数,则 ${a}_{1}$ 不是完全平方数,矛盾; 若 $a$ 为偶数,取 $n = {p}^{a + 1}$ ,则 $n{a}_{1}$ 不是完全平方数,矛盾; 若 $d \geq  1$ ,则 ${S}_{n} = \dfrac{n\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{n}}\right) }{2} =  \dfrac{n\left( {2{a}_{1} - d + {nd}}\right) }{2}$ ,若 $2{a}_{1} - d \neq  0$ ,取 $n = \left| {2{a}_{1} - d}\right|$ ,则 ${S}_{n} =  \dfrac{{\left| 2{a}_{1} - d\right| }^{2}\left( {d + 1}\right) }{2}$ 或 ${S}_{n} = \dfrac{{\left| 2{a}_{1} - d\right| }^{2}\left( {d - 1}\right) }{2}$ ,当 $d$ 为偶数时,此时 $\dfrac{{\left| 2{a}_{1} - d\right| }^{2}\left( {d + 1}\right) }{2},\dfrac{{\left| 2{a}_{1} - d\right| }^{2}\left( {d - 1}\right) }{2}$ 均不是完全平方数; 当 $d$ 为奇数时,取 $n = 4,{S}_{n} = 2\left( {2{a}_{1} + {3d}}\right) ,2{a}_{1} + {3d}$ 为奇数,故此时 ${S}_{n}$ 不是完全平方数,故 $2{a}_{1} - d = 0$ ,即 $2{a}_{1} =  d$ ,故 ${S}_{n} = {a}_{1}{n}^{2}$ ,设 ${a}_{1} = {k}^{2}$ ,故 ${S}_{n} = {k}^{2}{n}^{2}$ ,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${a}_{n} = {S}_{n}  - {S}_{n - 1} = {k}^{2}{n}^{2} - {k}^{2}{\left( n - 1\right) }^{2} = {k}^{2}\left( {{2n} - 1}\right)$ ,又 ${a}_{1} = {k}^{2}$ 适合上式,即 ${a}_{n} = {k}^{2}\left( {{2n} - 1}\right) ,k \in  \mathbf{Z}$ . 故存在无穷多个“完全平方数列”的等差数列, 故  \ding{173}  是真命题. 故选: A.

\section*{三、解答题}

23. ( 1 )设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ ，则由条件，得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}_{1} + d = 5 \\  {a}_{1} + {8d} + 7 = 2\left( {{a}_{1} + {5d}}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 ${a}_{1} = 3,d = 2$ ,故 ${a}_{n} = {a}_{1} +  \left( {n - 1}\right) d = {2n} + 1;$

(2)由(1)可得 ${a}_{n + 1} = {2n} + 3$ ，则 ${b}_{n} = {\left( 2n + 3\right) }^{2} - {\left( 2n + 1\right) }^{2} =  8\left( {n + 1}\right)$ . 所以 ${b}_{n + 1} - {b}_{n} = 8$ ,故数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是以 ${b}_{1} = {16}$ 为首项、 8 为公差的等差数列,故 ${S}_{m} = \dfrac{m\left\lbrack  {{16} + 8\left( {m + 1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }{2} = {4m}\left( {m + 3}\right)$ . 因为 ${S}_{m} > {432}$ ,所以 ${m}^{2} + {3m} > {108}$ ,所以 $\left( {m + {12}}\right) \left( {m - 9}\right)  > 0$ ,所以 $m > 9$ 或 $m <  - {12}$ . 因为 $m$ 为正整数,所以 $m$ 的最小值是 10 .

\section*{考点 4 等比数列及其前 $n$ 项和}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 189

【解析】 ${S}_{6} = \dfrac{3\left( {1 - {2}^{6}}\right) }{1 - 2} = 3 \times  \left( {{2}^{6} - 1}\right)  = 3 \times  {63} = {189}$ . 2. $\dfrac{9}{2}$

【解析】 ${S}_{2} = 1 + {a}_{1} = 4 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = 3 \Longrightarrow  q = \dfrac{1}{3} \Longrightarrow  S = \dfrac{{a}_{1}}{1 - q} =  \dfrac{3}{1 - \dfrac{1}{3}} = \dfrac{9}{2}$

3. $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$

【解析】依题意, ${S}_{3} > {S}_{4}$ ,即 ${S}_{4} - {S}_{3} = {a}_{4} = {a}_{1}{q}^{3} = {q}^{3} < 0$ , 所以 $q < 0$ . 故答案为: $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ .

4. 31

【解析】 ${S}_{5} = \dfrac{1 - {2}^{5}}{1 - 2} = {31}$ .

5. 4

【解析】 ${a}_{2024} + {a}_{2023} = {a}_{2024} + \dfrac{1}{2}{a}_{2024} = \dfrac{3}{2}{a}_{2024} = 6$ ,解得 ${a}_{2024} = 4$ .

6. 121

【解析】 ${a}_{2} = 3,{a}_{5} = {81} \Longrightarrow  {q}^{3} = \dfrac{{a}_{5}}{{a}_{2}} = {27},q = 3,{a}_{1} = 1,{S}_{5} =  \dfrac{{a}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{5}}\right) }{1 - q} = {121}.$

7. ${a}_{n} = {2}^{n}$

【解析】 ${a}_{1} = 2,{a}_{2} + {a}_{3} = {12},{a}_{1}q + {a}_{1}{q}^{2} = {2q} + 2{q}^{2} = {12}$ , $q > 0,q = 2,{a}_{n} = {2}^{n}.$

8. 10

【解析】 ${a}_{1} = 1,{a}_{5} = 1 \Longrightarrow  1 = 1 \times  {q}^{4} \Longrightarrow  q = 1,{S}_{10} = {10}{a}_{1} = {10}$ . 9. 4

【解析】设等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q$ ,由 ${a}_{1}{a}_{10} = {a}_{8}$ ,则 ${a}_{1}{a}_{1}{q}^{9} = {a}_{1}{q}^{7}$ ,解得 ${a}_{1} = {q}^{-2}$ ,由 ${S}_{4} - {S}_{1} = \dfrac{21}{4}$ ,则 $\dfrac{{a}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{4}}\right) }{1 - q} -  {a}_{1} = \dfrac{21}{4}$ ,将 ${a}_{1} = {q}^{-2}$ 代入上式可得 $\dfrac{1 - {q}^{4}}{\left( {1 - q}\right) {q}^{2}} - \dfrac{1}{{q}^{2}} = \dfrac{21}{4}$ ,去分母可得 $4{q}^{4} - {21}{q}^{3} + {21}{q}^{2} - {4q} = 0$ ,易知 $q \neq  0$ ,可得 $4{q}^{3} - {21}{q}^{2}  + {21q} - 4 = 0$ ,分解因式可得 $\left( {q - 1}\right) \left( {{4q} - 1}\right) \left( {q - 4}\right)  = 0$ ,易知 $q \neq  1$ ,解得 $q = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 或 4,当 $q = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时, ${a}_{1} = {16}$ ,则 ${a}_{n} = {16} \times  {\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{n - 1} = {4}^{3 - n}$ ,单调递减,不合题意. 故答案为: 4 .

10. $\dfrac{1}{3}$

【解析】由题意可得 ${a}_{n} = n,{b}_{n} = {q}^{n - 1}\left( {q > 0}\right) ,{a}_{n} \cdot  {b}_{n} = n{q}^{n - 1}$ , ${S}_{3} = 1 + {2q} + 3{q}^{2} = 2$ ,解得 $q = \dfrac{1}{3}$ 或 $q =  - 1$ (舍),故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{3}$ . 11. -1

【解析】由等比数列的前 $n$ 项和为 ${S}_{n} = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{n} + a$ ,可得 ${a}_{1} =  {S}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{2} + a,{a}_{2} = {S}_{2} - {S}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{4} + a - \left( {\dfrac{1}{2} + a}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{4},{a}_{3} =  {S}_{3} - {S}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{8} + a - \left( {\dfrac{1}{4} + a}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,所以 ${\left( -\dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{2} = \left( {\dfrac{1}{2} + a}\right)  \times  \left( {-\dfrac{1}{8}}\right)$ ,解得 $a =  - 1$ ,经检验符合题意. 故答案为: -1 .

12. 2

【解析】 ${a}_{1} = 1,{a}_{n} = 2{a}_{n + 1}$ ,即 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} = \dfrac{1}{2},\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等比数列, 公比为 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}{a}_{i} = \dfrac{1}{1 - \dfrac{1}{2}} = 2$ . 故答案为: 2 .

13. $q = 3$ (或 $q =  - 2$ ,答案不唯一)

【解析】 ${12}{a}_{2},{a}_{4},2{a}_{3}$ 成等差数列,则 $2{a}_{4} = {12}{a}_{2} + 2{a}_{3}$ ,即 ${q}^{2} = 6 + q$ ,解得 $q = 3$ 或 $q =  - 2$ ,故 “ ${12}{a}_{2},{a}_{4},2{a}_{3}$ 成等差数列”的一个充分非必要条件是 $q = 3$ (或 $q =  - 2$ ). 故答案为: $q = 3$ (或 $q =  - 2$ ,答案不唯一)

14. 512 或 $\dfrac{1}{64}$

【解析】因为 ${\log }_{2}{a}_{1} + {\log }_{2}{a}_{4} = {\log }_{2}\left( {{a}_{1} \cdot  {a}_{4}}\right)  = 3$ ,所以 ${a}_{1} \cdot  {a}_{4} = {a}_{2} \cdot  {a}_{3} = 8$ ,因为 ${2}^{{a}_{2}} \cdot  {2}^{{a}_{3}} = {2}^{{a}_{2} + {a}_{3}} = {64}$ ,所以 ${a}_{2} + {a}_{3} =$ 6,解得 ${a}_{2} = 2,{a}_{3} = 4$ 或 ${a}_{2} = 4,{a}_{3} = 2$ .

 \ding{172}  当 ${a}_{2} = 2,{a}_{3} = 4$ 时， $q = 2 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{10} = 2 \times  {2}^{8} = {512}$ ；

 \ding{173}  当 ${a}_{2} = 4,{a}_{3} = 2$ 时， $q = \dfrac{1}{2} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{10} = 4 \times  {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{8} = \dfrac{1}{64}$ .

综上,答案为:512或 $\dfrac{1}{64}$ .

15. $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$

【解析】因为 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为无穷等比数列, $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}{a}_{i} =  - 2$ ,所以 $0 <  \left| q\right|  < 1$ ,则 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}{a}_{i} = \dfrac{{a}_{1}}{1 - q} =  - 2$ ,则 ${a}_{1} =  - 2\left( {1 - q}\right)$ ,因为 $\dfrac{\left| {a}_{n + 1}\right| }{\left| {a}_{n}\right| } = \left| \dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}}\right|  = \left| q\right|$ ,所以 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|$ 是以 $\left| q\right|$ 为公比的等比数列,且 $0 < \left| q\right|  < 1$ ,此时 $1 - q > 0$ ,所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}\left| {a}_{i}\right|  = \dfrac{\left| {a}_{1}\right| }{1 - \left| q\right| } =  \dfrac{\left| -2\left( 1 - q\right) \right| }{1 - \left| q\right| } = \dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 - \left| q\right| }$ ,当 $0 < q < 1$ 时, $\dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 - \left| q\right| } = \dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 - q}  = 2$ ; 当 $- 1 < q < 0$ 时, $\dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 - \left| q\right| } = \dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 + q} = \dfrac{4 - 2\left( {1 + q}\right) }{1 + q} =  \dfrac{4}{1 + q} - 2$ ,因为 $- 1 < q < 0$ ,所以 $0 < 1 + q < 1$ ,故 $\dfrac{4}{1 + q} > 4$ ,则 $\dfrac{4}{1 + q} - 2 > 2$ ; 综上: $\dfrac{2\left( {1 - q}\right) }{1 - \left| q\right| } \geq  2$ ,即 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}\left| {a}_{i}\right|  \geq  2$ ,故 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{+\infty }}\left| {a}_{i}\right|$ 的取值范围为 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ . 故答案为: $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ .

16. $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$

【解析】由题设有 ${a}_{n} = {a}_{1}{q}^{n - 1} > 0$ ,因为 ${a}_{1} > 0,q > 1$ ,所以 ${a}_{n + 1} > {a}_{n}$ ,故 $\left\lbrack  {{a}_{n},{a}_{n + 1}}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1}{q}^{n - 1},{a}_{1}{q}^{n}}\right\rbrack$ ,

(1)当 $n = 1$ 时， $x$ ， $y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} - {a}_{2},{a}_{2} - {a}_{1}}\right\rbrack   =  {I}_{1}$ ，此时 ${I}_{1}$ 为闭区间；

(2)当 $n \geq  2$ 时， \ding{172}  若 $x \geq  y$ ，

(i) 若 $x$ 、 $y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right\rbrack$ ，则 $x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {0,{a}_{2} - {a}_{1}}\right\rbrack$ ；

(ii) 若 $y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right\rbrack  ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{n},{a}_{n + 1}}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{n} - {a}_{2}}\right.$ , $\left. {{a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{1}}\right\rbrack$ ;

(iii) 若 $x,y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{n},{a}_{n + 1}}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {0,{a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n}}\right\rbrack$ ;

综上, $x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {0,{a}_{2} - {a}_{1}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {0,{a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{n} - {a}_{2},{a}_{n + 1} - }\right.  \left. {a}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {I}_{n\text{ 右 }}$ ,因为 ${I}_{n}$ 为闭区间,且 ${a}_{2} - {a}_{1} < {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1} -  {a}_{1}$ ,所以 ${I}_{n\text{ 右 }} = \left\lbrack  {0,{a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{1}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {a}_{n} - {a}_{2} \leq  {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n}$ 对任意 $n \geq  2$ 恒成立 $\Longrightarrow  {a}_{n + 1} - 2{a}_{n} + {a}_{2} \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1}{q}^{n - 1}\left( {q - 2}\right)  + {a}_{1}q \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  {q}^{n - 2}\left( {q - 2}\right)  + 1 \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  q - 2 \geq   - \dfrac{1}{{q}^{n - 2}}$ 对任意的 $n \geq  2$ 恒成立,因为 $q > 1$ ,所以当 $n \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $- \dfrac{1}{{q}^{n - 2}} \rightarrow  0 \Longrightarrow  q - 2 \geq  0$ ,即 $q \geq  2$ ;

 \ding{173}  若 $x < y$ ,同理可得 $x - y \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} - {a}_{n + 1},{a}_{2} - {a}_{n}}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{n} - }\right.  \left. {{a}_{n + 1},0}\right)  \cup  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} - {a}_{2},0}\right)  = {I}_{n\text{ 左 }}$ ,因为 ${I}_{n}$ 为闭区间,且 ${a}_{2} - {a}_{1} <  {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{1} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} - {a}_{n + 1} < {a}_{n} - {a}_{n + 1} < {a}_{1} - {a}_{2}$ ,所以 ${I}_{n\text{ 左 }} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} - {a}_{n + 1},0}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {a}_{n} - {a}_{n + 1} \leq  {a}_{2} - {a}_{n}$ 对任意 $n \geq  2$ 恒成立，后面同 \ding{172} ，得 $q \geq  2$ ；由 \ding{172}  \ding{173} ，知 ${I}_{n} = \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} - {a}_{n + 1},{a}_{n + 1}}\right.  -  \left. {a}_{1}\right\rbrack$ . 综上, $q$ 的取值范围是 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ .

点睛:与等比数列性质有关的不等式恒成立，可利用基本量法把恒成立转为关于与公比有关的不等式恒成立，必要时可利用参变分离来处理.

\section*{二、选择题}

17. C

【解析】因为 ${S}_{2023} < {S}_{2025} < {S}_{2024}$ ,所以 ${S}_{2025} - {S}_{2023} > 0$ , ${S}_{2025} - {S}_{2024} < 0$ ,即 ${a}_{2024} + {a}_{2025} > 0$ 且 ${a}_{2025} < 0$ ,所以 ${a}_{2024} >  - {a}_{2025} > 0$ ,且 ${a}_{2024} > 0$ ,两边都除以 ${a}_{2024}$ ,得 $1 >  - q > 0$ ,可得 $- 1 < q < 0$ . 对于 ${A}$ ,由 ${a}_{2025} = {a}_{1}{q}^{2024} < 0$ ,可得 ${a}_{1} < 0$ ,故 A 项不正确; 对于 B,由于 $- 1 < q < 0$ ,所以 $q > 0$ 不成立,故 B 不正确; 对于 ${C}$ ,因为 $- 1 < q < 0$ ,所以 $0 < 1 - {q}^{n} \leq  1 - q$ ,可得 $0 <  \dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q} \leq  1$ . 结合 ${S}_{n} = \dfrac{{a}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{n}}\right) }{1 - q}$ ,可得 $\left| {S}_{n}\right|  = \left| {a}_{1}\right|  \cdot  \left| \dfrac{1 - {q}^{n}}{1 - q}\right|  \leq  \left| {a}_{1}\right|$ ,故 C 正确; 对于 ${D}$ ,根据 $- 1 < q < 0$ 且 ${a}_{1} < 0$ ,当 ${a}_{1} =  - 1,q =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, $\left| {S}_{1}\right|  = 1 > \left| q\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,此时 $\left| {S}_{n}\right|  < \left| q\right|$ 不成立，故 D 不正确. 故选:C.

\section*{三、解答题}

18.(1)设等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q$ ，由 ${a}_{3}{a}_{4}{a}_{5} = \dfrac{{a}_{4}}{q} \cdot  {a}_{4}$ . ${a}_{4}q = {1000}$ ,可得 ${a}_{4}^{3} = {1000}$ ,即 ${a}_{4} = {10}$ . 又由 $4{a}_{3},3{a}_{4},2{a}_{5}$ 成等差数列,可得 $4{a}_{3} + 2{a}_{5} = 6{a}_{4}$ ,即 $\dfrac{40}{q} + {20q} = {60}$ ,解得 $q = 1$ 或 2,又 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格增数列,所以 $q = 2$ ,故 ${a}_{n} = {a}_{4}{q}^{n - 4} =  {10} \cdot  {2}^{n - 4} = 5 \cdot  {2}^{n - 3};$

(2)由 ${S}_{n} = 3\left( {1 - {2}^{n}}\right)$ ，可得当 $n \geq  2$ 时， ${b}_{n} = {S}_{n} - {S}_{n - 1} =  3\left( {{2}^{n - 1} - {2}^{n}}\right)  =  - 3 \cdot  {2}^{n - 1}$ ,又 ${b}_{1} = {S}_{1} =  - 3 =  - 3 \cdot  {2}^{1 - 1}$ ,所以对一切正整数 $n$ ,都有 ${b}_{n} =  - 3 \cdot  {2}^{n - 1}$ ,所以 ${t}_{n} = \sqrt{{a}_{n}^{2} + {b}_{n}^{2}} =  \sqrt{{\left( 5 \cdot  {2}^{n - 3}\right) }^{2} + {\left( -3 \cdot  {2}^{n - 1}\right) }^{2}} = {13} \cdot  {2}^{n - 3}$ ,所以 $\left\{  {t}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 $n$ 和为 $\dfrac{13}{4}\left( {1 + 2 + \cdots  + {2}^{n - 1}}\right)  = \dfrac{13}{4} \cdot  \dfrac{1 - {2}^{n}}{1 - 2} = \dfrac{13}{4}\left( {{2}^{n} - 1}\right)$ .

19. ( 1 )因为 $d = 2$ ，且 ${S}_{5} = 5{a}_{1} + \dfrac{5 \times  4}{2} \times  2 = {15}$ ，所以 ${a}_{1} =$ -1,所以 ${a}_{n} = {2n} - 3$ . 因为 ${b}_{1} = 1$ ,且 ${b}_{4} - {b}_{2} = 6$ ,所以 ${q}^{3} -  q = 6$ ,所以 $q = 2$ ,所以 ${b}_{n} = {2}^{n - 1}$ ;

(2)由题可知， ${c}_{n} = {2}^{{2n} - 3} + {2}^{{2n} - 1} = \dfrac{5}{8} \times  {4}^{n}$ ， $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{c}_{n}$ 为等比数列求和，首项为 $\dfrac{5}{2}$ ，公比 4，所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{c}_{n} = \dfrac{\dfrac{5}{2} \times  \left( {1 - {4}^{n}}\right) }{1 - 4} = \dfrac{5}{6}\left( {{4}^{n} - 1}\right)$ .

\section*{考点 5 数列中的数学文化}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 10

【解析】由题意设每个月的收入为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,其前 $n$ 项和记作 ${S}_{n}$ ,前 6 个月的收入成等比数列,且公比为 $\dfrac{6}{5}$ ,第 7 个月开始收入成等差数列,公差为 2,则 ${S}_{6} = \dfrac{{20} \times  \left\lbrack  {1 - {\left( \dfrac{6}{5}\right) }^{6}}\right\rbrack  }{1 - \dfrac{6}{5}} =  {100} \times  {\left( \dfrac{6}{5}\right) }^{6} - {100} \approx  {198.6}$ ,又 ${a}_{7} = {a}_{6} + 2 = {20} \times  {\left( \dfrac{6}{5}\right) }^{5} +  2 \approx  {51.77},{a}_{8} = {53.77},{a}_{9} = {55.77},{a}_{10} = {57.77}$ ,而 ${S}_{9} \approx  {198.6} +  {51.77} + {53.77} + {55.77} = {359.91},{S}_{10} = {S}_{9} + {a}_{10} = {417.68} >$ 400 , 所以该企业用所得收入偿还 400 万元贷款只需 10 个月. 故答案为: 10 .

2. 15

【解析】因为在 Rt $\bigtriangleup {MOF}$ 中, $\angle F = {30}^{ \circ  },{OM} = {20}$ ,所以 ${OF} = {20}\sqrt{3},{EF} = {20}\sqrt{3} - {30}$ ,故 $A{A}_{1} = {20}\sqrt{3} - {30}$ ,故 $\left\{  {d}_{k}\right\}$ 是以 ${20}\sqrt{3} - {30}$ 为首项，以 0.1 为公差的等差数列，故 ${S}_{k} =  \left( {{20}\sqrt{3} - {30}}\right) k + \dfrac{k\left( {k - 1}\right) }{2} \times  {0.1}$ ,而 ${S}_{14} = {74.06} < {80},{S}_{15} \approx$ 80.1>80 ,故 ${k}_{\min } = {15}$ . 所以至少需要 15 次才能将整个警戒区域扫描完毕. 故答案为: 15 .

3. 134

【解析】设第一层有 $m$ 根,共有 $n$ 层,则 ${S}_{n} = {nm} + \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2} =$ 2024 得: $n\left( {{2m} + n - 1}\right)  = {4048} = {2}^{4} \times  {11} \times  {23}$ ,因为 $n$ 和 ${2m} +  n - 1$ 中一个奇数一个偶数,故可能 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} n = {11} \\  m = {179} \end{array},\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} n = {16} \\  m = {119} \end{array},\left\{  \begin{array}{l} n = {23} \\  m = {77} \end{array}\right. }\right. }\right.$ , 因为每增加一层高度增加 $5\sqrt{3}$ 厘米,当 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} n = {11} \\  m = {179} \end{array}\text{ 时, }h = {10} \times  }\right.  5\sqrt{3} + {10} \approx  {96.6}$ 厘米 $< {150}$ 厘米,此时最下层有 189 根; 当 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} n = {16} \\  m = {119} \end{array}\text{ 时, }h = {15} \times  5\sqrt{3} + {10} \approx  }\right.$ 139.9 厘米 $< {150}$ 厘米,此时最下层有 134 根; 当 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} n = {23} \\  m = {77} \end{array}\right.$ 时, $h = {22} \times  5\sqrt{3} + {10} \approx  {200.52} > {150}$ 厘米,超过. 综上所述: 134 根占用场地面积最小.

\section*{二、解答题}

4. (1)设从 2023 年 1 月起第 $n$ 个月处理后的废水排放量为 ${a}_{n}$ 吨,则由已知条件知: 数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是首项为 10,公差为 2 的等差数列,故 ${a}_{n} = {2n} + 8$ . 当 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{a}_{n} \geq  {800} + {2n}$ 时,即 $\dfrac{n\left\lbrack  {{10} + \left( {{2n} + 8}\right) }\right\rbrack  }{2} \geq  {800} + {2n}$ ,化简得 ${n}^{2} + {7n} - {800} \geq  0$ ,解得 $n \geq  {25}$ 或 $n \leq   - {32}$ ,由 $n$ 是正整数,故 $n \geq  {25}$ ,故该厂在 2025 年1月底第一次将废水池中的废水排放完毕;

(2)设从 2023 年 1 月起第 $n$ 个月深度净化的废水量为 ${b}_{n}$ 吨, 由已知条件, ${b}_{1} = {b}_{2} = \cdots  = {b}_{6} = 0$ ,当 $n \geq  7$ 时,数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是首项为 5,公比为 1.2 的等比数列,故 ${b}_{n} = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} 0,1 \leq  n \leq  6 \\  5 \times  1.{2}^{n - 7},n \geq  7 \end{array}\right.$ (n 为正整数); 显然,当 $1 \leq  n \leq  6$ 时, ${a}_{n} > {b}_{n}$ ; 当 $n \geq  7$ 时,由 ${a}_{n} \leq  {b}_{n}$ ,得 ${2n} + 8 \leq  5 \times  {1.2}^{n - 7}\left( *\right)$ ,设 ${c}_{n} = {2n} + 8 - 5 \times  {1.2}^{n - 7}$ , 方法一:令 ${c}_{n + 1} \geq  {c}_{n}$ ，则 ${2n} + 2 + 8 - 5 \times  {1.2}^{n - 6} \geq  {2n} + 8 - 5 \times$ 1. ${2}^{n - 7} \Longrightarrow  2 \geq  {0.2} \times  5 \times  {1.}{2}^{n - 7} \Longrightarrow  {1.}{2}^{n - 7} \leq  2 \Longrightarrow  n - 7 < {3.81} \Longrightarrow  n < {10.81}$ ,得 ${c}_{7} < {c}_{8} < {c}_{9} < {c}_{10} < {c}_{11} > {c}_{12} > \cdots  > {c}_{19} > 0 > {c}_{20}$ . 方法二: 则 ${c}_{n} - {c}_{n - 1} = 2 - 1.{2}^{n - 8}$ ,所以当 $7 \leq  n \leq  {11}$ 时,数列 $\left\{  {c}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格增数列,且 ${c}_{n} > 0$ ; 当 $n \geq  {12}$ 时,数列 $\left\{  {c}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格减数列. 由于 ${c}_{19} \approx  {1.42} > 0,{c}_{20} \approx   - {5.50} < 0$ .

所以不等式 $\left( *\right)$ 的解为 $n \geq  {20}$ ( $n$ 为正整数). 故该厂在 2024 年 8 月开始计划排放的废水能被全部净化.

\section*{考点 6 数列求和}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 21

【解析】由 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列,则 ${a}_{2} + {a}_{4} + {a}_{6} = 3{a}_{4} = 9$ ,即 ${a}_{4} =$ 3，则有 ${S}_{7} = \dfrac{\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{7}}\right)  \times  7}{2} = \dfrac{2{a}_{4} \times  7}{2} = 7{a}_{4} = {21}$ . 故答案为:21. 2. 110

【解析】等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}  ,{a}_{1} + {a}_{2} = 2{a}_{1} + d = 6,{a}_{2} + {a}_{3} = 2{a}_{1} +  {3d} = {10}$ ,解得 ${a}_{1} = d = 2$ ,故 ${a}_{n} = {2n}$ ,则 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 10 项和为 ${S}_{10} = {10}{a}_{1} + \dfrac{{10} \times  9}{2}d = {20} + {90} = {110}$ . 故答案为:110.

3. 6

【解析】 ${a}_{1} =  - 1,{a}_{n} = {2n} - 3,{S}_{n} = \dfrac{\left( {-1 + {2n} - 3}\right) n}{2} = {n}^{2} -  {2n},{S}_{m} = {m}^{2} - {2m} = {24},{m}^{2} - {2m} - {24} = 0,\left( {m - 6}\right) \left( {m + 4}\right)  =  0 \Longrightarrow  m = 6\left( {m =  - 4\text{ ,舍去 }}\right)$ ,故答案为: 6 .

4. ${2}^{1012} - 1$

【解析】因为 ${a}_{1} = 1,{a}_{n} \in  \{ 1, - 1\} ,\left( {n \geq  2}\right)$ ,可知 ${S}_{n}$ 的奇偶性与 $n$ 的奇偶性一致,对于 \ding{172} : 存在小于 1013 的正整数 $t$ , 使得 ${S}_{{2t} + 1} =  - 1$ ,对于 \ding{173} : 对任意的正整数 $k$ 和 $m$ ,都有 $\left| {{S}_{2m} - {S}_{{2k} - 1}}\right|  \leq  1$ ,可知 $\left| {{S}_{2m} - {S}_{{2k} - 1}}\right|$ 为奇数,即 $\left| {{S}_{2m} - {S}_{{2k} - 1}}\right|  =$ 1,令 $k = t + 1$ ,则 $\left| {{S}_{2m} - {S}_{{2t} + 1}}\right|  = \left| {{S}_{2m} + 1}\right|  = 1$ ,可得 ${S}_{2m} = 0$ 或 ${S}_{2m} =  - 2$ ; 令 $k = 1$ ,则 $\left| {{S}_{2m} - {S}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {{S}_{2m} - 1}\right|  = 1$ ,可得 ${S}_{2m} = 0$ 或 ${S}_{2m} = 2$ ; 综上所述: 对任意的正整数 $m,{S}_{2m} = 0$ . 且 ${a}_{1} = 1$ ,可得 ${a}_{2} =  - 1,{a}_{{2k} + 1} + {a}_{{2k} + 2} = 0,k \in  \mathbf{N},k \geq  1$ ,即 ${a}_{1},{a}_{2}$ 确定, ${a}_{{2k} + 1},{a}_{{2k} + 2}$ 不相等,有 2 种可能,此时 ${S}_{2n} = 0$ , ${S}_{{2n} - 1} =  \pm  1$ ,条件  \ding{173}  满足,对于数列 $\left\{  {S}_{n}\right\}  \left( {1 \leq  n \leq  {2025}}\right)$ 可知: $\left( {{a}_{3},{a}_{4}}\right) ,\left( {{a}_{5},{a}_{6}}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{a}_{2023},{a}_{2024}}\right) ,{a}_{2025}$ 均有 2 种可能,则满足条件的数列共有 ${2}^{\dfrac{{2024} - 2}{2} + 1} = {2}^{1012}$ 个,又因为存在小于 1013 的正整数 $t$ ,使得 ${S}_{{2t} + 1} =  - 1$ ,可知对任意正整数 $k,k \leq  {1012},{a}_{{2k} + 1} = 1$ 不成立,即 ${a}_{3} = {a}_{5} = \cdots  = {a}_{2025} = 1$ 这种情况不符合题意，综上所述:符合题意的数列共有 ${2}^{1012} -$ 1 个. 故答案为: ${2}^{1012} - 1$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

5. C

【解析】对于 \ding{172} : 必要性: 若 ${a}_{1} \cdot  {a}_{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {a}_{k} = 0$ ,不妨设 ${a}_{i} =  0\left( {1 \leq  i \leq  k}\right)$ ,则 ${S}_{{2i} - 1} = \left( {{2i} - 1}\right) {a}_{i} = 0$ ,即必要性成立; 充分性: 举反例: 当 ${a}_{n} = {2n} - 3$ 时,则 ${S}_{2} = 0$ ,故 ${S}_{1} \cdot  {S}_{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {S}_{{2k} - 1} = 0$ ,但是 ${a}_{1} \cdot  {a}_{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {a}_{k} \neq  0$ ,即充分性不成立; 综上所述, \ding{172} 为真命题; 对于 \ding{173} : 充分性: 当 ${a}_{k} + {a}_{k + 1} = 0$ 时,得 ${a}_{k}\left( {1 + q}\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $q =  - 1$ ,此时 ${S}_{2} = {S}_{4} = {S}_{6} = \cdots  = 0$ ,所以 ${S}_{1} \cdot  {S}_{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {S}_{k} = 0$ 成立,充分性成立; 必要性: 若 ${S}_{1} \cdot  {S}_{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {S}_{k} = 0$ ,则必有 ${S}_{i} = 0  \left( {1 \leq  i \leq  k}\right)$ . 显然 $q \neq  1$ ,故 ${S}_{i} = \dfrac{{a}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{i}}\right) }{1 - q} = 0$ ,所以 ${q}^{i} = 1$ ,故只能是 $q =  - 1$ ,此时 ${a}_{k} + {a}_{k + 1} = 0$ . 综上所述, \ding{173} 为真命题. 故选 C.

6. C

【解析】等差数列前 $n$ 项和 ${S}_{n} = k{n}^{2} + {bn}$ ,当 $n$ 无穷大时, $\left| {S}_{n}\right|$ 也无穷大,当 $n$ 足够大时, $\left| {S}_{n}\right|$ 一定单调递增,不可能满足 $\left| {S}_{k}\right|  > \left| {S}_{k + 1}\right|$ ,所以选项 ${A},{B},{D}$ 都错误,所以选 C.

\section*{三、解答题}

7. (1)设等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公差为 $d$ ，又因为 ${S}_{n} = n{a}_{1} +  \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2}d$ ,且 ${a}_{1} = 2$ ,所以 ${S}_{5} = {10} + {10d} = {20}$ ,故 $d = 1$ . 所以 ${a}_{n} = n + 1$

(2)由(1)可知， ${a}_{4} = 5$ ，又 ${a}_{4} + {b}_{4} = 9$ ，所以 ${b}_{4} = 4$ . 因为 $q =  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,可得 ${b}_{1} = \dfrac{{b}_{4}}{{q}^{3}} = {32}$ ,所以 ${T}_{n} = \left( {{a}_{1} - {b}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {{a}_{2} - {b}_{2}}\right)  + \cdots  +  \left( {{a}_{n} - {b}_{n}}\right)  = \left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{n}}\right)  - \left( {{b}_{1} + {b}_{2} + \cdots  + {b}_{n}}\right)  =  \dfrac{n\left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{n}}\right) }{2} - \dfrac{{b}_{1}\left( {1 - {q}^{n}}\right) }{1 - q} = \dfrac{n\left( {n + 3}\right) }{2} + {2}^{6 - n} - {64}.$

8. (1) ${S}_{n} = {n}^{2} + n \Longrightarrow  {S}_{n - 1} = {\left( n - 1\right) }^{2} + n - 1\left( {n \geq  2}\right) ,{a}_{n} =  {S}_{n} - {S}_{n - 1} = {2n}\left( {n \geq  2}\right)$ ,又 ${a}_{1} = {S}_{1} = 2$ ,所以 ${a}_{n} = {2n}$ ;

(2) $\dfrac{1}{{a}_{n}{a}_{n + 1}} = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {\dfrac{1}{n} - \dfrac{1}{n + 1}}\right)$ ，所以 ${H}_{n} = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{1}{2} - }\right.  \left. {\dfrac{1}{3} + \cdots  + \dfrac{1}{n} - \dfrac{1}{n + 1}}\right)$ ,所以 ${H}_{n} = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{n + 1}}\right)  = \dfrac{n}{4\left( {n + 1}\right) }$ .

\section*{考点 7 数列与不等式、最值及范围问题}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$

【解析】由题意可得等差数列的公差为 $t - 1$ ,所以 ${a}_{n} = 1 +  \left( {n - 1}\right) \left( {t - 1}\right)$ ,所以 ${a}_{6} = {5t} - 4$ ,等比数列的公比为 $t$ ,则 ${b}_{n} = {b}_{1}{t}^{n - 1} \Longrightarrow  {b}_{6} = {t}^{5}$ ,因为 ${a}_{6} + {b}_{6} > {38}$ ,即 ${t}^{5} + {5t} - 4 > {38}$ ,即 ${t}^{5} + {5t} - {42} > 0$ ,设 $f\left( t\right)  = {t}^{5} + {5t} - {42}$ ,由复合函数的单调性可得 $f\left( t\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上单调递增,再由二分法确定当 $t = 2$ 时, $f\left( t\right)  =$ 0,所以实数 $t$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ . 故答案为: $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ .

2. 25

【解析】 ${a}_{n} = 3 \times  {\left( 3\sqrt{3}\right) }^{n - 1} = {3}^{\dfrac{3}{2}n - \dfrac{1}{2}} \Longrightarrow  {b}_{n} = {\log }_{3}{a}_{n} = \dfrac{3n}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}$ , ${\log }_{3}{a}_{1} - {\log }_{3}{a}_{2} + {\log }_{3}{a}_{3} - {\log }_{3}{a}_{4} + \cdots  + {\left( -1\right) }^{n + 1}{\log }_{3}{a}_{n} > {18} \Longrightarrow  n$ 为奇数, ${S}_{n} = {b}_{1} + \left( {-{b}_{2} + {b}_{3}}\right)  + \left( {-{b}_{4} + {b}_{5}}\right)  + \cdots  + \left( {-{b}_{2k} + }\right.  \left. {b}_{{2k} + 1}\right)  = 1 + \dfrac{3k}{2} > {18},k > \dfrac{34}{3},{k}_{\min } = {12} \Longrightarrow  n = {2k} + 1 = {25}$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

3. C

【解析】 ${b}_{n} = {\log }_{0.5}{a}_{n} = {\log }_{0.5}\left( {{a}_{1} \cdot  {q}^{n - 1}}\right)  = {\log }_{0.5}\dfrac{1}{64} +  {\log }_{0.5}{q}^{n - 1} = 6 + \left( {n - 1}\right) {\log }_{0.5}q = n{\log }_{0.5}q + 6 - {\log }_{0.5}q$ ,所以 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是以 ${b}_{1} = 6$ 为首项, $d = {\log }_{0.5}q$ 为公差的等差数列,若当且仅当 $n = 4$ 时, $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 $n$ 项和 ${B}_{n}$ 取得最大值,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {b}_{4} > 0 \\  {b}_{5} < 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} 6 + 3{\log }_{0.5}q > 0 \\  6 + 4{\log }_{0.5}q < 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\log }_{0.5}q >  - 2 \\  {\log }_{0.5}q <  - \dfrac{3}{2} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\log }_{0.5}q > {\log }_{0.5}{0.5} - \dfrac{3}{2} \\  {\log }_{0.5}q < {\log }_{0.5}{0.5}{}^{-\dfrac{3}{2}} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right. }\right. }\right.$ 0. ${5}^{-\dfrac{3}{2}} < q < {0.5}^{-2}$ 即, $2\sqrt{2} < q < 4$ ,故选: C.

4. C

【解析】对于 ${A}$ 选项,若 $0 < a < 1$ ,则因为 $a < 6,{a}_{3} = {3a} +  3 > a = {a}_{4},0 < a < 1$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 单调递减, ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}$ ,集合 $A$ 中有无穷多个元素,矛盾,故 $a > 1$ ,若 $1 < a < 6$ ,则 $a - 6 < 0$ ,所以 ${a}_{1} > {a}_{2} > {a}_{3}$ ,又 ${a}_{3} = {3a} + 3 > a = {a}_{4}$ ,所以 ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}(k = 1$ , $2,3,4)$ ,而 $a > 1$ ,所以 ${a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1}$ 对 $n \geq  4$ 恒成立,所以 ${h}_{k} =  {a}_{4}\left( {k \geq  4}\right)$ ,故集合 $A$ 共有 4 个元素,满足; 若 $a = 6$ ,则 ${a}_{1} =  {a}_{2} = {a}_{3} = {21} > {a}_{4}$ ,且 ${a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1}$ 对 $n \geq  4$ 恒成立,所以集合 $A = \{ {21},6\}$ ,矛盾; 若 $a > 6$ ,则 ${a}_{1} < {a}_{2} < {a}_{3}$ ,且 ${a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1}$ 对 $n \geq  4$ 恒成立,集合 $A$ 中最多只有两个元素,矛盾; 因此 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {1,6}\right) ,{A}$ 选项正确; 对于 ${C}$ 选项,若 $0 < a < 1$ ,则 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}  \left( {1 \leq  n \leq  4}\right)$ 单调递减, ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}\left( {k = 1,2,3,4}\right)$ ,要使集合 $A$ 中仅有 4 个元素,则 ${4a} - 3 \leq  0$ ,即 $0 < a \leq  \dfrac{3}{4}$ 也满足要求,所以 ${C}$ 选项必定错误; 对于 ${B},{D}$ 选项,当 $a \geq  6$ 时, ${h}_{k} = {a}_{1}(1 \leq  k \leq  m)$ ,当 $k > m$ 时, ${h}_{k} = \min \left\{  {{a}_{1},{a}_{m + 1}}\right\}$ ,集合 $A$ 最多只有两个元素,矛盾; 若 $1 < a < 6$ ,则 ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}\left( {1 \leq  k \leq  m}\right)$ ,所以 $m \leq  4$ ,若 $m = 4$ ,则当 ${a}_{5} = {a}^{2} \geq  {4a} - 3 = {a}_{4}$ 即 $a \geq  3$ 时,集合 $A$ 有 4 个元素,此时 $A = \left\{  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3},{a}_{4}}\right\}$ ,且 $a = 3$ 时 ${a}_{5} =  {a}_{4} \in  A$ ,所以 D 选项正确; 若 $m = 3$ ,由 ${A}$ 选项分析知 ${a}_{4} \in  A,{a}_{5} \notin  A$ ,若 $m = 1$ 或 2,则集合 $A$ 不可能有 4 个元素,矛盾; 若 $0 < a < 1$ ,则 ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}\left( {1 \leq  k \leq  m}\right)$ ,所以 $m \leq  4$ ,若 $m = 4$ , 则同 ${C}$ 选项分析知 $0 < a \leq  \dfrac{3}{4}$ ，此时 ${a}_{4} \in  A$ ，若 $m \leq  3$ ，则 ${h}_{k} = {a}_{k}\left( {k > 3}\right)$ ,集合 $A$ 中有无穷多个元素,矛盾; 综上可知, 无论 $m$ 和 $a$ 取何值，只要集合 $A$ 中仅有 4 个元素时，都有 ${a}_{4} \in  A$ , B 正确. 故选: C.

5. A

【解析】对于 \ding{172} :因为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}  ,\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 均为等差数列,设 ${a}_{n} = {kn} + m,{b}_{n} = {cn} + d$ ,则 ${a}_{n} - {b}_{n} = \left( {k - c}\right) n + m - d$ ,若 $k - c > 0$ ,可知当 $n >  - \dfrac{m - d}{k - c}$ 时, ${a}_{n} - {b}_{n} > 0$ 恒成立,不满足交错数列; 若 $k - c = 0$ ,可知 ${a}_{n} - {b}_{n}$ 的符号不变,不满足交错数列; 若 $k - c < 0$ ,可知当 $n >  - \dfrac{m - d}{k - c}$ 时, ${a}_{n} - {b}_{n} < 0$ 恒成立,不满足交错数列; 综上所述: 对任意等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 、 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}  ,\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 均不是 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的交错数列,故 \ding{172} 正确；对于 \ding{173} :因为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 为等比数列,设 ${a}_{n} = a{q}^{n},{aq} \neq  0$ ,等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q$ ,不妨假设 $a > 0,q > 0,{b}_{n} = a{\left( -2q\right) }^{n}$ ,此时等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $- {2q} < 0$ ,当 $n$ 为奇数,则 ${b}_{n} =  - a{q}^{n} \cdot  {2}^{n} <  a{q}^{n} = {a}_{n}$ ; 当 $n$ 为偶数,则 ${b}_{n} = a{q}^{n} \cdot  {2}^{n} > a{q}^{n} = {a}_{n}$ ,满足 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的交错数列; 若等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q < 0$ ,根据对称结构,上述结论依然成立,同理若 $a < 0,q > 0,{b}_{n} = a{\left( -2q\right) }^{n}$ ,此时等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $- {2q} < 0$ ,当 $n$ 为奇数,则 ${b}_{n} =  - a{q}^{n} \cdot  {2}^{n} > a{q}^{n} = {a}_{n}$ ; 当 $n$ 为偶数,则 ${b}_{n} = a{q}^{n} \cdot  {2}^{n} < a{q}^{n} =  {a}_{n}$ ,满足 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的交错数列,若等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为 $q < 0$ ,根据对称结构,上述结论依然成立,综上所述: 对任意给定的等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,都存在等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ ,使得 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的交错数列,故 \ding{173} 正确；故选:A.

6. D

【解析】由 “ $G$ 数列” 的定义,对任意的 $n \geq  2$ ,存在大于 1 的整数 $m$ ,使得 $\left( {{S}_{m} - {a}_{n}}\right) \left( {{S}_{m} - {a}_{n + 1}}\right)  < 0$ ,成立,则对任意的 $n \geq  2$ ,存在大于 1 的整数 $m$ ,使得 ${a}_{n} < {S}_{m} < {a}_{n + 1}$ ,对于命题  \ding{172}  不成立，理由如下:假设存在 ${a}_{n} < {S}_{m} < {a}_{n + 1} < {S}_{m + 1} < \cdots$ , 当 $d > 0$ 时,总存在 ${a}_{k} > {2d}$ ,由于对任意正整数 $n$ ,有 ${a}_{n + 1} -  {a}_{n} = d$ ,所以总存在正整数 $k$ ,使得 ${S}_{k - 1}$ 与 ${S}_{k} - {S}_{k - 1} > {2d}$ , 所以不会存在 ${a}_{n} < {S}_{k - 1} < {a}_{n + 1} < {S}_{k} < {a}_{n + 2}$ ,当 $d < 0$ 时,总存在 ${a}_{k} < {2d}$ ,由于对任意正整数 $n$ ,有 ${a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} = d$ ,所以总存在正整数 $k$ ,使得 ${S}_{k - 1}$ 与 ${S}_{k} - {S}_{k - 1} < {2d}$ ,所以不会存在 ${a}_{n} < {S}_{k - 1} < {a}_{n + 1} < {S}_{k} < {a}_{n + 2}$ ; 对于命题 \ding{173} 不成立,理由如下: 举例说明: 如 ${a}_{n} = {2}^{n}$ ,有 ${S}_{n} = {2}^{n + 1} - 2$ ,因为 ${a}_{n} < {S}_{m} < {a}_{n + 1}$ , 所以 ${2}^{n} < {2}^{m + 1} - 2 < {2}^{n + 1}$ ,可以取 $m = n$ ,就可以保证不等式成立,综上所述:  \ding{172} 不成立, \ding{173} 不成立. 故选: D.

\section*{三、解答题}

7. (1)因为数列 ${10},7,m,4,3$ 具有性质 “ $P\left( 2\right)$ ”，所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{7}{2} < m < 7 \\  \dfrac{1}{2}m < 4 < m \end{array} \Longrightarrow  4 < m < 7,m \in  \mathbf{N}}\right.$ ,所以 $m = 5,6$ ;

(2)假设存在 $t$ 使得数列 ${a}_{n} = \left( {n + 1}\right) {\left( \dfrac{t}{9}\right) }^{n}$ 具有性质“ $P\left( t\right)$ ”, 所以 $\dfrac{1}{t}\left( {n + 1}\right) {\left( \dfrac{t}{9}\right) }^{n} < \left( {n + 2}\right) {\left( \dfrac{t}{9}\right) }^{n + 1} < \left( {n + 1}\right) {\left( \dfrac{t}{9}\right) }^{n} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {t}^{2} > \dfrac{9\left( {n + 1}\right) }{n + 2} \\  t < \dfrac{9\left( {n + 1}\right) }{n + 2} \end{array}\text{ ,因为 }\dfrac{9\left( {n + 1}\right) }{n + 2} = 9 - \dfrac{9}{n + 2}}\right.$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递增,所以 $9 - \dfrac{9}{n + 2} \in  \lbrack 6,9)$ ,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {t}^{2} \geq  9 \\  t < 6 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  t \in  \lbrack 3,6)}\right.$ ,所以存在 $t \in  \lbrack 3,6)$ 使得数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 “ $P\left( t\right)$ ”;

(3)证明:因为 ${a}_{1} = \dfrac{2}{3},{a}_{n + 1} = f\left( {a}_{n}\right)  = \ln \left( {{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}\right)  - \ln {a}_{n}$ ， 所以 ${a}_{1} = \dfrac{2}{3},{a}_{n + 1} = \ln \left( {{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}\right)  - \ln {a}_{n}$ ,所以 ${a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} =  \ln \left( {{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}\right)  - \ln {a}_{n} - {a}_{n} = \ln \dfrac{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}{{a}_{n} \cdot  {{e}}^{{a}_{n}}}$ ,令 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 1 - x$ . ${{e}}^{x},x > 0$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - x{{e}}^{x} < 0$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减,所以 $g\left( x\right)  < g\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,即 ${{e}}^{x} - 1 < x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ ,所以 $0 <  \dfrac{{{e}}^{x} - 1}{x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}} < 1$ ,即 $0 < \dfrac{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}{{a}_{n} \cdot  {{e}}^{{a}_{n}}} < 1,\ln \dfrac{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}{{a}_{n} \cdot  {{e}}^{{a}_{n}}} < 0$ ,所以 ${a}_{n + 1} <  {a}_{n},\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n} - {a}_{n + 1} = \dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n} - \ln \left( {{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}\right)  + \ln {a}_{n} = \ln {{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n}} -  \ln \left( {{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}\right)  + \ln {a}_{n} = \ln \dfrac{{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n}} \cdot  {a}_{n}}{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1}$ ,令 $h\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x} \cdot  x - {{e}}^{x} + 1$ , $x > 0$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x} \cdot  x + {{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x} - {{e}}^{x} = {{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x}\left( {\dfrac{1}{3}x + 1 - {{e}}^{\dfrac{2}{3}x}}\right)$ , 令 $\varphi \left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}x + 1 - {{e}}^{\dfrac{2}{3}x},x > 0$ ,则 ${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3} - \dfrac{2}{3}{{e}}^{\dfrac{2}{3}x}$ 是减函数，所以 ${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < {\varphi }^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3} - \dfrac{2}{3} =  - \dfrac{1}{3} < 0$ ，所以 $\varphi \left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减,所以 $\varphi \left( x\right)  < \varphi \left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,即 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  <  0,h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上单调递减,所以 $h\left( x\right)  < h\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,即 ${{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x} \cdot  x < {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,所以 $0 < \dfrac{{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}x} \cdot  x}{{{e}}^{x} - 1} < 1$ ,即 $0 < \dfrac{{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n}} \cdot  {a}_{n}}{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1} < 1$ , 所以 $\ln \dfrac{{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n}} \cdot  {a}_{n}}{{{e}}^{{a}_{n}} - 1} < 0$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1}$ . 综上: $\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1} <  {a}_{n}$ ,所以数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 具有性质 “ $P\left( 3\right)$ ”. $\dfrac{1}{3}{a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} >  \dfrac{1}{3}$ ，所以当 $n \geq  2$ 时， ${a}_{n} = \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{{a}_{n - 1}} \cdot  \dfrac{{a}_{n - 1}}{{a}_{n - 2}} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  \dfrac{{a}_{2}}{{a}_{1}} \cdot  {a}_{1} > \dfrac{2}{3}$ . ${\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n - 1} = 2 \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}$ ,当 $n = 1$ 时, ${S}_{1} = {a}_{1} = \dfrac{2}{3} = 1 - \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${S}_{n} > 2 \cdot  \dfrac{\dfrac{1}{3}\left( {1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}}\right) }{1 - \dfrac{1}{3}} = 1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}$ . 综上,当 $n =$ 1 时, ${S}_{n} = 1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}$ ,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${S}_{n} > 1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{n}$ .

\section*{考点 8 数列与其他知识点的关联问题}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 5600

【解析】甲、乙、丙三条生产线生产数量分别为 $x,y,z$ , ${2y} = x + z \Longrightarrow  x + y + z = {3y} = {16800},y = {5600}.$

2. 3

【解析】因为数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是公差为 1 的等差数列,可设: ${a}_{n} =  {a}_{1} + n - 1$ . 所以 $f\left( {a}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {a}_{2}\right)  + f\left( {a}_{3}\right)  = f\left( {a}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {{a}_{1} + }\right.$ 1) $+ f\left( {{a}_{1} + 2}\right)  = \left( {{a}_{1}^{2} - 8{a}_{1} + {10}}\right)  + \left\lbrack  {{\left( {a}_{1} + 1\right) }^{2} - 8\left( {{a}_{1} + 1}\right)  + }\right.  {10}\rbrack  + \left\lbrack  {{\left( {a}_{1} + 2\right) }^{2} - 8\left( {{a}_{1} + 2}\right)  + {10}}\right\rbrack   = 3{a}_{1}^{2} - {18}{a}_{1} + {11}$ ,所以当 ${a}_{1} =  - \dfrac{-{18}}{2 \times  3} = 3$ 时, $f\left( {a}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {a}_{2}\right)  + f\left( {a}_{3}\right)$ 的值最小. 故答案为: 3 .

3. 2023

【解析】函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {\left( x + 1\right) }^{3} + 1$ ,可看成 $y = {x}^{3}$ 向左平移 1 个单位,向上平移 1 个单位得到,因为 $y = {x}^{3}$ 的对称中心为 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {\left( x + 1\right) }^{3} + 1$ 的对称中心为 $( - 1$ , 1),所以 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {-2 - x}\right)  = 2$ ,因为正项等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 满足 ${a}_{1012} = \dfrac{1}{10}$ ,所以 ${a}_{1} \cdot  {a}_{2023} = {a}_{2} \cdot  {a}_{2022} = \cdots  = {a}_{1012}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{100}$ , 所以 $\lg {a}_{1} + \lg {a}_{2023} = \lg {a}_{2} + \lg {a}_{2022} = \cdots  = 2\lg {a}_{1012} =  - 2$ ,所以 $f\left( {\lg {a}_{1}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2023}}\right)  = f\left( {\lg {a}_{2}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2022}}\right)  = \cdots  = 2$ , $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2023}}f\left( {\lg {a}_{k}}\right)  = f\left( {\lg {a}_{1}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{3}}\right)  + \cdots  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2023}}\right)$  \ding{172} , $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2023}}f\left( {\lg {a}_{k}}\right)  = f\left( {\operatorname{lga}}_{2023}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2022}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2021}}\right)  + \cdots  +  f\left( {\lg {a}_{1}}\right)$  \ding{173} ，则  \ding{172}  +  \ding{173}  得: $2\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2023}}f\left( {\lg {a}_{k}}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {f\left( {\lg {a}_{1}}\right)  + }\right.  \left. {f\left( {\lg {a}_{2023}}\right) }\right\rbrack   + \left\lbrack  {f\left( {\lg {a}_{2}}\right)  + f\left( {\lg {a}_{2022}}\right) }\right\rbrack   + \cdots  + \left\lbrack  {f\left( {\lg {a}_{2023}}\right)  + }\right.  \left. {f\left( {\lg {a}_{1}}\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,即 $2\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2023}}f\left( {\lg {a}_{k}}\right)  = {2023} \times  2$ ,所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{2023}}f\left( {\lg {a}_{k}}\right)  =$ 2023. 故答案为:2023.

\section*{二、选择题}

4. D

【解析】对于 ${A}$ ,若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x - {a}_{2}}\right|$ 是奇函数,则 $f\left( 0\right)  = \left| {0 - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {0 - {a}_{2}}\right|  = 0 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = {a}_{2} = 0,d = 0$ ,与题意不符,所以 ${A}$ 错误; 对于 ${B}$ ,若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  +  \mid  x -  {a}_{2} \mid$ 是偶函数,则 $f\left( x\right)  = f\left( {-x}\right)  \Longrightarrow  \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x - {a}_{2}}\right|  =  \mid   - x -  {a}_{1}\left| +\right|  - x - {a}_{2}\left|  = \right| x + {a}_{1}\left| +\right| x + {a}_{2} \mid  , \Longrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - {a}_{1} = {a}_{1} \\   - {a}_{2} = {a}_{2} \end{array}\right.$ 或 ${a}_{1} + {a}_{2} = 0$ ,所以 ${B}$ 错误; 对于 ${C}$ ,若 $d = 0$ ,则函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x - {a}_{2}}\right|  = 2\left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|$ ,函数图像关于 $x = {a}_{1}\left( {a}_{1}\right.$ 不恒为 0 ) 对称所以不是偶函数，所以 C 错误；对于 ${D}$ ，若 $d \neq$ 0,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x - {a}_{2}}\right|  \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} f\left( {x + {a}_{1}}\right)  = \left| {x + {a}_{1} - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x + {a}_{1} - {a}_{2}}\right|  = \left| x\right|  + \left| {x - d}\right| \\  f\left( {{a}_{2} - x}\right)  = \left| {{a}_{2} - x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {{a}_{2} - x - {a}_{2}}\right|  = \left| x\right|  + \left| {x - d}\right|  \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  f\left( {x + {a}_{1}}\right)  = f\left( {{a}_{2} - x}\right) ,f\left( x\right)  = \left| {x - {a}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {x - {a}_{2}}\right|$ 的图像是轴对称图形,对称轴为 $x = \dfrac{{a}_{1} + {a}_{2}}{2}$ ,所以 ${D}$ 正确. 故选 ${D}$ . 5. A

【解析】由 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  \leq  {2}^{x}$ ,可得 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)  - f(x +  1) \leq  {2}^{x + 1}$ ,因为 $f\left( {x + 2}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  \geq  3 \times  {2}^{x}$ ,所以 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  -  f\left( x\right)  =  - \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x + 2}\right)  - f\left( {x + 1}\right) }\right\rbrack   + f\left( {x + 2}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  \geq  - {2}^{x + 1} + 3 \times  {2}^{x} = {2}^{x}$ ,又因为 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  \leq  {2}^{x}$ ,所以 $f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)  = {2}^{x}$ ,则 $f\left( {2025}\right)  - f\left( 0\right)  = \lbrack f\left( {2025}\right)  -  f\left( {2024}\right) \rbrack  + \left\lbrack  {f\left( {2024}\right)  - f\left( {2023}\right) }\right\rbrack   + \cdots  + \left\lbrack  {f\left( 1\right)  - f\left( 0\right) }\right\rbrack   =  {2}^{2024} + {2}^{2023} + \cdots  + 2 + 1 = \dfrac{1 - {2}^{2025}}{1 - 2} = {2}^{2025} - 1$ ,所以 $f\left( {2025}\right)  =  {2}^{2025} + {2024}$ . 故选: A.

6. D

【解析】 ${b}_{n + T} = {b}_{n} \Longrightarrow  \cos \left( {a}_{n + T}\right)  = \cos \left( {a}_{n}\right)$ ,取 ${a}_{n + T} = {a}_{n} +  {2\pi }$ ,则公差 $d = \dfrac{2\pi }{T}$ ,当 $T = 4$ 时, $d = \dfrac{2\pi }{4} = \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,此时角度序列为: ${a}_{1},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{\pi }{2},{a}_{1} + \pi ,{a}_{1} + \dfrac{3\pi }{2}$ ,取 ${a}_{1} = 0$ ,则对应的余弦值为 $1,0, - 1,0$ ,此时 $X = \{ 1,0, - 1\}$ ,三个元素合题意; 当 $T = 5$ 时, $d = \dfrac{2\pi }{5}$ ,此时角度序列为: ${a}_{1},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{2\pi }{5},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{4\pi }{5}$ , ${a}_{1} + \dfrac{6\pi }{5},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{8\pi }{5}$ ,取 ${a}_{1} = 0$ ,则对应的余弦值为 $1,\cos \dfrac{2\pi }{5}$ , $\cos \dfrac{4\pi }{5},\cos \dfrac{6\pi }{5},\cos \dfrac{8\pi }{5}$ ,又 $\cos \dfrac{4\pi }{5} = \cos \dfrac{6\pi }{5},\cos \dfrac{8\pi }{5} = \cos \dfrac{2\pi }{5}$ , 此时 $X$ 也是三个元素,合题意; 当 $T = 6$ 时, $d = \dfrac{2\pi }{6} = \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ,此时角度序列为: ${a}_{1},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{\pi }{3},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{2\pi }{3},{a}_{1} + \pi ,{a}_{1} + \dfrac{4\pi }{3},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{5\pi }{3}$ , 取 ${a}_{1} = \dfrac{\pi }{6}$ ,则对应的余弦值为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},0, - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}, - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2},0,\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ,此时 $X$ 也是三个元素,合题意; 当 $T = 7$ 时, $d = \dfrac{2\pi }{7}$ ,此时角度序列为: ${a}_{1},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{2\pi }{7},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{4\pi }{7},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{6\pi }{7},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{8\pi }{7},{a}_{1} + \dfrac{10\pi }{7}$ , ${a}_{1} + \dfrac{12\pi }{7}$ ,由于 7 是质数,角度间隔 $\dfrac{2\pi }{7}$ 无法分解为更小的对称单元, 余弦值的对称性不足以将 7 个不同角度映射为 3 个不同值，故选:D.

\section*{三、解答题}

7. ( 1 )由等差数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的前 $n$ 项和为 ${S}_{n}$ ，公差 $d = 2$ ，因为 ${S}_{10} = {100}$ ,可得 ${10}{a}_{1} + \dfrac{{10} \times  9}{2} \times  2 = {100}$ ,解得 ${a}_{1} = 1$ ,所以 ${a}_{n} =  1 + \left( {n - 1}\right)  \times  2 = {2n} - 1$ ,即数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的通项公式为 ${a}_{n} = {2n} - 1$ ;

(2)由题意，从集合 $\left\{  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3},{a}_{4},{a}_{5},{a}_{6}}\right.$ \} 中任取 3 个元素， 共有 20 种不同的取法, 其中这 3 个元素能成等差数列有 $\left\{  {\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {{a}_{1},{a}_{3},{a}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{a}_{2},{a}_{3},{a}_{4}}\right) ,\left( {{a}_{2},{a}_{4},{a}_{6}}\right) ,}\right.  \left. {\left( {{a}_{3},{a}_{4},{a}_{5}}\right) ,\left( {{a}_{4},{a}_{5},{a}_{6}}\right) }\right\}$ ,有 6 种不同的取法,所以事件 $A$ 的概率为 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{6}{20} = \dfrac{3}{10}$ .

8. (1) ${a}_{2} - {b}_{2} = {a}_{3} - {b}_{3} = {b}_{4} - {a}_{4} \Longrightarrow$

$\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {b}_{3} - {b}_{2} = {a}_{3} - {a}_{2} = d \Longrightarrow  4{b}_{1} - 2{b}_{1} = d \Longrightarrow  d = 2{b}_{1}, \\  {b}_{3} + {b}_{4} = {a}_{3} + {a}_{4} \Longrightarrow  8{b}_{1} + 4{b}_{1} = 2{a}_{1} + {5d} \end{array}\right.$

$\Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} = {b}_{1}$ ;

(2)由(1)得 $d = 2{a}_{1},{a}_{n} = {a}_{1} + d\left( {n - 1}\right)  = \left( {{2n} - 1}\right) {a}_{1},{b}_{n} =  {2}^{n - 1}{a}_{1},{b}_{k} = {a}_{m} + {a}_{1},1 \leq  m \leq  {50}$ ,所以 ${b}_{k} = {a}_{m} + {a}_{1} = {2m}{a}_{1} =  {2}^{k - 1}{a}_{1} \Longrightarrow  {2}^{k - 1} = {2m},k = 1 + {\log }_{2}^{m},m = 1,2,4,8,{16},{32} \Longrightarrow  k =$ 1,2,3,4,5,6,集合中的元素个数为 6 个.

9.(1)因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是实数集 $\mathbf{R}$ 上的严格增函数，所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = m + \cos x > 0$ 对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都成立，因为函数 $y = m + \cos x$ 的最小值为 $m - 1$ ,所以 $m > 1$ ;

(2) ${b}_{n} = \sin {a}_{n} + m{a}_{n}$ ，若 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列，则 ${b}_{n} + {b}_{n + 2} =  2{b}_{n + 1}$ 对一切正整数 $n$ 成立，即 $\sin {a}_{n} + m{a}_{n} + \sin {a}_{n + 2} +  m{a}_{n + 2} = 2\sin {a}_{n + 1} + {2m}{a}_{n + 1}$ ,将 ${a}_{n} + {a}_{n + 2} = 2{a}_{n + 1}$ 代入化简得 $\sin {a}_{n} + \sin {a}_{n + 2} = 2\sin {a}_{n + 1}$ ,即 $\sin \left( {{a}_{n + 1} - d}\right)  + \sin \left( {{a}_{n + 1} + }\right.  d) = 2\sin {a}_{n + 1}$ ,展开化简得 $2\sin {a}_{n + 1} \cdot  \left( {\cos d - 1}\right)  = 0$ 对一切正整数 $n$ 成立，所以 $\cos d = 1$ ，故 $d = {2k\pi }\left( {k \neq  0,k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ；

此时 ${b}_{n} = \sin {a}_{n} + m{a}_{n} = \sin \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1} + \left( {n - 1}\right) {2k\pi }}\right\rbrack   + m\left\lbrack  {{a}_{1}+(n - }\right.$ 1) ${2k\pi }\rbrack  = m\left( {n - 1}\right) {2k\pi } + m{a}_{1} + \sin {a}_{1}$ ,所以 ${b}_{n + 1} - {b}_{n} = {m2k\pi }$ 为常数，故 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列；

(3)令 $g\left( x\right)  = \left( {x + \sin x}\right)  - \left( {{kx} + b}\right)  = \left( {1 - k}\right) x + \sin x - b$ 则当 $m \in  \mathbf{Z}$ 时, $g\left( {\dfrac{b}{1 - k} + {2m\pi }}\right)  = 2\left( {1 - k}\right) {m\pi } + \sin \dfrac{b}{1 - k},k > 1$ 时,存在 $m \in  \mathbf{Z}$ 使得 $g\left( {\dfrac{b}{1 - k} + {2m\pi }}\right)  < 0$ ,即存在 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 使得 $f\left( x\right)  < {kx} + b$ ,与题意不符; 同理, $k < 1$ 时,存在 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 使得 $f\left( x\right)  < {kx} + b$ ,与题意不符; $k = 1$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  = \sin x - b$ ,当 $b >$ -1 时,显然存在 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 使得 $g\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,即存在 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 使得 $f\left( x\right)  < {kx} + b$ ; 当 $b <  - 1$ 时,对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $g\left( x\right)  > 0$ ; 当 $b = 1$ 时,存在 ${x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = k{x}_{0} + b$ ,且对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $g\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ ,即对任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  \geq  {kx} +  b$ . 综上,存在直线 $y = {kx} + b$ 满足题意,直线方程为 $y = x - 1$ . 10. (1) 任取 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - x + A\sin \left( {-\dfrac{\pi x}{2}}\right)  =  - x - A\sin \left( \dfrac{\pi x}{2}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,因此函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数;

(2)反证法:假设数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等差数列，公差为 $d$ ，由数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格增数列可知 $d > 0$ . 因为 ${a}_{n + 1} = {a}_{n} + A\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2}$ ,所以 $A\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2} = d$ ,即 $\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2} = \dfrac{d}{A} =$ 非零常数,因为 $\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{1}}{2} =  \sin \dfrac{\pi \left( {{a}_{1} + d}\right) }{2} = \sin \dfrac{\pi \left( {{a}_{1} + {2d}}\right) }{2} = \cdots  \neq  0$ ,所以 $d = {4k}$ (其中 $k$ 是正整数). 因为 $d \geq  4,0 < A < 4$ ,所以 $\dfrac{d}{A} > 1$ . 方程 $\sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2} = \dfrac{d}{A}$ 无解, 矛盾. 假设不成立,即当 $A < 4$ 时,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不是等差数列;

(3)若数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是等比数列,则其各项均非零,设其公比为 $q$ , 由 ${a}_{n + 1} = {a}_{n} + 8\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2}$ 得 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} = 1 + \dfrac{8}{{a}_{n}}\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2}$ ,即 $\sin \dfrac{\pi {a}_{n}}{2} =  \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{8}\left( {q - 1}\right)$ . 考虑方程 $\sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2} = \dfrac{q - 1}{8}x,{a}_{n}$ 均为该方程 (记为 \ding{172} ) 的解. 由函数 $y = \sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2}$ 的值域为 $\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 可知 $\left| {\dfrac{q - 1}{8}x}\right|  \leq  1$ , 即 $\left| x\right|  \leq  \dfrac{8}{\left| q - 1\right| }$ ,所以 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  \leq  \dfrac{8}{\left| q - 1\right| }$ . 若 $q <  - 1$ ,则当 $n$ 充分大时 $\left( {n > {\log }_{\left| q\right| }\dfrac{8}{{a}_{1}\left| {q - 1}\right| } + 1\text{ 时 }}\right) ,\left| {a}_{n}\right|  > \dfrac{8}{\left| q - 1\right| }$ ,这与 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  \leq  \dfrac{8}{\left| q - 1\right| }$ 矛盾,从而不合题意. 若 $- 1 < q < 0$ ,函数 $y = \sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2} -  \dfrac{q - 1}{8}x$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 是严格增函数,由 $x = 0$ 时 $y = 0$ ,可知函数当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,0)\cup (0,1}\right\rbrack$ 时,均有 $y \neq  0$ ,因此函数的零点 (即方程 \ding{172} 的解)的绝对值均大于 1，即 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  > 1$ . 但若 $- 1 < q < 0$ ，由 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  = {a}_{1}{\left| q\right| }^{n - 1}$ ,则当 $n$ 充分大时 $\left( {n > 1 + {\log }_{\left| q\right| }\dfrac{1}{{a}_{1}}\text{ 时 }}\right)$ ,将有 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  < 1$ ,这与 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|  > 1$ 矛盾,从而不合题意. 综上,只能有 $q =  - 1$ . 此时方程 \ding{172} 为 $\sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{4}x$ ,记 $g\left( x\right)  = \sin \dfrac{\pi x}{2} +  \dfrac{x}{4},x \in  \mathbf{R}$ . 因为 $g\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} > 0,g\left( 3\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{4} < 0$ ,所以存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {2,3}\right)$ ,使 ${x}_{0}$ 是方程 \ding{172} 的解. 进而由函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数, $- {x}_{0}$ 也是方程 \ding{172} 的解. 因此只需取 ${a}_{n} = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0},n = {2k} - 1, \\   - {x}_{0},n = {2k}, \end{array}\right.$ 其中 $k$ 是正整数即可. 综合上述,存在公比为负数的无穷等比数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ ,其公比只能是 -1 .

11. (1) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,在 $\left( {{a}_{n},f\left( {a}_{n}\right) }\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y -  \ln {a}_{n} = \dfrac{1}{{a}_{n}}\left( {x - {a}_{n}}\right) ,x = 0$ 时, $y = \ln {a}_{n} - 1$ ,即 ${a}_{n + 1} = \ln {a}_{n} - 1$ ; (2)作差法， ${a}_{n + 1} - \left( {{a}_{n} - 2}\right)  = \ln {a}_{n} - {a}_{n} + 1$ ，设 $g\left( x\right)  = \ln x -  x + 1,{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  x = 1,{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0 \Longrightarrow  0 < x < 1,  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0 \Longrightarrow  x > 1$ ,所以 $g{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = g\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ , 即 ${a}_{n + 1} \leq  {a}_{n} - 2$ ,当 ${a}_{n} = 1$ 时等号成立;

(3)公差 $d = {a}_{k} - {a}_{k - 1} = \ln {a}_{k - 1} - {a}_{k - 1} - 1$ ,设 $h\left( x\right)  = \ln x - x -  1,{h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  x = 1,{h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0 \Longrightarrow  0 < x < 1$ ,此时 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格增, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0 \Longrightarrow  x > 1$ ,此时 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格减,所以 $h{\left( x\right) }_{\max } =  h\left( 1\right)  =  - 2$ ,即 $h\left( x\right)  \leq   - 2$ ,所以 $d \leq   - 2$ ,数列递减,因为 $x \rightarrow  0$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  \rightarrow   - \infty ,x \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  \rightarrow   - \infty$ ,所以 $d = \ln {a}_{k - 1} -  {a}_{k - 1} - 1$ 最多两解,此时 $d <  - 2$ ,即最多三项成等差数列, $k = 3$ . 12. (1) 曲线 $y = \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {{x}_{0},\ln {x}_{0}}\right)$ 处的切线斜率为 $\dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}}$ ,又 $\ln \dfrac{1}{{e}} =  - 1$ ,故曲线 $y = \ln x$ 在点 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{{e}}, - 1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y + 1 = {e}\left( {x - \dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right)$ ,令 $y = 0$ ,得 $x = \dfrac{2}{{e}}$ . 所以 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{2}{{e}}$ ;

( 2 )由题, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 ${x}_{n}$ 处的切线方程为 $y - f\left( {x}_{n}\right)  =  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{n}\right) \left( {x - {x}_{n}}\right)$ ,令 $y = 0$ ,可得 ${x}_{n + 1} = {x}_{n} - \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{n}\right) }{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{n}\right) }$ ,即 ${x}_{n + 1} =  \dfrac{{x}_{n}^{2} + 4}{2{x}_{n}}$ . 故 $\dfrac{{x}_{n + 1} + 2}{{x}_{n + 1} - 2} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{n} + 2\right) }^{2}}{{\left( {x}_{n} - 2\right) }^{2}}$ ,即 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} = 2$ . 又 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{13}{6}$ ,故 ${a}_{1} = {\log }_{3}{25}$ . 因此 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是以 ${\log }_{3}{25}$ 为首项,2 为公比的等比数列; (3)由题， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{a - {x}^{2}}{{\left( a + {x}^{2}\right) }^{2}}$ ，故以 $\left( {{x}_{0},\dfrac{{x}_{0}}{a + {x}_{0}^{2}}}\right)$ 为切点的切线方程为 $y - \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{a + {x}_{0}^{2}} = \dfrac{a - {x}_{0}^{2}}{{\left( a + {x}_{0}^{2}\right) }^{2}}\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ . 令 $y = 0$ ,可得到 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{3}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}.$

 \ding{172}  当 $a < 0$ 时，函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{x}{a + {x}^{2}}$ 的大致图像如图所示: 因为 $\left| \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{3}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}\right|  > \left| {x}_{0}\right|$ 等价于 ${x}_{0}^{2} >  - a$ ,因此,当 ${x}_{0}^{2} >  - a$ 时,数列 $\left\{  \left| {x}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 严格增; 同理,当 ${x}_{0}^{2} <  - a$ 时，数列 $\left\{  \left| {x}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 严格减. 所以不存在 ${x}_{0}$ 使得 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期数列.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/60_1278_529_301_255_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

 \ding{173}  当 $a > 0$ 时，函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{x}{a + {x}^{2}}$ 的大致图像如图所示: 令 ${x}_{1} =  - {x}_{0}$ ,可得 $\dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{3}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a} =  - {x}_{0}$ , 即 ${x}_{0}^{2} = \dfrac{a}{3}$ . 依此类推,显然可得 ${x}_{2} =  - {x}_{1},\cdots ,{x}_{n} =  - {x}_{n - 1}$ . 所以,当 ${x}_{0} =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3a}}{3}$ 时,数列 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 为周期数列,且周期 $T = 2$ . 下证唯一性: 当 ${x}_{0}^{2} < \dfrac{a}{3}$ 时, $\left| \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{3}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}\right| \left| {x}_{0}\right|  =  \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{2}}{a - {x}_{0}^{2}}\left| {x}_{0}\right|  < \left| {x}_{0}\right|$ ; 因此,数列 ${\left\{  \left| {x}_{n}\right| \right\}  }^{\text{ 正 }}$ 格减; 当 ${x}_{0}^{2} > \dfrac{a}{3}$ 时, $\dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a} = 2 + \dfrac{2a}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a} \in  \left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ ,所以 $\left| \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{3}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}\right|  =  \left| \dfrac{2{x}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - a}\right| \left| {x}_{0}\right|  > \left| {x}_{0}\right|$ ,因此数列 $\left\{  \left| {x}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 严格增. 综上,当 $a < 0$ 时,不存在 ${x}_{0}$ ,使得 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 为周期数列; 当 $a > 0$ 时,当且仅当 ${x}_{0} =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3a}}{3}$ 时,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 关于 ${x}_{0}$ 的“ $N$ 数列” $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 为周期数列,且周期 $T = 2$ .
