\end{center}
 

13. (1) 因为 ${a}_{n + {2m}} = \sin \left( {\dfrac{\pi }{m}\left( {n + {2m}}\right)  + \dfrac{\pi }{3}}\right)  = \sin \left( {\dfrac{\pi n}{m} + \dfrac{\pi }{3} + }\right.  {2\pi }) = \sin \left( {\dfrac{\pi n}{m} + \dfrac{\pi }{3}}\right)  = {a}_{n}$ ,所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 ${2m}$ 的周期数列;

(2) \ding{172}  当 ${a}_{1} = {a}_{2}$ 时， $\sin {a}_{1} = 0,{a}_{1} = {k\pi }\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ，所以当 ${a}_{1} =  {k\pi }\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 1 的周期数列, \ding{173}  当 ${a}_{1} \neq  {k\pi }  \left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时,记 $f\left( x\right)  = x + \sin x$ ,则 ${a}_{n + 1} = f\left( {a}_{n}\right) ,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  1 + \cos x \geq  0$ ,当且仅当 $x = \left( {2{k}_{1} + 1}\right) \pi \left( {{k}_{1} \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时等号成立, 即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \cos x > 0$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,若 ${a}_{1} <  {a}_{2}$ ,则 $f\left( {a}_{1}\right)  < f\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ ,即 ${a}_{2} < {a}_{3}$ ,进而可得 ${a}_{1} < {a}_{2} < {a}_{3} <  {a}_{4} < \cdots$ ,即 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格增数列,不是周期数列; 同理,若 ${a}_{1} >  {a}_{2}$ ,可得 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是严格减数列,不是周期数列. 综上,当 ${a}_{1} = {k\pi }  \left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 1 的周期数列; 当 ${a}_{1} \neq  {k\pi }\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时， $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不是周期数列；

(3)必要性:若存在 ${a}_{1}$ ，使得 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期数列，设 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的周期为 ${T}_{0}$ ,则 ${b}_{n + {T}_{0}} = {a}_{n + {T}_{0} + 1} - \sin {a}_{n + {T}_{0}} = {a}_{n + 1} - \sin {a}_{n} = {b}_{n}$ ,所以 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 ${T}_{0}$ 的周期数列,充分性: 若 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期数列, 设它的周期为 $T$ ,记 ${a}_{1} = x$ ,则 ${a}_{1} = {f}_{0}\left( x\right)  = x,{a}_{2} = {f}_{1}\left( x\right)  =  {b}_{1} + \sin x$ ,是关于 $x$ 的连续函数; ${a}_{3} = {f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = {b}_{2} + \sin {f}_{1}\left( x\right)$ , 是关于 $x$ 的连续函数; ${a}_{T} = {f}_{T - 1}\left( x\right)$ ,是关于 $x$ 的连续函数; ${a}_{T + 1} = {b}_{T} + \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( x\right)$ ,令 $g\left( x\right)  = x - {b}_{T} - \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( x\right)$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)$ 是连续函数,且 $g\left( {{b}_{T} + 2}\right)  = 2 - \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( x\right)  > 0,g\left( {{b}_{T} - }\right.  2) =  - 2 - \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 存在零点 $c$ ,于是 $c -  {b}_{T} - \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( c\right)  = 0$ ,取 ${a}_{1} = c$ ,则 ${a}_{T + 1} = {b}_{T} + \sin {f}_{T - 1}\left( c\right)  =  c = {a}_{1}$ ,从而 ${a}_{T + 2} = {b}_{T + 1} + \sin {a}_{T + 1} = {b}_{1} + \sin {a}_{1} = {a}_{2},{a}_{T + 3} =  {b}_{T + 2} + \sin {a}_{T + 2} = {b}_{2} + \sin {a}_{2} = {a}_{3}$ ,一般地, ${a}_{n + T} = {a}_{n}$ 对任何正整数 $n$ 都成立,即 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 是周期为 $T$ 的周期数列.

14.(1)因为 ${a}_{2}^{2} - {a}_{1}{a}_{3} = {a}_{2}^{2} - \left( {{a}_{2} - d}\right) \left( {{a}_{2} + d}\right)  = {d}^{2} > 0$ ，所以 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不是等比数列;

(2) $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = {a}_{i}$ 处的切线方程为 $y - f\left( {a}_{i}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {a}_{i}\right) (x -  \left. {a}_{i}\right)$ ,令 $y = 0$ 得 $x = {a}_{i} - \dfrac{f\left( {a}_{i}\right) }{{f}^{\prime }\left( {a}_{i}\right) }$ ,因此,欲使 $f\left( x\right)$ 满足条件, 只需使 $\dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) } =  - d$ ,令 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-\dfrac{x}{d}}$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{d}{{e}}^{-\dfrac{x}{d}}$ , 满足条件,因此,存在指数函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-\dfrac{x}{d}}$ 满足条件;

(3)取 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ : —2,1,4 ，则1， -2,4 成等比数列，所以 $m = 3$ 满足条件. 当 $m \geq  4$ ,首先, $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 不可能所有项均为正数或均为负数,否则,对应的等比数列 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 的公比为正,等比数列严格增或严格减,从而 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 即为等比数列,不可能. 其次,因为 $\left\{  {b}_{n}\right\}$ 是等比数列,所以 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 也是等比数列,不妨设 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 严格增,则 $\left\{  \left| {b}_{n}\right| \right\}$ 的前三项即为 $\left| {a}_{n}\right|$ 中最小的三项,则一定对应于 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 中的连续三项 ${a}_{k},{a}_{k + 1},{a}_{k + 2}\left( {{a}_{k} < 0,{a}_{k + 2} > 0}\right)$ , 不妨设 ${a}_{k + 1} > 0$ ,则 $\left| {a}_{k + 2}\right|  - \left| {a}_{k}\right|  = {a}_{k + 2} + {a}_{k} = 2{a}_{k + 1} > 0$ .

 \ding{172}  若 $\left| {a}_{k}\right|  < \left| {a}_{k + 1}\right|$ ，则 $\left| {a}_{k}\right|  < \left| {a}_{k + 1}\right|  < \left| {a}_{k + 2}\right|$ ，则 ${a}_{k}$ ， ${a}_{k + 1}$ ， ${a}_{k + 2}$ 成等比数列，不可能；

 \ding{173}  若 $\left| {a}_{k}\right|  > \left| {a}_{k + 1}\right|$ ，则 $\left| {a}_{k + 1}\right|  < \left| {a}_{k}\right|  < \left| {a}_{k + 2}\right|$ ，则 ${a}_{k + 1},{a}_{k}$ ， ${a}_{k + 2}$ 成等比数列,所以 ${a}_{k}^{2} = {a}_{k + 1}{a}_{k + 2}$ ,即 ${a}_{k}^{2} = \left( {{a}_{k} + d}\right) \left( {{a}_{k} + }\right.  {2d})$ ,得 ${a}_{k} =  - \dfrac{2}{3}d,{a}_{k + 1} = \dfrac{1}{3}d,{a}_{k + 2} = \dfrac{4}{3}d$ ,而除了这三项外, $\left| {a}_{n}\right|$ 最小值为 $\left| {a}_{k - 1}\right|  = \dfrac{5}{3}d$ 或 $\left| {a}_{k + 3}\right|  = \dfrac{7}{3}d$ ,但 ${a}_{k - 1}$ 和 ${a}_{k + 3}$ 均无法与 ${a}_{k + 1},{a}_{k},{a}_{k + 2}$ 构成等比数列,因此不符合条件. 综上,所有可能的 $m$ 的值是 3 .

15. ( 1 )由于数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的各项均不为 0，所以 ${a}_{n + 2}{a}_{n} =  {a}_{n + 1}{a}_{n} + {a}_{n + 1}^{2}$ 可变形为 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 2}}{{a}_{n + 1}} - \dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} = 1$ ( $n$ 是正整数),所以数列 $\left\{  \dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}}\right\}$ 是首项为 $\dfrac{{a}_{2}}{{a}_{1}} = 1$ ,公差为 1 的等差数列,所以 $\dfrac{{a}_{n + 1}}{{a}_{n}} = 1 + n - 1 = n$ ,又 ${a}_{n} = \dfrac{{a}_{n}}{{a}_{n - 1}} \cdot  \dfrac{{a}_{n - 1}}{{a}_{n - 2}} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  \dfrac{{a}_{3}}{{a}_{2}} \cdot  \dfrac{{a}_{2}}{{a}_{1}} \cdot  {a}_{1} =  \left( {n - 1}\right) !,{a}_{1} = 1$ 也符合上式,所以 ${a}_{n} = \left( {n - 1}\right) !$ ;

(2) \ding{172}  先证: ${g}_{3}\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ . 根据已知 ${g}_{3}\left( x\right)  = 1 - {{e}}^{-x}\left( {1 + \dfrac{x}{1!} + }\right.  \left. {\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2!} + \dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!}}\right) \left( {x \geq  0}\right)$ ,得 ${g}_{3}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}\dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!}$ ,由 $x \geq  0,{g}_{3}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {{e}}^{-x}\dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!} \geq  0$ ,当且仅当 $x = 0$ 时等号成立,于是 ${g}_{3}\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 0$ , $+ \infty )$ 上是严格增函数,故 ${g}_{3}\left( x\right)  \geq  {g}_{3}\left( 0\right)  = 0$ 成立. 再证: ${g}_{3}\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}_{4}\left( x\right)  - {f}_{3}\left( x\right)$ . 又 ${f}_{4}\left( x\right)  - {f}_{3}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{4!}$ ,记 ${h}_{3}\left( x\right)  =  {g}_{3}\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{4!}$ ,则 ${h}_{3}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {g}_{3}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!} = \dfrac{{x}^{3}}{3!}\left( {{{e}}^{-x} - 1}\right)$ ,由 $x \geq  0$ , ${{e}}^{-x} - 1 \leq  0$ ,故 ${h}_{3}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ 且仅当 $x = 0$ 时等号成立,于是 ${h}_{3}\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上是严格减函数,故 ${h}_{3}\left( x\right)  \leq  {h}_{3}\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,于是 $0 \leq  {g}_{3}\left( x\right)  \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{4}}{4!}$ ,证毕.

 \ding{173}  由题意知, ${T}_{n} = 1 + \dfrac{2}{1!} + \dfrac{{2}^{2}}{2!} + \cdots  + \dfrac{{2}^{n - 1}}{\left( {n - 1}\right) !} = {f}_{n - 1}\left( 2\right)$ , 下面研究 $y = {f}_{n}\left( x\right)$ . 将  \ding{172}  推广至一般情形. ${g}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {{e}}^{-x}\left\lbrack  {\left( {1 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{n}\dfrac{1}{k!}{x}^{k}}\right)  - \left( {1 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{{n - 1}}\dfrac{1}{k!}{x}^{k}}\right) }\right\rbrack   = {{e}}^{-x}\dfrac{{x}^{n}}{n!}$ ,由 $x \geq  0,n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * },{g}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}\dfrac{{x}^{n}}{n!} \geq  0$ ,当且仅当 $x = 0$ 时等号成立,于是 ${g}_{n}\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上是严格增函数,故 ${g}_{n}\left( x\right)  \geq  {g}_{n}\left( 0\right)  = 0$ 成立.  \ding{172} ,再证: ${g}_{n}\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}_{n + 1}\left( x\right)  - {f}_{n}\left( x\right) ,{f}_{n + 1}\left( x\right)  -  {f}_{n}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{n + 1}}{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}$ ,记 ${h}_{n}\left( x\right)  = {g}_{n}\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{{x}^{n + 1}}{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}$ ,则 ${h}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {g}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - \dfrac{{x}^{n}}{n!} = \dfrac{{x}^{n}}{n!}\left( {{{e}}^{-x} - 1}\right)$ ,由 $x \geq  0,n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * },{{e}}^{-x} - 1 \leq  0$ ,故 ${h}_{n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ 当且仅当 $x = 0$ 时等号成立,于是 ${h}_{n}\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack 0$ , $+ \infty )$ 上是严格减函数,故 ${h}_{n}\left( x\right)  \leq  {h}_{n}\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,于是 $0 \leq  {g}_{n}\left( x\right)  \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{n + 1}}{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}$ ,所以 $0 \leq  1 - {{e}}^{-x}\left( {1 + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{k = 1}}^{n}\dfrac{1}{k!}{x}^{k}}\right)  \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{n + 1}}{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}$ ,即对任意 $x \geq  0,0 \leq  {g}_{n}\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}_{n + 1}\left( x\right)  - {f}_{n}\left( x\right)$ . 于是对 $n \geq  2,0 \leq  {g}_{n - 1}\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}_{n}\left( x\right)  - {f}_{n - 1}\left( x\right)$ ,整理得 $0 \leq  {{e}}^{x} -  {f}_{n - 1}\left( x\right)  \leq  \dfrac{{x}^{n}}{n!}{{e}}^{x}$ ,令 $x = 2$ ,得 $0 \leq  {{e}}^{x} - {f}_{n - 1}\left( 2\right)  \leq  \dfrac{{2}^{n}}{n!}{{e}}^{2}$ ,即 $0 \leq  {{e}}^{2} - {T}_{n} \leq  \dfrac{{{e}}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{n}}{n!}$ ,故 $\left| {{T}_{n} - {{e}}^{2}}\right|  \leq  \dfrac{{{e}}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{n}}{n!}$ .

方法一:当 $n \geq  6$ 时， $\left( {n - 1}\right) \left( {n - 2}\right)  \geq  5 \times  4 > {16}$ ，故 ${2}^{n} = {2}^{4} \times  {2}^{n - 4} < \left\lbrack  {1 \cdot  \left( {n - 2}\right)  \cdot  \left( {n - 1}\right) }\right\rbrack   \cdot  \left\lbrack  {2 \times  3 \times  \cdots  \times  \left( {n - 3}\right) }\right\rbrack   = (n -$ 1)! 即 ${2}^{n} < \left( {n - 1}\right) !$ ,从而 $\left| {{T}_{n} - {{e}}^{2}}\right|  \leq  \dfrac{{{e}}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{n}}{n \cdot  \left( {n - 1}\right) !} < \dfrac{{{e}}^{2}}{n}$ . 对于任意给定的正实数 $u$ ,令 $\dfrac{{{e}}^{2}}{m} < u$ ,则取 $m$ 为大于 $\dfrac{{{e}}^{2}}{u}$ 且不小于 6 的最小整数,则当 $n \geq  m$ 时, $\left| {{T}_{n} - {{e}}^{2}}\right|  < \dfrac{{{e}}^{2}}{n} \leq  \dfrac{{{e}}^{2}}{m} < u$ 恒成立，因此，数列 $\left\{  {T}_{n}\right\}$ 的极限 $T$ 为 ${{e}}^{2}$ .

方法二: 而对于任意 $u > 0$ ,只需 $n > \dfrac{2{{e}}^{2}}{u}$ 且 $n > 4$ 时,可得 $\dfrac{{{e}}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{n}}{n!}  = {{e}}^{2}\dfrac{\overset{n\text{ 个 }}{\overbrace{2 \cdot  2 \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  2}}}{1 \cdot  2 \cdot  3 \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  n} < \dfrac{2{{e}}^{2}}{\dfrac{2{{e}}^{2}}{u}} = u$ . 故存在 $m = \max \left\{  {\dfrac{2{{e}}^{2}}{u},5}\right\}$ , 当 $n \geq  m$ 时,恒有 $\left| {{T}_{n} - T}\right|  < u$ ,因而 ${T}_{n}$ 的极限 $T = {{e}}^{2}$ .

点睛:本题主要考查数列的通项、求和，另外考查数列和函数的结合以及新定义知识, 难度较大, 本题主要思维方法:

1. 基本方法求通项: 定义法, 累乘法;

2. 不等式的证明,借助构造函数利用导数分析单调性,求最值;

3. 新定义考查,主要是结合导数的最值分析和不等式的放缩思维, 要求高, 难度大.

\section*{第 7 章 平面解析几何}

\section*{考点 1 直线方程与圆的方程}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$

【解析】 $\vv{n} = \left( {1, - 1}\right) ,\vv{d} = \left( {1,1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  k = \tan \alpha  = 1,\alpha  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ .

另解: 设直线 $x - y + 1 = 0$ 的倾斜角为 $\theta ,\theta  \in  \lbrack 0,\pi )$ ,易知直线 $x - y + 1 = 0$ 的斜率为 1,所以 $\tan \theta  = 1$ ,解得 $\theta  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$ .

2. arctan3

【解析】 $k = \tan \alpha  = 3,\alpha  = \arctan 3$ .

3. 0

【解析】已知法向量为 $\left( {1,a}\right)$ 和 $\left( {a,1}\right)$ ,则 $a + a = 0 \Longrightarrow  a = 0$ . 4. $\dfrac{\pi }{3}$

【解析】设直线 $\sqrt{3}x - y + 1 = 0$ 的倾斜角为 $\theta$ ,则 $\tan \theta  = \sqrt{3}$ , 又 $\theta  \in  \lbrack 0,\pi )$ ,所以 $\theta  = \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ,故答案为 $\dfrac{\pi }{3}$ .

点睛: 一般地,如果直线方程的一般式为 ${Ax} + {By} + C = 0  \left( {B \neq  0}\right)$ ,那么直线的斜率为 $k =  - \dfrac{A}{B}$ ,且 $k = \tan \theta$ ,其中 $\theta$ 为直线的倾斜角,注意它的范围是 $\lbrack 0,\pi )$ .

5. $\dfrac{3\pi }{4}$

【解析】 $l : y = {kx} + 2,\left( {1,1}\right)  \in  l \Longrightarrow  1 = k + 2,k =  - 1$ , $\alpha  = \dfrac{3\pi }{4}$

6. $\sqrt{3}$

【解析】将圆 $C$ 的方程化为标准方程可得 ${\left( x + 2\right) }^{2} + (y -  3{)}^{2} = 3$ ,故圆 $C$ 的半径为 $\sqrt{3}$ . 故答案为: $\sqrt{3}$ .

7. 1

【解析】 ${x}^{2} + {2x} + {y}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {x + 1}\right)  + {y}^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  r = 1$ .

8. $\dfrac{\pi }{6}$

【解析】因为直线 $x =  - 2$ 的斜率不存在,倾斜角为 $\dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,直线 $\sqrt{3}x - y + 1 = 0$ 的斜率为 $\sqrt{3}$ ，倾斜角为 $\dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ，故直线 $x =  - 2$ 与直线 $\sqrt{3}x - y + 1 = 0$ 的夹角为 $\dfrac{\pi }{2} - \dfrac{\pi }{3} = \dfrac{\pi }{6}$ ,故答案为: $\dfrac{\pi }{6}$ .

9. ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} = 3$

【解析】由圆的标准方程的定义可得 ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} =$ 3. 故答案为: ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} = 3$ .

10. 1

【解析】因为 ${l}_{1}$ 与 ${l}_{2}$ 平行,所以 $1 \times  2 = m\left( {1 + m}\right)$ ,解得 $m =  - 2$ 或 $m = 1$ . 当 $m = 1$ 时,直线 ${l}_{1} : x + {2y} - 1 = 0$ ,直线

${l}_{2} : x + {2y} + 8 = 0$ ,两直线平行. 当 $m =  - 2$ 时,直线 ${l}_{1} : x -  y - 4 = 0$ ,直线 ${l}_{2} :  - {2x} + {2y} + 8 = 0$ ,化简 ${l}_{2}$ 为 $x - y - 4 = 0$ , 此时两直线重合, 不符合要求, 舍去. 故答案为: 1 .

11. $x - y - 1 = 0$

【解析】圆 ${x}^{2} - {2x} + {y}^{2} = 0$ 的标准方程为 ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ , 圆心坐标为 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,因为直线 $l$ 与直线 $y = x$ 平行,且经过圆 ${x}^{2} - {2x} + {y}^{2} = 0$ 的圆心,所以直线 $l$ 的方程为 $x - y - 1 = 0$ . 故答案为: $x - y - 1 = 0$ .

12. 3

【解析】直线 ${3x} + {4y} - 5 = 0$ 和直线 ${3x} + {4y} + {10} = 0$ 互相平行,故点 $P$ 与点 $Q$ 之间距离的最小值即两条直线间的距离,且两条直线间的距离: $d = \dfrac{\left| -5 - {10}\right| }{\sqrt{{3}^{2} + {4}^{2}}} = 3$ . 故答案为: 3 . 13. ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} = {53}$

【解析】圆心为 $C\left( {3,4}\right)$ ,圆过点 $\left( {1, - 3}\right)$ ,则圆的半径 $r =  \sqrt{{\left( 3 - 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( 4 + 3\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{53}$ ,所以圆的标准方程是 ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} +  {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} = {53}$ . 故答案为: ${\left( x - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 4\right) }^{2} = {53}$ .

14. $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$

【解析】设直线 ${2x} - y - 3 = 0$ 与直线 $x - {3y} - 5 = 0$ 的倾斜角分别为 $\alpha$ 、 $\beta$ ，则 $\tan \alpha  = 2,\tan \beta  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ，且 $\alpha$ 、 $\beta  \in  \lbrack 0,\pi )$ ，所以 $\alpha  > \beta$ ,因为 $\tan \left( {\alpha  - \beta }\right)  = \dfrac{\tan \alpha  - \tan \beta }{1 + \tan \alpha \tan \beta } = \dfrac{2 - \dfrac{1}{3}}{1 + \dfrac{2}{3}} = 1$ ,所以 $\alpha  -  \beta  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ ,即两条直线的夹角为 $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$ ,故答案为: $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$ .

15. -3

【解析】 ${x}^{2} + {\left( y - 2\right) }^{2} = m + 4$ ,由题意 $m + 4 = 1 \Longrightarrow  m =  - 3$ . 16. $\sqrt{3}$

【解析】由直线 ${l}_{2}$ 方程: $y = x\tan {80}^{ \circ  }$ 得 ${l}_{2}$ 的倾斜角为 ${80}^{ \circ  }$ , 所以 ${l}_{1}$ 的倾斜角为 ${60}^{ \circ  }$ ,即 ${l}_{1}$ 的斜率为 $\tan {60}^{ \circ  } = \sqrt{3}$ . 故答案为: $\sqrt{3}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/62_1333_1565_239_213_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

17. $4 - \sqrt{2}$

【解析】 $P\left( {1 + \cos \alpha ,\sin \alpha }\right)  \Longrightarrow  d =  {d}_{1} + {d}_{2} = \left| {1 + \cos \alpha  + 1}\right|  + \left| {2 - \sin \alpha }\right|  \Longrightarrow  d = {d}_{1} + {d}_{2} = 4 - \left( {\sin \alpha  - \cos \alpha }\right)  = 4 -  \sqrt{2}\sin \left( {\alpha  - \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \geq  4 - \sqrt{2}.$

18. $\dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{5}$

【解析】由题可设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},2{x}_{1} - 5}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{2} + \ln {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 $M  \left( {\dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2},\dfrac{2{x}_{1} - 5 + {x}_{2} + \ln {x}_{2}}{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${\left| OM\right| }^{2} = {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( \dfrac{2{x}_{1} - 5 + {x}_{2} + \ln {x}_{2}}{2}\right) }^{2}$ 即 $4{\left| OM\right| }^{2} = {\left\lbrack  {x}_{2} - \left( -{x}_{1}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} + \left\lbrack  \left( {{x}_{2} + }\right. \right.  \left. {\left. \ln {x}_{2}) - \left( -2{x}_{1} + 5\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}\right\rbrack$ ,即 $4{\left| OM\right| }^{2}$ 的最小值为 $Q\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{2} + }\right.  \left. {\ln {x}_{2}}\right)$ 到 $N\left( {-{x}_{1}, - 2{x}_{1} + 5}\right)$ 距离平方的最小值,其中,点 $Q$ 在曲线 $y = x + \ln x$ 上, $N$ 在直线 $y = {2x} + 5$ 上, $\left| {QN}\right|$ 的最

小值为在曲线 $y = x + \ln x$ 上与直线 $y = {2x} + 5$ 平行的切线的切点到直线 $y = {2x} + 5$ 的距离,设切点为 $\left( {{x}_{0},{x}_{0} + }\right.  \ln {x}_{0}$ ),因为曲线 $y = x + \ln x$ 导数 ${y}^{\prime } = 1 + \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,则 $1 + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}} = 2$ , 解得 ${x}_{0} = 1$ ，所以切点为 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ ， 所以 ${\left| QN\right| }_{\min } = \dfrac{\left| 2 - 1 + 5\right| }{\sqrt{{1}^{2} + {2}^{2}}} = \dfrac{6}{\sqrt{5}}  = \dfrac{6\sqrt{5}}{5}$ ,所以 ${\left| OM\right| }_{\min } = \dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{5}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{5}$ .

\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/63_524_306_297_255_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{二、选择题}

19. C

【解析】直线倾斜角的取值范围为 $\lbrack 0,\pi )$ ,故选 C.

20. A

【解析】 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} - 1 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - {x}_{1} - {x}_{2} + 1 +  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left( {{x}_{1} - 1}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - 1}\right)  + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0$ ,则当 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ 时, $\vv{AP} =  \left( {1 - {x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right) ,\vv{BP} = \left( {1 - {x}_{2}, - {y}_{2}}\right) ,\vv{AP} \cdot  \vv{BP} = \left( {1 - {x}_{1}}\right) (1 -  \left. {x}_{2}\right)  + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0$ ,即当 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ 时, ${AP} \bot  {BP}$ 恒成立,则 \ding{172} 是真命题；设 $A\left( {3\cos \theta ,3\sin \theta }\right) ,B\left( {2\sqrt{3}\cos \beta ,2\sqrt{3}\sin \beta }\right)$ ，则 $\vv{OA} =  \left( {3\cos \theta ,3\sin \theta }\right) ,\vv{OB} = \left( {2\sqrt{3}\cos \beta ,2\sqrt{3}\sin \beta }\right)$ ,又 $\cos \angle {AOB} =  \dfrac{\vv{OA} \cdot  \vv{OB}}{\left| \vv{OA}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{OB}\right| } = \dfrac{6\sqrt{3}\cos \theta \cos \beta  + 6\sqrt{3}\sin \theta \sin \beta }{3 \cdot  2\sqrt{3}} = \cos \left( {\theta  - \beta }\right)$ ,则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOB}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {OA}\right| \left| {OB}\right| \sin \angle {AOB} = 3\sqrt{3}\left| {\sin \left( {\theta  - \beta }\right) }\right|$ . 因 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} +  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} - 1$ ,则 $6\sqrt{3}\cos \theta \cos \beta  + 6\sqrt{3}\sin \theta \sin \beta  = 3\cos \theta  +  2\sqrt{3}\cos \beta  - 1$ ,则 $6\sqrt{3}\cos \left( {\theta  - \beta }\right)  = 3\cos \theta  + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \beta  - 1$ ,令 $\theta  -  \beta  = \alpha$ ,则 $6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  = 3\cos \theta  + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \left( {\theta  - \alpha }\right)  - 1$ ,即 $6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  =  3\cos \theta  + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \theta \cos \alpha  + 2\sqrt{3}\sin \theta \sin \alpha  - 1$ ,则 $6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  + 1 =  \left( {3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha }\right) \cos \theta  + 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \sin \theta  = \sqrt{{\left( 3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \right) }^{2}}  \cos \left( {\theta  - \gamma }\right)$ ,其中 $\tan \gamma  = \dfrac{2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha }{3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha },\gamma  \in  \left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\cos (\theta  -  \gamma ) = \dfrac{6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  + 1}{\sqrt{{\left( 3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \right) }^{2}}}$ ,因 $\cos \left( {\theta  - \gamma }\right)  \in  \lbrack  - 1$ , 1],则 $\dfrac{6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  + 1}{\sqrt{{\left( 3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \right) }^{2}}} \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{\left( 6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  + 1\right) }^{2}}{{\left( 3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \right) }^{2}} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {\left( 6\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha  + 1\right) }^{2} \leq  {\left( 3 + 2\sqrt{3}\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\sqrt{3}\sin \alpha \right) }^{2}$ ,则 ${108}{\cos }^{2}\alpha  \leq  {20} \Longrightarrow  {108}\left( {1 - {\sin }^{2}\alpha }\right)  \leq  {20} \Longrightarrow  {\sin }^{2}\alpha  \geq  \dfrac{22}{27} \Longrightarrow  \left| {\sin \alpha }\right|  \geq  \dfrac{\sqrt{22}}{3\sqrt{3}}$ ,则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOB}} = 3\sqrt{3}\left| {\sin \left( {\theta  - \beta }\right) }\right|  = 3\sqrt{3}\left| {\sin \alpha }\right|  \geq  \sqrt{22}$ ,故 \ding{173} 是真命题. 故选: A.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/63_580_1753_240_242_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

点睛: 对于命题 \ding{172} ,关键为注意到 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} - {x}_{1} - {x}_{2} + 1 = \left( {{x}_{1} - }\right.$ 1) $\left( {{x}_{2} - 1}\right)$ ; 对于命题 \ding{173} ，难点在于确定 $\left| {\sin \left( {\theta  - \beta }\right) }\right|$ 的范围， 为此首先将 $\theta  - \beta$ 看作整体,随后将 $\theta  - \beta$ 从相关等式中分离出来,最后利用三角函数的值域确定范围.

\section*{考点 2 直线与圆的位置关系及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\pm  1$

【解析】圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4$ ,圆心为 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,半径为 2; 圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} -  {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 1 = 0$ ，转化为标准形式: ${\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ，即圆心为 $\left( {a,0}\right)$ ,半径为 1; 当两圆内切时,圆心距 $\sqrt{{\left( a - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( 0 - 0\right) }^{2}} =  2 - 1 = 1$ ,解得 $a =  \pm  1$ . 故答案为: $\pm  1$ .

2. $2\sqrt{3}$

【解析】由 $C : {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {4x} - {2y} = 0$ ,即 $C : {\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} + (y -  1{)}^{2} = 5$ ,所以圆心为 $C\left( {2,1}\right)$ ,半径为 $r = \sqrt{5}$ ,所以 $C\left( {2,1}\right)$ 到 $l : y = x + 1$ 的距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| 2 - 1 + 1\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \sqrt{2}$ ,综上直线与圆的相交弦长为 $2\sqrt{{r}^{2} - {d}^{2}} = 2\sqrt{3}$ . 故答案为: $2\sqrt{3}$ .

3. $\left( {0,2\sqrt{3}}\right)$

【解析】 ${C}_{1} : {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {2ax} + {a}^{2} - 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  {\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ , ${G}_{1}\left( {a,0}\right) ,r = 1,{C}_{2} : {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {4y} - 5 = 0 \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + {\left( y - 2\right) }^{2} = 9$ , ${G}_{2}\left( {0,2}\right) ,R = 3$ ,两圆相交,则 $R - r < \left| {{G}_{1}{G}_{2}}\right|  < R + r \Longrightarrow  3 -  1 < \sqrt{{a}^{2} + 4} < 4 \Longrightarrow  a \in  \left( {0,2\sqrt{3}}\right)$ .

4. 1

【解析】圆 ${\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {\left( y + a - 2\right) }^{2} = {r}^{2}\left( {r > 0}\right)$ 的圆心 $(a$ , $2 - a)$ ,半径 $r$ ,圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 的圆心 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,半径为 1,由两圆有公共点,得 $\left| {r - 1}\right|  \leq  \sqrt{{\left( a - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2 - a - 0\right) }^{2}} \leq  r + 1,d =  \sqrt{{\left( a - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2 - a - 0\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{2{\left( a - 1\right) }^{2} + 2} \geq  \sqrt{2}$ ,当且仅当 $a = 1$ 时取等号,当 $a = 1$ 时, $d$ 取得最小值 $\sqrt{2},r$ 取得最小值 $\sqrt{2} - 1$ ，此时两圆外切，满足两圆有公共点，所以当 $r$ 取到最小值时，a 的值为 1 . 故答案为:1 .

5. 1.172

【解析】以点 $O$ 为原点,直线 ${OA}$ 为 $y$ 轴建立平面直角坐标系，连接 ${OE}$ ，令圆 $O$ 的半径为 $r$ ，则 $r - 1 = r\cos {60}^{ \circ  }$ ，解得 $r = 2$ ， 设 $P\left( {2\cos \theta ,2\sin \theta }\right) ,\dfrac{\pi }{6} \leq  \theta  \leq  \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,因此 ${PM} + {PN} = 2 - 2\sin \theta  +  2 - 2\cos \theta  = 4 - 2\sqrt{2}\sin \left( {\theta  + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \geq  4 -  2\sqrt{2}$ ,当且仅当 $\theta  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ 时取等号, $4 -  2\sqrt{2} \approx  {1.172}$ ,所以步行道 ${PM},{PN}$ 长度之和的最小值是 ${1.172}{{km}}$ . 故答案为: 1.172 .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/63_1308_1475_222_230_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

6. 2.73

【解析】以 $A$ 为原点建系, $E\left( {{0.2},{0.2}}\right) ,F\left( {{0.8},{0.8}}\right) ,{l}_{EF} : y =  x$ ,不妨设 ${EF}$ 中点为 $M,M\left( {{0.5},{0.5}}\right)$ ,直线 ${EF}$ 中垂线所在直线方程为 $l : y =  - x + 1$ ，因为圆 $O$ 与 ${AD}$ 只有一个公共点，所以圆 $O$ 与 ${AD}$ 相切，设圆 $O$ 半径为 $r$ ,则圆心 $O\left( {r, - r + 1}\right)$ ,因

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/63_1237_1908_293_258_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

为 $\left| {OE}\right|  = r$ ,所以 $\sqrt{{\left( r - {0.2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( -r + 1 - {0.2}\right) }^{2}} =  r$ ,即 ${r}^{2} - {2r} + {0.68} = 0$ ,解得 $r =  1 \pm  \dfrac{2}{5}\sqrt{2}$ ,结合题意可知, $r = 1 -  \dfrac{2}{5}\sqrt{2} \approx  {0.434},C = {2\pi r} \approx  {2.73}.$

7. 6

【解析】设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\left| {OA}\right|  = \left| {OB}\right|  = 1,{S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOB}}  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{x}_{1}{y}_{2} - {x}_{2}{y}_{1}}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,由 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOB}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {OA}\right| \left| {OB}\right| \sin \angle {AOB} =$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/64_592_304_278_271_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

$\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,得 $\angle {AOB} = \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,直线 $l : x +  y - 2 = 0$ ,取 ${AB}$ 中点 $G$ ,

$\left| {{x}_{1} + {y}_{1} - 2}\right|  + \left| {{x}_{2} + {y}_{2} - 2}\right|  =  \sqrt{2}\left( {\left| {AE}\right|  + \left| {BD}\right| }\right)  = 2\sqrt{2}\left| {GH}\right|  \leq  2\sqrt{2}\left( {\left| {OG}\right|  + \left| {OI}\right| }\right)  =  2\sqrt{2}\left( {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{2}}}\right)  = 6.$ 8. $\dfrac{5}{2} - {d}^{2}$

【解析】由曲线方程 ${x}^{2} - 2\left| x\right| y + {y}^{2} - \sqrt{2}\left| x\right|  + 2\sqrt{2}y = 0$ , 由点 $M\left( {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}, - \sqrt{2}}\right)$ 到 “爱心线” 上任意一点且点 $M$ 在 $y$ 轴的右侧,所以点 $M$ 到 “爱心线” 上任意一点的最小距离 $d$ ,一定出现在爱心线位于 $y$ 轴的右侧的点,当 $x \geq  0$ 时,可得 ${x}^{2} +  {y}^{2} - \sqrt{2}x + 2\sqrt{2}y = {2xy}$ ,设曲线上任意一点 $P\left( {x,y}\right) (x \geq  0)$ ,且 $M\left( {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}, - \sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,有 ${\left| PM\right| }^{2} = {\left( x - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - \sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} =  {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - \sqrt{2}x + 2\sqrt{2}y + \dfrac{5}{2} = {2xy} + \dfrac{5}{2}$ ,因为 ${\left| PM\right| }^{2}$ 的最小值为 ${d}^{2}$ ,所以 ${2xy}$ 的最小值为 ${d}^{2} - \dfrac{5}{2}$ ,当 $y > 0$ 时,“心吧”面积为 $S = {2x}\left| y\right|  = {2xy}$ 的最小值为 ${d}^{2} - \dfrac{5}{2}$ ; 当 $y < 0$ 时,“心吧”面积为 $S = {2x}\left| y\right|  =  - {2xy}$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{5}{2} - {d}^{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{5}{2} - {d}^{2}$ . 9. $\dfrac{\sqrt{6} - \sqrt{2}}{4}$

【解析】点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 在单位圆周上, $\vv{OA} \cdot  \vv{OB} =  \left| \vv{OA}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{OB}\right|  \cdot  \cos \angle {AOB} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $\angle {AOB} = \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ,求点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 到直线 $x + y - 1 = 0$ 的距离之和最小. 由题意点 $A,B$ 在直线 $x + y - 1 = 0$ 同侧时 ${AE} + {BF} =  {2PC}$ ,当点 $A,B$ 在直线 $x + y -  1 = 0$ 异侧时,所求值为 $\sin \theta$ ,其中 $\theta$ 为直线 ${AB}$ 与直线 $x + y -  1 = 0$ 的夹角. 根据图像可知最小值为 $\sin {15}^{ \circ  } = \dfrac{\sqrt{6} - \sqrt{2}}{4}$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

10. C

【解析】设动圆 $M$ 的圆心 $M$ 坐标为 $\left( {m,m}\right)$ ,即圆 $M$ 半径 $r =  m$ ,由题意 $m > 0$ ,设 $\left| {OA}\right|  = a,\left| {OB}\right|  = b$ ,圆 $M$ 与直线 ${AB}$ 相切于点 $N$ ,则 $\left| {AN}\right|  = m - a,\left| {BN}\right|  = m - b$ ,所以 $\left| {OA}\right|  +  \left| {OB}\right|  + \left| {AB}\right|  = \left| {OA}\right|  + \left| {OB}\right|  + \left| {AN}\right|  + \left| {BN}\right|  = a + b +  m - a + m - b = {2m}$ ,即 $\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 的周长为 ${2m}$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 的周长最小即为圆 $M$ 半径 $m$ 最小,因为 $\left| {PM}\right|  \geq  r = m$ ,则 $\sqrt{{\left( m - 2\right) }^{2} + {\left( m - 1\right) }^{2}} \geq  m$ ,整理得 ${m}^{2} - {6m} + 5 \geq  0$ ,解得 $m \geq  5$ 或 $m \leq  1$ , 当 $m \leq  1$ 时,圆心 $M$ 在 $\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 内,不合题意; 当 $m \geq  5$ 时,符合题意,即圆 $M$ 半径的最小值为 $5,\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 周长的最小值为 ${2m} = {10}$ . 故选: C.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/64_1362_351_214_214_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

11. C

【解析】点集 $\{ \left( {x,y}\right) \left| \right| x - a\left| { \leq  1,}\right| y - b \mid   \leq  1\}$ 表示一个以 $P\left( {a,b}\right)$ 为中心，边长为 2 的正方形及其内部的点构成的区域，曲线 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {8x} - {4y} + {16} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {\left( x - 4\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 2\right) }^{2} =$ 4，一个以 $M\left( {4,2}\right)$ 为圆心，2 为半径的圆，当 $P$ 在圆 $M$ 上运动时,正方形随之运动,其中离原点 $O$ 最近的点 ${Q}_{P}$ ,分为两种情况:

(1)当正方形位于 $x$ 轴上方时， ${Q}_{P}$ 为正方形左下角顶点，取 $N\left( {3,1}\right)$ ,易知 ${PQ}//{MN},{PQ} = {MN},{QN} = {PM} = 2$ ,所以此时 ${Q}_{P}$ 的轨迹是以 $N\left( {3,1}\right)$ 为圆心,以 2 为半径的圆的一段圆弧,如图 1 ;

(2)当正方形与 $x$ 轴有公共点时, ${Q}_{P}$ 为正方形与 $x$ 轴的交点. (离原点近的一个),此时 ${Q}_{P}$ 的轨迹是一个线段,如图 2. 故点 ${Q}_{P}$ 的轨迹是一段圆弧及一个线段, \ding{172}  错误;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/64_939_1114_281_212_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

图 1

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/64_1264_1114_288_210_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

图 2

如图3所示，当点 $O$ 、 $N$ 、 $Q$ 三点共线且 $Q$ 在中间时, $f\left( P\right)$ 取最小值为 $\sqrt{10} - 2$ ,当点 $O,N,Q$ 三点共线且 $N$ 在中间时, $f\left( P\right)$ 取最大值为 $\sqrt{10} + 2$ ，故 \ding{173} 正确: 故选 C.

\section*{考点 3 椭圆方程及其性质}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 2

【解析】因为椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,所以 $a = 2,b = \sqrt{3} \Longrightarrow  c =  \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = 1$ ,故焦距为 2 .

2. $\dfrac{1}{2}$

【解析】在椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 中， $a = 2$ ， $b = \sqrt{3}$ ， $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = 1$ ， 因此,椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 的离心率是 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{2}$ . 3. $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{25} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{16} = 1$

【解析】依题意, $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = {10} > 6 = \left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|$ ,则点 $P$ 的轨迹是以 ${F}_{1}$ 、 ${F}_{2}$ 为左右焦点,长轴长 ${2a} = {10}$ 的椭圆,由 ${2c} = 6$ ,得 $b = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {c}^{2}} = \sqrt{{5}^{2} - {3}^{2}} = 4$ ,所以动点 $P$ 的轨迹方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{25} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{16} = 1$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{25} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{16} = 1$ .

4. 2

【解析】由题意得椭圆离心率为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - 1}}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ,解得 $a = 2$ , 故答案为: 2 .

5. $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$

【解析】椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + {y}^{2} = 1\left( {a > 1}\right)$ 长轴长为 4,即 ${2a} = 4,a =$ 2， $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - 1} = \sqrt{3}$ ，故 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ .

6. 2

【解析】椭圆 $C : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {a > b > 0}\right)$ 的左、右焦点分别为 ${F}_{1},{F}_{2}$ ,左顶点为 $A$ ,若椭圆 $C$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{1}{3}$ ,则 $\dfrac{\left| {F}_{2}A\right| }{\left| A{F}_{1}\right| } =  \dfrac{a + c}{a - c} = \dfrac{1 + e}{1 - e} = \dfrac{1 + \dfrac{1}{3}}{1 - \dfrac{1}{3}} = 2$ . 故答案为: 2 .

7. $\left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$

【解析】圆 $N$ 的圆心为 $N\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,半径为 2 . 由极化恒等式,得 $\vv{PE} \cdot  \vv{PF} = {\left| \vv{NP}\right| }^{2} - 4$ ,椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 的 $a = 2,b = \sqrt{3},c =  1,N\left( {1,0}\right)$ 为椭圆的右焦点,设 $P\left( {x}_{0}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{0}\right) ,{x}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ ,则由焦半径公式,知 $\left| {NP}\right|  = a - \dfrac{c}{a}{x}_{0} = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{0} \in  \lbrack 2 - 1,  2 + 1\rbrack  = \left\lbrack  {1,3}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  \vv{PE} \cdot  \vv{PF} \in  \left\lbrack  {-3,5}\right\rbrack$ . 8. $\dfrac{\pi }{6}$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/65_607_1196_212_185_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

【解析】如图,设 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = x$ ,则 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  =  {2a} - x$ ,因 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{3} - 1$ ,故 $c = (\sqrt{3} -$ 1) $a$ ,由余弦定理, ${\left| P{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} = {\left| P{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} +  {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} - 2\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right| \cos \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ,即 ${\left( 2a - x\right) }^{2} = 4{c}^{2} + {x}^{2} -  {2cx}$ ,将 $c = \left( {\sqrt{3} - 1}\right) a$ 代入,整理得: $4{a}^{2} - {4ax} + {x}^{2} = 4(4 -  2\sqrt{3}){a}^{2} + {x}^{2} - 2\left( {\sqrt{3} - 1}\right) {ax}$ ,解得 $x = \dfrac{4\sqrt{3} - 6}{3 - \sqrt{3}}a = \left( {\sqrt{3} - 1}\right) a$ , 则有 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left( {\sqrt{3} - 1}\right) a,\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = \left( {3 - \sqrt{3}}\right) a$ ,由正弦定理: $\dfrac{\left| P{F}_{2}\right| }{\sin \angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} = \dfrac{\left| P{F}_{1}\right| }{\sin \dfrac{\pi }{3}}$ ,即 $\dfrac{\left( {\sqrt{3} - 1}\right) a}{\sin \angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} = \dfrac{\left( {3 - \sqrt{3}}\right) a}{\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}}$ ,解得 $\sin \angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,因 $0 < \angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} < \dfrac{2\pi }{3}$ ,故 $\angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} = \dfrac{\pi }{6}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{\pi }{6}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/65_607_1433_212_165_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

9. 0.31

【解析】设变轨前的椭圆的长半轴长为 ${a}_{1}$ ,短半轴长为

${b}_{1}$ ,半焦距为 ${c}_{1}$ ,离心率为 ${e}_{1}$ ,变轨后的椭圆的长半轴长为 ${a}_{2}$ ,短半轴长为 ${b}_{2}$ ,半焦距为 ${c}_{2}$ ,离心率为 ${e}_{2}$ ,由题意可得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {a}_{1} - {c}_{1} = {a}_{2} - {c}_{2} \\  4\left( {{a}_{1} + {c}_{1}}\right)  = {a}_{2} + {c}_{2} \\  {2.5}\dfrac{{c}_{1}}{{a}_{1}} = \dfrac{{c}_{2}}{{a}_{2}} \end{array}\text{ ,化简得 }\dfrac{5}{2} \cdot  \dfrac{{c}_{1}}{{a}_{1}} = \dfrac{3{a}_{1} + 5{c}_{1}}{5{a}_{1} + 3{c}_{1}}\text{ ,即 }\dfrac{5}{2}{e}_{1} = }\right.  \dfrac{3 + 5{e}_{1}}{5 + 3{e}_{1}}$ ,解得 ${e}_{1} = \dfrac{-5 + \sqrt{65}}{10} \approx  {0.31}$ (负值舍去). 故答案为: 0.31 .

\section*{二、选择题}

10. C

【解析】由题知,设椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {a > b > 0}\right)$ ,则将 $\left( {0,b}\right)$ 代入圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4$ ，可得 ${b}^{2} = 4$ ，将椭圆焦点 $\left( {c,0}\right)$ 代入圆,可得 ${c}^{2} = 4$ ,所以椭圆中 ${a}^{2} = {b}^{2} + {c}^{2} = 8$ ,所以椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1$ , A 错; 过椭圆在 $y$ 轴上的顶点 $\left( {0,2}\right)$ ,作圆 $O$ 的切线,明显一条, B 错; 圆 $O$ 上的点与椭圆 $\Gamma$ 上的点的距离的最大值是 $a + c = 2\sqrt{2} + 2,{C}$ 对; 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1 \\  x + y + 2\sqrt{2} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $3{x}^{2} +  8\sqrt{2}x + 8 = 0$ ,由 ${\left( 8\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  3 \times  8 = {32} > 0$ ,所以直线 $x +  y + 2\sqrt{2} = 0$ 与椭圆 $\Gamma$ 有交点,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4 \\  x + y + 2\sqrt{2} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ 得 ${x}^{2} +  2\sqrt{2}x + 2 = 0,{\left( 2\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  1 \times  2 = 0$ ,所以直线 $x + y + 2\sqrt{2} =$ 0 与圆 $O$ 相切，D 错. 故选:C.

11. B

【解析】方法一:椭圆是封闭图形,将椭圆采用仿射变换化为圆之后,在椭圆上任意一点 $A$ ,可得到圆上的对应点 ${A}^{\prime }$ , 必存在圆上的点 ${B}^{\prime },{C}^{\prime }$ ,使 $\bigtriangleup {A}^{\prime }{B}^{\prime }{C}^{\prime }$ 为正三角形,则 $O$ 为 $\bigtriangleup {A}^{\prime }{B}^{\prime }{C}^{\prime }$ 的重心,而圆上的 $\bigtriangleup {A}^{\prime }{B}^{\prime }{C}^{\prime }$ 对应于椭圆上的 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ ,其重心也是对称中心 $O$ ,得椭圆都是 “自稳定曲线”, 故 \ding{172} 是真命题； 方法二: 设椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1(a >  b > 0),A\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 是 $\Gamma$ 上任一点, $B\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 是 $\Gamma$ 上两点,则 ${b}^{2}{x}_{0}^{2} + {a}^{2}{y}_{0}^{2} = {a}^{2}{b}^{2}$ ,由 $O$ 是 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心,知 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - {x}_{0} \\  {y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - {y}_{0} \end{array}\right.$ ,得直线 ${BC}$ 过点 $M\left( {-\dfrac{{x}_{0}}{2}, - \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{2}}\right)$ ,  \ding{172}  当 ${x}_{0}{y}_{0} = 0$ 时，(i) 当 ${y}_{0} = 0$ 时，若 $A\left( {a,0}\right)$ ，则 ${y}_{2} =  - {y}_{1} \Longrightarrow  {x}_{2} = {x}_{1} =  - \dfrac{a}{2}$ ,符合题意; 若 $A\left( {-a,0}\right)$ ,则 ${y}_{2} =  - {y}_{1} \Longrightarrow  {x}_{2} = {x}_{1} = \dfrac{a}{2}$ ,符合题意; (ii) 当 ${x}_{0} = 0$ ,即 $A\left( {0, \pm  b}\right)$ 时,同理存在两点 $B,C$ ,使得 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心为原点 $O$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/65_1316_1405_211_163_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

 \ding{173}  当 ${x}_{0}{y}_{0} \neq  0$ 时，因为 ${k}_{BC}{k}_{OM} =  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{{a}^{2}}$ ，所以 ${k}_{BC} =  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}}  \Longrightarrow$ 直线 ${BC}$ 方程为 $y + \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{2} =  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}}\left( {x + \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{2}}\right)$ ,即 $y =  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}}x  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}^{2} + {a}^{2}{y}_{0}^{2}}{2{a}^{2}{y}_{0}} =  - \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}}x - \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2{y}_{0}}$ ,代入 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1(a > b >  0)$ ,得 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + {b}^{2}{\left( \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}}x + \dfrac{1}{2{y}_{0}}\right) }^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}^{2}}{{a}^{4}{y}_{0}^{2}}{x}^{2} + \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}^{2}}x +  \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{4{y}_{0}^{2}} - 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}^{2}}{{a}^{2}{y}_{0}^{2}}{x}^{2} + \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{y}_{0}^{2}}x + \dfrac{{a}^{2}{b}^{2}}{4{y}_{0}^{2}} - {a}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{{y}_{0}^{2}}{x}^{2} +  \dfrac{{b}^{2}{x}_{0}}{{y}_{0}^{2}}x + \dfrac{{a}^{2}{b}^{2}}{4{y}_{0}^{2}} - {a}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + {x}_{0}x + \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}{y}_{0}^{2} = 0$ ,因为 $\dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - 1  =  - \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}$ ,所以 $\Delta  = {x}_{0}^{2} - {a}^{2} + \dfrac{4{a}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}{y}_{0}^{2} =  - \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}{y}_{0}^{2} + \dfrac{4{a}_{2}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}{y}_{0}^{2} = \dfrac{3{a}^{2}}{{b}^{2}}  {y}_{0}^{2} > 0$ ,即直线 ${BC}$ 与椭圆交于两点,且 $O$ 是 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心. 综上,在椭圆上任意点 $A$ ,在椭圆上都存在两点 $B,C$ ,使得 $O$ 为 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心,故 \ding{172} 为真命题; (共同) 双曲线不是封闭图形,且对称中心在曲线两侧,如双曲线 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1(a$ , $b > 0)$ ,取点 $A\left( {a,0}\right)$ ,假设 $O$ 为 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心,则 $B\left( {x}_{1}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{1}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 必在左支上 $\Longrightarrow  \dfrac{0 + {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{3} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {y}_{2} =  - {y}_{1} \Longrightarrow  B,C$ 关于 $x$ 轴对称 $\Longrightarrow  {x}_{2} = {x}_{1} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{a + 2{x}_{1}}{3} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{1} =  - \dfrac{a}{2}$ ,与 ${x}_{1} \leq   - a$ 矛盾，得双曲线不是“自稳定曲线”，故 \ding{173} 是假命题. 综上,选 B.

点睛:涉及直线被圆锥曲线所截弦中点及直线斜率问题，可以利用“点差法”, 设出弦的两个端点坐标, 代入曲线方程作差求解,还要注意验证.

\section*{三、解答题}

12.(1)如图，已知当 ${x}_{A} = 2$ 时，点 $A\left( {2,{y}_{A}}\right)$ 为椭圆 ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1(0 <  b < 2\sqrt{2}$ ) 与双曲线 ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} - {y}^{2} = 1$ 在第一象限的公共点. 将 $x = 2$ 代入椭圆方程得 $\dfrac{{2}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,即 $\dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,解得 ${y}^{2} = \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2}$ . 把 $x = 2,{y}^{2} = \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2}$ 代入双曲线方程 $\dfrac{{2}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} - \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ,整理得 $\dfrac{4}{{b}^{2}} - \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ,设 $t = {b}^{2}\left( {t > 0}\right)$ ,则 $\dfrac{4}{t} - \dfrac{t}{2}  = 1$ ,即 $8 - {t}^{2} = {2t},{t}^{2} + {2t} - 8 = 0,\left( {t + 4}\right) \left( {t - 2}\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $t =$ 2 或 $t =  - 4$ (舍去),所以 ${b}^{2} = 2$ ,即 $b = \sqrt{2}$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/66_642_1003_226_178_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(2)当 $b = \sqrt{2}$ 时，椭圆 ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ，双曲线 ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} -  {y}^{2} = 1$ . 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,由 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ 得 ${x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 8$ ,由 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ 得 ${x}^{2} - 2{y}^{2} = 2$ ,两式相减消去 ${x}^{2}$ 得 $6{y}^{2} = 6$ , ${y}^{2} = 1$ ,代入双曲线方程得 ${x}^{2} = 4$ ,因为点 $A$ 在第一象限,所以 $A\left( {2,1}\right)$ . 设 $E\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,F\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) .\vv{AD} = \left( {0, - 1}\right) ,\vv{EF} =  \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1},{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}\right)$ ,由 $\vv{AD} \cdot  \vv{EF} = 2$ 得 $- \left( {{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}\right)  = 2$ ,即 ${y}_{1} - {y}_{2} = 2$ ,故 ${y}_{1} > {y}_{2}$ . 当直线 $l$ 斜率不存在时,直线 $l$ 方程为 $x = 2$ ,此时 $\vv{AD} = \left( {0, - 1}\right)$ ,则 $E\left( {2,1}\right) ,F\left( {2, - 1}\right) ,\vv{EF} =  \left( {0, - 2}\right) ,\vv{AD} \cdot  \vv{EF} = 2$ 成立. 当直线 $l$ 斜率存在时,由题知交点 $E$ 、 $F$ 必定在直线 $x = 2$ 两侧,即左侧为 $l$ 与椭圆的交点,右侧为直 $l$ 与双曲线的交点,易知当交点在位于第一象限椭圆上曲线段 ${QA},Q\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 之间时, ${y}_{1} > {y}_{A} = 1$ ,此时 ${y}_{2} <  - 1$ ,故 ${y}_{1} - {y}_{2} = 2$ 不可能,舍去; 因为双曲线 ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ 的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}x$ ,故直线 ${QD} : y =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}x + \sqrt{2}$ 与双曲线 ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ 没有横坐标大于 2 的交点，即当交点位于椭圆第二象限时，不可能， 舍去; 同理: 当直线 $l$ 与椭圆交于 $x$ 轴下方时,也舍去. 综上: 直线 $l$ 方程为 $x = 2$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/66_641_2017_229_174_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)如图，已知 $A\left( {2,1}\right)$ ，由(1)知椭圆 ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ,双曲线 ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} -  {y}^{2} = 1$ . 直线 $m$ 过点 $P\left( {0,1}\right)$ 且斜率为 $k\left( {k \geq  1}\right)$ ,则直线 $m$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} +$ 1. 设 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AMN}} = \lambda \tan \angle {MAN}$ ,根据三角形面积公式 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AMN}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| \vv{AM}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{AN}\right| \sin \angle {MAN}$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{2}\left| \vv{AM}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{AN}\right| \sin \angle {MAN} = \lambda \dfrac{\sin \angle {MAN}}{\cos \angle {MAN}}$ ,即 $\lambda  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| \vv{AM}\right|$ . $\left| \vv{AN}\right| \cos \angle {MAN} = \dfrac{1}{2}\vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{AN} \cdot  \vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{AN} = \left( {{x}_{1} - 2,{y}_{1} - 1}\right)$ . $\left( {{x}_{2} - 2,{y}_{2} - 1}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{1} - 2}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - 2}\right)  + \left( {{y}_{1} - 1}\right) \left( {{y}_{2} - 1}\right)  =  \left( {{x}_{1} - 2}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - 2}\right)  + {k}^{2}{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 2\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 4$ , 因为双曲线的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}x$ ,而 $k \geq  1$ ,故直线 $y = {kx} + 1$ 与双曲线右支无交点,故 $M,N$ 均为直线 $y = {kx} + 1$ 与椭圆的交点，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1\left( {x \leq  2}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ，则 $\left( {4{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} +  {8kx} - 4 = 0$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8k}{4{k}^{2} + 1},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{4}{4{k}^{2} + 1},\vv{AM}$ . $\vv{AN} = \dfrac{-4\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{4{k}^{2} + 1} + \dfrac{16k}{4{k}^{2} + 1} + 4 = \dfrac{-4{k}^{2} - 4 + {16k} + {16}{k}^{2} + 4}{4{k}^{2} + 1}  = \dfrac{{12}{k}^{2} + {16k}}{4{k}^{2} + 1} = 3 + \dfrac{{16k} - 3}{4{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,令 $t = {16k} - 3 \geq  {13},k = \dfrac{t + 3}{16}$ ,则 $\vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{AN} = 3 + \dfrac{t}{4{\left( \dfrac{t + 3}{16}\right) }^{2} + 1} = 3 + \dfrac{64t}{{t}^{2} + {6t} + {73}} = 3 + \dfrac{64}{t + \dfrac{73}{t} + 6}$ , 因为 ${\left( t + \dfrac{73}{t}\right) }^{\prime } = 1 - \dfrac{73}{{t}^{2}} > 0$ 在 $t \geq  {13}$ 时成立,故 $t + \dfrac{73}{t}$ 严格增且 $t + \dfrac{73}{t} > 0$ ,所以 $3 + \dfrac{64}{t + \dfrac{73}{t} + 6}$ 严格减,所以当 $t = {13}$ , $k = 1$ 时 $\vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{AN}$ 取得最大值 $\dfrac{28}{5}$ ,又 $\lambda  = \dfrac{1}{2}\vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{AN}$ ,所以 $\lambda$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{14}{5}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/66_1350_461_226_177_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

13. ( 1 )由题意，得 $a = 2,c = \sqrt{4 - 1} = \sqrt{3} \Longrightarrow  e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ；

(2)由题意，得 $T\left( {1,0}\right)$ 、 $Q\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，设直线 ${PQ}$ 方程为: $y =  {kx} + 1$ ,则 ${d}_{T \rightarrow  {PQ}} = \dfrac{\left| k + 1\right| }{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{5}} \Longrightarrow  5{k}^{2} + {10k} + 5 = {k}^{2} + 1 \Longrightarrow  4{k}^{2} + {10k} + 4 = 0 \Longrightarrow  k =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 或 $k =  - 2$ ,

 \ding{172}  联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1 \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ，消去 $y$ ，得 $2{x}^{2} - {4x} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 0 \\  y = 1 \end{array}\right.$ (舍) 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 2 \\  y = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $P\left( {2,0}\right)$ ;  \ding{173}  联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - {2x} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,消去 $y$ ,得 ${17}{x}^{2} -  {16x} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 0 \\  y = 1 \end{array}\right.$ (舍) 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = \dfrac{16}{17} \\  y =  - \dfrac{15}{17} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ (舍); 综上, $P\left( {2,0}\right)$ ;

(3)不妨设 $P$ 在第一象限，法一:如图,设 $\vv{TP} = \left( {m,n}\right) ,m,n \geq  0$ , \ding{172}  若 $t \geq  0$ ,由题意,得 $\vv{TQ} = \left( {-n,m}\right)  \Longrightarrow  P(t  + m,n),Q\left( {-\left( {n - t}\right) ,m}\right)  = \left( {t - n,m}\right) ,$ 分别代入 $\Gamma  : {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ ，得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\left( t + m\right) }^{2} + 4{n}^{2} = 4 \\  {\left( t - n\right) }^{2} + 4{m}^{2} = 4 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \left( {{2t} + m - n}\right) \left( {m + n}\right)  + 4\left( {n + m}\right) \left( {n - m}\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  {2t} + m - n +  {4n} - {4m} = 0 \Longrightarrow  m = \dfrac{{2t} + {3n}}{3} \Longrightarrow  {\left( t + \dfrac{{2t} + {3n}}{3}\right) }^{2} = 4\left( {1 - {n}^{2}}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {\left( 5t + 3n\right) }^{2} = {36}\left( {1 - {n}^{2}}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {5t} + {3n} =  \pm  6\sqrt{1 - {n}^{2}}$ (负舍) $\Longrightarrow  {5t} = 6\sqrt{1 - {n}^{2}} - {3n},n \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {5t} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,6}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  t \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{6}{5}}\right\rbrack$ ;  \ding{173}  若 $t < 0$ ，同理可得 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{6}{5},0}\right)$ ；综上，存在 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{6}{5}}\right.$ ， $\left. \dfrac{6}{5}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $\bigtriangleup {TPQ}$ 是以点 $T$ 为直角顶点的等腰直角三角形.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/67_584_377_235_147_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

法二: 设 $\angle {PTx} = \alpha ,\alpha  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ , $\left| {TP}\right|  = \left| {TQ}\right|  = r$ ,如图,则 $P(t +  r\cos \alpha ,r\sin \alpha ),Q\left( {t + r\cos \left( {\alpha  + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right) ,}\right.  \left. {r\sin \left( {\alpha  + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right) }\right)  = \left( {t - r\sin \alpha ,r\cos \alpha }\right)$ ,分别代入 $\Gamma  : {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2}  = 4\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ ,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\left( t + r\cos \alpha \right) }^{2} + 4{r}^{2}{\sin }^{2}\alpha  = 4\text{  \ding{172} , } \\  {\left( t - r\sin \alpha \right) }^{2} + 4{r}^{2}{\cos }^{2}\alpha  = 4\text{  \ding{173} , } \end{array}\right.$  \ding{172} -  \ding{173} ,得 ${2rt}\left( {\cos \alpha  + \sin \alpha }\right)  + {r}^{2}\left( {{\cos }^{2}\alpha  - {\sin }^{2}\alpha }\right)  + 4{r}^{2}\left( {{\sin }^{2}\alpha  - {\cos }^{2}\alpha }\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  {2t} + r\left( {\cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha }\right)  + {4r}\left( {\sin \alpha  - \cos \alpha }\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  3\left( {\cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha }\right) r =  {2t}$ ,(i) 当 $\alpha  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ 时, $t = 0$ ; (ii) 当 $\alpha  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ 时, $r = \dfrac{2t}{3\left( {\cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha }\right) }$  \ding{174} , \ding{172} + \ding{173} ,得 $2{t}^{2} + {2tr}\left( {\cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha }\right)  +  {r}^{2} + 4{r}^{2} = 8$ ,将 \ding{174} 代入，得 $2{t}^{2} + {2t} \cdot  \dfrac{2t}{3} + 5 \cdot  \dfrac{4{t}^{2}}{9{\left( \cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha \right) }^{2}} =$ 8,令 $u = \cos \alpha  - \sin \alpha  =  - \sqrt{2}\sin \left( {\alpha  - \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ ,因为 $\alpha  - \dfrac{\pi }{4} \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right.$ , 0) $\cup  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 $\sin \left( {\alpha  - \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  u \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,0)\cup (0,1}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {u}^{2} \in  (0,1\rbrack  \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{10}{t}^{2}}{3} + \dfrac{{20}{t}^{2}}{9{u}^{2}} = 8 \Longrightarrow  {30}{u}^{2}{t}^{2} +  {20}{t}^{2} = {72}{u}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {t}^{2} = \dfrac{{72}{u}^{2}}{{30}{u}^{2} + {20}} = \dfrac{72}{{30} + \dfrac{20}{{u}^{2}}} \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{72}{50}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  {t}^{2} \leq  \dfrac{36}{25} \Longrightarrow  - \dfrac{6}{5} \leq  t \leq  \dfrac{6}{5}$ ,且 $t \neq  0$ ; 综上,存在 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{6}{5},\dfrac{6}{5}}\right\rbrack$ ,使得 $\bigtriangleup {TPQ}$ 是以点 $T$ 为直角顶点的等腰直角三角形.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/67_576_982_243_158_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

14. ( 1 )椭圆的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ，故长半轴 $a = 3$ ，短半轴 $b = 1$ ，焦距 $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = 2\sqrt{2}$ ，右焦点 $F$ 的坐标为 $\left( {2\sqrt{2},0}\right)$ . 椭圆上任意一点到右焦点的距离的最小值为右顶点到右焦点的距离: ${\left| MF\right| }_{\min } = \left| {3 - 2\sqrt{2}}\right|  = 3 - 2\sqrt{2}$ ;

(2)直线 ${l}_{1}$ 的方程为 $y = x - 3$ ，代入椭圆方程: $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + {(x - }  3{)}^{2} = 1$ 得 $\dfrac{10}{9}{x}^{2} - {6x} + 8 = 0$ . 解得 $x = 3$ 或 $x = \dfrac{12}{5}$ ,分别对应点 $P\left( {3,0}\right)$ 和点 $Q\left( {\dfrac{12}{5}, - \dfrac{3}{5}}\right)$ .

则 ${PQ} = \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{12}{5} - 3\right) }^{2} + {\left( -\dfrac{3}{5} + 0\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{9}{25} + \dfrac{9}{25}} = \dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{5}$ .

方法一: 设直线 $y = x + b$ 与椭圆相切,将其代入椭圆方程 ${10}{x}^{2} + {18bx} + 9{b}^{2} - 9 = 0$ ,此时, $\Delta  = {\left( {18}b\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  {10} \times  \left( {9{b}^{2} - }\right.  9) =  - {36}{b}^{2} + {360} = 0$ ,得 $b =  \pm  \sqrt{10}$ ,取距离 ${PQ}$ 更远的切线 $y = x + \sqrt{10}$ ,其与 ${PQ}$ 的距离为: $\dfrac{\sqrt{10} + 3}{\sqrt{2}}$ ,将 $y = x +  \sqrt{10}$ 代入椭圆方程，解得切点为 $M\left( {-\dfrac{9\sqrt{10}}{10},\dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}}\right)$ . 面积最大值 ${S}_{\max } = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{5} \times  \dfrac{\sqrt{10} + 3}{\sqrt{2}} = \dfrac{3\sqrt{10} + 9}{10}$ . 所以面积最大值为 $\dfrac{3\sqrt{10} + 9}{10}$ ,点 $M$ 坐标为 $\left( {-\dfrac{9\sqrt{10}}{10},\dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}}\right)$ . 方法二: 设点 $M$ 的坐标为 $\left( {3\cos \theta ,\sin \theta }\right)$ . 点 $M$ 到直线 ${l}_{1}$ 的距离为: $d = \dfrac{\left| 3\cos \theta  - \sin \theta  - 3\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{10}\left( \dfrac{3\sqrt{10}}{10}\cos \theta  - \dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}\sin \theta \right)  - 3\right| }{\sqrt{2}} =  \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{10}\sin \left( \varphi  - \theta \right)  - 3\right| }{\sqrt{2}}$ . 当 $\sin \left( {\varphi  - \theta }\right)  =  - 1$ 时, $d$ 取到最大值 $\dfrac{\sqrt{10} + 3}{\sqrt{2}}$ ,可得面积最大值 ${S}_{\max } = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{5} \times  \dfrac{\sqrt{10} + 3}{\sqrt{2}} =  \dfrac{3\sqrt{10} + 9}{10}$ . 此时 $\theta  = \varphi  - \dfrac{3\pi }{2}$ ,则 $\cos \theta  =  - \sin \varphi  =  - \dfrac{3\sqrt{10}}{10},\sin \theta  =  \cos \varphi  = \dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}$ ,因此点 $M$ 坐标: $\left( {-\dfrac{9\sqrt{10}}{10},\dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}}\right)$ . 所以面积最大值为 $\dfrac{3\sqrt{10} + 9}{10}$ ,点 $M$ 坐标为 $\left( {-\dfrac{9\sqrt{10}}{10},\dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{10}}\right)$ ;

(3)方法一:设点 $Q\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 满足椭圆方程 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ，由题意 ${y}_{0} \neq  0$ ,则对称点 $R\left( {-{x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right)$ ,直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为: $y = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3}\left( {x - 3}\right) ,x =  - 3$ 时, ${y}_{D} = \dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3}$ ,故 $D\left( {-3,\dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3}}\right)$ . 直线 ${PR}$ 的方程为: $y = \dfrac{-{y}_{0}}{-{x}_{0} - 3}\left( {x - 3}\right)$ ,当 $x =  - 3$ 时, ${y}_{D} =  \dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3}$ ,故 $E\left( {-3,\dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 3}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {DE}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3} - \dfrac{-6{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 3}}\right|  =  \left| \dfrac{-6{y}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0} + 3}\right)  + 6{y}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0} - 3}\right) }{{x}_{0}^{2} - 9}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{-{36}{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 9}\right|$ ,由椭圆方程 ${x}_{0}^{2} =  9\left( {1 - {y}_{0}^{2}}\right)$ ,代入分母 ${x}_{0}^{2} - 9 =  - 9{y}_{0}^{2} \Longrightarrow  \left| {DE}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{-{36}{y}_{0}}{-9{y}_{0}^{2}}\right|  =  \dfrac{4}{\left| {y}_{0}\right| }$ ,由题意 $0 < \left| {y}_{0}\right|  < 1$ ,所以 $\left| {DE}\right|  \geq  4$ .

方法二:参数方程法

设椭圆上点 $Q\left( {3\cos \theta ,\sin \theta }\right) ,\sin \theta  \neq  0$ ，其关于原点的对称点 $R\left( {-3\cos \theta , - \sin \theta }\right)$ . 所以直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为:

$y = \dfrac{\sin \theta }{3\left( {\cos \theta  - 1}\right) }\left( {x - 3}\right)$ ,当 $x =  - 3$ 时, ${y}_{D} = \dfrac{2\sin \theta }{1 - \cos \theta }$ ,因此, $D\left( {-3,\dfrac{2\sin \theta }{1 - \cos \theta }}\right)$ ,直线 ${PR}$ 的方程为: $y = \dfrac{\sin \theta }{3\left( {\cos \theta  + 1}\right) }\left( {x - 3}\right)$ , 当 $x =  - 3$ 时, $y = \dfrac{-2\sin \theta }{1 + \cos \theta }$ ,因此, $E\left( {-3,\dfrac{-2\sin \theta }{1 + \cos \theta }}\right)$ ,

$\left| {DE}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{2\sin \theta }{1 - \cos \theta } - \dfrac{-2\sin \theta }{1 + \cos \theta }}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{2\sin \theta }{1 - \cos \theta } + \dfrac{2\sin \theta }{1 + \cos \theta }}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{2\sin \theta \left( {1 + \cos \theta }\right)  + 2\sin \theta \left( {1 - \cos \theta }\right) }{\left( {1 - \cos \theta }\right) \left( {1 + \cos \theta }\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{4\sin \theta }{{\sin }^{2}\theta }\right|  = \dfrac{4}{\left| \sin \theta \right| },$

因为 $0 < \left| {\sin \theta }\right|  \leq  1$ ,所以 $\left| {DE}\right|  \geq  4$ .

15. (1)设 $A\left( {2,y}\right)$ ，因为点 $A$ 为椭圆上一点，得 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{2}^{2}}{6} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} =  1,{y}^{2} = \dfrac{2}{3}$ ,又 ${F}_{1}\left( {-2,0}\right) ,\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( 2 - \left( -2\right) \right) }^{2} + {\left( y - 0\right) }^{2}}  = \dfrac{5\sqrt{6}}{3}$ ;

(2)设 $A\left( {x,y}\right) ,{xy} \neq  0$ ，则 ${S}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right| \left| y\right|  = 2\left| y\right| ,{S}_{2} =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{M}_{1}{M}_{2}}\right| \left| x\right|  = \sqrt{2}\left| x\right|$ ,因为 ${S}_{1} \geq  {S}_{2}$ ,即 $2\left| y\right|  \geq  \sqrt{2}\left| x\right|$ ,即 $2{y}^{2} \geq  {x}^{2}$ ,又 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{6} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ,所以 $2{y}^{2} \geq  6 - 3{y}^{2}$ ,得 $\dfrac{6}{5} \leq  {y}^{2} < 2$ , 所以 $\left| {OA}\right|  = \sqrt{{x}^{2} + {y}^{2}} = \sqrt{6 - 3{y}^{2} + {y}^{2}} = \sqrt{6 - 2{y}^{2}} \in  \left( {\sqrt{2},\dfrac{3\sqrt{10}}{5}}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 $\left| {OA}\right|$ 的范围是 $\left( {\sqrt{2},\dfrac{3\sqrt{10}}{5}}\right\rbrack$ ;

(3)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， ${y}_{1} > 0$ ， $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ， 由图像对称性，得 $A,C$ 关于 $y$ 轴对称，所以 $C\left( {-{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ，又 ${F}_{1}\left( {-2,0}\right)$ 、 ${F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ . 所以 $\vv{{F}_{1}A} = \left( {{x}_{1} + 2,{y}_{1}}\right)$ , $\vv{{F}_{1}B} = \left( {{x}_{2} + 2,{y}_{2}}\right) ,\vv{{F}_{1}C} = \left( {-{x}_{1} + 2}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{1}\right)$ ,所以 $\vv{{F}_{1}A} + \vv{{F}_{1}B} + \vv{{F}_{1}C} = \left( {{x}_{2} + }\right.  \left. {6,{y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,同理 $\vv{{F}_{2}A} + \vv{{F}_{2}B} + \vv{{F}_{2}C} = \left( {{x}_{2} - 6,{y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,因为 $\vv{{F}_{1}A} + \vv{{F}_{1}B} + \vv{{F}_{1}C} = \lambda \left( {\vv{{F}_{2}A} + \vv{{F}_{2}B} + \vv{{F}_{2}C}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left( {\vv{{F}_{1}A} + }\right)  \vv{{F}_{1}B} + \vv{{F}_{1}C})//\left( {\vv{{F}_{2}A} + \vv{{F}_{2}B} + \vv{{F}_{2}C}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left( {{x}_{2} + 6}\right) \left( {{y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1}}\right)  =  \left( {{x}_{2} - 6}\right) \left( {{y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,所以 ${y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1} = 0$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}_{2} + 2{y}_{1} \neq  0 \\  {x}_{2} + 6 = {x}_{2} - 6 \end{array}\right.$ (无解), 设直线 $A{F}_{2} : x = {my} + 2$ ,与椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{6} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ 联立得 $\left( {{m}^{2} + 3}\right) {y}^{2} + {4my} - 2 = 0$ ,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - {y}_{1} =  - \dfrac{4m}{{m}^{2} + 3} \\  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} =  - 2{y}_{1}^{2} = \dfrac{-2}{{m}^{2} + 3} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  m = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{5},{y}_{1} = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{4},{x}_{1} = m{y}_{1} + 2 = \dfrac{9}{4} \Longrightarrow  C\left( {-\dfrac{9}{4},\dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{4}}\right)$ .

16. (1) 由 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,得

$\left| {MF}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( {x}_{1} - 1\right) }^{2} + {y}_{1}^{2}} = \sqrt{{x}_{1}^{2} - 2{x}_{1} + 1 + 3\left( {1 - \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4}}\right) }$

\[
= \sqrt{\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} - 2{x}_{1} + 4} = \left| {\dfrac{{x}_{1}}{2} - 2}\right|  = 2 - \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{2};
\]

(2)设直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + m$ ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1, \\  y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + m, \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $y$ ,得 ${x}^{2} + {mx} + {m}^{2} - 3 = 0$ . 由 $\Delta  = {m}^{2} - 4\left( {{m}^{2} - 3}\right)  > 0$ ,得 $- 2 < m < 2$ . 从而 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - m,{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  +  {2m} = \dfrac{3m}{2}$ . 又 $\vv{QF} = \left( {1, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right) ,\vv{QM} + \vv{QN} = \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2},{y}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {{y}_{2} - 1}\right)  = \left( {-m,\dfrac{3m}{2} - 1}\right)$ . 由 $\vv{QF}$ 与 $\vv{QM} + \vv{QN}$ 平行,得 $1 \times  \left( {\dfrac{3m}{2} - 1}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \left( {-m}\right)$ ,解得 $m = 1$ ,故直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1;$

(3)设直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + m$ ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1, \\  y = {kx} + m, \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $y$ ,得 $\left( {3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {8kmx} + 4{m}^{2} - {12} = 0$ ,从而 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8km}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}, \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{m}^{2} - {12}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}. \end{array}\text{ 由 }\left| {MF}\right|  + \left| {NF}\right|  + \left| {MN}\right|  = 4}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {2 - \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{2}}\right)  + \left( {2 - \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{2}}\right)  + \left| {MN}\right|  = 4$ ,即 $\left| {MN}\right|  = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}$ ,亦即 $\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}} \cdot  \sqrt{{\left( -\dfrac{8km}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) }^{2} - 4 \cdot  \dfrac{4{m}^{2} - {12}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}} = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  \left( {-\dfrac{8km}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}}\right) ,$ 化简,整理得 ${12}{k}^{4} + {21}{k}^{2} + 9 - 3{m}^{2} - 4{k}^{2}{m}^{2} = 0$ ,即 $(3 +  \left. {4{k}^{2}}\right) \left( {3{k}^{2} + 3 - {m}^{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,从而 ${m}^{2} = 3\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right)$ . 又圆心 $C$ 到直线 $l$ 的距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| m\right| }{\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) }}{\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}} = \sqrt{3} = r$ ,故直线 $l$ 与圆 $C$ 相切.

17. ( 1 )由 $a = 3$ ， $b = \sqrt{3}$ 得 $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = \sqrt{6}$ ，所以焦距 ${2c} =  2\sqrt{6}$ ,离心率 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{3}$ ;

(2) $P\left( {3,0}\right)$ ，设直线 ${AP}$ 的方程 $x =  {ty} + 3$ ,与椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,联立 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x = {ty} + 3 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\text{ 得: }\dfrac{{\left( ty + 3\right) }^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1,}\right.$ 整理得: $\left( {{t}^{2} + 3}\right) {y}^{2} + {6ty} = 0,{y}_{A} + {y}_{P} = \dfrac{-{6t}}{{t}^{2} + 3},{y}_{A}{y}_{P} = 0$ ,因为点 $M$ 与点 $P$ 不重合, $M$ 为 ${AP}$ 中点,所以 ${y}_{M} = \dfrac{{y}_{A} + {y}_{P}}{2}  = \dfrac{-{3t}}{{t}^{2} + 3}$ ,代入方程 $x = {ty} + 3$ ,解得 ${x}_{M} = \dfrac{-3{t}^{2}}{{t}^{2} + 3} + 3 = \dfrac{9}{{t}^{2} + 3}$ , 所以可得点 $M\left( {\dfrac{9}{{t}^{2} + 3},\dfrac{-{3t}}{{t}^{2} + 3}}\right)$ ,于是由 ${k}_{OM} = \dfrac{-{3t}}{9} =  - \dfrac{1}{3}$ 得 $t = 1$ ,直线 ${AP}$ 的方程: $x - y - 3 = 0$ ;

(3) \ding{172}  当直线 ${AB}$ 斜率存在时，设方程为: $y = {kx} + m$ ，与椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + m \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,得: $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + \dfrac{{\left( kx + m\right) }^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,整理得: $\left( {3{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} + {6kmx} + 3{m}^{2} -  9 = 0$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由韦达定理得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{6km}}{3{k}^{2} + 1} \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{3{m}^{2} - 9}{3{k}^{2} + 1} \end{array}\right.$ ,且 $\Delta  =  {36}{k}^{2}{m}^{2} - 4\left( {3{k}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {3{m}^{2} - 9}\right)  > 0$ ,化简得 ${m}^{2} - 9{k}^{2} - 3 < 0$ , 又 $P\left( {3,0}\right)$ ,从而 $M\left( {\dfrac{{x}_{1} + 3}{2},\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{2}}\right) ,N\left( {\dfrac{{x}_{2} + 3}{2},\dfrac{{y}_{2}}{2}}\right)$ ,由 ${OM} \bot  {ON}$ 可得 $\vv{OM} \cdot  \vv{ON} = 0$ ,从而 $\left( {{x}_{1} + 3}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + 3}\right)  + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0$ ,又因为 ${y}_{1} = k{x}_{1} + m,{y}_{2} = k{x}_{2} + m$ ,所以上式化为: $\left( {{x}_{1} + 3}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + 3}\right)  +  \left( {k{x}_{1} + m}\right) \left( {k{x}_{2} + m}\right)  = 0$ ,整理得: $\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + \left( {{km} + 3}\right)  \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + {m}^{2} + 9 = 0$ ,韦达定理代入: $\dfrac{\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {3{m}^{2} - 9}\right) }{3{k}^{2} + 1} +  \dfrac{-{6km} \cdot  \left( {{km} + 3}\right) }{3{k}^{2} + 1} + {m}^{2} + 9 = 0$ ,化简得: $9{k}^{2} - {9km} + 2{m}^{2} = 0$ . $\left( {{3k} - {2m}}\right) \left( {{3k} - m}\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $m = {3k}$ 或 $m = \dfrac{3}{2}k$ ,当 $m = {3k}$ 时,直线 ${AB}$ 为: $y = {kx} + {3k} = k\left( {x + 3}\right)$ ,直线 ${AB}$ 经过点 $\left( {-3,0}\right)$ ,舍去; 当 $m = \dfrac{3}{2}k$ 时,直线 ${AB}$ 为: $y = {kx} + \dfrac{3}{2}k =  k\left( {x + \dfrac{3}{2}}\right)$ ,此时 $\dfrac{9}{4}{k}^{2} - 9{k}^{2} - 3 < 0$ 成立,直线 ${AB}$ 经过定点 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right)$ ;  \ding{173}  当直线 ${AB}$ 斜率不存在时,设 $A\left( {m,n}\right) ,B(m$ , $- n)$ ,则 $M\left( {\dfrac{m + 3}{2},\dfrac{n}{2}}\right) ,N\left( {\dfrac{m + 3}{2},\dfrac{-n}{2}}\right) ,\vv{OM} = \left( {\dfrac{m + 3}{2},\dfrac{n}{2}}\right)$ , $\vv{ON} = \left( {\dfrac{m + 3}{2},\dfrac{-n}{2}}\right)$ ,代入 $\vv{OM} \cdot  \vv{ON} = 0$ ,得 ${n}^{2} = {\left( m + 3\right) }^{2}$ 与 $\dfrac{{m}^{2}}{9} +  \dfrac{{n}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 联立得: $2{m}^{2} + {9m} + 9 = 0$ 解得 $m =  - \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,此时直线 ${AB}$ 也经过点 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right)$ . 综上,直线 ${AB}$ 经过定点 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right)$ .

18.(1)由题意 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ， ${F}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，设点 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ，则有 $\vv{P{F}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{P{F}_{2}} = \left( {-1 - {x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right)  \cdot  \left( {1 - {x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right)  = {x}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{1}^{2} -  1 = 3$ ,即 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{1}^{2} = 4$ ,显然点 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 既在圆上又在椭圆上. $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{5} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1 \\  {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,由题意点 $P$ 的坐标为 $P\left( {0, \pm  2}\right)$ ;

(2)由题意 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right) ,{F}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ， 直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为 $x = {my} + 1$ ,与椭圆方程联立得: $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{5} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1 \\  x = {my} + 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,化简得 $\left( {4{m}^{2} + 5}\right) {y}^{2} + {8my} - {16} = 0$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} +  {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8m}{4{m}^{2} + 5},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{16}{4{m}^{2} + 5}$ ,所以 $\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  =  \sqrt{{\left( {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{y}_{1}{y}_{2}} = 8\sqrt{5}\dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}{4{m}^{2} + 5}$ ,面积 $S = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  2 \times  {\left| {y}_{1}\right| }^{- - }  {y}_{2} \mid   = 8\sqrt{5}\dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}{4{m}^{2} + 5} = \dfrac{40}{21}$ ,解得 ${m}^{2} = 4$ ,即 $m =  \pm  2$ ,所以满足条件的直线方程为: $x + {2y} - 1 = 0$ 和 $x - {2y} - 1 = 0$ ;

(3)设 $A\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ，因为 $A$ ， $B$ 两点在椭圆上，所以 $4{x}_{3}^{2} + 5{y}_{3}^{2} = {20}$  \ding{172} ， $4{x}_{4}^{2} + 5{y}_{4}^{2} = {20}$  \ding{173} ， \ding{172}  -  \ding{173} 得 $4\left( {{x}_{3}^{2} - {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  +  5\left( {{y}_{3}^{2} - {y}_{4}^{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,即 $4 + 5\dfrac{{y}_{M}}{{x}_{M}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{3} - {y}_{4}}{{x}_{3} - {x}_{4}} = 0$ ,即 ${k}_{OM} \cdot  {k}_{AB} =  - \dfrac{4}{5}$ , 所以 ${k}_{OA} \cdot  {k}_{OB} =  - \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{y}_{3}{y}_{4}}{{x}_{3}{x}_{4}} =  - \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,得 $\left( {4{x}_{3}{x}_{4} + }\right.  \left. {5{y}_{3}{y}_{4}}\right) {}^{2} = 0$ ,即 ${16}{x}_{3}^{2}{x}_{4}^{2} + {25}{y}_{3}^{2}{y}_{4}^{2} + {40}{x}_{3}{x}_{4}{y}_{3}{y}_{4} = 0$  \ding{174} , \ding{172}  $\times$  \ding{173}  得 ${16}{x}_{3}^{2}{x}_{4}^{2} + {25}{y}_{3}^{2}{y}_{4}^{2} + {20}\left( {{x}_{3}^{2}{y}_{4}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}{y}_{3}^{2}}\right)  = {400}$  \ding{175} ,由 \ding{174} 、 \ding{175} 可得 ${x}_{3}^{2}{y}_{4}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}{y}_{3}^{2} - 2{x}_{3}{x}_{4}{y}_{3}{y}_{4} = {20}$ 即 ${\left( {x}_{3}{y}_{4} - {x}_{4}{y}_{3}\right) }^{2} = {20}$ ,因为直线 ${OA}$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{{y}_{3}}{{x}_{3}}x$ ,即 ${y}_{3}x - {x}_{3}y = 0$ ,点 $B$ 到直线 ${OA}$ 的距离为 $d = \dfrac{\left| {y}_{3}{x}_{4} - {x}_{3}{y}_{4}\right| }{\sqrt{{x}_{3}^{2} + {y}_{3}^{2}}}$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 的面积 $S = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {OA}\right| d =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{x}_{3}{y}_{4} - {x}_{4}{y}_{3}}\right|$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup {OAB}$ 的面积为定值 $\sqrt{5}$ .

19. ( 1 )设 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 、 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 的方程分别为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {a > b > 0}\right)$ 与 ${x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = {c}^{2}\left( {c > 0}\right)$ ,由 ${\left( \dfrac{5}{3}\right) }^{2} - {\left( \dfrac{4}{3}\right) }^{2} = {c}^{2}$ ,得 $c = 1$ ,故 ${F}_{1}$ 、 ${F}_{2}$ 的坐标分别为 $\left( {-1,0}\right) ,\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,所以 ${2a} = \left| {M{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {M{F}_{2}}\right|  =  \dfrac{4}{3}\sqrt{5} + \dfrac{2}{3}\sqrt{5} = 2\sqrt{5}$ ,故 $a = \sqrt{5},b = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {c}^{2}} = 2$ ,故 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 与 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 的方程分别为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{5} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1$ 与 ${x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

(2)当点 $P$ 在第四象限时,直线 $P{F}_{1}$ 、 $P{F}_{2}$ 的倾斜角都为钝角,不适合题意; 当 $P$ 在第一象限时,由直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的倾斜角是直线 $P{F}_{1}$ 的倾斜角的 2 倍,可知 $\angle {F}_{2}{F}_{1}P = \angle {F}_{2}P{F}_{1}$ ,故 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2$ ,设 $P$ 点坐标为 $\left( {x,y}\right)$ ,可知 ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} +  {y}^{2} = 4$ 且 ${x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 1\left( {x > 0,y > 0}\right)$ ,解得 $x = 2,y = \sqrt{3}$ ,故点 $P$ 的坐标为 $\left( {2,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ ;

(3)设直线 $P{F}_{1}$ 、 $P{F}_{2}$ 的斜率分别为 ${k}_{1}$ 、 ${k}_{2}$ ，点 $P$ 、 $A$ 、 $B$ 的坐标分别为 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 、 $\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，则 ${x}_{0}^{2} - {y}_{0}^{2} = 1$ ， ${k}_{1}{k}_{2} =  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 1} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 1} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 1} = \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2} - 1}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 1} = 1,P{F}_{1}$ 的方程为 $y = k(x +$ 1),代入 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{5} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1$ 可得 $\left( {4 + 5{k}^{2}}\right) {y}^{2} - {8ky} - {16}{k}^{2} = 0$ ,故 ${y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{-{16}{k}^{2}}{4 + 5{k}^{2}}$ ,所以 $m = \left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}_{1}^{2}}} \cdot  \left| {y}_{1}\right|$ . $\sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}_{1}^{2}}} \cdot  \left| {y}_{2}\right|  = \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}_{1}^{2}}}\right) \left| {{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{{16}\left( {{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) }{4 + 5{k}_{1}^{2}}$ ,同理可得 $n = \dfrac{{16}\left( {{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\right) }{4 + 5{k}_{2}^{2}}$ ,又 ${k}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{{k}_{1}}$ ,故 $n = \dfrac{{16}\left( {1 + {k}_{1}^{2}}\right) }{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 5}$ ,故 $\dfrac{1}{m} + \dfrac{1}{n} =  \dfrac{4 + 5{k}_{1}^{2}}{{16}\left( {{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) } + \dfrac{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 5}{{16}\left( {{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) } = \dfrac{9\left( {{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) }{{16}\left( {{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) } = \dfrac{9}{16}$ ,即 $m + n = \dfrac{9}{16}{mn}$ , 所以存在 $s = \dfrac{9}{16}$ ,使得 $m + n = {smn}$ .

点睛:方法点睛:求定值问题常见的方法有两种；

(1)从特殊入手，求出定值，再证明这个值与变量无关.

(2)直接推理、计算，并在计算推理的过程中消去变量，从而得到定值.

20. ( 1 )由对称性不妨设 $A\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {-2,0}\right)$ 、 $P\left( {2,0}\right)$ ，则 $\bigtriangleup {PAB}$ 的面积为 $2\sqrt{2}$ ；

(2)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，直线 ${AB} : y = x + t$ ，由 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = x + t \\  {x}^{2} + 2{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\text{ ,消去 }y\text{ 得 }3{x}^{2} + {4tx} + 2{t}^{2} - 4 = 0,\text{ 由 }\Delta  > 0\text{ 得 }\left| t\right|  < }\right.  \sqrt{6}$ ,由韦达定理得,弦 ${AB}$ 的中点 $M$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-\dfrac{2t}{3},\dfrac{t}{3}}\right)$ ,消去参数 $t$ ,点 $M$ 的轨迹方程是 $x + {2y} = 0\left( {-\dfrac{2\sqrt{6}}{3} < x < \dfrac{2\sqrt{6}}{3}}\right)$ ;

(3) 设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,直线 ${AB} : y = {2x} + t$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {2x} + t \\  2{x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,消去 $y$ 得 $9{x}^{2} + {8tx} + 2{t}^{2} - 4 = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} +  {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8t}{9},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{2{t}^{2} - 4}{9}$ ,设 $P\left( {x}_{0}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{0}\right)$ ,因为 ${k}_{PA} + {k}_{PB} = 0$ 恒成立,所以 $\dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}} + \dfrac{{y}_{2} - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{2} - {x}_{0}} = 0$ , 即 $\dfrac{2{x}_{1} + t - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}} + \dfrac{2{x}_{2} + t - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{2} - {x}_{0}} = 0$ ,化简得: $\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) \left( {t - 2{x}_{0}}\right.  \left. {-{y}_{0}}\right)  + 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 2{x}_{0}\left( {t - {y}_{0}}\right)  = 0$ ,再将 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8t}{9},{x}_{1}{x}_{2}  = \dfrac{2{t}^{2} - 4}{9}$ 代入上式化简得: $\left( {4{y}_{0} - {x}_{0}}\right) t - 8 + 9{x}_{0}{y}_{0} = 0$ ,上式看成关于 $t$ 的方程且有无数个解,则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 4{y}_{0} - {x}_{0} = 0 \\   - 8 + 9{x}_{0}{y}_{0} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} = \dfrac{4\sqrt{2}}{3} \\  {y}_{0} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{3} \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{4\sqrt{2}}{3} \\  {y}_{0} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{3} \end{array}\right.$ ,经验证符合椭圆方程 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2}  = 1$ ,所以存在点 $P\left( {\dfrac{4\sqrt{2}}{3},\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{3}}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-\dfrac{4\sqrt{2}}{3}, - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{3}}\right)$ 符合题意.

21. (1)由题得， ${F}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，设点 $P(1$ ， $\left. {y}_{P}\right)$ ,代入椭圆方程,得 ${y}_{P}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,因而 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ . 由 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  =  2\sqrt{2}$ ,得 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = \dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ;

(2)设动点 $S\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，则 ${\left| ST\right| }^{2} = {(x - }  t{)}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {x}^{2} - {2tx} + {t}^{2} + 1 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} = \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} -  {2tx} + {t}^{2} + 1 = \dfrac{1}{2}{\left( x - 2t\right) }^{2} + 1 - {t}^{2}$ ,由题, $\left| {ST}\right|$ 取得最小值时点 $S$ 恰与点 $A$ 重合,即函数 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}{\left( x - 2t\right) }^{2} + 1 - {t}^{2}$ 在 $x = \sqrt{2}$ 处取得最小值,又 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,因而 ${2t} \geq  \sqrt{2}$ ,得 $t \geq  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ . 因此,实数 $t$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ ;

(3)设 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ， $R\left( {x,y}\right)$ ， 由 $\vv{OR} = \lambda \vv{OM} + \mu \vv{ON}$ ,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = \lambda {x}_{1} + \mu {x}_{2} \\  y = \lambda {y}_{1} + \mu {y}_{2} \end{array}\right.$ , 又点 $R$ 在椭圆上，代入得 ${\left( \lambda {x}_{1} + \mu {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} +  2{\left( \lambda {y}_{1} + \mu {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} = 2$ ,化简得 ${\lambda }^{2}\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + }\right.  \left. {2{y}_{1}^{2}}\right)  + {\mu }^{2}\left( {{x}_{2}^{2} + 2{y}_{2}^{2}}\right)  + {2\lambda \mu }\left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + }\right.  \left. {2{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}\right)  = 2$ ,又点 $M,N$ 在椭圆上,得 $2{\lambda }^{2} + 2{\mu }^{2} + {2\lambda \mu }\left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + }\right.  \left. {2{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}\right)  = 2\left( *\right)$ . 由题,可设直线 $l : \left( {x - 1}\right)  - {my} = 0$ . 联立直线与椭圆方程,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {my} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} + 2{y}^{2} = 2 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {{m}^{2} + 2}\right) {y}^{2} + {2my} - 1 = 0$ . 故 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = \dfrac{-{2m}}{{m}^{2} + 2},{y}_{1} \cdot  {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} + 2}$ ,因而 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + 2{y}_{1}{y}_{2} =  \left( {m{y}_{1} + 1}\right) \left( {m{y}_{2} + 1}\right)  + 2{y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \left( {{m}^{2} + 2}\right) \dfrac{-1}{{m}^{2} + 2} + m\dfrac{-{2m}}{{m}^{2} + 2} +  1 = \dfrac{-2{m}^{2}}{{m}^{2} + 2}$ . 代入 $\left( *\right)$ 式,得 $2{\lambda }^{2} + 2{\mu }^{2} - {4\lambda \mu } \cdot  \dfrac{{m}^{2}}{{m}^{2} + 2} = 2$ ,因而 ${\lambda }^{2} + {\mu }^{2} = 1 + {2\lambda \mu } \cdot  \dfrac{{m}^{2}}{{m}^{2} + 2} \geq  {2\lambda \mu }$ (等号当且仅当 $\lambda  = \mu$ 时成立),即 ${\lambda \mu } \leq  \dfrac{{m}^{2} + 2}{4}$ (等号当且仅当 $\lambda  = \mu$ 时成立). 所以 ${\lambda \mu }$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{{m}^{2} + 2}{4}$ .

22. (1) 由 $\Gamma$ 的离心率是短轴的长的 $\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{4}$ 倍,得 $\dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - 1}}{a} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,即 $\sqrt{2\left( {{a}^{2} - 1}\right) } = a$ ,又 $a > 1$ ,则 $a = \sqrt{2}$ ,故椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

(2)设 $\Gamma$ 的左焦点为 ${F}_{1}$ ，连接 $C{F}_{1}$ ，因为 $\vv{CO} = \vv{OB}$ ，所以点 $B$ 、 $C$ 关于点 $O$ 对称,又 $\vv{CF} \cdot  \vv{AB} = 0$ ,则 ${CF} \bot  {AB}$ ,由椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的对称性可得, ${CF} \bot  C{F}_{1}$ ,且 $\bigtriangleup {OC}{F}_{1}$ 与 $\bigtriangleup {OBF}$ 全等,则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {CBF}} =  {S}_{\bigtriangleup C{F}_{1}F} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {C{F}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {CF}\right|$ ,又 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left| {C{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {CF}\right|  = 2\sqrt{2} \\  {\left| C{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} + {\left| CF\right| }^{2} = {\left| {F}_{1}F\right| }^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ , 化简整理得, $\left| {C{F}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {CF}\right|  = 2$ ,则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {CBF}} = 1$ ; (3) 设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ , $C\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,又 $\vv{OA} + \vv{OB} = \vv{CO}$ ,则 ${x}_{0} =  - \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) ,{y}_{0} =  - \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right) ,$ 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} + {y}^{2} = 1 \\  y = {kx} + m \end{array}\right.$ 得, $\left( {1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {4mkx}  + 2{m}^{2} - 2 = 0,\Delta  = {16}{m}^{2}{k}^{2} - 8\left( {1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) \left( {{m}^{2} - 1}\right)  = 8\left( {2{k}^{2} - }\right.  \left. {{m}^{2} + 1}\right)$ ,由韦达定理得, ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{4mk}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{2{m}^{2} - 2}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}$ , 又 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = k\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + {2m} = \dfrac{2m}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}$ ,则 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{4mk}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}$ , ${y}_{0} = \dfrac{-{2m}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}$ ,因为点 $C$ 在椭圆 $\Gamma$ 上,所以 ${\left( \dfrac{4mk}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) }^{2} +  2{\left( \dfrac{-{2m}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) }^{2} = 2$ ,化简整理得, $4{m}^{2} = 1 + 2{k}^{2}$ ,此时 $\Delta  = 8\left( {2{k}^{2} - }\right.  \left. {{m}^{2} + 1}\right)  = 8\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1 - \dfrac{2{k}^{2} + 1}{4}}\right)  = 6\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  > 0$ ,则

$\left| \vv{AB}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( {x}_{2} - {x}_{1}\right) }^{2} + {\left( {y}_{2} - {y}_{1}\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) {\left( {x}_{2} - {x}_{1}\right) }^{2}}$

$= \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}} \cdot  \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{-{4mk}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) }^{2} - 4\left( \dfrac{2{m}^{2} - 2}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) }$

$= \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}} \cdot  \dfrac{\sqrt{6\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }}{1 + 2{k}^{2}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{6 + 6{k}^{2}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}},$

令 $t = 1 + 2{k}^{2}$ ,即 $t \geq  1$ ,则 $\dfrac{6 + 6{k}^{2}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}} = \dfrac{{3t} + 3}{t} = 3 + \dfrac{3}{t} \in  (3,6\rbrack$ , 则 $\left| \vv{AB}\right|$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {\sqrt{3},\sqrt{6}}\right\rbrack$ .

23. ( 1 )在椭圆 $C : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 中，左、右顶点分别为 ${A}_{1}( - 2$ , $0),{A}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ ,设点 $Q\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{0} \neq   \pm  2}\right)$ ,则 ${k}_{Q{A}_{1}} \cdot  {k}_{Q{A}_{2}} =  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 2} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 2} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 4} = \dfrac{3\left( {1 - \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{4}}\right) }{{x}_{0}^{2} - 4} =  - \dfrac{3}{4};$

(2)设 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，由已知可得 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ， $\vv{N{F}_{1}} = \left( {-1 - {x}_{2}}\right.$ , $\left. {-{y}_{2}}\right) ,\vv{{F}_{1}M} = \left( {{x}_{1} + 1,{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,由 $\vv{N{F}_{1}} =  2\vv{{F}_{1}M}$ 得 $\left( {-1 - {x}_{2}, - {y}_{2}}\right)  = 2\left( {{x}_{1} + 1}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{1}\right)$ ,化简得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{2} =  - 3 - 2{x}_{1} \\  {y}_{2} =  - 2{y}_{1} \end{array}\right.$ ,代入 $\dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4} +  \dfrac{{y}_{2}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 可得 $\dfrac{{\left( -3 - 2{x}_{1}\right) }^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{\left( -2{y}_{1}\right) }^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,联立 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} =  - \dfrac{7}{4} \\  {y}_{1} =  \pm  \dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{8} \end{array}\right.$ ,由 $\vv{N{F}_{1}} = 2\vv{{F}_{1}M}$ 得直线 $l$ 过点 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right)$ , $M\left( {-\dfrac{7}{4}, \pm  \dfrac{3}{8}\sqrt{5}}\right)$ ，所以，所求直线方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ； (3)设 $M\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ ， $N\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ，易知直线 $l$ 的斜率不为 0,设其方程为 $x = {my} + t\left( {t \neq  2}\right)$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {my} + t \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ , 可得 $\left( {3{m}^{2} + 4}\right) {y}^{2} + {6mty} + 3{t}^{2} - {12} =$ 0,由 $\Delta  = {36}{m}^{2}{t}^{2} - 4\left( {3{m}^{2} + 4}\right) \left( {3{t}^{2} - {12}}\right)  > 0$ ,得 ${t}^{2} < 3{m}^{2} + 4$ . 由韦达定理,得 ${y}_{3} + {y}_{4} =  - \dfrac{6mt}{3{m}^{2} + 4},{y}_{3}{y}_{4} = \dfrac{3{t}^{2} - {12}}{3{m}^{2} + 4}$ . 因为 ${k}_{1}{k}_{2} =  - \dfrac{9}{4}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{y}_{3}}{{x}_{3} - 2} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{4}}{{x}_{4} - 2} =  - \dfrac{9}{4}$ . 可化为 $4{y}_{3}{y}_{4} +  9\left( {m{y}_{3} + t - 2}\right) \left( {m{y}_{4} + t - 2}\right)  = 0$ ,整理即得 $\left( {4 + 9{m}^{2}}\right) {y}_{3}{y}_{4} +  {9m}\left( {t - 2}\right) \left( {{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}\right)  + 9{\left( t - 2\right) }^{2} = 0$ ,所以 $\left( {4 + 9{m}^{2}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{3{t}^{2} - {12}}{3{m}^{2} + 4} + {9m}\left( {t - 2}\right) \left( {-\dfrac{6mt}{3{m}^{2} + 4}}\right)  + 9{\left( t - 2\right) }^{2} = 0$ ,由 $t - 2 \neq$ 0,进一步得 $\dfrac{\left( {4 + 9{m}^{2}}\right) \left( {t + 2}\right) }{3{m}^{2} + 4} - \dfrac{{18}{m}^{2}t}{3{m}^{2} + 4} + 3\left( {t - 2}\right)  = 0$ ,化简可得 ${16t} - {16} = 0$ ,解得 $t = 1$ ,直线 ${MN}$ 的方程为 $x = {my} + 1$ , 恒过定点 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ .

24. (1) $b = 1,e = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ，所以椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ；

(2)因为命题“对任意直线 $l$ ，线段 ${MN}$ 的中点为定点”为真命题，可以取直线 $l$ 为 $y =  - x - 1$ ,与 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} +  {y}^{2} = 1$ 联立得 $B\left( {-\dfrac{8}{5},\dfrac{3}{5}}\right) ,C\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ ,则 ${l}_{AB} : y = \dfrac{1}{4}x +$ 1,得 $M\left( {-4,0}\right) ,N\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,故重心 $G$ 坐标为 $x = \dfrac{1}{3}( - 4 + 0  + 0) =  - \dfrac{4}{3},y = \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {0 + 0 + 1}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,即 $G\left( {-\dfrac{4}{3},\dfrac{1}{3}}\right)$ ;
