(3)设 $l : y - 1 = k\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ， $B\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $C\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，则直线 ${AB} : y - 1 = \dfrac{{y}_{1} - 1}{{x}_{1}}x$ ,令 $y = 0$ ,得 ${x}_{M} = \dfrac{-{x}_{1}}{{y}_{1} - 1}$ ,同理得: ${x}_{N} =  \dfrac{-{x}_{2}}{{y}_{2} - 1},{x}_{N} - {x}_{M} = \dfrac{-{x}_{2}}{{y}_{2} - 1} - \dfrac{-{x}_{1}}{{y}_{1} - 1} = \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{k\left( {{x}_{1} + 2}\right) } - \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{k\left( {{x}_{2} + 2}\right) } =  \dfrac{2}{k} \cdot  \dfrac{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}{\left( {{x}_{1} + 2}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + 2}\right) }$ ,设直线 $l$ 与 $y$ 轴交于 $Q$ 点, $Q(0,{2k} +$ 1), $\left| {AQ}\right|  =  - {2k},{S}_{\bigtriangleup {AMN}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {MN}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{k} \cdot  \dfrac{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}{\left( {{x}_{1} + 2}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + 2}\right) }$ , ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABC}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AQ}\right| \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right)$ ,因为 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AMN}} = 2{S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABC}}$ ,故得 $\left( {{x}_{1} + }\right.$ 2) $\left( {{x}_{2} + 2}\right)  = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + 2\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 4 = \dfrac{1}{2{k}^{2}},\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + {2k} + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \left( {1 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + \left( {{16}{k}^{2} + {8k}}\right) x + {16}{k}^{2} + {16k} = 0$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{{16}{k}^{2} + {8k}}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{{16}{k}^{2} + {16k}}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}$ ,代入得 $\dfrac{{16}{k}^{2} + {16k}}{1 + 4{k}^{2}} - \dfrac{{32}{k}^{2} + {16k}}{1 + 4{k}^{2}} +  4 = \dfrac{1}{2{k}^{2}}$ ,解得 $k =  \pm  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,由图知道 $k =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,故直线 $l$ 的方程为: $x + {2y} = 0$ .

25. ( 1 )由题设知椭圆 $\Gamma$ 中， $a = 2$ ， $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ 得 $c = \sqrt{3}$ ，由 ${a}^{2} = {b}^{2} + {c}^{2}$ 得 $b = 1$ ，所以椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ；

(2)设 $M\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，由 $\left| {MA}\right|  = 2\left| {MB}\right|$ 得 ${\left( x + 2\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4$ [( $x -  \left. {1{)}^{2} + {y}^{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,化简得 ${\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 4$ . 表示的是以 $\left( {2,0}\right)$ 为圆心,2 为半径的圆,其面积为 ${4\pi }$ ;

(3)设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0} \neq  0}\right)$ ，且 ${x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 4$ ，设过点 $P$ 的直线 $y = {kx} + m$ 与椭圆相切，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + m \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ，化简得(1+ $\left. {4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {8kmx} + 4\left( {{m}^{2} - 1}\right)  = 0$ ,由 $\Delta  = {64}{k}^{2}{m}^{2} - {16}\left( {{m}^{2} - 1}\right)  \left( {4{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  = 0$ 得 ${m}^{2} = 4{k}^{2} + 1$ ,点 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 在直线 $y = {kx} +  m$ 上,得 $m = {y}_{0} - k{x}_{0}$ 代入上式, ${\left( {y}_{0} - k{x}_{0}\right) }^{2} = 4{k}^{2} + 1$ ,化简得 $\left( {4 - {x}_{0}^{2}}\right) {k}^{2} + 2{x}_{0}{y}_{0}k + 1 - {y}_{0}^{2} = 0$ ,因为 ${l}_{1},{l}_{2}$ 是椭圆的两条切线,所以 ${k}_{1},{k}_{2}$ 是上面方程的两根,由韦达定理得 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} =  \dfrac{2{x}_{0}{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 4}$ ,由 ${x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 4$ 得 ${x}_{0}^{2} - 4 =  - {y}_{0}^{2}$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = \dfrac{2{x}_{0}{y}_{0}}{-{y}_{0}^{2}}  = \dfrac{2{x}_{0}}{-{y}_{0}}$ ,又 ${k}_{0} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}}$ ,所以 ${k}_{0}\left( {{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{2{x}_{0}}{-{y}_{0}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} =  - 2$ .

26. (1)设 $\Gamma$ 的半长轴长为 $a$ ，半短轴长为 $b$ ，半焦距为 $c$ ，则 $a = \sqrt{6},b = \sqrt{3}$ ,则 $c = \sqrt{3}$ ,所以 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{\sqrt{6}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ;

(2)依题意，设 $M\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，则 $- \sqrt{6} \leq  x \leq  \sqrt{6},\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{6} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ，故 ${y}^{2} =  3 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2}$ ,则 $\left| {MN}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2}} = \sqrt{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + 3 - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2}} =  \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2} - {2x} + 4} = \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2}{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} + 2}$ ,所以由二次函数的性质可知,当 $x = 2$ 时, $\left| {MN}\right|$ 取得最小值为 $\sqrt{2}$ ,当 $x =  - \sqrt{6}$ 时, $\left| {MN}\right|$ 取得最大值为 $\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2}{\left( -\sqrt{6} - 2\right) }^{2} + 2} = 1 + \sqrt{6}$ ;

(3)设 $P\left( {a,3 - a}\right)$ ， $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，又 $T\left( {2,1}\right)$ ，易得 ${k}_{OT} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则直线 $l$ 为 $y - \left( {3 - a}\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {x - a}\right)$ ,即 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 3 - \dfrac{3}{2}a$ ,而 ${\left| \vv{PT}\right| }^{2} = {\left( a - 2\right) }^{2} + {\left( 3 - a - 1\right) }^{2} =  2{\left( a - 2\right) }^{2},\vv{PA} = \left( {{x}_{1} - a,{y}_{1} - 3 + a}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{1} - a,\dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{1} + 3 - \dfrac{3}{2}a - 3 + a}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{1} - a,\dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{1} - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right)$ , $\vv{PB} = \left( {{x}_{2} - a,{y}_{2} - 3 + a}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{2} - a,\dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{2} + 3 - \dfrac{3}{2}a - 3 + a}\right)  = \left( {{x}_{2} - a,\dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{2} - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right)$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 3 - \dfrac{3}{2}a \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{6} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,消去 $y$ ,得 ${x}^{2} + 2\left( {2 - a}\right) x + 3{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} - 4 = 0$ ,则 $\Delta  = 4{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  \left\lbrack  {3{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} - 4}\right\rbrack   =  - 8\left( {{a}^{2} - {4a} + 2}\right)  > 0$ ,得 $2 - \sqrt{2} < a < 2 + \sqrt{2}$ , 所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - 2\left( {2 - a}\right) ,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = 3{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} - 4$ ,故 $\left| {\vv{PA} \cdot  \vv{PB}}\right|  =  \left| {\left( {{x}_{1} - a}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - a}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {{x}_{1} - a}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - a}\right) }\right|  = \dfrac{5}{4} \mid  \left( {{x}_{1} - a}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - }\right.  a)\left| { = \dfrac{5}{4}}\right| {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - a\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + {a}^{2}\left| { = \dfrac{5}{4}}\right| 3{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} - 4 + {2a}(2 -  a) + {a}^{2}\left| { = \dfrac{5}{4}}\right| 2{\left( 2 - a\right) }^{2} \mid$ ,所以 $\left| {\vv{PA} \cdot  \vv{PB}}\right|  = \dfrac{5}{4}{\left| \vv{PT}\right| }^{2}$ ,故存在 $\lambda  = \dfrac{5}{4}$ ,使得 $\left| {\vv{PA} \cdot  \vv{PB}}\right|  = \lambda {\left| \vv{PT}\right| }^{2}$ 恒成立.

点睛: 利用韦达定理法解决直线与圆锥曲线相交问题的基本步骤如下:

(1)设直线方程，设交点坐标为 $\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ；

(2)联立直线与圆锥曲线的方程，得到关于 $x$ (或 $y$ )的一元二次方程，注意 $\Delta$ 的判断；

(3)列出韦达定理；

(4)将所求问题或题中的关系转化为 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2}$ 、 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2}$ (或 ${y}_{1} +  \left. {{y}_{2},{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}\right)$ 的形式;

(5)代入韦达定理求解.

27.(1)由题意，得 $c = \sqrt{3},a = 2$ ，所以 $b = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {c}^{2}} = 1$ ，故椭圆的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

(2)设动点 $T\left( {x,y}\right)$ ， $\vv{{F}_{2}{F}_{1}} = \left( {-2\sqrt{3},0}\right)$ ， $\vv{PT} = \left( {x,y - t}\right)$ ， $\vv{PT} \cdot  \left( {\vv{T{F}_{1}} - \vv{T{F}_{2}}}\right)  = \vv{PT} \cdot  \vv{{F}_{2}{F}_{1}} =  - 2\sqrt{3}x$ ，因为 $x \in  \lbrack  - 2$ , 2]，所以 $\vv{PT} \cdot  \left( {\vv{T{F}_{1}} - \vv{T{F}_{2}}}\right)$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {-4\sqrt{3},4\sqrt{3}}\right\rbrack$ ；

(3)显然直线的斜率存在，所以可以设直线 $l : y = {kx} + t$ ，联立得到 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + t \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 整理，得 $\left( {1 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {8ktx} + \left( {4{t}^{2} - 4}\right)  =$ 0,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8kt}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},{x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{t}^{2} - 4}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}$ ,则 ${x}_{\text{ 中 }} =  - \dfrac{4kt}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},{y}_{\text{ 中 }} = k{x}_{\text{ 中 }} + t = \dfrac{t}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}$ ,所以 $M\left( {-\dfrac{4kt}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},\dfrac{t}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}}\right)$ , 又因为直线 $l$ 与椭圆交于两点,所以 ${\Delta }_{1} = {64}{k}^{2}{t}^{2} - 4(1 +  \left. {4{k}^{2}}\right) \left( {4{t}^{2} - 4}\right)  > 0$ ,化简得 ${64}{k}^{2} + {16} - {16}{t}^{2} > 0$ ,则 ${k}^{2} > \dfrac{{t}^{2} - 1}{4}$  \ding{172} , 所以 ${k}_{OM} =  - \dfrac{1}{4k}$ ,如果 $\vv{OM}//\vv{PQ}$ ,则 ${k}_{PQ} = {k}_{OM} =  - \dfrac{1}{4k}$ ,设直线 ${PQ}$ 为 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{4k}x + t,\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \dfrac{1}{4k}x + t \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,整理得 $\left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{4{k}^{2}}}\right) {x}^{2} -  \dfrac{2t}{k}x + 4{t}^{2} - 4 = 0$ ,要使得存在点 $Q$ ,则 ${\Delta }_{2} = \dfrac{4{t}^{2}}{{k}^{2}} - 4(1 +  \left. \dfrac{1}{4{k}^{2}}\right) \left( {4{t}^{2} - 4}\right)  \geq  0$ ,整理得 ${16} + \dfrac{4}{{k}^{2}} - {16}{t}^{2} \geq  0$ ,所以 ${k}^{2} \leq  \dfrac{1}{4{t}^{2} - 4}$  \ding{173} ，由  \ding{172}  \ding{173}  式得，所以 $\dfrac{{t}^{2} - 1}{4} < {k}^{2} \leq  \dfrac{1}{4{t}^{2} - 4}$ ，则 $\dfrac{{t}^{2} - 1}{4} <  \dfrac{1}{4{t}^{2} - 4}$ ,解得 $- \sqrt{2} < t < \sqrt{2}$ ,所以当 $t \geq  \sqrt{2}$ 时,不存在点 $Q$ ,使得 $\vv{OM}//\vv{PQ}$ .

28.(1)设椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的半长轴为 $a > 0$ ，半短轴为 $b > 0$ ，半焦距为 $c > 0$ ,则 $a = 2,b = \sqrt{2},c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = \sqrt{2}$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup A{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 的周长为 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  + \left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a} + {2c} = 4 + 2\sqrt{2}$ ; (2)由题意可知:直线 $l$ 经过 $\Gamma$ 的右顶点 $\left( {2,0}\right)$ ,圆 $C$ 的圆心为 $O(0$ , $0)$ ,半径 $r = \sqrt{2}$ ,若直线 $l$ 与圆 $C$ 相切,显然直线 $l$ 的斜率存在,设直线 $l : y = k\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,即 ${kx} - y - {2k} = 0$ , 则 $\dfrac{\left| 0 - 0 - 2k\right| }{\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}} = \sqrt{2}$ ,解得 $k =  \pm  1$ , 所以直线 $l$ 的方程为 $x - y - 2 = 0$ 或 $x + y - 2 = 0$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/72_1324_472_256_266_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)设 $D\left( {m,2}\right)$ ， $A\left( {2\cos \theta ,\sqrt{2}\sin \theta }\right)$ ， 则 $\vv{OD} = \left( {m,2}\right) ,\vv{OA} = (2\cos \theta$ , $\left. {\sqrt{2}\sin \theta }\right)$ ,若 ${OA} \bot  {OD}$ ,则 $\vv{OD} \cdot  \vv{OA} =  {2m}\cos \theta  + 2\sqrt{2}\sin \theta  = 0$ ,即 $\sin \theta  =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}m\cos \theta$ ,又因为 $\vv{DA} = \left( {2\cos \theta  - }\right)  m,\sqrt{2}\sin \theta  - 2) = \left( {2\cos \theta  - m, - m\cos \theta  - 2}\right)$ ,设直线 ${AD}$ 上任一点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ ,则 $\vv{DP} = \left( {x - m,y - 2}\right)$ ,因为 $\vv{DA}//\vv{DP}$ ,则 $(2\cos \theta  -  m)\left( {y - 2}\right)  = \left( {-m\cos \theta  - 2}\right) \left( {x - m}\right)$ ,整理得 $\left( {m\cos \theta  + 2}\right) x +  \left( {2\cos \theta  - m}\right) y - \left( {{m}^{2} + 4}\right) \cos \theta  = 0$ ,即直线 $l$ 的方程为 $\left( {m\cos \theta  + 2}\right)  x + \left( {2\cos \theta  - m}\right) y - \left( {{m}^{2} + 4}\right) \cos \theta  = 0$ ,圆心 $O\left( {0,0}\right)$ 到直线 ${AD}$ 的距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| \left( {m}^{2} + 4\right) \cos \theta \right| }{\sqrt{{\left( m\cos \theta  + 2\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2\cos \theta  - m\right) }^{2}}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 4}\left| {\cos \theta }\right| }{\sqrt{1 + {\cos }^{2}\theta }} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 4}\left| {\cos \theta }\right| }{\sqrt{2{\cos }^{2}\theta  + {\sin }^{2}\theta }} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 4}\left| {\cos \theta }\right| }{\sqrt{2{\cos }^{2}\theta  + \dfrac{1}{2}{m}^{2}{\cos }^{2}\theta }} = \sqrt{2}$ ,所以直线 ${AD}$ 与圆 $C$ 相切.

\end{center}
 

29. (1) 由椭圆方程可知: $a = 2\sqrt{2},b = 2,c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = 2$ , 则 ${F}_{1}\left( {-2,0}\right) ,{F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ ,设直线 ${OA} : y = {kx},k > 0,A\left( {x}_{0}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{0}\right) ,{x}_{0} > 0,{y}_{0} > 0$ ,可得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}_{0} = k{x}_{0} \\  \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{4} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 ${x}_{0}^{2} = \dfrac{8}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}$ ,则 $\left| {OA}\right|  = \sqrt{{x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2}} = \sqrt{{x}_{0}^{2} + {k}^{2}{x}_{0}^{2}} = 2\sqrt{2}\sqrt{\dfrac{1 + {k}^{2}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}} = \sqrt{6}$ ,解得 ${k}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{2},k = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,则 ${x}_{0}^{2} = 4$ ,即 ${x}_{0} = 2,{y}_{0} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}{x}_{0} = \sqrt{2}$ ,所以 $A\left( {2,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ;

(2)因为 $\vv{A{F}_{1}} = \left( {-2 - {x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right) ,\vv{A{F}_{2}} = \left( {2 - {x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right)$ ，可得 $\vv{A{F}_{1}} + 3\vv{A{F}_{2}} = \left( {4 - 4{x}_{0}, - 4{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 $\left| {\vv{A{F}_{1}} + 3\vv{A{F}_{2}}}\right|  =  \sqrt{{\left( 4 - 4{x}_{0}\right) }^{2} + {\left( 4{y}_{0}\right) }^{2}} = 4\sqrt{{x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} - 2{x}_{0} + 1}$ ,因为 $\dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{4}  = 1$ ,则 ${y}_{0}^{2} = 4 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{0}^{2},{x}_{0} \in  \left( {0,2\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,可得 $\left| {\vv{A{F}_{1}} + 3\vv{A{F}_{2}}}\right|  =  4\sqrt{{x}_{0}^{2} + 4 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{0}^{2} - 2{x}_{0} + 1} = 4\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2}{\left( {x}_{0} - 2\right) }^{2} + 3} \in  \lbrack 4\sqrt{3}$ , $\left. {4\sqrt{5}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {\vv{A{F}_{1}} + 3\vv{A{F}_{2}}}\right|$ 的取值范围为 $\lbrack 4\sqrt{3},4\sqrt{5})$ ;

(3) 设 $C\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,由题意可知: $A\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) ,B\left( {-{x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right) ,E\left( {{x}_{0},0}\right)$ , 则 $k = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}},{k}_{BC} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{2{x}_{0}} = \dfrac{1}{2}k$ ,且 ${k}_{AC} =  \dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}},{k}_{BC} = \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} + {x}_{0}}$ ,因为点 $A,C$ 均在椭圆上,则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{4} = 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{4} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,两式相减得 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{0}^{2}}{8} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} - {y}_{0}^{2}}{4} =$ 0,整理可得 $\dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} - {y}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{0}^{2}} = \dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{0}}{{x}_{1} + {x}_{0}} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 ${k}_{AC}$ . ${k}_{BC} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 ${k}_{AC}\left( {\dfrac{1}{2}k}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 ${k}_{AC} \cdot  k =  - 1$ ,可知 ${AB} \bot  {AC}$ ,又因为 $\tan \angle {ABC} = \dfrac{k - \dfrac{1}{2}k}{1 + k \times  \dfrac{1}{2}k} = \dfrac{k}{{k}^{2} + 2}$ ,则 $\left| {AC}\right|  =  \left| {AB}\right| \tan \angle {ABC} = \dfrac{k}{{k}^{2} + 2}\left| {AB}\right|$ ,可得 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 面积 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABC}} =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right|  \cdot  \left| {AC}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{k}{{k}^{2} + 2}{\left| AB\right| }^{2} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{k}{{k}^{2} + 2} \times  4{\left| OA\right| }^{2} =  \dfrac{2k}{{k}^{2} + 2} \times  8\left( \dfrac{1 + {k}^{2}}{1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{{16}\left( {{k}^{3} + k}\right) }{2{k}^{4} + 5{k}^{2} + 2},$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/73_581_225_238_181_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

方法一: $S = \dfrac{{16}\left( {k + \dfrac{1}{k}}\right) }{2{\left( k + \dfrac{1}{k}\right) }^{2} + 1}$ ，设 $t = k + \dfrac{1}{k}$ ，由 $k > 0$ ，得 $t \geq  2$ ，当且仅当 $k = 1$ 时取等号. 因为 $S = \dfrac{16t}{2{t}^{2} + 1}$ 在 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ 上为严格减函数,所以当 $t = 2$ 即 $k = 1$ 时, $S$ 取最大值为 $\dfrac{32}{9}$ .

方法二: 设 $f\left( k\right)  = \dfrac{{k}^{3} + k}{2{k}^{4} + 5{k}^{2} + 2},k > 0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( k\right)  =$

$\dfrac{\left( {2{k}^{4} + 3{k}^{2} + 2}\right) \left( {k + 1}\right) \left( {1 - k}\right) }{{\left( 2{k}^{4} + 5{k}^{2} + 2\right) }^{2}}$ ,当 $0 < k < 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( k\right)  > 0$ ; 当 $k > 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( k\right)  < 0$ ; 可知 $f\left( k\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 内严格增,在 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 内严格减，则 $f\left( k\right)  \leq  f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{2}{9}$ ，所以 $\bigtriangleup  {ABC}$ 面积的最大值为 ${16} \times  \dfrac{2}{9} = \dfrac{32}{9}$ .

30. ( 1 ) $\left| {AF}\right|  = \sqrt{{b}^{2} + {c}^{2}} = a = 2$ ，直线 $l : x - {my} - 1 = 0$ 过 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,所以右焦点 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,即 $c = 1$ ,所以 ${b}^{2} = {a}^{2} - {c}^{2} = 4 -  1 = 3$ ,椭圆方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ;

(2)当 $m = \sqrt{3}$ ，直线 $l : x = \sqrt{3}y + 1$ ，

$\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x = \sqrt{3}y + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {13}{y}^{2} + 6\sqrt{3}y - 9 = 0}\right.$ ,解得 ${y}_{1} = \dfrac{-6\sqrt{3} + {24}}{26} =  \dfrac{-3\sqrt{3} + {12}}{13},{y}_{2} = \dfrac{-6\sqrt{3} - {24}}{26} = \dfrac{-3\sqrt{3} - {12}}{13}$ ,所以 $\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  =  \dfrac{24}{13} \Longrightarrow  \left| {PQ}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {\left( \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}}\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{48}{13}$ ,设 $M\left( {{x}_{0},0}\right)$ ,到直线距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{0} - 1\right| }{\sqrt{1 + {\left( \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}}} = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{0} - 1\right| }{2}$ ,由面积 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {MPQ}} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{48}{13} \times  \dfrac{\left| {x}_{0} - 1\right| }{2} = \dfrac{6}{13}$ ,得 $\left| {{x}_{0} - 1}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2},{x}_{0} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ 或 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 $M\left( {\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right)$ 或 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},0}\right)$ ;

(3)设 $P\left( {{x}_{P},{y}_{P}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{Q},{y}_{Q}}\right) ,G(5$ ， $k)$ ,因为向量 $\vv{PG}$ 在直线 $l$ 上的投影为向量 $\vv{PF}$ ，故 ${FG} \bot  {PQ}$ ，故直线 ${FG}$ 的斜率为 $- m$ ，故直线 ${FG}$ 的方程为 $y =  - m\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,故 $G\left( {5, - {4m}}\right)$ ,而 $\left| {PF}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {m}^{2}}\left| {y}_{P}\right|$ , $\left| {QF}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {m}^{2}}\left| {y}_{Q}\right| ,\left| {FG}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {m}^{2}}\left| {5 - 1}\right|  = 4\sqrt{1 + {m}^{2}},$ 故 $\tan \angle {PGQ} = \dfrac{\tan \angle {PGF} + \tan \angle {QGF}}{1 - \tan \angle {PGF}\tan \angle {QGF}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{\left| {y}_{P}\right| }{4} + \dfrac{\left| {y}_{Q}\right| }{4}}{1 - \dfrac{\left| {y}_{P}{y}_{Q}\right| }{16}} =  \dfrac{4\left| {{y}_{P} - {y}_{Q}}\right| }{{16} - \left| {{y}_{P}{y}_{Q}}\right| }$ ,联立 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x = {my} + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {3{m}^{2} + 4}\right) {y}^{2} + {6my} - 9 = }\right.  0,{y}_{P} + {y}_{Q} =  - \dfrac{6m}{3{m}^{2} + 4},{y}_{P}{y}_{Q} =  - \dfrac{9}{3{m}^{2} + 4},\Delta  = {144}\left( {{m}^{2} + 1}\right)  >$ 0,故 $\tan \angle {PGQ} = \dfrac{{48}\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}{{16}\left( {3{m}^{2} + 4}\right)  - 9} = \dfrac{{48}\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}{{48}\left( {{m}^{2} + 1}\right)  + 7} =  \dfrac{48}{{48}\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1} + \dfrac{7}{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}}$ ,设 $t = \sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1} > 1$ ,设 $y = {48t} + \dfrac{7}{t}$ , $t > 1$ ,由双勾函数的性质可得 $y = {48t} + \dfrac{7}{t}$ 在 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 为严格增函数,故 ${48t} + \dfrac{7}{t} > {55}$ ,故 $\tan \angle {PGQ} < \dfrac{48}{55} < 1$ ,所以 $\angle {PGQ} < \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ .

31. ( 1 )由题，椭圆 ${C}_{1}$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{3}$ ，椭圆 ${C}_{2}$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - {2a}}}{a},\dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{3} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - {2a}}}{a}$ ,解得 $a = 6$ ;

(2)由题， $A\left( {\sqrt{3},0}\right)$ ， $B\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，所以 $\left| {AB}\right|  = 2$ ，直线 ${AB}$ 的方程为 $y =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}x + 1$ ,设 ${C}_{2}$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} + 3{y}^{2} = \lambda \left( {\lambda  > 0}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ , $D\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,联立直线 ${AB}$ 与椭圆 ${C}_{2} : \left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}x + 1 \\  {x}^{2} + 3{y}^{2} = \lambda  \end{array}\right.$ ,代入整理得 $2{x}^{2} - 2\sqrt{3}x + 3 - \lambda  = 0$ ,故 $\left| {CD}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {\left( -\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\right) }^{2}}\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  =  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \times  \dfrac{\sqrt{{12} - 8\left( {3 - \lambda }\right) }}{2} = 3\left| {AB}\right|  = 6$ ,即 $\sqrt{{12} - 8\left( {3 - \lambda }\right) } =  6\sqrt{3}$ ,解得 $\lambda  = {15}$ . 所以 ${C}_{2}$ 的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{15} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{5} = 1$ ;

(3)设 ${C}_{2}$ 的方程为 $3{x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = \lambda \left( {\lambda  > 0}\right)$ ，由题意， ${AP}\bot {AQ}$ 且 ${\left| AP\right| }^{2} = {\left| AQ\right| }^{2}$ ,任取 ${C}_{1}$ 上一点 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ (不与点 $A$ 重合), 则 ${\left| AP\right| }^{2} = {\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2},{k}_{AP} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}}$ ,设 $Q\left( {{x}_{Q},{y}_{Q}}\right)$ ,则 ${\left| AQ\right| }^{2} = {\left( {x}_{Q} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2} + {y}_{Q}^{2}$ ,直线 ${AQ}$ 的方程为 $x = \dfrac{1}{{k}_{AQ}}y +  \sqrt{3} =  - {k}_{AP}y + \sqrt{3}$ ,故 ${x}_{Q} - \sqrt{3} =  - {k}_{AP}{y}_{Q}$ ,代入得 ${\left| AQ\right| }^{2} =  {\left( {x}_{Q} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2} + {y}_{Q}^{2} = \left( {{k}_{AP}^{2} + 1}\right) {y}_{Q}^{2} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2}}{{\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}}{y}_{Q}^{2} =  \dfrac{{\left| AP\right| }^{2}}{{\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}}{y}_{Q}^{2}$ ,因为 ${\left| AP\right| }^{2} = {\left| AQ\right| }^{2}$ ,解得 ${y}_{Q}^{2} = {\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}$ , 由对称性,不妨设 ${y}_{Q} = {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}$ ,代回直线 ${AQ}$ 方程可解得 $Q\left( {\sqrt{3} - {y}_{0},{x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}}\right)$ ,而点 $Q$ 位于 ${C}_{2}$ 上,所以 $\lambda  = 3{\left( \sqrt{3} - {y}_{0}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( {x}_{0} - \sqrt{3}\right) }^{2} = 3\left( {{y}_{0}^{2} - 2\sqrt{3}{y}_{0} + 3}\right)  + {x}_{0}^{2} - 2\sqrt{3}{x}_{0} + 3 = \left( {{x}_{0}^{2} + 3{y}_{0}^{2}}\right)  +  {12} - 2\sqrt{3}\left( {{x}_{0} + 3{y}_{0}}\right) ,P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 为 ${C}_{1}$ 上任一点,所以 ${x}_{0}^{2} + 3{y}_{0}^{2} =$ 3 为定值,化简得 $\lambda  = {15} - 2\sqrt{3}\left( {{x}_{0} + 3{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,设 ${x}_{0} + 3{y}_{0} = b$ , $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 为 ${C}_{1}$ 上任一点,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{3} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 1 \\  {x}_{0} + 3{y}_{0} = b \end{array}\right.$ 有解,整理得 ${12}{y}_{0}^{2} -  {6b}{y}_{0} + {b}^{2} - 3 = 0,\Delta  = {36}{b}^{2} - {48}\left( {{b}^{2} - 3}\right)  = {12}\left( {{12} - {b}^{2}}\right)  \geq  0$ ,解得 $b \in  \left\lbrack  {-2\sqrt{3},2\sqrt{3}}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 $\lambda  = {15} - 2\sqrt{3}b \in  \left\lbrack  {3,{27}}\right\rbrack$ . 故 ${C}_{2}$ 的长轴长 $2\sqrt{\lambda } \in  \left\lbrack  {2\sqrt{3},6\sqrt{3}}\right\rbrack$ .

32. (1) 设椭圆的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1,a > 0,b > 0$ ,由已知可得 $c = 1$ ，因为点 $P\left( {-1,\dfrac{3}{2}}\right)$ 在椭圆上，所以 $\dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{9}{4{b}^{2}} =$ 1,又 ${a}^{2} = {b}^{2} + 1$ ,所以 ${a}^{2} = 4,{b}^{2} = 3$ ,所以椭圆方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} +  \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,所以 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ;

(2) \ding{172}  $P{F}_{2} = \dfrac{5}{2}$ ，直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的方程为 $y =  - \dfrac{3}{4}x + \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,因为 $\bigtriangleup {PQ}{F}_{2}$ 的面积为 3,所以 $P{F}_{2}$ 边上的高为 $\dfrac{12}{5}$ ,过 $Q$ 做 $P{F}_{2}$ 的平行线,则直线 ${QR}$ 的方程式为 $y =  - \dfrac{3}{4}x - \dfrac{9}{4}$ ,联立方程组 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  y =  - \dfrac{3}{4}x - \dfrac{9}{4} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得: $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} =  - 1 \\  {y}_{1} =  - \dfrac{3}{2} \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} =  - \dfrac{11}{7} \\  {y}_{1} =  - \dfrac{15}{14} \end{array}\right.$ , 所以点 $Q$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-1, - \dfrac{3}{2}}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-\dfrac{11}{7}, - \dfrac{15}{14}}\right)$ ;

(3) \ding{172}  若 ${AB}$ 或 ${CD}$ 垂直于 $x$ 轴，则 $\left| {AB}\right|  + \left| {CD}\right|  = 7$ ， \ding{173}  若 ${AB}$ 和 ${CD}$ 不垂直于 $x$ 轴,设直线 ${AB}$ 的解析式为 $y = k(x +$ 1),点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,联立方程组 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  y = k\left( {x + 1}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + 8{k}^{2}x + 4{k}^{2} - {12} = 0$ ,从而 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-8{k}^{2}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}$ , ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{k}^{2} - {12}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}},\left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}} \cdot  \sqrt{\left( \dfrac{-8{k}^{2}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right)  - 4 \cdot  \dfrac{4{k}^{2} - {12}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}}}  = \dfrac{{12}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{3 + 4{k}^{2}}$ ,同理 $\left| {CD}\right|  = \dfrac{{12}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{4 + 3{k}^{2}}$ ,

$\left| {AB}\right|  + \left| {CD}\right|  = \dfrac{{12}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{3 + 4{k}^{2}} + \dfrac{{12}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{4 + 3{k}^{2}} = \dfrac{{84}\left( {{k}^{4} + 2{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{{12}{k}^{4} + {25}{k}^{2} + {12}} =  7 \cdot  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{{12}{k}^{2} + \dfrac{12}{{k}^{2}} + {25}}}\right)$ ,因为 ${12}{k}^{2} + \dfrac{12}{{k}^{2}} + {25} \geq  {49}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{48}{7} \leq  \left| {AB}\right|  + \left| {CD}\right|  < 7$ ,综上, $\left| {AB}\right|  + \left| {CD}\right|$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{48}{7},7}\right\rbrack$ .

33. ( 1 )抛物线 ${C}_{2}$ 的焦点为 $F\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，故 $p = 2$ ；

(2)若直线 ${MN}$ 与 $y$ 轴重合，则该直线与抛物线 ${C}_{2}$ 只有一个公共点,不合乎题意,所以直线 ${MN}$ 的斜率存在,设直线 ${MN}$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + 1$ ,点 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} = {4y} \end{array}\right.$ 可得 ${x}^{2} - {4kx} - 4 = 0,\Delta  = {16}{k}^{2} + {16} > 0$ 恒成立, 则 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 4,\vv{OM} \cdot  \vv{ON} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} \cdot  \dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4} =  - 4 + 1 =  - 3$

(3)设直线 ${NO},{MO}$ 的斜率分别为 ${k}_{1},{k}_{2}$ ，其中 ${k}_{1} > 0,{k}_{2} < 0$ ， 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {k}_{1}x \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ 可得 $\left( {4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} = 4$ ,解得 $x =  \pm  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}}$ , 点 $A$ 在第三象限,则 ${x}_{A} =  - \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}}$ ,点 $B$ 在第四象限,同理可得 ${x}_{B} = \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}}$ ,且 ${k}_{1}{k}_{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}{16} =  - \dfrac{1}{4},\dfrac{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {OMN}}}{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {OAB}}} =  \dfrac{\left| {OM}\right|  \cdot  \left| {ON}\right| }{\left| {OB}\right|  \cdot  \left| {OA}\right| } = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{1}\right|  \cdot  \left| {x}_{2}\right| }{\dfrac{2}{\sqrt{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}} \cdot  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}}} = \sqrt{\left( {4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\right) }  = \sqrt{4{k}_{1}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{4{k}_{1}^{2}} + 2} \geq  \sqrt{2\sqrt{4{k}_{1}^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{4{k}_{1}^{2}}} + 2} = 2$ ,当且仅当 ${k}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时，等号成立. 故所求的取值范围为 $\lbrack 2, + \infty )$ .

34.(1)由题意得， $a = 2$ ， $c = 1$ ，所以 ${a}^{2} = 4$ ， ${b}^{2} = 3$ ，所以椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ;

(2)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ ，则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + {x}_{3} =  0,\left| {FA}\right|  = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{1},\left| {FB}\right|  = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{2},\left| {FC}\right|  = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{3}$ ,所以 $\left| {FA}\right|  + \left| {FB}\right|  + \left| {FC}\right|  = 6$ ;

(3)设 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ， $Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ， $N\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ ， $P\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ， $T\left( {1,1}\right)$ ，因为 $\vv{MT} = 3\vv{TQ}$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1 - {x}_{1} = 3\left( {{x}_{2} - 1}\right) \\  1 - {y}_{1} = 3\left( {{y}_{2} - 1}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{2} = \dfrac{4 - {x}_{1}}{3} \\  {y}_{2} = \dfrac{4 - {y}_{1}}{3} \end{array}\right.$ ,又 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 都在椭圆上,所以 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{3} = 1,\dfrac{1}{4}{\left( \dfrac{4 - {x}_{1}}{3}\right) }^{2}  + \dfrac{1}{3}{\left( \dfrac{4 - {y}_{1}}{3}\right) }^{2} = 1$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  \dfrac{1}{4}{\left( 4 - {x}_{1}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}{\left( 4 - {y}_{1}\right) }^{2} = 9 \end{array}\right.$ ,于是, $\dfrac{1}{4}\left( {4 - 2{x}_{1}}\right)  \cdot  4 + \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {4 - 2{y}_{1}}\right)  \cdot  4 = 8$ ,即 $\dfrac{1}{4}\left( {2 - {x}_{1}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{3}(2 -  \left. {y}_{1}\right)  = 1$ ,又 $\vv{NT} = 3\vv{TP}$ ,同理得 $\dfrac{1}{4}\left( {2 - {x}_{3}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {2 - {y}_{3}}\right)  = 1$ , 所以直线 ${MN}$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{1}{4}\left( {2 - x}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {2 - y}\right)  = 1$ ,即 ${3x} +  {4y} - 2 = 0.$

35. (1) 由椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,得 ${a}^{2} = 4,{b}^{2} = 3,{c}^{2} = 1$ ,所以 $a = 2,c = 1$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup A{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 的周长为 ${2a} + {2c} = 6$ ;

(2)设 $Q\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{0} > 0,{y}_{0} > 0}\right)$ ，由 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABQ}} > {S}_{\bigtriangleup {F}_{1}{F}_{2}Q}$ ，得 $\dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right| \left| {x}_{0}\right|  > \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right| \left| {y}_{0}\right|$ ,所以 $\sqrt{3}\left| {x}_{0}\right|  > \left| {y}_{0}\right|$ ,即 $3{x}_{0}^{2} > {y}_{0}^{2}$ ,又因为 $\dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,所以 $0 < {y}_{0}^{2} = 3 - \dfrac{3{x}_{0}^{2}}{4} < 3{x}_{0}^{2}$ ,解得 $\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5} < {x}_{0} < 2$ ,即点 $Q$ 的横坐标的取值范围为 $\left( {\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5},2}\right)$ ;

(3) $C\left( {-2,0}\right)$ ， $D\left( {2,0}\right)$ ，设直线 ${CP}$ 的方程为 $y = {3k}\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ,直线 ${DQ}$ 的方程为 $y = k\left( {x - 2}\right) ,k \neq  0$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  y = k\left( {x - 2}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,消 $y$ 得 $\left( {4{k}^{2} + 3}\right) {x}^{2} -  {16}{k}^{2}x + {16}{k}^{2} - {12} = 0$ ,则 $2{x}_{Q} = \dfrac{{16}{k}^{2} - {12}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}$ ,所以 ${x}_{Q} =  \dfrac{8{k}^{2} - 6}{4{k}^{2} + 3}$ ,所以 ${y}_{Q} = k\left( {\dfrac{8{k}^{2} - 6}{4{k}^{2} + 3} - 2}\right)  = \dfrac{-{12k}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}$ ,故 $Q\left( {\dfrac{8{k}^{2} - 6}{4{k}^{2} + 3},}\right.  \dfrac{-{12k}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  y = {3k}\left( {x + 2}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,消 $y$ 得 $\left( {{12}{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} + {48}{k}^{2}x +  {48}{k}^{2} - 4 = 0$ ,则 $- 2{x}_{P} = \dfrac{{48}{k}^{2} - 4}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,所以 ${x}_{P} = \dfrac{-{24}{k}^{2} + 2}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,所以 ${y}_{P}  = {3k}\left( {\dfrac{-{24}{k}^{2} + 2}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1} + 2}\right)  = \dfrac{12k}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,故 $P\left( {\dfrac{-{24}{k}^{2} + 2}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1},\dfrac{12k}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}}\right)$ , 当 ${x}_{P} \neq  {x}_{Q}$ ,即 ${k}^{2} \neq  \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时, ${k}_{PQ} = \dfrac{\dfrac{-{12k}}{4{k}^{2} + 3} - \dfrac{12k}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}}{\dfrac{8{k}^{2} - 6}{4{k}^{2} + 3} - \dfrac{-{24}{k}^{2} + 2}{{12}{k}^{2} + 1}} = \dfrac{4k}{1 - 4{k}^{2}}$ , 则直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为 $y + \dfrac{-{12k}}{4{k}^{2} + 3} = \dfrac{4k}{1 - 4{k}^{2}}\left( {x - \dfrac{8{k}^{2} - 6}{4{k}^{2} + 3}}\right)$ ,即 $y = \dfrac{4k}{1 - 4{k}^{2}}\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,过定点 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ; 当 ${x}_{P} = {x}_{Q}$ ,即 ${k}^{2} =  \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时,此时 ${x}_{P} = {x}_{Q} =  - 1$ ,直线 ${PQ}$ 过定点 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ,设 $H\left( {x,y}\right)$ ,因为 ${AH} \bot  {PQ},{AO} \bot  O{F}_{1}$ ,所以过点 $A,H,O(O$ 为坐标原点) 三点的圆即为过点 $A,{F}_{1},O\left( O\right.$ 为坐标原点 $)\text{ 三 }$ 点的圆,因为 $\left( {x + 1}\right) x + y\left( {y - \sqrt{3}}\right)  = 0$ 过原点,点 ${F}_{1}( - 1$ , $0)$ ,点 $A\left( {0,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ ,所以过点 $A,H,O(O$ 为坐标原点) 三点的圆是定圆 ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( y - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right) }^{2} = 1$ .

36. (1) $K : {x}^{2} = {4y}$ 焦点 $F\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，椭圆 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ( $a >  b > 0)$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},e = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{e}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}},c = 1 \Longrightarrow  {a}^{2} = 2$ , ${b}^{2} = 1,\Gamma  : \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} + {x}^{2} = 1;$

(2) \ding{172}  当直线斜率为 0 时，符合题意，此时 $\left| {AC}\right|  = \left| {BD}\right|$ ；  \ding{173}  当直线斜率存在且不为 0 时，设直线方程为:

$l : y = {kx} + 1,\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + 1 \\  \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} + {x}^{2} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {{k}^{2} + 2}\right) {x}^{2} + {2kx} - 1 = 0,}\right.  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{2k}{{k}^{2} + 2} \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{-1}{{k}^{2} + 2} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  = }\right.  \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\sqrt{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{2\sqrt{2}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{{k}^{2} + 2} < 2\sqrt{2},  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} = {4y} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} - {4kx} - 4 = 0,\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{3} + {x}_{4} =  - {4k} \\  {x}_{3}{x}_{4} =  - 4 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left| {CD}\right|  = }\right. }\right.  \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\left| {{x}_{3} - {x}_{4}}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\sqrt{{\left( {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{3}{x}_{4}} =  \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\sqrt{{16}{k}^{2} + {16}} = 4\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right)  > 4,\left| {AC}\right|  - \left| {BD}\right|  =  \left( {\left| {AC}\right|  + \left| {AD}\right| }\right)  - \left( {\left| {BD}\right|  + \left| {AD}\right| }\right)  = \left| {CD}\right|  - \left| {AB}\right|  > 0 \Longrightarrow  \left| {AC}\right|  > \left| {BD}\right|$ . 综上所述: $\left| {AC}\right|  \geq  \left| {BD}\right|$ ;

(3)当直线 ${AB}$ 斜率不存在时,此时 $\left| {AB}\right|  = {2a} = 2\sqrt{2},{x}^{2} =  4 \times  1,x =  \pm  2,\left| {EG}\right|  = 4,{S}_{AEBD} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right|  \times  \left| {EG}\right|  = 4\sqrt{2}$ ; 当直线 ${AB}$ 斜率存在时且 $k \neq  0,{l}_{EG} : y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}x + 1$ , $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}x + 1 \\  {x}^{2} = {4y} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + \dfrac{4}{k}x - 4 = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left| {EG}\right|  = 4\left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}^{2}}}\right) ,}\right.  {S}_{AEBD} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right|  \times  \left| {EG}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2\sqrt{2}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{{k}^{2} + 2} \times  4\left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}^{2}}}\right)  =  4\sqrt{2} \times  \dfrac{{\left( {k}^{2} + 1\right) }^{2}}{{k}^{2}\left( {{k}^{2} + 2}\right) } = 4\sqrt{2} \times  \dfrac{{k}^{4} + 2{k}^{2} + 1}{{k}^{4} + 2{k}^{2}} > 4\sqrt{2}$ ,综上所述,四边形 ${AEBG}$ 面积的最小值为 $4\sqrt{2}$ .

37. ( 1 )由题意可知， $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = \sqrt{{4}^{2} - {3}^{2}} = 1$ ，所以 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,又因为当直线 $l$ 垂直于 $x$ 轴时,直线 $l$ 的方程为 $x =$ 1,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得, $y =  \pm  \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,所以弦 ${AB}$ 的长为 $2 \times  \dfrac{3}{2} = 3$ ; (2)因为 $\angle {PAB} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ，且直线 $l$ 过点 $F$ ，所以 $\angle {PAF} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ， 在 Rt $\bigtriangleup {PAF}$ 中, $P\left( {-3,0}\right) ,F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,所以斜边 ${PF}$ 的中点 $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ,恰为椭圆的左焦点 ${F}^{\prime }$ ,所以 $A{F}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2}{PF} = 2$ ,又由椭圆的定义可得 ${AF} = {2a} - {A{F}^{\prime }} = 4 - 2 = 2$ ，所以点 $A$ 在线段 $F{F}^{\prime }$ 的垂直平分线上，又 $A$ 在椭圆上，所以 $A$ 为椭圆的上顶点或下顶点,所以 $A\left( {0, - \sqrt{3}}\right)$ 或 $A\left( {0,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ ;

(3)当直线 ${AB}$ 的斜率不存在时,不妨设 $A\left( {1,\dfrac{3}{2}}\right) ,B(1$ , $\left. {-\dfrac{3}{2}}\right)$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} = \dfrac{0 - \dfrac{3}{2}}{3 - 1} =  - \dfrac{3}{4},{k}_{2} = \dfrac{0 + \dfrac{3}{2}}{3 - 1} = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,故 ${k}_{1} +  {k}_{2} = 0$ ; 当直线 ${AB}$ 的斜率存在时,设斜率为 $k$ ,则直线 ${AB}$ : $y = k\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = k\left( {x - 1}\right) \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得, $\left( {3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - 8{k}^{2}x + \left( {4{k}^{2} - {12}}\right)  = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  \dfrac{8{k}^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 3},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{k}^{2} - {12}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} - 3} + \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} - 3} =  \dfrac{k\left( {{x}_{1} - 1}\right) }{{x}_{1} - 3} + \dfrac{k\left( {{x}_{2} - 1}\right) }{{x}_{2} - 3} = \dfrac{k\left( {2{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 4\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 6}\right) }{{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 3\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 9}$ ,化简得 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = \dfrac{k\left( \dfrac{8{k}^{2} - {24} - {32}{k}^{2} + {24}{k}^{2} + {18}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}\right) }{\dfrac{4{k}^{2} - {12}}{4{k}^{2} + 3} - \dfrac{{24}{k}^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 3}} =  - \dfrac{6k}{{16}{k}^{2} + {15}},$  \ding{172}  当 $k > 0,0 > {k}_{1} + {k}_{2} =  - \dfrac{6}{{16k} + \dfrac{15}{k}} \geq   - \dfrac{6}{2\sqrt{{16k} \times  \dfrac{15}{k}}} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{20}$ ,当且仅当 $k = \dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{4}$ 时等式成立;  \ding{173}  当 $k < 0,0 <  {k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = \dfrac{6}{-\left( {{16k} + \dfrac{15}{k}}\right) } \leq  \dfrac{6}{2\sqrt{\left( -{16}k\right) } \times  \left( {-\dfrac{15}{k}}\right) } = \dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{20}$ ,当且仅当 $k =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{4}$ 时等式成立;  \ding{174}  当 $k = 0,{k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = 0$ ; 综上所述可得, ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2}$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{20},\dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{20}}\right\rbrack$ .

38.(1)由题意， $a = \sqrt{2}$ ， $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}} = 1$ ，所以离心率， $e =  \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$

(2)由题意， $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ， $A\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，所以直线 ${AF}$ 的方程为: $y =  - x + 1$ ,设 $R\left( {{x}_{0}, - {x}_{0} + 1}\right)$ ,显然 $\angle {BAR} = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ , \ding{172}  当 $\angle {ABR} =  \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ 时, $\vv{BA} = \left( {0,2}\right) ,\vv{BR} = \left( {{x}_{0}, - {x}_{0} + 2}\right)$ ,由 $\cos \angle {ABR} =  \dfrac{\vv{BA} \cdot  \vv{BR}}{\left| \vv{BA}\right| \left| \vv{BR}\right| } = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,得: ${x}_{0}^{2} - 6{x}_{0} + 6 = 0$ ,解得 ${x}_{0} = 3 + \sqrt{3}$ (舍去) 或 ${x}_{0} = 3 - \sqrt{3}$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $\angle {ARB} = \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ 时, $\vv{RA} = \left( {-{x}_{0},{x}_{0}}\right)$ , $\vv{RB} = \left( {-{x}_{0},{x}_{0} - 2}\right)$ ,由 $\cos \angle {ABR} = \dfrac{\vv{BA} \cdot  \vv{BR}}{\left| \vv{BA}\right| \left| \vv{BR}\right| } = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,得: $3{x}_{0}^{2} - 6{x}_{0} + 2 = 0$ ,解得 ${x}_{0} = 1 - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}$ (舍去)或 ${x}_{0} = 1 + \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}$ ,综上所述,点 $R$ 的坐标是 $\left( {3 - \sqrt{3}, - 2 + \sqrt{3}}\right)$ 或 $\left( {1 + \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}, - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ ;

(3)假设存在定点 $M\left( {m,0}\right)$ 满足题意，当 ${PQ}$ 的斜率存在时,设直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + b,P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ , 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} + {y}^{2} = 1 \\  y = {kx} + b \end{array}\right.$ 得 $\left( {1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {4kbx} + 2{b}^{2} - 2 = 0$ ,由题意, $\Delta  = {16}{k}^{2}{b}^{2} - 4\left( {1 + 2{k}^{2}}\right) \left( {2{b}^{2} - 2}\right)  > 0$ ,即 $2{k}^{2} - {b}^{2} + 1 > 0$  \ding{172} . ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{4kb}{1 + 2{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{2{b}^{2} - 2}{1 + 2{k}^{2}},{k}_{AP} + {k}_{AQ} = \dfrac{{y}_{1} - 1}{{x}_{1}} +  \dfrac{{y}_{2} - 1}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{2}\left( {k{x}_{1} + b}\right)  - {x}_{2} + {x}_{1}\left( {k{x}_{2} + b}\right)  - {x}_{1}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = {2k} +  \dfrac{\left( {b - 1}\right) \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) }{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = {2k} - \dfrac{\left( {b - 1}\right)  \cdot  {2kb}}{2\left( {{b}^{2} - 1}\right) } = \dfrac{2k}{b + 1} = 2$ ,所以 $k =  b + 1$ ,代入 \ding{172} ,得: ${b}^{2} + {4b} + 3 > 0$ ,所以 $b <  - 3$ 或 $b >  - 1$ ,即存在直线 ${PQ}$ 使得直线 ${AP}$ 与直线 ${AQ}$ 的斜率之和为 2 . 直线 ${PQ}$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + k - 1$ ,直线 ${AH}$ 的方程为 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}x + 1$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + k - 1 \\  y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}x + 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,得: $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = \dfrac{{2k} - {k}^{2}}{{k}^{2} + 1} \\  y = \dfrac{k - 2}{{k}^{2} + 1} + 1 \end{array}\right.$ , 即 $H\left( {\dfrac{{2k} - {k}^{2}}{{k}^{2} + 1},\dfrac{k - 2}{{k}^{2} + 1} + 1}\right)$ ,所以 ${\left| \vv{MH}\right| }^{2} = {\left( m - \dfrac{{2k} - {k}^{2}}{{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( \dfrac{k - 2}{{k}^{2} + 1} + 1\right) }^{2} = {m}^{2} + 1 + \dfrac{\left( {k - 2}\right) \left( {{2m} + 1}\right) k}{{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,所以当 $m =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, $\left| \vv{MH}\right|$ 为定值 $\dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}$ ; 当直线 ${PQ}$ 斜率不存在时,设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 ${k}_{AP} + {k}_{AQ} = \dfrac{{y}_{0} - 1}{{x}_{0}} + \dfrac{-{y}_{0} - 1}{{x}_{0}} =  2,{x}_{0} =  - 1$ ,此时 $H\left( {-1,1}\right) ,\left| \vv{MH}\right|  = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}$ 满足题意. 所以存在定点 $P\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2},0}\right)$ ,使得 $\left| \vv{MH}\right|$ 为定值且定值为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}$ .

39. (1)依题意， ${F}_{1}\left( {-2,0}\right)$ ， ${F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ ，而 $P\left( {2,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ，则直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{4}\left( {x + 2}\right)$ ,即 $x - 2\sqrt{2}y + 2 = 0$ ,所以点 ${F}_{2}$ 到直线 $l$ 的距离 $d = \dfrac{\left| 2 - 2\sqrt{2} \times  0 + 2\right| }{\sqrt{{1}^{2} + {\left( -2\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2}}} = \dfrac{4}{3}$ ;

(2)由 $P{F}_{1} \bot  P{F}_{2}$ ，得点 $P$ 在以线段 ${F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 为直径的圆 ${x}^{2} +  {y}^{2} = {c}^{2}$ 上, ${c}^{2} = {a}^{2} - {b}^{2}$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {c}^{2} \\  {b}^{2}{x}^{2} + {a}^{2}{y}^{2} = {a}^{2}{b}^{2} \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $y$ 得 $\left( {{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} = {a}^{2}\left( {{c}^{2} - {b}^{2}}\right)$ ,即 ${c}^{2}{x}^{2} = {a}^{2}\left( {{c}^{2} - {b}^{2}}\right)$ ,当 $b = c$ 时, $x = 0,y =  \pm  b$ ,因此点 $P\left( {0, \pm  b}\right)$ ,共 2 个; 当 $b < c$ 时, ${c}^{2}{x}^{2} = {a}^{2}\left( {{c}^{2} - {b}^{2}}\right)$ ,解得 ${x}^{2} = \dfrac{{a}^{2}\left( {{c}^{2} - {b}^{2}}\right) }{{c}^{2}},{y}^{2} = \dfrac{{b}^{4}}{{c}^{2}}$ ,因此点 $P\left( {\pm \dfrac{a\sqrt{{c}^{2} - {b}^{2}}}{c}, \pm  \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{c}}\right)$ ,共 4 个,所以当 $b = c$ 时,点 $P$ 的个数为 2; 当 $b < c$ 时,点 $P$ 的个数为 4; (3)设 $P\left( {\dfrac{c}{2},{y}_{P}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{Q},{y}_{Q}}\right)$ ，由 $\vv{{F}_{1}Q} = \lambda \vv{QP}$ ,且 ${F}_{1}$ 在线段 ${PQ}$ 上,得 $- 1 < \lambda  < 0$ ,则 $\left( {{x}_{Q} + c,{y}_{Q}}\right)  = \lambda \left( {\dfrac{c}{2} - }\right.  {x}_{Q},{y}_{P} - {y}_{Q}$ ),解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{Q} = \dfrac{c}{2} \cdot  \dfrac{\lambda  - 2}{\lambda  + 1} \\  {y}_{Q} = \dfrac{\lambda }{\lambda  + 1}{y}_{P} \end{array}\right.$ ,而 $e = \dfrac{c}{a}$ ,由点 $P,Q$ 在 $\Gamma$ 上,得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{4{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}_{P}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \\  \dfrac{{c}^{2}{\left( \lambda  - 2\right) }^{2}}{4{a}^{2}{\left( \lambda  + 1\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{{\lambda }^{2}}{{\left( \lambda  + 1\right) }^{2}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{P}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ , 即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{e}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}_{P}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \\  \dfrac{{e}^{2}{\left( \lambda  - 2\right) }^{2}}{4{\lambda }^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}_{P}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = \dfrac{{\left( \lambda  + 1\right) }^{2}}{{\lambda }^{2}} \end{array}\right.$ ,整理得 ${e}^{2}\left( {-\lambda  + 1}\right)  = {2\lambda } + 1$ , 即 ${e}^{2} = \dfrac{{2\lambda } + 1}{-\lambda  + 1}$ ,由 $e > \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,得 $\dfrac{1}{4} < \dfrac{{2\lambda } + 1}{-\lambda  + 1} < 1$ ,解得 $- \dfrac{1}{3} <  \lambda  < 0$ ,所以 $\lambda$ 的取值范围是 $- \dfrac{1}{3} < \lambda  < 0$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/76_1340_1183_236_193_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

40. (1) 由题可知,直线为 $x = 1$ ,代入椭圆方程 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ , 解得 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{2}$ ,所以 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {OPQ}} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \sqrt{6} \times  1 = \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{2}$ ；

(2)设 $M\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ，当 ${x}_{0} =  \pm  2$ 时， ${y}_{0} =  \pm  \sqrt{2}$ ，不妨取 $M(2$ ， $\sqrt{2}),A\left( {2,0}\right) ,B\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\vv{AM} = \left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right) ,\vv{BM} = \left( {2,0}\right)$ ,所以 $\vv{AM} \cdot  \vv{BM} = 0$ ,即 ${MA} \bot  {MB}$ 成立; 当 ${x}_{0} \neq   \pm  2$ 时,设 ${MA}$ 、 ${MB}$ 的斜率分别为 ${k}_{1},{k}_{2}$ ,直线 ${MA} : y - {y}_{0} = k\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y - {y}_{0} = k\left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right) \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} + {4k}\left( {{y}_{0} - k{x}_{0}}\right) x + 2\left( {k{x}_{0} - }\right. }\right.  {\left. {y}_{0}\right) }^{2} - 4 = 0$ ,因为直线 ${MA}$ 与椭圆相切,所以 $\Delta  = 0$ ,即 ${16}{k}^{2}{\left( k{x}_{0} - {y}_{0}\right) }^{2} - 4\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right) \left\lbrack  {2{\left( k{x}_{0} - {y}_{0}\right) }^{2} - 4}\right\rbrack   = 0$ ,化简可得 ${\left( k{x}_{0} - {y}_{0}\right) }^{2} - 2\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  = 0$ ,化为关于 $k$ 的一元二次方程为 $\left( {{x}_{0}^{2} - 4}\right) {k}^{2} - 2{x}_{0}{y}_{0}k + {y}_{0}^{2} - 2 = 0$ , 所以 ${k}_{1} \cdot  {k}_{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2} - 2}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 4}$ . 因为 $M\left( {x}_{0}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{0}\right)$ 在圆上,所以 ${x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 6$ ,代入上式可得 ${k}_{1} \cdot  {k}_{2} = \dfrac{6 - {x}_{0}^{2} - 2}{{x}_{0}^{2} - 4} =  - 1$ ,综上可得 ${MA} \bot  {MB}$ ；

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/77_580_515_239_226_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3) 设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 、 $R\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{4}}\right) ,S\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ,直线 ${PN},{QN}$ 的斜率分别为 ${k}_{PN}$ 、 ${k}_{QN}$ ,设直线 ${l}_{1} : x = {ky} + 1$ ,与椭圆联立得 $\left( {{k}^{2} + 2}\right) {y}^{2} + {2ky} -  3 = 0$ ,则 $\Delta  > 0,{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{2k}{{k}^{2} + 2},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{3}{{k}^{2} + 2}$ ,由 $\angle {ONP} = \angle {ONQ}$ 得 ${k}_{PN} + {k}_{QN} = 0$ ,即 ${k}_{PN} + {k}_{QN} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} - n} +  \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} - n} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}\left( {k{y}_{2} + 1 - n}\right)  + {y}_{2}\left( {k{y}_{1} + 1 - n}\right) }{\left( {k{y}_{1} + 1 - n}\right) \left( {k{y}_{2} + 1 - n}\right) } = 0$ ,计算分子部分: ${y}_{1}\left( {k{y}_{2} + 1 - n}\right)  + {y}_{2}\left( {k{y}_{1} + 1 - n}\right)  = {2k}{y}_{1}{y}_{2} + \left( {1 - n}\right)  \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  = {2k} \cdot  \left( {-\dfrac{3}{{k}^{2} + 2}}\right)  + \left( {1 - n}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{-{2k}}{{k}^{2} + 2} = \dfrac{-{8k} + {2kn}}{{k}^{2} + 2}  = 0$ ,所以 $n = 4$ ,设直线 ${l}_{2} : x = {py} + 4$ ,与双曲线联立得 $\left( {{p}^{2} - 4}\right) {y}^{2} + {8py} + {12} = 0$ ,则 ${p}^{2} - 4 \neq  0,\Delta  > 0,{y}_{3} + {y}_{4} =  - \dfrac{8p}{{p}^{2} - 4},{y}_{3}{y}_{4} = \dfrac{12}{{p}^{2} - 4}$ ,所以 ${k}_{TR} + {k}_{TS} = \dfrac{{y}_{3}}{{x}_{3} - 1} + \dfrac{{y}_{4}}{{x}_{4} - 1} =  \dfrac{{y}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4} - 1}\right)  + {y}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3} - 1}\right) }{\left( {{x}_{3} - 1}\right) \left( {{x}_{4} - 1}\right) }$ ,计算分子部分 ${y}_{3}\left( {{x}_{4} - 1}\right)  + {y}_{4}\left( {{x}_{3} - }\right.  1) = {y}_{3}\left( {p{y}_{4} + 3}\right)  + {y}_{4}\left( {p{y}_{3} + 3}\right)  = {2p}{y}_{3}{y}_{4} + 3\left( {{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}\right)  =  {2p} \cdot  \dfrac{12}{{p}^{2} - 4} + 3 \cdot  \dfrac{-{8p}}{{p}^{2} - 4} = 0$ ,所以 ${k}_{TR} + {k}_{TS} = 0$ ,综上可得 $n = 4,{k}_{TR} + {k}_{TS} = 0$ 均为定值.

41. (1) 椭圆的离心率 $e = \dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - 1}}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ，所以 $a = \sqrt{2}$ ；

(2)显然直线 $P{F}_{1}$ 的斜率是存在的， $a = \sqrt{2}$ ， $b = 1$ ，所以 $c =  \sqrt{2 - 1} = 1$ ,所以 ${F}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right) ,\vv{PQ} = \left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{0},{y}_{1} - {y}_{0}}\right) ,\vv{O{F}_{1}} =  \left( {-1,0}\right) ,\vv{PQ} \cdot  \vv{O{F}_{1}} = {x}_{0} - {x}_{1}$ ,直线 ${k}_{P{F}_{1}} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 1}$ ,过点 ${F}_{1}$ 的直线方程为 $y = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 1}\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,与椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 联立得到 ${\left\lbrack  {\left( {x}_{1} + 1\right) }^{2} + 2{y}_{0}\right\rbrack  }^{2}{y}^{2} - 2{y}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right) y - {y}_{0}^{2} = 1$ ,所以 ${y}_{1}{y}_{0} =  \dfrac{-{y}_{0}^{2}}{{\left( {x}_{1} + 1\right) }^{2} + 2{y}_{0}^{2}}$ ,因为 ${x}_{0}^{2} + 2{y}_{0}^{2} = 2$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} =  - \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{2{x}_{0} + 3}$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{\left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right) {y}_{1}}{{y}_{0}} - 1 =  - \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{2{x}_{0} + 3}\left( \dfrac{{x}_{0} + 1}{{y}_{0}}\right)  - 1 =  - \dfrac{{x}_{0} + 1}{2{x}_{0} + 3} - 1,  \vv{PQ} \cdot  \vv{O{F}_{1}} = {x}_{0} + \dfrac{{x}_{0} + 1}{2{x}_{0} + 3} + 1 = \dfrac{12}{5}$ ,因为 ${x}_{0} > 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{0} = 1$ ; (3) $a = 2$ 时，椭圆方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ，斜率不存在时，过任意点 $T\left( {0,t}\right)$ 的唯一的直线 $L : x = 0$ 与椭圆交于 $M$ 、 $N$ 两点坐标 $\left( {0,1}\right) ,\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ ,此时 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2$ 恒成立; 斜率存在时,设过任意点 $T\left( {0,t}\right)$ 的直线 $L$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + t$ ,与椭圆 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} +  {y}^{2} = 1$ 联立得到 ${\left( 1 + 4k\right) }^{2}{x}^{2} + {8ktx} + 4{t}^{2} - 4 = 0,\Delta  = {64}{k}^{2}{t}^{2}  - 4{\left( 1 + 4k\right) }^{2}\left( {4{t}^{2} - 4}\right)  = {64}{k}^{2} + {16} - {16}{t}^{2} > 0,\left| {MN}\right|  =  \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\dfrac{\sqrt{{64}{k}^{2} + {16} - {16}{t}^{2}}}{1 + 4{k}^{2}} = 2,{12}{k}^{2} + 3 - 4{t}^{2} - 4{k}^{2}{t}^{2} = 0,  t = \sqrt{3}$ 时,方程 ${12}{k}^{2} + 3 - 4{t}^{2} - 4{k}^{2}{t}^{2} = 0$ 方程无解, $t \neq  \sqrt{3}$ 时, ${k}^{2} = \dfrac{4{t}^{2} - 3}{{12} - 4{t}^{2}} \geq  0$ ,当 $t = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ 时,存在直线斜率为 0 的直线 $y = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ,使得 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2$ ; 当 $\dfrac{3}{4} < {t}^{2} < 3$ 时,即 $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} < t < \sqrt{3}$ ,存在 $k =  \pm  \sqrt{\dfrac{4{t}^{2} - 3}{{12} - 4{t}^{2}}}$ 的两条直线,使得 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2$ ; 所以 $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} < t < \sqrt{3}$ 存在 3 条直线,使得 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2;t = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ 存在 2 条直线,使得 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2;t \geq  \sqrt{3}$ 或 $0 \leq  t < \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ 存在 1 条直线,使得 $\left| {MN}\right|  = 2$ . 42. (1)由题意知， $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{5} = 1\left( {a > \sqrt{5}}\right)$ ，则 ${b}^{2} = 5$ ，由右焦点

$\left( {2,0}\right)$ ,可知 $c = 2$ ,则 $a = \sqrt{5 + {c}^{2}} = 3$ ,故离心率 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{2}{3}$ ;

(2)由题意 $A\left( {4,0}\right) ,M\left( {0,m}\right) \left( {m > 0}\right) ,P\left( {{x}_{P},{y}_{P}}\right)$ ,由 $\vv{PA} =  2\vv{MP}$ 得, $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 4 - {x}_{P} = 2{x}_{P} \\   - {y}_{P} = 2{y}_{p} - {2m} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $P\left( {\dfrac{4}{3},\dfrac{2m}{3}}\right)$ ,代入 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{16} +  \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{5} = 1$ ,得 $\dfrac{1}{9} + \dfrac{4{m}^{2}}{45} = 1$ ,又 $m > 0$ ,解得 $m = \sqrt{10}$ ; (3)由线段 ${AM}$ 的中垂线 $l$ 的斜率为 2,所以直线 ${AM}$ 的斜率为 $- \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 $\dfrac{m - 0}{0 - a} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,解得 $m = \dfrac{a}{2}$ ,由 $A(a$ , $0),M\left( {0,\dfrac{a}{2}}\right)$ 得 ${AM}$ 中点坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{a}{2},\dfrac{a}{4}}\right)$ ,故直线 $l : y = {2x} - \dfrac{3}{4}a$ ,显然直线 $l$ 过椭圆内点 $\left( {\dfrac{3}{8}a,0}\right)$ ,故直线与椭圆恒有两不同交点,设 $C\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ , $D\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {2x} - \dfrac{3}{4}a \\  5{x}^{2} + {a}^{2}{y}^{2} = 5{a}^{2} \end{array}\right.$ 消 $y$ 得 $\left( {4{a}^{2} + 5}\right) {x}^{2} - 3{a}^{3}x +  \dfrac{9}{16}{a}^{4} - 5{a}^{2} = 0$ ,由韦达定理得 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{3{a}^{3}}{4{a}^{2} + 5},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  \dfrac{\dfrac{9}{16}{a}^{4} - 5{a}^{2}}{4{a}^{2} + 5}$ ,因为 $\angle {CMD}$ 为钝角,则 $\vv{MC} \cdot  \vv{MD} < 0$ ,且 $M\left( {0,\dfrac{a}{2}}\right)$ ,则有 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + \left( {{y}_{1} - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right) \left( {{y}_{2} - \dfrac{a}{2}}\right)  < 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + \left( {2{x}_{1} - \dfrac{5a}{4}}\right) \left( {2{x}_{2} - \dfrac{5a}{4}}\right)  = 5{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \dfrac{5}{2}a\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  +  \dfrac{25}{16}{a}^{2} < 0$ ,即 $5\left( {\dfrac{9}{16}{a}^{4} - 5{a}^{2}}\right)  - \dfrac{5a}{2} \times  3{a}^{3} + \dfrac{25}{16}{a}^{2}\left( {4{a}^{2} + 5}\right)  < 0$ , 解得 ${a}^{2} < {11}$ ,又 $a > \sqrt{5}$ ,故 $\sqrt{5} < a < \sqrt{11}$ ,即 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {\sqrt{5},\sqrt{11}}\right)$ .

\section*{考点 4 双曲线方程及其性质}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\left( {2,0}\right)$

【解析】 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} - {y}^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {c}^{2} = 4,c = 2,{F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ ,故答案为 $\left( {2,0}\right)$ .

2. $2\sqrt{5}$

【解析】 ${x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {2c} = 2\sqrt{5}$ .

3. $x \pm  \sqrt{2}y = 0$

【解析】 ${x}^{2} - 2{y}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  x \pm  \sqrt{2}y = 0$ .

4. $\dfrac{5}{4}$

【解析】 $\dfrac{32}{16} - \dfrac{9}{m} = 1 \Longrightarrow  m = 9,a = 4,b = 3,c = 5,e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{5}{4}$ . 5. $\sqrt{3}$

【解析】 $a = 1,{c}^{2} = 1 + 2 = 3,c = \sqrt{3},e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{3}$ .

6. $\sqrt{2}$

【解析】两条渐近线互相垂直, 由对称性可知, 两渐近线的倾斜角分别为 $\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{3\pi }{4}$ ,渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  x$ ,故 $a = b$ ,所以双曲线的离心率为 $\dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{{a}^{2}}} = \sqrt{2}$ . 故答案为: $\sqrt{2}$ .

7. $\dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{3}$

【解析】因为 $a = \sqrt{3},b = \sqrt{2}$ ,所以 $c = \sqrt{5}$ ,双曲线 $C$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{3}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{3}$ .

8. $y =  \pm  {2x}$

【解析】双曲线 ${x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1$ 的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{b}{a}x$ 即 $y =  \pm  {2x}$ ,故答案为: $y =  \pm  {2x}$ .

9. $\dfrac{3}{2}$

【解析】 $a = 2,c = 3 \Longrightarrow  e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ .

10. $y =  \pm  x$

【解析】由题知: $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{2} \\  {c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  a = b}\right.$ ,双曲线的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  x$ ,故答案为 $y =  \pm  x$ .

11. $\dfrac{3}{5}$

【解析】依题意,知两条渐近线为 ${2x} \pm  y = 0 \Longrightarrow  {\vv{n}}_{1} = \left( {2,1}\right)$ , ${\vv{n}}_{2} = \left( {2, - 1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  \cos \theta  = \dfrac{{\vv{n}}_{1} \cdot  {\vv{n}}_{2}}{\left| {\vv{n}}_{1}\right|  \cdot  \left| {\vv{n}}_{2}\right| } = \dfrac{3}{5}.$

12. 3

【解析】由双曲线的定义, ${2c} = 6,{2a} = 7 - 5 = 2 \Longrightarrow  a = 1,c =$ 3, $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = 3$ .

13. $\dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}$

【解析】显然 ${P}_{1}\left( {4, - 3}\right) ,{P}_{3}\left( {-4,3}\right) ,{P}_{4}\left( {-2,0}\right)$ 在双曲线上,显然 $a = 2$ ,设双曲线方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,把 ${P}_{1}\left( {4, - 3}\right)$ 代入双曲线可得 ${b}^{2} = 3,{c}^{2} = 7,c = \sqrt{7}$ ,可得 $e = \dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}$ .

14. $\dfrac{4}{3}$

【解析】双曲线 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{m} = 1$ 的渐近线为 $\sqrt{m}x \pm  {2y} = 0$ ,离心率 $e = \dfrac{\sqrt{4 + m}}{2}$ ,右焦点 $\left( {\sqrt{4 + m},0}\right)$ ,依题意, $\dfrac{\sqrt{m} \cdot  \sqrt{4 + m}}{\sqrt{4 + m}}  = \dfrac{\sqrt{4 + m}}{2}$ ,所以 $m = \dfrac{4}{3}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{4}{3}$ .

15. $\sqrt{13}$

【解析】因为 $\left| {AB}\right|  : \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  : \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = 3 : 4 : 5$ ,设 $\left| {AB}\right|  = {3k}$ , $\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = {4k},\left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = {5k}$ ,由 $\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  -  \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a} \Longrightarrow  \left( {\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  + {3k}}\right)  - {4k} = {2a} \Longrightarrow  \left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  = {2a} + k$ ,由 $\left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  - \left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  =  {2a} \Longrightarrow  \left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  = {5k} - {2a}$ ,所以 ${5k} - {2a} =  {2a} + k \Longrightarrow  k = a$ ,所以 $\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = {4a},\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  =  {3a} \Longrightarrow  \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  = {6a}$ ,在 Rt $\bigtriangleup B{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中, ${\left| B{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} + {\left| B{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} = 4{c}^{2}$ ,即 ${16}{a}^{2} +  {36}{a}^{2} = 4{c}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {c}^{2} = {13}{a}^{2} \Longrightarrow  e = \sqrt{13}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/78_1350_970_227_249_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

16. $\dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{2}$

【解析】双曲线 ${x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 的焦点 ${F}_{1}\left( {-2,0}\right) ,{F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ , 渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \sqrt{3}x$ ,依题意,直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的方程为 $y =  - \sqrt{3}\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \sqrt{3}\left( {x - 2}\right) \\  3{x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 3 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $x = \dfrac{5}{4},y = \dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{4}$ ,则点 $P$ 的坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{5}{4},\dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{4}}\right)$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 的面积为 $\dfrac{1}{2} \times  4 \times  \dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{4} = \dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{3\sqrt{3}}{2}$ .

17. 4

【解析】如图,设 $\left| {{F}_{1}A}\right|  = m$ ,则 $\left| {{F}_{1}B}\right|  = {2m}$ ,根据对称性知 $\left| {OA}\right|  = \left| {OB}\right|$ ,又 $\left| {O{F}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {O{F}_{2}}\right|$ ,所以四边形 $A{F}_{2}B{F}_{1}$ 是平行四边形,所以 $\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = m$ ,因为 $\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2 \Longrightarrow  m = 2$ ,所以 $\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  = 4,\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2$ ,所以在 $\bigtriangleup B{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中, $\cos \angle {F}_{1}B{F}_{2} =  \dfrac{{\left| B{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} + {\left| B{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} - {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2}}{2\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right| } = \dfrac{{16} + 4 - {28}}{2 \cdot  4 \cdot  2} =  - \dfrac{1}{2},$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/78_1277_1960_302_213_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

所以 $\cos \angle A{F}_{2}B = \cos (\pi  -  \left. {\angle {F}_{1}B{F}_{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $\vv{{F}_{2}A}$ .

$\vv{{F}_{2}B} = \left| \vv{{F}_{2}A}\right| \left| \vv{{F}_{2}B}\right| \cos \angle A{F}_{2}B  = 2 \times  4 \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = 4$ .

18. $\sqrt{3}$

【解析】如图, ${c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + 6 - {a}^{2} = 6,\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {AB}\right|  - \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a},{S}_{\bigtriangleup B{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  \cdot  \sin \dfrac{\pi }{3} = \dfrac{1}{2}.  2\sqrt{6} \cdot  {2a} \cdot  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} = 3\sqrt{6} \Longrightarrow  a = \sqrt{3}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_590_244_230_206_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

19. $\sqrt{5}$

【解析】设 $P{F}_{1}$ 中点为 $H$ ,连接 ${OH},P{F}_{2}$ ,如图所示: 在 $\bigtriangleup P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中,因为 $O,H$ 分别为 ${F}_{1}{F}_{2},P{F}_{1}$ 的中点,故 ${OH}//P{F}_{2}$ ,且 $\left| {OH}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|$ ; 由题可知, $\left| {OH}\right|  = a$ ,且 ${OH} \bot  P{F}_{1}$ ,故 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a}$ ,且 $P{F}_{2} \bot  P{F}_{1}$ ; 根据双曲线定义可知, $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = {2a} + \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a} + {2a} = {4a}$ ,又 $\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2c}$ ,故在 $\bigtriangleup P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中,由勾股定理 ${\left| P{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} +  {\left| P{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} = {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2}$ ,也即 ${16}{a}^{2} + 4{a}^{2} =  4{c}^{2}$ ,整理得 $\dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} = 5$ ,故 $\dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{5}$ ,即该双曲线的离心率为 $\sqrt{5}$ . 故答案为: $\sqrt{5}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_578_700_241_203_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

20. $\sqrt{2} + 1$

【解析】由题意,知 ${F}_{2}\left( {\dfrac{p}{2},0}\right)$ ,即 $\dfrac{p}{2} = c \Longrightarrow  p = {2c} \Longrightarrow  {F}_{2}\left( {c,0}\right)$ , ${C}_{2} : {y}^{2} = {4cx}$ ,法一: 因为 $\angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ ,所以直线 $P{F}_{1} :  x = y - c$ ,联立 ${y}^{2} = {4cx}$ ,得 ${y}^{2} = {4cy} - 4{c}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {y}^{2} - {4cy} + 4{c}^{2} = 0  \Longrightarrow  {\left( y - 2c\right) }^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  y = {2c} \Longrightarrow  x = {2c} - c = c \Longrightarrow  P\left( {c,{2c}}\right)$ . 法二: 则抛物线准线为 $l : x =  - c$ , 作 ${PH} \bot  l$ ,垂足为 $H$ ,如图,因为 $\angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ ,所以 $\angle H{F}_{1}P = \dfrac{\pi }{4} \Longrightarrow  \left| {H{F}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {PH}\right|$ ,又因为 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  =  \left| {PH}\right|$ ,所以 $\left| {H{F}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  \Longrightarrow  P{F}_{2} \bot  x$ 轴,得 $P\left( {c,{2c}}\right)$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_576_1156_243_253_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

法 1: $\Longrightarrow  \bigtriangleup P{F}_{2}{F}_{1}$ 是等腰直角三角形 $\Longrightarrow  \left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = \sqrt{2} \cdot  {2c} = 2\sqrt{2}c$ , 由双曲线定义,知 $2\sqrt{2}c - {2c} = {2a} \Longrightarrow  e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{2} - 1} = \sqrt{2} + 1$ .

法 2: 代入 ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{c}^{2} - {a}^{2}} = 1$ ,得 $\dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{4{c}^{2}}{{c}^{2} - {a}^{2}} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {c}^{4} -  {a}^{2}{c}^{2} - 4{a}^{2}{c}^{2} = {a}^{2}{c}^{2} - {a}^{4} \Longrightarrow  {c}^{4} - 6{a}^{2}{c}^{2} + {a}^{4} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {e}^{4} - 6{e}^{2} +  1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  {e}^{2} = \dfrac{6 \pm  \sqrt{32}}{2}$ (负舍) $= 3 + 2\sqrt{2} \Longrightarrow  e = \sqrt{2} + 1.$

21. $\dfrac{4\sqrt{7}}{7}$

【解析】如图所示: 设圆半径为 $6,\left| {AB}\right|  = \left| {BC}\right|  = \left| {CD}\right|  \Longrightarrow  C\left( {2,0}\right) ,M\left( {3\sqrt{2},3\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,该双曲线方程为: $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1,a =  2,\dfrac{{\left( 3\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{\left( 3\sqrt{2}\right) }^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2} = \dfrac{36}{7},  {c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2} = \dfrac{64}{7},{e}^{2} = \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} = \dfrac{16}{7},  e = \dfrac{4\sqrt{7}}{7}.$ 22. $\dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{2}$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_572_1910_247_236_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

【解析】方法1: 如图所示, 设 $\left| {{F}_{1}M}\right|  = {r}_{1},\left| {{F}_{2}M}\right|  = {r}_{2},  \bigtriangleup {F}_{1}M{F}_{2}$ 中, $\Longrightarrow  \cos {120}^{ \circ  } =  \dfrac{{r}_{1}^{2} + {r}_{2}^{2} - {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2}}{2{r}_{1}{r}_{2}} =  - \dfrac{1}{2},{r}_{1}^{2} +  {r}_{2}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} =  - {r}_{1}{r}_{2}$ ,由极化恒等式得 $\vv{M{F}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{M{F}_{2}} =  {r}_{1}{r}_{2}\cos {120}^{ \circ  } = O{M}^{2} - {c}^{2},{OM} = \dfrac{\sqrt{21}}{3}b,{r}_{1}^{2} + {r}_{2}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} =  - {r}_{1}{r}_{2}  \Longrightarrow  {\left( 2a + {r}_{2}\right) }^{2} + {r}_{2}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} =  - \left( {{2a} + {r}_{2}}\right) {r}_{2} \Longrightarrow  3{r}_{2}\left( {{r}_{2} + {2a}}\right)  +  4{a}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} = 0,\left( {{2a} + {r}_{2}}\right) {r}_{2} \times  \left( {-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{21}{9}{b}^{2} - {c}^{2} \Longrightarrow  \left( {{2a} + }\right.  \left. {r}_{2}\right) {r}_{2} = 2{c}^{2} - \dfrac{42}{9}{b}^{2},3 \times  \left( {-\dfrac{{42}{b}^{2}}{9} + 2{c}^{2}}\right)  + 4{a}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  3 \times  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{{42}\left( {{c}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right) }{9} + 2{c}^{2}}\right\rbrack   + 4{a}^{2} - 4{c}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  2{c}^{2} = 3{a}^{2},{e}^{2} = \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} =  \dfrac{3}{2} \Longrightarrow  e = \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{2}$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_1233_220_295_194_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

方法 2: 由焦点三角形面积公式得 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup M{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} = {b}^{2}\cot {60}^{ \circ  } =  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  {2c} \times  {y}_{M} \Longrightarrow  {y}_{M} = \dfrac{{b}^{2}}{\sqrt{3}c},\dfrac{{x}_{M}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}_{M}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{M}^{2} = {a}^{2} + \dfrac{{a}^{2}{b}^{2}}{3c},  {x}_{M}^{2} + {y}_{M}^{2} = O{M}^{2} = \dfrac{{21}{b}^{2}}{9} \Longrightarrow  {a}^{2} = \dfrac{2{c}^{2}}{3},{e}^{2} = \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{2} \Longrightarrow  e = \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{2}$ .

23. $2 + 2\sqrt{2}$

【解析】由题意可知, $\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  = p = 2\sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1}$ ,如图,过点 $P$ 作准线的垂线,垂足为 $D$ ,则 $\left| {PD}\right|  = p$ ,则 $\cos \angle P{F}_{1}{F}_{2} =  \cos \angle {F}_{1}{PD} = \dfrac{\left| DP\right| }{\left| P{F}_{1}\right| } = \dfrac{p}{\left| P{F}_{1}\right| } = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,得 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = \sqrt{2}p$ ,在 $\bigtriangleup P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中由余弦定理可得, ${\left| P{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} = {\left| P{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} + {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} -  2\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right| \left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right| \cos \dfrac{\pi }{4} = 2{p}^{2} + {p}^{2} -  2\sqrt{2}{p}^{2} \times  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} = {p}^{2}$ ,即 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = p$ ,则由双曲线的定义可得 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  =  \left( {\sqrt{2} - 1}\right) p = 2$ ,得 $p = 2 + 2\sqrt{2}$ ,则 ${b}^{2} =  \dfrac{{p}^{2}}{4} - 1 = 2 + 2\sqrt{2}$ ,故答案为: $2 + 2\sqrt{2}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/79_1287_1317_241_270_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{二、选择题}

24. D

【解析】 ${e}_{1}^{2} = \dfrac{{c}_{1}^{2}}{{a}_{1}^{2}} = \dfrac{6}{4},{e}_{2}^{2} = \dfrac{{c}_{2}^{2}}{{a}_{2}^{2}} = \dfrac{6}{4} \Longrightarrow  {e}_{1} = {e}_{2}$ ,故选 D.

25. B

【解析】由题意得,双曲线的离心率 ${e}^{2} = {\left( \dfrac{c}{a}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{{a}^{2} + {\left( a + 1\right) }^{2}}{{a}^{2}}  = 1 + {\left( 1 + \dfrac{1}{a}\right) }^{2}$ ,因为 $\dfrac{1}{a}$ 是减函数,所以当 $a > 1$ 时, $0 < \dfrac{1}{a} <$ 1,所以 $2 < {e}^{2} < 5$ ,所以 $\sqrt{2} < e < \sqrt{5}$ ,故选 B.

点睛:本题主要考查了双曲线的几何性质及其应用，其中解答中涉及到双曲线的标准方程及简单的几何性质的应用, 函数的单调性及函数的最值等知识点的综合考查, 着重考查了学生分析问题和解答问题的能力, 以及推理与运算、转化与化归思想的应用，本题的解答中把双曲线的离心率转化为 $\dfrac{1}{a}$ 的函数，利用函数的单调性是解答的关键，试题有一定的难度, 属于中档题.

26. A

【解析】设曲线上一点为 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,则 ${a}^{2} - {b}^{2} = 1$ ,则 $a =  \sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1},{k}_{AB} = \dfrac{2 - 1}{1 - 0} = 1,{AB}$ 方程为: $y - 1 = x$ ,即 $x - y +  1 = 0$ ,根据点到直线的距离公式, $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 到 ${AB}$ 的距离为: $\dfrac{\left| a - b + 1\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1} - b + 1\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1} - b + 1}{\sqrt{2}}$ ,设 $f\left( b\right)  =  \sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1} - b = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{b}^{2} + 1} + b}$ ,由于 $b \geq  0$ ,显然 $f\left( b\right)$ 关于 $b$ 单调递减, $f{\left( b\right) }_{\max } = f\left( 0\right)$ ,无最小值,即 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 中, ${AB}$ 边上的高有最大值,无最小值,又 ${AB}$ 一定,故面积有最大值,无最小值. 故选: A.

27. D

【解析】如图,因为 $\left| {OM}\right|  = a$ ,所以 $M$ 点的轨迹是以 $O$ 为圆心,以 $a$ 为半径的圆,方程为: ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {a}^{2}$ . 因为点 $O$ 到直线 $l : {x}_{0}x + {y}_{0}y - {a}^{2} = 0$ 的距离为: $d = \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{\sqrt{{x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2}}} = \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{a} = a$ , 所以直线 $l$ 与圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {a}^{2}$ 相切. 又 $l$ 过点 $F\left( {-c,0}\right)$ ,且 ${c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2}$ ,直线 $l$ 与双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的右支在第一象限内交于 $P$ 点，所以直线 $l$ 的斜率为: ${k}_{l} = \dfrac{\left| OM\right| }{\left| FM\right| } = \dfrac{a}{b}$ . 又一、三象限双曲线的渐近线的斜率为: $\dfrac{b}{a}$ . 又 $\dfrac{b}{a} > \dfrac{a}{b} \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2} > {a}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {c}^{2} -  {a}^{2} > {a}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {c}^{2} > 2{a}^{2} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} > 2$ . 即 ${e}^{2} > 2 \Longrightarrow  e > \sqrt{2}$ . 故选: D.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/80_629_801_239_231_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

28. D

【解析】如下图建系,令双曲线为 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,且 $\left| {OP}\right|  = 2$ , 则 ${y}_{M} =  - 1,a = 1$ ,如图, $\left| {OH}\right|  = 2,\left| {OE}\right|  = 4$ ,则 $\left| {HE}\right|  =  \left| {HF}\right|  = 2\sqrt{3}$ ,故 $F\left( {2\sqrt{3}, - 2}\right)$ ,将 $F$ 代入 ${y}^{2} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,得 4- $\dfrac{12}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,可得 ${b}^{2} = 4$ ,故渐近线为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{1}{2}x$ ,若它们的夹角为 $\theta$ ,且 $\tan \dfrac{\theta }{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 $\tan \theta  = \dfrac{2\tan \dfrac{\theta }{2}}{1 - {\tan }^{2}\dfrac{\theta }{2}} = \dfrac{4}{3}$ ,故 $\cos \theta  = \dfrac{3}{5}$ . 故选: D.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.5\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/80_198_1780_671_267_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{三、解答题}

29. ( 1 )直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1$ . 由方程组 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1, \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1, \end{array}\right.$ 得 ${x}^{2} - {4x} - 8 = 0$ . 设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = 4,{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 8,\left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{4}}\sqrt{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}  = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{4}} \times  \sqrt{{16} + {32}} = 2\sqrt{15};$

(2)设点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，则点 $Q$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-x,y}\right)$ . 因为 $\vv{MP} =  \left( {x,y - 1}\right) ,\vv{MQ} = \left( {-x,y - 1}\right)$ ,所以 $k =  - {x}^{2} + {\left( y - 1\right) }^{2} =  - 2 - 2{y}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {2y} + 1 =  - {y}^{2} - {2y} - 1 =  - {\left( y + 1\right) }^{2}$ . 因为 $y \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,所以 $k \in  ( - \infty ,0\rbrack$ .

30. (1) 根据题意双曲线 ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {0 < b < 3}\right)$ ,因为 ${e}_{1}{e}_{2} = \dfrac{\sqrt{9 - {b}^{2}}}{3} \times  \dfrac{\sqrt{9 + {b}^{2}}}{3} = \dfrac{4\sqrt{5}}{9}$ ,解得 $b = 1$ ,双曲线 ${C}_{1}$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} - {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

(2)由(1)知， ${C}_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} - {y}^{2} = 1$ ， ${C}_{2} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{9} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ，设 $P\left( {x}_{1}\right.$ ， $\left. {y}_{1}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,因为 $\left| {PM}\right|  = 2\left| {MQ}\right|$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{x}_{1} - 1}{1 - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{-{y}_{2}} = 2 \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} - 1 = 2 - 2{x}_{2} \Longrightarrow  {x}_{2} = \dfrac{3 - {x}_{1}}{2} \\  {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{2} \end{array}\text{ ,由点 }Q\text{ 在椭圆 }{C}_{2}\text{ 上,则 }}\right.  \dfrac{{\left( \dfrac{3 - {x}_{1}}{2}\right) }^{2}}{9} + {\left( -\dfrac{1}{2}{y}_{1}\right) }^{2} = 1$ ,又点 $P$ 为双曲线 ${C}_{1}$ 上任意一点,则 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{9} - {y}_{1}^{2} = 1$ ,联立 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{9 - 6{x}_{1} + {x}_{1}^{2}}{36} + \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{4} = 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{36} - \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{4} = \dfrac{1}{4} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \dfrac{9 - 6{x}_{1} + 2{x}_{1}^{2}}{36} = \dfrac{5}{4} \Longrightarrow  2{x}_{1}^{2} - 6{x}_{1}  - {36} = 0$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} = 6 \\  {y}_{1} =  \pm  \sqrt{3} \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} =  - 3 \\  {y}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,故点 $P$ 的坐标为 $\left( {6, - \sqrt{3}}\right)$ 或 $\left( {6,\sqrt{3}}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-3,0}\right)$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/80_1277_1377_303_179_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)当 $P,B$ 重合时, $\lambda  \in  \mathbf{R}$ ; 当 $P,B$ 不重合时,存在实数 $\lambda  =  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,使得 ${k}_{AP} = \dfrac{1}{2}{k}_{BR}$ . 理由如下, \ding{172}  当 $P,B$ 重合时, $R,A$ 重合,则 ${k}_{AP} = {k}_{BR} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \lambda  \in  \mathbf{R}$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $P,B$ 不重合时,则 ${k}_{BR} \neq  0$ ,设直线 ${PR}$ 的方程为 $x = {ty} + 1,P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,R\left( {x}_{3}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{3}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {ty} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} - 9{y}^{2} = 9 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {{t}^{2} - 9}\right) {y}^{2} + {2ty} - 8 = 0$ ,因为双曲线的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{x}{3}$ ,又直线 ${PM}$ 交双曲线的左支于点 $R$ ,右支于点 $P$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{t} \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  \cup  \left( {-\dfrac{1}{3},0}\right)  \Longrightarrow  t \in  ( - \infty$ , $- 3) \cup  \left( {3, + \infty }\right)$ ,由韦达定理,得 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{3} = \dfrac{-{2t}}{{t}^{2} - 9},{y}_{1}{y}_{3} = \dfrac{-8}{{t}^{2} - 9} \Longrightarrow  \lambda  =  \dfrac{{k}_{AP}}{{k}_{BR}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} + 3}}{\dfrac{{y}_{3}}{{x}_{3} - 3}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{t{y}_{1} + 4}}{\dfrac{{y}_{3}}{t{y}_{3} - 2}} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{t{y}_{1} + 4} \times  \dfrac{t{y}_{3} - 2}{{y}_{3}} = \dfrac{t{y}_{1}{y}_{3} - 2{y}_{1}}{t{y}_{1}{y}_{3} + 4{y}_{3}} =  \dfrac{\dfrac{-{8t}}{{t}^{2} - 9} - 2{y}_{1}}{\dfrac{-{8t}}{{t}^{2} - 9} + 4\left( {\dfrac{-{2t}}{{t}^{2} - 9} - {y}_{1}}\right) } = \dfrac{-{8t} - 2{y}_{1}\left( {{t}^{2} - 9}\right) }{-{16t} - 4{y}_{1}\left( {{t}^{2} - 9}\right) } = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以存在实数 $\lambda  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,使得 ${k}_{AP} = \dfrac{1}{2}{k}_{BR}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/80_1330_2037_247_135_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

31. (1) 依题意,将 $a = \sqrt{5},x = 3,y = 4$ 代入 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ 中,解得 ${b}^{2} = {20} \Longrightarrow  c = \sqrt{5 + {20}} = 5$ ;

(2)证明:依题意，可设直线 $l : y =  k\left( {x + c}\right)$ ,代入 $\Gamma  : {b}^{2}{x}^{2} - {a}^{2}{y}^{2} = {a}^{2}{b}^{2}$ , 得 ${b}^{2}{x}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}\left( {{x}^{2} + {2cx} + {c}^{2}}\right)  = {a}^{2}{b}^{2}  \Longrightarrow  \left( {{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - 2{a}^{2}c{k}^{2}x - {a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} -  {a}^{2}{b}^{2} = 0\left( *\right)$ ,如图,因 $A{F}_{2} \bot  {F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ ,故点 $A$ 的横坐标为 $c$ 恰是方程 $\left( *\right)$ 的解, 则 $\left( {{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}}\right) {c}^{2} - 2{a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} - {a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} -  {a}^{2}{b}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2}{c}^{2} - 4{a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} - {a}^{2}{b}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  4{a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} = {b}^{4}$ ,因为 $a,b,c$ 是等比数列,所以 ${b}^{2} = {ac}$ ,代入此式, 得 $4{a}^{2}{c}^{2}{k}^{2} = {a}^{2}{c}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {k}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,因为过点 ${F}_{1}$ 的直线 $l$ 与 $\Gamma$ 右支在 $x$ 轴上方交于点 $A$ ，所以 $k = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，即直线 $l$ 的斜率为定值；
