(3)如图，因为点 $A$ 在双曲线右支上，所

以 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a}$ ,由 $\left| {AB}\right|  = \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|$ ,

得 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {AB}\right|  = {2a}$ ,即 $\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  = {2a}$ ,又

因为点 $B$ 在左支上,所以 $\left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  - \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|$

$= {2a} \Longrightarrow  \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = {4a}$ ,设 $\theta  = \angle B{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ ,因为

点 $A$ 在 $x$ 轴上方的右支上,所以 $0 <$

$\tan \theta  < \dfrac{b}{a} \Longrightarrow  \cos \theta  = \dfrac{1}{\sec \theta } = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{{\tan }^{2}\theta  + 1}} \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{\dfrac{{b}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + 1}},1}\right)  = \left( {\dfrac{a}{c},1}\right)  = \left( {\dfrac{a}{3},1}\right)$ ,在 $\bigtriangleup B{F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 中, $\cos \theta  =  \dfrac{4{a}^{2} + {36} - {16}{a}^{2}}{2 \cdot  {2a} \cdot  6} = \dfrac{{36} - {12}{a}^{2}}{24a} = \dfrac{3 - {a}^{2}}{2a} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{3 - {a}^{2}}{2a} > \dfrac{a}{3} \\  \dfrac{3 - {a}^{2}}{2a} < 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} 9 - 3{a}^{2} > 2{a}^{2} \\  3 - {a}^{2} < {2a} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {a}^{2} < \dfrac{9}{5} \\  a <  - 3\left( \text{ 舍 }\right) \text{ 或 }a > 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  1 < a < \dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{5}}\right. }\right.$ ,当且仅当 $a \in  \left( {1,\dfrac{3\sqrt{5}}{5}}\right)$ 时,存在直线 $l$ ,使得 $\left| {AB}\right|  = \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|$ 成立.

32. ( 1 )若 $y = x$ 是双曲线 $C$ 的一条渐近线，则 $\dfrac{1}{a} = 1$ ，可得 $a = 1$ ,此时,双曲线 $C$ 的离心率为 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}}}{a} = \sqrt{2}$ ;

(2)若 $a = \sqrt{2}$ ，不妨设点 $P$ 位于第一象限，且 $c = \sqrt{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}} =  \sqrt{3}$ ,则 $\left| {{F}_{1}{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2c} = 2\sqrt{3}$ ,由双曲线的定义可得 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  -  \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2\sqrt{2}$ ,又因为 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = 4$ ,则 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = 2 +  \sqrt{2},\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2 - \sqrt{2}$ ,所以 ${\left| P{F}_{1}\right| }^{2} + {\left| P{F}_{2}\right| }^{2} = {\left( 2 + \sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( 2 - \sqrt{2}\right) }^{2} = {12} = {\left| {F}_{1}{F}_{2}\right| }^{2}$ ,所以 $\angle {F}_{1}P{F}_{2} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ,故 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup P{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \left( {2 + \sqrt{2}}\right)  \times  \left( {2 - \sqrt{2}}\right)  = 1;$

(3)取点 $B$ 关于原点 $O$ 的对称点 $E$ ， 由双曲线的对称性可知，点 $E$ 在双曲线上,连接 $B{F}_{1},E{F}_{2}$ ,则 $O$ 为 ${BE}$ 、 ${F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 的中点,所以四边形 $B{F}_{1}E{F}_{2}$ 为平行四边形,所以 $\vv{{F}_{1}B} = \vv{E{F}_{2}}$ ,又因为 $\vv{{F}_{2}A} = \lambda \vv{{F}_{1}B}$ ,则 $\vv{{F}_{2}A} = \lambda \vv{E{F}_{2}}$ ,即 $A,{F}_{2},E$ 三点共线, 易知,直线 ${AE}$ 不与 $x$ 轴重合,设直线 ${AE}$ 的方程为 $x =  {my} + c$ ,设点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,E\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,因为 ${S}_{\text{ 四边形 }{AB}{F}_{1}{F}_{2}} =  {S}_{\bigtriangleup {BO}{F}_{1}} + {S}_{\text{ 四边形 }{ABO}{F}_{2}} = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {OE}{F}_{2}} + {S}_{\text{ 四边形 }{ABO}{F}_{2}} = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABE}} =  2{S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOE}} = 2\sqrt{{a}^{2} + 1} = {2c}$ ,所以 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOE}} = c = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {O{F}_{2}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {{y}_{1} - }\right.  {y}_{2}\left| { = \dfrac{1}{2}c \cdot  \left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right| }\right.$ ,则 $\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = 2$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {my} + c \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 可得 $\left( {{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right) {y}^{2} + {2mcy} + 1 = 0$ ,由题意可得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {m}^{2} - {a}^{2} \neq  0 \\  \Delta  = 4{m}^{2}{c}^{2} - 4\left( {{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right)  > 0,\text{ 可得 } - a < m < a,\text{ 由韦达定理 } \\  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}} < 0 \end{array}\right.$ 可得 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{2mc}{{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}}$ ,所以 ${\left| {y}_{1} - {y}_{2}\right| }^{2}  = {\left( {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{y}_{1}{y}_{2} = {\left( -\dfrac{2mc}{{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{4}{{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}} =  \dfrac{4{m}^{2}{c}^{2} - 4\left( {{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right) }{{\left( {m}^{2} - {a}^{2}\right) }^{2}} = 4$ ,整理可得 ${m}^{4} - 3{a}^{2}{m}^{2} + {a}^{4} - {a}^{2} = 0$ ,令 $t  = {m}^{2}$ ,则 $0 \leq  t < {a}^{2}$ ,则关于 $t$ 的二次方程 ${t}^{2} - 3{a}^{2}t + {a}^{4} - {a}^{2} = 0$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,{a}^{2}}\right)$ 上有解,设 $f\left( t\right)  = {t}^{2} - 3{a}^{2}t + {a}^{4} - {a}^{2}$ ,则二次函数 $f\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,{a}^{2}}\right)$ 上单调递减,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} f\left( 0\right)  = {a}^{4} - {a}^{2} \geq  0 \\  f\left( {a}^{2}\right)  =  - {a}^{4} - {a}^{2} < 0,\text{ 解 } \\  a > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $a \geq  1$ ，因此， $a$ 的取值范围是 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/81_1272_290_256_149_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

33. ( 1 )因为双曲线 $C$ 的渐近线方程为 $x \pm  \sqrt{3}y = 0$ ，所以设双曲线方程为 ${x}^{2} - 3{y}^{2} = \lambda \left( {\lambda  \neq  0}\right)$ ，又双曲线过点 $P\left( {3,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ， 则 $\lambda  = 9 - 3 \times  2 = 3$ ,所以双曲线的方程为 ${x}^{2} - 3{y}^{2} = 3$ ,即 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} - {y}^{2} = 1;$

(2)由(1)可知 $F\left( {2,0}\right)$ ， $l$ 的斜率存在且不为 0，所以设 $l$ 的方程为 $y = k\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = k\left( {x - 2}\right) \\  {x}^{2} - 3{y}^{2} = 3 \end{array}\right.$ ，消去 $y$ 得 $\left( {1 - 3{k}^{2}}\right)  {x}^{2} + {12}{k}^{2}x - {12}{k}^{2} - 3 = 0$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由题意得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1 - 3{k}^{2} \neq  0 \\  \Delta  > 0 \\  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{12}{k}^{2}}{1 - 3{k}^{2}} > 0 \\  {x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{12}{k}^{2} - 3}{1 - 3{k}^{2}} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $k \in  \left( {-\infty , - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)  \cup  \left( \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}\right.$ , $+ \infty )$ ,所以 ${k}_{AQ} + {k}_{BQ} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} - \dfrac{3}{2}} + \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} - \dfrac{3}{2}} = \dfrac{k\left( {{x}_{1} - 2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - \dfrac{3}{2}} +  \dfrac{k\left( {{x}_{2} - 2}\right) }{{x}_{2} - \dfrac{3}{2}} = \dfrac{k\left\lbrack  {2{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \dfrac{7}{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 6}\right\rbrack  }{{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - \dfrac{3}{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + \dfrac{9}{4}} =  \dfrac{k\left\lbrack  {2\left( {-{12}{k}^{2} - 3}\right)  + \dfrac{7}{2} \times  {12}{k}^{2} + 6\left( {1 - 3{k}^{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack  }{-{12}{k}^{2} - 3 + \dfrac{3}{2} \times  {12}{k}^{2} + \dfrac{9}{4}\left( {1 - 3{k}^{2}}\right) } = 0$ ,所以 ${k}_{AQ} =  - {k}_{BQ}$ ,即 $\angle {AQF} = \angle {BQF}$ 得证;

(3)由(2)可知 $\Delta  = {12}{k}^{2} + {12} > 0$ 恒成立， ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{12}{k}^{2}}{1 - 3{k}^{2}}$ ，所以圆心到 $x = \dfrac{3}{2}$ 的距离 $d = \left| {\dfrac{6{k}^{2}}{3{k}^{2} - 1} - \dfrac{3}{2}}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{3{k}^{2} + 3}{2\left( {3{k}^{2} - 1}\right) }\right|$ , 半径 $r = \dfrac{\left| AB\right| }{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}}\dfrac{\sqrt{{12}{k}^{2} + {12}}}{\left| 1 - 3{k}^{2}\right| } = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) }{\left| 3{k}^{2} - 1\right| }$ ,设 $\overset{\frown}{MN}$ 所对圆心角为 $\theta$ ,则 $\cos \dfrac{\theta }{2} = \dfrac{d}{r} = \left| \dfrac{3\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) }{2\left( {3{k}^{2} - 1}\right) }\right|$ . $\left| \dfrac{3{k}^{2} - 1}{\sqrt{3}\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) }\right|  = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ,因为 $\overset{\frown}{MN}$ 为劣弧,所以 $\theta  \in  \left( {0,\pi }\right)$ ,所以 $\dfrac{\theta }{2} = \dfrac{\pi }{6}$ ,所以 $\theta  = \dfrac{\pi }{3}$ ,即 $\overset{\frown}{MN}$ 所对圆心角的大小为定值 $\dfrac{\pi }{3}$ .

34. (1) 由 $e = \sqrt{2}$ ,得 $c = \sqrt{2}a$ ,又 ${c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2}$ 得 ${a}^{2} = {b}^{2}$ ,又双曲线 $E$ 经过点 $\left( {\sqrt{2},1}\right)$ ,有 $\dfrac{2}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{1}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ,所以 ${a}^{2} = 1$ ,所以, 双曲线方程为 ${x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

( 2 )由已知得 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ，渐近线方程为 ${bx} \pm  {2y} = 0$ ，焦点坐标为 $\left( {\pm \sqrt{4 + {b}^{2}},0}\right)$ ，焦点到双曲线 $E$ 的渐近线的距离为 $\sqrt{3} = \dfrac{b\sqrt{4 + {b}^{2}}}{\sqrt{4 + {b}^{2}}}$ ,所以 $b = \sqrt{3}$ ,由双曲线定义知, $\left| {M{F}_{2}}\right|  = 4$ , 所以, $\cos \angle {F}_{1}M{F}_{2} = \dfrac{{8}^{2} + {4}^{2} - {\left( 2\sqrt{7}\right) }^{2}}{2 \times  8 \times  4} = \dfrac{13}{16}$ ;

(3)因为直线 $l : y = {kx} + m$ 与圆 $O$ 相切，且 $R = 2$ ，则圆心到直线的距离为 $\dfrac{\left| m\right| }{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} = 2$ ，化简得 ${m}^{2} = 4{k}^{2} + 4$ ，又 $\angle {AOB} =  \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\vv{OA} \cdot  \vv{OB} = 0$ ,即 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} +  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0$ ,则 $\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {km}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + {m}^{2} = 0\left( *\right)$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + m \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $\left( {{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - 2{a}^{2}{kmx} - {a}^{2}{m}^{2} - {a}^{2}{b}^{2} = 0$ , 则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{2{a}^{2}{mk}}{{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{a}^{2}\left( {{m}^{2} + {b}^{2}}\right) }{{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}}$ 代入 $\left( *\right)$ ,得 $\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) \left\lbrack  {-{a}^{2}\left( {{m}^{2} + {b}^{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack   + {km} \cdot  2{a}^{2}{mk} + {m}^{2}\left( {{b}^{2} - {a}^{2}{k}^{2}}\right)  =$ 0,将 ${m}^{2} = 4{k}^{2} + 4$ 代入,进一步化简得 $\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {4{a}^{2} + {a}^{2}{b}^{2}}\right.  \left. {-4{b}^{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,则 $4{a}^{2} + {a}^{2}{b}^{2} - 4{b}^{2} = 0$ ,又 ${c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2}$ ,由 $\dfrac{4{a}^{2} + {a}^{2}\left( {{c}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right)  - 4\left( {{c}^{2} - {a}^{2}}\right) }{{a}^{2}} = 0$ ,得 $\dfrac{{c}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} = \dfrac{8 + {b}^{2}}{4} > 2$ ,则 $e =  \dfrac{c}{a} > \sqrt{2}$ ,所以,离心率 $e$ 的取值范围 $\left( {\sqrt{2}, + \infty }\right)$ .

35. (1)因为双曲线的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{12} = 1$ ，所以双曲线的左右顶点为 $\left( {\pm \sqrt{3},0}\right)$ ,设椭圆方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {a > b > 0}\right)$ ,所以 ${2a} = 4\sqrt{3},c = \sqrt{3}$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {a}^{2} = {12} \\  {b}^{2} = {a}^{2} - {c}^{2} = 9 \end{array}\right.$ ,所以椭圆 $C$ 的标准方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{12} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{9} = 1$ ;

(2)因为双曲线的渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  {2x}$ ，不妨设 $P\left( {t,{2t}}\right)$ ( $t >$ 0),又 $\vv{PA} = \vv{AQ}$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{Q} - 2 = 2 - t \\  {y}_{Q} - 2 = 2 - {2t} \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $Q\left( {4 - t,4 - {2t}}\right)$ ,又因为 $Q$ 是双曲线 $\Gamma$ 上一点,所以 $\dfrac{{\left( 4 - t\right) }^{2}}{3} - \dfrac{{\left( 4 - 2t\right) }^{2}}{12} = 1$ ,解得 $t = \dfrac{9}{4}$ ,所以 $P\left( {\dfrac{9}{4},\dfrac{9}{2}}\right) ,Q\left( {\dfrac{7}{4}, - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {OP}\right|  =  \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{9}{4} - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{9}{2} - 0\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{9\sqrt{5}}{4}$ ,又 $Q$ 到直线 ${OP} : {2x} - y = 0$ 的距离 $d =  \dfrac{\left| 2 \times  \dfrac{7}{4} - \left( -\dfrac{1}{2}\right) \right| }{\sqrt{1 + 4}} = \dfrac{4\sqrt{5}}{5}$ ,所以 $S =  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  d \times  \left| {OP}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{4\sqrt{5}}{5} \times  \dfrac{9\sqrt{5}}{4}  = \dfrac{9}{2};$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/82_1334_565_245_268_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)设 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - {4x} + m \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{12} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 可得 ${12}{x}^{2} - {8mx} + {m}^{2} +  {12} = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{8m}{12} = \dfrac{2m}{3}$ , ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{{m}^{2} + {12}}{12}$ ,且 $\Delta  = {64}{m}^{2} - 4 \times  {12} \times  \left( {{m}^{2} + {12}}\right)  > 0$ ,即 ${m}^{2} > {36}$ ,又因为 $M,N$ 为左支上两点, 所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{2m}{3} < 0$ ,所以 $m <  - 6$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1} - 2}{{x}_{1} - 2} +  \dfrac{{y}_{2} - 2}{{x}_{2} - 2} = \dfrac{-4{x}_{1} + m - 2}{{x}_{1} - 2} + \dfrac{-4{x}_{2} + m - 2}{{x}_{2} - 2} = \dfrac{-4{x}_{1} + 8 + m - {10}}{{x}_{1} - 2} +  \dfrac{-4{x}_{2} + 8 + m - {10}}{{x}_{2} - 2}$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} =  - 8 + \dfrac{m - {10}}{{x}_{1} - 2} + \dfrac{m - {10}}{{x}_{2} - 2} =  - 8 + \left( {m - {10}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} - 4}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 2\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 4}$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} =  - 8 + \left( {m - {10}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{\dfrac{2m}{3} - 4}{\dfrac{{m}^{2} + {12}}{12} - 2 \times  \dfrac{2m}{3} + 4} =  - 8 + \left( {m - {10}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{\dfrac{2}{3}\left( {m - 6}\right) }{\dfrac{\left( {m - 6}\right) \left( {m - {10}}\right) }{12}}$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} =  - 8 + 8 = 0$ ,所以 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2}$ 为定值 0 .

\end{center}
 

36. ( 1 )直线 $l : x - {my} - 2 = 0$ 经过点 ${F}_{2}$ ，令 $y = 0$ ，则 $x =$ 2,即 ${F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right) { \Longrightarrow  }_{C} = 2 \Longrightarrow  a = \sqrt{3}$ ,因为 $\left| {M{F}_{1}}\right|  = 2$ ,所以点 $M$ 在左支，所以 $\left| {M{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2 + 2\sqrt{3}$ ；

(2)点 $O,A,P,B$ 满足 $\vv{OA} = \lambda \vv{BP},\vv{OB} = \mu \vv{AP}$ ，所以四边形 ${OAPB}$ 为平行四边形，因为双曲线 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} - {y}^{2} = 1$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x - {my} - 2 = 0 \\  {x}^{2} - 3{y}^{2} = 3 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {{m}^{2} - 3}\right) {y}^{2} + {4my} + 1 = 0$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  \dfrac{-{4m}}{{m}^{2} - 3},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} - 3}$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = m{y}_{1} + 2 + m{y}_{2} + 2 =  m\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + 4 = \dfrac{-4{m}^{2}}{{m}^{2} - 3} + \dfrac{4\left( {{m}^{2} - 3}\right) }{{m}^{2} - 3} = \dfrac{-{12}}{{m}^{2} - 3},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  \left( {m{y}_{1} + 2}\right) \left( {m{y}_{2} + 2}\right)  = {m}^{2}{y}_{1}{y}_{2} + {2m}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + 4,{AB}$ 的中点 $\left( {\dfrac{6}{3 - {m}^{2}},\dfrac{2m}{3 - {m}^{2}}}\right)$ ,即 ${OP}$ 中点与 ${AB}$ 中点相同,所以 $3 =  \dfrac{6}{3 - {m}^{2}} \Longrightarrow  m = 1 \Longrightarrow  {y}_{p} = 2$ . $\dfrac{2m}{3 - {m}^{2}} = 2$ ,所以 $P\left( {6,2}\right)$ ;

\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/83_511_346_301_189_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 、 $G\left( {-{x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {S}_{\bigtriangleup {AGB}} =  2{S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOB}},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} - 3} < 0 \Longrightarrow  {m}^{2} < 3,\vv{OA} \cdot  \vv{OB} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} +  {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \left( {{m}^{2} + 1}\right) {y}_{1}{y}_{2} + {2m}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + 4 \geq  {17}$ ,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{m}^{2} + 1}{{m}^{2} - 3} + {2m}\dfrac{-{4m}}{{m}^{2} - 3} + 4 \geq  {17} \\  {m}^{2} - 3 < 0 \end{array}\text{ ,所以 }2 \leq  {m}^{2} < 3}\right.$ ,所以 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {GAB}} =  2 \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  2\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = 2\sqrt{{\left( {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{y}_{1}{y}_{2}} = 4\sqrt{3}\dfrac{\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1}}{3 - {m}^{2}},$ 令 $\sqrt{{m}^{2} + 1} = t,t \in  \lbrack \sqrt{3},2) \Longrightarrow  {m}^{2} = {t}^{2} - 1,f\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{4\sqrt{3}t}{3 - \left( {{t}^{2} - 1}\right) } =  \dfrac{4\sqrt{3}t}{4 - {t}^{2}} = \dfrac{4\sqrt{3}}{\dfrac{4}{t} - t}$ ,因为 $\dfrac{4}{t} - t$ 在 $t \in  \lbrack \sqrt{3},2)$ 单调递减, $f\left( t\right)$ 在 $t \in  \lbrack \sqrt{3},2)$ 单调递增,所以 $f\left( t\right)$ 在 $t = \sqrt{3}$ 时取得最小值为 12，所以 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {GAB}} \in  \lbrack {12}, + \infty )$ .

37.(1)已知双曲线 $\Gamma  : {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ，则 $a = 1,b = \sqrt{3}$ ，所以渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \sqrt{3}x$ ;

( 2 )双曲线 $\Gamma  : {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 的右焦点 $F(2$ ， $0)$ ,又 $A\left( {2,3}\right)$ ,所以 ${AB} \bot  {OF}$ ,则 $B(2$ , $- 3)$ ,因为 $\tan \angle {BAC} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 ${k}_{AC} =  \tan \left( {\angle {BAC} + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{\tan \angle {BAC}} =  - 2,$

则直线 ${AC} : y - 3 =  - 2\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,即 $y =  - {2x} + 7$ ,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \\  y =  - {2x} + 7 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} - {28x} + {52} = 0}\right.$ ,解得 ${x}_{1} = 2,{x}_{2} = {26}$ ,即 ${x}_{C} =$

26，则 ${y}_{C} =  - 2 \times  {26} + 7 =  - {45}$ ，所以点 $C$ 的坐标为 $\left( {{26}, - {45}}\right)$ ；

(3) 设直线 ${AB} : x = {ty} + 2,A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,C\left( {x}_{3}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{3}\right) ,\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x = {ty} + 2 \\  {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {3{t}^{2} - 1}\right) {y}^{2} + {12ty} + 9 = 0,{t}^{2} \neq  \dfrac{1}{3},\Delta  = }\right.  {36}\left( {{t}^{2} + 1}\right)  > 0$ ,则 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{12t}{3{t}^{2} - 1}$ , ${y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{9}{3{t}^{2} - 1}$ ,因为直线 $l$ 过点 $F$ 且与双曲线 $\Gamma$ 右支交于 $A,B$ 两点,所以 $t \in  \left( {-\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3},\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ ,又因为 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心 $G$ 在 $x$ 轴上,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} + {y}_{3} = 0$ ,由点 $Q$ 在点 $F$ 的右侧,可得 ${y}_{3} < 0$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} > 0$ ,解得 $t > 0$ ,所以 $t \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ ,而 $\left| {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}$ ,代入可得 ${y}_{1} - {y}_{2} =  - \dfrac{6\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}{3{t}^{2} - 1}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{S}_{2}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABG}}}}{\dfrac{{S}_{2}}{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {ACG}}}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{\left| AF\right| }{\left| AB\right| }}{\dfrac{\left| CQ\right| }{\left| AC\right| }} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}}{\dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{3}}{{y}_{1} - {y}_{3}}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}}{\dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{2{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}} =  \dfrac{\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} + {y}_{1}}\right) \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} - {y}_{2}}\right) }{\left( {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right) \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right) } = \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}} + 1 - \dfrac{{y}_{1}{y}_{2}}{\left( {{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right) \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right) },$ 代入化简可得: $\dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{S}_{2}} = \dfrac{{13}{t}^{2} + 1}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}} + 1$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{S}_{2}} = \dfrac{{13}{t}^{2} + 1}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}} + 1 =  \dfrac{{12}{t}^{2}}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}} + \dfrac{{t}^{2} + 1}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}} + 1 \geq  2\sqrt{\dfrac{{12}{t}^{2}}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}} \cdot  \dfrac{{t}^{2} + 1}{{8t}\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}} +  1 = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} + 1$ ,当且仅当 $t = \dfrac{\sqrt{11}}{11}$ 时等号成立,所以 $\dfrac{{S}_{1}}{{S}_{2}}$ 的最小值为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} + 1$ .

38.(1)由题， $a = 1$ ， $c = 2$ ，因此双曲线 $C$ 的离心率为 $e =  \dfrac{c}{a} = 2$

(2)方法一:当直线斜率不存在时，设直线为 $x = 2$ ，则得 $P\left( {2,3}\right)$ ， $Q\left( {2, - 3}\right)$ ，此时 $\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ} =  - 5$ ；当直线斜率存在时， 设直线方程为: $y = k\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,联立方程得: $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = k\left( {x - 2}\right) \\  3{x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 3 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {3 - {k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + 4{k}^{2}x - 4{k}^{2} - 3 = 0}\right.$ ,则由题意得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 3 - {k}^{2} \neq  0 \\  \Delta  = {16}{k}^{4} + 4\left( {4{k}^{2} + 3}\right) \left( {3 - {k}^{2}}\right)  = {36}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  > 0 \\  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-4{k}^{2}}{3 - {k}^{2}} > 0 \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{-4{k}^{2} - 3}{3 - {k}^{2}} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 所以 ${k}^{2} > 3,\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {k}^{2}\left( {{x}_{1} - }\right.$ 2) $\left( {{x}_{2} - 2}\right)  = \left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) {x}_{1}{x}_{2} - 2{k}^{2}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 4{k}^{2} = \dfrac{-5{k}^{2} + 3}{{k}^{2} - 3} =  - 5 + \dfrac{-{12}}{{k}^{2} - 3} <  - 5$ ,因而 $\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ}$ 的取值范围为 $( - \infty , - 5\rbrack$ . 方法二: 由题意,直线不与 $x$ 轴重合,因而设方程为: $x =  {my} + 2$ ,设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则联立方程得: $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x = {my} + 2 \\  3{x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 3 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left( {3{m}^{2} - 1}\right) {y}^{2} + {12my} + 9 = 0}\right.$ ,由题意,直线与双曲线恒有交点,且交点均在右支上,则 ${y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{9}{3{m}^{2} - 1} < 0  \Longrightarrow  0 \leq  {m}^{2} < \dfrac{1}{3},\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ} = {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \left( {m{y}_{1} + 2}\right) \left( {m{y}_{2} + }\right.$ 2) $+ {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \left( {1 + {m}^{2}}\right) {y}_{1}{y}_{2} + {2m}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  + 4 = \left( {1 + {m}^{2}}\right)$ . $\dfrac{9}{3{m}^{2} - 1} + {2m} \cdot  \dfrac{-{12m}}{3{m}^{2} - 1} + 4 =  - 1 + \dfrac{4}{3{m}^{2} - 1}$ ,由 $- 1 \leq  3{m}^{2} - 1 < 0$ , 得 $\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ} \leq   - 5$ ,因而 $\vv{OP} \cdot  \vv{OQ}$ 的取值范围为 $( - \infty , - 5\rbrack$ ;

(3)由题,渐近线方程为 $\sqrt{3}x \pm  y = 0$ ,设点 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ ,则 $\left| {PM}\right|  + \left| {PN}\right|  = \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} + {y}_{1}\right| }{2} + \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} - {y}_{1}\right| }{2} \geq$

$\dfrac{\left| \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} + {y}_{1} + \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} - {y}_{1}\right| }{2} = \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} \geq  \sqrt{3}$ ,因而不存在点 $P$ ,使得 $\left| {PM}\right|  + \left| {PN}\right|  = \sqrt{2}$ 成立,

另解: $\left| {PM}\right|  \cdot  \left| {PN}\right|  = \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} + {y}_{1}\right| }{2} \cdot  \dfrac{\left| \sqrt{3}{x}_{1} - {y}_{1}\right| }{2} =  \dfrac{\left| 3{x}_{1}^{2} - {y}_{1}^{2}\right| }{4} = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,又因为 $\left| {PM}\right|  + \left| {PN}\right|  \geq  2\sqrt{\left| {PM}\right|  \cdot  \left| {PN}\right| }  = \sqrt{3}$ ,因而不存在点 $P$ ,使得 $\left| {PM}\right|  + \left| {PN}\right|  = \sqrt{2}$ 成立.

39.(1)由离心率为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{3}$ 得， $\dfrac{\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}{t} = \dfrac{\sqrt{10}}{3}$ ，又 $t > 0$ ，即 $t = 3$ ,则焦距为 $2\sqrt{10}$ ;

( 2 )由条件得直线 ${F}_{1}M$ 的方程为 ${tx} - y + t\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1} = 0$ ，它与渐近线 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{t}x$ 交于 $M$ 点，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \dfrac{1}{t}x \\  {tx} - y + t\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ，解得 $y = \dfrac{t\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}{{t}^{2} + 1}$ ,即 $M$ 点的纵坐标为 $\dfrac{t\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}{{t}^{2} + 1}$ ,则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {MO}{F}_{1}} =  \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  \sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1} \cdot  \dfrac{t\sqrt{{t}^{2} + 1}}{{t}^{2} + 1} = \dfrac{1}{2}t = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 $t = 1$ ,则所求的双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = 1$ . 另一种情况,与渐近线 $y = \dfrac{1}{t}x$ 交于 $M$ 点,得出关于 $t$ 的方程 $\left| {t - \dfrac{1}{t}}\right|  = {t}^{2} + 1$ ,在 $0 < t < 1$ 时,方程 ${t}^{3} + {t}^{2} + t - 1 = 0$ 有一解,近似值为 $t = {0.54};\Gamma$ : $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{0.54}^{2}} - {y}^{2} = 1.$

(3) 设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {kx} - y + m = 0 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{2} - {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得, $(1 -  \left. {2{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - {4kmx} - 2{m}^{2} - 2 = 0$ ,又 $\Delta  = {16}{k}^{2}{m}^{2} - 4\left( {1 - 2{k}^{2}}\right)  \left( {-2{m}^{2} - 2}\right)  > 0$ ,则 ${m}^{2} - 2{k}^{2} + 1 > 0$ . 因为 ${\left| \vv{OP} + \vv{OQ}\right| }^{2} =  {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( {y}_{1} + {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} = \left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right) {\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} + {4mk}\left( {{x}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {x}_{2}\right)  + 4{m}^{2}$ ,又 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{4km}{1 - 2{k}^{2}},\left| {\vv{OP} + \vv{OQ}}\right|  = 4$ ,则 $\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  {\left( \dfrac{4km}{1 - 2{k}^{2}}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{{\left( 4km\right) }^{2}}{1 - 2{k}^{2}} + 4{m}^{2} = {16}$ ,整理得, $4{k}^{2}{m}^{2} + {m}^{2} = 4(1 -  2{k}^{2}{)}^{2}$ ,则 ${m}^{2} = \dfrac{4{\left( 1 - 2{k}^{2}\right) }^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 1}$ . 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {m}^{2} = \dfrac{4{\left( 1 - 2{k}^{2}\right) }^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 1} \\  {m}^{2} - 2{k}^{2} + 1 > 0 \end{array}\right.$ 得, $\dfrac{4{\left( 1 - 2{k}^{2}\right) }^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 1} - 2{k}^{2} + 1 > 0$ ,整理得, $4{\left( 1 - 2{k}^{2}\right) }^{2} + \left( {1 - 2{k}^{2}}\right)  \left( {4{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  > 0$ ,即 $\left( {4{k}^{2} - 5}\right) \left( {2{k}^{2} - 1}\right)  > 0$ ,则 ${k}^{2} > \dfrac{5}{4}$ 或 ${k}^{2} <  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,又 $k > 0$ ,即 $0 < k < \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ 或 $k > \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}$ ,则 $k$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}, + \infty }\right) .$

40. (1) 两条渐近线方程为 $\sqrt{2}x \pm  y = 0,{\vv{n}}_{1} = \left( {\sqrt{2},1}\right) ,{\vv{n}}_{2} =  \left( {\sqrt{2}, - 1}\right)$ ,设两条直线夹角为 $\theta$ ,则 $\cos \theta  = \left| \dfrac{2 - 1}{\sqrt{3} \cdot  \sqrt{3}}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ , 所以双曲线的两条渐近线夹角的余弦值为 $\dfrac{1}{3}$ ;

(2)设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{1} > 1,{y}_{1} > 0}\right)$ ，由已知得 $A\left( {-1,0}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {1,0}\right) ,\vv{PA} = \left( {-1 - {x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right) ,\vv{PB} = \left( {1 - {x}_{1}, - {y}_{1}}\right)$ ,则 $\vv{PA} \cdot  \vv{PB} = {x}_{1}^{2} - 1 + {y}_{1}^{2} \leq  9$ ,得 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{1}^{2} \leq  {10}$ ,又点 $P$ 在双曲线上,有 ${x}_{1}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{2} = 1$ 即 ${y}_{1}^{2} = 2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right)$ ,从而 ${x}_{1}^{2} + 2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right)  \leq$ 10,解得 ${x}_{1}^{2} \leq  4$ . 又点 $P$ 是双曲线在第一象限的点,所以 ${x}_{1}^{2} \in  (1,4\rbrack .{\left| \vv{OP}\right| }^{2} = {x}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{1}^{2} = {x}_{1}^{2} + 2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right)  = 3{x}_{1}^{2} - 2 \in$

$(1,{10}\rbrack$ ,所以 $\left| \vv{OP}\right|  \in  (1,\sqrt{10}\rbrack$ ;

(3)椭圆 $C$ 中 $a = \sqrt{2},b = 1$ ，焦点在 $y$ 轴上，标准方程为 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} + {x}^{2} = 1$ ，设 $Q\left( {x}_{2}\right.$ ， $\left. {y}_{2}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} > 0,{y}_{2} > 0}\right)$ ,直线 ${AP}$ 的斜率为 $k\left( {k > 0}\right)$ ,则直线 ${AP}$ 的方程为 $y =  k\left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,联立方程组 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = k\left( {x + 1}\right) \\  \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} + {x}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $\left( {2 + {k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + 2{k}^{2}x +  {k}^{2} - 2 = 0$ ,该方程的两根分别为-1和 ${x}_{2} = \dfrac{2 - {k}^{2}}{2 + {k}^{2}}$ ,同理可得 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{2 + {k}^{2}}{2 - {k}^{2}}$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = 1$ ,记 ${S}_{1} = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {POA}} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  1 \times  {y}_{1} =  \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{2},{S}_{2} = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {QAB}} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  2 \times  {y}_{2} = {y}_{2}$ ,则 ${S}_{1}^{2} - {S}_{2}^{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{4} - {y}_{2}^{2} =  \dfrac{1}{4} \times  2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right)  - 2\left( {1 - {x}_{2}^{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{1}^{2} + 2{x}_{2}^{2} - \dfrac{5}{2} = \left( {\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{2} + \dfrac{2}{{x}_{1}^{2}}}\right)  -  \dfrac{5}{2} \geq  2\sqrt{1} - \dfrac{5}{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,当且仅当 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{2} = \dfrac{2}{{x}_{1}^{2}}$ 即 ${x}_{1}^{2} = 2$ 时取等号, 所以 ${S}_{1}^{2} - {S}_{2}^{2}$ 的最小值为 $- \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,此时点 $P$ 的坐标为 $\left( {\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2}}\right)$ . 另解: ${k}_{AP} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} + 1},{k}_{AQ} = \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} + 1}$ ,因为 ${k}_{AP} = {k}_{AQ}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} + 1}  = \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} + 1}$ ,即 ${\left( \dfrac{{y}_{1}}{{x}_{1} + 1}\right) }^{2} = {\left( \dfrac{{y}_{2}}{{x}_{2} + 1}\right) }^{2}$ ,又 ${y}_{1}^{2} = 2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right) ,{y}_{2}^{2} = 2(1  \left. {-{x}_{2}^{2}}\right)$ ,代入上式化简得 $\dfrac{{x}_{1} - 1}{{x}_{1} + 1} = \dfrac{1 - {x}_{2}}{{x}_{2} + 1}$ ,整理得 ${x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{2} = 1$ . 41. (1) 因为双曲线 $\Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{5} = 1$ ,所以 ${a}^{2} = 4,{b}^{2} = 5$ ,所以 $c = 3$ ,即 ${F}_{1}\left( {-3,0}\right) ,{F}_{2}\left( {3,0}\right)$ ,所以双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的渐近线方程是 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2}x$ ;

(2)由题意可知 $\left| {PD}\right|  = \left| {PE}\right| ,\left| {{F}_{1}D}\right|  = \left| {{F}_{1}F}\right| ,\left| {{F}_{2}F}\right|  =  \left| {{F}_{2}E}\right|$ ,所以 $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left( {\left| {PD}\right|  + \left| {D{F}_{1}}\right| }\right)  - (\left| {PE}\right|  +  \left. \left| {E{F}_{2}}\right| \right)  = \left| {D{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {E{F}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {F{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {F{F}_{2}}\right|  = {2a} = 4$ ,所以 $F(2$ , $0)$ ,即 $F$ 是椭圆右顶点. 设圆 $C$ 的半径为 $r\left( {r > 0}\right)$ ,因为圆 $C$ 的面积为 ${4\pi }$ ,则 $\pi {r}^{2} = {4\pi }$ ,即 $r = 2$ ,因为 ${CF} \bot  {F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ ,所以 $C\left( {2,2}\right)$ , 设直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的斜率为 $k$ ,则直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的方程为 $y = k\left( {x - 3}\right)$ ,即 ${kx} - y - {3k} = 0$ ,由圆心 $C$ 到直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的距离等于圆的半径,可得 $\dfrac{\left| 2k - 2 - 3k\right| }{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} = 2$ ,解得直线 $P{F}_{2}$ 的斜率为 $k = \dfrac{4}{3}$ ;

(3)假设存在过点 ${F}_{2}$ 的直线 $l$ 与双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的左、右两支分别交于 $A,B$ 两点,且使得 $\angle {F}_{1}{AB} = \angle {F}_{1}{BA}$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {x}_{2}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{2}\right) ,{AB}$ 中点为 $M\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,又 ${F}_{1}\left( {-3,0}\right) ,{F}_{2}\left( {3,0}\right)$ ,由 $\angle {F}_{1}{AB}  = \angle {F}_{1}{BA}$ ,可知 $\bigtriangleup {F}_{1}{AB}$ 为等腰三角形, $\left| {{F}_{1}A}\right|  = \left| {{F}_{1}B}\right|$ ,且直线 $l$ 不与 $x$ 轴重合,于是 ${F}_{1}M \bot  {AB}$ ,即 ${F}_{1}M \bot  M{F}_{2}$ ,因此 ${k}_{{F}_{1}M} \cdot  {k}_{M{F}_{2}} =  - 1,\dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} + 3} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3} =  - 1,{x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 9\left( {I}\right)$ ,点 $A,B$ 在双曲线 $\Gamma$ 上,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{5} = 1\text{  \ding{172}  } \\  \dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4} - \dfrac{{y}_{2}^{2}}{5} = 1\text{  \ding{173}  } \end{array}\right.$  \ding{173}  简整理得: $\dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{5}{4},\dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{5}{4}$ ,则 ${k}_{OM}$ . ${k}_{AB} = \dfrac{5}{4}$ ,可得 $\dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0}} \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 3} = \dfrac{5}{4},4{y}_{0}^{2} = 5{x}_{0}^{2} - {15}{x}_{0}$ (II),联立(I) (II)得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{0}^{2} + {y}_{0}^{2} = 9 \\  4{y}_{0}^{2} = 5{x}_{0}^{2} - {15}{x}_{0} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  3{x}_{0}^{2} - 5{x}_{0} - {12} = 0}\right.$ ,得 ${x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{4}{3}$ 或 ${x}_{0} = 3$ (舍),所以 $M\left( {-\dfrac{4}{3}, \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{65}}{3}}\right)$ ,由 ${k}_{OM} \cdot  {k}_{AB} = \dfrac{5}{4}$ ,得 ${k}_{AB} =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{65}}{13}$ ,所以直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{65}}{13}\left( {x - 3}\right)$ .

42.(1)根据双曲线定义得: $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2,\left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {B{F}_{2}}\right|  = 2$ ,两式相加得 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left( {\left| {A{F}_{2}}\right|  + }\right.  \left. {\mid B{F}_{2} \mid  }\right)  = 4$ ,即 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  + \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  - \left| {AB}\right|  = 4$ ,由已知 $\left| {AB}\right|  = 6$ 得 $\left| {A{F}_{1}}\right|  +  \left| {B{F}_{1}}\right|  = {10}$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup {AB}{F}_{1}$ 的周长为 16 ;

(3)设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{0} > 1,{y}_{0} > 0}\right)$ ，由 $\vv{OQ} = 2\vv{OP}$ 知 $Q\left( {2{x}_{0},2{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,若直线 $l$ 斜率不存在,则 $l : x = 2$ ,此时 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ , $Q\left( {2,0}\right)$ 与点 ${F}_{2}\left( {2,0}\right)$ 重合,不符题意, 舍去; 设直线 $l$ 方程为: $y = k\left( {x - 2}\right)$ , 与双曲线 $C : {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 联立化简得 $\left( {{k}^{2} - 3}\right) {x}^{2} - 4{k}^{2}x + 4{k}^{2} + 3 = 0,{k}^{2} - 3 \neq  0,\Delta  > 0$ 显然成立, 设交点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,由韦达定理: ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  \dfrac{4{k}^{2}}{{k}^{2} - 3},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{k}^{2} + 3}{{k}^{2} - 3}$ ,由 $\vv{AB} = 2\vv{Q{F}_{2}}$ 得 $\left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{1},{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}\right)  = 2\left( {2 - 2{x}_{0}, - 2{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,从而 ${x}_{2} - {x}_{1} = 2\left( {2 - 2{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,即 $\left( {{x}_{1} - }\right.  {\left. {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} = {16}{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}$ ,将韦达定理代入 ${\left( \dfrac{4{k}^{2}}{{k}^{2} - 3}\right) }^{2} - 4 \times  \dfrac{4{k}^{2} + 3}{{k}^{2} - 3}  = {16}{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}$ ,化简得 $9\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  = 4{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}{\left( {k}^{2} - 3\right) }^{2}$ (※), 因为 $k = {k}_{Q{F}_{2}} = \dfrac{2{y}_{0}}{2{x}_{0} - 2} = \dfrac{{y}_{0}}{{x}_{0} - 1}$ ,即 ${y}_{0} = k\left( {{x}_{0} - 1}\right)$ ,由已知 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 在双曲线上,得 $3{x}_{0}^{2} - {y}_{0}^{2} = 3$ ,从而 $3{x}_{0}^{2} - \left\lbrack  {k\left( {{x}_{0} - }\right. }\right.  1){\rbrack }^{2} = 3$ ,化简得 ${k}^{2} = \dfrac{3{x}_{0}^{2} - 3}{{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{3\left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right) }{{x}_{0} - 1}$ 代入 (※) 式, $9 \times  \left( {\dfrac{3{x}_{0} + 3}{{x}_{0} - 1} + 1}\right)  = 4{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}{\left( \dfrac{3{x}_{0} + 3}{{x}_{0} - 1} - 3\right) }^{2}$ ,化简得 $9 \times  \dfrac{4{x}_{0} + 2}{{x}_{0} - 1} = 4{\left( {x}_{0} - 1\right) }^{2}{\left( \dfrac{6}{{x}_{0} - 1}\right) }^{2}$ ,即 $9 \times  \dfrac{4{x}_{0} + 2}{{x}_{0} - 1} = 4 \times  {36}$ ,解得 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,则点 $P$ 的坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{3}{2},\dfrac{\sqrt{15}}{2}}\right)$ .

( 1 )由题意得 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{c}{1} = 2$ ，则 $c = 2 \Longrightarrow  b = \sqrt{{2}^{2} - 1} = \sqrt{3}$ ； ( 2 )当 $b = \dfrac{2\sqrt{6}}{3}$ 时，双曲线 $\Gamma  : {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{\dfrac{8}{3}} = 1$ ，其中 $M\left( {-2,0}\right)$ ， ${A}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，因为 $\bigtriangleup  M{A}_{2}P$ 为等腰三角形，则

\dfrac{16}{11}$ (舍) $\Longrightarrow  {y}_{0}^{2} = 9 - 1 = 8 \Longrightarrow  {y}_{0} =  2\sqrt{2}$ ,即 $P\left( {2,2\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ; 综上, $P\left( {2,2\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ; (3)由题知 ${A}_{1}\left( {-1,0}\right) ,{A}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，当直线 $l \bot  y$ 轴时, $P\left( {1,0}\right) ,Q\left( {-1,0}\right)$ , $R\left( {1,0}\right)$ 或 $P\left( {-1,0}\right) ,Q\left( {1,0}\right) ,R( - }\right.$ 1) $- {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 1$ ,因为 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 在直线 $l$ 上,所以 ${x}_{1} = m{y}_{1} - 2,{x}_{2} = m{y}_{2} - 2$ ,即 $- \left( {m{y}_{2} - 3}\right) \left( {m{y}_{1} - {10} = 0$ ,所以 ${m}^{2} = \dfrac{10}{{b}^{2}} - 3$ ,代入到 ${b}^{2}{m}^{2} - 1 \neq  0$ ,得 ${10} -  3{b}^{2} \neq  1 \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2} \neq  3$ ,且 ${m}^{2} = \dfrac{10}{{b}^{2}} - 3 \geq  0$ ,解得 ${b}^{2} \leq  \dfrac{10}{3}$ ,又因为 $b >$ 0,所以 $0 < {b}^{2} \leq  \dfrac{10}{3}$ ,综上知, ${b}^{2} \in  \left( {0,3}\right)  \cup  \left( {3,\dfrac{10}{3}}\right\rbrack   \Longrightarrow  b \in  \left( {0,\sqrt{3}}\right)  \cup  \left( {\sqrt{3},\dfrac{\sqrt{30}}{3}}\right\rbrack  .$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/85_1285_672_243_192_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/85_1285_1170_243_191_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

点睛: 本题第三问的关键是采用设线法, 为了方便运算可设 $l : x = {my} - 2$ ,将其与双曲线方程联立得到韦达定理式,再写出相关向量，代入计算，要注意排除联立后的方程的二次项系数不为 0 .

\section*{考点 5 抛物线方程及其性质}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\left( {0,1}\right)$

【解析】抛物线 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ 的焦点在 $y$ 轴上,开口向上,且 ${2p} =$ 4,所以 $\dfrac{p}{2} = 1$ ,所以抛物线 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ 的焦点坐标为 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,故答案为: $\left( {0,1}\right)$ .

2. ${y}^{2} = {4x}$

【解析】由抛物线准线方程 $x =  - 1$ 可知,抛物线开口向右, 其中 $\dfrac{p}{2} = 1$ ,得 $p = 2$ ,所以抛物线标准方程为 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ . 故答案为: ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ .

3. 2

【解析】 ${x}^{2} = {my} = 2 \cdot  \dfrac{m}{2}y$ ,因为 $m$ 为正实数,则 $\dfrac{m}{4} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ , 则 $m = 2$ ,故答案为: 2 .

4. ${x}^{2} =  - {4y}$

【解析】抛物线的准线方程为 $y = 1$ ,则该抛物线的标准方程为: ${x}^{2} =  - {4y}$ ,故答案为: ${x}^{2} =  - {4y}$ .

5. $x =  - 2$

【解析】 ${y}^{2} = {8x},p = 4$ ,准线方程为 $x =  - \dfrac{p}{2} =  - 2$ .

6. $x =  - 1$

【解析】 ${y}^{2} = {4x} \Longrightarrow  p = 2,\dfrac{p}{2} = 1,l : x =  - 1$ .

7. 2

【解析】焦点 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,准线方程 $x =  - 1$ ,所以焦点到准线的距离是 2 .

8. 6

【解析】 $\left| {AB}\right|  = 2\left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  = 6$ .

9. $\dfrac{1}{4}$

【解析】抛物线 ${y}^{2} = x$ 的焦点为 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{4},0}\right)$ ,顶点为 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,所以该抛物线的焦点到其顶点的距离为 $\dfrac{1}{4}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{4}$ .

10. $y =  - 1$

【解析】因为抛物线的标准方程为 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ ,焦点在 $y$ 轴上, 所以 ${2p} = 4$ ,即 $p = 2$ ,所以 $\dfrac{p}{2} = 1$ ,所以准线方程为: $y =$ -1,故答案是: $y =  - 1$ .

11. 1

【解析】设抛物线 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ 上一点 $A\left( {\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4},y}\right)$ ，则点 $A$ 到点 $B\left( {1,0}\right)$ 的距离为 $d = \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} - 1\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{{y}^{4}}{16} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} + 1} =  \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} + 1\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} + 1$ ,因 $y \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则 ${y}^{2} \geq  0$ ,故当 $y = 0$ 时,抛物线 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ 上任一点 $A$ 到点 $B\left( {1,0}\right)$ 的距离最小值为 1 . 故答案为: 1 .

12. 9

【解析】抛物线 $C : {x}^{2} = {2my}$ 的准线为 $y =  - \dfrac{m}{2}$ ,由点 $P$ 到 $y$ 轴的距离为 3,得点 $P$ 的纵坐标 ${y}_{P} = \dfrac{9}{2m}$ ,由点 $P$ 到 $C$ 的焦点的距离为 5,得 $\dfrac{9}{2m} + \dfrac{m}{2} = 5$ ,解得 $m = 1$ 或 $m = 9$ ,而 $m >$ 2,所以 $m = 9$ . 故答案为:9.

13. $\left( {0,2}\right)$

【解析】由题意知焦点坐标为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{4}a}\right)$ ,准线为 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{4}a$ , 且知准线 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{4}a$ 到 $x$ 轴的距离为 2,则 $\dfrac{1}{4}a = 2 \Longrightarrow  a = 8$ , 则焦点坐标为 $\left( {0,2}\right)$ .

14. $4\sqrt{2}$

【解析】由 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ 知抛物线的准线方程为 $x =  - 1$ ,设点 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,由题意得 ${x}_{0} + 1 = 9 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{0} = 8$ ,代入抛物线方程 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ ,得 ${y}_{0}^{2} = {32}$ ,解得 ${y}_{0} =  \pm  4\sqrt{2}$ ,则点 $P$ 到 $x$ 轴的距离为 $4\sqrt{2}$ .

15. 5

【解析】因为 $M\left( {x,y}\right)$ 为抛物线 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ 上任意一点，所以 $x = \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4},y \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,所以 $x + {2y} + m = \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} + {2y} + m = \dfrac{1}{4}{\left( y + 4\right) }^{2} +  m - 4$ ,所以当 $y =  - 4$ 时 $x + {2y} + m$ 取得最小值 $m - 4$ ,依题意可得 $m - 4 = 1$ ,所以 $m = 5$ . 故答案为: 5 .

16. 2

【解析】由抛物线方程 ${y}^{2} = 4\sqrt{3}x$ 知, $F\left( {\sqrt{3},0}\right)$ ,又直线 $l$ 的法向量 $\vv{n} = \left( {1, - \sqrt{3}}\right)$ ，所以直线 $l$ 的方程为 $x - \sqrt{3}y -  \sqrt{3} = 0$ ,令 $x = 0$ ,得 $y =  - 1$ ,所以 $A\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ ,设 $B\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ , 由 $\vv{BF} = 2\vv{BA}$ ,得 $\left( {\sqrt{3} - {x}_{0}, - {y}_{0}}\right)  = 2\left( {-{x}_{0}, - 1 - {y}_{0}}\right)$ ,所以 ${x}_{0} =  - \sqrt{3},{y}_{0} =  - 2$ ,设双曲线方程 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1\left( {a,b > 0}\right)$ ,将 $\left( {-\sqrt{3}, - 2}\right)$ 代入得 $\dfrac{3}{{a}^{2}} - \dfrac{4}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ，因为抛物线 ${y}^{2} = 4\sqrt{3}x$ 的焦点 $F$ 是双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的右焦点,所以 ${a}^{2} + {b}^{2} = 3$ ,解得 $a = 1,b =  \sqrt{2}$ ，所以双曲线 $T$ 的实轴长 ${2a} = 2$ . 故答案为:2.

17. $2\sqrt{6} - 1$

【解析】设抛物线 ${y}^{2} = {8x}$ 上的点 $P$ 坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{8},y}\right)$ ,圆 ${\left( x - 5\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 的圆心为 $C\left( {5,0}\right)$ ,半径 $r = 1$ . 点 $P$ 到圆心 $C$ 的距离 $d = \sqrt{{\left( \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{8} - 5\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2}} = \sqrt{\dfrac{{y}^{4}}{64} - \dfrac{5}{4}{y}^{2} + {25} + {y}^{2}} =  \sqrt{\dfrac{{y}^{4}}{64} - \dfrac{1}{4}{y}^{2} + {25}}$ . 令 $t = {y}^{2}\left( {t \geq  0}\right)$ ,则 $d = \sqrt{\dfrac{{t}^{2}}{64} - \dfrac{1}{4}t + {25}}  = \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{64}{\left( t - 8\right) }^{2} + {24}}$ ,对其求最小值,根据二次函数性质,当 $t = 8$ 时, $d$ 最小为 $2\sqrt{6}$ . 则 $P$ 与 $Q$ 两点间最短距离为 ${d}_{\min } - r =  2\sqrt{6} - 1$ . 故答案为: $2\sqrt{6} - 1$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/86_1395_1896_182_242_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

18. $\dfrac{1}{3}$

【解析】如图所示,过点 $F$ 作 ${FH} \bot  {NM}$ 于点 $H$ ,显然抛物线 $\Gamma  : {y}^{2} = {4x}$ 的焦点为 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,准线为 $l : x =  - 1$ ,由抛物线定义有 $\left| {MF}\right|  = \left| {MN}\right|$ ,结合 $\angle {NFM} = {30}^{ \circ  }$ 得 $\angle {NMF} = {180}^{ \circ  } -  2 \times  {30}^{ \circ  } = {120}^{ \circ  }$ ,而 $\left| {MF}\right|  = \left| {MN}\right|  = {x}_{M} + 1$ , $\left| {MH}\right|  = \left| {MF}\right| \cos {60}^{ \circ  } = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{x}_{M} + 1}\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {MN}\right|  + \left| {MH}\right|  = {x}_{M} + 1 + \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{x}_{M} + 1}\right)  =  1 - \left( {-1}\right)  = 2 \Leftrightarrow  {x}_{M} = \dfrac{1}{3}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{3}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/87_633_285_186_234_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

19. ${y}^{2} = {8x}$

【解析】设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,因为 $\left| {BF}\right|  - \left| {AF}\right|  = 4$ , 所以 $\left( {{x}_{2} + \dfrac{p}{2}}\right)  - \left( {{x}_{1} + \dfrac{p}{2}}\right)  = 4$ ,所以 ${x}_{2} - {x}_{1} = 4$ ,又因为 $\left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {k}_{AB}^{2}} \times  \left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right|  = 4\sqrt{2}$ ,所以 ${k}_{AB}^{2} = 1$ ,因为 $A$ , $B$ 都在第一象限,所以 ${k}_{AB} = 1$ ,又因为 ${k}_{AB} = \dfrac{{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}} = \dfrac{{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}{\dfrac{{y}_{2}^{2}}{2p} - \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}}{2p}} =  \dfrac{2p}{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}} = 1$ 且 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = 4 \times  2 = 8$ ,所以 ${2p} = 8$ ,所以 $p = 4$ ,所以抛物线方程为 ${y}^{2} = {8x}$ ,故答案为: ${y}^{2} = {8x}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/87_578_716_243_281_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

20. $7 + \sqrt{29}$

【解析】如图所示曲线 ${C}_{1} : {y}^{2} =  {4x}\left( {x \leq  1}\right)$ ,曲线 ${C}_{2} : {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2}  = {29}\left( {x \geq  6}\right)$ ,把 $x = 6$ 代入 ${C}_{2}(6 -  1{)}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {29} \Longrightarrow  y =  \pm  2$ ,当 ${B}_{1},C,{D}_{1}$ 三点共线时, $A{B}_{1} + {B}_{1}C + C{D}_{1} =  2 + 5 + \sqrt{29} = 7 + \sqrt{29}$ ,即 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 周长的最小值为 $7 + \sqrt{29}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/87_576_1053_244_236_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{二、选择题}

21. D

【解析】 ${y}^{2} = {2px}\left( {p > 0}\right)$ 焦点在 $x$ 轴正半轴， $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{16} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} = 1$ 在 $x$ 轴正半轴交点为 $\left( {4,0}\right) ,\dfrac{p}{2} = 4,p = 8$ .

22. C

【解析】抛物线的标准方程为: ${x}^{2} = {2py}$ ,焦点坐标为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{p}{2}}\right)$ ,由题意得 $\dfrac{p}{2} = \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,所以 $p = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,所以抛物线的标准方程为: ${x}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{2}y$ . 故选: C.

23. A

【解析】由题意知,将点 $P\left( {2,2\sqrt{2}}\right)$ 代入方程 ${y}^{2} = {2px}$ ,即 $8 = {4p}$ ,得 $p = 2$ ,则抛物线 $C$ 的焦点 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ . 当点 $M$ 的坐标为 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ 时,点 $M$ 与抛物线的焦点重合,由抛物线的定义知, 必有 $d = \left| {PM}\right|$ ; 当 $d = \left| {PM}\right|$ 时,点 $M$ 的坐标不一定为 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,理由如下: 如图,连接 ${PF}$ ,当 $\left| {PF}\right|  = \left| {PM}\right|$ 时, $d =  \left| {PF}\right|  = \left| {PM}\right|$ . 因此 “点 $M$ 的坐标为 (1，0)”是 “ $d = \left| {PM}\right|$ ” 的充分不必要条件. 故选: A.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/87_595_1941_226_236_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{三、解答题}

24. ( 1 )由题设 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的离心率为 $\dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，且右焦点 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，即为 ${\Gamma }_{2} : {y}^{2} = {2px}\left( {p > 0}\right)$ 的焦点,所以 $\dfrac{p}{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  p = 2$ ,所以 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ : ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ ,综上,椭圆 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的离心率 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,抛物线 ${\Gamma }_{2} : {y}^{2} = {4x}$ ;

( 2 )由题设，知 $l : {y =  - \left( {x - 1}\right) }$ ，联立 ${\Gamma }_{1} : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ，则 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,所以 $7{x}^{2} - {8x} - 8  = 0$ ,显然 $\Delta  > 0$ ,则 ${x}_{A} + {x}_{B} = \dfrac{8}{7}$ ,则 ${y}_{A} + {y}_{B} = 2 - \left( {{x}_{A} + {x}_{B}}\right)  = \dfrac{6}{7}$ ,所以 ${AB}$ 中点 $M$ 的坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{4}{7},\dfrac{3}{7}}\right)$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/87_1289_495_241_255_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)依题意，若直线 ${AB}$ 斜率为 0，则 $\left| {AB}\right|  = 4$ ， $\left| {CD}\right|  = 4$ ，此时 ${S}_{ACBD} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right| \left| {CD}\right|  = 8$ ; 若直线 ${AB}$ 斜率不为 0,设直线 ${AB}$ 为 $y = k\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,则直线 ${CD}$ 为 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,且 $k \neq  0$ ,联立 $y = k\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 与 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,得 $\left( {3 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} -  8{k}^{2}x + 4{k}^{2} - {12} = 0$ ,且 $\Delta  > 0$ ,则 ${x}_{A} + {x}_{B} = \dfrac{8{k}^{2}}{3 + 4{k}^{2}},{x}_{A}{x}_{B} =  \dfrac{4\left( {{k}^{2} - 3}\right) }{3 + 4{k}^{2}}$ ,所以 $\left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}} \cdot  \left| {{x}_{A} - {x}_{B}}\right|  = \dfrac{{12}\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) }{3 + 4{k}^{2}}$ , 联立 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{k}\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 与 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ ,得 ${x}^{2} - \left( {2 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) x + 1 = 0$ , 且 $\Delta  > 0$ ,则 ${x}_{C} + {x}_{D} = 4{k}^{2} + 2,{x}_{C}{x}_{D} = 1$ ,所以 $\left| {CD}\right|  =  \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{{k}^{2}}} \cdot  \left| {{x}_{C} - {x}_{D}}\right|  = 4\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right)$ ,所以

${S}_{ACBD} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AB}\right| \left| {CD}\right|  = 6 \times  \dfrac{{\left( {k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{16}}{\dfrac{3}{4} + {k}^{2}}  = 6 \times  \left\lbrack  {\left( {{k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{{16}\left( {{k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}}\right) } + \dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack  \geq  6 \times  \left\lbrack  {2\sqrt{\left( {{k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{1}{{16}\left( {{k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}}\right) }} + \dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack   = 6,$

而 ${k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4} \geq  \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,即等号不成立,结合对勾函数性质知: ${S}_{ACBD} > 8$ ; 综上, ${S}_{ACBD} \geq  8$ , 在 ${k}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{4} \in  \left( {\dfrac{3}{4}, + \infty }\right)$ 上递增,即 ${S}_{ACBD} > 8$ ; 综上, ${S}_{ACBD} \geq  8$ , 且直线 ${AB}$ 斜率为 0,面积取得最小值为 8 .

25. (1)设 $A\left( {x,y}\right) \left( {x > 0,y > 0}\right)$ ，因为点 $A$ 在抛物线上，所以点 $A$ 到抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的焦点的距离等于它到抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的准线 $y =  - 1$ 的距离,所以 $y + 1 = 3$ ,则 $y = 2$ ,所以 $x = 2\sqrt{y} =  2\sqrt{2}$ ,故点 $A$ 的坐标是 $\left( {2\sqrt{2},2}\right)$ ;

(2)设 $B\left( {b,\dfrac{{b}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ ，则 $C\left( {{2b} - 4,\dfrac{{b}^{2}}{2} - 4}\right)$ ，由题意 ${2b} - 4 = 0$ ，所以 $b = 2$ ，所以点 $B$ 的坐标为 $\left( {2,1}\right)$ ，则 ${k}_{AB} = \dfrac{4 - 1}{4 - 2} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ，所以， 直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y - 1 = \dfrac{3}{2}\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,即直线 $l$ 的方程为 ${3x} -  {2y} - 4 = 0$ ,所以原点 $O$ 到直线 $l$ 的距离为 $d = \dfrac{4}{\sqrt{{3}^{2} + {\left( -2\right) }^{2}}}  = \dfrac{4\sqrt{13}}{13}$ ;

(3)设 $D\left( {{x}_{0},\dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ ，若直线 $l$ 的斜率不存在时，则直线 $l$ 与抛物线 $\Gamma$ 只有一个交点，不合乎题意，所以，直线 $l$ 斜率必然存在,设其方程为 $y - b = k\left( {x - a}\right)$ ,代入 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ 中,得 ${x}^{2} -  {4kx} + {4ka} - {4b} = 0$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},\dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  {4k},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = {4ka} - {4b}$ ,因为 ${DA} \bot  {DB}$ ,且 $\vv{DA} = \left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}}\right.$ , $\left. \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{0}^{2}}{4}\right) ,\vv{DB} = \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{0},\dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2} - {x}_{0}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ ,所以 $\vv{DA} \cdot  \vv{DB} = \left( {{x}_{1} - }\right.  \left. {x}_{0}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{0}}\right)  + \dfrac{\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} - {x}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + {x}_{0}}\right) }{16} = 0$ , 显然 ${x}_{1} - {x}_{0} \neq  0,{x}_{2} - {x}_{0} \neq  0$ ,则 $1 + \dfrac{\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{2} + {x}_{0}}\right) }{16} = 0$ , 所以 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) {x}_{0} + {x}_{0}^{2} + {16} = 0$ ,故 ${4ka} - {4b} + {4k}{x}_{0} +  {x}_{0}^{2} + {16} = 0$ ,即 $4\left( {a + {x}_{0}}\right) k + {x}_{0}^{2} + {16} - {4b} = 0$ . 由题意,得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} =  - a \\  {x}_{0}^{2} = {4b} - {16} \end{array}\text{ ,因此 }{a}^{2} = {4b} - {16}}\right.$ .

26. ( 1 )易知抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的焦点 $F$ 的坐标为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{p}{2}}\right)$ ，准线为 $y =  - \dfrac{p}{2}$ ,由抛物线的定义,得 $4 + \dfrac{p}{2} = \left| {MF}\right|  = 5 \Longrightarrow  p = 2$ ,故抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ . 将 $M\left( {m,4}\right)$ 代入 $\Gamma$ 的方程,得 $x =  \pm  4$ ,所以点 $M$ 的坐标为: $\left( {4,4}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-4,4}\right)$ ;

(2)由(1)知 $F\left( {0,1}\right)$ ，又由条件知直线 $l$ 的斜率存在，设直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + 1$ ,并设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则由 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + 1 \\  {x}^{2} = {4y} \end{array}\text{ ,得 }{x}^{2} - {4kx} - 4 = 0 \Longrightarrow  \Delta  = {16}\left( {{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  > 0,}\right.$ 且 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = {4k},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 4$ ,由抛物线的定义,可知 $\left| {AF}\right|  =  {y}_{1} + 1,\left| {BF}\right|  = {y}_{2} + 1$ ,又因圆 ${x}^{2} + {\left( y - 1\right) }^{2} = 1$ 的圆心为 $F\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,半径为 1,于是 $\left| {AC}\right|  = \left| {AF}\right|  - 1 = {y}_{1},\left| {BD}\right|  = \left| {FB}\right|  -  1 = {y}_{2} \Longrightarrow  \left| {AC}\right|  \cdot  \left| {BD}\right|  = {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} \cdot  \dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{1}{x}_{2}\right) }^{2}}{16} = 1;$

(3) 由 ${x}^{2} = {4y}$ ,得 $y = \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4}$ ,而 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{2}x$ ,故过点 $A  \left( {{x}_{1},\dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ 的抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的切线 ${l}_{1}$ 的方程为 $y - \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{4} = \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{2}(x -  \left. {x}_{1}\right)$ ,即 ${x}_{1}x - {2y} - \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2}}{2} = 0$  \ding{172} ,同理,过点 $B\left( {{x}_{2},\dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{4}}\right)$ 的抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的切线 ${l}_{2}$ 的方程为 ${x}_{2}x - {2y} - \dfrac{{x}_{2}^{2}}{2} = 0$  \ding{173} ,由 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 可得: ${x}_{P} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} = {2k},{y}_{P} =  \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) {x}_{P} - \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack   =  \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {{2k} \cdot  {4k} - \dfrac{{\left( 4k\right) }^{2} - 2\left( {-4}\right) }{2}}\right\rbrack  =  - 1$ ,即 $P\left( {{2k}, - 1}\right)$ ,得点 $P$ 到直线 $l : {kx} - y + 1 = 0$ 的距离为 $d = \dfrac{\left| k \cdot  {2k} - \left( -1\right)  + 1\right| }{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} =  2\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1} \Longrightarrow  {S}_{\bigtriangleup {ACP}} + {S}_{\bigtriangleup {BDP}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AC}\right|  \cdot  d + \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {BD}\right|  \cdot  d =  \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {\left| {AC}\right|  + \left| {BD}\right| }\right)  \cdot  d = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right)  \cdot  d = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}}{4}\right)  \cdot  d  = \dfrac{1}{8}\left\lbrack  {{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}\right\rbrack   \cdot  d = \dfrac{1}{8}\left( {{16}{k}^{2} + 8}\right)  \cdot  2\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1} =  2\left( {2{k}^{2} + 1}\right)  \cdot  \sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,故当 $k = 0$ ,即直线 $l : y = 1$ 时, ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {ACP}} + {S}_{\bigtriangleup {BDP}}$ 有最小值 2 .

27. (1) 因为 $t > 0$ ，所以点 $P\left( {s,t}\right)$ 在曲线 $y = 2\sqrt{x}$ 上运动， ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}$ ,所以切线 $l$ 的斜率为 $\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{s}}$ ,所以 $l$ 的直线方程为 $y -  2\sqrt{s} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{s}}\left( {x - s}\right)$ ,将 $s = 1$ 代入,则 $l$ 的直线方程为 $y = x + 1$ ; (2)设 $Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ，将 $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ 代入方程 $y - 2\sqrt{s} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{s}}\left( {x - s}\right)$ ， 得 $s = 1$ ,而直线 $m$ 的方程为 $y - 2\sqrt{s} =  - \sqrt{s}\left( {x - s}\right)$ ,将 $s = 1$ 代入，则直线 $m$ 的方程为 $y =  - x + 3$ ，联立 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ ，则 ${y}^{2} +  {4y} - {12} = 0$ ,由韦达定理得 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} =  - 4$ ,而 ${y}_{1} = t = 2\sqrt{s} =$ 2,所以 ${y}_{2} =  - 6$ ,所以三角形 ${OPQ}$ 的面积 $S = \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  3 \cdot  \lbrack 2 -  \left( {-6}\right) \rbrack  = {12}$ ;

(3)设 $Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,y - {2\sqrt{s}} =  - \sqrt{s}\left( {x - s}\right)$ 联立方程 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ ， 得 ${y}^{2} + \dfrac{4}{\sqrt{s}}y - 8 - {4s} = 0$ ,因为 $t = 2\sqrt{s}$ ,所以 ${y}_{2} =  - 2\sqrt{s} -  \dfrac{4}{\sqrt{s}}$ ,所以 ${x}_{2} = 4 + s + \dfrac{4}{s}$ ,因为 $P\left( {s,2\sqrt{s}}\right) ,Q\left( {4 + s + \dfrac{4}{s}}\right.$ , $\left. {-2\sqrt{s} - \dfrac{4}{\sqrt{s}}}\right)$ ,所以 ${\left| PQ\right| }^{2} = {16}\dfrac{{\left( 1 + s\right) }^{3}}{{s}^{2}}$ . 对于 $u = \dfrac{1 + s}{{s}^{\dfrac{2}{3}}} =  {s}^{-\dfrac{2}{3}} + {s}^{\dfrac{1}{3}},{u}^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{2}{3}{s}^{-\dfrac{5}{3}} + \dfrac{1}{3}{s}^{-\dfrac{2}{3}} =  - \dfrac{2}{3}{s}^{-\dfrac{5}{3}}\left( {1 - \dfrac{s}{2}}\right)$ ,因为 $s = 2,{u}^{\prime } = 0;0 < s < 2,{u}^{\prime } < 0;s > 2,{u}^{\prime } > 0$ ,所以 ${u}_{\min } = {2}^{-\dfrac{2}{3}} +  {2}^{\dfrac{1}{3}},{\left| PQ\right| }^{2} = {16}{u}^{3},{\left| PQ\right| }_{\min }^{2} = {16}{\left( {2}^{-\dfrac{2}{3}} + {2}^{\dfrac{1}{3}}\right) }^{3} = {16}\left( {\dfrac{1}{4} + }\right.  \left. {\dfrac{3}{2} + 3 + 2}\right)  = {108},{\left| PQ\right| }_{\min } = 6\sqrt{3}$ .

28. (1) 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = {2px} \\  {x}^{2} = {2py} \end{array}\right.$ ,由点 $K\left( {{x}_{K},{y}_{K}}\right)$ 在第一象限,得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{K} = {2p} \\  {y}_{K} = {2p} \end{array}\text{ ,由 }\left| {OK}\right|  = 4\sqrt{2}}\right.$ ,得 $2\sqrt{2}p = 4\sqrt{2}$ ,所以 $p = 2$ ;

(2)曲线 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 和 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 关于直线 $y = x$ 对称，取 $N$ 关于 $y = x$ 的对称点 ${N}^{\prime }$ ,则 ${N}^{\prime }$ 在曲线 ${y}^{2} = {4x}\left( {0 \leq  x \leq  4,y \geq  0}\right)$ 上,所以 ${\left( \left| MN\right|  + \left| MT\right| \right) }_{\min } = {\left( \left| M{N}^{\prime }\right|  + \left| MT\right| \right) }_{\min }$ ,又因为 $\left| {M{N}^{\prime }}\right|  +  \left| {MT}\right|  \geq  \left| {T{N}^{\prime }}\right|$ ,所以只需求 $T$ 到 ${y}^{2} = {4x}\left( {0 \leq  x \leq  4,y \geq  0}\right)$ 上动点 ${N}^{\prime }$ 的距离 $\left| {T{N}^{\prime }}\right|$ 的最小值,令 ${N}^{\prime }\left( {x,2\sqrt{x}}\right) (0 \leq  x \leq$ 4),则 $\left| {T{N}^{\prime }}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left( 4 - x\right) }^{2} + {4x}} = \sqrt{{x}^{2} - {4x} + {16}}$ ,当 $x = 2$ 时, $\left| {T{N}^{\prime }}\right|$ 的最小值为 $2\sqrt{3}$ ,所以 ${\left( \left| MN\right|  + \left| MT\right| \right) }_{\min } =  2\sqrt{3}$ ，所以(当 $M\left( {8 - 4\sqrt{2},8 - 4\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ， $N\left( {2\sqrt{2},2}\right)$ 时) $\left| {MN}\right|  +  \left| {MT}\right|$ 的最小值为 $2\sqrt{3}$ ；

(3)法 1:由(1)可得 $\left| {AC}\right|  = \dfrac{2\left| {{x}_{1} - {y}_{1}}\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \sqrt{2}\left| {{x}_{1} - \sqrt{{2p}{x}_{1}}}\right|  \left( {0 \leq  {x}_{1} \leq  {2p}}\right) ,\left| {BD}\right|  = \dfrac{2\left| {{x}_{2} - {y}_{2}}\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \sqrt{2}\left| {{x}_{2} - \sqrt{{2p}{x}_{2}}}\right|  \left( {{2p} < {x}_{2} \leq  8}\right)$ ,因此当 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{p}{2}$ 时, $m = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}p$ ,当 ${x}_{2} = 8$ 时, $t =  4\sqrt{2}\left( {2 - \sqrt{p}}\right)$ ,由 $\dfrac{m}{t} \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ ,得 $\dfrac{1}{2} \leq  \dfrac{p}{{16} - 8\sqrt{p}} \leq  2$ ,解得 ${16} - 8\sqrt{3} \leq  p \leq  {160} - {64}\sqrt{6}.$

法 2: 由 ${y}^{2} = {2px}\left( {0 \leq  x \leq  8,y \geq  0}\right)$ 得 $y = \sqrt{2px}\left( {0 \leq  x \leq  8}\right)$ , ${y}^{\prime } = p{\left( 2px\right) }^{-\dfrac{1}{2}}$ ,令 $p{\left( 2px\right) }^{-\dfrac{1}{2}} = 1$ 得 $x = \dfrac{p}{2}$ ,当 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{p}{2}$ 时, 点 $A\left( {\dfrac{p}{2},p}\right)$ 到直线 $y = x$ 的距离最大,此时, $m = {\left| AC\right| }_{\max } =  \dfrac{2\left| {\dfrac{1}{2}p - p}\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}p$ ,当 ${x}_{2} = 8$ 时,点 $B\left( {8,4\sqrt{p}}\right)$ 到直线 $y =  x$ 的距离最大,此时, $t = {\left| BD\right| }_{\max } = \dfrac{2\left| {8 - 4\sqrt{p}}\right| }{\sqrt{2}} = 4\sqrt{2}(2 -  \sqrt{p})$ ,由 $\dfrac{m}{t} \in  \left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ ,得 $\dfrac{1}{2} \leq  \dfrac{p}{{16} - 8\sqrt{p}} \leq  2$ ,解得 ${16} -  8\sqrt{3} \leq  p \leq  {160} - {64}\sqrt{6}$

29. ( 1 )由已知可得 ${2p} = 4$ ，即 $p = 2$ ，所以点 $F$ 的坐标为 $\left( {1,0}\right)$ ， 点 $F$ 到准线 $l$ 的距离为 2 ;

(2)由已知可知直线 ${l}_{1},{l}_{2}$ 的斜率均存在且不等于 0 并过点 $F\left( {1,0}\right)$ ,设 ${l}_{1}$ 的方程为 $y = k\left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {k \neq  0}\right)$ ,则 ${l}_{2}$ 的斜率为 $- \dfrac{1}{k}$ , 设 ${l}_{1}$ 与 $C$ 相交于 ${P}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,{P}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ , 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = {4x} \\  y = k\left( {x - 1}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ 得 ${k}^{2}{x}^{2} - \left( {2{k}^{2} + 4}\right) x +  {k}^{2} = 0$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{2{k}^{2} + 4}{{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = 1,\left| {{P}_{1}{P}_{2}}\right|  = {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} +  2 = \dfrac{2{k}^{2} + 4}{{k}^{2}} + 2 = \dfrac{4{k}^{2} + 4}{{k}^{2}}$ ,同理可得 $\left| {{Q}_{1}{Q}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{4{\left( -\dfrac{1}{k}\right) }^{2} + 4}{{\left( -\dfrac{1}{k}\right) }^{2}} =  4{k}^{2} + 4$ ，所以 $\dfrac{1}{\left| {P}_{1}{P}_{2}\right| } + \dfrac{1}{\left| {Q}_{1}{Q}_{2}\right| } = \dfrac{{k}^{2}}{4{k}^{2} + 4} + \dfrac{1}{4{k}^{2} + 4} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ；

30. ( 1 )抛物线 ${y}^{2} = {4x}$ 的准线为 $x =  - 1$ ，因为点 $A$ 到抛物线 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 焦点的距离为 2,所以点 $A$ 到抛物线 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 准线的距离为 2,所以点 $A$ 的横坐标为 1,故点 $A$ 的坐标为 $\left( {1,2}\right)$ ;

(2)设 $C\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ，则线段 ${AC}$ 的中点坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{4 + {x}_{0}}{2},\dfrac{4 + {y}_{0}}{2}}\right)$ ， 由题意, $\dfrac{4 + {y}_{0}}{2} = 0$ ,故 ${y}_{0} =  - 4$ ,所以 $C\left( {8, - 4}\right)$ ,所以直线 $l$ 的方程为: ${2x} + y - {12} = 0$ ,所以原点 $O$ 到直线 $l$ 的距离 $d =  \dfrac{12}{\sqrt{{2}^{2} + {1}^{2}}} = \dfrac{{12}\sqrt{5}}{5};$

(3)由题意，直线 $l$ 的斜率 $k$ 显然存在且 $k \neq  0$ ，设直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + b$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,D\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right) ,C  \left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ,由 $\vv{AB} = 2\vv{CD}$ ,得 ${y}_{3} - {y}_{1} = 2\left( {{y}_{2} - {y}_{4}}\right)$  \ding{172} ,

由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = {4x} \\  y = {kx} + b \end{array}\right.$ ,得: $\dfrac{k}{4}{y}^{2} - y + b = 0$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = \dfrac{4}{k},{y}_{1}{y}_{2} =  \dfrac{4b}{k}$ ,同理, ${y}_{3} + {y}_{4} = \dfrac{2}{k},{y}_{3}{y}_{4} = \dfrac{2b}{k}$ ,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = 2\left( {{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}\right)$  \ding{173} , ${y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 2{y}_{3}{y}_{4}$  \ding{174} ,由 \ding{172} 、 \ding{173} 得: ${y}_{2} =  - {y}_{3}$ ,代入 \ding{174} 得 ${y}_{1} =  - 2{y}_{4}$ ,代入 \ding{173} 得 $3{y}_{2} = 4{y}_{4}$ ，所以 $\dfrac{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {AOD}}}{{S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCE}}} = \dfrac{{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}{{y}_{3} - {y}_{4}} = \dfrac{-2{y}_{4} - \dfrac{4}{3}{y}_{4}}{-\dfrac{4}{3}{y}_{4} - {y}_{4}} = \dfrac{10}{7}$ .

31. ( 1 )准线为 $x =  - 1$ ，所以 ${x}_{A} = 2$ ，所以 $a = {y}_{A} = 2\sqrt{2}$ ；

(2) $A\left( {4,4}\right)$ ，设 $B\left( {b,0}\right)$ ，线段 ${AB}$ 的中点为 $\left( {\dfrac{4 + b}{2},2}\right)$ ，所以 $4 = 2\left( {4 + b}\right)  \Longrightarrow  b =  - 2$ ,即 $B\left( {-2,0}\right)$ ,所以直线 ${AB}$ 为 ${2x} -  {3y} + 4 = 0$ ,原点 $O$ 到直线 ${AB}$ 的距离 $d = \dfrac{4}{\sqrt{13}} = \dfrac{4\sqrt{13}}{13}$ ;

(3)设 $P\left( {\dfrac{{p}^{2}}{4},p}\right)$ ，因为 $A\left( {\dfrac{{a}^{2}}{4},a}\right)$ ，所以直线 ${AP} : {4x} - (a +  p)y + {ap} = 0$ ,所以 $Q\left( {-3,\dfrac{{ap} - {12}}{a + p}}\right) ,H\left( {-3,p}\right)$ ,所以 $\left| {HQ}\right|  = \left| {\dfrac{{ap} - {12}}{a + p} - p}\right|  = \dfrac{{p}^{2} + {12}}{a + p} > 4$ ,即 ${\left( p - 2\right) }^{2} > 4\left( {a - 2}\right)$ 对 $p \in  \left( {0,a}\right)  \cup  \left( {a, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立,当 $a = 2$ 时, $p \neq  2,{\left( p - 2\right) }^{2} >  4\left( {a - 2}\right)$ 成立; 当 $a - 2 < 0$ 时,即 $a < 2$ 时, ${\left( p - 2\right) }^{2} > 4(a -$ 2) 成立,此时 $0 < a < 2$ ; 所以 $a$ 的取值范围为 $(0,2\rbrack$ .

32. ( 1 )焦点 $F\left( {2,0}\right)$ ，斜率 $k = 1$ ，故直线 ${l}_{3}$ 的方程为 $y =  x - 2;$

( 2 )联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = {8x}, \\  y = {kx} + {m}_{1}, \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $x$ ,整理,得 $k{y}^{2} - {8y} + 8{m}_{1} = 0$ . 设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${y}_{1}{y}_{2} = \dfrac{8{m}_{1}}{k},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2}{y}_{2}^{2}}{64} =  \dfrac{{m}_{1}^{2}}{{k}^{2}}$ . 由 $\vv{O{A}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{O{B}_{1}} = 0$ ,即 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 0$ ,得 $\dfrac{{m}_{1}^{2}}{{k}^{2}} + \dfrac{8{m}_{1}}{k} = 0$ , 即 ${m}_{1} =  - {8k}$ ,直线 ${l}_{1} : y = {kx} - {8k}$ ,故直线 ${l}_{1}$ 过定点 $\left( {8,0}\right)$ ; (3)当 $k = 1$ 时， ${l}_{i} : {y = x + {m}_{i}}$ ，设 $T\left( {t,0}\right)$ ，则 ${d}_{i} = \dfrac{\left| t + {m}_{i}\right| }{\sqrt{2}} =  - \dfrac{t + {m}_{i}}{\sqrt{2}}$ . 由 ${d}_{2}^{2} = {d}_{1} \cdot  {d}_{3}$ ,得 ${\left( t + {m}_{2}\right) }^{2} = \left( {t + {m}_{1}}\right)  \cdot  \left( {t + {m}_{3}}\right)$ , 即 ${m}_{2}^{2} + 2{m}_{2}t = {m}_{1}{m}_{3} + \left( {{m}_{1} + {m}_{3}}\right) t$  \ding{172} ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = {8x} \\  y = x + {m}_{i}, \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $y$ ,整理,得 ${x}^{2} + 2\left( {{m}_{i} - 4}\right) x + {m}_{i}^{2} = 0$ . 由 $\Delta  = 4{\left( {m}_{i} - 4\right) }^{2} -  4{m}_{i}^{2} > 0$ ,得 ${m}_{i} < 2$ . 于是, $\left| {{A}_{i}{B}_{i}}\right|  = \sqrt{2} \cdot  \sqrt{4{\left( {m}_{i} - 4\right) }^{2} - 4{m}_{i}^{2}} =  8\sqrt{2 - {m}_{i}}$ . 由 ${S}_{2}^{2} = {S}_{1} \cdot  {S}_{3},{d}_{2}^{2} = {d}_{1} \cdot  {d}_{3}$ ,且 ${S}_{i} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ . $\left| {{A}_{i}{B}_{i}}\right|  \cdot  {d}_{i}$ ,得 ${\left| {A}_{2}{B}_{2}\right| }^{2} = \left| {{A}_{1}{B}_{1}}\right|  \cdot  \left| {{A}_{3}{B}_{3}}\right|$ ,从而 $2 - {m}_{2} =  \sqrt{\left( {2 - {m}_{1}}\right) \left( {2 - {m}_{3}}\right) }$ ,即 ${\left( 2 - {m}_{2}\right) }^{2} = \left( {2 - {m}_{1}}\right) \left( {2 - {m}_{3}}\right)$ ,化简, 得 ${m}_{2}^{2} - 4{m}_{2} = {m}_{1}{m}_{3} - 2\left( {{m}_{1} + {m}_{3}}\right)$  \ding{173} , \ding{172}  \ding{173} 相减,整理,得 $(t +$ 2) $\left( {2{m}_{2} - {m}_{1} - {m}_{3}}\right)  = 0$ . 而 $2\left( {2 - {m}_{2}}\right)  = 2\sqrt{\left( {2 - {m}_{1}}\right) \left( {2 - {m}_{3}}\right) } <  \left( {2 - {m}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {2 - {m}_{3}}\right)$ ,即 $2{m}_{2} > {m}_{1} + {m}_{3}$ ,故 $t + 2 = 0$ ,即 $t =$ -2 . 又当 $t =  - 2$ 时,比如取 ${m}_{1} =  - 1,{m}_{3} = 1,{m}_{2} = 2 - \sqrt{3}$ 满足题意，故存在点 $T\left( {-2,0}\right)$ 满足题意.

33. ( 1 )因抛物线方程为 ${y}^{2} = x$ ，则焦点坐标为 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{4},0}\right)$ ；

(2)证明:设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 、 $C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ 、 $D\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ . 若 ${x}_{1} = {x}_{2},{x}_{3} = {x}_{4}$ ,则直线 ${AB},{CD}$ 斜率不存在,由对称性,可知 $M,N,H$ 均在 $x$ 轴上,则 $M,H,N$ 三点共线; 若 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ , ${x}_{3} \neq  {x}_{4}$ ,则直线斜率存在,直线 ${AB}$ 方程为: $y = \dfrac{{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}(x -  \left. {x}_{1}\right)  + {y}_{1}$ ,结合 ${y}_{1}^{2} = {x}_{1},{y}_{2}^{2} = {x}_{2}$ ,则 $y = \dfrac{{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}}{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\left( {x - {x}_{1}}\right)  + {y}_{1}  \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{\left( {y - {y}_{1}}\right) \left( {{y}_{2}^{2} - {y}_{1}^{2}}\right) }{{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}} + {y}_{1}^{2} = x \Longrightarrow  \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}\right) y - {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = x$ ,同理可得 ${CD}$ 方程: $\left( {{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}\right) y - {y}_{3}{y}_{4} = x,{AC}$ 方程: $\left( {{y}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {y}_{3}\right) y - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} = x,{BD}$ 方程: $\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right) y - {y}_{2}{y}_{4} = x$ . 设 $M\left( {{x}_{M},{y}_{M}}\right) ,N\left( {{x}_{N},{y}_{N}}\right)$ ,因 ${AB}//{CD}$ ,则 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = {y}_{3} + {y}_{4} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}{2} \Longrightarrow  {y}_{M} = {y}_{N}$ . 则直线 ${MN}$ 与 $x$ 轴平行,设直线 ${MN}$ 与线段 ${AC},{BD}$ 交点为 $P\left( {{x}_{P},{y}_{P}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{Q},{y}_{Q}}\right)$ . 将 $y = \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{2}$ 代入直线 ${AC}$ 方程,则 ${x}_{P} = \left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{1} + {y}_{2}}{2} -  {y}_{1}{y}_{3} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3}}{2}$ ; 将 $y = \dfrac{{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}{2}$ 代入直线 ${BD}$ 方程,则 ${x}_{Q} = \left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{{y}_{3} + {y}_{4}}{2} - {y}_{2}{y}_{4} =  \dfrac{{y}_{4}^{2} + {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  - {y}_{2}{y}_{4}}{2}$ . 注意到 ${x}_{P} - {x}_{Q} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} - {y}_{4}^{2} - {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  + {y}_{2}{y}_{4}}{2}  = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} - \left( {{y}_{4}^{2} + {y}_{3}{y}_{4} - {y}_{2}{y}_{4}}\right) }{2}  = \dfrac{{y}_{1}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{2} - {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{4}\left( {{y}_{4} + {y}_{3} - {y}_{2}}\right) }{2}  = \dfrac{{y}_{1}{y}_{4} - {y}_{4}{y}_{1}}{2} = 0,$

又 ${y}_{P} = {y}_{Q} = {y}_{M} = {y}_{N}$ ,则 $P,Q$ 两点重合,即 $P,Q$ 为线段 ${AC}$ 与 ${BD}$ 交点 $H$ ,且点 $M,H,N$ 三点共线; (3)由(2)，直线 ${MN}$ 与 $x$ 轴平行，则 $2\left| {HM}\right|  = \left| {HN}\right|  = 2 \Longrightarrow  2\left( {{x}_{H} - {x}_{M}}\right)  = {x}_{N} - {x}_{H} = 2$ . 又 ${x}_{M} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}^{2}}{2}$ ,同理可得 ${x}_{N} = \dfrac{{y}_{3}^{2} + {y}_{4}^{2}}{2}$ ,又由 $\left( 2\right) {x}_{H} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3}}{2} =  \dfrac{{y}_{4}^{2} + {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  - {y}_{2}{y}_{4}}{2}$ ,则 ${x}_{H} - {x}_{M} = \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3}}{2}  - \dfrac{{y}_{1}^{2} + {y}_{2}^{2}}{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} - {y}_{2}^{2} = 2,{x}_{N} - {x}_{H} = 2 =  \dfrac{{y}_{3}^{2} + {y}_{4}^{2}}{2} - \dfrac{{y}_{4}^{2} + {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  - {y}_{2}{y}_{4}}{2} \Longrightarrow  {y}_{3}^{2} - {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{4}}\right)  + {y}_{2}{y}_{4} =$ 4,由 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} = {y}_{3} + {y}_{4}$ ,则 ${y}_{3}^{2} - {y}_{3}\left( {{y}_{2} + {y}_{1} + {y}_{2} - {y}_{3}}\right)  + {y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + }\right.  \left. {{y}_{2} - {y}_{3}}\right)  = 4$ ,即 $2{y}_{3}^{2} + {y}_{2}^{2} - 3{y}_{2}{y}_{3} - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} = 4$ . 则 $2{y}_{3}^{2} +  {y}_{2}^{2} - 3{y}_{2}{y}_{3} - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} + {y}_{1}{y}_{2} - \left\lbrack  {{y}_{2}\left( {{y}_{1} + {y}_{3}}\right)  - {y}_{1}{y}_{3} - {y}_{2}^{2}}\right\rbrack   = 2 \Longrightarrow  2{y}_{3}^{2} + 2{y}_{2}^{2} - 4{y}_{2}{y}_{3} = 2 \Longrightarrow  {\left( {y}_{3} - {y}_{2}\right) }^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  \left| {{y}_{3} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = 1$ . 如图，过 $B$ 作 ${MN}$ 平行线，交 ${CD}$ 为 $E$ ，则四边形 ${MBEN}$ 为平行四边形， 结合 $2\left| {HM}\right|  = \left| {HN}\right|  = 2$ ，则 $\left| {BE}\right|  =$ 3, ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCE}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {BE}\right|  \cdot  \left| {{y}_{3} - {y}_{2}}\right|  = \dfrac{3}{2}$ . 因 ${AB}//{CD}$ ，则 ${\bigtriangleup {AHB}} \backsim  {\bigtriangleup {CHD}}$ ，结合 $2\left| {HM}\right|  = \left| {HN}\right|$ ,则 $\dfrac{\left| AB\right| }{\left| CD\right| } = \dfrac{\left| MH\right| }{\left| NH\right| } = \dfrac{\left| AH\right| }{\left| CH\right| } = \dfrac{\left| BH\right| }{\left| DH\right| } =  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,又 $M$ 为 ${AB}$ 中点, $N$ 为 ${CD}$ 中点,则 $E$ 为 ${CN}$ 中点. 则 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCE}} = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {BNE}} = \dfrac{1}{4}{S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCD}},{S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCD}} = 2{S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABD}}$ ,则四边形 ${ABCD}$ 的面积 $S = {S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCD}} + {S}_{\bigtriangleup {ABD}} = 6{S}_{\bigtriangleup {BCE}} = 6 \times  \dfrac{3}{2} = 9$ .
