\end{center}
 

\section*{考点 6 直线与圆锥曲线的位置关系及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/90_1340_1362_236_183_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

1. 5

【解析】如图,取双曲线右焦点 ${F}^{\prime }$ , 连接 $P{F}^{\prime }$ ,由题知, $a = 3,b = 4$ ,所以 $c = 5$ ,因为 $O$ 为 $F{F}^{\prime }$ 中点, $T$ 为 ${PF}$ 的中点,所以 ${TO}$ 为 $\bigtriangleup {PF}{F}^{\prime }$ 的中位线,可得 $\left| {OT}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {P{F}^{\prime }}\right| ,\left| {FT}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {PF}\right|$ . 又 $\left| {MT}\right|  = \left| {FT}\right|  -  \left| {FM}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {PF}\right|  - \left| {FM}\right|$ ,所以 $\left| {OT}\right|  - \left| {MT}\right|  = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( \left| {P{F}^{\prime }}\right| \right.  - \left| {PF}\right| ) + \left| {FM}\right|  = \left| {FM}\right|  - 3,\left| {FM}\right|  = \sqrt{{\left| OF\right| }^{2} - {3}^{2}} = 4$ , 所以 $\left| {OT}\right|  - \left| {MT}\right|  = 1$ ,又 ${\left| OT\right| }^{2} = {\left| MT\right| }^{2} + {\left| OM\right| }^{2}$ ,所以 ${\left| OT\right| }^{2} = {\left( \left| OT\right|  - 1\right) }^{2} + 9$ ,解得 $\left| {OT}\right|  = 5$ . 故答案为: 5 . 2. $\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 1$

【解析】方法 $1 : a,b,c,d > 0,{a}^{2} - {ab} + 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  b = a + \dfrac{1}{a}$ , 可知点 $M\left( {a,b}\right)$ 是函数 $y = x + \dfrac{1}{x}$ 的图像上一点,由 ${c}^{2} + {d}^{2}  = 1$ 可知点 $N\left( {c,d}\right)$ 是单位圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 上一点,如图所示, 函数 $y = x + \dfrac{1}{x}$ 的图像是 $y$ 轴和直线 $y = x$ 为渐近线的双曲线, 焦点位于两条渐近线所夹锐角的平分线上, 设焦点所在

直线为 $y = {kx}$ ,满足 $k = \tan \dfrac{3\pi }{8} =  \sqrt{2} + 1$ ,即焦点所在直线为 $y = (\sqrt{2} +$ 1) $x$ ,由双曲线与圆的性质,可知, 当点 $N\left( {c,d}\right)$ 为单位圆 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} =$ 1 与 $y = \left( {\sqrt{2} + 1}\right) x$ 的交点，点 $M  \left( {a,b}\right)$ 为双曲线在第一象限的顶点时, 它们之间的距离最小, 同时 $+ \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,故答案为: $1 + \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/91_553_154_261_314_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

${\left( a - c\right) }^{2} + {\left( b - d\right) }^{2}$ 也取得最小值. 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = x + \dfrac{1}{x} \\  y = \left( {\sqrt{2} + 1}\right) x \end{array}\right.$ 消去 $y$ ,

整理得 ${x}^{2} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,即 ${a}^{2} = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,所以点 $M\left( {a,b}\right) ,{ab} = {a}^{2} + 1 = 1$

方法 2: 可将 ${\left( a - c\right) }^{2} + {\left( b - d\right) }^{2}$ 转化为 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 与 $\left( {c,d}\right)$ 两点间距离的平方,由 ${a}^{2} - {ab} + 1 = 0$ ,得 $b = a + \dfrac{1}{a}$ ,而 ${c}^{2} + {d}^{2} = 1$ 表示以 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ 为圆心,1 为半径的圆, $\left( {c,d}\right)$ 为圆上一点,则 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 与圆心 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ 的距离为: $\sqrt{{a}^{2} + {b}^{2}} = \sqrt{{a}^{2} + {\left( a + \dfrac{1}{a}\right) }^{2}} =  \sqrt{2{a}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} + 2} \geq  \sqrt{2\sqrt{2{a}^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} + 2}} = \sqrt{2\sqrt{2} + 2}$ ,当且仅当 $2{a}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ ,即 $a =  \pm  \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2}}$ 时等号成立,此时 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 与圆心 $(0$ , 0) 的距离最小,即 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 与 $\left( {c,d}\right)$ 两点间距离的平方最小,即 ${\left( a - c\right) }^{2} + {\left( b - d\right) }^{2}$ 取得最小值. 当 $a = \sqrt[4]{\dfrac{1}{2}}$ 时, ${ab} = {a}^{2} + 1 =  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 1$ ,故答案为: $\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} + 1$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

3. D

【解析】排除法: 设动点为 $Q$ , \ding{172}  当点 $A$ 在圆内不与圆心 $C$ 重合,连接 ${CQ}$ 并延长,交于圆上一点 $B$ ,由题意知 ${QB} =  {QA}$ ,又 ${QB} + {QC} = R$ ,所以 ${QA} + {QC} = R$ ,即 $Q$ 的轨迹为一椭圆; 如图.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/91_604_1556_214_219_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

 \ding{173}  如果是点 $A$ 在圆 $C$ 外,由 ${QC} - R =  {QA}$ ,得 ${QC} - {QA} = R$ ,为一定值,即 $Q$ 的轨迹为双曲线的一支;

 \ding{174}  当点 $A$ 与圆心 $C$ 重合,要使 ${QB} =  {QA}$ ,则 $Q$ 必然在与圆 $C$ 的同心圆,即 $Q$ 的轨迹为一圆; 则本题选 D. 故选 D.

\section*{三、解答题}

4. ( 1 ) $m = 2 \Longrightarrow  \Gamma  : \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} = 1,a = 2,c = 1,e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ；

(2) ${A}_{1}\left( {m,0}\right)$ ， ${A}_{2}\left( {-m,0}\right)$ ，设 $E\left( {p,1}\right)$ ， 所以 $\dfrac{{p}^{2}}{{m}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{3} = 1$ ,即 ${p}^{2} = \dfrac{2}{3}{m}^{2}$ ,由 $\vv{E{A}_{1}} = \left( {m - p, - 1}\right) ,\vv{E{A}_{2}} = ( - m - p,$ -1),所以 $\vv{E{A}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{E{A}_{2}} = {p}^{2} - {m}^{2} + 1 =$ -2,因为 ${p}^{2} = \dfrac{2}{3}{m}^{2}$ ,代入求得 $m = 3$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/91_583_1908_243_264_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)设直线 $y = \sqrt{3}x + t$ ，联立椭圆可得 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{m}^{2}} + \dfrac{{\left( \sqrt{3}x + t\right) }^{2}}{3} = 1$ ， 整理得 $\left( {3{m}^{2} + 3}\right) {x}^{2} + 2\sqrt{3}t{m}^{2}x + \left( {{t}^{2} - 3}\right) {m}^{2} = 0$ ，由 $\Delta  \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  {t}^{2} \leq  3{m}^{2} + 3$  \ding{172} ，联立双曲线可得 $\dfrac{{\left( \sqrt{3}x + t\right) }^{2}}{5{m}^{2}} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{5} = 1$ ，整理得 $\left( {3 - {m}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + 2\sqrt{3}{tx} + \left( {{t}^{2} - 5{m}^{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,由 $\Delta  = 0 \Longrightarrow  {t}^{2} = 5{m}^{2}$ -15 \ding{173} ,结合 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 可得， $5{m}^{2} - {15} \leq  3{m}^{2} + 3 \Longrightarrow   - 3 \leq  m \leq  3$ , $5{m}^{2} - {15} \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  m \geq  \sqrt{3}$ ,因为 $m \neq  \sqrt{3}$ ,综上所述, $m \in  (\sqrt{3},3\rbrack$ .

5. (1) 由题意， ${F}_{1}\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ 、 ${F}_{2}\left( {1,0}\right)$ ，所以直线 $l$ 的方程是 $y = 1$ ,代入 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 中,得 $x =  \pm  \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,所以 $\left| {AB}\right|  = 3$ ;

(2)设 $A\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) \left( {{x}_{0} < 0}\right)$ ，则 $\vv{{F}_{1}A} = \left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0} + 1}\right) ,\vv{{F}_{2}A} =  \left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0} - 1}\right)$ ; 所以 $\vv{{F}_{1}A} \cdot  \vv{{F}_{2}A} = {x}_{0}^{2} + \left( {{y}_{0} + 1}\right) \left( {{y}_{0} - 1}\right)  =  \dfrac{9}{4}$ ,又 $\dfrac{{y}_{0}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{3}{2}, \\  {y}_{0} =  \pm  1. \end{array}\right.$ 所以 $A$ 点坐标是 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{2},1}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-\dfrac{3}{2}, - 1}\right)$ ,所以直线 $l$ 的方程是 $y = 1$ 或 $y =  \dfrac{4}{3}x + 1$

(3)当直线 $l$ 的斜率存在时,设直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y = {kx} + 1$ ，代入 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{3} = 1$ 中,得 $\left( {3{k}^{2} + 4}\right) {x}^{2} + {6kx} - 9 = 0$ ,此时 $\Delta  = {144}{k}^{2} + {144} > 0$ ,设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  \dfrac{-{6k}}{3{k}^{2} + 4}$ ,所以 ${AB}$ 中点 $M\left( \dfrac{-{3k}}{3{k}^{2} + 4}\right.$ , $\left. \dfrac{4}{3{k}^{2} + 4}\right)$ . 又 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心在 $x$ 轴上,所以 ${y}_{1} + {y}_{2} + {y}_{3} = 0$ , 即 $k\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  + 2 + {y}_{3} = 0$ ,故 ${y}_{3} =  - \dfrac{8}{3{k}^{2} + 4}$ ,因为 $\left| {CA}\right|  =  \left| {CB}\right|$ ,所以 ${MC} \bot  {AB}$ ,所以 $\vv{MC} \cdot  \vv{AB} = \left( {{x}_{3} + \dfrac{3k}{3{k}^{2} + 4},{y}_{3} - }\right.  \left. \dfrac{4}{3{k}^{2} + 4}\right)  \cdot  \left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2},{y}_{1} - {y}_{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,因为 ${x}_{1} - {x}_{2} \neq  0$ ,所以 ${x}_{3}  + \dfrac{3k}{3{k}^{2} + 4} + \left( {{y}_{3} - \dfrac{4}{3{k}^{2} + 4}}\right) k = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{3} = \dfrac{9k}{3{k}^{2} + 4}$ ,所以 $C\left( {\dfrac{9k}{3{k}^{2} + 4}, - \dfrac{8}{3{k}^{2} + 4}}\right)$ ,因为点 $C$ 在椭圆上,所以 $\dfrac{{\left( -\dfrac{8}{3{k}^{2} + 4}\right) }^{2}}{4}  + \dfrac{{\left( \dfrac{9k}{3{k}^{2} + 4}\right) }^{2}}{3} = 1$ ,解得 $k = 0$ 或 $k =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}$ . 当直线 $l$ 的斜率不存在时,直线 $l$ 的方程为 $x = 0$ ,此时 $A,B$ 恰为长轴顶点,点 $C$ 为短轴顶点,满足题意. 综上所述,存在直线 $l$ 使得 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 的重心在 $x$ 轴上,其方程为: $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}x + 1$ 或 $y = 1$ 或 $x = 0$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/91_1292_942_236_259_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

6. ( 1 )由条件知 $b = 1,c = \sqrt{3}$ ，所以 ${a}^{2} = {b}^{2} + {c}^{2} = 4$ ，于是椭圆 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1$ ;

(2)由条件知: 直线 ${PA}$ 的斜率为 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1$ ,

则由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = \dfrac{1}{2}x + 1, \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得, ${x}^{2} + {2x} = 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{A} =  - 2$ . 从而 $A\left( {-2,0}\right)$ . 由于 ${k}_{PA} \cdot  {k}_{PB} = 1$ ,所以直线 ${PB}$ 的方程为 $y =  {2x} + 1$ ,同理可得 $B\left( {-\dfrac{16}{17}, - \dfrac{15}{17}}\right)$ . 所以直线 $l$ 的斜率为 ${k}_{AB} =  \dfrac{0 - \left( {-\dfrac{15}{17}}\right) }{-2 - \left( {-\dfrac{16}{17}}\right) } =  - \dfrac{5}{6}$ ,从而直线 $l$ 的方程为 $y - 0 =  - \dfrac{5}{6}(x +$ 2),即 $y =  - \dfrac{5}{6}x - \dfrac{5}{3}$ (或 ${5x} + {6y} + {10} = 0$ );

(3)假设存在满足条件的直线 $l$ ，并设直线 ${PA}$ 的方程为 $y =  {mx} + 1\left( {m \neq  0}\right)$ ,则由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {mx} + 1, \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ 得 $\left( {4{m}^{2} + 1}\right) {x}^{2} + {8mx} = 0$ , 所以 ${x}_{A} =  - \dfrac{8m}{4{m}^{2} + 1}$ . 由于 ${k}_{PA} \cdot  {k}_{PB} = 1$ ,所以直线 ${PB}$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{m}x + 1$ ,同理可得 ${x}_{B} =  - \dfrac{8 \cdot  \dfrac{1}{m}}{4{\left( \dfrac{1}{m}\right) }^{2} + 1} =  - \dfrac{8m}{{m}^{2} + 4}$ , 故直线 $l$ 的斜率为 $k = \dfrac{{y}_{A} - {y}_{B}}{{x}_{A} - {x}_{B}} = \dfrac{\left( {m{x}_{A} + 1}\right)  - \left( {\dfrac{1}{m}{x}_{B} + 1}\right) }{{x}_{A} - {x}_{B}} = \dfrac{m{x}_{A} - \dfrac{1}{m}{x}_{B}}{{x}_{A} - {x}_{B}}  = \dfrac{m\left( {-\dfrac{8m}{4{m}^{2} + 1}}\right)  - \dfrac{1}{m}\left( {-\dfrac{8m}{{m}^{2} + 4}}\right) }{\left( {-\dfrac{8m}{4{m}^{2} + 1}}\right)  - \left( {-\dfrac{8m}{{m}^{2} + 4}}\right) } = \dfrac{-\dfrac{{m}^{2}}{4{m}^{2} + 1} + \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2} + 4}}{-\dfrac{m}{4{m}^{2} + 1} + \dfrac{m}{{m}^{2} + 4}}  = \dfrac{4{m}^{2} + 1 - {m}^{2}\left( {{m}^{2} + 4}\right) }{m\left\lbrack  {\left( {4{m}^{2} + 1}\right)  - \left( {{m}^{2} + 4}\right) }\right\rbrack  } =  - \dfrac{{m}^{4} - 1}{{3m}\left( {{m}^{2} - 1}\right) } =  - \dfrac{{m}^{2} + 1}{3m}  =  - \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {m + \dfrac{1}{m}}\right) ,$

当 $\bigtriangleup {PAB}$ 为直角三角形时,只有可能 $\angle {PAB} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ,或 $\angle {PBA} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ,于是 $k =  - \dfrac{1}{m}$ ,或 $k =  - m$ . 若 $k =  - \dfrac{1}{m}$ ,由 $- \dfrac{1}{m} =  - \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {m + \dfrac{1}{m}}\right)$ ,可得 $m =  \pm  \sqrt{2}$ ,从而 $k =  \mp  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ; 若 $k =  - m$ ,由 $- m =  - \dfrac{1}{3}\left( {m + \dfrac{1}{m}}\right)$ ,可得 $m =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,也有 $k =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ . 因此,直线 $l$ 的斜率为 $\pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ .

7. ( 1 )由题意，得 ${a}^{2} = 1$ ， ${b}^{2} = 4 \Longrightarrow  {c}^{2} = {a}^{2} + {b}^{2} = 5$ ，因此，双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的离心率 $e = \dfrac{c}{a} = \sqrt{5}$ ;

(2)由直线 $l$ 与圆 $O$ 相切，得 $\dfrac{\left| b\right| }{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} = 1$ ，即 ${b}^{2} = {k}^{2} + 1$ ，联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {b}^{2}{x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = {b}^{2} \\  y = {kx} + b \end{array}\right.$ ,得 $\left( {{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - {2kbx} - 2{b}^{2} = 0$ ,即 ${x}^{2} -  {{2kbx} - 2{b}^{2} = 0},$ 因为 $\Delta  = 4{k}^{2}{b}^{2} + 8{b}^{2} > 0$ ，所以有两个不等的实根,且两根之积为 $- 2{b}^{2} < 0$ ,因此两根一正一负,即 $l$ 与双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的左右两支各有一个公共点;

(3)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 、 $C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ 、 $D\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ，联立 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1 \\  y = {kx} + b \end{array}\text{ ,得 }\left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {2kbx} + }\right.  {b}^{2} - 1 = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = \dfrac{-{2kb}}{1 + {k}^{2}} \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{{b}^{2} - 1}{1 + {k}^{2}} \end{array},{\Delta }_{1} = }\right.  4{k}^{2}{b}^{2} + 4\left( {1 - {b}^{2}}\right) \left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right)  > 0 \Longrightarrow  {k}^{2}{b}^{2}  + 1 + {k}^{2} - {b}^{2} - {b}^{2}{k}^{2} > 0 \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2} - {k}^{2} < 1$ . 联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {b}^{2}{x}^{2} - {y}^{2} = {b}^{2} \\  y = {kx} + b \end{array}\right.$ , 得 $\left( {{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} - {2kbx} - 2{b}^{2} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{3} + {x}_{4} = \dfrac{2kb}{{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}} \\  {x}_{3}{x}_{4} = \dfrac{-2{b}^{2}}{{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}} \end{array}\right.$ ,由 ${x}_{3} <  0 < {x}_{4} \Longrightarrow$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/92_1337_159_239_223_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

$\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\Delta }_{2} = 4{k}^{2}{b}^{2} + 8{b}^{2}\left( {{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}}\right)  > 0 \Longrightarrow  {k}^{2}{b}^{2} + 2{b}^{4} - 2{b}^{2}{k}^{2} \Longrightarrow  {b}^{2} - {k}^{2} > 0 \\  {b}^{2} - {k}^{2} > 0 \end{array},}\right.$

又因为 $\vv{PA} \cdot  \vv{PC} = \vv{PB} \cdot  \vv{PD}$ ,又 $C,A,B,D$ 四个点在同一直线上,所以 $\left| \vv{PA}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{PC}\right|  = \left| \vv{PB}\right|  \cdot  \left| \vv{PD}\right|  \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{\left| PA\right| }{\left| PB\right| } = \dfrac{\left| PD\right| }{\left| PC\right| }  \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{\left| {x}_{1}\right| }{\left| {x}_{2}\right| } = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{4}\right| }{\left| {x}_{3}\right| } \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{4}}{{x}_{3}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}} = \dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{4}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} + \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{{x}_{1}} = \dfrac{{x}_{4}}{{x}_{3}} + \dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{4}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}{{x}_{3}{x}_{4}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}\right) }^{2}}{{x}_{3}{x}_{4}}$ ,

$\Longrightarrow  \dfrac{\dfrac{4{k}^{2}{b}^{2}}{{\left( 1 + {k}^{2}\right) }^{2}}}{\dfrac{{b}^{2} - 1}{1 + {k}^{2}}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{4{k}^{2}{b}^{2}}{{\left( {b}^{2} - {k}^{2}\right) }^{2}}}{\dfrac{-2{b}^{2}}{{b}^{2} - {k}^{2}}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{2{b}^{2}}{{k}^{2} + 1} = \dfrac{{b}^{2} - 1}{{k}^{2} - {b}^{2}},k = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ 代入,得 $\dfrac{2{b}^{2}}{\dfrac{1}{2} + 1} = \dfrac{{b}^{2} - 1}{\dfrac{1}{2} - {b}^{2}} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{2{b}^{2}}{3} = \dfrac{{b}^{2} - 1}{1 - 2{b}^{2}} \Longrightarrow  2{b}^{2} - 4{b}^{4} = 3{b}^{2} - 3 \Longrightarrow  4{b}^{4}  + {b}^{2} - 3 = 0$ ,解得 ${b}^{2} = \dfrac{3}{4} \Longrightarrow  b =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ (负舍),经检验,此时 $l$ 与 $\Gamma$ 两支分别有交点,故 $b = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ 为唯一满足条件的实数 $b$ .

点睛:本题第三问的关键是多次联立，得到韦达定理，再将向量式化简得 $\dfrac{\left| {x}_{1}\right| }{\left| {x}_{2}\right| } = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{4}\right| }{\left| {x}_{3}\right| }$ ,即 $\dfrac{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2}}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{\left( {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}\right) }^{2}}{{x}_{3}{x}_{4}}$ ,再代入韦达定理式计算即可.

8. (1) $a = 2,b = 1 \Longrightarrow  c = \sqrt{3}$ ,当 $P{F}_{1} \bot  {F}_{1}{F}_{2}$ 时, $\left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  =  \left| {y}_{P}\right|  = \sqrt{1 - \dfrac{{\left( -\sqrt{3}\right) }^{2}}{4}} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,故 $\left| {P{F}_{2}}\right|  = 4 - \left| {P{F}_{1}}\right|  = \dfrac{7}{2}$ ;

(2) $A\left( {2,0}\right)$ ， $\left| {AP}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{3}{4}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}$ ，若存在这样的点 $Q$ ，因为 ${S}_{\bigtriangleup {APQ}} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AP}\right|  \cdot  {d}_{Q \rightarrow  {l}_{AP}}$ ,所以 ${d}_{Q \rightarrow  {l}_{AP}} = \dfrac{2{S}_{\bigtriangleup {APQ}}}{\left| AP\right| } =  \dfrac{\sqrt{3} + 1}{\dfrac{\sqrt{7}}{2}} = \dfrac{2\left( {\sqrt{3} + 1}\right) }{\sqrt{7}}$ ,故点 $Q$ 为与直线 ${AP}$ 相距 $\dfrac{2\left( {\sqrt{3} + 1}\right) }{\sqrt{7}}$ 且

与直线 ${AP}$ 平行的直线 $l$ 与椭圆的交点, 法一: ${l}_{AP} : x = \dfrac{2 - \dfrac{1}{3}}{0 - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}}y + 2$ ,即 $x +  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}y - 2 = 0$ ,设 $l : x + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}y + t = 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{2\left( {\sqrt{3} + 1}\right) }{\sqrt{7}} = \dfrac{\left| t + 2\right| }{\sqrt{1 + \dfrac{4}{3}}} \Longrightarrow  \left| {t + 2}\right|  = 2 + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \Longrightarrow  t = \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}$ 或 $t =  - 4 -  \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}$ ,当 $t = \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}$ 时,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}y + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}} = 0 \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 0 \\  y =  - 1 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x =  - \sqrt{3} \\  y = \dfrac{1}{2} \end{array}\text{ ; 当 }t =  - 4 - \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}\text{ 时,联立 }\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}}y - 4 - \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{3}} = 0 \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\text{ ,方程 }}\right. }\right.$ 组无解; 故存在这样的点 $Q$ ,且坐标为 $\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-\sqrt{3},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ; 法二: 直线 ${l}_{AP} : y - 0 =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,即 $\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}x + y - \sqrt{3} = 0$ , 设 $l : \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}x + y + t = 0$ ,则 $\dfrac{2\left( {\sqrt{3} + 1}\right) }{\sqrt{7}} = \dfrac{\left| t + \sqrt{3}\right| }{\sqrt{\dfrac{3}{4} + 1}}$ ,解得 $t = 1$ 或者 $t =  - 2\sqrt{3} - 1$ . 当 $t = 1$ 时,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}x + y + 1 = 0 \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ , 解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 0 \\  y =  - 1 \end{array}\right.$ 或 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x =  - \sqrt{3} \\  y = \dfrac{1}{2} \end{array}\right.$ ; 当 $t =  - 2\sqrt{3} - 1$ 时, $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}x + y - 2\sqrt{3} - 1 = 0, \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ 方程组无解,故存在这样的点 $Q$ ,且坐标为 $\left( {0, - 1}\right)$ 或 $\left( {-\sqrt{3},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ;

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/92_1350_1992_227_180_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(3)由题可知，直线 $l$ 的斜率 $k$ 存在且不为零,设 $l : y = {kx} + m$ ,联立 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + m \\  {x}^{2} + 4{y}^{2} = 4 \end{array}\right.$ ,化简得 $\left( {1 + 4{k}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} +  {8kmx} + 4{m}^{2} - 4 = 0\left( *\right)$ ,设 $P\left( {x}_{1}\right.$ , $\left. {y}_{1}\right) ,T\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{8km}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},{x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{4{m}^{2} - 4}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}$ . 取线段 ${PT}$ 的中点 $M$ ,则点 $M$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-\dfrac{4km}{1 + 4{k}^{2}},\dfrac{m}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}}\right)$ , 法一: 因为 $\vv{PT} \cdot  \left( {\vv{BP} + \vv{BT}}\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $\vv{PT} \cdot  2\vv{BM} = 0 \Longrightarrow  \vv{PT} \bot  \vv{BM}.$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/93_588_1182_231_174_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

法二: 因为 $\vv{PT} \cdot  \left( {\vv{BP} + \vv{BT}}\right)  = \left( {\vv{BT} - \vv{BP}}\right)  \cdot  \left( {\vv{BT} + \vv{BP}}\right)  =  {\left| \vv{BT}\right| }^{2} - {\left| \vv{BP}\right| }^{2} = 0$ ,所以 $\left| \vv{BT}\right|  = \left| \vv{BP}\right|  \Longrightarrow  \vv{BM} \bot  \vv{PT}$ ,(共同) $\Longrightarrow  {k}_{BM} \cdot  k =  - 1$ ,即 $\dfrac{1 - \dfrac{m}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}}{\dfrac{4km}{1 + 4{k}^{2}}} \cdot  k =  - 1 \Longrightarrow  1 + 4{k}^{2} - m =  - {4m} \Longrightarrow   - {3m} = 4{k}^{2} + 1 > 1 \Longrightarrow  m <  - \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,由于方程 $\left( *\right)$ 有两个不同的解,故 $\Delta  = {64}{k}^{2}{m}^{2} + {16}\left( {1 - {m}^{2}}\right) \left( {1 + 4{k}^{2}}\right)  > 0 \Longrightarrow  4{k}^{2}{m}^{2} + 1 + 4{k}^{2} - {m}^{2} - 4{k}^{2}{m}^{2} > 0 \Longrightarrow  {m}^{2} < 4{k}^{2} + 1 =  - {3m} \Longrightarrow  m \in  \left( {-3, - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)$ .

9. ( 1 )将点 $\left( {-1,\dfrac{3}{2}}\right)$ 代入 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{{b}^{2}} = 1$ ，得 $\dfrac{1}{4} + \dfrac{9}{4{b}^{2}} = 1$ ，得 ${b}^{2} = 3$ ，所以 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{3} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} = 1\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ ，渐近线方程: $y =  \pm  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}x$ ；

(2)因 $b = \sqrt{2}$ ，则 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ ， $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} = 1\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ ，  \ding{172}  当 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},\sqrt{2}}\right\rbrack$ 时， $\left| \vv{QT}\right|$ 取到最小值时，点 $Q$ 一定在 $\dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ 上,设点 $Q\left( {x,y}\right)$ ,则 ${x}^{2} = 4\left( {1 - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2}}\right) (0 \leq  y \leq  \sqrt{2})$ ,则 $\left| \vv{TQ}\right|  = \sqrt{{x}^{2} + {\left( y - t\right) }^{2}} = \sqrt{4 - 2{y}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {2ty} + {t}^{2}} =  \sqrt{-{\left( y + t\right) }^{2} + 4 + 2{t}^{2}}\left( {0 \leq  y \leq  \sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,当 $t =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ 时,则 $y = 0$ 或 $\sqrt{2}$ 时, $\left| \vv{QT}\right|$ 取最小值 $\dfrac{3\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,此时 $Q\left( {\pm 2,0}\right)$ 或 $Q\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ; 当 $- \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2} < t \leq  \sqrt{2}$ 时,当 $y = \sqrt{2}$ 时, $\left| \vv{QT}\right|$ 取最小值 $\sqrt{2} - t$ ,此时 $Q\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $t \in  (\sqrt{2},3\sqrt{2}\rbrack$ 时, $\left| \vv{QT}\right|$ 取到最小值时,点 $Q$ 一定在 $\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} = 1\left( {y \geq  0}\right)$ 上,设点 $Q\left( {x,y}\right)$ ,则 ${x}^{2} = 4\left( {\dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} - 1}\right)  \left( {y \geq  \sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\left| {TQ}\right|  = \sqrt{2{y}^{2} - 4 + {y}^{2} - {2ty} + {t}^{2}} =  \sqrt{3{\left( y - \dfrac{t}{3}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{2}{3}{t}^{2} - 4}\left( {y \geq  \sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,因 $\sqrt{2} < t \leq  3\sqrt{2}$ ,则 $\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{3} <  \dfrac{t}{3} \leq  \sqrt{2}$ ,故当 $y = \sqrt{2}$ 时, $\left| \vv{QT}\right|$ 取最小值 $t - \sqrt{2}$ ,此时 $Q\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ . 综上可知,曲线 $\Gamma$ 上存在点 $Q\left( {0,\sqrt{2}}\right)$ ,使得 $\left| \vv{TQ}\right|$ 达到最小;

(3)设 ${l}_{1} : y = {k}_{1}x + 1,{l}_{2} : y = {k}_{2}x + 1$ ，设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {x}_{2}\right.$ ， $\left. {y}_{2}\right) ,M\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right) ,N\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {k}_{1}x + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{-8{k}_{1}}{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}  \left( {0 < {k}_{1} < \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,则 $A\left( {\dfrac{-8{k}_{1}}{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1},\dfrac{-4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {k}_{2}x + 1 \\  \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} + {y}^{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 ${x}_{2} = \dfrac{-8{k}_{2}}{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2} < {k}_{2} < 0}\right)$ ,则 $B\left( {\dfrac{-8{k}_{2}}{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1},\dfrac{-4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} y = {k}_{1}x + 1 \\  {y}^{2} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} = 1 \end{array}\text{ ,得 }{x}_{3} = \dfrac{-8{k}_{1}}{4{k}_{1}^{2} - 1}\left( {0 < {k}_{1} < \dfrac{1}{2}}\right) \text{ ,则 }M\left( {-\dfrac{8{k}_{1}}{4{k}_{1}^{2} - 1},}\right. }\right.  \left. \dfrac{-4{k}_{1}^{2} - 1}{4{k}_{1}^{2} - 1}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {k}_{2}x + 1 \\  {y}^{2} - \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{4} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,得 ${x}_{4} = \dfrac{-8{k}_{2}}{4{k}_{2}^{2} - 1}\left( {-\dfrac{1}{2} < {k}_{2} < 0}\right)$ , 则 $N\left( {-\dfrac{8{k}_{2}}{4{k}_{2}^{2} - 1},\dfrac{-4{k}_{2}^{2} - 1}{4{k}_{2}^{2} - 1}}\right)$ ,则 ${k}_{AB} = \dfrac{\dfrac{-4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1} - \dfrac{-4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}}{\dfrac{-8{k}_{1}}{4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1} - \dfrac{-8{k}_{2}}{4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}}  = \dfrac{\left( {-4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\right)  - \left( {-4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\right) \left( {4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) }{\left( {-8{k}_{1}}\right) \left( {4{k}_{2}^{2} + 1}\right)  - \left( {-8{k}_{2}}\right) \left( {4{k}_{1}^{2} + 1}\right) }$

$= \dfrac{{k}_{2}^{2} - {k}_{1}^{2}}{\left( {{k}_{2} - {k}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - 4{k}_{1}{k}_{2}}\right) } = \dfrac{{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}{1 - 4{k}_{1}{k}_{2}}$ ,同理可得, ${k}_{MN} =  \dfrac{{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}{1 + 4{k}_{1}{k}_{2}}$ ,若存在非零实数 $\lambda$ 使得 $\vv{MN} = \lambda \vv{AB}$ 成立,则 ${k}_{AB} =  {k}_{MN}$ ,即 $\dfrac{{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}{1 - 4{k}_{1}{k}_{2}} = \dfrac{{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}{1 + 4{k}_{1}{k}_{2}}$ ,即 ${k}_{1}{k}_{2}\left( {{k}_{1} + {k}_{2}}\right)  = 0$ ,则 ${k}_{1} +  {k}_{2} = 0$ 或 ${k}_{1}{k}_{2} = 0$ ,若 ${k}_{1}{k}_{2} = 0$ ,则 ${k}_{1} = 0$ 或 ${k}_{2} = 0$ ,此时直线 ${l}_{1}$ 或 ${l}_{2}$ 的方程为 $y = 1$ ，不符合题意，故当 ${k}_{1} + {k}_{2} = 0$ 且 ${k}_{1}$ 、 ${k}_{2}$ 均不为零时,存在非零实数 $\lambda$ 使得 $\vv{MN} = \lambda \vv{AB}$ 成立.

10. ( 1 )由 $\vv{O{A}_{n}} = \vv{O{A}_{1}} + \vv{{A}_{1}{A}_{2}} + \cdots  + \vv{{A}_{n - 1}{A}_{n}}$ ，可得 ${X}_{n} =  - \dfrac{1}{4}  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{{n - 1}}\left( {{2i} - 1}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{4} + \dfrac{\left( {1 + {2n} - 3}\right) \left( {n - 1}\right) }{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{4} + {\left( n - 1\right) }^{2};$

(2)设点 ${B}_{n}$ 的坐标为 $\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，

则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {X}_{n} + \dfrac{{2n} - 1}{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{4} + {\left( n - 1\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{{2n} - 1}{2} \\  \left| y\right|  = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\left( {{2n} - 1}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ , 即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {\left( n - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} \\  \left| y\right|  = \sqrt{3}\left( {n - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 ${y}^{2} = 3{\left( x - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} = {3x}$ ,所以点 ${B}_{1},{B}_{2},\cdots ,{B}_{n}\cdots$ 在抛物线 $\Gamma  : {y}^{2} = {3x}$ 上;

(3)抛物线 $\Gamma  : {y}^{2} = {3x}$ 的焦点为 $F\left( {\dfrac{3}{4},0}\right)$ ,互相垂直的弦 ${AC}$ 和 ${BD}$ 显然都不垂直坐标轴,设直线 ${AC}$ 的方程为 $x = {my} + \dfrac{3}{4},m \neq  0$ ,代入 $\Gamma$ : ${y}^{2} = {3x}$ ,得 ${y}^{2} - {3my} - \dfrac{9}{4} = 0$ ,则有 ${y}_{A} + {y}_{C} = {3m},{y}_{A} \cdot  {y}_{C} =  - \dfrac{9}{4}$ ,所以 $\left| {AC}\right|  = \sqrt{1 + {m}^{2}}{ \mid  }_{{y}_{A}} -  {y}_{C}\left| { = 3\left( {1 + {m}^{2}}\right) }\right.$ ,将上式中的 $m$ 用 $- \dfrac{1}{m}$ 代换,得 $\left| {BD}\right|  =  3\left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2}}}\right)$ ,于是 ${S}_{ABCD} = \dfrac{1}{2}\left| {AC}\right|  \cdot  \left| {BD}\right|  = \dfrac{9}{2}\left( {1 + {m}^{2}}\right) (1 +  \left. \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{9}{2}\left( {2 + {m}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2}}}\right)  \geq  {18}$ . 当且仅当 ${m}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{{m}^{2}}$ ,即 $m =  \pm  1$ 时,取等号,所以四边形 ${ABCD}$ 面积的最小值为 18 .

\end{center}
 

11. ( 1 )双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的标准方程为 ${x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1$ ，则 $c = \sqrt{3}$ ，所以双曲线 $\Gamma$ 的焦点坐标为 $\left( {\pm \sqrt{3},0}\right)$ ，渐近线方程为 $y =  \pm  \sqrt{2}x$ ； (2)设 $P\left( {m,n}\right)$ ，则 $2{m}^{2} - {n}^{2} = 2$ ， 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = \sqrt{2}x \\  y - n =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\left( {x - m}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = \dfrac{m + \sqrt{2}n}{3} \\  y = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}m + {2n}}{3} \end{array}\right.$ , 所以 ${P}_{1}\left( {\dfrac{m + \sqrt{2}n}{3},\dfrac{\sqrt{2}m + {2n}}{3}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y =  - \sqrt{2}x \\  y - n = \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\left( {x - m}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/94_636_1624_233_270_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = \dfrac{m - \sqrt{2}n}{3} \\  y = \dfrac{-\sqrt{2}m + {2n}}{3} \end{array}\right.$ ,

所以 ${P}_{2}\left( {\dfrac{m - \sqrt{2}n}{3},\dfrac{-\sqrt{2}m + {2n}}{3}}\right)$ ,

所以 $\vv{P{P}_{1}} = \left( {\dfrac{-{2m} + \sqrt{2}n}{3},\dfrac{\sqrt{2}m - n}{3}}\right)$ , $\vv{P{P}_{2}} = \left( {\dfrac{-{2m} - \sqrt{2}n}{3},\dfrac{-\sqrt{2}m - n}{3}}\right) ,$

所以 $\vv{P{P}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{P{P}_{2}} = \dfrac{\left( {\sqrt{2}n - {2m}}\right) \left( {-{2m} - \sqrt{2}n}\right) }{9}$

$+ \dfrac{\left( {\sqrt{2}m - n}\right) \left( {-\sqrt{2}m - n}\right) }{9}$

$= \dfrac{4{m}^{2} - 2{n}^{2} + {n}^{2} - 2{m}^{2}}{9}$

$= \dfrac{2{m}^{2} - {n}^{2}}{9} = \dfrac{2}{9},$

即 $\vv{P{P}_{1}} \cdot  \vv{P{P}_{2}} = \dfrac{2}{9}$ ;

(3)设切点 $M\left( {s,t}\right)$ ，则切线 $l$ 的方程为 ${sx} + {ty} = 2$ ，且 ${s}^{2} +$

${t}^{2} = 2$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = 2 \\  {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {y}^{2} = \dfrac{2}{3} \\  {x}^{2} = \dfrac{4}{3} \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left| s\right|  < \dfrac{2\sqrt{3}}{3} \\  \dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{3} < \left| t\right|  \leq  \sqrt{2} \end{array}\right.$ , 设 $P\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,Q\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,A\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{4},{y}_{4}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {sx} + {ty} = 2 \\  {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 1 \end{array}\right.$ ,消去 $y$ 得 $\left( {2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} + {4sx} - 4 - 2{t}^{2} = 0$ ,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} =  - \dfrac{4s}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}} \\  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} = \dfrac{-4 - 2{t}^{2}}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}} \end{array}\text{ ,由 }\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {sx} + {ty} = 2 \\  {x}^{2} - \dfrac{{y}^{2}}{2} = 0,\text{ 消去 }y\text{ 得 }\left( {2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) {x}^{2} +  \end{array}\right. }\right.$ 4s $x - 4 = 0$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{3} + {x}_{4} =  - \dfrac{4s}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}} \\  {x}_{3}{x}_{4} = \dfrac{-4}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}} \end{array}\right.$ ; 所以 $\left| {PQ}\right|  =  \sqrt{{1}^{2} + {\left( -\dfrac{s}{t}\right) }^{2}}\left| {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right| ,\left| {AB}\right|  = \sqrt{{1}^{2} + {\left( -\dfrac{s}{t}\right) }^{2}}\left| {{x}_{3} - {x}_{4}}\right| ,$ 所以 $\lambda  = \dfrac{\left| PQ\right| }{\left| AB\right| } = \dfrac{\left| {x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right| }{\left| {x}_{3} - {x}_{4}\right| } = \dfrac{\sqrt{{\left( {x}_{1} + {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}}{\sqrt{{\left( {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}\right) }^{2} - 4{x}_{3}{x}_{4}}} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{{\left( -\dfrac{4s}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{{16} + 8{t}^{2}}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}}}{\sqrt{{\left( -\dfrac{4s}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{16}{2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{16}{s}^{2} + \left( {{16} + 8{t}^{2}}\right) \left( {2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) }}{\sqrt{{16}{s}^{2} + {16}\left( {2{t}^{2} - {s}^{2}}\right) }} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{{32}{t}^{2} + {16}{t}^{4} - 8{t}^{2}{s}^{2}}}{\sqrt{{32}{t}^{2}}} = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{2}{t}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4}{s}^{2}}$ ,又 ${s}^{2} = 2 - {t}^{2}$ ,所以 $\sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{2}{t}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4}{s}^{2}} = \sqrt{1 + \dfrac{1}{2}{t}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {2 - {t}^{2}}\right) } = \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}{t}^{2}},$ 因为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{6}}{3} < \left| t\right|  \leq  \sqrt{2}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{2}{3} < {t}^{2} \leq  2$ , 所以 $1 < \dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}{t}^{2} \leq  2$ ,所以 $1 <  \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{3}{4}{t}^{2}} \leq  \sqrt{2}$ ,即 $\lambda  \in  (1,\sqrt{2}\rbrack$ .

\section*{第 11 章 导数及其应用}

\section*{考点 1 导数的基本计算及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 80

【解析】 ${S}^{\prime } =  - {10t} + {100}$ ,当 $t = 2$ 时, ${S}^{\prime } =  - {10} \times  2 + {100} =$ 80，故答案为 80 .

2. $\dfrac{5\pi }{3}$

【解析】由题可得: ${d}^{\prime } = 2\cos t = 1$ ,可得 $\cos t = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,又 $t \in  \left( {2,6}\right)$ , 可得 $t = \dfrac{5\pi }{3}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{5\pi }{3}$ .

3. 2

【解析】函数 $d = 5\sin t - 2\cos t$ ,求导得 ${d}^{\prime } = 5\cos t + 2\sin t$ ,则 ${\left. {d}^{\prime }\right| }_{t = \dfrac{\pi }{2}} = 5\cos \dfrac{\pi }{2} + 2\sin \dfrac{\pi }{2} = 2$ ,所以所求瞬时速度为 $2{m}/{s}$ . 故答案为: 2 .

4. $\dfrac{40}{3\pi }$

【解析】设 $t$ 时刻水的深度为 $h{{cm}}$ ,水面半径为 $r{{cm}}$ ,则 $\dfrac{h}{r} =  \dfrac{8}{3}$ ,得 $r = \dfrac{3}{8}h$ ,所以当水深为 $4{{cm}}$ 时,酒杯中水面的半径为 $\dfrac{3}{2}{{cm}}$ ,此时水的体积为 $V = \dfrac{1}{3}\pi  \times  {\left( \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} \times  4 = {3\pi }$ ,设当水深为 $4{{cm}}$ 的时刻为 ${t}_{0}$ ,可得 ${30}{t}_{0} = {3\pi }$ ,可得 ${t}_{0} = \dfrac{\pi }{10}{s}$ ; 又由题意可得 ${30t} = \dfrac{1}{3}\pi {r}^{2}h = \dfrac{1}{3}\pi  \times  {\left( \dfrac{3}{8}h\right) }^{2} \times  h = \dfrac{3}{64}\pi {h}^{3}$ ,则 $h =  {\left( \dfrac{640t}{\pi }\right) }^{\dfrac{1}{3}}$ ,所以 ${h}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  {\left( \dfrac{640}{\pi }\right) }^{\dfrac{1}{3}}{t}^{-\dfrac{2}{3}}$ ,所以当 $t = \dfrac{\pi }{10}$ 时, $v =  {h}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  {\left( \dfrac{640}{\pi }\right) }^{\dfrac{1}{3}} \times  {\left( \dfrac{\pi }{10}\right) }^{-\dfrac{2}{3}} = \dfrac{40}{3\pi }\left( {{{cm}}/{s}}\right)$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{40}{3\pi }$ . 5. ${r}_{0} \cdot  x\left( t\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x\left( t\right) }{N}}\right)$

【解析】根据假设,可得 $r = {r}_{0} - k \cdot  x\left( t\right) \left( {k > 0}\right)$ ,当 $x\left( t\right)  =  N$ 时, $r = 0$ ,代入可得 $0 = {r}_{0} - k \cdot  N$ ,解得 $k = \dfrac{{r}_{0}}{N}$ ,由单位时间内的人口增长量 ${x}^{\prime }\left( t\right)$ 与 $x\left( t\right)$ 成正比,可得 ${x}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = {rx}\left( t\right)$ ,将 $r = {r}_{0} - k \cdot  x\left( t\right)$ ,代入可得 ${x}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \left\lbrack  {{r}_{0} - \dfrac{{r}_{0}}{N}x\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \cdot  x\left( t\right)  =  {r}_{0} \cdot  x\left( t\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x\left( t\right) }{N}}\right)$ ,所以假设下建立的人口增长模型 ${x}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  =  {r}_{0} \cdot  x\left( t\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x\left( t\right) }{N}}\right)$ . 故答案: ${r}_{0} \cdot  x\left( t\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{x\left( t\right) }{N}}\right)$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

6. C

【解析】由已知得 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{f\left( {{x}_{0} + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {{x}_{0} - {\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x}  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{f\left( {{x}_{0} + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {{x}_{0} - {\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x}  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{f\left( {{x}_{0} + {\Delta x}}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{\Delta x} + \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{{\Delta x} \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {{x}_{0} - {\Delta x}}\right) }{\Delta x}  = 2{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {2a}$ ,故选: C.

7. C

【解析】当 $x > 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ 时,此时 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ; 当 $x < 0$ , ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ 时,此时 $x \in  \left( {-3, - 1}\right)$ ,综上所述: 选 C.

8. B

【解析】由题意可得,若对应的 $y = t$ 的 $x$ 取值的情况可以有 1 个,2 个或 3 个,且对应 2 个根的情况的时候, $t$ 的取值只要 2 个,若 $y = a,y = b,y = c$ 对应的根的个数为1,1,2,则符合要求的集合的个数为 $1 \times  1 \times  \left( {{2}^{2} - 1}\right)  = 3$ , $A$ 有可能; 若 $y = a,y = b,y = c$ 对应的根的个数为2,2,3,则符合要求的集合的个数为 $\left( {{2}^{2} - 1}\right)  \times  \left( {{2}^{2} - 1}\right)  \times  \left( {{2}^{3} - 1}\right)  = {63}$ , C 有可能; 若 $y = a,y = b,y = c$ 对应的根的个数为3,3,3,则符合要求的集合的个数为 $\left( {{2}^{3} - 1}\right)  \times  \left( {{2}^{3} - 1}\right)  \times  \left( {{2}^{3} - 1}\right)  = {343}$ , D 有可能. 故选: B.

9. D

【解析】当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x},{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}$ ,当 $x \in  (1$ , 2) 时, $g\left( x\right)  = {g}^{\prime }\left( {x - 1}\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1},x \in  \left( {2,3}\right)$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  = {g}^{\prime }(x -  2) = {{e}}^{x - 2},\cdots$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {n,n + 1}\right) ,g\left( x\right)  = {g}^{\prime }\left( {x - n}\right)  = {{e}}^{x - n}$ ,对于 \ding{172} , $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数,因为 $k \neq  0,y = g\left( x\right)$ 与 $y =  {kx} + b$ 至多有 2 个不同交点，如图 1 所示，所以  \ding{172}  错；对于  \ding{173} ，当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时， $h\left( x\right)  = {x}^{10}$ ， ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {10}{x}^{9}$ . 当 $x \in  \left( {1,2}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {x - 1}\right)  = {10}{\left( x - 1\right) }^{9}$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {2,3}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  =  {h}^{\prime }\left( {x - 1}\right)  = {10} \times  9{\left( x - 2\right) }^{8},\cdots$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {9,{10}}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  =  {h}^{\prime }\left( {x - 1}\right)  = {10}!\left( {x - 9}\right)$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {{10},{11}}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  = {h}^{\prime }(x -  1) = {10}$ !,当 $x \in  \left( {{11},{12}}\right)$ 时, $h\left( x\right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {x - 1}\right)  = 0,y = h\left( x\right)$ , 当 $k = {10}$ ! 时,存在 $b$ 使得直线 $y = {kx} + b,y = {10}!x - 9 \times  {10}!,k = {10}!,b =  - 9 \times  {10}!$ 可以与 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {9,{10}}\right)$ 的函数部分重合, 因而有无穷个交点, 如图 2 所示, 所以  \ding{173}  正确，故选 D.

\end{center}
