10. ${B}$

【解析】 $\left| {{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) }\right|  \leq  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0,g\left( x\right)$ 为增函数, $\left| {{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) }\right|  \leq  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow   - {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  +  {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  + g\left( x\right)$ 为增函数, $f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right)$ 为减函数; 对于  \ding{172} ,不妨设 ${x}_{1} > {x}_{2}$ ,则 $\mid  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  -  f\left( {x}_{2}\right) \left|  \leq  \right| g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \mid   \Leftrightarrow  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  -  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \leq  g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) ,f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right)  \geq  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ 且 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \geq  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,故  \ding{172}  是真命题；对于 \ding{173} ， $g\left( x\right)$ 为增函数,且 $g\left( x\right)$ 的值域为 $\left\lbrack  {m,M}\right\rbrack  ,f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right)$ 为减函数, $f\left( 1\right)  - g\left( 1\right)  \leq  f\left( {-1}\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  M - m \leq  g\left( 1\right)  -  g\left( {-1}\right)  \leq  M - m \Longrightarrow  g\left( 1\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right)  = M - m,g\left( 1\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right)  =  M - m \Longrightarrow  0 \leq  g\left( {-1}\right)  - m = g\left( 1\right)  - M \Longrightarrow  g\left( 1\right)  = M,g\left( {-1}\right)  = m$ , $\left| {f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {-1}\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right) }\right|  = g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  f\left( x\right)  \leq  g\left( x\right) \left( \mid \right) ,\left| {f\left( x\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {g\left( 1\right)  - g\left( x\right) }\right|  = g\left( 1\right)  -  g\left( x\right)  \Longrightarrow  f\left( x\right)  \geq  g\left( x\right)$ (ii),综合 (i) (ii) 可得 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ ,所以  \ding{173}  为真命题，故选 B.

11. C

【解析】对于选项 A: 因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{-x}$ 的导函数为 $g\left( x\right)  =  - {{e}}^{-x}$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  =  - {{e}}^{-{x}_{0}} \cdot  {{e}}^{{x}_{0}} =  - 1 \neq$ 1，故选项 A 错误；对于选项 B:因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x$ 的导函数为 $g\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = \cos {x}_{0} \cdot  \sin \left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  =  \cos {x}_{0} \cdot  \left( {-\sin {x}_{0}}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{2}\sin 2{x}_{0}$ ,而 $\sin 2{x}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack  ,1 \notin  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,故选项 B 错误; 对于选项 ${C}$ : 因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x}$ 的导函数为 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x}$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = \left( {{{e}}^{{x}_{0}} - {{e}}^{-{x}_{0}}}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{-{x}_{0}} + }\right.  \left. {{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\right)  = {{e}}^{2{x}_{0}} - {{e}}^{-2{x}_{0}} = {{e}}^{2{x}_{0}} - \dfrac{1}{{{e}}^{2{x}_{0}}}$ . 令 ${{e}}^{2{x}_{0}} - \dfrac{1}{{{e}}^{2{x}_{0}}} = 1$ ,解得 ${{e}}^{2{x}_{0}} =  \dfrac{1 + \sqrt{5}}{2},{x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{2}\ln \left( \dfrac{1 + \sqrt{5}}{2}\right)$ ,即存在实数 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{2}\ln \left( \dfrac{1 + \sqrt{5}}{2}\right)$ , 使得 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = 1$ 成立,所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有 ${x}_{0}$ 性质,故选项 C 正确; 对于选项 D: 因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + }\right.$ 1) 的导函数为 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x}{{x}^{2} + 1}$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}$ . $\dfrac{1}{2}\ln \left\lbrack  {{\left( -{x}_{0}\right) }^{2} + 1}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{2\left( {{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right) } \cdot  \ln \left( {{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right)$ . 令 $\dfrac{{x}_{0}}{2\left( {{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right) }$ . $\ln \left( {{x}^{2}{}_{0} + 1}\right)  = 1$ ,显然 ${x}_{0} \neq  0$ ,化简得: $\ln \left( {{x}_{0}^{2} + 1}\right)  =  2\left( {{x}_{0} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}}}\right)$ . 下面证明方程 $\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  = 2\left( {x + \dfrac{1}{x}}\right) \left( *\right)$ 无解; 当 $x < 0$ 时 $\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  > \ln 1 = 0,2\left( {x + \dfrac{1}{x}}\right)  < 0$ ,方程 $\left( *\right)$ 无解; 当 $x > 0$ 时, $2\left( {x + \dfrac{1}{x}}\right)  > {2x}$ ,而 $\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  < {2x}$ : 令 $h\left( x\right)  =  \ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  - {2x},x > 0$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2x}{{x}^{2} + 1} - 2 \leq  \dfrac{2x}{2\sqrt{{x}^{2} \times  1}} - 2 =  1 - 2 =  - 1 < 0$ ,所以 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格减. 又因为 $h\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $h\left( x\right)  <  h\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,即 $h\left( 0\right)  < 0$ ,所以 $\ln \left( {{x}^{2} + 1}\right)  < {2x} < 2\left( {x + \dfrac{1}{x}}\right)$ . 综上, 方程(*)无解. 所以不存在实数 ${x}_{0}$ ,使得 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right) f\left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  = 1$ 成立, 故选项 D 错误. 故选: C.

\section*{考点 2 求切线方程及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $y = x - 1$

【解析】 ${y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,当 $x = 1$ 时, ${y}^{\prime } = 1$ ,所以曲线 $y = \ln x$ 在点 (1,0)处的切线方程为 $y = x - 1$ . 故答案为: $y = x - 1$ .

2. $- \dfrac{1}{2}$

【解析】函数 $y = \dfrac{2}{x - 1}$ ,求导得 ${y}^{\prime } =  - \dfrac{2}{{\left( x - 1\right) }^{2}}$ ,则 ${\left. {y}^{\prime }\right| }_{x = 3} =  - \dfrac{2}{{\left( 3 - 1\right) }^{2}} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $l$ 的斜率为 $- \dfrac{1}{2}$ . 故答案为: $- \dfrac{1}{2}$ . 3. $y = x$

【解析】由已知得 $a = 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} + {2ax} + 1 = 3{x}^{2} + 1$ , $k = {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ,所以切线方程为 $y = x$ .

4. ${3x} + y - 4 = 0$

【解析】 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 2{x}^{-2} - 1,{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  =  - 3,l : y - 1 = \left( {-3}\right)  \left( {x - 1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  l : {3x} + y - 4 = 0.$

5. 1 或 4

【解析】设切点为 $Q\left( {{x}_{0},\dfrac{1}{3}{x}_{0}^{3}}\right)$ , $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3} \Longrightarrow  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2},{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  {x}_{0}^{2}$ ,切线的方程为 $l : y - \dfrac{1}{3}{x}_{0}^{3} = {x}_{0}^{2}(x  \left. {-{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,点 $P\left( {2,\dfrac{8}{3}}\right)$ 在切线上, $\dfrac{8}{3} -  \dfrac{1}{3}{x}_{0}^{3} = {x}_{0}^{2}\left( {2 - {x}_{0}}\right)  \Longrightarrow  2{x}_{0}^{3} - 6{x}_{0}^{2} + 8 = 0$

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/134_1348_415_228_287_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

$\Longrightarrow  {x}_{0} =  - 1$ 或 ${x}_{0} = 2$ ,当 ${x}_{0} =  - 1$ ,斜率 $k = {x}_{0}^{2} = 1$ ; 当 ${x}_{0} = 2$ ,斜率 $k = {x}_{0}^{2} = 4$ ，故答案为 1 或 4 .

\section*{二、解答题}

6. ( 1 )已知 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数，则 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right) ,f\left( {-x}\right)  =  a \cdot  {{e}}^{-x} + b \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ ,所以 $a \cdot  {{e}}^{-x} + b \cdot  {{e}}^{x} = a \cdot  {{e}}^{x} + b \cdot  {{e}}^{-x}$ ,即 $\left( {a - b}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x}}\right)  = 0$ 对任意 $x$ 恒成立. 因为 ${{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x}$ 不恒为 0,所以 $a = b$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)  = a\left( {{{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x}}\right)$ . 又因为 ${{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x} \geq  2\sqrt{{{e}}^{x} \cdot  {{e}}^{-x}} = 2$ (当且仅当 ${{e}}^{x} = {{e}}^{-x}$ ,即 $x = 0$ 时等号成立). 因为 ${ab} \neq  0$ ,所以 $a \neq  0$ ,又 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的最小值为 4,所以 $a > 0$ 且 ${{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{-x} \geq  2$ ,即 ${2a} = 4$ 解得 $a = 2$ . 所以 $a = 2,b = 2$ ;

(2)当 $a = 1$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + b \cdot  {{e}}^{-x}$ ，已知 $f\left( x\right)  \geq  g\left( x\right)$ 恒成立,即 ${{e}}^{x} + b \cdot  {{e}}^{-x} \geq  {{e}}^{x} - x$ 恒成立,即 $b \geq   - x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ 恒成立. 接下来求 $y =  - x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ 的最大值. 设 $h\left( x\right)  =  - x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \left( {{{e}}^{x} + x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}}\right)  =  - {{e}}^{x}\left( {x + 1}\right) .$

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$x$ & $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$ & -1 & $\left( {-1, + \infty }\right)$ \\
\cline{1-4}
${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & + & 0 & - \\
\cline{1-4}
$h\left( x\right)$ & ↗ & 极大值 $\dfrac{1}{{e}}$ & ↘ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在 $x =  - 1$ 处取得最大值, $h\left( {-1}\right)  =  - \left( {-1}\right)$ . ${{e}}^{-1} = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ . 因为 $b \geq   - x \cdot  {{e}}^{x}$ 恒成立,所以 $b \geq  h{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,即实数 $b$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{{e}}, + \infty }\right)$ .

7. (1)设 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} = {2py}$ ，又 $\dfrac{p}{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ，得 ${2p} = 1$ ，即 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的方程为 $y = {x}^{2},{\Gamma }_{2}$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} + {\left( y - a\right) }^{2} = 1\left( {x > 0,y < a}\right)$ ;

(2)设直线 $y = x + 2$ 与 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的交点为 $M\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $N\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ ， 线段 ${MN}$ 中点为 $G\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = x + 2, \\  y = {x}^{2}, \end{array}\right.$ 可得 ${x}^{2} - x - 2 =$ 0,故 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} = \dfrac{1}{2},{y}_{0} = {x}_{0} + 2 = \dfrac{5}{2}$ ,由点 $G$ 在 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 上,可知 $\dfrac{1}{4} + {\left( \dfrac{5}{2} - a\right) }^{2} = 1$ 且 $\dfrac{5}{2} < a$ ,解得 $a = \dfrac{5 + \sqrt{3}}{2}$ ;

(3)设 $D\left( {x,y}\right)$ 为 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 上任一点，则 $y = a - \sqrt{1 - {x}^{2}}\left( {0 < x < 1}\right)$ . 点 $D$ 在 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 的上方等价于 $a - \sqrt{1 - {x}^{2}} > {x}^{2}$ ,即 $a > {x}^{2} +  \sqrt{1 - {x}^{2}}$ 对于 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 恒成立,令 $\sqrt{1 - {x}^{2}} = t$ ,由 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,可得 $t \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,故 ${x}^{2} + \sqrt{1 - {x}^{2}} =  - {t}^{2} + t + 1 =  - {\left( t - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{5}{4}$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{5}{4}$ ,可得 $a > \dfrac{5}{4}$ . 设直线 $y = {kx} + b$ 与 ${\Gamma }_{2}$ 相切,被 ${\Gamma }_{1}$ 截得的线段长为 $L$ ,则 $k > 0,b < a - 1$ ,且 $\dfrac{a - b}{\sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}} = 1$ ,可得 $a - b = \sqrt{{k}^{2} + 1}$ ,又由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = {kx} + b, \\  y = {x}^{2}, \end{array}\right.$ 可得 ${x}^{2} - {kx} - b = 0$ ,设它的两个实根为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,则 ${L}^{2} = \left( {1 + {k}^{2}}\right) {\left( {x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} = (1 +  \left. {k}^{2}\right) \left( {{k}^{2} + {4b}}\right)$ ,设 $a - b = n$ ,则 $n > 1,n = \sqrt{1 + {k}^{2}},{L}^{2} =  {n}^{2}\left( {{n}^{2} - 1 - {4n} + {4a}}\right)  = {n}^{4} - 4{n}^{3} + \left( {{4a} - 1}\right) {n}^{2}$ ,令 $f\left( n\right)  =  {n}^{4} - 4{n}^{3} + \left( {{4a} - 1}\right) {n}^{2}$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( n\right)  = 4{n}^{3} - {12}{n}^{2} + \left( {{8a} - 2}\right) n =  n\left\lbrack  {4{\left( n - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} + {8a} - {11}}\right\rbrack$ ,当且仅当 ${8a} - {11} < 0$ ,即 $a < \dfrac{11}{8}$ 时,存在 ${n}_{1} = \dfrac{3 + \sqrt{{11} - {8a}}}{2}$ ,使得在 $\left( {{1.5},{n}_{1}}\right)$ 与 $\left( {{n}_{1}, + \infty }\right)$ 上， ${f}^{\prime }\left( n\right)$ 分别小于 0 和大于 0，故 $f\left( n\right)$ 分别严格增与严格减，故在( 1.5, $+ \infty$ )上必存在两个不同的 $n$ 值，对应的 $f\left( n\right)$ 相等,即存在两个不同的正数 $k$ ,使得对应的 $L$ 值相等. 所以存在 $a$ 满足题中条件,且 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {\dfrac{5}{4},\dfrac{11}{8}}\right)$ .

8. (1) 当 $a =  - 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}} + \dfrac{1}{x} + 1,{f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 3$ ,所以曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $P\left( {1,2}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y = {3x} - 1$ ;

( 2 )由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ，得: $\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}} - \dfrac{a}{x} - a = 0$ ，令 $t = \sqrt{x}$ ，则 $t > 0$ ， 原方程可化为: $a{t}^{2} - t + a = 0$  \ding{172} ,则 ${t}_{1} = \sqrt{{x}_{1}},{t}_{2} = \sqrt{{x}_{2}}$ 是方程 \ding{172} 的两个不同的根，所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \Delta  = 1 - 4{a}^{2} > 0 \\  \dfrac{1}{a} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ，解得 $0 < a <  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 2\left( {\sqrt{{x}_{1}} + \sqrt{{x}_{2}}}\right)  - a\left( {\ln {x}_{1} + }\right.  \ln {x}_{2}) - a\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right)  - 2 = 2\left( {{t}_{1} + {t}_{2}}\right)  - a\ln \left( {{t}_{1}^{2}{t}_{2}^{2}}\right)  - a\left( {{t}_{1}^{2} + {t}_{2}^{2}}\right)  - 2 = {2a} + \dfrac{1}{a} - 2$ ，因为 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，所以 ${2a} + \dfrac{1}{a} - 2 > 2\sqrt{2} -  2 > 0$ ,所以 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > 0$ ;

(3)由题意， $g\left( x\right)  = 2\sqrt{x} - \ln x - 1$ ，所以 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\sqrt{x} - 1}{x}$ ，当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时， ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，所以函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格减，当 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ，所以函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,因为 $0 < {a}_{1} < 1$ ,所以 ${a}_{2} = g\left( {a}_{1}\right)  >  g\left( 1\right)  = 1,{a}_{3} = g\left( {a}_{2}\right)  > g\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,以此类推,当 $n \geq  2$ 时, ${a}_{n + 1} =  g\left( {a}_{n}\right)  > g\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,又 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2 \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}} - 1 - \dfrac{1}{x} =  \dfrac{-{\left( \sqrt{x} - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{3}{4}}{x} < 0$ ,所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减，当 $n \geq  2$ 时， $f\left( {a}_{n}\right)  = g\left( {a}_{n}\right)  - {a}_{n} < f\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ，所以 ${a}_{n + 1} <  {a}_{n}$ ,所以 $f\left( {a}_{n + 1}\right)  > f\left( {a}_{n}\right)$ ,即 ${a}_{n + 2} - {a}_{n + 1} > {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n}$ ,故 ${a}_{n} +  {a}_{n + 2} > 2{a}_{n + 1}$ .

9. (1) 由题可知, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{-x}$ ,所以切线的斜率为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,且 $f\left( 0\right)  = 2$ ,所以函数在点 $\left( {0,f\left( 0\right) }\right)$ 切线方程为 $y - 2 = 0\left( {x - 0}\right)$ ,即 $y = 2$ ;

( 2 )由题可知 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - a\sin x + b\cos x$ ，又因为定义域上对任意的实数 $x$ 满足 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - k{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ，所以 $a\cos x - b\sin x =  {ak}\sin x - {bk}\cos x$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - b = {ak} \\  a =  - {bk} \end{array}\right.$ ,当 $k \in  \mathbf{R}$ 且 $k \neq  0$ 时, $a = b = 0$ , 当 $k = 1$ 时， $a + b = 0$ ，当 $k =  - 1$ 时， $a - b = 0$ ；

(3)因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在定义域 $\mathbf{R}$ 上是奇函数，所以 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  \cdot  {\left( -x\right) }^{\prime } =  - {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  =  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数,因为 $f\left( x\right)  + f\left( {2 - x}\right)  = 3$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {2 - x}\right)  \cdot  {\left( 2 - x\right) }^{\prime } = {\left( 3\right) }^{\prime }$ ,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( {2 - x}\right)  = 0$ , 即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  =  {f}^{\prime }\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {2 + x}\right)$ ,所以 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 是周期为 2 的函数,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x + 2}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {2 - x}\right)  =  {f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x + 2}\right) .$

点睛: 利用导数求函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在其上一点 $\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 处的切线方程的基本步骤如下:(1)对函数求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ; (2)计算切线的斜率 $k = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ; (3) 利用点斜式写出切线方程.

10. ( 1 )点 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ，理由如下:设 $Q\left( {q,{q}^{2}}\right)$ ，因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x}$ ,所以曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $Q$ 处的切线方程为: $y = {2qx} - {q}^{2}$ ,将点 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ 坐标代入,得: ${2q} - {q}^{2} = 0$ ,所以 $q = 0$ 或 2,即函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上存在与 $P$ 不同的一点 $Q\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,使得直线 ${PQ}$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图像在点 $Q$ 处的切线,故点 $P\left( {1,0}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ;

(2) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 6{x}^{2} - {8x} + 2$ ，设 $P\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},y}\right) \left( {-1 \leq  y \leq  1}\right) ,Q(q$ , $\left. {2{q}^{3} - 4{q}^{2} + {2q}}\right)$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像在 $Q$ 处的切线方程为: $y = \left( {6{q}^{2} - {8q} + 2}\right) \left( {x - q}\right)  + 2{q}^{3} - 4{q}^{2} + {2q}$  \ding{172} ,当 $y = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时,点 $P$ 在函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上,将 $x = \dfrac{1}{2},y = \dfrac{1}{4}$ 代入  \ding{172}  式,得: $4{q}^{3} - 7{q}^{2} + {4q} - \dfrac{3}{4} = 0$  \ding{173} ,令 $g\left( q\right)  = 4{q}^{3} - 7{q}^{2} +  {4q} - \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,则 $g\left( {0.6}\right)  =  - \dfrac{3}{500} < 0,g\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{4} > 0$ ,所以关于 $q$ 的方程 \ding{173} 必有实数解 $x = q$ ，且 $q \neq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上存在与 $P$ 不同的一点 $Q$ ,使得直线 ${PQ}$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图像在点 $Q$ 处的切线,即点 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ; 当 $y \neq  \dfrac{1}{4}$ 时,点 $P$ 不在函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上,将 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}$ 代入  \ding{172}  式,得: $y =  - 4{q}^{3} + 7{q}^{2} - {4q} + 1$  \ding{174} ,令 $h\left( q\right)  =  - 4{q}^{3} + 7{q}^{2} -  {4q} + 1$ ,则 $h\left( 0\right)  = 1,h\left( 2\right)  =  - {11} <  - 1$ ,所以当 $- 1 \leq  y \leq  1$ 时, 关于 $q$ 的方程 \ding{174} 必有解，故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上存在与 $P$ 不同的一点 $Q$ ,使得直线 ${PQ}$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图像在点 $Q$ 处的切线,即点 $P\left( {\dfrac{1}{2},y}\right) \left( {-1 \leq  y \leq  1,y \neq  \dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ , 综上所述,线段 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {-1 \leq  y \leq  1}\right)$ 上的所有点均具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ;

(3)设 $Q\left( {q,{{e}}^{q}}\right)$ ，函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像在 $Q$ 处的切线方程为: $y = {{e}}^{q}x - {{e}}^{q} \cdot  q + {{e}}^{q}$ ,必要性: 若点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ,则点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ 应满足方程 $y = {{e}}^{q}x - {{e}}^{q} \cdot  q + {{e}}^{q}$ ,令 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} -  y = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{q}x + {{e}}^{q} \cdot  q - {{e}}^{q}$ ,则由 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - {{e}}^{q} = 0$ ,得: $x = q$ , 当 $x < q$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,当 $x > q$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $x = q$ 时取得最小值 $g\left( q\right)  = 0$ ,因为 $P$ 与 $Q$ 是不相同的点, 所以点 $P$ 的横坐标 $x \neq  q$ ，因此 ${{e}}^{x} - y > 0$ ，即 $y < {{e}}^{x}$ . 充分性: 当 $y < {{e}}^{x}$ 时，令 $h\left( q\right)  = x{{e}}^{q} - q{{e}}^{q} + {{e}}^{q} - y = \left( {x - q + 1}\right) {{e}}^{q} - y$ ， 对于函数 $t = h\left( q\right)$ ,当 $q$ 趋向 $+ \infty$ 时, $h\left( q\right)$ 趋向 $- \infty$ ,又 $h\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - y > 0$ ,故关于 $q$ 的方程 $h\left( q\right)  = 0$ 必然有解,即存在点 $Q\left( {q,{{e}}^{q}}\right)$ 使得直线 ${PQ}$ 是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像的切线, 所以点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ,综上所述,“点 $P\left( {x,y}\right)$ 具有性质 ${M}_{f}$ ”的充要条件是“ $y < {{e}}^{x}$ ”.

11. ( 1 ) $\omega  = 1$ 时， $g\left( x\right)  = \sin x$ ，则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ ，从而 $k =  {g}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  = \cos \dfrac{\pi }{2} = 0$ ,所以直线 $l$ 的方程是 $y = 1$ ;

(2)由 $\dfrac{2\pi }{\omega } = {2\pi }$ ，可知 $\omega  = 1$ ，则 $h\left( x\right)  = x\sin x - \ln x\left( {x > 0}\right)$ ，当 $h\left( x\right)  = 0$ 时, $\sin x = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ , \ding{172}  当 $0 < x < 1$ 时, $\sin x > 0,\dfrac{\ln x}{x} < 0$ , 此时函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 没有零点;  \ding{173}  当 $x \geq  1$ 时,因为 ${\left( \dfrac{\ln x}{x}\right) }^{\prime } =  \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,可知 $y = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {1,{e}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,在 $\lbrack {e}, + \infty )$ 严格减, 又 $y = \sin x$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {1,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{\pi }{2},{e}}\right\rbrack$ 严格减,所以 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,{e}}\right\rbrack$ 时, $y = \sin x$ 在 $x = {e}$ 时有最小值 $\sin {e},y = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 在 $x = {e}$ 时有最大值 $\dfrac{\operatorname{lne}}{{e}} = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,因为 $\sin  \geq  \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,所以 $\sin x = \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {1,{e}}\right\rbrack$ 上没有交点,即 $h\left( x\right)  = x\sin x - \ln x$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {1,{e}}\right\rbrack$ 上没有零点,所以函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的零点 ${x}_{n}$ 满足 ${e} < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < \cdots  < {x}_{n} < \cdots$ ,因为 $y =  \dfrac{\ln x}{x}$ 在 $\lbrack {e}, + \infty )$ 严格减，所以 $\dfrac{\ln {x}_{1}}{{x}_{1}} > \dfrac{\ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{2}} > \cdots  > \dfrac{\ln {x}_{n}}{{x}_{n}} > \cdots$ 又因为 $\sin {x}_{n} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{n}}{{x}_{n}}$ ,所以数列 $\left\{  {\sin {x}_{n}}\right\}$ 是严格减数列;

(3)因为 $f\left( x\right)  =  - f\left( {x + {2a}}\right)  =  - \left\lbrack  {-f\left( {x + {4a}}\right) }\right\rbrack   = f(x +  {4a})$ ,所以 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是以 ${4a}$ 为周期的周期函数,因为任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,{2a}}\right\rbrack$ ,当 $x \neq  a$ 时,都有 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( a\right)$ 且 $f\left( a\right)  = 1$ ,所以当 $x = a$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,{2a}}\right\rbrack$ 上有唯一的最大值 1,由 $\omega  = \pi$ 得 $g\left( x\right)  = \sin {\pi x},F\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + \sin {\pi x},G\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  +  \cos {\pi x}$ ,假设存在 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,使得 $F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = G\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + 4$ 成立,即 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + \sin \pi {x}_{1} - \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + \cos \pi {x}_{2}}\right\rbrack   = 4$ 成立，故当 ${x}_{1} = a +  {4ka}\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ 取得最大值 1; 当 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{2} + {2m}\left( {m \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $\sin \pi {x}_{1}$ 取得最大值 1,由 $a + {4ka} = \dfrac{1}{2} + {2m}$ ,可知 $a =  \dfrac{{4m} + 1}{{8k} + 2}$  \ding{172}  时， ${\left( f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + \sin \pi {x}_{1}\right) }_{\max } = 2$ ，又因为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,所以当 $x =  - a$ 时, $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-{2a},0}\right\rbrack$ 上有唯一的最小值 -1,故,当 ${x}_{2} =  - a + {4na}\left( {n \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 取得最小值-1; 当 ${x}_{2} = 1 + {2t}\left( {t \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时, $\cos \pi {x}_{2}$ 取得最小值-1,由 $- a + {4na} =  1 + {2t}$ ,可知 $a = \dfrac{{2t} + 1}{{4n} - 1}$  \ding{173} 时， ${\left( f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + \cos \pi {x}_{2}\right) }_{\min } =  - 2$ ,若 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + \sin \pi {x}_{1} - \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + \cos \pi {x}_{2}}\right\rbrack   = 4$ 成立,则由 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 得 $\dfrac{{4m} + 1}{{8k} + 2} = \dfrac{{2t} + 1}{{4n} - 1}$ ,即 $\left( {{4m} + 1}\right) \left( {{4n} - 1}\right)  = \left( {{2t} + 1}\right) \left( {{8k} + 2}\right)$ ,因为 $m,n,k,t \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,此时等式左边为奇数,等式右边为偶数,所以等式不成立. 故当 $w = \pi$ 时,不存在 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,使得 $F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  =  G\left( {x}_{2}\right)  + 4$ 成立. 12. (1) ${y}^{\prime } = {2x}$ ，所以曲线 $y = {x}^{2}$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y - 1 = 2\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,即 ${2x} - y - 1 = 0$ ,因为圆 ${\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} =  {r}^{2}\left( {r > 0}\right)$ 与曲线 $y = {x}^{2}$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处 “一线切”,所以直线 ${2x} - y - 1 = 0$ 与圆 ${\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = {r}^{2}\left( {r > 0}\right)$ 在点 $\left( {1,1}\right)$ 处相切,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\left( 1 - a\right) }^{2} + 1 = {r}^{2} \\  \dfrac{\left| 2a - 1\right| }{\sqrt{5}} = r \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 $a = 3$ ;

(2)设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} + 2,{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x + 1}$ ，由题意， $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{0}\right) \\  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {g}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \end{array}\right.$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0}^{2} + 2{x}_{0} + a = \ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right) \\  2{x}_{0} + 2 = \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0} + 1} \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 ${x}_{0} =  - 1 + \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},a = \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}\ln 2;$

(3)假设存在 $P\left( {{x}_{0},1}\right)$ 满足题意，则有 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \sin {x}_{0} = 1$ ，对函数 $y = f\left( x\right) \sin x$ 求导得: ${y}^{\prime } = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) \sin x + f\left( x\right) \cos x$ ,于是 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \sin {x}_{0} + f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \cos {x}_{0} = 0$ ,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \sin {x}_{0} =  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \cos {x}_{0}$ , 平方得 ${\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}{\sin }^{2}{x}_{0} = {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}{\cos }^{2}{x}_{0} = {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}(1 -  \left. {{\sin }^{2}{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,即有 ${\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}{\sin }^{2}{x}_{0} + {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}{\sin }^{2}{x}_{0} = {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}$ , 因此 ${\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{{\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}} + 1 = {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}$ ,整理得 ${\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}  + {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} = {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{4}$ ,而恒有 $\left| {{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) }\right|  \geq  \left| {f\left( x\right) }\right|$ 成立,则有 ${\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} \geq  {\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}$ ,从而 ${\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{4} \geq  2{\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}$ ,显然 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \neq  0$ ,于是 ${\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} \geq  2$ ,即 $\left| {f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right|  \geq  \sqrt{2}$ 与 $\left| {f\left( x\right) }\right|  < \sqrt{2}$ 恒成立矛盾，所以假设不成立，即不存在点 $P$ 满足条件.

13. ( 1 )函数 $y = {x}^{2} - 2\left| x\right| ,x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 是偶函数，其一条 2 点切线方程为: $y =  - 1$ ;

( 2 )因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - \ln x$ ，所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - \dfrac{1}{x}$ . 记 $h\left( x\right)  =  {{e}}^{x} - \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,由 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} > 0$ ,知函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上为严格增函数,因此,对于函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 的图像上任意两点, ${P}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right) ,{P}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right)$ , ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \neq  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,所以其切线斜率不相等,切线不可能重合,因此函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不具有 $k$ 点切线性质;

(3) $g\left( x\right)  = \cos x + {2x},{g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2 - \sin x$ ，故函数在 $x = {x}_{0}$ 处的切线方程为: $y - \cos {x}_{0} - 2{x}_{0} = \left( {2 - \sin {x}_{0}}\right) \left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right)$ ,即 $y = (2 -  \left. {\sin {x}_{0}}\right) x + {x}_{0}\sin {x}_{0} + \cos {x}_{0}$ ,一方面取 $m$ 个点 $\left( {0,1}\right) ,\left( {{2\pi },{4\pi } + 1}\right)$ 、 $\left( {{4\pi },{8\pi } + 1}\right) ,\cdots ,\left( {{2m\pi } - {2\pi },{4m\pi } - {4\pi } + 1}\right)$ ,在该 $m$ 个点处的切线方程均为: $y = {2x} + 1$ ,所以该函数具有 $m$ 点切线性质. 另一方面,若在点 ${P}_{i}\left( {{a}_{i},f\left( {a}_{i}\right) }\right) \left( {i = 1,2,\cdots ,m}\right)$ 处的切线重合. 则有 $\begin{cases} 2 - \sin {a}_{1} = 2 - \sin {a}_{2} &  = 2 - \sin {a}_{3} = \cdots  = 2 - \sin {a}_{m} \\  {a}_{1}\sin {a}_{1} + \cos {a}_{1} &  = {a}_{2}\sin {a}_{2} + \cos {a}_{2} = {a}_{3}\sin {a}_{3} + \cos {a}_{3} = \cdots . \\   &  = {a}_{m}\sin {a}_{m} + \cos {a}_{m} \end{cases}$

由 $\sin {a}_{1} = \sin {a}_{2} = \sin {a}_{3}$ 可以知道角 ${a}_{1}$ 与 ${a}_{2}$ 终边相同或关于 $y$ 轴对称、角 ${a}_{1}$ 与 ${a}_{3}$ 终边相同或关于 $y$ 轴对称、角 ${a}_{2}$ 与 ${a}_{3}$ 终边相同或关于 $y$ 轴对称,因 ${a}_{1},{a}_{2},{a}_{3}$ 中至少 2 个角终边相同,不妨设角 ${a}_{1}$ 与 ${a}_{2}$ 终边相同,则 ${a}_{2} = {a}_{1} + {2t\pi }\left( {t \neq  0}\right)$ . 此时 $\cos {a}_{2} = \cos {a}_{1}$ ,且 ${a}_{1}\sin {a}_{1} = {a}_{2}\sin {a}_{2} = \left( {{a}_{1} + {2t\pi }}\right) \sin \left( {{a}_{1} + {2t\pi }}\right)  =  \left( {{a}_{1} + {2t\pi }}\right) \sin {a}_{1}$ ,则 ${2t\pi }\sin {a}_{1} = 0$ ,故 $\sin {a}_{1} = 0$ ,则 ${a}_{1} = {2n\pi }$ 或 ${a}_{1} = {2n\pi } + \pi$ ,此时切线方程为 $y = {2x} + 1$ 或 $y = {2x} - 1$ .

14.(1)由导函数 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ，得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = {e} - 1$ ，故切线方程为 $y - f\left( 1\right)  = \left( {{e} - 1}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,即 $y = \left( {{e} - 1}\right) x$ ;

(2) $F\left( x\right)  = a\left( {{{e}}^{x} - x}\right)  - {{e}}^{-x} - x$ ,

导函数 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a\left( {{{e}}^{x} - 1}\right)  + {{e}}^{-x} - 1 = \dfrac{\left( {a{{e}}^{x} - 1}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} - 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,  \ding{172}  当 $a = {e}$ 时， $F\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x + 1} - {e}x - {{e}}^{-x} - x$ ， 令 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\left( {{{e}}^{x + 1} - 1}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} - 1}\right) }{{{e}}^{x}} = 0$ ,得 $x = 0$ 或 $x =  - 1$ ,

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$x$ & (−∞,-1) & -1 & (-1,0) & 0 & (0，+∞) \\
\cline{1-6}
${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & + & 0 & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-6}
$F\left( x\right)$ & ↗ & 极大值 & ↘ & 极小值 & ↗ \\
\cline{1-6}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以 $F\left( x\right)$ 的单调增区间为 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$ 和 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,单调减区间为 $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ ; 极大值 $F\left( {-1}\right)  = 2$ ,极小值 $F\left( 0\right)  = {e} - 1$ ,又 $F\left( 4\right)  = {{e}}^{5} - 4{e} - \dfrac{1}{{{e}}^{4}} - 4 > 2,F\left( {-4}\right)  = {{e}}^{-3} + 4{e} - {{e}}^{4} + 4 < {e} - 1,$ 结合单调性,故函数 $y = F\left( x\right)  - m$ 有三个零点时 $m$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {F\left( 0\right) ,F\left( {-1}\right) }\right)$ 即 $\left( {{e} - 1,2}\right)$ ;

 \ding{173}  令 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ 得 ${{e}}^{x} = 1$ 或 ${{e}}^{x} = \dfrac{1}{a} > 1,x = 0$ 或 $x = \ln \dfrac{1}{a} =  - \ln a > 0,$

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$x$ & $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ & 0 & (0,-lna) & $- \ln a$ & $\left( {-\ln a, + \infty }\right)$ \\
\cline{1-6}
${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & + & 0 & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-6}
$F\left( x\right)$ & ↗ & 极大值 & ↘ & 极小值 & ↗ \\
\cline{1-6}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以 ${x}_{1} = 0,{x}_{2} =  - \ln a$ ,故 $F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = F\left( 0\right)  = a - 1 < 0,F\left( {x}_{2}\right)  =  F\left( {-\ln a}\right)  = a\left( {\dfrac{1}{a} + \ln a}\right)  - a + \ln a = \left( {a + 1}\right) \ln a + 1 - a <  F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < 0$ ,所以 $t < 0$ ,设 $\varphi \left( a\right)  = F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {tF}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = a - 1 +  t\left\lbrack  {\left( {a + 1}\right) \ln a + 1 - a}\right\rbrack  ,a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,可知 $\varphi \left( 1\right)  = 0,{\varphi }^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = 1 +  t\left( {\ln a + \dfrac{a + 1}{a} - 1}\right)  = 1 + t\left( {\ln a + \dfrac{1}{a}}\right) ,a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,令 $m\left( a\right)  =  \ln a + \dfrac{1}{a},a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,其导函数为 ${m}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  = \dfrac{1}{a} - \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} = \dfrac{a - 1}{{a}^{2}}$ ,可得 ${m}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  < 0$ ,所以在 $a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 上 $m\left( a\right)$ 严格减,且 $m\left( a\right)  >  m\left( 1\right)  = 1,$

${1}^{ \circ  }t \leq   - 1,{\varphi }^{\prime }\left( a\right)  \leq  1 - \left( {\ln a + \dfrac{1}{a}}\right)  < 1 - 1 = 0$ ,所以 $\varphi \left( a\right)$ 在 $a \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格减, $\varphi \left( a\right)  > \varphi \left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,符合题意;

${2}^{ \circ  } - 1 < t < 0$ ,存在 ${a}_{0} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,使得 ${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( {a}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$a$ & $\left( {0,{a}_{0}}\right)$ & ${a}_{0}$ & ( ${a}_{0},1$ ) \\
\cline{1-4}
${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( a\right)$ & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-4}
$\varphi \left( a\right)$ & ↘ & 极小值 & ↗ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以在 $a \in  \left( {{a}_{0},1}\right)$ 上 $\varphi \left( a\right)$ 严格增,且 $\varphi \left( a\right)  < \varphi \left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,不符合题意; 综上所述,实数 $t$ 的取值范围为 $( - \infty , - 1\rbrack$ .

点睛:不等式恒成立或存在型问题，可构造函数，利用导数研究函数的单调性，求出最值，进而得出相应的含参不等式， 从而求出参数的取值范围；也可分离变量，构造函数，直接把问题转化为函数的最值问题.

15. (1) $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {f}_{2}\left( x\right) , \\  {f}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right) , \end{array}\left( {*1}\right) }\right.$ 即为 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {5x} + 1 = {x}^{3} + {2x}, \\  5 = 3{x}^{2} + 2, \end{array}\right.$ 也即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{3} - {3x} - 1 = 0, \\  x =  \pm  1, \end{array}\right.$ 由 $x = 1$ 与 $x =  - 1$ 都不满足方程 ${x}^{3} -  {3x} - 1 = 0$ ,故 $\left( {*1}\right)$ 无解,所以 ${f}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 与 ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)$ 非 “局部趋同”; (2) $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {g}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {g}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right) , \\  {g}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {g}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right) , \end{array}\right.$ 即为 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - {x}^{2} + {ax} = b{{e}}^{x}, \\   - {2x} + a = b{{e}}^{x}, \end{array}\right.$ 等价于 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} - \left( {a + 2}\right) x + a = 0, \\   - {2x} + a = b{{e}}^{x}, \end{array}\left( {*2}\right) ,\text{ 令 }g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - \left( {a + 2}\right) x + a,}\right.$ 对于任意正数 $a$ ,由 $g\left( 0\right)  = a > 0,g\left( \dfrac{a}{2}\right)  =  - \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{4} < 0$ ,又 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{a}{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上的图像是连续不间断的,故 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{a}{2}}\right)$ 上至少有一个零点,设 ${x}_{0}$ 是其中一个零点,则存在正数 $b =  \dfrac{-2{x}_{0} + a}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}$ ,使得 $\left( {*2}\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上有解 ${x}_{0}$ ,故对任意的正数 $a$ ,都存在正数 $b$ ,使得函数 ${g}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 与 ${g}_{2}\left( x\right)$ “局部趋同”;

(3) $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {h}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {h}_{2}\left( x\right) , \\  {h}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {h}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right) , \end{array}\left( *\right) }\right.$ 即为 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {mx} + \dfrac{n}{x} = \ln x, \\  m - \dfrac{n}{{x}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{x}, \end{array}\right.$ 等价于 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {2mx} - 1 = \ln x, \\  n = m{x}^{2} - x, \end{array}\left( {*3}\right) ,\text{ 令 }h\left( x\right)  = \ln x,\text{ 则 }{h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x},h\left( x\right) }\right.$ 的图像在点 $\left( {t,\ln t}\right)$ 处的切线的方程为 $y - \ln t = \dfrac{1}{t}\left( {x - t}\right)$ , 即 $y = \dfrac{1}{t}x + \ln t - 1$ ,令 $\ln t - 1 =  - 1$ ,可得 $t = 1$ ,此时上述切线方程为 $y = x - 1$ ,故当且仅当 ${2m} = 1$ 时,直线 $y = {2mx} - 1$ 与 $h\left( x\right)$ 的图像相切，由图像可知，当且仅当 ${2m} \leq  1$ 时,直线 $y = {2mx} - 1$ 与 $h\left( x\right)$ 的图像有公共点 (在 $y$ 轴右侧),故当且仅当 $m \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, ${2mx} - 1  = \ln x$ 有正数解 ${x}_{0}$ ,此时存在 $n =  m{x}_{0}^{2} - {x}_{0}$ ,使得 $\left( {*3}\right)$ 有正数解,从而 ${h}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 与 ${h}_{2}\left( x\right)$ “局部趋同”. 所以满足条件的实数 $m$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\rbrack$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/137_1288_1074_242_239_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{考点 3 公切线问题}

\section*{一、选择题}

1. B

【解析】 $F\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - {kx} \Longrightarrow  {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - k$ ,作出与直线 $y = {kx} + m$ 平行的函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的所有切线,各切线与函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的切点的横坐标依次为 $a,b,c,d,e,f\left( x\right)$ 在 $a,b,c,d,e$ ,处的导数都等于 $k$ ,在 $\left( {0,a}\right) ,\left( {b,c}\right) ,(d$ , $e)$ 上, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > k,{F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,F\left( x\right)$ 严格增,在 $\left( {a,b}\right) ,\left( {c,d}\right) ,(e$ , $+ \infty )$ 上, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < k,{F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ , $F\left( x\right)$ 严格减，因此函数 $F\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  - {kx}$ 有三个极大值点,有两个极小值点. 故选: B.

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/137_1232_1731_298_241_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{二、解答题}

2. ( 1 )显然直线 $y = 1$ 切 $y = \sin x$ 的图像于点 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{2},1}\right)$ , $\left( {\dfrac{5\pi }{2},1}\right)$ ，直线 $y = 1$ 是 $y = \sin x$ 的图像的一条 “自公切线”， 因此函数 ${f}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 的图像存在 “自公切线”; 对于 ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = \ln x$ , ${f}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x}\left( {x > 0}\right)$ 是严格减函数,则 ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)$ 在不同点处的切线斜率不同，所以函数 ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)$ 的图像不存在 “自公切线”；

(2)由 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{\cos }^{2}x} - 1 = \dfrac{{\sin }^{2}x}{{\cos }^{2}x} = {\tan }^{2}x \geq  0$ 恒成立，且仅当 $x = 0$ 时 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,则 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上的严格增函数，可得它至多有一个零点，

令 ${g}_{1}\left( x\right)  = \sin x - \left( {x - a}\right) \cos x\left( {x \in  \left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ ,由 $y =  {g}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 的图像是连续曲线,且 ${g}_{1}\left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right) {g}_{1}\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  =  - 1 < 0$ , 因此 ${g}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上存在零点,即在 $\left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{g}_{1}\left( x\right) }{\cos x}$ 存在零点,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 有唯一零点; 假设 $g\left( x\right)$ 的图像存在 “自公切线”,则存在 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 且 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ ,使得 $g\left( x\right)$ 的图像在 $x = {x}_{1}$ 与 $x = {x}_{2}$ 处的切线重合,即 ${\tan }^{2}{x}_{1} = {\tan }^{2}{x}_{2}$ ,有 ${x}_{2} =  - {x}_{1}$ ,不妨设 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ , 切线 ${l}_{1} : y - \tan {x}_{1} + {x}_{1} - a = {\tan }^{2}{x}_{1} \cdot  \left( {x - {x}_{1}}\right) ,{l}_{2} : y -  \tan {x}_{2} + {x}_{2} - a = {\tan }^{2}{x}_{2} \cdot  \left( {x - {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,有相同截距,即 $- {x}_{1}{\tan }^{2}{x}_{1} + \tan {x}_{1} - {x}_{1} + a =  - {x}_{2}{\tan }^{2}{x}_{2} + \tan {x}_{2} - {x}_{2} +  a$ ,而 ${x}_{2} =  - {x}_{1}$ ,则 $- {x}_{1}{\tan }^{2}{x}_{1} + \tan {x}_{1} - {x}_{1} = {x}_{1}{\tan }^{2}{x}_{1} -  \tan {x}_{1} + {x}_{1}$ ,即 ${x}_{1}\left( {1 + {\tan }^{2}{x}_{1}}\right)  = \tan {x}_{1}$ ,则有 ${x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1}$ , 即 $2{x}_{1} = \sin 2{x}_{1}$ ,令 $\varphi \left( x\right)  = x - \sin x,0 < x < \pi ,{\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 -  \cos x > 0$ ,即函数 $\varphi \left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\pi }\right)$ 上严格增, $\varphi \left( x\right)  > \varphi \left( 0\right)  = 0$ , 因此当 $x \in  \left( {0,\pi }\right)$ 时, $x > \sin x$ ,即 $2{x}_{1} = \sin 2{x}_{1}$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上无解,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 的图像不存在 “自公切线”;

(3)对给定的 $n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ，由(2)知 $h\left( x\right)$ 有唯一零点，即 ${x}_{n}$ 唯一确定,又 $h\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {t,\sin t}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y - \sin t =  \cos t\left( {x - t}\right)$ ,即 $y = x\cos t + \sin t - t\cos t,h\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {s,\sin s}\right)$ 处的切线方程为 $y = x\cos s + \sin s - s\cos s$ ,若存在 $s \in  \left( {{2\pi }, + \infty }\right)$ ,使得点 $\left( {s,\sin s}\right)$ 与 $\left( {t,\sin t}\right)$ 是函数 $y = \sin x$ 图像的一对“同切点”, 则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \cos s = \cos t\left( {s \neq  t}\right) \\  \sin s - s\cos s = \sin t - t\cos t > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,又 $t \in  \left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ,则 $\cos t >$ 0,所以 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \cos s = \cos t\left( {s \neq  t}\right) \\  \tan s - s = \tan t - t \end{array},\cos s = \cos t}\right.$ 且 $\tan s =  - \tan t$ ,从而存在 $n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,使得 $s = {2n\pi } - t$ ,代入 $\tan s - s = \tan t - t$ ,可得 $\tan t - t + {n\pi } = 0$ ,则 ${x}_{n} = t$ ,即 $t$ 是数列 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项; 反之,若 $t$ 是数列 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项,则存在 $n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,使得 ${x}_{n} = t$ ,即 $\tan t - t  + {n\pi } = 0$ ,由 (2) 中的 $g\left( x\right)$ 严格增,可知 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格增,又 $h\left( 0\right)  = {n\pi } > 0$ 且 $h\left( t\right)  = 0$ ,可知 $t < 0$ ,令 $s = {2n\pi } - t$ ,则 $s \in  \left( {{2\pi }, + \infty }\right)$ 且 $\cos s = \cos t,\tan s - s - \left( {\tan t - t}\right)  = 2(t - \tan t -  {n\pi }) = 0$ ,即 $\tan s - s = \tan t - t$ ,可得 $\sin s - s\cos s = \sin t -  t\cos t$ ,所以存在 $s \in  \left( {{2\pi }, + \infty }\right)$ ,使得点 $\left( {s,\sin s}\right)$ 与 $\left( {t,\sin t}\right)$ 是函数 $y = \sin x$ 的图像的一对“同切点”. 所以存在 $s \in  ({2\pi }$ , $+ \infty )$ ,使得点 $\left( {s,\sin s}\right)$ 与 $\left( {t,\sin t}\right)$ 是函数 $y = \sin x$ 图像的一对“同切点”的充要条件是“ $t$ 是数列 $\left\{  {x}_{n}\right\}$ 中的项”.

3. (1) 由题意 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3\left( {a - 2}\right) {x}^{2} - {2x} + 5 + \dfrac{1 - a}{x}$ ,由 1 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的驻点,得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 3\left( {a - 2}\right)  + 3 + \left( {1 - a}\right)  = 0$ , 因此 $a = 1$ ;

(2)当 $a = 0$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  =  - 2{x}^{3} - {x}^{2} + {5x} + \ln x$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 6{x}^{2} - {2x} + 5 + \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,原问题等价于是否存在 ${x}_{0} > 0$ ,使得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + 1 = 0$ ,令 $g\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  + 1 =  - 6{x}^{2} - {2x} + 6 + \dfrac{1}{x}  \left( {x > 0}\right)$ ,因为函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},1}\right\rbrack$ 上是一段连续曲线,且 $g\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = \dfrac{11}{2} > 0,g\left( 1\right)  =  - 1 < 0$ ,由零点存在定理,存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2},1}\right)$ ,使得 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + 1 = 0$ ,即曲线 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 存在切线与直线 $x - y - 1 = 0$ 互相垂直;

(3)当 $a = 2$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{2} + {5x} - \ln x$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - {2x} +  5 - \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,假设存在等差数列 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}\left( {0 < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3}}\right)$ 满足题意,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{3} - {x}_{1}}$ ,即 $- 2{x}_{2} + 5 - \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}} =  - \dfrac{{x}_{3}^{2} - {x}_{1}^{2}}{{x}_{3} - {x}_{1}} + 5 - \dfrac{\ln {x}_{3} - \ln {x}_{1}}{{x}_{3} - {x}_{1}}$ ,将 ${x}_{2} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{3}}{2}$ 代入上式得, $\dfrac{2\left( {{x}_{3} - {x}_{1}}\right) }{{x}_{3} + {x}_{1}} = \ln {x}_{3} - \ln {x}_{1}$ ,即 $\dfrac{2\left( {\dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}} - 1}\right) }{\dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}} + 1} - \ln \dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}} = 0\left( *\right)$ ,令 $\dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}} =  t,h\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{2\left( {t - 1}\right) }{t + 1} - \ln t\left( {t > 1}\right)$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{4}{{\left( t + 1\right) }^{2}} - \dfrac{1}{t} =  \dfrac{-{\left( t - 1\right) }^{2}}{t{\left( t + 1\right) }^{2}} < 0$ ,因此函数 $y = h\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上为严格减函数,由题意 $\dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}} = t > 1,h\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $h\left( t\right)  < 0$ ,即 $h\left( \dfrac{{x}_{3}}{{x}_{1}}\right)  < 0$ , 故等式 $\left( *\right)$ 不成立，因此，不存在等差数列 ${x}_{1}$ ， ${x}_{2}$ ， ${x}_{3}\left( {0 < {x}_{1} < }\right.  {x}_{2} < {x}_{3}$ )满足题目条件.

4.(1)因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \cos x \geq  0$ ，当且仅当在 $x = \pi$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ，所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,{2\pi }}\right\rbrack$ 上是增函数 (区间开闭都对)；

(2)由题意得， $\left( {k - 1}\right) x < \sin x$ ，于是 $k - 1 < \dfrac{\sin x}{x}$ . 令 $h\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{\sin x}{x}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x\cos x - \sin x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,令 $u\left( x\right)  = x\cos x - \sin x$ ,则 ${u}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - x\sin x < 0,x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 $u\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上是严格减函数,于是 $u\left( x\right)  < u\left( 0\right)  = 0,x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ . 由于 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{x\cos x - \sin x}{{x}^{2}} < 0,x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,于是 $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上是严格减函数，所以 ${h}_{\min }\left( x\right)  = h\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{\pi }$ ，因此 $k - 1 < \dfrac{2}{\pi }$ ，即 $k <  \dfrac{2}{\pi } + 1$

(3)设 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right)$ 、 $B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ 、 $C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ ，则曲线在 $A$ 、 $B$ 、 $C$ 三点处的切线分别为直线 ${l}_{1} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{1}}\right) x - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} +  \sin {x}_{1},{l}_{2} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{2}}\right) x - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} + \sin {x}_{2},{l}_{3} : y = (1 +  \left. {\cos {x}_{3}}\right) x - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3} + \sin {x}_{3}$ . 因为直线 ${l}_{1},{l}_{2},{l}_{3}$ 互相重合,所以 $\cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} = \cos {x}_{3}$ ,且 $- {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} + \sin {x}_{1} =  - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} +  \sin {x}_{2} =  - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3} + \sin {x}_{3}$ . 因为 $\cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} = \cos {x}_{3}$ ,所以 $\sin {x}_{1} =  \pm  \sin {x}_{2},\sin {x}_{2} =  \pm  \sin {x}_{3},\sin {x}_{3} =  \pm  \sin {x}_{1}.$

 \ding{172}  若 $\sin {x}_{1} =  - \sin {x}_{2},\sin {x}_{2} =  - \sin {x}_{3},\sin {x}_{3} =  - \sin {x}_{1}$ . 则 $\sin {x}_{1} = 0,\sin {x}_{2} = 0,\sin {x}_{3} = 0$ ,于是 $- {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} =  - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} =  - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3}$ ,因为 $\cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} = \cos {x}_{3} =  \pm  1 \neq  0$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} =  {x}_{2} = {x}_{3}$ ,与 $A,B,C$ 三点互不重合矛盾;

 \ding{173}  若 $\sin {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2},\sin {x}_{2} = \sin {x}_{3},\sin {x}_{3} = \sin {x}_{1}$ 中至少一个成立,不妨设 $\sin {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2}$ 成立,则 ${x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2}$ ,若 $\cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} \neq  0$ ,则 ${x}_{1} = {x}_{2}$ ,矛盾,舍去,于是 $\cos {x}_{1} =  \cos {x}_{2} = 0,\sin {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2} =  \pm  1$ ,所以满足要求的切线方程为 $y = x + 1$ 或 $y = x - 1$ .

解法 2: 假设存在三个不同点 $A\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{3}\text{ , }}\right.  {y}_{3}$ ) 在曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 上满足条件,则 ${y}_{1} = {x}_{1} + \sin {x}_{1},{y}_{2} =  {x}_{2} + \sin {x}_{2},{y}_{3} = {x}_{3} + \sin {x}_{3}$ ,且 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ 互不相同. 曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $A,B,C$ 三点处的切线方程分别为:

${l}_{1} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{1}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1},$

${l}_{2} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{2}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{2} - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2},$

${l}_{3} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{3}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{3} - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3},$

依题意, 有

$\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} = \cos {x}_{3}, \\  \sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2} - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} = \sin {x}_{3} - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3}, \end{array}\right.$

由 \ding{172} 得, ${x}_{2} = {2k\pi } \pm  {x}_{1},{x}_{3} = {2n\pi } \pm  {x}_{1},k,n \in  \mathbf{Z}$ .

情形 1: 若 ${x}_{2} = {2k\pi } + {x}_{1},{x}_{3} = {2n\pi } + {x}_{1},k,n \neq  0,k \neq  n$ ,代入  \ding{173} 得， $\sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{1} - \left( {{2k\pi } + {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{1} -  \left( {{2n\pi } + {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1}$ . 即 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \left( {2k\pi }\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0, \\  \left( {2n\pi }\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\text{ ,而 }k,n \neq  0}\right.$ ,故 $\cos {x}_{1} =  0,\sin {x}_{1} =  \pm  1$ ,此时满足条件的切线方程为 $y = x \pm  1$ .

情形 2: 若 ${x}_{2} = {2k\pi } - {x}_{1},{x}_{3} = {2n\pi } - {x}_{1},k \neq  n$ ,代入 \ding{173} 得, $\sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} =  - \sin {x}_{1} - \left( {{2k\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1}  =  - \sin {x}_{1} - \left( {{2n\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1}$ .

即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \sin {x}_{1} + \left( {{k\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0, \\  \sin {x}_{1} + \left( {{n\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,两式相减,得 $\left( {k - n}\right) \pi$ . $\cos {x}_{1} = 0$ ,由于 $k \neq  n$ ,故 $\cos {x}_{1} = 0$ ,此时 $\sin {x}_{1} = 0$ ,与 ${\sin }^{2}{x}_{1}  + {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} = 1$ 矛盾,舍去.

情形 3: 若 ${x}_{2} = {2k\pi } + {x}_{1},{x}_{3} = {2n\pi } - {x}_{1},k \neq  0$ ,代入 \ding{173} 得, $\sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{1} - \left( {{2k\pi } + {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1}$

$=  - \sin {x}_{1} - \left( {{2n\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1}.$

即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {2k\pi }\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0, \\  \sin {x}_{1} + \left( {{n\pi } - {x}_{1}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,故 $\cos {x}_{1} = 0$ ,则 $\sin {x}_{1} = 0$ ,与 ${\sin }^{2}{x}_{1} + {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} = 1$ 矛盾,舍去.

情形 4: 若 ${x}_{2} = {2k\pi } - {x}_{1},{x}_{3} = {2n\pi } + {x}_{1},n \neq  0$ ,与情形 3 完全类似,舍去. 综上,满足条件的切线方程为 $y = x \pm  1$ . 解法 3: 假设存在三个不同点 $A  \left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,B\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right) ,C\left( {{x}_{3},{y}_{3}}\right)$ 在曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 上满足条件,则 ${y}_{1} = {x}_{1}  + \sin {x}_{1},{y}_{2} = {x}_{2} + \sin {x}_{2},{y}_{3} = {x}_{3} +  \sin {x}_{3}$ ,且 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ 互不相同. 曲线 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $A,B,C$ 三点处的切线方程分别为:

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/139_572_1684_245_250_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

${l}_{1} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{1}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1}$ ,

${l}_{2} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{2}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{2} - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2},$

${l}_{3} : y = \left( {1 + \cos {x}_{3}}\right) x + \sin {x}_{3} - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3},$

依题意,有 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \cos {x}_{1} = \cos {x}_{2} = \cos {x}_{3},\left( 1\right) \\  \sin {x}_{1} - {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2} - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} \\   \end{array}\right.$ 由 \ding{172} 得, $\left| {\sin {x}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {\sin {x}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {\sin {x}_{3}}\right|$ ,由 \ding{173} ,令 $\sin {x}_{1} -  {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} = \sin {x}_{2} - {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} = \sin {x}_{3} - {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3} = t$ ,则 $\sin {x}_{1} =  t + {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1},\sin {x}_{2} = t + {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2},\sin {x}_{3} = t + {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3}$ ,即有 $\left| {t + {x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1}}\right|  = \left| {t + {x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2}}\right|  = \left| {t + {x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3}}\right|$ ,平方,得 ${t}^{2} + {2t}{x}_{1}\cos {x}_{1} + {x}_{1}^{2}{\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} = {t}^{2} + {2t}{x}_{2}\cos {x}_{2} + {x}_{2}^{2}{\cos }^{2}{x}_{2} =  {t}^{2} + {2t}{x}_{3}\cos {x}_{3} + {x}_{3}^{2}{\cos }^{2}{x}_{3},$

即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{2}^{2}}\right) {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} + {2t}\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0 \\  \left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{3}^{2}}\right) {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} + {2t}\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{3}}\right) \cos {x}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,由于 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ 互不相同,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}\right) {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} + {2t}\cos {x}_{1} = 0 \\  \left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{3}}\right) {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} + {2t}\cos {x}_{1} = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,相减,得 $\left( {{x}_{2} - }\right.  \left. {x}_{3}\right) {\cos }^{2}{x}_{1} = 0$ ,于是 $\cos {x}_{1} = 0$ ,则 $\sin {x}_{1} =  \pm  1$ ,此时满足条件的切线方程为 $y = x \pm  1$ .

\section*{考点 4 利用导数判断函数单调性及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3}$

【解析】如图,建立平面直角坐标系,得 $M\left( {0,1}\right) ,N\left( {3,4}\right)$ ,设抛物线方程为 $y =  a{x}^{2} + 1$ ,则 $a = \dfrac{4 - 1}{9} = \dfrac{1}{3} \Longrightarrow  y = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{2} + 1$ , 设 $P\left( {t,\dfrac{{t}^{2}}{3} + 1}\right) ,t \in  \left\lbrack  {0,3}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $\left| {PR}\right|  =  4 - t,\left| {PQ}\right|  = \dfrac{{t}^{2}}{3} + 1 \Longrightarrow  {S}_{BQPR} = f\left( t\right)  = \left( {4 - t}\right) \left( {\dfrac{{t}^{2}}{3} + 1}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{3}{t}^{3} + \dfrac{4}{3}{t}^{2} - t + 4 \Longrightarrow  {f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  =  - {t}^{2} + \dfrac{8}{3}t - 1$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $t = \dfrac{4 \pm  \sqrt{7}}{3}$ ,所以当 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{4 - \sqrt{7}}{3}}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  < 0,f\left( t\right)$ 严格减; 当 $t \in  \left( {\dfrac{4 - \sqrt{7}}{3},\dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3}}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  > 0,f\left( t\right)$ 严格增; 当 $t \in  \left( {\dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3},3}\right\rbrack$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  < 0,f\left( t\right)$ 严格减,又 $f\left( 0\right)  = f\left( 3\right)  = 4$ , $f\left( 3\right)  < f\left( \dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3}\right)$ ,所以当 $t = \dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3}$ 时, $f\left( t\right)$ 取得最大值,此时， ${AQ} = \dfrac{4 + \sqrt{7}}{3}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/139_1315_852_213_206_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

2. $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3},\sqrt{3}}\right\rbrack$

【解析】 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \cos x - a\sin x$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x + a\cos x$ 在 $\left( {\dfrac{2\pi }{3},\dfrac{7\pi }{6}}\right)$ 上是严格单调函数,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 或 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ , 当 $x = \pi$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( \pi \right)  =  - 1,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 不符合题意; 由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ 时,得 $a\sin x \geq  \cos x$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{2\pi }{3},\pi }\right)$ 时, $\sin x > 0$ ,所以 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{\tan x}$ 在 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{2\pi }{3},\pi }\right)$ 上恒成立,即求 $a \geq  {\left( \dfrac{1}{\tan x}\right) }_{\max }$ ,因为 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{2\pi }{3},\pi }\right)$ ,所以 $\tan x \in  \left( {-\sqrt{3},0}\right) ,\dfrac{1}{\tan x} \in  \left( {-\infty , - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right)$ ,所以 $a \geq   - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}$ ; 当 $x \in  \left( {\pi ,\dfrac{7\pi }{6}}\right)$ 时, $\sin x < 0$ ,所以 $a \leq  \dfrac{1}{\tan x}$ 在 $x \in  \left( {\pi ,\dfrac{7\pi }{6}}\right)$ 上恒成立,即求 $a \leq  {\left( \dfrac{1}{\tan x}\right) }_{\min }$ ,因为 $x \in  \left( {\pi ,\dfrac{7\pi }{6}}\right)$ ,所

以 $\tan x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3}}\right) ,\dfrac{1}{\tan x} \in  \left( {\sqrt{3}, + \infty }\right)$ ,即 $a \leq  \sqrt{3}$ ; 综上所述, $- \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3} \leq  a \leq  \sqrt{3}$ . 故答案为: $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{3},\sqrt{3}}\right\rbrack$ .

3. $\left( {4{{e}}^{-\dfrac{3}{4}}, + \infty }\right)$

【解析】 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{x \rightarrow  {x}_{0}}}\dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - x + {x}_{0}}{x - {x}_{0}} > 0$ ,即 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{x \rightarrow  {x}_{0}}}\dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - \left( {x - {x}_{0}}\right) }{x - {x}_{0}} > 0$ ,即 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{x \rightarrow  {x}_{0}}}\dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }{x - {x}_{0}} -  1 > 0$ ,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - 1 > 0$ 对任意 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 恒成立, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  a{{e}}^{x} - {4x}$ ,即 $a{{e}}^{x} - {4x} - 1 > 0$ 对任意 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 恒成立, $a >  \dfrac{{4x} + 1}{{{e}}^{x}}$ 对任意 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 恒成立,则 $a > {\left( \dfrac{{4x} + 1}{{{e}}^{x}}\right) }_{\max }$ ,设 $h\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{{4x} + 1}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{3 - {4x}}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,令 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{3 - {4x}}{{{e}}^{x}} = 0$ ,解得 $x = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{3}{4}}\right)$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,此时 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格增,当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{3}{4},1}\right)$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,此时 $h\left( x\right)$ 严格减,则 $h{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = h\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right)  =  4{{e}}^{-\dfrac{3}{4}}$ ,则 $a \in  \left( {4{{e}}^{-\dfrac{3}{4}}, + \infty }\right)$ ,故答案为: $\left( {4{{e}}^{-\dfrac{3}{4}}, + \infty }\right)$ .

4. $(0,6\sqrt{3}\rbrack$

【解析】依题意,设 $\angle {OAB} = x\left( {0 < x < \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${OA} =  {AB}\cos x = 8\cos x,{AP} = {OA}\cos x = 8{\cos }^{2}x$ ,因此 $\bigtriangleup {OAP}$ 的面积 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}{OA} \cdot  {AP}\sin x = {32}\sin x{\cos }^{3}x,0 < x < \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ,求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {32}\left( {{\cos }^{4}x - 3{\sin }^{2}x{\cos }^{2}x}\right)  = {32}{\cos }^{4}x(1 -  \left. {3{\tan }^{2}x}\right)$ ,当 $0 < x < \dfrac{\pi }{6}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,当 $\dfrac{\pi }{6} < x < \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  <$ 0,即函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{6}}\right)$ 上严格增,在 $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{6},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上严格减,因此 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\max } = f\left( \dfrac{\pi }{6}\right)  = {32} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  {\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right) }^{3} = 6\sqrt{3}$ ,而 $f\left( 0\right)  =  f\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  = 0$ ,则 $0 < f\left( x\right)  \leq  6\sqrt{3}$ ,所以 $\bigtriangleup {OAP}$ 面积的取值范围是 $(0,6\sqrt{3}\rbrack$ . 故答案为: $(0,6\sqrt{3}\rbrack$ .

5. $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{9}{4}}\right)$

【解析】由题意知 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + {2x} - {2k} = \dfrac{2{x}^{2} - {2kx} + 1}{x} > 0$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 内有解,即 $2{x}^{2} - {2kx} + 1 > 0$ ,即 ${2k} < {2x} + \dfrac{1}{x}$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 内有解,设 $g\left( x\right)  = {2x} + \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,则该函数在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)$ 上严格减,在 $\left( {\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,且 $g\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = 3,g\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{9}{2}$ ,故 $g\left( x\right)  = {2x} + \dfrac{1}{x}$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2},2}\right\rbrack$ 上的最大值为 $\dfrac{9}{2}$ ,故 ${2k} < \dfrac{9}{2}$ ,即实数 $k$ 的取值范围是 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{9}{4}}\right)$ ,故答案为: $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{9}{4}}\right)$ .

6. $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2{{e}}^{2}},\dfrac{1}{3{e}}}\right)$

【解析】原不等式可化为: $a\left( {x - 2}\right)  > x{{e}}^{x}$ ,令 $f\left( x\right)  = x{{e}}^{x}$ , ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \left( {x + 1}\right) {{e}}^{x}$ ,显然 $x <  - 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 严格

减; $x >  - 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,所以 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } =  f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,且 $x \rightarrow   - \infty$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \rightarrow  0,x \rightarrow   + \infty ,f\left( x\right)  \rightarrow  + \infty$ ,同一坐标系中,作出 $f\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = a\left( {x - 2}\right)$ (过定点 $(2$ , 0))的图像,据图可知,满足题意的整

$\left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - 2{{e}}^{-2} \geq  a\left( {-2 - 2}\right) \\   - {{e}}^{-1} < a\left( {-1 - 2}\right) ,\text{ 解得 }\dfrac{1}{2{{e}}^{2}} \leq  a < \dfrac{-3 - 2 - 1}{y = a\left( {x - 2}\right) } - 1 \end{array}\right. \left\{  \begin{array}{l} y = f\left( x\right) \\  \dfrac{1}{2} < x \end{array}\right.  \dfrac{1}{3{e}}$ . 故答案为: $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2{{e}}^{2}},\dfrac{1}{3{e}}}\right)$ .

7. -12

【解析】由题意, ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{3} + m\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 为 ${x}^{3} + {mx}$ 向右平移得到,即 $\left| {{x}^{3} + {mx}}\right|  \leq  {16}$ 区间长度与原题一致,不妨设 $f\left( x\right)  =  {x}^{3} + {mx}$ ,易得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} + m \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  x \geq  \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}$ 或 $x \leq  - \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}$ ,即 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty , - \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}}\right)$ 和 $\left( {\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,在 $\left( {-\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}},\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}}\right)$ 上严格减,由 $f\left( x\right)$ 关于 $\left( {0,0}\right)$ 对称,仅考虑 $x > 0$ 即可,分类讨论:

当 $f\left( \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{-m}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} + m\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} =  - {16} \Longrightarrow  m =  - {12}$ 时,易得 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - {12x} = {16} \Longrightarrow  {x}_{1} =  - 2,{x}_{2} = 4$ ,即 $T = 8$ ; 当 $f\left( \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{-m}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} + m\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} >  - {16} \Longrightarrow  m >  - {12}$ 时, $f\left( 4\right)  = {64} + {4m} > {16} \Longrightarrow  T < 8$ ; 当 $f\left( \sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{-m}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} +  m\sqrt{\dfrac{-m}{3}} <  - {16} \Longrightarrow  m <  - {12}$ 时,如图,不妨设 ${x}^{3} + {mx} = {16}$ 的三个跟分别为 ${x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ , ${x}_{6}$ ,不妨设 ${x}^{3} + {mx} =  - {16}$ 的三个跟分别为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{4},{x}_{5}$ ,由三次韦达定理可得 ${x}_{2} +  {x}_{3} + {x}_{6} = 0,{x}_{1} + {x}_{4} + {x}_{5} = 0,T = {x}_{2} -  {x}_{1} + {x}_{4} - {x}_{3} + {x}_{6} - {x}_{5} = 2\left( {{x}_{4} - {x}_{3}}\right)  < 8,$ 综上,当且仅当 $m =  - {12}$ 时, $T = 8$ . 故答案为: -12 .
