\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/140_1379_1095_200_475_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

\section*{二、选择题}

8. B

【解析】函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是偶函数,则 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ,两边求导得: $- {f}^{\prime }\left( {-x}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,所以可知导函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 是奇函数,由于函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 与它的导函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的定义域均为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,所以 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,又因为 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上是严格增函数,所以 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,由于 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是可导函数,所以它的图像是连续曲线,

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/140_1340_1944_239_231_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

如图所示，

则 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上恒为正数且递增,即 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增, 且变化率越来越大, 故 AC 显然错误,而 D 的变化率越来越小,所以只能选 B，故选:B. 9. C

【解析】依题意,因 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ 在区间 $I$ 上恒成立,则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $I$ 上严格减, ${a}_{{2n} + 2} - {a}_{2n} = f\left( {a}_{{2n} + 1}\right)  - f\left( {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right)$ , $n \in  {\mathbf{N}}^{ * }$ ,因数列 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 的各项均不相同,且 ${a}_{1} \in  I$ , \ding{172}  若 ${a}_{{2n} + 1} >  {a}_{{2n} - 1}$ ,则 $f\left( {a}_{{2n} + 1}\right)  < f\left( {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {a}_{{2n} + 2} < {a}_{2n}$ ,此时数列 $\left\{  {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right\}$ 严格增,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{2n}\right\}$ 严格减;  \ding{173}  若 ${a}_{{2n} + 1} < {a}_{{2n} - 1}$ ,则 $f\left( {a}_{{2n} + 1}\right)  >  f\left( {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  {a}_{{2n} + 2} > {a}_{2n}$ ,此时数列 $\left\{  {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right\}$ 严格减,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{2n}\right\}$ 严格增. 综上所述,数列 $\left\{  {a}_{{2n} - 1}\right\}$ 与 $\left\{  {a}_{2n}\right\}$ 中的一个是严格增数列, 另一个是严格减数列. 故选 C.

10. C

【解析】对于 \ding{172} : 设 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right) ,x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x + 1}\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,因为 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上为严格增函数,故 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x + 1}\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,即 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x + 1}\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,由于 ${x}_{1} >  {x}_{2}$ ,故 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > f\left( {{x}_{2} + 1}\right)  -  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ . 即 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {{x}_{2} + 1}\right)$ ; 当 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + }\right.$ 1) $+ f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {{x}_{2} + 1}\right)$ 成立时,即 $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 1}\right)  -  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > f\left( {{x}_{2} + 1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,由于 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( {x + 1}\right)  - f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,故 ${x}_{1} > {x}_{2}$ ,故 “ ${x}_{1} > {x}_{2}$ ” 是 “ $f\left( {{x}_{1} + 1}\right)  +  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {{x}_{2} + 1}\right)$ ”的充要条件, \ding{172}  为真命题; 对于  \ding{173} ，当 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上为严格增函数时，由对任意 $x < 0$ ，则都有 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 0\right)$ 成立; 当对任意 $x < 0$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 0\right)$ 时,假设 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上不为严格增函数,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 不恒大于等于 0,即存在 $a$ ,使得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( a\right)  < 0$ ,由于 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上为严格增函数,故 $x \in  ( - \infty ,a\rbrack$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,此时 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $( - \infty ,a\rbrack$ 上严格减，且其图像为一个严格递减的凹型曲线， 故当 $x$ 趋近于负无穷时, $f\left( x\right)$ 的值将趋近于正无穷大,这与对任意 $x < 0$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 0\right)$ 矛盾,则假设不成立,即 “ $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上为严格增函数”成立，即“对任意 $x < 0$ 都有 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( 0\right)$ ”是“ $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上为严格增函数”的充要条件， \ding{173} 为真命题，故选:C.

\section*{三、解答题}

11. (1) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{4}{{x}^{2}}$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{4}{{x}^{2}} > 0$ ，解得 $x <  - 2$ 或者 $x > 2$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{4}{{x}^{2}} < 0$ ,解得 $- 2 < x < 0$ 或 $0 <  x < 2$ ,所以,该函数的严格增区间为 $\left( {-\infty , - 2}\right)$ 和 $\left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ , 严格减区间为 $\left( {-2,0}\right)$ 和 $\left( {0,2}\right)$ ;

(2) $f\left( x\right)  < {2x}$ ，即 $x - \dfrac{4}{x} > 0$ ， $\dfrac{{x}^{2} - 4}{x} > 0$ ，即: $x\left( {{x}^{2} - 4}\right)  > 0$ ， 利用穿根法解得 $x \in  \left( {-2,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ . 所以解集为 $( - 2$ , $0) \cup  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ .

12. (1) $f\left( x\right)$ 定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{2}{x} + 1$ ，显然 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,且 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 0$ . 所以当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ; 当 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ . 所以 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的单调减区间为 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,单调增区间为 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ;

(2) $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  + {2\ln }x = {{e}}^{x - 1} + x$ ，假设存在 $A$ 、 $B$ 、 $C$ 三点满足条件,设 $A,B,C$ 三点的横坐标分别为 $s - t,s,s + t\left( {t \neq  0}\right)$ . 则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( s\right)  = \dfrac{g\left( {s + t}\right)  - g\left( {s - t}\right) }{2t}$ ,所以 ${{e}}^{s - 1} + 1 = \dfrac{{{e}}^{s - 1 + t} - {{e}}^{s - 1 - t}}{2t} + 1$ , 即 $\dfrac{{{e}}^{t} - {{e}}^{-t}}{2t} = 1$ ,即 ${{e}}^{t} - {{e}}^{-t} = {2t}$ ,令 $h\left( t\right)  = {{e}}^{t} - {{e}}^{-t} - {2t}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = {{e}}^{t} + {{e}}^{-t} - 2 \geq  0$ ,当且仅当 $t = 0$ 时等号成立,所以 $h\left( t\right)$ 严格增,只有 $t = 0$ 一个零点,矛盾. 所以不存在满足条件的 $A,B,C$ 三点;

(3)令 $F\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - 2\ln x + {4x} - 6 - {\left( x - 2\right) }^{3}$ ，只需证明当 $x > 0$ 时， $F\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 恒成立. 由 $F\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ， ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{2}{x} +  4 - 3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2},{F}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时,显然 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 严格增, 所以 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ . 当 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 是,分两段, \ding{172}  当 $x \in  (1,3\rbrack$ 时, $3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} \leq  1$ ,所以 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{2}{x} + 4 - 3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} >  1 - 2 + 4 - 3 = 0$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $x \in  \left( {3, + \infty }\right)$ 时， ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - \dfrac{2}{x} +  4 - 3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} > {{e}}^{x - 1} - 3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2}$ ,令 $m\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} -  3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2}$ ,则 ${m}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - 6\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,再令 $n\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} -  6\left( {x - 2}\right)$ ,则 ${n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x - 1} - 6$ ,当 $x > 3$ 时, ${n}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,所以 $n\left( x\right)$ 严格增,所以 $n\left( x\right)  > n\left( 3\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - 6 > 0$ ,即 ${m}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,所以 $m\left( x\right)$ 严格增,所以 $m\left( x\right)  > m\left( 3\right)  = {{e}}^{2} - 3 > 0$ ,所以 ${{e}}^{x - 1} >  3{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2}$ ,所以 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,综上可知, $F{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = F\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,所以 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 图像上任意一点都不落在函数 $y = {\left( x - 2\right) }^{3} -  3\left( {x - 2}\right)$ 图像的下方.

13.(1)由题得 $f\left( x\right)  = {2x},{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2$ ，所以 $F\left( x\right)  = {2x} + 2$ ， 因为 $f\left( x\right)  \in  {A}_{\left( 1,m\right) }$ ,所以 ${2x} + 2 = 1$ ,解得 $x =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $m = 1$ ,固着点 $x =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ;

( 2 )由题得 $f\left( x\right)  = x + \sin x$ ，则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \cos x$ ，所以 $F\left( x\right)  = x + 1 + \sqrt{2}\sin \left( {x + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ ,因为 $F\left( x\right)$ 是 $D$ 上的严格增函数，所以 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \sqrt{2}\cos \left( {x + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \geq  0$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {s,t}\right\rbrack$ 上恒成立,由 $\cos \left( {x + \dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)  \geq   - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}$ ,得到 ${2k\pi } + \pi  \leq  x \leq  {2k\pi } + \dfrac{5\pi }{2}\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ , 所以 $\left\lbrack  {s,t}\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \left\lbrack  {{2k\pi } + \pi ,{2k\pi } + \dfrac{5\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack  \left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ,所以 $t - s \leq  \left( {{2k\pi } + \dfrac{5\pi }{2}}\right)  - \left( {{2k\pi } + \pi }\right)  = \dfrac{3\pi }{2}$ ,因此 $t - s$ 的最大值是 $\dfrac{3\pi }{2}$ ;

(3)(方法一)由题得 $f\left( x\right)  = a{{e}}^{x} - x,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a{{e}}^{x} - 1$ ，所以 $F\left( x\right)  = {2a}{{e}}^{x} - x - 1$ ,因为 $f\left( x\right)  \in  {A}_{\left( 0,1\right) }$ ,且 $t$ 是 $f\left( x\right)$ 的固着点,所以 ${2a} = \dfrac{x + 1}{{{e}}^{x}}\left( *\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上有唯一的解 $t$ ,记 $g\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x + 1}{{{e}}^{x}}\left( {x > 0}\right)$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{x}{{{e}}^{x}} < 0$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $(0$ , $+ \infty )$ 是严格减函数,从而 $g\left( x\right)  < g\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ,又当 $x \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $g\left( x\right)  \rightarrow  0$ ,故 $g\left( x\right)$ 的值域是 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,所以 $0 < {2a} < 1$ ,即 $a \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,记 $h\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x + 1}{{{e}}^{x}} - {2a}$ ,则由上述可知 $h\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 的严格减函数且 $h\left( t\right)  = 0,h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)  = \dfrac{\ln \dfrac{1}{2a} + 1}{{{e}}^{\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}} -  {2a} =  - {2a}\ln \left( {2a}\right)$ ,因为 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $\ln \left( {2a}\right)  < 0$ ,所以 $h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)  > 0$  \ding{172} ，又 $h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  = \dfrac{\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} + 1}{{{e}}^{\dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}} - {2a} = {a}^{2} - 2{a}^{2}\ln a -$

${2a}$ ,记 $m\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 2{x}^{2}\ln x - {2x}\left( {0 < x < \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,则 ${m}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - {2x}\left( {2\ln x + 1}\right)$ ,因为 $0 < x < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $2\ln x + 1 < 2\ln \dfrac{1}{2} + 1 < 0$ , 所以 ${m}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,所以 $m\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 上的严格增函数,故 $m\left( x\right)  < m\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} - 2 \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} \cdot  \ln \dfrac{1}{2} - 2 \cdot  \dfrac{1}{2} < 0,$ 从而 $h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  < 0$  \ding{173} ，由  \ding{172}   \ding{173}  可知， $h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  < 0 <  h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)$ ,即 $h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  < h\left( t\right)  < h\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)$ ,又 $h\left( x\right)$ 是 $(0$ , $+ \infty )$ 的严格减函数,所以 $\ln \dfrac{1}{2a} < t < \ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ ,故 $\dfrac{1}{2a} < {{e}}^{t} < \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ .

(方法二) 由题得 $f\left( x\right)  = a{{e}}^{x} - x,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = a{{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,所以 $F\left( x\right)  = {2a}{{e}}^{x} - x - 1$ ,因为 $f\left( x\right)  \in  {A}_{\left( 0,1\right) }$ 且 $t$ 是 $f\left( x\right)$ 的固着点，所以 $F\left( x\right)  = 0\left( *\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上有唯一的解 $t$ ，求导得 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2a}{{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,当 $a \leq  0$ 时， ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,F\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的严格减函数,所以 $F\left( x\right)  < F\left( 0\right)  = {2a} - 1 \leq   - 1$ ,所以方程 $\left( *\right)$ 无解; 当 $a > 0$ 时,

(i) 当 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > {2a} - 1 \geq  0$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立,故 $F\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上的严格增函数,所以 $F\left( x\right)  > F\left( 0\right)  =  {2a} - 1 \geq  1 > 0$ ,所以方程 $\left( *\right)$ 无解;

(ii) 当 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时,如下表

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$x$ & $\left( {0,\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)$ & $\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}$ & $\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ \\
\cline{1-4}
${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-4}
$F\left( x\right)$ & ↘ & 极小值 & ↗ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

可知 $F\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)$ 严格减，在 $\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ 严格增，又 $F\left( 0\right)  = {2a} - 1 < 0,F\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)  = \ln \left( {2a}\right)  < 0$ ,当 $x \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $F\left( x\right)  \rightarrow   + \infty$ ,所以方程 $\left( *\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)$ 无解,在 $\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ 有唯一解,满足题意的 $a$ 的取值范围 $0 < a <  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,因为 $t$ 是 $\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a}, + \infty }\right)$ 的唯一解,所以 $t > \ln \dfrac{1}{2a}$ ,又 $F\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{a} + 2\ln a - 1$ ,令 $h\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{x} + 2\ln x - 1,0 < x <  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{2}{{x}^{2}} + \dfrac{2}{x} =  - 2{\left( \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2} <  - 4$ ,所以 $h\left( x\right)$ 是 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 上的严格减函数,所以 $h\left( x\right)  > h\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  = 4 +  2\ln \dfrac{1}{2} - 1 \approx  {1.614} > 0$ ,即 $F\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)  > 0$ ,又当 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, $\ln \dfrac{1}{2a} - \ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}} = \ln \dfrac{a}{2} < 0$ ,所以 $\ln \dfrac{1}{2a} < \ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ ,又 $F\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {\ln \dfrac{1}{2a},\ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}}\right)$ 上有唯一的零点,则 $\ln \dfrac{1}{2a} < t < \ln \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ ,综上, $a \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,此时 $\dfrac{1}{2a} < {{e}}^{t} < \dfrac{1}{{a}^{2}}$ .

14. (1) 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = x + \dfrac{1}{x},x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ ,求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  1 - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} = \dfrac{{x}^{2} - 1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ; 当 $x \in  \left( {-1,0}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)$ 上严格增, $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ 上严格减，于是函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上不是单调函数,任意 $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right) ,f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 2$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上的值域为 $( - \infty , - 2\rbrack$ ,不存在常数 $b$ ,使得 $f\left( x\right)  \geq  b$ 对任意的 $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 成立，所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ ， $x \in  D$ 不具有性质 $H$ ;

(2)函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}{x}^{3} - x + 1$ ，求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 1 = (x +$ 1) $\left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,当 $x <  - 1$ 或 $x > 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ; 当 $- 1 < x < 1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty , - 1}\right) ,\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,在 $\left( {-1,1}\right)$ 上严格减，由函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left( {m,2}\right)$ 具有性质 $H$ , 且 $b = \dfrac{1}{3}$ 是该函数的一个下界,得 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } \geq  \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,当 $m < 1$ 时, 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {m,2}\right)$ 上不单调, $f\left( {-1}\right)  = \dfrac{5}{3},f\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,由 $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,即 ${x}^{3} - {3x} + 2 = 0$ ,整理得 $\left( {x + 2}\right) {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} = 0$ ,解得 $x =  - 2$ 或 $x = 1$ ,当 $x <  - 2$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( {-2}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ; 当 $- 2 \leq  x < 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \geq  f\left( {-2}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,因此任意 $x \in  \lbrack  - 2,1)$ , $f\left( x\right)  \geq  \dfrac{1}{3}$ ,则 $- 2 \leq  m < 1$ ,所以当且仅当 $m \in  \lbrack  - 2,1)$ 时,函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  D$ 具有性质 $H$ ,且 $b = \dfrac{1}{3}$ 是该函数的一个下界;

(3)当 $a \in  (0,1\rbrack ,x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时，函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln \left( {x + 1}\right)  + a{x}^{2} -  {2ax}$ ,求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x + 1} + {2ax} - {2a} = \dfrac{{2a}{x}^{2} - \left( {{2a} - 1}\right) }{x + 1}$ ,当 $0 < a \leq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, ${2a} - 1 \leq  0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,不符合题意; 当 $\dfrac{1}{2} < a \leq  1$ 时, $0 < {2a} - 1 \leq  1$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ , 得 $0 < x < \sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}}$ ; 由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,得 $\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}} < x < 1$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}}}\right)$ 上严格减，在 $\left( {\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}},1}\right)$ 上严格增,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上不是单调函数, $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } =  f\left( \sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)  = \ln \left( {\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}} + 1}\right)  + a - \dfrac{1}{2} - {2a}\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}},  b \leq  f{\left( x\right) }_{\min }$ ,因此 $\dfrac{1}{2} < a \leq  1$ ,令 $\sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}} = t \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,则 ${2a} = \dfrac{1}{1 - {t}^{2}}$ ,令 $g\left( t\right)  = f\left( \sqrt{1 - \dfrac{1}{2a}}\right)  = \ln \left( {t + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1 - {2t}}{2\left( {1 - {t}^{2}}\right) } -  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,求导得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{1}{1 + t} + \dfrac{-2\left( {1 - {t}^{2}}\right)  - \left( {1 - {2t}}\right) \left( {-{2t}}\right) }{2{\left( 1 - {t}^{2}\right) }^{2}} =  \dfrac{{t}^{3} - 2{t}^{2}}{{\left( 1 - {t}^{2}\right) }^{2}} < 0$ ,函数 $y = g\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减, $g{\left( t\right) }_{\min } =  g\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\right)  = \ln \dfrac{2 + \sqrt{2}}{2} - \sqrt{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,由当 $a$ 变化时, $b$ 总是该函数

的下界,得 $b \leq  g{\left( t\right) }_{\min } = \ln \dfrac{2 + \sqrt{2}}{2} - \sqrt{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\dfrac{1}{2} < a \leq  1,b$ 的取值范围是 $b \leq  \ln \dfrac{2 + \sqrt{2}}{2} - \sqrt{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}$ .

15. ( 1 )当 $M\left( {0,0}\right)$ 时， $s\left( x\right)  = {\left( x - 0\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{1}{x} - 0\right) }^{2} = {x}^{2} +  \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} \geq  2\sqrt{{x}^{2} \cdot  \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}} = 2$ ,当且仅当 ${x}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}$ ,即 $x =  \pm  1$ 时取等号,故对于点 $M\left( {0,0}\right)$ ,存在点 $P\left( {1,1}\right)$ 或 $P\left( {-1, - 1}\right)$ ,使得该点是 $M\left( {0,0}\right)$ 在 $f\left( x\right)$ 的“最近点”;

(2)由题设可得 $s\left( x\right)  = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( {{e}}^{x} - 0\right) }^{2} = {\left( x - 1\right) }^{2} +  {{e}}^{2x}$ ,则 ${s}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2\left( {x - 1}\right)  + 2{{e}}^{2x}$ ,因为 $y = 2\left( {x - 1}\right) ,y = 2{{e}}^{2x}$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,所以 ${s}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2\left( {x - 1}\right)  + 2{{e}}^{2x}$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增, 而 ${s}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,故当 $x < 0$ 时, ${s}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, ${s}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ , 得 $s{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = s\left( 0\right)  = 1 + 1 = 2$ ,此时 $P\left( {0,1}\right)$ ,而 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} \Longrightarrow  k = {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ,故 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $P$ 处的切线方程为 $y = x + 1$ . 而 ${k}_{MP} = \dfrac{0 - 1}{1 - 0} =  - 1 \Longrightarrow  {k}_{MP} \cdot  k =  - 1$ ,故直线 ${MP}$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $P$ 处的切线垂直;

(3) 设 ${s}_{1}\left( x\right)  = {\left( x - t + 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( f\left( x\right)  - f\left( t\right)  + g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}$ ,

${s}_{2}\left( x\right)  = {\left( x - t - 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( f\left( x\right)  - f\left( t\right)  - g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2},$ 则 ${s}_{1}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2\left( {x - t + 1}\right)  + 2\left( {f\left( x\right)  - f\left( t\right)  + g\left( t\right) }\right) {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ , ${s}_{2}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2\left( {x - t - 1}\right)  + 2\left( {f\left( x\right)  - f\left( t\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right) {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,由对任意的 $t \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，存在点 $P$ 同时是 ${M}_{1}$ 、 ${M}_{2}$ 在 $f\left( x\right)$ 的“最近点”,设 $P\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 ${x}_{0}$ 既是 ${s}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 的最小值点,也是 ${s}_{2}\left( x\right)$ 的最小值点,因为两函数的定义域均为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,所以 ${x}_{0}$ 也是两函数的极小值点,则存在 ${x}_{0}$ ,使得 ${s}_{1}{}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {s}_{2}{}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即 ${s}_{1}{}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 2\left( {{x}_{0} - t + 1}\right)  + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right)  + g\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   = 0$  \ding{172} , ${s}_{2}{}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 2\left( {{x}_{0} - t - 1}\right)  + 2{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   = 0$  \ding{173} ,  \ding{172} - \ding{173} ，得 $4 + {4g}\left( t\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ，即 $1 + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) g\left( t\right)  = 0$ ， 即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{g\left( t\right) }$ ,又因为函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在定义域 $\mathbf{R}$ 上恒正, 所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{g\left( t\right) } < 0$ 恒成立,接下来证明 ${x}_{0} = t$ ,因为 ${x}_{0}$ 既是 ${s}_{1}\left( x\right)$ 的最小值点,也是 ${s}_{2}\left( x\right)$ 的最小值点,所以 ${s}_{1}\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \leq  s\left( t\right) ,{s}_{2}\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \leq  s\left( t\right)$ ,即 ${\left( {x}_{0} - t + 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right)  + g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} \leq  1 + {\left( g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}\left( 3\right) ,{\left( {x}_{0} - t - 1\right) }^{2} + {\left( f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right)  - g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} \leq  1 +  {\left( g\left( t\right) \right) }^{2}$  \ding{175} , \ding{174}  +  \ding{175} 得 $2{\left( {x}_{0} - t\right) }^{2} + 2 + 2{\left\lbrack  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} +  2{g}^{2}\left( t\right)  \leq  2 + 2{g}^{2}\left( t\right)  \Longrightarrow  {\left( {x}_{0} - t\right) }^{2} + {\left( f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} \leq  0$ ,因为 ${\left( {x}_{0} - t\right) }^{2} \geq  0,{\left( f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right) \right) }^{2} \geq  0$ ,所以 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} - t = 0 \\  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( t\right)  = 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,解得 ${x}_{0} = t$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{g\left( t\right) } < 0$ 恒成立,由 $t$ 的任意性,得 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 严格减.

点睛:本题第三问的关键是结合最值点和极小值的定义得到 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{g\left( t\right) }$ ,再利用最值点定义得到 ${x}_{0} = t$ 即可.

16. (1) ${F}_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( x\right)  = \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right) f\left( 0\right)  + \dfrac{1}{2}f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {\dfrac{1}{2}x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}\cos 0 + \dfrac{1}{2}\cos {2\pi x} - \cos {\pi x}  = \dfrac{1}{2} + {\cos }^{2}{\pi x} - \dfrac{1}{2} - \cos {\pi x}  = {\cos }^{2}{\pi x} - \cos {\pi x}  = {\left( \cos \pi x - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4},x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,$

因为 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,所以 ${\pi x} \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\pi }\right\rbrack  ,\cos {\pi x} \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ,所以当 $\cos {\pi x} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 $x = \dfrac{1}{3}$ 时, ${F}_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( x\right)  = {\left( \cos \pi x - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4}$ 取得最小值,最小值为 $- \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,当 $\cos {\pi x} =  - 1$ ,即 $x = 1$ 时,

${F}_{\dfrac{1}{2}}\left( x\right)  = {\left( \cos \pi x - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{1}{4}$ 取得最大值,最大值为 2,故函数值域为 $\left\lbrack  {-\dfrac{1}{4},2}\right\rbrack$ ;

(2) $f\left( x\right)  = a{x}^{2} + \ln \left( {1 + x}\right)$ ，故 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ，

$f\left( {\dfrac{1}{3}x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{9}a{x}^{2} + \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right) ,$

${F}_{\dfrac{1}{3}}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{3}f\left( 0\right)  + \dfrac{1}{3}f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {\dfrac{1}{3}x}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}a{x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}\ln \left( {1 + x}\right)  - \dfrac{1}{9}a{x}^{2} - \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right)$

$= \dfrac{2}{9}a{x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}\ln \left( {1 + x}\right)  - \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right) ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,  \dfrac{2}{9}a{x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}\ln \left( {1 + x}\right)  - \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right)  \geq  0$ 对任意的 $0 \leq  x \leq  1$ 恒成立,令 $t\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{9}a{x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}\ln \left( {1 + x}\right)  - \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right) ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $t\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,其中 ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{4}{9}{ax} + \dfrac{1}{3 + {3x}} - \dfrac{1}{3 + x},x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,显然 ${t}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,令 $q\left( x\right)  = {t}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,则 ${q}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{4}{9}a - \dfrac{3}{{\left( 3 + 3x\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{\left( 3 + x\right) }^{2}},x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,令 $w\left( x\right)  = {q}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{4}{9}a - \dfrac{3}{{\left( 3 + 3x\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{\left( 3 + x\right) }^{2}},x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,{w}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{18}{{\left( 3 + 3x\right) }^{3}} -  \dfrac{2}{{\left( 3 + x\right) }^{3}} = \dfrac{2}{{\left( \sqrt[3]{3} + \sqrt[3]{3}x\right) }^{3}} - \dfrac{2}{{\left( 3 + x\right) }^{3}}$ ,令 $e\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{{x}^{3}}$ ,则 $e\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{2}{{x}^{3}}$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减，又 $r\left( x\right)  = \sqrt[3]{3} + \sqrt[3]{3}x - \left( {3 + x}\right)  = \sqrt[3]{3}  - 3 + \left( {\sqrt[3]{3} - 1}\right) x$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增, $r\left( 1\right)  = \sqrt[3]{3} - 3 + \sqrt[3]{3} -  1 < 0$ ,故 $\sqrt[3]{3} + \sqrt[3]{3}x < 3 + x$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上恒成立,故 ${w}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{{\left( \sqrt[3]{3} + \sqrt[3]{3}x\right) }^{3}} - \dfrac{2}{{\left( 3 + x\right) }^{3}} > 0$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 恒成立,故 ${q}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,其中 ${q}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{4}{9}a - \dfrac{2}{9}$ ,若 ${q}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  \geq  0$ ,即 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, ${q}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上恒成立, ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{4}{9}{ax} + \dfrac{1}{3 + {3x}} - \dfrac{1}{3 + x}$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增, ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  {t}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,故 $t\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{2}{9}a{x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{3}\ln \left( {1 + x}\right)  - \ln \left( {1 + \dfrac{1}{3}x}\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增, $t\left( x\right)  \geq  t\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,满足要求; 若 $a < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 ${q}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{4}{9}a - \dfrac{2}{9} < 0$ ,故存在适当的 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,使得 $x \in  \left( {0,{x}_{1}}\right)$ 时, ${q}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,故 $q\left( x\right)  = {t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {0,{x}_{1}}\right)$ 上严格减，又 $t\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ，故 $t\left( x\right)  < 0$ 在 $x \in  \left( {0,{x}_{1}}\right)$ 恒成立，不合要求. 综上,实数 $a$ 的取值范围是 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ ; (3) $p \in  \left( {0,1}\right) ,{F}_{p}\left( x\right)  = \left( {1 - p}\right) f\left( 0\right)  + {pf}\left( x\right)  - f\left( {px}\right)$ , $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ,由于 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,故 ${F}_{p}\left( x\right)  = {pf}\left( x\right)  - f\left( {px}\right) ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,{F}_{p}{}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = p{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - p{f}^{\prime }\left( {px}\right)  = p\left\lbrack  {{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( {px}\right) }\right\rbrack  ,$ 因为 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上是严格增函数, $x > {px}$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( {px}\right)  > 0,{F}_{p}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = p\left\lbrack  {{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  - {f}^{\prime }\left( {px}\right) }\right\rbrack   > 0$ ,故 ${F}_{p}\left( x\right)  = {pf}\left( x\right)  - f\left( {px}\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,又 ${F}_{p}\left( 0\right)  = 0$ , 故 ${pf}\left( x\right)  > f\left( {px}\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上恒成立, ${pf}\left( x\right)  > f\left( {px}\right)$ 两边同时除以 ${px}$ 得 $\dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{x} > \dfrac{f\left( {px}\right) }{px}$ ,由于 $p$ 为 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上的任意数,故函数 $y = \dfrac{f\left( x\right) }{x}$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上是严格增函数.

17. (1) 记 ${F}_{0}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\sin x - \sin 0}{x - 0} = \dfrac{\sin x}{x},x \in  \left( {-1,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0,1}\right)$ , 显然是偶函数,当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, ${F}_{0}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\sin x}{x} > 0$ ,故 ${F}_{0}\left( x\right)  >$ 0 在 $x \in  \left( {-1,0}\right)  \cup  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 上恒成立,得 $y = \sin x$ 具有性质 $P\left( 0\right)$ ; (2)由 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - x \Longrightarrow  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  >$ 0，得 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 严格增；当 $x < 0$ 时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，得 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 严格减. 因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P\left( a\right)$ ，所以 ${F}_{a}\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( a\right) }{x - a} > 0$ 对 $x \in  \left( {a - 1,a}\right)  \cup  \left( {a,a + 1}\right)$ 恒成立.

 \ding{172}  若 $a - 1 \geq  0$ ，即 $a \geq  1$ ，则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {a - 1, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,得 ${F}_{a}\left( x\right)  > 0$ 恒成立,此时函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 具有性质 $P\left( a\right)$ ;  \ding{173}  若 $a \leq  0$ ，则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {a - 1,a}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减，

${F}_{a}\left( {a - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{f\left( {a - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  - f\left( a\right) }{-\dfrac{1}{2}} < 0$ ,故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不具有性质 $P\left( a\right)$ ;

 \ding{174}  若 $0 < a < 1$ ，则函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {a,a + 1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增，

“ ${F}_{a}\left( x\right)  > 0$ 对 $x \in  \left( {a - 1,a}\right)  \cup  \left( {a,a + 1}\right)$ 恒成立”等价于

“ $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( a\right)$ 对 $x \in  \left( {a - 1,a}\right)$ 恒成立”. 而 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\lbrack a -$ 1,0]上严格减,在 $\left\lbrack  {0,a}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,故 $f\left( {a - 1}\right)  \leq  f\left( a\right)$ ,

即 ${{e}}^{a - 1} - \left( {a - 1}\right)  \leq  {{e}}^{a} - a \Leftrightarrow  {{e}}^{a} - \dfrac{{{e}}^{a}}{{e}} \geq  1 \Leftrightarrow  {{e}}^{a} \geq  \dfrac{{e}}{{e} - 1} \Leftrightarrow  1 > a \geq  \ln \dfrac{{e}}{{e} - 1}$ . 综上, $a$ 的取值范围是 $\lbrack \ln \dfrac{{e}}{{e} - 1}, + \infty )$ ;

(3)对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ 及 $t \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ，都有

${F}_{a}\left( {a - t}\right)  < {F}_{a}\left( {a + t}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{f\left( {a - t}\right)  - f\left( a\right) }{-t} < \dfrac{f\left( {a + t}\right)  - f\left( a\right) }{t},$ 即对任意 $t \in  \left( {0,1}\right) ,x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x - t}\right)  < f\left( {x + t}\right)  -  f\left( x\right) \left( *\right)$ . 假设存在 $b \in  \mathbf{R}$ 使得 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 不具有性质 $P\left( b\right)$ , 则存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {b - 1,b}\right)  \cup  \left( {b,b + 1}\right)$ 使得 ${F}_{b}\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \leq  0$ .

 \ding{172}  若 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {b - 1,b}\right)$ ，则 $b - {x}_{0} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ， $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \geq  f\left( b\right)$ .

当 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > f\left( b\right)$ 时，则在 $\left( *\right)$ 中取 $t = b - {x}_{0} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ，

对任意 $n \geq  1,n \in  \mathbf{N}$ ,有 $f\left( {b - \left( {n - 1}\right) t}\right)  - f\left( {b - {nt}}\right)  < \cdots  <  f\left( {b - t}\right)  - f\left( {b - {2t}}\right)  < f\left( b\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,于是 $f\left( b\right)  - f\left( {b - {nt}}\right)  <  n\left\lbrack  {f\left( b\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,即 $f\left( {b - {nt}}\right)  - f\left( b\right)  > n\left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( b\right) }\right\rbrack$ . 而当 $n > \max \left\{  {\dfrac{b}{t},\dfrac{-f\left( b\right) }{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( b\right) }}\right\}$ 时, $b - {nt} < 0,f\left( {b - {nt}}\right)  < 0$ , 故有 $f\left( {b - {nt}}\right)  - f\left( b\right)  <  - f\left( b\right)  < n\left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( b\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,矛盾. 当 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = f\left( b\right)$ 时,记 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{{x}_{0} + b}{2} > {x}_{0}$ ,则 ${x}_{1} - {x}_{0} = b - {x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,由 $\left( *\right)$ 知 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < f\left( b\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,得 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  -  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < \dfrac{\left\lbrack  {f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right\rbrack   + \left\lbrack  {f\left( b\right)  - f\left( {x}_{1}\right) }\right\rbrack  }{2} = 0$ ,故 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  >  f\left( {x}_{1}\right) ,{x}_{1} - {x}_{0} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ . 与当 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > f\left( b\right)$ 时,同理可得矛盾.

 \ding{173}  若 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {b,b + 1}\right)$ ，则 ${x}_{0} - b \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ， $f\left( b\right)  \geq  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ，与 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {b - 1,b}\right)$ 时，同理可得矛盾. 综上，假设不成立，即函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 对任意实数 $a$ 均具有性质 $P\left( a\right)$ .

18.(1)由于对任意 ${x}_{1}$ 、 ${x}_{2} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ，当 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ 时，都有 $2{x}_{1} \leq  {x}_{1} + {x}_{2} \leq  2{x}_{2}$ ,即有 $2{x}_{1} \leq  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{2}^{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} \leq  2{x}_{2}$ ,故由控制函数的定义,得 $y = {2x}$ 是函数 $y = {x}^{2}$ 的控制函数;

( 2 )关于 $x$ 的方程 $\dfrac{\ln b - \ln a}{b - a} = \dfrac{1}{x}$ 在区间 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 上有实数解 $\Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{1}{b} < \dfrac{\ln b - \ln a}{b - a} < \dfrac{1}{a} \Leftrightarrow  a\left( {\ln b - \ln a}\right)  < b - a < b\left( {\ln b - \ln a}\right)$

方法一: $\Leftrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \ln b - \ln a < \dfrac{b - a}{a} \\  \ln a - \ln b < \dfrac{a - b}{b} \end{array} \Leftrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} \ln \dfrac{b}{a} - \dfrac{b}{a} + 1 < 0 \\  \ln \dfrac{a}{b} - \dfrac{a}{b} + 1 < 0 \end{array}\right. }\right.$ ,

记 $F\left( x\right)  = \ln x - x + 1$ ,则 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - 1 = \dfrac{1 - x}{x}$ ,当 $x \in  (0$ ,

1) 时 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,F\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增; 当 $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时

${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,F\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减; 而 $0 < \dfrac{a}{b} < 1 < \dfrac{b}{a}$ ,

故 $F\left( \dfrac{a}{b}\right)  < F\left( 1\right)  = 0,F\left( \dfrac{b}{a}\right)  < F\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,于是所要证的结

论成立;

方法二: $\Leftrightarrow  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} a\ln b - b + a - a\ln a < 0 \\  b\ln a - a + b - b\ln b < 0 \end{array}\right.$ .

 \ding{172}  记 $F\left( x\right)  = a\ln x - x + a - a\ln a$ ,则 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{a}{x} - 1 =  \dfrac{a - x}{x}$ ,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ 时 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ ,故 $F\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减， $F\left( b\right)  < F\left( a\right)  = 0$ ；

 \ding{173}  记 $G\left( x\right)  = b\ln x - x + b - b\ln b$ ，则 ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{b}{x} - 1 =  \dfrac{b - x}{x}$ ,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ 时 ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ ,故 $G\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增, $G\left( a\right)  < G\left( b\right)  = 0$ ,综上,所要证的结论成立;

(3)设 $0 < a < b$ ，由 $f\left( x\right)  = x\ln x$ ，得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \ln x + 1$ 在区间 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 上严格增,

 \ding{172}  先证引理:对任意 $0 < a < b$ ，关于 $x$ 的方程 $\dfrac{f\left( b\right)  - f\left( a\right) }{b - a} =  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {a,b}\right)$ 上恒有实数解 $\Leftrightarrow  \ln a + 1 < \dfrac{b\ln b - a\ln a}{b - a} <  \ln b + 1 \Leftrightarrow  \left( {\ln a + 1}\right) \left( {b - a}\right)  < b\ln b - a\ln a < \left( {\ln b + 1}\right) \left( {b - a}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  b\left( {\ln a + 1}\right)  - a\left( {\ln a + 1}\right)  < b\ln b - a\ln a < b\left( {\ln b + 1}\right)  - a\left( {\ln b + 1}\right)  \Leftrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} b - a < b\left( {\ln b - \ln a}\right) \\  a\left( {\ln b - \ln a}\right)  < b - a \end{array} \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{1}{b} < \dfrac{\ln b - \ln a}{b - a} < \dfrac{1}{a}}\right.$ ,由 (2) 知结论成立;

 \ding{173}  (证控制函数的唯一性) 假设 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 存在 “控制函数” $y = g\left( x\right)$ ,由上述引理知,对任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,当 ${x}_{1} <  {x}_{2}$ 时,都存在 $c \in  \left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ ,使得 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( c\right)  \leq  g\left( {x}_{2}\right) \cdots  \left( *\right)$ ,下证: $g\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ . 若存在 ${t}_{1} \in  (0$ , $+ \infty )$ 使得 $g\left( {t}_{1}\right)  > {f}^{\prime }\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ ,考虑到 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \ln x + 1$ 是值域为 $\mathbf{R}$ 的严格增函数,故存在 ${t}_{2} > {t}_{1}$ 使得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {t}_{2}\right)  = g\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ . 由 (*)

知存在 ${c}_{0} \in  \left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)$ 使得 $g\left( {t}_{1}\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( {c}_{0}\right)  \leq  g\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ ,于是有 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {c}_{0}\right)  \geq  g\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的单调性知 ${c}_{0} \geq  {t}_{2}$ ,矛盾. 故对任意 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 都有 $g\left( x\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ; 同理可证,对任意 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 都有 $g\left( x\right)  \geq  {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ,从而 $g\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ ;

 \ding{174}  (证控制函数的存在性)最后验证, $g\left( x\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 是 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的一个“控制函数”,对任意 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,当 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ 时,都存在 $c \in  \left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ ,使得 $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = {f}^{\prime }\left( c\right)$ ,而由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的单调性知 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( c\right)  \leq  {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,即 $g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \leq  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} \leq  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ . 综上,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 存在唯一的控制函数 $y = \ln x + 1$ .

19. (1) 当 $a = {e}$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} + k \cdot  {{e}}^{-x}$ ,可得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} -  k \cdot  {{e}}^{-x},k \leq  0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增, 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上无最值; $k > 0$ 时,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,可得 $x =  \dfrac{1}{2}\ln k$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1}{2}\ln k}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1}{2}\ln k}\right\rbrack$ 上为严格减函数; 当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{2}\ln k, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{2}\ln k, + \infty }\right)$ 上为严格增函数,所以,当 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}\ln k$ 时,函数取得最小值,最小值为 $f\left( {\dfrac{1}{2}\ln k}\right)  =  2\sqrt{k}$ ，无最大值. 综上，当 $k \leq  0$ 时，函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上无最值； 当 $k > 0$ 时，最小值为 $2\sqrt{k}$ ，无最大值；

(2)因为 “ $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数” $\Leftrightarrow$ “对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，都有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ”,即对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,并且 ${a}^{x} + k \cdot  {a}^{-x} = {a}^{-x} + k \cdot  {a}^{x}$ ,即对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R},\left( {k - 1}\right)  \left( {{a}^{x} - {a}^{-x}}\right)  = 0$ ,可得 $k = 1$ ,所以 $k = 1$ 是 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为偶函数的充要条件. 因为 “ $y = f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数” $\Leftrightarrow$ “对于任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ , 都有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  =  - f\left( x\right)$ ”,即对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,并且 $- {a}^{x} - k \cdot  {a}^{-x} = {a}^{-x} + k \cdot  {a}^{x}$ ,即对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ , $\left( {k + 1}\right) \left( {{a}^{x} + {a}^{-x}}\right)  = 0$ ,可得 $k =  - 1$ ,所以 $k =  - 1$ 是 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 为奇函数的充要条件,当 $k \neq   \pm  1$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是非奇非偶函数;

(3) \ding{172}  当 $k < 0$ 时，函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 有对称中心 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2}\log \left( {-k}\right) ,0}\right)$ ， 当 $k < 0$ 时，对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，并且 $f\left( {{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right) }\right.  - x) =  - f\left( x\right)$ . 证明: 当 $k < 0$ 时,令 $f\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,解得 $x =  \dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right)$ 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的零点,由 $f\left( x\right)  = {a}^{x} + k$ . ${a}^{-x}$ ,可得 $f\left( {{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right)  - x}\right)  = {a}^{{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right)  - x} + k \cdot  {a}^{-\left( {{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right)  - x}\right) } =  - k \cdot  {a}^{-x} - {a}^{x} =  - f\left( x\right) ;$

 \ding{173}  答案 1: 当 $k > 0$ 时,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 有对称轴 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{a}k$ . 即当 $k > 0$ 时,对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $- x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,并且 $f\left( {{\log }_{a}k}\right.  - x) = f\left( x\right)$ ,参考证明: 当 $k > 0$ 时,由 $f\left( x\right)  = {a}^{x} + k$ . ${a}^{-x}$ ,可得 $f\left( {{\log }_{a}k - x}\right)  = {a}^{{\log }_{a}k - x} + k \cdot  {a}^{-\left( {{\log }_{a}k - x}\right) } = k \cdot  {a}^{-x} +  {a}^{x} = f\left( x\right)$ ; 答案 2: 当 $k = 1$ 时, $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像关于 $y$ 轴对称, 即对于任意的 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，都有 $f\left( {-x}\right)  = f\left( x\right)$ ；答案 3 : 当 $k < 0$ 时， 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的零点为 $x = \dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right)$ ,即 $f\left( {\dfrac{1}{2}{\log }_{a}\left( {-k}\right) }\right)  = 0$ .

点睛:解决函数极值、最值综合问题的策略:(1)求极值、最值时,要求步骤规范,含参数时,要讨论参数的大小; (2) 求函数最值时,不可想当然地认为极值点就是最值点,要通过比较才能下结论; (3) 函数在给定闭区间上存在极值,一般要将极值与端点值进行比较才能确定最值.

20. ( 1 )取 $s = t = \dfrac{\pi }{2}$ ，则 $\sin \left( {\dfrac{\pi }{2} + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)  = 0$ ， $\sin \dfrac{\pi }{2} + \sin \dfrac{\pi }{2} =$ 2,因为 $0 < 2$ ,故函数 $y = \sin x$ 不是 “ $\sum$ 增函数”;

(2)因为函数 $y = {2}^{x - 1} - x - a$ 是 “ $\sum$ 增函数”，故任意的 $s,t \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,有 ${2}^{s + t - 1} - \left( {s + t}\right)  - a > {2}^{s - 1} - s - a + {2}^{t - 1} -  t - a$ 恒成立，即 ${2}^{s + t - 1} - {2}^{s - 1} - {2}^{t - 1} >  - a$ 恒成立，所以 $\dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{2}^{s} - 1}\right) \left( {{2}^{t} - 1}\right)  > \dfrac{1}{2} - a$ 恒成立. 又 $s,t \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ , 故 ${2}^{s},{2}^{t} \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,则 $\dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{2}^{s} - 1}\right) \left( {{2}^{t} - 1}\right)  \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ , 则 $\dfrac{1}{2} - a \leq  0$ ,即 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ;

(3)记 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}\left\lbrack  {\ln \left( {x + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{x + 1}}\right\rbrack$ ，根据题意，得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  {{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\left\lbrack  {\ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0} + 1}}\right\rbrack   = 1$ 可得方程的一个解 ${x}_{0} = 0$ ,

当 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-1,0}\right)$ 时,得

${{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\left\lbrack  {\ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0} + 1}}\right\rbrack   = 1$ 且 ${{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  = {x}_{0},$

化简得: ${{e}}^{{x}_{0}} + {x}_{0}^{2} - 1 = 0,\ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  - \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{1 - {x}_{0}^{2}} = 0$ ,

可以证明函数 $y = {{e}}^{{x}_{0}} + {x}_{0}^{2} - 1$ 在 $\left( {-1,{x}_{1}}\right)$ 上是严格减函数, 在 $\left( {{x}_{1},0}\right)$ 上是严格增函数,且 ${{e}}^{-{0.6}} + {\left( -{0.6}\right) }^{2} - 1 < 0$ , 故 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-1, - {0.6}}\right)$ ;

同理可以证明函数 $y = \ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  - \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{1 - {x}_{0}^{2}}$ 在 $\left( {-1,{x}_{2}}\right)$ 上是严格减函数,在 $\left( {{x}_{2},0}\right)$ 上是严格增函数,

且 $\ln \left( {-{0.6} + 1}\right)  - \dfrac{-{0.6}}{1 - {\left( -{0.6}\right) }^{2}} > 0$ ,

$\ln \left( {-{0.4} + 1}\right)  - \dfrac{-{0.4}}{1 - {\left( -{0.4}\right) }^{2}} < 0$ 故 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-{0.6}, - {0.4}}\right)$ , 综上可知 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-1,0}\right)$ 不符合题意.

当 ${x}_{0} > 0$ 时, ${\left( {g}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) \right) }^{\prime } = {\left( {{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\right) }^{\prime }\left\lbrack  {\ln \left( {{x}_{0} + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0} + 1}}\right\rbrack   +  {{e}}^{{x}_{0}}\left\lbrack  {{\left( \ln \left( {x}_{0} + 1\right) \right) }^{\prime } + {\left( \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0} + 1}\right) }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,$

${\left( {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) \right) }^{\prime } = {{e}}^{x}\left\lbrack  {\ln \left( {x + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{x + 1} - \dfrac{1}{{\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}}}\right\rbrack  ,$

令 $h\left( x\right)  = \ln \left( {x + 1}\right)  + \dfrac{2}{x + 1} - \dfrac{1}{{\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}}$ ,

设 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x + 1} - \dfrac{2}{{\left( x + 1\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{2}{{\left( x + 1\right) }^{3}} = \dfrac{{x}^{2} + 1}{{\left( x + 1\right) }^{3}} > 0$ ,故 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,又因为 $h\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ,故 $h\left( x\right)  > 0$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立,故 ${\left( {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right) \right) }^{\prime } > 0$ ,故 $y = {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,所以 ${x}_{0} > 0$ 不符合题意. 设 $w\left( s\right)  = g\left( {s + t}\right)  - g\left( s\right)  - g\left( t\right)$ ,其中 $s > 0,t > 0.{w}^{\prime }\left( s\right)  =  {g}^{\prime }\left( {s + t}\right)  - {g}^{\prime }\left( s\right)$ ,由 $y = {g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数以及 $t > 0$ ,得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( {s + t}\right)  > {g}^{\prime }\left( s\right)$ ,即 ${w}^{\prime }\left( s\right)  = {g}^{\prime }\left( {s + t}\right)  -  {g}^{\prime }\left( s\right)  > 0$ ,所以 $w\left( s\right)  = g\left( {s + t}\right)  - g\left( s\right)  - g\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,因为 $s > 0$ ,则 $w\left( s\right)  > w\left( 0\right)  =  - g\left( 0\right)  = 0$ ,故 $g\left( {s + t}\right)  > g\left( s\right)  + g\left( t\right)$ ,得证.

\section*{考点 5 求极值、最值及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. -7

【解析】因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} + {2m}{x}^{2} - {nx} + m$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} + {4mx} - n$ ,依题意可得 $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} f\left( 1\right)  = 0 \\  {f}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  }\right.  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1 + {2m} - n + m = 0 \\  3 + {4m} - n = 0 \end{array}\right.$ . 解得 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} m =  - 2 \\  n =  - 5 \end{array}\right.$ ,经检验符合题意,

所以 $m + n =  - 7$ . 故答案为:-7.

2. 1.2

【解析】设底面短边长为 $x{m}$ ,则长边长为 $\left( {x + {0.5}}\right) {m}$ ,高为 $\dfrac{{14.8} - {4x} - 4\left( {x + {0.5}}\right) }{4} = \left( {{3.2} - {2x}}\right) {m}$ ,则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x > 0 \\  {3.2} - {2x} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ , 解得 $0 < x < 1$ . 6,则容器的容积 $V\left( x\right)  = x\left( {x + {0.5}}\right) ({3.2} -  {2x}) =  - 2{x}^{3} + {2.2}{x}^{2} + {1.6x},0 < x < 1.6$ ,则 ${V}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 6{x}^{2} + {4.4x} + {1.6} =  - \left( {{6x} + {1.6}}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right)$ ,令 ${V}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,得 $0 < x < 1$ ,令 ${V}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,得 $1 < x < 1.6$ ,所以函数 $V\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,在 $\left( {1,{1.6}}\right)$ 上严格减,所以当 $x = 1$ 时, $V\left( x\right)$ 取得最大值, 所以要使该容器的容积最大, 则容器的高为 3.2 - $2 = {1.2}{m}$ . 故答案为: 1.2 .

3. 3

【解析】当 $0 < a < 6$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = \sqrt{x}\left( {x - a}\right) ,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{3x} - a}{2\sqrt{x}}$ , 当 $0 < x < \dfrac{1}{3}a$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,f\left( x\right)$ 严格减,当 $\dfrac{1}{3}a < x < 2$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,故 $x = \dfrac{1}{3}a$ 时, $f\left( x\right)$ 取得最小值 $f\left( \dfrac{a}{3}\right)  =  - \dfrac{2a}{3} \times  \sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} =  - 2$ ,解得, $a = 3$ . 故答案为: 3 .

4. $\left( {-\infty ,2}\right)$

【解析】记 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - x,x \in  \mathbf{R},g\left( x\right)  = x - \ln x,x > 0$ ,则 $a < {\left( \left| {{e}}^{{x}_{1}} - {x}_{1}\right|  + \left| {x}_{2} - \ln {x}_{2}\right| \right) }_{\min }$ ,因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  x = 0$ ,当 $x < 0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  >$ 0,故 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = f\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ; 因为 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{1}{x}$ ,令 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  x = 1$ ,当 $0 < x < 1$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,当 $x > 1$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 $g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = g\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,所以 $a < 2$ .

5. $\arcsin \dfrac{2}{5}$

【解析】在 $\bigtriangleup {OAD}$ 中, ${AD} = \dfrac{OA}{\tan \theta } =  \dfrac{4\cos \theta }{\sin \theta }$ ,又因为 $\angle {AOC} = \angle {ADO} = \theta$ , 所以弧 $\overset{\frown}{ACB}$ 长为 $l = 4 \cdot  {2\theta } = {8\theta }$ ,所以 $W = 2 \cdot  {AD} \cdot  {0.4} + l \cdot  {2.5} = 2 \cdot  \dfrac{4\cos \theta }{\sin \theta } \cdot  {0.4} + {8\theta } \cdot  {2.5} =$ 3. $2 \times  \dfrac{\cos \theta }{\sin \theta } + {20\theta }$ ,设 $f\left( \theta \right)  = {3.2} \times  \dfrac{\cos \theta }{\sin \theta } + {20\theta }$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( \theta \right)  =  {20} - \dfrac{3.2}{{\sin }^{2}\theta }$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( \theta \right)  = 0$ 得 $\sin \theta  = \dfrac{2}{5}$ ,即 $\theta  = \arcsin \dfrac{2}{5},\theta  =  \arcsin \dfrac{2}{5}$ 时,函数 $f\left( \theta \right)$ 取得最小值,此时桥面修建总费用最低. 6. $\dfrac{\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}{2}$

\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/146_628_1683_240_160_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

【解析】设半圆步道直径为 $x$ 百米, 连接 ${AE},{BE}$ ,显然 $\angle {AEB} = {90}^{ \circ  }$ ,由点 $O$ 为线段 ${AB},{CD}$ 的中点,得两个半圆步道及直道 ${CE},{DF}$ 都关于过点 $O$ 垂直于 ${AB}$ 的直线对称,则 ${AC} = \dfrac{1}{2} - x,{BC} = \dfrac{1}{2} + x$ ,又 ${CE} \bot  {AB}$ ,则 $\operatorname{Rt} \bigtriangleup  {ACE} \backsim  \operatorname{Rt} \bigtriangleup  {ECB}$ ,有 $C{E}^{2} = {AC} \cdot  {BC}$ ,即有 ${DF} = {CE} = \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{4} - {x}^{2}},$ 因此步道长 $f\left( x\right)  = 2\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{4} - {x}^{2}} + {\pi x} =  \sqrt{1 - 4{x}^{2}} + {\pi x},0 < x < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,求导得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{4x}{\sqrt{1 - 4{x}^{2}}} + \pi$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,得 $x = \dfrac{\pi }{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}$ ,当 $0 < x < \dfrac{\pi }{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ , 函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,当 $\dfrac{\pi }{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}} < x < \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 严格减,因此当 $x = \dfrac{\pi }{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}$ 时, $f{\left( x\right) }_{\max } =  \sqrt{1 - 4{\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}\right) }^{2}} + \dfrac{{\pi }^{2}}{2\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}} = \dfrac{\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}{2}$ ,所以步道的最大长度为 $\dfrac{\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}{2}$ 百米. 故答案为: $\dfrac{\sqrt{{\pi }^{2} + 4}}{2}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/146_1342_234_237_144_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

7. 4.90

【解析】若球的半径为 $r$ ,则 $\dfrac{4}{3}\pi {r}^{3} = {36\pi }$ ,可得 $r = 3$ ,又 $2\sqrt{3} \leq  l \leq  3\sqrt{3}$ ,对于正四棱锥,设底面边长为 $a$ ,高为 $h$ ,则 ${h}^{2} = {l}^{2} -  {\left( \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}a\right) }^{2} = {l}^{2} - \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{2}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{{a}^{2}}{2} = {l}^{2} - {h}^{2}$ ,即 ${a}^{2} = 2\left( {{l}^{2} - {h}^{2}}\right)$ ,又 ${\left( h - r\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{{a}^{2}}{2} = {r}^{2}$ ,则 $\dfrac{{a}^{2}}{2} = {6h} - {h}^{2}$ ,故 ${6h} = {l}^{2}$ ,即 $h = \dfrac{{l}^{2}}{6}$ ,纪念碑体积 $V = \dfrac{1}{3}{a}^{2}h = \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \left( {{l}^{2} - \dfrac{{l}^{4}}{36}}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{{l}^{2}}{6} = \dfrac{1}{9} \times  \left( {{l}^{4} - \dfrac{{l}^{6}}{36}}\right)$ ,令 $t = {l}^{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {{12},{27}}\right\rbrack$ ,对于 $y = {t}^{2} - \dfrac{{t}^{3}}{36}$ ,则 ${y}^{\prime } = {2t} -  \dfrac{{t}^{2}}{12} = \dfrac{{24t} - {t}^{2}}{12}$ ,当 ${12} \leq  t < {24}$ 时 ${y}^{\prime } > 0$ ,即 $y = {t}^{2} - \dfrac{{t}^{3}}{36}$ 在 $\lbrack {12}$ , 24) 上严格增,当 ${24} < t \leq  {27}$ 时 ${y}^{\prime } < 0$ ,即 $y = {t}^{2} - \dfrac{{t}^{3}}{36}$ 在 (24, 27] 上严格减，所以 ${y}_{\max } = {24}^{2} - \dfrac{{24}^{3}}{36} = {192}$ ，故 ${V}_{\max } = \dfrac{1}{9} \times  {192}  = \dfrac{64}{3}$ ,此时 $l = 2\sqrt{6} \approx  {4.90}$ 米. 故答案为: 4.90 .

\section*{二、解答题}

8.(1)由 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}$ 知，代入 ${\left( f\left( x\right) \right) }^{2} = f\left( x\right)  + 2$ 得 ${\left( {{e}}^{x}\right) }^{2} = {{e}}^{x} +$ 2,即 $\left( {{{e}}^{x} - 2}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} + 1}\right)  = 0$ ,解得 ${{e}}^{x} = 2$ ,即 $x = \ln 2$ ;

(2)不等式 $x + b \leq  f\left( x\right)$ 即 $x + b \leq  {{e}}^{x}$ ，原不等式可化为 $b \leq  {{e}}^{x} - x$ 对于一切 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ 都成立,令 $g\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - x$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {{e}}^{x} - 1$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,当 $x < 0$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,所以函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 上严格减,在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,故当 $x = 0$ 时, $g{\left( x\right) }_{\min } = g\left( 0\right)  = 1$ ,所以 $b \leq  1$ .

9. ( 1 )因为 $f\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,故 $1 - m - 2 + 0 = 0$ ，故 $m =  - 1$ ，故 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - x - \ln x$ ,故 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  {x}^{2} - 1$ 即为 $x + \ln x \geq  1$ ,设 $s\left( x\right)  = x + \ln x,x > 0$ ,则 ${s}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \dfrac{1}{x} > 0$ ,故 $s\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上为严格增函数,而 $x + \ln x \geq  1$ 即为 $s\left( x\right)  \geq  s\left( 1\right)$ ,故 $x \geq  1$ ,故原不等式的解为 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ ;

(2) $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 有极大值即为有极大值点. ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} -  \left( {m + 2}\right)  + \dfrac{m}{x} = \dfrac{2{x}^{2} - \left( {m + 2}\right) x + m}{x} = \dfrac{\left( {{2x} - m}\right) \left( {x - 1}\right) }{x}$ ,若 $m \leq$ 0,则 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 $x = 1$ 为 $f\left( x\right)$ 的极小值点,无极大值点,故舍；若 $0 < \dfrac{m}{2} < 1$ 即 $0 < m < 2$ ,则 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{m}{2},1}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{m}{2}}\right)  \cup  (1$ , $+ \infty )$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 $x = \dfrac{m}{2}$ 为 $f\left( x\right)$ 的极大值点,符合题设要求; 若 $m = 2$ ,则 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0,f\left( x\right)$ 无极值点,舍; 若 $\dfrac{m}{2} > 1$ 即 $m > 2$ ,则 $x \in  \left( {1,\dfrac{m}{2}}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ , $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)  \cup  \left( {\dfrac{m}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 $x = 1$ 为 $f\left( x\right)$ 的极大值点,符合题设要求; 综上, $m > 0$ 且 $m \neq  2$ .

10. ( 1 )当倾斜角 $\alpha  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ 时，冰箱倾斜后实际高度(即冰箱最高点到地面的距离 $h = {0.8}\sin \dfrac{\pi }{4} + {2.4}\cos \dfrac{\pi }{4} = \dfrac{8\sqrt{2}}{5} < {2.3}$ , 故冰箱能够按要求运送入客户家中；

(2)延长 ${EF}$ 与直角走廊的边相交于 $M$ 、 $N$ ，则 ${MN} = {OM} +  {ON} = \dfrac{1.8}{\sin \beta } + \dfrac{1.8}{\cos \beta },{EM} = \dfrac{1.2}{\tan \beta },{FN} = {1.2}\tan \beta$ ,又 ${EF} = {MN} -  {ME} - {NF}$ ,设 ${EF} = f\left( \beta \right) ,\beta  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,则 $f\left( \beta \right)  = \dfrac{1.8}{\sin \beta } + \dfrac{1.8}{\cos \beta } -$ 1. $2\left( {\tan \beta  + \dfrac{1}{\tan \beta }}\right)  = \dfrac{{1.8}\left( {\sin \beta  + \cos \beta }\right)  - {1.2}}{\sin \beta \cos \beta },\beta  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right\rbrack$ .

${f}^{\prime }\left( \beta \right)  =$

$\dfrac{{1.8}\left( {\cos \beta  - \sin \beta }\right) \left( {\sin \beta \cos \beta }\right)  - \left( {{1.8}\left( {\sin \beta  + \cos \beta }\right)  - {1.2}}\right) \left( {{\cos }^{2}\beta  - {\sin }^{2}\beta }\right) }{{\sin }^{2}\beta  \cdot  {\cos }^{2}\beta }  = \dfrac{-{1.8}\left( {{\cos }^{3}\beta  - {\sin }^{3}\beta }\right)  + {1.2}\left( {{\cos }^{2}\beta  - {\sin }^{2}\beta }\right) }{{\sin }^{2}\beta  \cdot  {\cos }^{2}\beta }  = \dfrac{{1.8}\left( {\sin \beta  - \cos \beta }\right) \left( {\cos \beta  - 1}\right) \left( {\sin \beta  - 1}\right) }{{\sin }^{2}\beta  \cdot  {\cos }^{2}\beta }$

求得驻点 $\beta  = \dfrac{\pi }{4}$ ,作表格得

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$\beta$ & $\left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{4}}\right)$ & $\dfrac{\pi }{4}$ & $\left( {\dfrac{\pi }{4},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ \\
\cline{1-4}
${f}^{\prime }\left( \beta \right)$ & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-4}
$f\left( \beta \right)$ & ↘ & 极小值 & ↗ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以 $f\left( \beta \right)$ 最小值 $f\left( \dfrac{\pi }{4}\right)  = \dfrac{{18}\sqrt{2} - {12}}{5} \approx  {2.69}$ . 由实际意义需向下取整，此情况下能顺利通过过道的冰箱高度的最大值为 ${2.6}{m}$ .

11. ( 1 ) $y = \sin x$ 的“ $W$ 函数”为 $y = 1$ ，“ $W$ 点”为 ${2k\pi } + \dfrac{\pi }{2}  \left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ;

(2)充分性:当 $M \cap  N \neq  \varnothing$ 时，存在 ${x}_{0} \in  M \cap  N$ ，则 ${x}_{0} \in  M$ 、 ${x}_{0} \in  N$ ,故 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = h\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,又 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”, $y = h\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为“ $W$ 函数”,则对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $h\left( x\right)  \geq  g\left( x\right)  \geq  f\left( x\right)$ ,故函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”; 必需性: 当函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 为 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”时,存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = h\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,又 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”, $y = h\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的 “ $W$ 函数”,则对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $h\left( x\right)  \geq  g\left( x\right)  \geq  f\left( x\right)$ ,从而 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = h\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,故 ${x}_{0} \in  M,{x}_{0} \in  N$ ,即 ${x}_{0} \in  M \cap  N$ , 从而 $M \cap  N \neq  \varnothing$ ;

(3)令 $F\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)  - f\left( x\right)  = {kx} + m - \dfrac{x}{{e}x}$ ,则 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = k -  \dfrac{1 - x}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,因为 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”,且“ $W$ 点”为 ${x}_{0}$ ,所以 $k = \dfrac{1 - {x}_{0}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}},m = \dfrac{{x}_{0}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}} - k{x}_{0} = \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}$ ,得 $F\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1 - {x}_{0}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}}x +  \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}} - \dfrac{x}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,当 ${x}_{0} > 1$ 时,设 ${x}_{1} = \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{x}_{0} - 1}$ ,则 $F\left( {x}_{1}\right)  =  - \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{1}}} < 0$ , 与 $F\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 恒成立矛盾,所以 $0 < {x}_{0} \leq  1$ ,令 $G\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{x}^{2}}{{{e}}^{x}}(x >  0)$ ,则 ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{2x} - {x}^{2}}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,当 $0 < x \leq  1$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,从而 $y =  G\left( x\right)$ 在 $(0,1\rbrack$ 上严格增,于是 $G\left( x\right)  \leq  G\left( 1\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,即 $m = \dfrac{{x}_{0}^{2}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{0}}} \leq  \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,当 $m = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ 时, ${x}_{0} = 1,k = 0,{F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{x - 1}{{{e}}^{x}},F\left( x\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{x}{{{e}}^{x}}$

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
$x$ & $\left( {-\infty ,1}\right)$ & 1 & $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ \\
\cline{1-4}
${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & - & 0 & + \\
\cline{1-4}
$F\left( x\right)$ & ↘ & 有极小值 $F\left( 1\right)  = 0$ & ↗ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以当 $m = \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ 时, $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $W$ 函数”, $m$ 的最大值为 $\dfrac{1}{{e}}$ .

12. (1) ${\Delta y} = \left| \dfrac{\left( {2{x}_{1} + 1}\right)  - \left( {2{x}_{2} + 1}\right) }{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right|  = \left| \dfrac{2\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right) }{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}}\right|  =  2 \geq  2$ . 所以 $f\left( x\right)$ 和 $g\left( x\right)$ 是满足 $Q\left( 2\right)$ 性质;

(2)由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 2\left( {x - 1}\right)$ 可知，驻点 $m = 1$ ，又 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  \dfrac{a - 1 - {ax}}{{{e}}^{x}}$ ,当 $a = 0$ 时, $g\left( x\right)$ 不存在驻点; 当 $a \neq  0$ 时, $g\left( x\right)$ 的驻点 $n = \dfrac{a - 1}{a}$ ,由题意可知 $\left| {\dfrac{a - 1}{a} - 1}\right|  \leq  1$ ,解得 $a \in  ( - \infty$ , -1] $\cup  \lbrack 1, + \infty )$ ;

(3)方法 1: $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ 的充要条件是 $k = 1$ . 首先证明必要性: 当 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ 时,由题意可知 $f\left( x\right)$ 不是常函数,所以 ${\Delta y} = \left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  = 1$ ,因为 $f\left( x\right)$ 和 $g\left( x\right)$ 满足 $Q\left( k\right)$ 性质,所以 ${\Delta y} \geq  k$ ,所以 $k \leq  1$ ,又 $k$ 是正整数,故 $k = 1$ . 其次证明充分性: 由题意可知 $- 1 \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  1,g\left( {-1}\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1,g\left( 1\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,且 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ ,

 \ding{172}  当 $x \neq  {4k} \pm  1\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时，可知 $g\left( x\right)  \neq  g\left( {-1}\right)$ ，否则，若存在 ${x}_{0} \neq  {4k} \pm  1\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ，有 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1$ ，因为 $- 1 <  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < 1$ ,所以 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - g\left( 1\right) }\right|  = \dfrac{\left| f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - 1\right| }{2} < 1$ 与已知矛盾. 同理, $g\left( x\right)  \neq  g\left( 1\right)$ ,故 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {-1}\right) }{g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  + 1}{g\left( x\right)  + 1}\right|  =  \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  + 1}{\left| g\left( x\right)  + 1\right| } \geq  1$ ,所以 $\left| {g\left( x\right)  + 1}\right|  \leq  f\left( x\right)  + 1$ ,即 $- f\left( x\right)  - 2 \leq  g\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( x\right)$ . 同理, $\left| \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{g\left( x\right)  - g\left( 1\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{f\left( x\right)  - 1}{g\left( x\right)  - 1}\right|  = \dfrac{1 - f\left( x\right) }{\left| g\left( x\right)  - 1\right| } \geq  1$ , 得 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  g\left( x\right)  \leq  2 - f\left( x\right)$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ ;

 \ding{173}  当 $x = {4k} - 1\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时， $f\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  =  - 1$ ，任意 $t \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ， 有 $- 1 < f\left( t\right)  < 1$ ,又由 \ding{172} 可知, $f\left( t\right)  = g\left( t\right)$ . 若存在 $k \in  \mathbf{Z},{t}_{0} \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ 有 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  = g\left( {t}_{0}\right)$ ,则 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ,所以 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  \neq  g\left( {-1}\right)$ . 由已知 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  - f\left( {-1}\right) }{g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ ,其中 $f\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1$ ，于是有 $0 \geq  1$ ，矛盾，所以 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  \neq  g\left( t\right)$ . 所以 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  - f\left( t\right) }{g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{-1 - f\left( t\right) }{g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right|  =  \dfrac{1 + f\left( t\right) }{\left| g\left( 4k - 1\right)  - f\left( t\right) \right| } \geq  1$ ,得 $- 1 \leq  g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  \leq  1 + {2f}\left( t\right)$ ,因为 $- 1 < f\left( t\right)  < 1$ ,所以 $- 1 < 1 + {2f}\left( t\right)  < 3$ ,从而 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  =$ -1,即 $g\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)  = f\left( {{4k} - 1}\right)$ ;

 \ding{174}  当 $x = {4k} + 1\left( {k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ 时， $f\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  = 1$ ，任意 $t \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ， 有 $- 1 < f\left( t\right)  < 1$ ,同理可得 $g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  \neq  g\left( t\right)$ ,所以

$\left| \dfrac{f\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  - f\left( t\right) }{g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{1 - f\left( t\right) }{g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  - g\left( t\right) }\right|  =$

$\dfrac{1 - f\left( t\right) }{\left| g\left( 4k + 1\right)  - f\left( t\right) \right| } \geq  1$ ,得 $- 1 + {2f}\left( t\right)  \leq  g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  \leq  1$ ,因为 $- 1 < f\left( t\right)  < 1$ ,所以 $- 3 < 1 + {2f}\left( t\right)  < 1$ ,从而 $g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  =$ -1,即 $g\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)  = f\left( {{4k} + 1}\right)$ . 综上, $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ ;

方法 2: (反证法),由题意可知 $- 1 \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  1,g\left( {-1}\right)  =  f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1,g\left( 1\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ ,且 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - g\left( {x}_{2}\right) }\right|  \geq  1$ .

 \ding{172}  任意 $x \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ 时，可知 $g\left( x\right)  \neq  g\left( {-1}\right)$ . 否则，若存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ,使 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1$ ,因为 $- 1 < f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < 1$ , 所以 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - g\left( 1\right) }\right|  = \dfrac{\left| f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - 1\right| }{2} < 1$ 与已知矛盾. 同理, $g\left( x\right)  \neq  g\left( 1\right) ;$

 \ding{173}  先证任意 $x \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ 时，有 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ . 反证，若存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-1,1}\right)$ ,使 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \neq  g\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ . 若 $1 > f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  > g\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ 时,则 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( 1\right) }{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - g\left( 1\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - 1}{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - 1}\right|  < 1$ 与已知矛盾. 若 $- 1 <  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  < g\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ 时,则 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {-1}\right) }{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - g\left( {-1}\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + 1}{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  + 1}\right|  < 1$ 与已知矛盾. 由于 $g\left( {-1}\right)  = f\left( {-1}\right)  =  - 1,g\left( 1\right)  = f\left( 1\right)  = 1$ , 可得,任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 时,有 $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ ;

 \ding{174}  下证任意 $x \in  \left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时,有 $f\left( x\right)  =  g\left( x\right)$ . 反证,若存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {-\infty , - 1}\right)  \cup  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,使 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \neq  g\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ . 由于 $- 1 \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  1$ ,可得存在 $m \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 满足 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = f\left( m\right)  = g\left( m\right)$ . 则 $\left| \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( m\right) }{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - g\left( m\right) }\right|  = \left| \dfrac{0}{g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right) }\right|  < 1$ 与已知矛盾. 综上, $f\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ .

点睛: 本题主要考查了函数新定义的应用, (1) (2) 问关键是理解函数的 $P\left( k\right)$ 性质和 $Q\left( k\right)$ 性质,即可解出,第 (3) 问利用必要性先得出 $k$ 的值,然后再将进行充分性证明,可以分类讨论,也可以利用反证法证出.

13. ( 1 )由 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - 1 = 0$ ，得 ${x}^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {x}_{1} =  - 1,{x}_{2} = 1$ ，又 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x}$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} = 0$ ,得到 $x = 0$ ,又当 $x < 0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} < 0$ ,当 $x > 0$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} > 0$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} -$ 1 只有一个极值点,且极值点为 ${x}_{0} = 0$ ,此时 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}$ ,所以函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上的极值点不偏移;

(2) $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - m{x}^{2} - x + m = {x}^{2}\left( {x - m}\right)  - \left( {x - m}\right)  = (x -  m)\left( {x - 1}\right) \left( {x + 1}\right)$ ,令 $f\left( x\right)  = 0$ ,因为 $D = \left( {0, + \infty }\right) ,m > 0$ 且 $m \neq  1$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} = 1,{x}_{2} = m$ 或 ${x}_{1} = m,{x}_{2} = 1$ ,则 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} = m +  1 > 0,{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - {2mx} - 1$ ,因为 $\Delta  = 4{m}^{2} + {12} > 0$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - {2mx} - 1 = 0$ 有两根,不妨设为 ${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$ ,且 ${t}_{1} < {t}_{2}$ , 因为 ${t}_{1} + {t}_{2} = \dfrac{2m}{3} > 0,{t}_{1}{t}_{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{3} < 0$ ,所以 ${t}_{1} < 0 < {t}_{2}$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {0,{t}_{2}}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,x \in  \left( {{t}_{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上只有一个极值点 ${x}_{0}$ ,且 ${x}_{0} = {t}_{2},{f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}\right)  =  f\left( \dfrac{m + 1}{2}\right)  = 3{\left( \dfrac{m + 1}{2}\right) }^{2} - {2m}\left( \dfrac{m + 1}{2}\right)  - 1 =  - \dfrac{1}{4}{m}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}m -  \dfrac{1}{4} =  - \dfrac{1}{4}{\left( m - 1\right) }^{2} < 0$ ,由图知 $\dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} <$

${t}_{2} = {x}_{0}$ ,故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上的极值点右偏移;

(3)由 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x - {ax}$ ，得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - a$ ，令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} -  a = 0$ ,因为 $0 < a < \dfrac{1}{{e}}$ ,所以 $x = \dfrac{1}{a} > {e}$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{a}}\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{a}, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,故 $x = \dfrac{1}{a}$ 是 $f\left( x\right)$ 的唯一极值点,记作 ${x}_{0}$ ,且 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{a}}\right)$ 上严格增,在区间 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{a}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减,又 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = f\left( \dfrac{1}{a}\right)  =  \ln \dfrac{1}{a} - 1 > 0$ ,当 $x \rightarrow  0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \rightarrow   - \infty$ ,当 $x \rightarrow   + \infty$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  \rightarrow  - \infty ,f\left( {e}\right)  = \ln {e} - a{e} > 1 - \dfrac{1}{{e}} \cdot  {e} > 0$ ,故 $f\left( x\right)  = 0$ 有两个零点,不妨设为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ ,且 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,则 $0 < {x}_{1} < {e} < {x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{a} < {x}_{2}$ , $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 0$ ,令 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - x}\right)  = \ln x - {ax} -  \ln \left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - x}\right)  + a\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - x}\right) \left( {0 < x < \dfrac{2}{a}}\right)$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - a +  \dfrac{1}{\dfrac{2}{a} - x} - a = \dfrac{1}{x} - {2a} + \dfrac{a}{2 - {ax}} = \dfrac{2 - {ax} - {2a}\left( {{2x} - a{x}^{2}}\right)  + {ax}}{x\left( {2 - {ax}}\right) } =$

$\dfrac{2\left( {1 - {2ax} + {a}^{2}{x}^{2}}\right) }{x\left( {2 - {ax}}\right) } = \dfrac{2{\left( 1 - ax\right) }^{2}}{x\left( {2 - {ax}}\right) } > 0$ ,得 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  -  f\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - x}\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\dfrac{2}{a}}\right)$ 上严格增,故 $g\left( \dfrac{1}{a}\right)  > g\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \Longrightarrow  0 >  f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - {x}_{1}}\right)  = f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - f\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - {x}_{1}}\right)  \Longrightarrow  f\left( {\dfrac{2}{a} - {x}_{1}}\right)  >  f\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,又因为 $\dfrac{2}{a} - {x}_{1} > \dfrac{2}{a} - \dfrac{1}{a} = \dfrac{1}{a},{x}_{2} > \dfrac{1}{a},y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{a}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减，所以 $\dfrac{2}{a} - {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{1}{a} < \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}$ ，即 ${x}_{0} < \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}$ ,因此当 $0 < a < {{e}}^{-1}$ 时,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $D$ 上的极值点左偏移.

点睛:本题第三问考查极值点偏移问题，解决极值点偏移的主要方法有: \ding{172}  构造对称函数； \ding{173}  比值换元； \ding{174}  对数平均不等式. 14. (1) $y = 2\left| x\right|$ 是含谷函数，谷点为 0，记 $f\left( x\right)  = x +  \cos x$ ,因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 - \sin x \geq  0$ ,所以 $y = x + \cos x$ 在 $\mathbf{R}$ 上严格增,因此不是含谷函数;

(2)设 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} - {2x} - m\ln \left( {x - 1}\right)$ ，则 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的定义域为 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ ,求导可得 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} - 2 - \dfrac{m}{x - 1}$ ,令 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ 得到函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的驻点 $x = 1 + \sqrt{\dfrac{m}{2}}\left( {x = 1 - \sqrt{\dfrac{m}{2}}}\right.$ 不在定义域内,舍),当 $1 < x < 1 + \sqrt{\dfrac{m}{2}}$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,函数 $y =  g\left( x\right)$ 严格减; 当 $x > 1 + \sqrt{\dfrac{m}{2}}$ 时, ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 严格增; 又 $\left\lbrack  {2,4}\right\rbrack$ 是含谷函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 的一个含谷区间,则 $2 <  1 + \sqrt{\dfrac{m}{2}} < 4$ ,得 $2 < m < {18}$ . 因此, $m$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {2,{18}}\right)$ ;

(3) ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 4\left( {1 - x}\right) \left( {{x}^{2} + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3p}{4}}\right) x + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3p}{4} - \dfrac{q}{2}}\right) }\right)$ , 若 ${x}^{2} + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3p}{4}}\right) x + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3p}{4} - \dfrac{q}{2}}\right)  \geq  0$ 恒成立,则函数 $y =  h\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \leq  1$ 时严格增,在 $x \geq  1$ 时严格减,因此不是含谷函数,与题设矛盾. 因此,关于 $x$ 的方程 ${x}^{2} + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3p}{4}}\right) x + (1 -  \left. {\dfrac{3p}{4} - \dfrac{q}{2}}\right)  = 0$ 有两个相异实根,即 $\Delta  > 0$ ,记为 $\alpha ,\beta$ ,不妨设 $\alpha  <  \beta$ . 因为 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  0 = h\left( 0\right)$ ,所以函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $( - \infty ,1\rbrack$ 上不为严格增. 但是当 $x < \min \{ 1,\alpha ,\beta \}$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,y = h\left( x\right)$ 严格增,所以 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $( - \infty ,1\rbrack$ 上的单调性至少改变了一次，从而必有一个驻点，即 $\alpha  < 1$ . 同理，因为 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  h\left( 2\right)$ ， 所以 $\beta  > 1$ . 因此， $y = h\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {-\infty ,\alpha }\right)$ 和 $\left\lbrack  {1,\beta }\right\rbrack$ 上严格增， 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,1}\right\rbrack$ 和 $\left( {\beta , + \infty }\right)$ 上严格减; 从而函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的含谷区间 $\left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ 必满足 $\left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ (直观来看， $y = h\left( x\right)$ 的图像呈“ $M$ ”形,而 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  h\left( 2\right)$ 且 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  0 = h\left( 0\right)$ ,所以驻点 $x = 1$ 只能是极小值点,即为谷点),即 $L\left( {p,q}\right)  = \beta  - \alpha  = \sqrt{\Delta } =  \sqrt{{\left( 1 - \dfrac{3}{4}p\right) }^{2} - 4\left( {1 - \dfrac{3}{4}p - \dfrac{q}{2}}\right) } = \sqrt{\dfrac{9}{16}{p}^{2} + \dfrac{3}{2}p - 3 + {2q}}.$ 此时,由 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  h\left( 2\right)$ 和 $h\left( 1\right)  \leq  0$ 得 $q \geq  {11} - {4p}$ 且 $q \geq  3 - {2p}$ . 即 $q \geq  \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {11} - {4p},p \leq  4, \\  3 - {2p},p > 4, \end{array}\right.$ 所以当 $p \leq  4$ 时， $L\left( {p,q}\right)  \geq  \sqrt{\dfrac{9}{16}{p}^{2} - \dfrac{13}{2}p + {19}} \geq  \sqrt{2}$ ,而当 $p > 4$ 时, $L\left( {p,q}\right)  \geq  \sqrt{\dfrac{9}{16}{p}^{2} - \dfrac{5}{2}p + 3} > \sqrt{2}$ . 因此 $L\left( {p,q}\right)$ 的最小值为 $\sqrt{2}$ ,当 $p = 4,q =  - 5$ 时取得.
