(2)对于函数 $y = f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,设 $h\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right) ,{h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1 - x}{{{e}}^{x}} - \dfrac{1 - \ln x}{{x}^{2}}$ ,当 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {1,{e}}\right\rbrack$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,严格递减, $h\left( 1\right) h\left( {e}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{{e}} \cdot  \left( {\dfrac{1}{{{e}}^{{e} - 1}} - \dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right)  < 0$ ,存在一个零点 ${x}_{0}$ ; 当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  > 0,g\left( x\right)  < 0,h\left( x\right)  =  f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,无零点; 当 $x \in  \left( {{e}, + \infty }\right)$ 时,由 (1) 得 $g\left( x\right)  <  \dfrac{1}{{e}},\dfrac{\ln x}{x} < 1$ ,所以 $1 < \ln x < x < {e}$ ,所以 $f\left( x\right)  < f\left( {\ln x}\right)  = g\left( x\right)$ , 所以 $h\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - g\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,无零点; 综上所述,曲线 $y =  f\left( x\right) ,y = g\left( x\right)$ 有唯一交点,且横坐标 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {1,{e}}\right)$ ;

(3)因为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增，值域为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right)$ ，所以 $y = a$ 和 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 上有一个交点,同理 $y = a$ 和 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 上有另一个交点; 因为 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {1,{e}}\right)$ 上严格增,值域为 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{{e}}}\right)$ ,所以 $y = a$ 和 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {1,{e}}\right)$ 上有一个交点,同理 $y = a$ 和 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {{e}, + \infty }\right)$ 上有另一个交点, 由题意, 共有三个不同交点, 则上述四个交点中有两个重合,于是 $y = a$ 和 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 交点横坐标为 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},y = a$ 和 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 交点横坐标为 ${x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ ,其中 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,1}\right) ,{x}_{2} \in  \left( {1,{e}}\right)$ , ${x}_{3} \in  \left( {{e}, + \infty }\right)$ ,由题意 $\dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{1}}} = \dfrac{{x}_{2}}{{{e}}^{{x}_{2}}} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{3}}{{x}_{3}}$ ,即 ${x}_{1} \cdot  {x}_{3} =  {{e}}^{{x}_{1}} \cdot  \ln {x}_{3}$ ,而 $\dfrac{\ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{2}}{{{e}}^{\ln {x}_{2}}}$ ,因为 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {0,1}\right) ,\ln {x}_{2} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,又因为 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,所以 ${x}_{1} = \ln {x}_{2}$ 即 ${x}_{2} = {{e}}^{{x}_{1}}$ , 同理 ${x}_{2} = \ln {x}_{3}$ ,综上, ${x}_{1}{x}_{3} = {{e}}^{{x}_{1}}\ln {x}_{3} = {x}_{2}{x}_{2}{x}_{2}^{2}$ ,所以 ${x}_{1}$ 、 ${x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ 成等比数列.

\section*{考点 6 利用导数研究函数的极值点及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\arccos \dfrac{40}{41}$

【解析】所消耗的总体力

$y = \dfrac{4\left( {{1.025} - \cos \theta }\right) }{\sin \theta } = \dfrac{{4.1} - 4\cos \theta }{\sin \theta },  {y}^{\prime } = \dfrac{4{\sin }^{2}\theta  - \left( {{4.1} - 4\cos \theta }\right) \cos \theta }{{\sin }^{2}\theta } = \dfrac{4 - {4.1}\cos \theta }{{\sin }^{2}\theta } = 0 \Longrightarrow  \cos \theta  = \dfrac{40}{41} \Longrightarrow  \theta  = \arccos \dfrac{40}{41}$ .

2. $\left( {-\infty , - 2\sqrt{2}}\right)$

【解析】易知函数 $f\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + {ax} + \ln x$ 的定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ , ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x} + a + \dfrac{1}{x} = \dfrac{2{x}^{2} + {ax} + 1}{x}$ ,因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 存在两个不同的极值点,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 内有两个不等根, 设 $g\left( x\right)  = 2{x}^{2} + {ax} + 1,x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,则只需 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} g\left( 0\right)  > 0 \\  \Delta  > 0 \\   - \dfrac{a}{2} > 0 \end{array}\right.$ ,即 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} 1 > 0 \\  {a}^{2} - 8 > 0,\text{ 所以 }a <  - 2\sqrt{2},\text{ 则 }a\text{ 的取值范围为 }\left( {-\infty , - 2\sqrt{2}}\right) . \\  a < 0 \end{array}\right.$ 故答案为: $\left( {-\infty , - 2\sqrt{2}}\right)$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

3. C

【解析】易知, $y = x{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 有三个零点 $x = 0, \pm  2$ ,因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 为二次函数，所以，它有两个零点 $x =  \pm  2$ ，由图像易知，当 $0 <  x < 2$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ; 当 $x > 2$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,故 $f\left( 2\right)$ 是极小值, 类似地可知, $f\left( {-2}\right)$ 是极大值. 故答案为 C.

\section*{三、解答题}

4. (1) 函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ . 对 ${x}_{0} = {2k\pi } + \dfrac{1}{2}\pi$ ， $k \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,以及任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,由 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = \sin x - 1$ 及 $- 1 \leq  \sin x \leq  1$ 知 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-2,0}\right\rbrack$ ,即 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  A$ , 所以 ${x}_{0} = {2k\pi } + \dfrac{1}{2}\pi ,k \in  \mathbf{Z}$ 是函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的一个 $A$ 值点, $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 1$ 为相应的 $A$ 值;

(2)函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - x$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ . 对任意 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ，取 $x =  {x}_{0} + 2$ ,仍有 $x = {x}_{0} + 2 \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,但 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  - \left( {{x}_{0} + 2}\right)  -  \left( {-{x}_{0}}\right)  =  - 2 \notin  A$ ,所以函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 不存在 $A$ 值,函数 $g\left( x\right)  =  {x}^{2} + x + 1$ 的定义域为 $\mathbf{R}$ . 由 $g\left( x\right)  = {x}^{2} + x + 1 = {\left( x + \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} +  \dfrac{3}{4}$ 易知,当 ${x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 时,对任意 $x \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,均有 $g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  =  {\left( x + \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} \geq  0$ ,即 $g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  A$ ; 又对任意 ${x}_{0} \neq   - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,取 $x =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 $g\left( x\right)  - g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0 - {\left( {x}_{0} + \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} < 0$ ,即 $g\left( x\right)  -  g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \notin  A$ ,所以 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{4}$ 是函数 $g\left( x\right)$ 仅有的一个 $A$ 值, ${x}_{0} =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 是相应的 $A$ 值点;

(3)函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + a{x}^{2}\left( {a \in  \mathbf{R}}\right)$ 的定义域为 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ，由题设,该函数存在 $A$ 值,设相应 $A$ 值点为 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)  - f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  A$ 即 $f\left( x\right)  \leq  f\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ 对任意 $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 成立,故函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 的 $A$ 值即为最大值, $A$ 值点即最大值点, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + {2ax}$ ,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ 得 ${2a}{x}^{2} + 1 = 0$ ,所以 $a < 0$ , 解得驻点 ${x}_{0} = \sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}}$ ,

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
0 & $\left( {0,\sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}}}\right)$ & $\sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}}$ & $\left( {\sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}}, + \infty }\right)$ \\
\cline{1-4}
${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ & + & 0 & - \\
\cline{1-4}
$h\left( x\right)$ & ↗ & 极大值 $\ln \sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}} - \dfrac{1}{2}$ & ↘ \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

所以若函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + a{x}^{2}$ 存在 $A$ 值,则 $a < 0$ ,

$A$ 值为 $\ln \sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}} - \dfrac{1}{2},A$ 值点为 $\sqrt{-\dfrac{1}{2a}}$ .

\section*{考点 7 导数与函数的基本性质结合问题}

\section*{一、选择题}

1. A

【解析】不等式 ${x}^{3} - m \leq  {ax} + b \leq  {x}^{3} + m$ 等价于 $- m \leq  - {x}^{3} + {ax} + b \leq  m$ 即 $\left| {-{x}^{3} + {ax} + b}\right|  \leq  m$ ,原命题等价于存在实数 $a,b$ ,对任意实数 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 不等式 $\left| {-{x}^{3} + {ax} + b}\right|  \leq  m$ 恒成立，等价于存在实数 $a$ 、 $b$ ，不等式 ${\left| -{x}^{3} + ax + b\right| }_{\max } \leq  m$ 成立,记 $f\left( x\right)  =  - {x}^{3} + {ax} + b$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  - 3{x}^{2} + a$ ,记 $g\left( b\right)  = {\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max }$

(1)当 $a \leq  0$ 时,对任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \leq  0$ 恒成立，即 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减, $a + b - 1 \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  b$ , \ding{172}  当 $a + b - 1 + b \geq  0$ , 即 $b \geq  \dfrac{1 - a}{2}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } = b$ , \ding{173}  当 $a + b - 1 + b < 0$ ,即 $b < \dfrac{1 - a}{2}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } =  - a - b + 1$ ,从而当 $a \leq  0$ 时, $g\left( b\right)  =  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} b,b \geq  \dfrac{1 - a}{2}, \\   - a - b + 1,b < \dfrac{1 - a}{2}, \end{array}\text{ 则 }g\left( b\right) \text{ 在 }\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1 - a}{2}}\right) \text{ 上严格减,在 }}\right.  \left( {\dfrac{1 - a}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,所以 $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = g\left( \dfrac{1 - a}{2}\right)  = \dfrac{1 - a}{2} \geq  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ; (2)当 $0 < a < 3$ 时,令 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 0$ ，解得 $x = \sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}},f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left\lbrack  {0,\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增,在 $\left\lbrack  {\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}},1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减, $f\left( 0\right)  = b$ , $f\left( \sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b,f\left( 1\right)  = a + b - 1$ , \ding{172}  当 $0 < a \leq  1$ 时, $a + b - 1 \leq  b$ ,此时 $a + b - 1 \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b$ ,(i) 当 $a + b - 1 + \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b < 0$ 即 $b < \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } =  - a - b + 1$ ,(ii) 当 $a + b - 1 + \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b \geq  0$ 即 $b \geq  \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } = \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b$ ,从而当 $0 < a \leq  1$ 时, $g\left( b\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - a - b + 1,b < \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, \\  \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b,b \geq  \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, \end{array}\right.$ 则 $g\left( b\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}}\right)$ 上严格减,在区间 $\left( {\dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,所以 $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = g\left( {\dfrac{1}{2} - }\right.  \left. {\dfrac{1}{2}a - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{a}{2} + \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ ;令 $t = \sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ ,则 $0 < t \leq  \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{3}},g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{3}{2}{t}^{2} + {t}^{3}$ ,记 $h\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{3}{2}{t}^{2} + {t}^{3}$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = 3{t}^{2} - {3t} = {3t}\left( {t - 1}\right)$ ,当 $t \in  \left( {0,\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{3}}}\right\rbrack$ 时, ${h}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  < 0$ 恒成立, 即 $h\left( t\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\sqrt{\dfrac{1}{3}}}\right\rbrack$ 上严格减，即 $h{\left( t\right) }_{\min } = h\left( \sqrt{\dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{9}$ , 即 $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } \geq  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{9}$ ;  \ding{173}  当 $1 < a < 3$ 时 $a + b - 1 > b$ ,此时 $b \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b$ ,(i) 当 $b + \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b < 0$ 即 $b <  - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } =  - b$ ,(ii) 当 $b + \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b \geq  0$ 即 $b \geq   - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } = \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b$ ,从而当 $1 < a < 3$ 时, $g\left( b\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l}  - b,b <  - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, \\  \dfrac{2a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} + b,b \geq   - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, \end{array}\right.$

则 $g\left( b\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {-\infty , - \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}}\right)$ 上严格减,

在区间 $\left( {-\dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,

所以 $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = g\left( {-\dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}}}\right)  = \dfrac{a}{3}\sqrt{\dfrac{a}{3}} > \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{9}$ ;

(3)当 $a \geq  3$ 时，对任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  \geq  0$ 恒成立，

即 $f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 上严格增, $b \leq  f\left( x\right)  \leq  a + b - 1$ ,

 \ding{172}  当 $a + b - 1 + b \geq  0$ ，即 $b \geq  \dfrac{1 - a}{2}$ 时， ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } = a + b - 1$ ，  \ding{173}  当 $a + b - 1 + b < 0$ ,即 $b < \dfrac{1 - a}{2}$ 时, ${\left| f\left( x\right) \right| }_{\max } =  - b$ ,从而当 $a \geq  3$ 时, $g\left( b\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} a + b - 1,b \geq  \dfrac{1 - a}{2}, \\   - b,b < \dfrac{1 - a}{2}, \end{array}\right.$ 则 $g\left( b\right)$ 在 $\left( {-\infty ,\dfrac{1 - a}{2}}\right)$ 上严格减,在 $\left( {\dfrac{1 - a}{2}, + \infty }\right)$ 上严格增,所以 $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = g\left( \dfrac{1 - a}{2}\right)  =  \dfrac{a - 1}{2} \geq  1$ ; 综上所述， $g{\left( b\right) }_{\min } = \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{9}$ ，所以 $m \geq  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{9}$ . 故选:A.

点睛:本题考查不等式的恒成立与有解问题，可按如下规则转化: 一般地,已知函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack  ,y = g\left( x\right)$ , $x \in  \left\lbrack  {c,d}\right\rbrack  ,\left( 1\right)$ 若任意 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ ,任意 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {c,d}\right\rbrack$ ,总有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 成立，则 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\max } < g{\left( x\right) }_{\min }$ ; (2) 若任意 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ ,存在 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {c,d}\right\rbrack$ ,有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  < g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 成立,则 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\max } <  g{\left( x\right) }_{\max }$ ; (3) 若存在 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ ,存在 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {c,d}\right\rbrack$ ,有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  <  g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ 成立，则 $f{\left( x\right) }_{\min } < g{\left( x\right) }_{\min }$ ；(4) 若任意 ${x}_{1} \in  \left\lbrack  {a,b}\right\rbrack$ ，存在 ${x}_{2} \in  \left\lbrack  {c,d}\right\rbrack$ ,有 $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = g\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,则 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的值域是 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 值域的子集.

\section*{二、解答题}

2. (1) $m = 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x + \dfrac{1}{x},{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} = \dfrac{x - 1}{{x}^{2}}$ . 故当 $x \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)$ 严格减; $x \in  \left( {1, + \infty }\right)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,即 $x = 1$ 时, $f\left( x\right)$ 取得极小值也是最小值 1 ;

(2)假设存在，则 ${x}_{2}^{2} = {x}_{1}{x}_{3}$ ，两边取对数得: ${2\ln }{x}_{2} = {ln}{x}_{1} +  \ln {x}_{3},f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  - {2f}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = \ln {x}_{1} + \dfrac{m}{{x}_{1}} + \ln {x}_{3} + \dfrac{m}{{x}_{3}} -  2\left( {\ln {x}_{2} + \dfrac{m}{{x}_{2}}}\right)  = \dfrac{m\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{3} - 2{x}_{2}}\right) }{{x}_{1}{x}_{3}} = 0$ ,当 $m \neq  0$ 时, ${x}_{1},{x}_{2}$ 、 ${x}_{3}$ 既成等差又成等比,常数数列,与 $0 < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3}$ 矛盾, 舍去; 当 $m = 0$ 时, $f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + f\left( {x}_{3}\right)  - {2f}\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = 0$ ,存在 ${x}_{1} =  1,{x}_{2} = 2,{x}_{3} = 4$ 满足题意(答案不唯一)；综上，当 $m = 0$ 时， 存在这样的 ${x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}$ 满足题意；

(3)充分性:当 $m = 0$ 时， $f\left( x\right)  = \ln x$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x}$ ，因为 ${x}_{2} >  {x}_{1} > 0$ ,设 $t = \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}},t \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,故 $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} >  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} > \dfrac{\ln {x}_{1} - \ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} \Leftrightarrow  \dfrac{{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{1}^{2}}{2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} < \ln \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} \Leftrightarrow  t - \dfrac{1}{t} < 2\ln t$ ,令 $h\left( t\right)  = t - \dfrac{1}{t} - 2\ln t$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = 1 + \dfrac{1}{{t}^{2}} - \dfrac{2}{t} =  {\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{t}\right) }^{2} > 0$ ,故 $h\left( t\right)$ 在 $t \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ 上是严格增函数,于是 $h\left( t\right)  < h\left( 1\right)  = 0$ 恒成立,即原不等式成立. 当 $m < 0$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  =  \ln x - \dfrac{m}{x},{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + \dfrac{m}{{x}^{2}}$ ,于是 $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} -  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} - \dfrac{\ln {x}_{1} - \ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} - \dfrac{m}{2}\left( {\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}^{2}} - \dfrac{2}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}}\right) ,$ 由前述可知 $\dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2{x}_{1}{x}_{2}} - \dfrac{\ln {x}_{1} - \ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} > 0$ ,又 $- \dfrac{m}{2}\left( {\dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}^{2}} + \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}^{2}} - }\right.  \left. \dfrac{2}{{x}_{1}{x}_{2}}\right)  =  - \dfrac{m}{2}{\left( \dfrac{1}{{x}_{1}} - \dfrac{1}{{x}_{2}}\right) }^{2} > 0$ ,于是 $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} >  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}$ ,所以, $m \leq  0$ 是 $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} >  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}$ 恒成立的充分条件. 必要性: 假设 $m > 0$ ,由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{m}{{x}^{2}}$ ,不妨取 ${x}_{2} = m,{x}_{1} = \dfrac{m}{2}$ ,则 $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  = \ln m +  1,f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = \ln \dfrac{m}{2} + 2 = \ln m + 2 - \ln 2$ ,于是, $\dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} =  \dfrac{-2\left( {1 - \ln 2}\right) }{m},\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} =  - \dfrac{1}{m},\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} -  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{1 - 2\ln 2}{m} < 0$ ,即 $m > 0$ 时, $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} >  \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}$ 不是恒成立的,舍去,于是 $m \leq  0$ . 综上, $m \leq  0$ 是 $\dfrac{{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  + {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{2} > \dfrac{f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{2}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}$ 恒成立的充要条件.

\section*{考点 8 利用导数研究函数的零点及其应用}

\section*{一、选择题}

1. C

【解析】 ${f}_{0}\left( x\right)  = {\left( t + x\right) }^{m}$ ,逐项求导, ${f}_{1}\left( x\right)  = m{\left( t + x\right) }^{m - 1}$ , ${f}_{2}\left( x\right)  = m\left( {m - 1}\right) {\left( t + x\right) }^{m - 2},\cdots ,{f}_{m}\left( x\right)  = m!{\left( t + x\right) }^{0}$ ,令 ${b}_{n} = m!,{b}_{n}$ 为常数列,故 \ding{172} 正确, ${f}_{{m}_{0}}\left( x\right)  = {ax} + b,{f}_{{m}_{0}}\left( x\right)  =$ 0,一次方程有 1 个零点, ${f}_{m - 1}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}a{x}^{2} + {bx} + c$ 二次方程有 2 个零点, ${f}_{m - 2}\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{6}a{x}^{3} + \dfrac{1}{2}b{x}^{2} + {cx} + d$ ,三次方程有 3 个零点,以此类推,存在 ${f}_{0}\left( x\right)$ ,使得 ${b}_{n}$ 为公差不为零的等差数列, 故  \ding{173}  正确. 所以选 C.

2. D

【解析】当 $x \in  \lbrack  - 1,0)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = x + 1,f\left( x\right)$ 严格增,当 $x \in  \lbrack 0,1)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{3x} - 2}{1 - x} = \dfrac{-3\left( {1 - x}\right)  + 1}{1 - x} =  - 3 + \dfrac{1}{1 - x}$ ,

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/151_1274_1885_249_287_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

根据反比例函数 $y = \dfrac{1}{-x}$ 的平移可得到 $f\left( x\right)$ ,则此时 $f\left( x\right)$ 严格增, 作出图像如图所示，

令 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - {mx} + \dfrac{2}{3}m = 0$ , 即 $f\left( x\right)  = {mx} - \dfrac{2}{3}m = m\left( {x - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ ,

则题意转化为函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 与直线

$y = m\left( {x - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ 在图像上有两个交点, $f\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right)  = 0$ ,且直线 $y  = m\left( {x - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ 过定点 $\left( {\dfrac{2}{3},0}\right)$ ,当直线经过点 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 时,代入得 $1 =  - \dfrac{2}{3}m$ ,解得 $m =  - \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,设此时直线为 ${l}_{1}$ ; 当直线经过点 $\left( {-1,0}\right)$ 时,代入得 $0 =  - \dfrac{5}{3}m$ ,解得 $m = 0$ ,设此时直线为 ${l}_{2}$ ; 当直线经过点 $\left( {0, - 2}\right)$ 时,代入得 $- 2 =  - \dfrac{2}{3}m$ ,解得 $m = 3$ ,设此时直线为 ${l}_{3}$ ; 当 $x \in  \lbrack 0,1)$ 时, $f\left( x\right)  =  - 3 + \dfrac{1}{1 - x},{f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {\left( 1 - x\right) }^{-2},{f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right)  = {\left( 1 - \dfrac{2}{3}\right) }^{-2} = 9$ ,则 $f\left( x\right)$ 在点 $\left( {\dfrac{2}{3},0}\right)$ 处的切线斜率为 9,设此时直线为 ${l}_{4}$ . 由图知当直线 $y = m  \left( {x - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ 在 ${l}_{1}$ 和 ${l}_{2}$ 或 ${l}_{3}$ 和 ${l}_{4}$ 之间转动时,存在两个交点,同时注意边界值是否取等可知 $m \in  \left( {-\dfrac{3}{2},0}\right\rbrack   \cup  \lbrack 3,9)$ ,故选: D.

\section*{二、解答题}

3. (1) 因为 $f\left( 2\right)  = \dfrac{21}{4}$ ,

所以 $\mathop{\lim }\limits_{{h \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{f\left( {2 + h}\right)  - f\left( 2\right) }{h}$

$= \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{h \rightarrow  0}}\dfrac{{\left( 2 + h\right) }^{3} - {\left( 2 + h\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {h + 2}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{4} - \dfrac{21}{4}}{h}  = \mathop{\lim }\limits_{{h \rightarrow  0}}\left( {{h}^{2} + {5h} + \dfrac{17}{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{17}{2},$

几何意义是函数在点 $\left( {2,\dfrac{21}{4}}\right)$ 处切线的斜率是 $\dfrac{17}{2}$ ;

( 2 )变形 $f\left( x\right)  = a + x$ 得到 $a = {x}^{3} - {x}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}x + \dfrac{1}{4}$ ，令 $h\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - {x}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}x + \dfrac{1}{4},{h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - {2x} - \dfrac{1}{2} =  3{\left( x - \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{2} - \dfrac{5}{6}$ ,又 ${h}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{2},{h}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  =  - \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,所以函数 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 上恒小于零,所以函数 $h\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - {x}^{2} -  \dfrac{1}{2}x + \dfrac{1}{4}$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 严格减,又 $h\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{1}{4},h\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{8}$ ,所以 $h\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - {x}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}x + \dfrac{1}{4}$ 值域为 $\left( {-\dfrac{1}{8},\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ ,所以 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left( {-\dfrac{1}{8},\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ ;

(3)由(2)知函数 $g\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - x$ 在 $\left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 严格减，且存在唯一的零点 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ 使得 $g\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 0$ ,即 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = {x}_{0}$ , ${x}_{1} = 0,{x}_{2} = f\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,所以 $\dfrac{1}{2} > \dfrac{1}{4} = {x}_{2} > {x}_{1} > 0$ ,根据函数单调性知 $f\left( {x}_{2}\right)  < f\left( {x}_{1}\right)$ ,即 ${x}_{3} > {x}_{2}$ ,依次类推,得到 $\dfrac{1}{2} > {x}_{0} > {x}_{n + 1} > {x}_{n} > 0$ ,同理 ${x}_{0} < {y}_{n + 1} < {y}_{n} < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 $0 <  {x}_{n} < {x}_{n + 1} < {x}_{n + 2} < \cdots  < {x}_{0} < \cdots  < {y}_{n + 2} < {y}_{n + 1} < {y}_{n} < \cdots  <  \dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{{y}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n + 1}}{{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}} = \dfrac{f\left( {y}_{n}\right)  - f\left( {x}_{n}\right) }{{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}} = {y}_{n}{}^{2} + {x}_{n}{y}_{n} + {x}_{n}{}^{2} - \left( {{x}_{n} + }\right.  \left. {y}_{n}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{2} \leq  {\left( {y}_{n} + {x}_{n}\right) }^{2} - \left( {{y}_{n} + {x}_{n}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{2} \leq  \left\lbrack  {\left( {y}_{n} + {x}_{n} - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2}\right.  \left. {+\dfrac{1}{4}}\right\rbrack$ ,因为 $0 < {y}_{n} + {x}_{n} < 1$ ,所以 ${\left\lbrack  \left( {y}_{n} + {x}_{n}\right)  - \dfrac{1}{2}\right\rbrack  }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{4} \in  \left( {\dfrac{1}{4},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $0 < \dfrac{{y}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n + 1}}{{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}} < \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以得到 $0 < {y}_{n + 1} -  {x}_{n + 1} < \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}}\right)$ ,所以 $0 < {y}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n + 1} < \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}}\right)  <  \dfrac{1}{{2}^{2}}\left( {{y}_{n - 1} - {x}_{n - 1}}\right)  < \cdots  < \dfrac{1}{{2}^{n}}\left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{b}_{n} = \left( {{y}_{n} - {x}_{n}}\right)  +  \left( {{y}_{n - 1} - {x}_{n - 1}}\right)  + \cdots  + \left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right)  < \dfrac{1}{{2}^{n - 1}}\left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right)  + \dfrac{1}{{2}^{n - 2}}\left( {{y}_{1} - }\right.  \left. {x}_{1}\right)  + \cdots  + \dfrac{1}{2}\left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right) ,\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{b}_{n} < \left( {\dfrac{1}{{2}^{n - 1}} + \dfrac{1}{{2}^{n - 2}}}\right.  \left. {+\cdots  + \dfrac{1}{2} + 1}\right) \left( {{y}_{1} - {x}_{1}}\right)  < \dfrac{1\left\lbrack  {1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{n}}\right\rbrack  }{1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\left( {\dfrac{1}{2} - 0}\right)$ ,所以 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{b}_{n} < 1 - {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{n} \in  \left( {0,1}\right) .$

4. (1) 记 $F\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - g\left( 0\right)  = {2}^{x} - 1 - \cos 0 = {2}^{x} - 2$ ,函数 $y =  F\left( x\right) ,x \geq  0$ 上的值域为 $\lbrack  - 1, + \infty )$ ,即 ${M}_{0} = \lbrack  - 1, + \infty )$ ;

(2)设 $G\left( x\right)  = f\left( x\right)  - g\left( a\right)  = {x}^{3} - 3{x}^{2} + a$ 在 $x \in  \lbrack a, + \infty )$ 上的最小值 ${G}_{\min } \geq  0.{G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} - {6x} = {3x}\left( {x - 2}\right)$ . 当 $x \in  \left( {-\infty ,0}\right)$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0,G\left( x\right)$ 严格增; 当 $x \in  \left( {0,2}\right)$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0,G\left( x\right)$ 严格减; 当 $x \in  \left( {2, + \infty }\right)$ 时, ${G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ , $G\left( x\right)$ 严格增. 当 $x = 2$ 时 $G\left( x\right)$ 取得极小值. 当 $a \leq  2$ 时, $\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} G\left( a\right)  \geq  0 \\  G\left( 2\right)  \geq  0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {a}^{3} - 3{a}^{2} + a \geq  0 \\   - 4 + a \geq  0 \end{array} \Longrightarrow  \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} a\left( {{a}^{2} - {3a} + 1}\right)  \geq  0, \\  a \geq  4, \end{array}\text{ 舍去, }}\right. }\right. }\right.$ 当 $a > 2$ 时, $G\left( a\right)  \geq  0 \Longrightarrow  a\left( {{a}^{2} - {3a} + 1}\right)  \geq  0,a \geq  \dfrac{3 + \sqrt{5}}{2}$ . 综上, $a \geq  \dfrac{3 + \sqrt{5}}{2};$

(3)(充分性)若 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数，则 $y = f\left( x\right)  - g\left( a\right)$ ， $x \geq  a$ 的最小值为 $f\left( a\right)  - g\left( a\right)$ ,而 ${M}_{a} = \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,故对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 $f\left( a\right)  = g\left( a\right)$ ,即 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 与 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是相同函数. 故 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数,所以 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 严格增函数, 故对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R},h\left( x\right)  = g\left( x\right)  - a$ 的零点个数有限. (必要性) 对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,都有 ${M}_{a} = \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ ,故 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的值域为 $\lbrack g\left( a\right) , + \infty )$ ,即 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \lbrack a, + \infty )$ 上的最小值为 $g\left( a\right)$ . 先证 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数. 对任意 ${a}_{1} < {a}_{2}$ , 函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  {a}_{1}$ 和 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  {a}_{2}$ 的最小值分别为 $g\left( {a}_{1}\right)$ 和 $g\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ ,则由最小值的定义, $g\left( {a}_{1}\right)  \leq  g\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ ,故函数 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 是增函数. 假设存在 ${a}_{1} < {a}_{2}$ ,使得 $g\left( {a}_{1}\right)  = g\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ , 则对任意 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {{a}_{1},{a}_{2}}\right\rbrack$ ,均有 $g\left( {a}_{1}\right)  \leq  g\left( x\right)  \leq  g\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ ,从而方程 $g\left( x\right)  = g\left( {a}_{2}\right)$ 的解有无限多个,与条件“对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,函数 $y = h\left( x\right)$ 零点个数均有限”矛盾. 故假设不成立,从而 $y =  g\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数. 再证对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的最小值为 $f\left( a\right)$ . 假设存在 ${x}_{0} > a$ 使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( a\right)$ ,取 $c \in  \left( {a,{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  c$ 的最小值为 $g\left( c\right)$ . 由于 $y =  g\left( x\right)$ 严格增,知 $g\left( c\right)  > g\left( a\right)$ . 而 ${x}_{0} \in  \lbrack c, + \infty )$ ,故 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \geq  g\left( c\right)  > g\left( a\right)$ ,矛盾. 所以假设不成立,对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的最小值为 $f\left( a\right)$ . 另证: 再证对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ , 函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的最小值为 $f\left( a\right) .f\left( a\right)  \geq  g\left( a\right)$ . 假设 $f\left( a\right)  > g\left( a\right)$ ,则由 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \lbrack a, + \infty )$ 上的最小值为 $g\left( a\right)$ ,存在 ${x}_{0} > a$ 使得 $f\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = g\left( a\right)$ ,故 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \left\lbrack  {{x}_{0}, + \infty }\right)$ 上的最小值为 $g\left( a\right)$ . 取 $c \in  \left( {a,{x}_{0}}\right)$ ,则 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $x \in  \lbrack c, + \infty )$ 上的最小值为 $g\left( c\right)$ ,故 $g\left( a\right)  = g\left( c\right)$ . 但由 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 为严格增函数,知 $g\left( c\right)  > g\left( a\right)$ ,矛盾. 所以假设不成立,所以 $f\left( a\right)  = g\left( a\right)$ . 即对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,函数 $y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的最小值为 $f\left( a\right)$ . 而对任意 $a \in  \mathbf{R},y = f\left( x\right) ,x \geq  a$ 的值域为 $\lbrack g\left( a\right) , + \infty )$ ,故 $f\left( a\right)  = g\left( a\right)$ . 于是 $y = g\left( x\right)$ 与 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 是相同函数,所以 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 是严格增函数.

5. (1) 由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \cos x - \left( {\cos x - x\sin x}\right)  = x\sin x$ ,当 $x \in  \left( {0,\pi }\right)$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,即函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\pi }\right)$ 上是严格增函数,且 $f\left( 0\right)  = 0,f\left( \pi \right)  = \pi$ ,所以 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,\pi }\right)$ 上的值域为 $\left( {0,\pi }\right)$ ;

(2)当 $x \in  \left( {{n\pi },\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }\right)$ 时， \ding{172}  当 $n$ 是偶数时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ，函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {{n\pi },\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }\right)$ 上是严格增函数； \ding{173}  当 $n$ 是奇数时， ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ，函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {{n\pi },\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }\right)$ 上是严格减函数; 且 $f\left( {n\pi }\right)  = {\left( -1\right) }^{n - 1}{n\pi }$ ,故 $f\left( {n\pi }\right)  \cdot  f\left( {\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }\right)  =  - n\left( {n + 1}\right) {\pi }^{2} < 0$ ,所以由零点存在定理可知,函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {{n\pi },\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }\right)$ 上有且仅有一个零点;

(3)由(2)可知函数 $f\left( x\right)$ 在( ${n\pi }$ ，( $\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi$ )上有且仅有一个零点 ${x}_{n}$ ,且满足 $f\left( {x}_{n}\right)  = \sin {x}_{n} - {x}_{n}\cos {x}_{n} = 0$ ,即 $\tan {x}_{n} = {x}_{n}$ (几何意义: ${x}_{n}$ 是 $y = \tan x$ 与 $y = x$ 交点的横坐标),又因为 $f\left( {{n\pi } + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)  = {\left( -1\right) }^{n}$ ,故 $f\left( {n\pi }\right)  \cdot  f\left( {{n\pi } + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)  =  - {n\pi } < 0$ ,所以由零点存在性定理可知,函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {{n\pi },{n\pi } + \dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 上有且仅有一个零点 ${x}_{n}$ ,于是 ${x}_{n} + \pi ,{x}_{n + 1} \in  \left( {\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi ,\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi  + }\right.  \left. \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right) ,{x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right)  \in  \left( {-\dfrac{\pi }{2},\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right) ,\tan \left( {{x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right) }\right)  =  \tan \left( {{x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n}}\right)  = \dfrac{\tan {x}_{n + 1} - \tan {x}_{n}}{1 + \tan {x}_{n + 1} \cdot  \tan {x}_{n}} = \dfrac{{x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n}}{1 + {x}_{n + 1} \cdot  {x}_{n}}$ , \ding{172}  因为 ${x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n} > 0$ ,得 $\tan \left( {{x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right) }\right)  > 0$ ,所以 ${x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + }\right.  \pi ) > 0$ ,即 $\pi  < {x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n}$ ; (或者 $\tan {x}_{n + 1} - \tan \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right)  =  \tan {x}_{n + 1} - \tan {x}_{n} = {x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n} > 0 \Longrightarrow  \tan {x}_{n + 1} > \tan \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right)  \Longrightarrow  \left. {{x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n} > \pi }\right)$ , \ding{173}  因为 $\tan \left( {{x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right) }\right)  =  \dfrac{{x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n}}{1 + {x}_{n + 1} \cdot  {x}_{n}} < \dfrac{\dfrac{3}{2}\pi }{{x}_{n}^{2}} < \dfrac{\dfrac{3}{2}\pi }{{n}^{2}{\pi }^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{2{n}^{2}\pi } < \dfrac{\pi }{n}$ ,由 (1) 可知,当 $x \in  \left( {0,\dfrac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ 时,有 $x < \tan x$ ,故 ${x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + \pi }\right)  < \tan \left( {{x}_{n + 1} - \left( {{x}_{n} + }\right. }\right.  \pi )) < \dfrac{\pi }{n}$ ,所以 ${x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n} < \pi  + \dfrac{\pi }{n}$ ;

由 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 可知 $\pi  < {x}_{n + 1} - {x}_{n} < \dfrac{\left( {n + 1}\right) \pi }{n}$ .

6. (1) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2x}$ ，由 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 1$ 可得 ${x}_{0} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，而 ${k}_{AB} =  \dfrac{0 - {n}^{2}}{0 - n} = n$ ,因为 $M$ 为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $(0,n)$ 上的“ $T$ 点”,故 $n = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  = 1$ ;

(2) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {2a}\cos {2x} - \sin x + 1$ ，因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 在区间 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上恰有 3 个 “ $T$ 点”，所以 ${2a}\cos {2x} - \sin x + 1 =  \dfrac{f\left( 0\right)  - f\left( {2\pi }\right) }{0 - {2\pi }} = \dfrac{0 - {2\pi }}{0 - {2\pi }} = 1$ 在 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上有三个不同的解,故 ${4a}{\sin }^{2}x + \sin x - {2a} = 0$ 在 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上有三个不同的解,设 $t =  \sin x,x \in  \left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ ,则 $t \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ,则 ${4a}{t}^{2} + t - {2a} = 0$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 上有两个不同的根 ${t}_{1},{t}_{2}$ ,且其中有且只有一个根为 -1 或 1 或 0 , 若一个根为 1 , 则 $a =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ ，此时另一个根为 $- \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,此时 $\sin x = 1$ 有一根 $x = \dfrac{\pi }{2},\sin x =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 在 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上有两个不同的解 $\dfrac{7\pi }{6},\dfrac{11\pi }{6}$ ,故此时 ${4a}{\sin }^{2}x + \sin x + \pi  = 1 - {2a}  = 0$ 在 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上有三个不同的解; 若一个根为 -1,则 $a =  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,此时另一个根为 $\dfrac{1}{2}$ ,同理可得 ${4a}{\sin }^{2}x + \sin x + \pi  - 1 -  {2a} = 0$ 在 $\left( {0,{2\pi }}\right)$ 上有三个不同的解; 若一个根为 0,则 $a = 0$ , 此时 ${4a}{t}^{2} + t - {2a} = 0$ 在 $\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ 上仅有一个根,舍; 综上, $a =  \pm  \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即 $a \in  \left\{  {-\dfrac{1}{2},\dfrac{1}{2}}\right\}$ ;

(3) ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} + {2bx}$ ，若存在含“和谐 $T$ 点”的区间 $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ ， 则 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}_{0} = \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} \\  \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}} + {2b}{x}_{0} = \dfrac{\ln {x}_{1} - \ln {x}_{2} + b\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} - {x}_{2}^{2}}\right) }{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} \end{array}\right.$ ,其中 $0 < {x}_{1} < {x}_{2}$ ,整理得到: $\dfrac{\ln {x}_{1} - \ln {x}_{2}}{{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}} = \dfrac{2}{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}$ 即 $\ln \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} = \dfrac{2\left( {\dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} - 1}\right) }{\dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} + 1}$ ,设 $t =  \dfrac{{x}_{1}}{{x}_{2}} \in  \left( {0,1}\right)$ ,故 $\ln t = \dfrac{2\left( {t - 1}\right) }{t + 1}$ ,设 $s\left( t\right)  = \ln t - \dfrac{2\left( {t - 1}\right) }{t + 1},0 <  t < 1$ ,则 ${s}^{\prime }\left( t\right)  = \dfrac{{\left( t - 1\right) }^{2}}{t{\left( t + 1\right) }^{2}} > 0$ ,故 $s\left( t\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上为严格增函数,故 $s\left( t\right)  < s\left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,故 $\ln t < \dfrac{2\left( {t - 1}\right) }{t + 1},0 < t < 1$ ,故 $\ln t =  \dfrac{2\left( {t - 1}\right) }{t + 1}$ 不成立,故函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的定义域内不存在一个包含“和谐 $T$ 点”的区间 $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ .

7.(1)因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ ，所以 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  = 0$ ， ${f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  =  \cos 0 = 1$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  = {f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{\pi }{2}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 0 \geq  0$ ,所以 $\dfrac{\pi }{2}$ 是函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x$ 的“ $T$ 类数”; 因为 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \cos x$ , 所以 ${y}_{1} = {f}^{\prime }\left( \dfrac{3\pi }{4}\right)  =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2},{f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{1}\right)  = \cos \left( {-\dfrac{\sqrt{2}}{2}}\right)  > 0$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{1}\right)  < 0$ ,所以 $\dfrac{3\pi }{4}$ 不是函数 $f\left( x\right)  = \sin x$ 的“ $T$ 类数”;

(2)因为函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $T$ 类集”为集合 $S$ ，且 $S \subset  \mathbf{R}$ ，所以存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,使得 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ 且 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  < 0$ ,若 ${x}_{0} = {y}_{0}$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  = {\left\lbrack  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2} \geq  0$ ,所以 ${x}_{0} \neq  {y}_{0}$ , 因为函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的图像是连续不断的,不妨设 ${x}_{0} < {y}_{0}$ , 由零点存在定理知,必存在 ${x}_{1} \in  \left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 使得 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = 0$ , 所以 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 存在零点,即 $M \neq  \varnothing$ ;

(3) $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{\omega }\cos \left( {{\omega x} + \varphi }\right) \left( {\omega  > 0}\right)$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \sin ({\omega x} +  \varphi )$ . 先证明 $\omega  \leq  \pi$ : 因为函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{\omega }\cos \left( {{\omega x} + \varphi }\right) (\omega  >$ 0) 的“ $T$ 类集”为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,所以对任意 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R}$ ,令 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,则 ${y}_{0} \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ,因为函数 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \sin \left( {{\omega x} + \varphi }\right) \left( {\omega  > 0}\right)$ 的值域为 $\left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ,所以当 ${y}_{0} \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 时,必有 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ,即 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \sin \left( {{\omega x} + \varphi }\right)  \geq  0\left( {\omega  > 0}\right)$ 对于 $x \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 恒成立,所以函数 $y = {f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 的最小正周期 $T$ 应有 $\dfrac{T}{2} \geq  1 - 0$ ,即 $T = \dfrac{2\pi }{\omega } \geq$ 2,则 $\omega  \leq  \pi$ . 再证明 $0 \in  A$ ,此时 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \sin \left( {\omega x}\right)$ ,对于任意 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R},{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right) {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  = {y}_{0}\sin \left( {\omega {y}_{0}}\right)$ . 当 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ 时, $0 \leq  \omega {y}_{0} \leq  \omega  \leq  \pi$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  ,{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ; 当 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack$ 时, $- \pi  \leq   - \omega  \leq  \omega {y}_{0} \leq  0$ ,则 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,0}\right\rbrack  ,{f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ,所以 $\varphi  = 0$ 时函数 $f\left( x\right)  =  - \dfrac{1}{\omega }\cos \left( {{\omega x} + \varphi }\right) \left( {\omega  > 0}\right)$ 的 “ $T$ 类集” 为 $\mathbf{R}$ ,即 $0 \in  A$ . 我们不难发现,上述过程中令 $\omega  = \pi$ 也成立. 因此, $\omega$ 的最大值是 $\pi$ . 点睛: (1) 第二问由 $S \subset  \mathbf{R}$ ,可知必存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \mathbf{R}$ 不是“ $T$ 类数”,即 ${y}_{0} = {f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ 且 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \cdot  {f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  < 0$ ,结合图像的连续性和零点存在性定理,可知必存在使 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = 0$ 的零点. (2)由三角函数的值域可知 ${y}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  {-1,1}\right\rbrack$ ，因为函数 $y =  f\left( x\right)$ 的“ $T$ 类集”为 $\mathbf{R}$ ，则当 ${y}_{0} \in  (0,1\rbrack$ 时，必有 ${f}^{\prime }\left( {y}_{0}\right)  \geq  0$ ， 结合三角函数的对称性，当 $\varphi  = 0$ 时， $\omega$ 有最大值，所以只需证明 $0 \in  A$ ,可求出 $\omega$ 的最大值.

8. ( 1 )当 $a = 2$ 时， $F\left( x\right)  = {x}^{3} - 3{x}^{2} + {2x} \Longrightarrow  {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 3{x}^{2} -  {6x} + 2$ ,因为 ${F}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = 2 > 0,{F}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  =  - 1 < 0$ ,即 ${F}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)$ . ${F}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  < 0$ ,又因为 ${F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $R$ 上连续,所以函数 $y = {F}^{\prime }\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上有零点;

(2) $G\left( x\right)  = \ln x + \dfrac{-\left( {a + 1}\right) {x}^{2} + {ax}}{{x}^{2}} = \ln x + \dfrac{a}{x} - \left( {a + 1}\right)$ ， $x \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)  \Longrightarrow  {G}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{a}{{x}^{2}}$ ,因为函数 $G\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 上处处相依,所以存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {n,m}\right) ,m > n > 0$ ,使得 $\dfrac{G\left( m\right)  - G\left( n\right) }{m - n} = {G}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)$ ,即存在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {n,m}\right)$ ,使得 ${G}^{\prime }\left( {x}_{0}\right)  \leq  1 \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{1}{{x}_{0}} - \dfrac{a}{{x}_{0}^{2}} \leq  1$ ,即 $a \geq   - {x}_{0}^{2} + {x}_{0}$ 在 ${x}_{0} \in  \left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ 恒成立,又因为 $- {x}_{0}^{2} + {x}_{0} =  - {\left( {x}_{0} - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{1}{4} \leq  \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,

所以 $a \geq  \dfrac{1}{4}$ ,故实数 $a$ 的取值范围为 $\left\lbrack  {\dfrac{1}{4}, + \infty }\right)$ ;

(3)当 $a = 0$ 时， $H\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}}{x},x > 0$ ,则 ${H}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{\left( {x - 1}\right) {{e}}^{x}}{{x}^{2}}$ ,

因为 $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2}}\right)$ 为函数 $y = H\left( x\right)$ 的在 ${x}_{0} = 1$ 的相依区间,

所以 $\dfrac{H\left( {x}_{2}\right)  - H\left( {x}_{1}\right) }{{x}_{2} - {x}_{1}} = {H}^{\prime }\left( 1\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  H\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = H\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,

因为 $0 < x < 1,{H}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,即 $H\left( x\right)$ 严格减, $x > 1$ ,

${H}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ ,即 $H\left( x\right)$ 严格增,所以 $0 < {x}_{1} < 1 < {x}_{2}$ ,

则 $1 < 2 - {x}_{1} < 2$ ,要证 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} > 2$ ,

即证 ${x}_{2} > 2 - {x}_{1}$ ,即证 $H\left( {x}_{2}\right)  > H\left( {2 - {x}_{1}}\right)$ ,

即证 $H\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > H\left( {2 - {x}_{1}}\right) ,0 < {x}_{1} < 1$ ,

令 $\varphi \left( x\right)  = H\left( x\right)  - H\left( {2 - x}\right)  = \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}}{x} - \dfrac{{{e}}^{2 - x}}{2 - x},0 < x < 1$ ,

则 ${\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}\left( {x - 1}\right) }{{x}^{2}} - \dfrac{-{{e}}^{2 - x}\left( {2 - x}\right)  + {{e}}^{2 - x}}{{\left( 2 - x\right) }^{2}}$

$= \dfrac{{{e}}^{x}\left( {x - 1}\right) }{{x}^{2}} - \dfrac{{{e}}^{2 - x}\left( {x - 1}\right) }{{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2}}$

$= \dfrac{\left( {x - 1}\right) \left\lbrack  {{{e}}^{x}{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} - {x}^{2}{{e}}^{2 - x}}\right\rbrack  }{{x}^{2}{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2}},$

令 $t\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}{\left( x - 2\right) }^{2} - {x}^{2}{{e}}^{2 - x},0 < x < 1$ ,

则 ${t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x}\left( {{x}^{2} - {4x} + 4}\right)  + {{e}}^{x}\left( {{2x} - 4}\right)  - {2x}{{e}}^{2 - x} + {x}^{2}{{e}}^{2 - x}$

$= {{e}}^{x}\left( {{x}^{2} - {2x}}\right)  + {{e}}^{2 - x}\left( {{x}^{2} - {2x}}\right)$

$= x\left( {x - 2}\right) \left( {{{e}}^{x} + {{e}}^{2 - x}}\right)  < 0$

$\Longrightarrow  {t}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,

即 $t\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格减 $\Longrightarrow  t\left( x\right)  > t\left( 1\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  {\varphi }^{\prime }\left( x\right)  < 0$ ,

即 $\varphi \left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格减，故 $\varphi \left( x\right)  > \varphi \left( 1\right)  = 0$ ,

即 $H\left( x\right)  > H\left( {2 - x}\right)$ ,即证得 $H\left( {x}_{1}\right)  > H\left( {2 - {x}_{1}}\right)$ 成立,

从而证得 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} > 2$ .

点睛:本题第三问解题的关键是根据相依区间的定义得到 $H\left( {x}_{1}\right)  = H\left( {x}_{2}\right)$ ,结合条件利用分析法转化为 $H\left( {x}_{1}\right)  >  H\left( {2 - {x}_{1}}\right) ,0 < {x}_{1} < 1$ ,构造函数证明.

\section*{第 12 章 排列、组合与二项式定理}

\section*{考点 1 排列、组合}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 12

【解析】先将甲乙看成一个整体,共有 ${{P}}_{2}^{2} = 2$ 种不同排法,再与剩余学生排列,共有 ${{P}}_{3}^{3} = 6$ 种不同排法,所以共有 $2 \times  6 = {12}$ 种不同排法. 故答案为: 12 .

2. 48

【解析】由捆绑法可得 ${{P}}_{2}^{2}{{P}}_{4}^{4} = {48}$ .

3. 144

【解析】 $n = {{P}}_{3}^{3} \times  {{P}}_{4}^{3} = {144}$ .

4. 96

【解析】 $n = 4{{P}}_{4}^{4} = {96}$ .

5. 1 或 3

【解析】根据组合数的性质,由 ${{C}}_{12}^{x - 1} = {{C}}_{12}^{{5x} - 5}$ 可知:

$\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} x - 1 = {5x} - 5 \\  0 \leq  x - 1 \leq  {12} \\  0 \leq  {5x} - 5 \leq  {12} \\  x \in  \mathbf{Z} \end{array}\text{ 或 }\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \left( {x - 1}\right)  + \left( {{5x} - 5}\right)  = {12} \\  0 \leq  x - 1 \leq  {12} \\  0 \leq  {5x} - 5 \leq  {12} \\  x \in  \mathbf{Z} \end{array}\text{ ,即 }\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 1 \\  1 \leq  x \leq  {13} \\  1 \leq  x \leq  \dfrac{17}{5} \\  x \in  \mathbf{Z} \end{array}\right. }\right. }\right.$

$\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = 3 \\  1 \leq  x \leq  {13} \\  1 \leq  x \leq  \dfrac{17}{5},\text{ 所以 }x = 1\text{ 和 }x = 3\text{ 均满足题意,所以该方程的 } \\  x \in  \mathbf{Z} \end{array}\right.$

解为: 1 或 3 . 故答案为: 1 或 3 .

6. 144

【解析】依题意,安排老师甲有 ${{C}}_{2}^{1}$ 种,从除甲外的 9 名老师中任选 2 人并安排值班有 ${{P}}_{9}^{2}$ 种,故不同的值班安排方法种数为 ${{C}}_{2}^{1}{{P}}_{9}^{2} = 2 \times  {72} = {144}$ (种).

7. 24

【解析】语文、数学选一门第一节课上共 ${{C}}_{2}^{1}$ 种,剩余情况使用插空法,共 ${{P}}_{2}^{2} \cdot  {{P}}_{3}^{2}$ 种,故不同的排法种数为 ${{C}}_{2}^{1} \cdot  {{P}}_{2}^{2}$ . ${{P}}_{3}^{2} = {24}.$

8. 18

【解析】三人值班的天数分别为 3、3、2 ，先确定值班 2 天的人，有 3 种选择，再将三个人全排即可，所以，不同的排法种数为 $3{{P}}_{3}^{3} = {18}$ 种. 故答案为: 18 .

9. 288

【解析】先选两位家长排在首尾有 ${{P}}_{4}^{2} = {12}$ 种排法; 再排队中的四人有 ${{P}}_{4}^{4} = {24}$ 种排法,故有 ${12} \times  {24} = {288}$ 种排法. 故答案为: 288 .

10. 41

【解析】若三个编号成等差数列, 若三个编号相同, 共有 9 种,若三个编号彼此都不相同,设这三个编号为 $a,b,c$ ,则 ${2b} = a + c$ ,则 $a,c$ 同为奇数或同为偶数,则 $b$ 可由 ${2b} = a + c$ 来确定, 奇数编号的小球共 5 个, 偶数编号的小球共 4 个, 若 $a,c$ 同为奇数，则 $a,c$ 的选择方法种数为 $2{{C}}_{5}^{2} = {20}$ 种；若 $a$ 、 $c$ 同为偶数,则 $a,c$ 的选择方法种数为 $2{{C}}_{4}^{2} = {12}$ 种. 当三个

编号彼此不同时,不同的摸法种数为 ${20} + {12} = {32}$ 种. 综上所述,不同的摸法种数为 $9 + {32} = {41}$ 种. 故答案为: 41 .

11. 329

【解析】由题意知集合中且至多只有一个奇数, 其余均是偶数. 首先讨论三位数中的偶数,  \ding{172}  当个位为 0 时, 则百位和十位在剩余的 9 个数字中选择两个进行排列, 则这样的偶数有 ${{P}}_{9}^{2} = {72}$ 个； \ding{173}  当个位不为 0 时，则个位有 ${{C}}_{4}^{1}$ 个数字可选， 百位有 ${{C}}_{8}^{1}$ 个数字可选，十位有 ${{C}}_{8}^{1}$ 个数字可选，根据分步计数原理这样的偶数共有 ${{C}}_{4}^{1}{{C}}_{8}^{1}{{C}}_{8}^{1} = {256}$ ; 然后再加上一个三位正奇数，得集合中元素个数的最大值为 ${72} + {256} + 1 = {329}$ 个.

12. 60

【解析】 $N = {{C}}_{5}^{1}{{C}}_{4}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1} = {60}$ .

13. 72

【解析】下面分两种情况,即 $C,A$ 同色与 $C,A$ 不同色来讨论. (1) $P$ 的着色方法有 4 种, $A$ 的着色方法有 3 种, $B$ 的着色方法有 2 种, $C,A$ 同色时, $C$ 的着色方法为 1 种, $D$ 的着色方法有 2 种. ( 2 ) $P$ 的着色方法有 4 种， $A$ 的着色方法有 3 种， $B$ 的着色方法有 2 种. $C$ 与 $A$ 不同色时 $C$ 的着色方法有 1 种, $D$ 的着色方法有 1 种,综上,两类共有 $4 \times  3 \times  2 \times  1 \times  2 + 4 \times  3 \times  2 \times  1 \times  1 = {48} + {24} = {72}$ (种). 故答案为:72.

14. 14

【解析】由函数定义,转化为给一个,2 安排对应的自变量,每一种对应方式，即为一个函数，给一 2 取 3 个自变量，则 2 对应 2 个自 1 个自变量,有 ${{C}}_{4}^{3}$ 种,给一 2 取 2 个自变量,则 2 对应 2 个自变量,有 ${{C}}_{4}^{2}$ 种,给一 2 取 1 个自变量,则 2 对应 3 个自变量,有 ${{C}}_{4}^{1}$ 种，所以由分类加法计数原理知，共有 ${{C}}_{4}^{3} + {{C}}_{4}^{2} + {{C}}_{4}^{1} = {14}$ 种不同的对应方式，故答案为:14.

15. 48

【解析】设 ${b}_{1} < {b}_{2} < {b}_{3} < {b}_{4}$ ,满足 $\left\{  {{a}_{i} + {a}_{j} \mid  1 \leq  i < j \leq  4}\right\}   =  \left\{  {{b}_{i} + {b}_{j} \mid  1 \leq  i < j \leq  4}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{a}_{i} + {a}_{j} \mid  6,{10},{12},{18},{20},{24}}\right\}$ ,则必有 ${b}_{1} + {b}_{2} = 6,{b}_{1} + {b}_{3} = {10},{b}_{2} + {b}_{4} = {20},{b}_{3} + {b}_{4} = {24}$ , \ding{172}  ${b}_{2} +  {b}_{3} = {12},{b}_{1} + {b}_{4} = {18} \Longrightarrow  {b}_{1} = 2,{b}_{2} = 4,{b}_{3} = 8,{b}_{4} = {16}$ , \ding{173}  ${b}_{2} +  {b}_{3} = {18},{b}_{1} + {b}_{4} = {12} \Longrightarrow  {b}_{1} =  - 1,{b}_{2} = 7,{b}_{3} = {11},{b}_{4} = {13}$ ,所以 2 种情况,综上所述共有 $2{{P}}_{4}^{4} = {48}$ 对.

16. 22

【解析】 ${108} = {2}^{2} \times  {3}^{3} = 2 \times  3 \times  3 \times  6 = 3 \times  3 \times  3 \times  4,{144} =  {2}^{4} \times  {3}^{2},{288} = {2}^{5} \times  {3}^{2},{432} = {2}^{4} \times  {3}^{3}$ ,当 ${108} = 2 \times  3 \times  3 \times  6$ 时, 不妨取 ${x}_{1} = 2,{x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} = 3,{x}_{4} = 6$ ,则 ${432} = {2}^{4} \times  {3}^{3} = 3 \times  3 \times  6 \times  8 = 8{x}_{2}{x}_{3}{x}_{4}$ ,此时 ${x}_{5} = 8$ ,而 ${144} = {2}^{4} \times  {3}^{2} = 2 \times  3 \times  3 \times  8,{288} = {2}^{5} \times  {3}^{2} = 2 \times  3 \times  6 \times  8$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} = 2,{x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} =$ 3， ${x}_{4} = 6$ ， ${x}_{5} = 8$ 满足题目条件；当 ${108} = 3 \times  3 \times  3 \times  4$ 时，不妨取 ${x}_{1} = 3,{x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} = 3,{x}_{4} = 4$ ,则 ${432} = {2}^{4} \times  {3}^{3} = 3 \times  3 \times  3 \times  {16} = {16}{x}_{1}{x}_{2}{x}_{3}$ ,此时 ${x}_{5} = {16}$ ,而从 ${x}_{1} = 3,{x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} = 3$ , ${x}_{4} = 4,{x}_{5} = {16}$ 中任取 4 个数相乘不能得出 144,所以 ${x}_{1} =  3,{x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} = 3,{x}_{4} = 4,{x}_{5} = {16}$ 不满足条件,综上所述, ${x}_{1} =$ 2. ${x}_{2} = 3,{x}_{3} = 3,{x}_{4} = 6,{x}_{5} = 8$ ,所以 ${x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + {x}_{3} + {x}_{4} + {x}_{5} =$ 22. 故答案为:22.

17. 1540

【解析】分类讨论: 第 1 类: 当 3 个数结构为 $\left( {a,a + 5,x}\right)$ , 当 3 个数为 $\left( {1,6,x}\right)$ ,则 ${11} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ ,有 20 种; 当 3 个数为 $(2$ , $7,x)$ ,则 ${12} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ ,有 19 种; $\cdots$ ; 当 3 个数为 $\left( {{20},{25},x}\right)$ ,则 ${30} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ ,有 1 种; 所以 $\left( {a,a + 5,x}\right)$ 结构有 $1 + 2 + \cdots  + {20}$ 个，第 2 类:当 3 个数结构为 $\left( {a,a + 6,x}\right)$ ，当 3 个数为 $(1,7$ , $x)$ ,则 ${12} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ ,有 19 种; 当 3 个数为 $\left( {2,8,x}\right)$ ,则 ${13} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ ,有 18 种; ...; 当 3 个数为 $\left( {{20},{26},x}\right)$ ,则 ${30} \leq  x \leq  {30}$ , 有 1 种; 所以 $\left( {a,a + 6,x}\right)$ 结构有 $1 + 2 + \cdots  + {19}$ 个,依此类推: 当 3 数结构为 $\left( {a,a + 7,x}\right)$ 时,有 $1 + 2 + \cdots  + {18}$ 个; 当 3 数结构为 $\left( {a,a + 8,x}\right)$ 时,有 $1 + 2 + \cdots  + {17}$ 个; $\cdots$ ; 当 3 数结构为 $\left( {a,a + {25},x}\right)$ 时,有 1 个; 综上,共有 $1 + \left( {1 + 2}\right)  + (1 +  2 + 3) + \cdots  + \left( {1 + 2 + \cdots  + {20}}\right)  = {1540}.$

18. 13122

【解析】由 ${a}_{n} < {a}_{n + 1} + 3 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} >  - 3$ ,因为 ${a}_{n} \in  \{ 1,2,3,\cdots ,{10}\}$ ,所以 ${a}_{n + 1} - {a}_{n} \geq   - 2$ ,第 1 步: 排 1、2，有 2 个排列; 第 2 步: 排 3,1、2 有 3 个空挡可插; 第 3 步: 排 4,1 之前不能插，2、3有 3 个空挡可插；第 4 步:排 5，1、2 之前不能插，3、4 有 3 个空挡可插；...；第 10 步:排 10，1、2、...，7 之前不能插，8、9 有 3 个空挡可插；由乘法原理，得 $\left\{  {a}_{n}\right\}$ 共有 $2 \times  {3}^{8} = {13122}$ 个.

\section*{二、选择题}

19. C

【解析】由题意可得每位同学有 4 种选择, 根据乘法原理, 共有 ${4}^{3} = {64}$ 种. 故选: C.

20. B

【解析】该校初中部和高中部分别有 500 和 300 名学生, 所以初中部应抽取 ${40} \times  \dfrac{500}{800} = {40} \times  \dfrac{5}{8} = {25}$ 名学生,高中部应抽取 ${40} \times  \dfrac{300}{800} = {40} \times  \dfrac{3}{8} = {15}$ 名学生,所以不同的抽样结果的种数为 ${{C}}_{500}^{25} \cdot  {{C}}_{300}^{15}$ . 故选: B.

21. A

【解析】对于选项 A: 例如 $m = n = 2$ ,则 ${{P}}_{2}^{2} + {{P}}_{2}^{1} = 4,{{P}}_{3}^{2} = 6$ , 即 ${{P}}_{2}^{2} + {{P}}_{2}^{1} \neq  {{P}}_{3}^{2}$ ,故 ${A}$ 错误; 对于选项 ${B}$ : 因为 ${{C}}_{n}^{m}{{P}}_{m}^{m} =  \dfrac{n!}{m! \cdot  \left( {n - m}\right) !} \cdot  m! = \dfrac{n!}{\left( {n - m}\right) !} = {{P}}_{n}^{m}$ ,故 B 正确; 对于选项 C: 因为 $n{{P}}_{n - 1}^{m - 1} = n \cdot  \dfrac{\left( {n - 1}\right) !}{\left( {n - m}\right) !} = \dfrac{n!}{\left( {n - m}\right) !} = {{P}}_{n}^{m}$ ,故 ${C}$ 正确; 对于选项 D: 因为 ${{C}}_{n}^{m} + {{C}}_{n}^{m - 1} = \dfrac{n!}{m! \cdot  \left( {n - m}\right) !} +  \dfrac{n!}{\left( {m - 1}\right) ! \cdot  \left( {n - m + 1}\right) !} = \dfrac{n!}{m! \cdot  \left( {n - m + 1}\right) !}\lbrack \left( {n - m + 1}\right)  +  m\rbrack  = \dfrac{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}{m! \cdot  \left( {n - m + 1}\right) !} = {{C}}_{n + 1}^{m}$ ,所以 ${{C}}_{n}^{m} + {{C}}_{n}^{m - 1} = {{C}}_{n + 1}^{m}$ ,故 ${D}$ 正确；故选: A. 另解: 也可以用特殊值法求解.

\section*{考点 2 二项式定理}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 6

【解析】由 ${\left( x + \dfrac{2}{x}\right) }^{3}$ 的二项展开式得通项为: ${T}_{r + 1} =  {{C}}_{3}^{r}{x}^{3 - r}{\left( \dfrac{2}{x}\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{3}^{r}{2}^{r}{x}^{3 - {2r}},r = 0,1,2,3$ ,使 $3 - {2r} = 1$ ,解得: $r = 1$ ,故 ${\left( x + \dfrac{2}{x}\right) }^{3}$ 的二项展开式中 $x$ 的系数为 ${{C}}_{3}^{1} \times  {2}^{1} = 6$ . 故答案为: 6 .

2. -1

【解析】根据二项式展开式的通项公式 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{n}^{r}{a}^{n - r}{b}^{r}$ ,则 ${\left( 1 - ax\right) }^{6}$ 展开式中 ${x}^{3}$ 项为 ${{C}}_{6}^{3} \times  {1}^{6 - 3} \times  {\left( -ax\right) }^{3}$ ,又 ${{C}}_{6}^{3} =  \dfrac{6!}{3!\left( {6 - 3}\right) !} = {20}$ ,则该项为 ${20} \times  {\left( -a\right) }^{3}{x}^{3}$ ,已知 ${x}^{3}$ 项的系数是 20,则 ${20} \times  {\left( -a\right) }^{3} = {20}$ ,即 ${\left( -a\right) }^{3} = 1$ ,解得 $a =  - 1$ . 故答案为: -1 .

3. 4

【解析】由二项式定理可知, ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{4}$ 的展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{4}^{r}{x}^{4 - r}{\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{4}^{r}{x}^{4 - {2r}}$ ,令 $4 - {2r} = 2$ ,解得 $r = 1$ ,所以 ${T}_{2} = {{C}}_{4}^{1}{x}^{2} = 4{x}^{2}$ ,所以二项式的展开式中含 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数为 4 . 故答案为: 4 .

4. 5

【解析】因为 ${\left( 1 + x\right) }^{n} = {{C}}_{n}^{0} + {{C}}_{n}^{1}x + \cdots  + {{C}}_{n}^{n}{x}^{n}$ ,且各项系数和为 32,令 $x = 1$ ,则 ${2}^{n} = {{C}}_{n}^{0} + {{C}}_{n}^{1} + \cdots  + {{C}}_{n}^{n} = {32}$ ,解得 $n = 5$ , 所以正整数 $n$ 的值为 5 . 故答案为: 5 .

5. 40

【解析】由题知,设 ${\left( x + 2\right) }^{5}$ 展开式中通项为 ${{C}}_{5}^{k} \cdot  {x}^{5 - k}$ . ${2}^{k}$ ,令 $5 - k = 3$ ,则 $k = 2,{{C}}_{5}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{2} = {40}$ . 故答案为: 40 .

6. 35

【解析】二项式 ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{7}$ 展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{7}^{r}{x}^{7 - r}{\left( -1\right) }^{r}  \left( {0 \leq  r \leq  7\text{ 且 }r \in  \mathbf{N}}\right)$ ,令 $7 - r = 3$ ,解得 $r = 4$ ,所以 ${T}_{5} =  {{C}}_{7}^{4}{x}^{3}{\left( -1\right) }^{4} = {35}{x}^{3}$ ,所以二项展开式中 ${x}^{3}$ 的系数为 35 . 故答案为: 35 .

7. 5

【解析】设 ${{C}}_{5}^{r}{\left( {x}^{6}\right) }^{5 - r}{\left( \dfrac{1}{x\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{x}^{{30} - \dfrac{15}{2}r}$ 为常数项,则 ${30} - \dfrac{15}{2}r = 0 \Longrightarrow  r = 4$ ,所以常数项为 ${{C}}_{5}^{4} = 5$ .

8. 10

【解析】因为 ${\left( x + {x}^{2}\right) }^{5} = {x}^{5}{\left( 1 + x\right) }^{5} = {x}^{5}(1 + {5x} + {10}{x}^{2} +  \left. {{10}{x}^{3} + 5{x}^{4} + {x}^{5}}\right)  = {a}_{0}{x}^{5} + {a}_{1}{x}^{6} + {a}_{2}{x}^{7} + {a}_{3}{x}^{8} + {a}_{4}{x}^{9} +  {a}_{5}{x}^{10}$ ,所以 ${a}_{3} = {10}$ . 故答案为: 10 .

9. 15

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( -1\right) }^{6 - r}{x}^{r},r = 4 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{5} = {{C}}_{6}^{4}{\left( -1\right) }^{6 - 4} = {15}$ . 10. -160

【解析】 ${a}_{4} = {{C}}_{6}^{3}{\left( -2\right) }^{3} =  - {160}$ .

11. 80

【解析】由通项公式 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r} \cdot  {2}^{5 - r} \cdot  {x}^{5 - r} \cdot  {\left( -1\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}$ . ${\left( -1\right) }^{r} \cdot  {2}^{5 - r}{x}^{5 - r}$ ,令 $5 - r = 3$ ,得 $r = 2$ ,可得 ${x}^{3}$ 项的系数为 ${{C}}_{5}^{2} \cdot  {\left( -1\right) }^{2} \cdot  {2}^{5 - 2} = {80}$ . 故答案为: 80 .

12. -672

【解析】通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{9}^{r}{\left( \sqrt{x}\right) }^{9 - r}{\left( -\dfrac{2}{x}\right) }^{r} =  {{C}}_{9}^{r}{\left( -2\right) }^{r}{x}^{\dfrac{9 - {3r}}{2}},0 \leq  r \leq  9,r \in  \mathbf{Z}$ ,故当 $r = 3$ 时,常数项为 ${T}_{4} = {{C}}_{9}^{3}{\left( -2\right) }^{3} =  - {672}$ . 故答案为:-672 .

13. 20

【解析】 ${\left( {x}^{2} + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{6}$ 的展开式中第 $k + 1$ 项为 ${T}_{k + 1} =  {{C}}_{6}^{k}{x}^{-k}{x}^{2\left( {6 - k}\right) } = {{C}}_{6}^{k}{x}^{{12} - {3k}}\left( {k = 0,1,2,\cdots ,6}\right)$ ,令 ${12} - {3k} = 3 \Longrightarrow  k = 3$ 得: ${x}^{3}$ 的系数为 ${{C}}_{6}^{3} = {20}$ . 故答案为: 20 .

14. 6

【解析】 ${\left( x - 1\right) }^{4}$ 展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{4}^{r}{x}^{4 - r} \cdot  {\left( -1\right) }^{r}$ , 取 $r = 2$ 得到 ${a}_{2} = {{C}}_{4}^{2} \cdot  {\left( -1\right) }^{2} = 6$ . 故答案为: 6 .

15. 10

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{x}^{5 - r}$ ,令 $r = 3$ ,故 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数是 ${{C}}_{5}^{3} = {10}$ . 16. -160

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( 2x\right) }^{6 - r}{\left( -1\right) }^{r},r = 3 \Longrightarrow  {x}^{3}$ 项的系数为 $- {{C}}_{6}^{3}{2}^{3} =  - {160}.$

17. 270

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{\left( 3{x}^{2}\right) }^{5 - r}{\left( {x}^{-1}\right) }^{r} = {3}^{5 - r} \times  {{C}}_{5}^{r}{x}^{{10} - {3r}},{10} -  {3r} = 4 \Longrightarrow  r = 2 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{3} = {3}^{3} \times  {{C}}_{5}^{2} = {270}.$

18. 160

【解析】因为 ${\left( \sqrt{x} + \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{6}$ 的展开式通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} =  {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( \sqrt{x}\right) }^{6 - r} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{2}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{r} = {2}^{r}{{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{3 - r}$ ,展开式中常数项,必有 3-r $= 0$ ,即 $r = 3$ ,所以展开式中常数项为 ${T}_{4} = {2}^{3}{{C}}_{6}^{3} = 8 \times  {20} =$ 160. 故答案为: 160 .

19. 560

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{7}^{r}{x}^{7 - r}{\left( -2\right) }^{r}{\left( {x}^{-\dfrac{1}{2}}\right) }^{r} \Longrightarrow  7 - \dfrac{3}{2}r = 1 \Longrightarrow  r = 4  \Longrightarrow  {a}_{5} = {{C}}_{7}^{4}{\left( -2\right) }^{4} = {560}$ .

20. -2

【解析】通项公式为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{a}^{5 - r}{x}^{{10} - {2r}} \cdot  {x}^{-r} =  {{C}}_{5}^{r}{a}^{5 - r}{x}^{{10} - {3r}}$ ,令 ${10} - {3r} = 4$ ,解得 $r = 2$ ,故 ${{C}}_{5}^{2}{a}^{3} =  - {80}$ ,解得 $a =  - 2$ . 故答案为: -2 .

21. 10

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{x}^{5 - r}{2}^{r}{x}^{-{2r}},5 - {3r} = 2 \Longrightarrow  r = 1$ ,故 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数为 ${{C}}_{5}^{1} \times  2 = {10}$ .

22. 21

【解析】 ${x}^{7} = {\left\lbrack  \left( x - 1\right)  + 1\right\rbrack  }^{7}$ ,通项 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{7}^{r}{\left( x - 1\right) }^{r}$ ,令 $r = 5$ ,得 ${a}_{5} = {{C}}_{7}^{5} = {21}$ .

23. -4320

【解析】由题意知 ${\left( 3 - 2x\right) }^{6}$ 的展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} =  {{C}}_{6}^{r}{3}^{6 - r}{\left( -2x\right) }^{r},r = 0,1,2,\cdots ,6$ ,故展开式中 ${x}^{3}$ 项的系数为 ${{C}}_{6}^{3}{3}^{6 - 3}{\left( -2\right) }^{3} =  - {4320}$ ,故答案为; -4320 .

24. 4

【解析】 ${\left( 1 - 2x\right) }^{n}$ 展开式的通项公式为: ${{C}}_{n}^{r}{\left( -2x\right) }^{r}$ ,分别令 $r = 0,r = 4$ ,所以 ${a}_{0} = 1,{a}_{4} = {16}{{C}}_{n}^{4}$ ,则 ${a}_{0} + {a}_{4} = {17}$ ,即 $1 +  {16}{{C}}_{n}^{4} = {17}$ ,解得: $n = 4$ . 故答案为: 4 .

25. 45

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{10}^{r}{x}^{r},r = 2$ ,则 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数为 ${{C}}_{10}^{2} = {45}$ .

26. 160

【解析】展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( 2x\right) }^{6 - r}{\left( {x}^{-1}\right) }^{r}$ ,令 6- $r - r = 0 \Longrightarrow  r = 3$ ,所以常数项为 ${{C}}_{6}^{3}{2}^{3} = {160}$ . 故答案为: 160 .

27. 2

【解析】 ${\left( ax + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{5}$ 展开式中 $x$ 的项为 ${{C}}_{5}^{2}{\left( ax\right) }^{3} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{2} =  {10}{a}^{3}x$ ,依题意, ${10}{a}^{3} = {80}$ ,所以 $a = 2$ . 故答案为: 2 .

28. 60

【解析】二项式 ${\left( 2x - \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{6}$ 的展开式的通项公式为: ${T}_{r + 1}$

$= {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( 2x\right) }^{6 - r}{\left( -\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{r} = {\left( -1\right) }^{r}{{C}}_{6}^{r}{2}^{6 - r}{x}^{6 - \dfrac{3}{2}r}$ ,令 $6 - \dfrac{3}{2}r =$ 0,解得 $r = 4$ ,所以二项式的展开式中的常数项为 ${\left( -1\right) }^{4}{{C}}_{6}^{4}{2}^{2}  = {60}$ . 故答案为:60.

29. 135

【解析】 ${\left( 3x - 1\right) }^{6}$ 的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( 3x\right) }^{6 - r}{\left( -1\right) }^{r} =  {\left( -1\right) }^{r} \times  {3}^{6 - r}{{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - r}$ ,使 $6 - r = 2$ ,解得 $r = 4$ ,即得 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数为 ${3}^{2}{{C}}_{6}^{4} = 9 \times  {15} = {135}$ . 故答案为: 135 .

30. 20

【解析】 ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{6}$ 的二项展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} =  {{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - r}{x}^{-r} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - {2r}}$ ,令 $6 - {2r} = 0$ ,得 $r = 3$ ,故 ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{6}$ 的二项展开式的常数项为 ${{C}}_{6}^{3} = {20}$ .

31. 28

【解析】在 ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{8}$ 的二项展开式中,含 ${x}^{4}$ 的项为 ${{C}}_{8}^{2}{x}^{6}$ . ${\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{2} = {28}{x}^{4}$ ,故 ${x}^{4}$ 项的系数为 28 .

32. -20

【解析】由题 ${\left( x - \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{6}$ 的二项展开式的第 $r + 1$ 项为 ${{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - r} \cdot  {\left( -1\right) }^{r} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{r} = {\left( -1\right) }^{r} \cdot  {{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - {2r}}$ . 令 $6 - {2r} = 0 \Longrightarrow  r = 3$ ,则常数项为 ${\left( -1\right) }^{3} \cdot  {{C}}_{6}^{3} =  - {20}$ . 故答案为: -20 .

33. 10

【解析】令 $x = 1$ ,则 ${2}^{n} = {32}$ ,解得 $n = 5$ ,故 ${\left( x + 1\right) }^{5}$ 的展开式通项公式为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r} \cdot  {x}^{5 - r}$ ,令 $5 - r = 2$ ,则 $r = 3$ ,得 ${T}_{4} =  {{C}}_{5}^{3}{x}^{2} = {10}{x}^{2}$ ,故答案为: 10 .

34. 1

【解析】 ${T}_{4} = {{C}}_{6}^{3}{x}^{3}{a}^{3}{x}^{-3} \Longrightarrow  {{C}}_{6}^{3}{a}^{3} = {20} \Longrightarrow  a = 1$ .

35. 60

【解析】由题意得二项式的展开式的通项公式为 ${T}_{r + 1} =  {{C}}_{6}^{r} \cdot  {x}^{6 - r} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{2}{x}\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{6}^{r} \cdot  {2}^{r}{x}^{6 - {2r}}$ ,令 $6 - {2r} = 2$ ,得 $r = 2$ ,所以 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数为 ${{C}}_{6}^{2} \cdot  {2}^{2} = {60}$ . 故答案为: 60 .

36. 15

【解析】二项展开式通项为: ${{C}}_{6}^{r} \cdot  {x}^{6 - r} \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{x}}\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}$ . ${x}^{6 - r} \cdot  {x}^{-\dfrac{r}{2}} = {{C}}_{6}^{r} \cdot  {x}^{6 - \dfrac{3r}{2}}$ ,当 $6 - \dfrac{3r}{2} = 0$ 时, $r = 4$ 所以常数项为: ${{C}}_{6}^{4} = {15}$ ,答案: 15 .

37. 2

【解析】 ${x}^{3}$ 项为 $1 \times  {{C}}_{6}^{3} \cdot  {1}^{3} \cdot  {\left( -x\right) }^{3} + \dfrac{t}{x} \times  {{C}}_{6}^{4} \cdot  {1}^{2} \cdot  {\left( -x\right) }^{4} =  \left( {-{20} + {15t}}\right) {x}^{3}$ ,由 $- {20} + {15t} = {10} \Longrightarrow  t = 2$ . 故答案为: 2 .

38. 1023

【解析】因为 ${\left( 1 + x\right) }^{n} = {a}_{0} + {a}_{1}x + {a}_{2}{x}^{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{n}{x}^{n}(n \geq  1$ , $n \in  \mathbf{N})$ ,且 ${a}_{5} > {a}_{4},{a}_{5} > {a}_{6}$ ,所以 ${a}_{5}$ 是二项式系数最大的项,则 $n = {10}$ ,令 $x = 0$ ,则 $1 = {a}_{0}$ ,令 $x = 1$ ,则 ${2}^{10} = {a}_{0} + {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{10}$ , 则 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{n}{a}_{i} = {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{10} = {2}^{10} - 1 = {1023}$ . 故答案为: 1023.

39. 210

【解析】因为 ${\left( {x}^{3} + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{n}$ 的二项展开式中各项系数和为 1024,即 ${\left( 1 + 1\right) }^{n} = {1024}$ ,故 $n = {10}$ . 设 ${\left( {x}^{3} + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{10}$ 的二项展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1}$ ,则 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{10}^{r}{x}^{-{2r}} \cdot  {x}^{3\left( {{10} - r}\right) } =  {{C}}_{10}^{r}{x}^{{30} - {5r}}$ ,令 ${30} - {5r} = 0$ ,得 $r = 6$ ,故展开式中常数项的值为 ${{C}}_{10}^{6} = {{C}}_{10}^{4} = \dfrac{{10} \times  9 \times  8 \times  7}{4 \times  3 \times  2 \times  1} = {210}$ . 故答案为:210.

40. -512

【解析】令 $x = 1$ ,得 $0 = {a}_{0} + {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{10}$  \ding{172} ,令 $x =  - 1$ , ${1024} = {a}_{0} - {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} - \cdots  + {a}_{10}$  \ding{173} , \ding{172} - \ding{173} 得: $- {1024} = 2{a}_{1} +  2{a}_{3} + \cdots  + 2{a}_{9}$ ,所以 ${a}_{1} + {a}_{3} + \cdots  + {a}_{9} =  - {512}$ .

41. 10

【解析】 ${x}^{2}$ 项的系数是常数项的 5 倍,即 ${{C}}_{n}^{2} \times  {3}^{n - 2} =  5{{C}}_{n}^{0}{3}^{n} \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2} = 5 \times  9,n = {10}$ ,故答案为 10 .

42. -84

【解析】方法一: ${{C}}_{9}^{6}{\left( -1\right) }^{3} =  - {84}$ . 方法二: ${T}_{r + 1} =$

${{C}}_{9}^{r}{\left( x\right) }^{9 - r}{\left( -\dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{r},9 - r - r = 3 \Longrightarrow  r = 3$ ,系数为 ${{C}}_{9}^{3}{\left( -1\right) }^{3} =$ -84 .

43. 1

【解析】 ${\left( x + a\right) }^{5} \Longrightarrow  {T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{a}^{5 - r}{x}^{r},r = 2,{{C}}_{5}^{2}{a}^{3} = {10} \Longrightarrow  a = 1$ . 44. -252

【解析】根据二项式定理,第 $r + 1$ 项为 ${{C}}_{10}^{r}{x}^{{10} - r} \cdot  {\left( -\dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{r}$ , 由于是常数, $r = {10} - r,r = 5$ ,其常数项系数为 $- {{C}}_{10}^{5} =  - {252}$ ,故答案为:-252.

45. 65

【解析】令 $x = 0$ ,则 ${a}_{0} = {2}^{4} = {16}$ ,再令 $x = 1$ ,则 ${a}_{0} + {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} +  {a}_{3} + {a}_{4} = {3}^{4} = {81} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + {a}_{3} + {a}_{4} = {81} - {16} = {65}.$

46. ${240}{x}^{4}$

【解析】 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{\left( 2x\right) }^{r} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{2}^{r}{x}^{r},\left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {{C}}_{6}^{r}{2}^{r} \geq  {{C}}_{6}^{r - 1}{2}^{r - 1} \\  {{C}}_{6}^{r}{2}^{r} \geq  {{C}}_{6}^{r + 1}{2}^{r + 1} \end{array} \Longrightarrow  r = }\right.$ 4, ${T}_{4 + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{4}{2}^{4}{x}^{4} = {240}{x}^{4}$ .

47. -8

【解析】因为 ${x}^{4} = {\left\lbrack  -2 + \left( x + 2\right) \right\rbrack  }^{4} = {{C}}_{4}^{0}{\left( -2\right) }^{4}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{0} +  {{C}}_{4}^{1}{\left( -2\right) }^{3}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{1} + {{C}}_{4}^{2}{\left( -2\right) }^{2} \cdot  {\left( x + 2\right) }^{2} + {{C}}_{4}^{3}\left( {-2}\right) {\left( x + 2\right) }^{3} +  {{C}}_{4}^{4}{\left( -2\right) }^{0}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{4}$ ,且有 ${x}^{4} = {a}_{0} + {a}_{1}\left( {x + 2}\right)  + {a}_{2}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{2} +  {a}_{3}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{3} + {a}_{4}{\left( x + 2\right) }^{4}$ ,所以 ${a}_{3} = {{C}}_{4}^{3}\left( {-2}\right)  =  - 8$ ,故答案为: -8 .

48. 17

【解析】 $x = 0 \Longrightarrow  {a}_{0} = 1,{a}_{4} = {{C}}_{4}^{4}{\left( -2\right) }^{4} = {16} \Longrightarrow  {a}_{0} + {a}_{4} = {17}$ . 49. ${3}^{10}$

【解析】令 $x = 2$ ,则 ${\left( 2 + 1\right) }^{10} = {a}_{0} + {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{10}$ ,即 ${a}_{0} + {a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{10} = {3}^{10}$ ,故答案为: ${3}^{10}$ .

50. 1

【解析】令 $x = 0$ 可得 ${a}_{0} = 1$ ,又 ${\left( 1 - x\right) }^{5}$ 展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{5}^{r}{\left( -x\right) }^{r}$ ,令 $r = 3$ 可得 ${{C}}_{5}^{3}{\left( -1\right) }^{3} =  - {10}$ ; 令 $r = 2$ ,可得 ${{C}}_{5}^{2}{\left( -1\right) }^{2} = {10}$ ,所以 ${a}_{3} = 1 \times  \left( {-{10}}\right)  + 1 \times  {10} = 0$ ,所以 ${a}_{0} + {a}_{3} = 1$ ,故答案为: 1 .

51. -5

【解析】 ${\left( x - y\right) }^{6}$ 展开式的通项为 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{6}^{r}{x}^{6 - r}{\left( -y\right) }^{r},r \in  \{ 0,1,2,3,4,5,6\}$ ,所以含 ${x}^{4}{y}^{3}$ 的项为 $x{{C}}_{6}^{3}{x}^{3}{\left( -y\right) }^{3} + y{{C}}_{6}^{2}{x}^{4}  {\left( -y\right) }^{2} =  - 5{x}^{4}{y}^{3}$ ,即 ${x}^{4}{y}^{3}$ 项的系数为 -5 . 故答案为: -5 .

52. 49

【解析】 ${a}_{k} = {{C}}_{100}^{k}{2023}^{k} + {\left( -1\right) }^{k}{{C}}_{100}^{k}{2023}^{{100} - k} < 0$ ,依题意 $k$ 为奇数，所以 ${2023}^{k} < {2023}^{{100} - k},k < {100} - k \Longrightarrow  k < {50} \Longrightarrow  {k}_{\max } = {49}$ .

53. 8

【解析】令 $x =  - 1,y = 2$ ,则 ${\left( {10} \times  \left( -1\right)  + 6 \times  2\right) }^{3} =  - a + {2b} - {4c} + {8d}$ ,所以 $- a + {2b} - {4c} + {8d} = {2}^{3} = 8$ ,故答案为:8.

54. 40

【解析】 $f\left( {3,0}\right)$ 为 ${x}^{3}$ 的系数, ${{C}}_{5}^{3}{{C}}_{3}^{0} = {10},f\left( {2,1}\right)$ 为 ${x}^{2}y$ 的系数, ${{C}}_{5}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{1} = {30},f\left( {3,0}\right)  + f\left( {2,1}\right)  = {40}$ .

55. 30

【解析】 ${a}_{1} = {{C}}_{m}^{1} + {{C}}_{n}^{1} = m + n = {12},{a}_{2} = {{C}}_{m}^{2} + {{C}}_{n}^{2} =  \dfrac{m\left( {m - 1}\right) }{2} + \dfrac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{{m}^{2} + {n}^{2} - \left( {m + n}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{{m}^{2} + {n}^{2} - {12}}{2} =  \dfrac{{m}^{2} + {n}^{2}}{2} - 6 = \dfrac{{\left( m + n\right) }^{2} - {2mn}}{2} - 6 = \dfrac{{12}^{2} - {2mn}}{2} - 6 = {66} -  {mn}$ ,因为 $m + n = {12} \geq  2\sqrt{mn}$ ,所以 ${mn} \leq  {36}$ ,当且仅当 $m =  n = 6$ 时等号成立,所以 ${a}_{2} = {66} - {mn} \geq  {30},{a}_{2}$ 的最小值为 30，故答案为:30.

\section*{二、选择题}

56. C

【解析】 ${\left( x - \dfrac{2}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{n}$ 中 ${T}_{r + 1} = {{C}}_{n}^{r}{x}^{n - r} \times  {\left( -2\right) }^{r}{x}^{-{2r}} =  {\left( -2\right) }^{r}{{C}}_{n}^{r}{x}^{n - {3r}},{\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{n}$ 中 ${T}_{k + 1} = {{C}}_{n}^{k}{x}^{n - k}{x}^{-k} = {{C}}_{n}^{k}{x}^{n - {2k}},$ 当 $n = 3$ 时 ${\left( x - \dfrac{2}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{n}$ 中含常数项,当 $n = 4,6$ 时 ${\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{n}$ 中含常数项, ${\left( x - \dfrac{2}{{x}^{2}}\right) }^{n} + {\left( x + \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{n}\left( {2 \leq  n \leq  {10},n \in  \mathbf{N}}\right)$ 的展开式中存在常数项,则 $n$ 的取值不可能是 5,故选 C.

1. 6216

【解析】设该果园的苹果产量预估值为 $x$ ,则 $\dfrac{{28} \times  {20}}{x} = \dfrac{20}{222}  \Longrightarrow  x = {222} \times  {28} = {6216}$ . 故答案为: 6216 .

2. 24

【解析】由表格中的数据可知, $\bar{x} = \dfrac{-1 + {10} + {13} + {18}}{4} = {10}$ , $y = \dfrac{{62} + {38} + {34} + m}{4} = \dfrac{m + {134}}{4}$ ,所以,样本中心点的坐标为 $\left( {{10},\dfrac{m + {134}}{4}}\right)$ ,将样本中心点的坐标代入回归直线方程可得 $- 2 \times  {10} + {59.5} = \dfrac{m + {134}}{4}$ ,解得 $m = {24}$ . 故答案为: 24 .

3. 56

【解析】数据 ${a}_{n}$ 从小到大排列:26,26,26,28,29,29,29, 31,31,33,34,34,34,35,36,38,38,40,41,41,44,53,56,60, 63,65,70,74,80,86,这组数据的第 75 百分位数为 $N = {30} \times  {75}\%  = {22.5},{a}_{23} = {56}.$

4. 14

【解析】由题意可知,第一支为 01,以后依次为17,09,08, 06,14,所以第 6 支水笔的编号为 14 . 故答案为: 14 .

5. B

【解析】从放有两个红球、一个白球的袋子中一次任意取出两个球的所有可能结果为 ${AB},{AC},{BC}$ ,所以它的一个样本空间为 $\{ {AB},{AC},{BC}\}$ . 故选: B.

6. D

【解析】对选项 A:高二和高三年级获奖同学共 120- ${120} \times  \dfrac{6}{24} = {90}$ ,错误; 对选项 B:不能确定银奖和铜奖的人数,错误; 对选项 C: 全奖人数为 ${120} \times  \dfrac{4}{24} = {20}$ ,银奖和铜奖的人数和为 100 人，故获奖同学中金奖所占比例不可能最高，错误；对选项 D:高一年级获奖人数为 30，金奖人数为 20 , 故获金奖的同学可能都在高一年级, 正确; 故选: D.

1. 21.5

【解析】将这 6 个数从小到大排列为16,18,21,22,22,31, 因数据有偶数个，则中位数是中间两个数的平均数，故中位数为 $\dfrac{{21} + {22}}{2} = {21.5}$ . 故答案为:21.5 .

2. 48
