(3)甲已经连胜两局，接下来甲获胜的情况有以下几种:第三局甲胜,此时比赛结束,甲获胜,这种情况的概率为 $\dfrac{2}{5}$ ,第三局乙胜,第四局甲胜,此时甲获胜,概率为 $\dfrac{2}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} = \dfrac{6}{25}$ ,第三局乙胜,第四局乙胜,第五局甲胜,概率为 $\dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} =  \dfrac{18}{125}$ ,所以甲获胜的总概率为 $\dfrac{2}{5} + \dfrac{6}{25} + \dfrac{18}{125} = \dfrac{98}{125} = {0.784}$ ,乙获胜的总概率为 $1 - \dfrac{98}{125} = \dfrac{27}{125} = {0.216}$ ,奖金共 10 万元,甲应得奖金为 ${10} \times  \dfrac{98}{125} = \dfrac{196}{25} = {7.84}$ 万元,乙应得奖金为 10 - ${7.84} = {2.16}$ 万元.

24.(1)600 名样本中睡眠时长大于等于 6 小时的频率为 $\dfrac{{270} + {180}}{600} = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,该市所有高三学生日均睡眠时长大于等于 6 小时的人数约为 $\dfrac{3}{4} \times  {66000} = {49500}$ 人;

(2)先求出各区间的中点值分别为:5、7、9，估计该市所有高三学生日均睡眠时长为 $\dfrac{{150} \times  5 + {270} \times  7 + {180} \times  9}{600} = \mathbf{7.1}$ 小时;

(3)因为从这 600 名学生中利用分层抽样的方法抽取 20 人，抽样比例为 $\dfrac{20}{600} = \dfrac{1}{30}$ ，所以睡眠时长在 $\lbrack 4,6)$ 抽取 150  \textbackslash <td >> 49 $\dfrac{1}{30} = 5\left( \text{ 人 }\right) ,$ 睡眠时长在 $\lbrack 6,8)$ 抽取 ${270} \times  \dfrac{1}{30} = 9\left( \text{ 人 }\right) ,$ 睡眠时长在 $\left\lbrack  {8,{10}}\right\rbrack$ 抽取 ${180} \times  \dfrac{1}{30} = 6\left( {A}\right)$ ,再从这 20 人中随机抽取 4 人，可能的情况有 ${{C}}_{20}^{4} = {4845}$ 种，设 $A$ 表示事件“这 4 人中既有睡眠充足，又有睡眠良好，也有睡眠不足学生”， $A$ 所包含的样本点有 ${{C}}_{5}^{2}{{C}}_{6}^{1}{{C}}_{9}^{1} + {{C}}_{5}^{1}{{C}}_{6}^{2}{{C}}_{9}^{1} + {{C}}_{5}^{1}{{C}}_{6}^{1}{{C}}_{9}^{2} = {2295}$ 个,故 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{2295}{4845} = \dfrac{9}{19}.$

25.(1)甲掷一次，两颗骰子点数相等的概率为 $\dfrac{6}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{1}{6}$ ， 所以两颗骰子点数不同的概率为 $1 - \dfrac{1}{6} = \dfrac{5}{6}$ ;

(2)甲的点数和恰好比乙的点数和大 7 点的情形如下表: 所以 $P = \dfrac{4 \times  1 + 3 \times  2 + 2 \times  3 + 1 \times  4}{{6}^{4}} = \dfrac{5}{324}$ .

\begin{center}
\adjustbox{width=\textwidth}{
\begin{tabular}{|c|c|c|c|}
\hline
甲的点数 & 乙的点数 & 甲的点数和 & 乙的点数和 \\
\cline{1-4}
(3,6)、(4,5)、(5,4)、 (6,3) & (1,1) & 9 & 2 \\
\cline{1-4}
(4,6)、(5,5)、(6,4) & (1,2)(2,1) & 10 & 3 \\
\cline{1-4}
(5,6)、(6,5) & (1,3)、(2,2)、(3,1) & 11 & 4 \\
\cline{1-4}
(6,6) & (1,4)、(2,3)、(3,2)、 (4,1) & 12 & 5 \\
\cline{1-4}
\hline
\end{tabular}
}
\end{center}

另解: 设掷一次两颗骰子的点数和为 $X$ ,则 $X = 2,3,4,\cdots$ , 12. 则 $P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = P\left( {X = {12}}\right)  = \dfrac{1 \times  1}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{1}{36};P\left( {X = 3}\right)  =  P\left( {X = {11}}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{1}{18};P\left( {X = 4}\right)  = P\left( {X = {10}}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{6 \times  6} =  \dfrac{1}{12};P\left( {X = 5}\right)  = P\left( {X = 9}\right)  = \dfrac{4}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{1}{9};P\left( {X = 6}\right)  = P(X =  8) = \dfrac{5}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{5}{36};P\left( {X = 7}\right)  = \dfrac{6}{6 \times  6} = \dfrac{1}{6}$ . 所以甲的点数和恰好比乙的点数和大 7 点的概率为 $\dfrac{1}{36} \times  \dfrac{1}{9} + \dfrac{1}{18} \times  \dfrac{1}{12} + \dfrac{1}{12} \times  \dfrac{1}{18} + \dfrac{1}{9} \times  \dfrac{1}{36} = \dfrac{5}{324}$

(3)由(2)可知掷两颗骰子点数和大于 6 的概率为 $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 7}}^{{12}}P(X =  i) = \dfrac{1}{6} + \dfrac{5}{36} + \dfrac{1}{9} + \dfrac{1}{12} + \dfrac{1}{18} + \dfrac{1}{36} = \dfrac{7}{12}$ . 若甲第一轮获胜,概率为 ${P}_{1} = \dfrac{7}{12}$ ; 若甲第二轮获胜,即第一轮投掷后两人的点数和都不大于 6,概率为 ${P}_{2} = {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{7}{12}$ ; 若甲第三轮获胜,即前两轮投掷后两人的点数和都不大于 6,概率为 ${P}_{3} = {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{4} \times  \dfrac{7}{12}\cdots$ ; 由以上可得,若甲第 $n\left( {n \geq  1}\right)$ 轮获胜,即前 $n - 1$ 轮投掷后两人的点数和都不大于 6,概率为 ${P}_{n} = {\left\lbrack  {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2}\right\rbrack  }^{n - 1} \times  \dfrac{7}{12} = {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{{2n} - 2} \times  \dfrac{7}{12}$ ; 于是, ${P}_{1},{P}_{2},{P}_{3},\cdots ,{P}_{n},\cdots$ 组成一个以 $\dfrac{7}{12}$ 为首项, ${\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2}$ 为公比的无穷等比数列. 因为 ${P}_{1} +  {P}_{2} + {P}_{3} + \cdots  + {P}_{n} = \dfrac{\dfrac{7}{12} \times  \left\lbrack  {1 - {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2n}}\right\rbrack  }{1 - {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2}}$ ,所以甲最终获胜的总概率为 ${P}_{1} + {P}_{2} + {P}_{3} + \cdots  + {P}_{n} + \cdots  = \dfrac{\dfrac{7}{12}}{1 - {\left( \dfrac{5}{12}\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{12}{17}$ .

26.(1)设学校高中三个年级一共有 $N$ 个学生，因为采用分层抽样法抽取一个容量为 100 的样本，在高一年级抽取了 35 人，高二年级抽取了 33 人，所以高三抽取的人数为: 100 - ${35} - {33} = {32}$ 人，又因为高三年级一共 512 人，所以有: $\dfrac{32}{512} =  \dfrac{100}{N}$ ,解得 $N = {1600}$ . 所以学校高中三个年级一共有 1600 个学生;

(2)因为抽取 100 名学生的样本极差为 $2,x < {10}$ ，所以 10- $x = 2 \Longrightarrow  x = 8,n = {100} - {32} - {13} - {11} - 4 = {40}$ . 又因为 ${100} \times  {0.4} = {40}$ ,所以样本的第 40 百分位数为: $\dfrac{8 + {8.5}}{2} = {8.25}$ ;

(3)因为 100 名学生的样本中随机抽取三个学生的总情况数为: ${{C}}_{100}^{3} = {161700}$ . 其中至少有 1 个数据来自高三学生的情况为: ${{C}}_{32}^{1} \cdot  {{C}}_{68}^{2} + {{C}}_{32}^{2} \cdot  {{C}}_{68}^{1} + {{C}}_{32}^{3} = {111584}$ ,所以至少有 1 个数据来自高三学生的概率为:

$P = \dfrac{{{C}}_{32}^{1} \cdot  {{C}}_{68}^{2} + {{C}}_{32}^{2} \cdot  {{C}}_{68}^{1} + {{C}}_{32}^{3}}{{{C}}_{100}^{3}} = \dfrac{111584}{161700} = \dfrac{2536}{3675}$ .

27.(1)由条件得,指数 $i = {50} \times  {60}\%  = {30}$ ，则这 50 人年龄的第 60 百分位数是将他们的年龄按从小到大的顺序排列后的第 30 人与第 31 人的年龄平均值，由茎叶图可知，第 30 人的年龄为 44，第 31 人的年龄为 45，则所求的第 60 百分位数是 44.5;

(2)由茎叶图可知，年龄在 $\lbrack {50},{59})$ 的被调查者共 9 人，其中 6 名男性，3 名女性，令 $A$ 为至少有三人投赞成票，依题意得, 被选中的 4 人中有两名女性一名男性投赞成票的概率是 $\dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{5}$ ,被选中的 4 人中有一名女性两名男性投赞成票的概率是 $\dfrac{{{C}}_{1}^{1}{{C}}_{2}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{2}} = \dfrac{2}{15}$ ,被选中的 4 人中有两名女性两名男性投赞成票的概率是 $\dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}{{C}}_{3}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{15}$ ,则被选中的 4 人中至少有 3 人投赞成票的概率为 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{1}{5} + \dfrac{2}{15} + \dfrac{1}{15} = \dfrac{2}{5}$ .

\section*{考点 7 条件概率}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\dfrac{2}{5}$

【解析】事件 $A$ (数字之和大于 5 )的基本事件数 (数字组合 $\left( {2,4}\right) ,\left( {3,3}\right) ,\left( {3,4}\right) ,\left( {4,4}\right) )$ ,共有 $2 \times  2 + {{C}}_{2}^{2} + 2 \times  2 + {{C}}_{2}^{2} =  4 + 1 + 4 + 1 = {10}$ 种; 而事件 $A \cap  B$ (最小数字是 2 且和大于 5,即 $\left( {2,4}\right)$ ) 的基本事件数有 $2 \times  2 = 4$ 种,由条件概率公式 $P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{\left| A \cap  B\right| }{\left| A\right| } = \dfrac{4}{10} = \dfrac{2}{5}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{2}{5}$ .

2. $\dfrac{1}{2}$

【解析】第一次没有摸到黑球,则还剩下一个黑球,一个不是黑球,共两个球,所以 $P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{1}{2}$ .

3. $\dfrac{4}{5}$

【解析】 $P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( A\right) } = \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{1}{{P}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1} - {{C}}_{2}^{1}{{C}}_{2}^{1}} = \dfrac{4}{5}$ . 4. $\dfrac{7}{15}$

【解析】由题意 $P\left( B\right)  = P\left( {B \cap  A}\right)  + P\left( {B \cap  \bar{A}}\right)  = P\left( A\right) P(B \mid  A) + P\left( {B \cap  \bar{A}}\right)$ ,所以 $P\left( {B \cap  \bar{A}}\right)  = P\left( B\right)  - P\left( A\right) P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{4} - \dfrac{1}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} = \dfrac{7}{60}$ ,所以 $P\left( {\bar{A} \mid  B}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {B \cap  \bar{A}}\right) }{P\left( B\right) } = \dfrac{\dfrac{7}{60}}{\dfrac{1}{4}} = \dfrac{7}{15}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{7}{15}$ .

5. $\dfrac{b}{a + b}$

【解析】由题意可设 ${A}_{1}$ 表示 (第一次取得红球), ${B}_{1}$ 表示 \{第一次取得白球\}, ${A}_{2}$ 表示\{第二次取得红球\}, ${B}_{2}$ 表示\{第二次取得白球 \},易知 $P\left( {A}_{1}\right)  = \dfrac{a}{a + b},P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  = \dfrac{b}{a + b},P\left( {{B}_{2} \mid  }\right.  \left. {A}_{1}\right)  = \dfrac{b}{a + b + c},P\left( {{B}_{2} \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  = \dfrac{b + c}{a + b + c}$ ,所以 $P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  = P\left( {A}_{1}\right)  P\left( {{B}_{2} \mid  {A}_{1}}\right)  + P\left( {B}_{1}\right) P\left( {{B}_{2} \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  = \dfrac{b}{a + b}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{b}{a + b}$ . 6. $\dfrac{3}{4}$

【解析】 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2} + {{C}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{5}^{2}} = \dfrac{4}{10} = \dfrac{2}{5},P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{5}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{10}$ . 由条件概率公式得 $P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( A\right) } = \dfrac{\dfrac{3}{10}}{\dfrac{2}{5}} = \dfrac{3}{4}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{3}{4}$ .

7. 0.91

【解析】设事件 ${B}_{i}$ 表示一批产品中有 $i$ 个次品 $(i = 0,1$ , 2),则 $P\left( {B}_{0}\right)  = {0.3},P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  = {0.5},P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  = {0.2}$ ,设事件 $A$ 表示这批产品通过检查,即抽样检查的 10 个产品都是合格品,则 $P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{0}}\right)  = 1,P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{99}^{10}}{{{C}}_{100}^{10}} = \dfrac{9}{10},P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right)  =  \dfrac{{{C}}_{98}^{10}}{{{C}}_{100}^{10}} = \dfrac{89}{110}$ ,所以 $P\left( A\right)  = P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{0}}\right) P\left( {B}_{0}\right)  + P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right) P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  +  P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right) P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  = 1 \times  {0.3} + \dfrac{9}{10} \times  {0.5} + \dfrac{89}{110} \times  {0.2} \approx  {0.91}$ . 故答案为:0.91 .

\section*{二、解答题}

8. (1) $\bar{x} = \dfrac{4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + 8}{5} = 6,\bar{y} = \dfrac{{76} + {73} + {67} + m + {60}}{5} =  \dfrac{m + {276}}{5}$ ,代入回归方程 $y =  - {4.3x} + {93.8}$ ,得 $\dfrac{m + {276}}{5} =  - {4.3} \times  6 + {93.8}$ ,解得 $m = {64}$ . 当 $x = {10}$ 时, $y =  - {4.3} \times  {10} + {93.8} = {50.8}$ ,即开放所有体验类项目时的平均等待时间约为 51 分钟;

(2)记事件 $A$ 为“等待总时间恰为 120 分钟”，事件 $B$ 为“选择的 3 个项目中至少包含 1 个互动类项目”, 因为全部的项目数为 15 个,其中互动类项目有 3 个,则事件 $B$ 共包含了 ${{C}}_{15}^{3} - {{C}}_{12}^{3}$ 种; 在事件 $B$ 的条件下,等待总时间恰为 120 分钟, 此时的可能情况有: \ding{172}  一个互动类项目，一个体验类项目， 一个演出类项目，此时共有 ${{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{8}^{1}{{C}}_{4}^{1}$ 种情况； \ding{173}  两个互动类项目,一个体验类项目,此时共有 ${{C}}_{3}^{2}{{C}}_{8}^{1}$ 种情况. 由条件概率公式得: $P\left( {A \mid  B}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( B\right) } = \dfrac{\left| A \cap  B\right| }{\left| B\right| } = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}{{C}}_{8}^{1} + {{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{4}^{1}{{C}}_{8}^{1}}{{{C}}_{15}^{3} - {{C}}_{12}^{3}}  = \dfrac{24}{47};$

(3)设小王参加第二关获得的游园币数为随机变量 $X$ ,则 $X$ 所有可能取值为-20,10,40,则 $P\left( {X =  - {20}}\right)  = {\left( 1 - p\right) }^{2}$ , $P\left( {X = {10}}\right)  = {{C}}_{2}^{1}p\left( {1 - p}\right) ,P\left( {X = {40}}\right)  = {p}^{2}$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   =  {40} \cdot  {p}^{2} + {10} \cdot  {{C}}_{2}^{1}p\left( {1 - p}\right)  + \left( {-{20}}\right)  \cdot  {\left( 1 - p\right) }^{2} = {60p} - {20}.$ 所以,当 $0 \leq  p < \dfrac{1}{3}$ 时, $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   < 0$ ,不建议小王继续闯关; 当 $p = \dfrac{1}{3}$ 时， $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0$ ，小王可根据自己的情况随机选择；当 $\dfrac{1}{3} < p \leq  1$ 时， $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   > 0$ ，建议小王继续闯关.

9. ( 1 )由频率分布直方图可得( 2 a +0.01 × 2 +0.015 × 2 ) $\times  {10} = 1$ ,解得: $a = {0.025}$ ,又 “青年人”占比为 $\left( {{0.015} + {0.025}}\right)$ X10=0.4,所以所抽取的“青年人”人数为 ${200} \times  {0.4} =$ 80 人；

(2) \ding{172}  先将 10 名参会者进行编号:1、2、...、10，并将 10 个号码写在完全相同的纸片上，放入某容器中充分混合均匀， 再取出 2 张, 2 张纸片上所对应的参会者就是要选取的人,  \ding{173} “青年人”“中年人”“老年人”的人数之比为 0.04 :0.05 ； ${0.01} = 4 : 5 : 1$ ，所以 10 人中“中年人”共有 5 人， 2 人均为 “中年人”的概率 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{5}^{2}}{{{C}}_{10}^{2}} = \dfrac{2}{9},2$ 人中至少有 1 人为男性的概率 $P\left( B\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{2}}{{{C}}_{10}^{2}} = \dfrac{13}{15},2$ 人均为 “中年人” 且至少有 1 人为男性的概率 $P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2} + {{C}}_{2}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{10}^{2}} = \dfrac{7}{45}$ ,因为 $P(A \cap  B) \neq  P\left( A\right)  \cdot  P\left( B\right)$ ,所以事件 $A$ 与事件 $B$ 不独立.

10. ( 1 )假设 ${H}_{0}$ : 患慢性气管炎与吸烟无关. ${\chi }^{2} =  \dfrac{{340} \times  {\left( {120} \times  {45} - {160} \times  {15}\right) }^{2}}{{280} \times  {60} \times  {135} \times  {205}} \approx  {6.581}$ ,由 $P\left( {{\chi }^{2} \geq  {3.841}}\right)  \approx$ 0.05，而 ${6.581} > {3.481}$ ，从而否定原假设，即有 ${95}\%$ 的把握认为患慢性气管炎与吸烟有关；

(2) $L\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {B \mid  A}\right) }{P\left( {\overline{B} \mid  A}\right) } = \dfrac{\dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( A\right) }}{\dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  \overline{B}}\right) }{P\left( A\right) }} = \dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( {A \cap  \overline{B}}\right) } =  \dfrac{\left| A \cap  B\right| }{\left| A \cap  \bar{B}\right| } = \dfrac{9}{32};$

(3)按分层抽样，不吸烟者 3 人，吸烟者 4 人， $X$ 的可能值为 0,1,2,3,其分布是 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 \\  \dfrac{4}{35} & \dfrac{18}{35} & \dfrac{12}{35} & \dfrac{1}{35} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{4}{35} + 1 \times  \dfrac{18}{35} + 2 \times  \dfrac{12}{35} + 3 \times  \dfrac{1}{35} = \dfrac{9}{7}.$

\section*{考点 8 全概率公式}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 0.18

【解析】 $p = {0.6} \times  {20}\%  + {0.3} \times  {16}\%  + {0.1} \times  {12}\%  = {0.18}$ .

2. 0.94

【解析】根据题意可知“至少一人命中目标”包括“仅有一人命中目标”和“两人同时命中目标”两个基本事件；可得“仅有一人命中目标”的概率为 ${P}_{1} = \left( {1 - {0.7}}\right)  \times  {0.8} + {0.7} \times  (1 -  {0.8}) = {0.38}$ ; “两人同时命中目标”的概率为 ${P}_{2} = {0.7} \times  {0.8} = {0.56}$ ; 所以至少一人命中目标的概率为 $P = {P}_{1} + {P}_{2} =$ 0.94 . 故答案为:0.94 .

3. 0.85

【解析】由题意知, $A,B,C$ 题库的比例为: $5 : 4 : 3$ ,各占比分别为 $\dfrac{5}{12},\dfrac{4}{12},\dfrac{3}{12}$ ,则根据全概率公式知,所求正确率 $p =  \dfrac{5}{12} \times  {0.92} + \dfrac{4}{12} \times  {0.86} + \dfrac{3}{12} \times  {0.72} = {0.85}.$

4. 3

【解析】由题意可知: $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{{2}^{n} - 2}{{2}^{n}},P\left( B\right)  = \dfrac{1 + n}{{2}^{n}}$ ,

$P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  = \dfrac{n}{{2}^{n}}$ ,因为 $A,B$ 独立,所以 $P\left( A\right)  \cdot  P\left( B\right)  =  P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)$ ,即 $\dfrac{{2}^{n} - 2}{{2}^{n}} \cdot  \dfrac{1 + n}{{2}^{n}} = \dfrac{n}{{2}^{n}} \Longrightarrow  {2}^{n - 1} = n + 1$ ,结合 ${2}^{n - 1}$ , $n + 1$ 均随 $n$ 的增大而增大,故 $n = 3$ . 故答案为: 3 .

\section*{二、选择题}

5. C

【解析】对于 ${A}$ ,由于 $A,B$ 是独立事件,故 $P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  =  P\left( A\right) P\left( B\right)$ , A 正确; 对于 ${B}$ ,由于 $A,B$ 是独立事件,则 $\bar{A}$ 、 $B$ 也是相互独立事件，故 $P\left( {\bar{A} \cap  B}\right)  = P\left( \bar{A}\right) P\left( B\right)$ ，B 正确； 对于 ${C},P\left( {A \cup  B}\right)  = P\left( A\right)  + P\left( B\right)  - P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)$ ,由于 $P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)$ 不一定为 0,故 $C$ 错误; 对于 $D$ ,由于 $A,B$ 是独立事件,则 $\bar{A},\bar{B}$ 也是相互独立事件, $P\left( {\bar{A} \cap  \bar{B}}\right)  = P\left( \bar{A}\right) P\left( \bar{B}\right)  = \left\lbrack  {1 - P\left( A\right) }\right\rbrack  \left\lbrack  {1 - P\left( B\right) }\right\rbrack$ , D 正确. 故选: C.

6. D

【解析】第一步:第一次掷骰子的概率:1. 掷出 6 点:概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6}$ ,弟弟直接吃 1 颗花生; 2 . 非 6 点: 概率为 $\dfrac{5}{6}$ ,记下点数 $k\left( {k = 1,2,3,4,5}\right)$ ,进入后续阶段. 第二步: 后续阶段的概率分析: 设小明掷骰子时弟弟吃到花生的概率为 $P$ ,弟弟掷骰子时弟弟吃到花生的概率为 $Q$ ,若小明掷骰子: (1) 掷出 $k$ : 概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6}$ ,弟弟吃 1 颗花生; (2) 排出 6 : 概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6}$ ,小明吃 3 颗花生; (3) 其他点数: 概率为 $\dfrac{4}{6}$ ,轮到弟弟掷骰子,此时概率为 $Q$ ,故有 $P = \dfrac{1}{6} \times  1 + \dfrac{1}{6} \times  0 + \dfrac{4}{6} \times  Q$  \ding{172} ; 若弟弟掷骰子: (1) 掷出 $k$ : 概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6}$ ,弟弟吃 1 颗花生;

(2)排出 6:概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6}$ ，小明吃 3 颗花生；

(3)其他点数:概率为 $\dfrac{4}{6}$ ，轮到小明掷骰子，此时概率为 $P$ ， 故有 $Q = \dfrac{1}{6} \times  1 + \dfrac{1}{6} \times  0 + \dfrac{4}{6} \times  P$  \ding{173} ；联立 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 两式，可得 $P = Q = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,即后续阶段弟弟吃到花生的概率为 $\dfrac{5}{6} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} =  \dfrac{5}{12}$ ,故任意一次游戏中弟弟能吃到 1 颗花生的概率为 $\dfrac{1}{6} +  \dfrac{5}{12} = \dfrac{7}{12}$ . 故选: D.

\section*{三、解答题}

7. ( 1 )当 $n = 6$ 时，盒中有 6 个白球，14 个黑球， $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{6}{20}  = \dfrac{3}{10},P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{5}{19},P\left( {B \mid  \bar{A}}\right)  = \dfrac{6}{19},P\left( B\right)  = P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  +  P\left( {\bar{A} \cap  B}\right)  = P\left( A\right) P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  + P\left( \bar{A}\right) P\left( {B \mid  \bar{A}}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{10} \times  \dfrac{5}{19} +  \dfrac{7}{10} \times  \dfrac{6}{19} = \dfrac{3}{10}$ ,则 $P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  \neq  P\left( B\right)$ ,所以事件 $A$ 与 $B$ 相互不独立;

(2)从 20 个球中取 10 个球，恰有 3 个白球的概率 $P =  \dfrac{{{C}}_{n}^{3}{{C}}_{{20} - n}^{7}}{{{C}}_{20}^{10}}$ ,设 $f\left( n\right)  = {{C}}_{n}^{3}{{C}}_{{20} - n}^{7}$ ,当 $3 \leq  n \leq  {12}$ 时, $\dfrac{f\left( {n + 1}\right) }{f\left( n\right) } =  \dfrac{{{C}}_{n + 1}^{3}{{C}}_{{19} - n}^{7}}{{{C}}_{n}^{3}{{C}}_{{20} - n}^{7}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{\left( {n + 1}\right) !}{3!\left( {n - 2}\right) !} \cdot  \dfrac{\left( {{19} - n}\right) !}{7!\left( {{12} - n}\right) !}}{\dfrac{n!}{3!\left( {n - 3}\right) !} \cdot  \dfrac{\left( {{20} - n}\right) !}{7!\left( {{13} - n}\right) !}} = \dfrac{-{n}^{2} + {12n} + {13}}{-{n}^{2} + {22n} - {40}},$

$- {n}^{2} + {12n} + {13} - \left( {-{n}^{2} + {22n} - {40}}\right)  = {53} - {10n}$ ,当 $3 \leq  n \leq  5$ 时, $f\left( {n + 1}\right)  > f\left( n\right)$ ,当 $6 \leq  n \leq  {12}$ 时, $f\left( {n + 1}\right)  < f\left( n\right)$ ,因此 $f\left( 3\right)  < f\left( 4\right)  < f\left( 5\right)  < f\left( 6\right)  > f\left( 7\right)  > \cdots  > f\left( {13}\right)$ ,而 $f\left( 3\right)  =  {{C}}_{17}^{7} > {{C}}_{13}^{3} = f\left( {13}\right)$ ,则 $f{\left( n\right) }_{\max } = f\left( 6\right) ,f{\left( n\right) }_{\min } = f\left( {13}\right)$ ,所以当 $n = 6$ 时,参与者获奖的可能性最大; 当 $n = {13}$ 时,参与者获奖的可能性最小.

8. (1)A 与 $B$ 比赛获胜的概率为: ${P}_{1} = \dfrac{4}{12} = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ，A 与 $C$ 比赛获胜的概率为: ${P}_{2} = \dfrac{5}{10} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ， $A$ 与 $D$ 比赛获胜的概率为: ${P}_{3} = \dfrac{12}{15} = \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,因为 “决赛中 $A$ 至少获胜一场” 的对立事件为 “决赛中 $A$ 一场都没有获胜”,则“决赛中 $A$ 一场都没有获胜”的概率为: ${P}_{0} = \left( {1 - {P}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - {P}_{2}}\right) \left( {1 - {P}_{3}}\right)  =  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right) \left( {1 - \dfrac{4}{5}}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{5} = \dfrac{1}{15}$ ,所以 “决赛中 $A$ 至少获胜一场”的概率为: $P = 1 - {P}_{0} = 1 - \dfrac{1}{15} = \dfrac{14}{15}$ ;

(2) \ding{172}  若出场顺序为 ${BCD}$ ，则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{1} = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{4}{5}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{4}{5} = \dfrac{1}{30} +  \dfrac{4}{15} = \dfrac{3}{10}$

 \ding{173}  若出场顺序为 ${BDC}$ ,则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{2} = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{2}{15} + \dfrac{4}{15}  = \dfrac{2}{5}$ ,

 \ding{174}  若出场顺序为 ${CBD}$ ,则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{3} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{4}{5}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{4}{5} = \dfrac{1}{30} + \dfrac{2}{15}  = \dfrac{1}{6}$ ,

 \ding{175}  若出场顺序为 ${CDB}$ ,则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{4} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{4}{15} + \dfrac{2}{15}  = \dfrac{2}{5}$ ,

 \circled{5}  若出场顺序为 ${DBC}$ ,则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{5} = \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{4}{5}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{2}{15} + \dfrac{1}{30}  = \dfrac{1}{6},$

 \circled{6}  若出场顺序为 ${DCB}$ ，则 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率为:

${P}_{6} = \dfrac{4}{5} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{3}}\right)  + \left( {1 - \dfrac{4}{5}}\right)  \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{4}{15} + \dfrac{1}{30}  = \dfrac{3}{10}$ , 因为 $\dfrac{2}{5} > \dfrac{3}{10} > \dfrac{1}{6}$ ,所以要使 $A$ 在这三场比赛中连胜两场的概率最大,出场顺序为 ${BDC}$ 或 ${CDB}$ .

9.(1)由题意小 $A$ 总出手 200 次，命中 120 次，命中率为: $\dfrac{120}{200} = {60}\%$ ,小 $B$ 总出手 200 次,命中 136 次,命中率为 $\dfrac{136}{200} = {68}\%  > {60}\%$ ,故小 $B$ 获校 ${MVP}$ ,所以小 $C$ 的想法不正确;

(2)(i)证明:若第一次投篮人物为小 $a,k\left( {3 \leq  k \leq  {20}}\right. ,k \in  \mathbf{Z})$ ,小 $a$ 获胜的概率为 ${P}_{a}$ ,小 $b$ 获胜的概率为 $1 - {P}_{a}$ ,则 ${P}_{a} \geq  {0.4} + {0.7} \times  {0.6} \times  {0.4} = {0.568} > {0.5} > 1 - {P}_{a}$ ,所以若小 $a$ 第一次投篮，小 $a$ 获胜概率大；

(ii)若第一次投篮人物为小 $b,k\left( {3 \leq  k \leq  {20},k \in  \mathbf{Z}}\right)$ ，小 $b$ 获胜的概率为 ${P}_{b}$ ,小 $a$ 获胜的概率为 $1 - {P}_{b}$ ,

则 ${P}_{b} = {0.3} + {0.3} \times  \left( {{0.7} \times  {0.6}}\right)  + \cdots  + {0.3} \times  {\left( {0.7} \times  {0.6}\right) }^{m} =  \dfrac{{0.3} \times  \left\lbrack  {1 - {0.42}^{m + 1}}\right\rbrack  }{0.58} = \dfrac{15}{29}\left( {1 - {0.42}^{m + 1}}\right) ,$

其中 $m = \left\{  \begin{array}{l} \dfrac{k - 2}{2},k \in  \{ 4,6,8,{10},{12},{14},{16},{18},{20}\} \\  \dfrac{k - 1}{2},k \in  \{ 3,5,7,9,{11},{13},{15},{17},{19}\}  \end{array}\right.$ ,

由指数函数的单调性可知: ${P}_{b} = \dfrac{15}{29}\left( {1 - {0.42}^{m + 1}}\right)$ 随着 $m$ 的增大而增大,计算可得: $f\left( 2\right)  < {0.5} < f\left( 3\right)$ ,所以当 $m \geq  3$ 也就是 $7 \leq  k \leq  {20}$ 时, ${P}_{b} > {0.5} > 1 - {P}_{b}$ ,当 $m < 3$ 也就是 $3 \leq  k \leq  6$ 时, ${P}_{b} < {0.5} < 1 - {P}_{b}$ ,综上: 若小 $b$ 第一次投篮, $k \in  \{ 3,4,5,6\}$ 时,小 $a$ 获胜概率大, $k \in  \{ k \mid  7 \leq  k \leq  {20},k \in  \mathbf{Z}\}$ 时, 小 $b$ 获胜概率大.

10. (1)估计该校学生的平均课外阅读时间，设各区间的中点值分别为 ${x}_{i}$ ,频率/组距为 $f$ ,组距为 $d\left( {d = {10}}\right)$ . 平均课外阅读时间 $\bar{x} = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{i}{x}_{i}{f}_{i}d$ . 假设频率分布直方图中各区间 $\lbrack 0,{10}),\lbrack {10},{20}),\lbrack {20},{30}),\lbrack {30},{40}),\lbrack {40},{50}),\lbrack {50},{60})$ 对应的频率/组距分别为0.005,0.015,0.020,0.030,0.025, 0.005. 各区间中点值分别为 ${x}_{1} = 5,{x}_{2} = {15},{x}_{3} = {25},{x}_{4} =$ 35, ${x}_{5} = {45},{x}_{6} = {55}$ . 则 $\bar{x} = {10}(5 \times  {0.005} + {15} \times  {0.015} + {25} \times  {0.020} + {35} \times  {0.030} + {45} \times  {0.025} + {55} \times  {0.005}) = {10}\left( {{0.025} + }\right.  {0.225} + {0.5} + {1.05} + {1.125} + {0.275}) = {32}$ (小时/月);

(2)估计该校学生课外阅读时间位于区间 $\lbrack {30},{60})$ 的概率. 根据频率分布直方图的性质,区间的概率等于频率/组距与组距的乘积之和. $P = \left( {{0.030} + {0.025} + {0.005}}\right)  \times  {10} =  \left( {0.06}\right)  \times  {10} = {0.6}$ ;

(3)设其他年级学生中喜欢阅读的比例为 $x$ . 记事件 $A$ 为“任选 1 人是初一年级学生”,事件 $B$ 为“任选 1 人是喜欢阅读的学生”. 根据全概率公式: $P\left( B\right)  = P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  \cdot  P\left( A\right)  + P\left( {B \mid  \bar{A}}\right)$ . $P\left( \bar{A}\right)$ . 代入已知数据: ${0.18} = {0.40} \times  {0.28} + x \times  {0.72}$ . 解得: $x =  \dfrac{{0.18} - {0.40} \times  {0.28}}{0.72} = \dfrac{{0.18} - {0.112}}{0.72} = \dfrac{0.068}{0.72} = \dfrac{17}{180} \approx  {9.4}\% .$

\section*{考点 9 正态分布指定区间的概率}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 0.2

【解析】因为 $X \sim  N\left( {3,{\sigma }^{2}}\right) ,P\left( {3 \leq  X \leq  5}\right)  = {0.3}  \Longrightarrow  P\left( {1 \leq  X \leq  3}\right)  = {0.3}$ ,

所以 $P\left( {X > 5}\right)  = \dfrac{1 - P\left( {3 \leq  X \leq  5}\right)  - P\left( {1 \leq  X \leq  3}\right) }{2} = {0.2}$ .

2. 0.48

【解析】若 $X \sim  N\left( {4,{2}^{2}}\right)$ ,且 $P\left( {X < 0}\right)  = {0.02}$ ,则 $P\left( {X > 8}\right)  = P\left( {X < 0}\right)  = {0.02}$ ,则 $P\left( {4 < X < 8}\right)  = {0.5} - P\left( {X > 8}\right)  =$ 0.48 . 故答案为: 0.48 .

3. $\dfrac{1 - p}{2}$

【解析】如图所示, $P\left( {{495} \leq  X \leq  {500}}\right)  = \dfrac{1 - p}{2}$ .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/173_1045_569_299_226_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

4. 0.3

【解析】 $P\left( {X > {115}}\right)  = \dfrac{1 - P\left( {{75} \leq  X \leq  {115}}\right) }{2} = \dfrac{1 - {0.4}}{2} =$ 0.3 .

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/173_1045_958_298_225_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

5. -6

【解析】由正态分布的对称性,得 $\left( {a - 3}\right)  + \left( {1 - {2a}}\right)  = 4$ ,所以 $a =  - 6$ . 故答案为:-6.

6. 0.14 .

【解析】因为 $X \sim  N\left( {2,{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $P\left( {X < 2}\right)  = P\left( {X > 2}\right)  =$ 0.5,因此 $P\left( {X > {2.5}}\right)  = P\left( {X > 2}\right)  - P\left( {2 < X \leq  {2.5}}\right)  = {0.5} -  {0.36} = {0.14}$ . 故答案为: 0.14 .

7. 1360

【解析】根据已知条件有数学成绩低于 80 分的概率为 $\dfrac{320}{2000} = \dfrac{4}{25}$ ,又 $X \sim  N\left( {{100},{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ ,所以数学分数属于闭区间 $\left\lbrack  {{80},{120}}\right\rbrack$ 的概率为 $1 - 2 \times  \dfrac{4}{25} = \dfrac{17}{25}$ ,所以数学分数属于闭区间间 $\left\lbrack  {{80},{120}}\right\rbrack$ 的学生人数约为 ${2000} \times  \dfrac{17}{25} = {1360}$ 人. 故答案为: 1360 .

8. 0.28

【解析】因为 $X \sim  N\left( {2,{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ 且 $P\left( {2 < X \leq  {2.5}}\right)  = {0.36}$ ,所以 $P\left( {{1.5} \leq  X < 2}\right)  = P\left( {2 < X \leq  {2.5}}\right)  = {0.36}$ ,则 $P(\left| {X - 2}\right|  >  {0.5}) = 1 - {2P}\left( {2 < X \leq  {2.5}}\right)  = 1 - 2 \times  {0.36} = {0.28}$ . 故答案为: 0.28 .

9. 0.4

【解析】因为随机变量 $\xi  \sim  N\left( {3,{\sigma }^{2}}\right) \left( {\sigma  > 0}\right)$ ,正态曲线关于 $x = 3$ 对称. $P\left( {\xi  \geq  1}\right)  = {0.9}$ ,则 $P\left( {\xi  \leq  1}\right)  = 1 - {0.9} = {0.1}$ , $P\left( {1 \leq  \xi  \leq  3}\right)  = {0.5} - {0.1} = {0.4}$ ,根据正态曲线的对称性 $P\left( {3 \leq  \xi  \leq  5}\right)  = P\left( {1 \leq  \xi  \leq  3}\right)  = {0.4}$ . 故答案为:0.4 .

10. 2.3\%

【解析】设每包糖果的实际质量为 $X$ ,则 $X \sim  N\left( {{500},{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ , 又 $P\left( {{495} < X < {505}}\right)  = {95.4}\%$ ,所以 $P\left( {X > {505}}\right)  =  \dfrac{1 - {95.4}\% }{2} = {2.3}\%$ ,故质量超过 505 克的可能性约为 2.3\%. 故答案为:2.3\%.

11. 0.2916

【解析】根据 $P\left( {\left| {X - 3}\right|  \leq  {0.5}}\right)  = {0.8}$ 可得 $P(\left| {X - 3}\right|  >  {0.5}) = 1 - {0.8} = {0.2}$ ,即 $P\left( {X > {3.5}}\right)  + P\left( {X < {2.5}}\right)  = {0.2}$ ,又由对称性可知 $P\left( {X > {3.5}}\right)  = P\left( {X < {2.5}}\right)  = {0.1}$ ,所以 $P\left( {X > {3.5}}\right)  = {0.1}$ ,即任取 1 个零件其长度大于 3.5 毫米的概率为 0.1 ; 因此 4 个零件逐一检查,仅有 1 个零件的长度大于 3.5 毫米的概率为 $P = {{C}}_{4}^{1} \times  {0.1} \times  {\left( 1 - {0.1}\right) }^{3} = {0.2916}$ ; 故答案为: 0.2916 .

12. 97.7

【解析】因为 $\mu  = {75},{\sigma }^{2} = {64}$ ,所以 $P\left( {X < {91}}\right)  \approx  {0.5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \times$ 95.4\% = 97.7\%，故答案为:97.7.

\section*{二、选择题}

13. A

【解析】由随机变量 $X$ 服从正态分布 $N\left( {0,{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ ,因为 $P\left( {X \leq  a}\right)  = \dfrac{5}{6}$ ,可得 $P\left( {X > a}\right)  = 1 - P\left( {X \leq  a}\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{5}{6} = \dfrac{1}{6}$ , 所以 $P\left( {\left| X\right|  \leq  a}\right)  = 1 - {2P}\left( {X > a}\right)  = 1 - 2 \times  \dfrac{1}{6} = \dfrac{2}{3}$ . 故选: A.

14. A

【解析】对于选项 A,该组数据有 10 个数,因为 ${10} \times  {60}\%  =$ 6，所以第 60 百分位数为 $\dfrac{{14} + {16}}{2} = {15}$ ，故选项 A 错误；对于选项 ${B},P\left( {3 < x < 4}\right)  = P\left( {x \leq  4}\right)  - {0.5} = {0.2}$ ,故选项 ${B}$ 正确; 对于选项 C, 若线性相关系数 $\left| r\right|$ 越接近 1, 则两个变量的线性相关程度越高, 正确; 对于选项 D, 因为回归方程经过 $\left( {m,{2.8}}\right)$ ,代入得 ${0.3m} - m = {2.8}$ ,解得 $m =  - 4$ ,正确.

15. B

【解析】依题意, $P\left( {{80} < X < {120}}\right)  = {0.75}$ ,而 $X$ 服从正态分布 $N\left( {{100},{\sigma }^{2}}\right)$ ,因此 $P\left( {X \geq  {120}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}P\left( {{80} < X < {120}}\right)  = {0.125}$ ,所以此次考试中成绩不低于 120 分的学生人数约为 ${3200} \times  {0.125} = {400}$ . 故选: B.

\section*{三、解答题}

16.(1)由题意， $\xi  \sim  N\left( {{500},{2.5}^{2}}\right)$ ， $\left| {\xi  - {500}}\right|  > 5$ 的概率等于 $P\left( {\left| {\xi  - {500}}\right|  > 5}\right)$ . 令 $Y = \dfrac{\xi  - {500}}{2.5}$ ,则 $Y \sim  N\left( {0,1}\right)$ . 因此, $P\left( {\left| {\xi  - {500}}\right|  > 5}\right)  = P\left( {\left| Y\right|  > 2}\right)  = 2\left( {1 - \Phi \left( 2\right) }\right)  \approx  {0.0456}$ . 故净含量误差超过 ${5g}$ 的概率约为 0.046 ;

(2)X 可能的取值为 0、1、2、3 . 由(1)可知，任取一包糖果，净含量小于 497.5g 的概率为 $\Phi \left( {-1}\right)  = 1 - \Phi \left( 1\right)  \approx  {0.1587}$ . 故 $X$ 服从二项分布 $B\left( {3,{0.1587}}\right)$ ,记 $p = {0.1587},P\left( {X = 0}\right)  =  {{C}}_{3}^{0}{\left( 1 - p\right) }^{3} = {0.595},P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{1}p{\left( 1 - p\right) }^{2} = {0.337},  P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{2}{p}^{2}\left( {1 - p}\right)  = {0.064},P\left( {X = 3}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{3}{p}^{3} = {0.004}$ 从而 $X$ 的分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 \\  {0.595} & {0.337} & {0.064} & {0.004} \end{matrix}\right)$

因此 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {np} = 3 \times  {0.1587} \approx  {0.476}$ .

\section*{考点 10 独立性检验为载体及其应用}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 44

【解析】 ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{100}{\left\lbrack  m\left( m - {30}\right)  - \left( {50} - m\right) \left( {80} - m\right) \right\rbrack  }^{2}}{{80} \cdot  {20} \cdot  {50} \cdot  {50}} \geq$ 3.841, $\dfrac{{\left( {m}^{2} - {30}m - {m}^{2} + {130}m - {4000}\right) }^{2}}{40000} \geq  {3.841}$ ,化简得 ${\left( {100}m - {4000}\right) }^{2} \geq  {153640}$ ,解得 $m \leq  {36.08}$ 或者 $m \geq  {43.92}$ , 因为 $m \geq  {40},m \in  \mathbf{N}$ ,所以 $m$ 的最小值为 44 .

\section*{二、选择题}

2. D

【解析】因为 ${\chi }^{2} \approx  {7.468} > {6.635}$ ,所以有 ${99}\%$ 的把握认为患慢性气管炎与吸烟有关，即“患慢性气管炎与吸烟没有关系”成立的可能性小于 1\%，当然更小于 5\%，故 \ding{172}  \ding{173} 正确， ${\chi }^{2}$ 分布概率表中的 0.05、0.01 等小概率值在统计上称为显著性水平，小概率事件一般认为不太可能发生，故 \ding{174} 正确.

\section*{三、解答题}

3. (1) 因为 ${121} + c = {134}$ ,解得 $c = {13},{121} + b = {283}$ ,解得 $b = {283} - {121} = {162}$ ,所以吸咽者为 ${339} - {134} = {205}$ ,吸烟者中患有慢性支气管炎的人数为 $d = {205} - {162} = {43}$ ，所以 $\dfrac{43}{205} \times$ 100\% = 20.98\%，所以，估算样本中吸烟者约有 20.98\% 患有慢性支气管炎；

(2)提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ :患慢性支气管炎与吸烟无关，计算 ${\chi }^{2}$ ， ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{n{\left( ad - bc\right) }^{2}}{\left( {a + b}\right) \left( {c + d}\right) \left( {a + c}\right) \left( {b + d}\right) } =$

$\dfrac{{339}{\left( {121} \times  {43} - {162} \times  {13}\right) }^{2}}{{283} \times  {56} \times  {134} \times  {205}} \approx  {7.468},$

${\chi }^{2} \approx  {7.468} > {6.635},$

从而 ${H}_{0}$ 不成立，所以我们有 ${99}\%$ 把握认为患慢性支气管炎与吸烟有关.

4.(1)由题知，一天中到该公园锻炼的平均人次约为: $\dfrac{{100} \times  {20} + {300} \times  {35} + {500} \times  {45}}{100} = {350}$ ,即一天中到该公园锻炼的平均人次的估计值为 350 ；

(2)根据所给数据，计算出 $x = 9$ ，则完成 $2 \times  2$ 列联表为:

空气质量好 & 36 & 39 & 75 \\

\cline{1-4}
总计 & 55 & 45 & 100 \\

}
\end{center}

提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ : 一天中到该公园锻炼的人次与该市当天的空气质量无关. ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{100}{\left( {36} \times  6 - {19} \times  {39}\right) }^{2}}{{55} \times  {45} \times  {25} \times  {75}} \approx  {5.9394}$ ,因为 ${\chi }^{2} > {3.841}$ ，所以拒绝原假设，即一天中到该公园锻炼的人次与该市当天的空气质量有关.

提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ : 生产方式与工作效率没有显著差异,确定显著性水平: $\alpha  = {0.05}$ ,即 $P\left( {{\chi }^{2} \geq  {3.841}}\right)  \approx  {0.05}$ ,计算 ${\chi }^{2} =  \dfrac{n{\left( ad - bc\right) }^{2}}{\left( {a + b}\right) \left( {c + d}\right) \left( {a + c}\right) \left( {b + d}\right) } \approx  {7.619} > {3.841}$ ,否定原假设, 故有 95\% 的把握认为两种生产方式的工作效率有显著差异.

6. ( 1 )由表可知锻炼时长不少于 1 小时的人数为占比 $\dfrac{{179} + {43} + {28}}{580} = \dfrac{25}{58}$ ,则估计该地区 29000 名学生中体育锻炼时长不少于 1 小时的人数为 ${29000} \times  \dfrac{25}{58} = {12500}$ ;

(2)估计该地区初中生的日均体育锻炼时长约为

$\dfrac{1}{580}\left( {\dfrac{0.5}{2} \times  {139} + \dfrac{{0.5} + 1}{2} \times  {191} + \dfrac{1 + {1.5}}{2} \times  {179} + \dfrac{{1.5} + 2}{2} \times  }\right.  \left. {{43} + \dfrac{2 + {2.5}}{2} \times  {28}}\right)  \approx  {0.9},$

故估计该地区初中学生日均体育锻炼的时长为 0.9 小时;

${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{580} \times  {\left( {45} \times  {308} - {177} \times  {50}\right) }^{2}}{{95} \times  {485} \times  {222} \times  {358}} \approx  {3.976} > {3.841}$ . 从而否定原假设,即有 ${95}\%$ 的把握认为学业成绩优秀与日均锻炼时长不小于 1 小时且小于 2 小时有关.

7. (1)由表可知，若一名志愿者是“志愿模范队”成员，则其周平均服务时长超过 2 小时的概率: $P = \dfrac{54}{72} = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ；

提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ : 是否“是“志愿模范队’成员”与“周平均服务时长超过 2 小时”无关,确定显著性水平 $\alpha  = {0.001}$ ,计算 ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{120}{\left( {54} \times  {30} - {18} \times  {18}\right) }^{2}}{{72} \times  {48} \times  {72} \times  {48}} = {16.875} > {10.828}$ ,从而否定原假设，即有 99.9\% 的把握认为 “是 ‘志愿模范队’ 成员” 与 “周平均服务时长超过 2 小时”有关系；

(3)由分层抽样可知，这 8 人中有 6 个来自“志愿模范队”，2 个不是“志愿模范队”成员，故随机变量 $X$ 可能为 0,1,2,

且 $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{8}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{28},P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{1}{{C}}_{6}^{1}}{{{C}}_{8}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{7},P\left( {X = 2}\right)  =  \dfrac{{{C}}_{6}^{2}}{{{C}}_{8}^{2}} = \dfrac{15}{28}$ ,故分布为: $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 \\  \dfrac{1}{28} & \dfrac{3}{7} & \dfrac{15}{28} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

所以期望: $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1}{28} + 1 \times  \dfrac{3}{7} + 2 \times  \dfrac{15}{28} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,

方差: $D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {\left( 0 - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{28} + {\left( 1 - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{7} + {\left( 2 - \dfrac{3}{2}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{15}{28} = \dfrac{9}{28}.$

8.(1)列联表为

}
\end{center}

${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{10}{\left( 0 - {15}\right) }^{2}}{5 \times  5 \times  7 \times  3} \approx  {4.286} > {3.841}$ ,可以判断商品品种的提升与该便利店是否是示范店有关.

(2)线性回归模型 \ding{172} : $y = {7.627x} - {15332.20}$ ,当 $x = {2024}$ 时,预测值为 104.9 ; 线性回归模型 $\text{  \ding{173}  }y = {5.89x} - {11828.41}$ , 当 $x = {2024}$ 时,预测值为 93.0 . 模型 \ding{172} 的预测不可靠，根据 (1)可以知道商品品种与便利店的品质有关，影响了利润额， 因此按照经济发展规律, 应该用比较新的数据即品种为 3000 种的数据进行预测; 模型 \ding{173} 的预测不可靠，2022 年可能因为受疫情影响或者其它不可因素，其利润额 60.5 为异常数据，应该剔除.

\section*{考点 11 离散型分布及期望、方差}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. $\dfrac{1}{3}$

【解析】随机变量 $X$ 服从二项分布 $B\left( {n,p}\right)$ ,若 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   =  {30},D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {20}$ ,可得 ${np} = {30},{np}\left( {1 - p}\right)  = {20}$ ,解得 $p = \dfrac{1}{3}$ , 故答案为 $\dfrac{1}{3}$ .

2. 12

【解析】 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {np} = {20} = {50p},p = {0.4},D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {np}(1 -  p) = {50} \times  {0.4} \times  {0.6} = {12}.$

3. 6.3

【解析】由题设有 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 5 \times  {0.2} + 6 \times  {0.3} + 7 \times  {0.5} = 1 +  {1.8} + {3.5} = {6.3}$ . 故答案为: 6.3 .

4. $\dfrac{7}{64}$

【解析】由题意可知, $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = {\left( \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{3} = \dfrac{1}{8},P\left( {X = 1}\right)  =  1 - \dfrac{1}{8} = \dfrac{7}{8}$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1}{8} + 1 \times  \dfrac{7}{8} = \dfrac{7}{8}$ ,所以 $D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{1}{8} \times  {\left( 0 - \dfrac{7}{8}\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{7}{8} \times  {\left( 1 - \dfrac{7}{8}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{7}{64}$ . 故答案为: $\dfrac{7}{64}$ .

\section*{二、选择题}

5. A

【解析】由随机变量 ${X}_{1},{X}_{2}$ 的取值情况,它们的期望分别为: $E\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {\dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2} + \dfrac{{x}_{2} + {x}_{3}}{2} + \dfrac{{x}_{3} + {x}_{4}}{2} + \dfrac{{x}_{4} + {x}_{1}}{2}}\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}}\right) ,$

$E\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {2{x}_{1} - {x}_{2} + 2{x}_{2} - {x}_{3} + 2{x}_{3} - {x}_{4} + 2{x}_{4} - {x}_{1}}\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{4}\left( {{x}_{1} + {x}_{2} + {x}_{3} + {x}_{4}}\right)$ ,即 $E\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   = E\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack$ ,

$D\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {{\left( \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{2} + {x}_{3}}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{3} + {x}_{4}}{2}\right) }^{2} + }\right.  \left. {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{4} + {x}_{1}}{2}\right) }^{2}\right\rbrack   - {\left( E\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack  \right) }^{2}$ ,而 ${\left( \dfrac{{x}_{1} + {x}_{2}}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{2} + {x}_{3}}{2}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{3} + {x}_{4}}{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{{x}_{4} + {x}_{1}}{2}\right) }^{2} =$

$\dfrac{2\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  + 2{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + 2{x}_{2}{x}_{3} + 2{x}_{3}{x}_{4} + 2{x}_{4}{x}_{1}}{4},$ 同理 $D\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {{\left( 2{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2{x}_{2} - {x}_{3}\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2{x}_{3} - {x}_{4}\right) }^{2}}\right.  \left. {+{\left( 2{x}_{4} - {x}_{1}\right) }^{2}}\right\rbrack   - {\left( E\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack  \right) }^{2}$ ,而 ${\left( 2{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2{x}_{2} - {x}_{3}\right) }^{2}  + {\left( 2{x}_{3} - {x}_{4}\right) }^{2} + {\left( 2{x}_{4} - {x}_{1}\right) }^{2} = 5\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  -  4\left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} + {x}_{4}{x}_{1}}\right)$ ,则 $D\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   - D\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   =  \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {5\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  - 4\left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} + {x}_{4}{x}_{1}}\right) }\right.  \left. {-\dfrac{\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  + \left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} + {x}_{4}{x}_{1}}\right) }{2}}\right\rbrack   =  \dfrac{9\left\lbrack  {\left( {{x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2}}\right)  - \left( {{x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} + {x}_{4}{x}_{1}}\right) }\right\rbrack  }{8}$ ,因为 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} \geq  2{x}_{1}{x}_{2},{x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} \geq  2{x}_{3}{x}_{2},{x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2} \geq  2{x}_{4}{x}_{3},{x}_{1}^{2} +  {x}_{4}^{2} \geq  2{x}_{4}{x}_{1}$ ,所以 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} + {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2} \geq  {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} +  {x}_{4}{x}_{1}$ ,因为 ${x}_{1} < {x}_{2} < {x}_{3} < {x}_{4}$ ,不能取等号,所以 ${x}_{1}^{2} + {x}_{2}^{2} +  {x}_{3}^{2} + {x}_{4}^{2} > {x}_{1}{x}_{2} + {x}_{2}{x}_{3} + {x}_{3}{x}_{4} + {x}_{4}{x}_{1}$ ,所以 $D\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   -  D\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   > 0$ ,所以 $D\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   > D\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack$ . 故选: A.

6. C

【解析】对于 $A$ ,事件 $A$ 和事件 $B$ 独立, $P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  =  P\left( A\right) P\left( B\right) ,A$ 错误; 对于 ${B},8 \times  {0.7} = {5.6}$ ,数据2,3,6,7,8, 10,11,13的第 70 百分位数为 ${10},{B}$ 正确; 对于 ${C},y$ 关于 $x$ 的回归方程为 $y =  - {0.5x} + {0.7}$ ,当 $x = 2$ 时, $y =  - {0.3}, - 1  - \left( {-{0.3}}\right)  =  - {0.7}$ ,则样本点 $\left( {2, - 1}\right)$ 的离差为 $- {0.7},{C}$ 正确; 对于 ${D},E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 1 \times  {0.2} + 2 \times  {0.3} + 3 \times  {0.3} = {1.7},{D}$ 错误;综上所述选 C.

\section*{三、解答题}

7.(1)甲投篮 3 次得 2 分，即只投中 1 次，概率 $p = {{C}}_{3}^{1}\dfrac{1}{2} \times  {\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{3}{8};$

(2)由题意知 $X$ 的所有可能取值为0,2,4,6,则 $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  =  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} = \dfrac{9}{50},P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{5}  + \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} = \dfrac{8}{25},P\left( {X = 4}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} = \dfrac{8}{25},P\left( {X = 6}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} \times  \dfrac{3}{5} = \dfrac{9}{50}$ ,随机变量 $X$ 的分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 2 & 4 & 6 \\  \dfrac{9}{50} & \dfrac{8}{25} & \dfrac{8}{25} & \dfrac{9}{50} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

期望 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{9}{50} + 2 \times  \dfrac{8}{25} + 4 \times  \dfrac{8}{25} + 6 \times  \dfrac{9}{50} = 3$ ;

(3)设甲三次投篮的得分 $Y$ ,则 $Y = 0,2,4,6$ ,可求得随机变量 $Y$ 的分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 2 & 4 & 6 \\  \dfrac{1}{8} & \dfrac{3}{8} & \dfrac{3}{8} & \dfrac{1}{8} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1}{8} +  2 \times  \dfrac{3}{8} + 4 \times  \dfrac{3}{8} + 6 \times  \dfrac{1}{8} = 3,D\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   = {0}^{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{8} + {2}^{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{8} + {4}^{2} \times  \dfrac{3}{8} + {6}^{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{8} - {3}^{2} = 3$ ,又可算得 $D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {0}^{2} \times  \dfrac{9}{50} + {2}^{2} \times  \dfrac{8}{25} +  {4}^{2} \times  \dfrac{8}{25} + {6}^{2} \times  \dfrac{9}{50} - {3}^{2} = \dfrac{97}{25}$ ,因为 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack  ,D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   >  D\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack$ ，所以甲最终的得分均值等于乙最终的得分均值，但乙赢得的分值不如甲稳定.

另解: 设甲三次投篮的次数为 $\xi ,\xi  = 0,1,2,3$ ,则 $E\left\lbrack  \xi \right\rbrack   = 3 \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{3}{2}$ ,设甲的投篮得分为 $Y$ ,则 $Y = {2\xi }$ ,从而 $E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   =  E\left\lbrack  {2\xi }\right\rbrack   = {2E}\left\lbrack  \xi \right\rbrack   = 3.$

8. (1)设小张答对的题数为 $X$ ,则 $P\left( {X = 9}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{9}^{9}}{{{C}}_{10}^{9}} = \dfrac{1}{10}$ ;

(2)设事件 $A$ 表示“输入的问题没有语法错误”，事件 $B$ 表示“一个问题能被 ChatGPT 正确回答”,由题意知 $P\left( \bar{A}\right)  =  {0.1},P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = {0.98},P\left( {B \mid  \bar{A}}\right)  = {0.18}$ ,则 $P\left( A\right)  = 1 -  P\left( \bar{A}\right)  = {0.9},P\left( B\right)  = P\left( {B \cap  A}\right)  + P\left( {B \cap  \bar{A}}\right)  = P\left( {B \mid  A}\right) P\left( A\right)  +  P\left( {B \mid  \bar{A}}\right) P\left( \bar{A}\right)  = {0.98} \times  {0.9} + {0.18} \times  {0.1} = {0.9};$

(3)设小张答对的题数为 $X$ ，则 $X$ 的可能取值是 8、9，且 $P\left( {X = 8}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{9}^{8}{{C}}_{1}^{1}}{{{C}}_{10}^{9}} = \dfrac{9}{10},P\left( {X = 9}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{9}^{9}}{{{C}}_{10}^{9}} = \dfrac{1}{10}$ ,设 ChatGPT 答对的题数为 $Y$ ,则 $Y$ 服从二项分布 $B\left( {9,\dfrac{9}{10}}\right)$ ,

$E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 8 \times  \dfrac{9}{10} + 9 \times  \dfrac{1}{10} = {8.1},$

$E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   = {np} = 9 \times  {0.9} = {8.1},$

$D\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {\left( 8 - \dfrac{81}{10}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{9}{10} + {\left( 9 - \dfrac{81}{10}\right) }^{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{10} = {0.09},$

$D\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   = {np}\left( {1 - p}\right)  = 9 \times  {0.9} \times  {0.1} = {0.81}.$

9. (1)提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ :中草药甲对预防此疾病无效，确定显著性水平 $\alpha  = {0.05}$ ，计算 ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{100} \times  {\left( {29} \times  9 - {16} \times  {46}\right) }^{2}}{{75} \times  {25} \times  {45} \times  {55}} =  \dfrac{1444}{297} \approx  {4.862} > {3.841}$ ,而 $P\left( {{\chi }^{2} \geq  {3.841}}\right)  \approx  {0.05},{\chi }^{2}$ 的值超过了 $\alpha$ 所确定的界限,从而否定原假设,即认为中草药甲对预防此疾病有效果；

(2)记 $A$ 表示服用中草药乙后治疗有效， ${B}_{1}$ 表示未服用过中草药甲, ${B}_{2}$ 表示服用过中草药甲,由题意可得 $P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  =  \dfrac{16}{25},P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  = \dfrac{9}{25}$ ,且 $P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2},P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{4}$ ,则 $P\left( A\right)  = P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  \times  P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  + P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  \times  P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{16}{25} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{9}{25} \times  \dfrac{3}{4} = \dfrac{59}{100}$ ,即中草药乙的治疗有效率 $P = \dfrac{59}{100}$ ,则 $X \sim  B\left( {2,\dfrac{59}{100}}\right)$ ,所以 $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = {{C}}_{2}^{0}{\left( \dfrac{41}{100}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{1681}{{10}^{4}},P(X =  1) = {{C}}_{2}^{1}\left( \dfrac{59}{100}\right) \left( \dfrac{41}{100}\right)  = \dfrac{4838}{{10}^{4}},P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = {{C}}_{2}^{2}{\left( \dfrac{59}{100}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{3481}{{10}^{4}},$ 所以随机变量 $X$ 的分布为: $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 \\  \dfrac{1681}{{10}^{4}} & \dfrac{4838}{{10}^{4}} & \dfrac{3481}{{10}^{4}} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

所以随机变量 $X$ 的数学期望

$E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1681}{{10}^{4}} + 1 \times  \dfrac{4838}{{10}^{4}} + 2 \times  \dfrac{3481}{{10}^{4}} = \dfrac{59}{50}.$

10. (1)由条件得:样本平均数为

$\bar{x} = \dfrac{{54} \times  5 + {57} \times  {21} + {60} \times  {46} + {63} \times  {25} + {66} \times  3}{100} = {60}$ ,由 $X \sim  N\left( {\mu ,{\sigma }^{2}}\right) ,\mu  \approx  \bar{x} = {60},{\sigma }^{2} = {16}$ ,得: $P\left( {{56} < X < {68}}\right)  =  P\left( {{60} - 4 < X < {60} + 2 \times  4}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {\mu  - \sigma  < X \leq  \mu  + \sigma }\right) }{2} +  \dfrac{P\left( {\mu  - {2\sigma } < X \leq  \mu  + {2\sigma }}\right) }{2} \approx  \dfrac{0.6827}{2} + \dfrac{0.9545}{2} = {0.8186};$

(2)(i)设 $A =$ “随机抽取该企业生产的一个零件，该零件为废品”， ${B}_{1} =$ “随机抽取的一个零件为第 1 条生产线生产”, ${B}_{2} =$ "随机抽取一件零件为第 2 条生产线生产". 则由题意可知 $P\left( {B}_{1}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{4},P\left( {B}_{2}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{4},P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  = {0.016}$ , $P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right)  = {0.012}$ . 于是由全概率公式,得 $P\left( A\right)  =  P\left( {B}_{1}\right) P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right)  + P\left( {B}_{2}\right) P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{4} \times  {0.016} + \dfrac{1}{4} \times  {0.012} = {0.015}$ . 即从该企业抽取的零件为废品的概率为 0.015 ;

(ii) 由条件概率公式, 抽取的零件为废品, 其来自第 1 生产线的概率为: $P\left( {{B}_{1} \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {{B}_{1} \cap  A}\right) }{P\left( A\right) } = \dfrac{P\left( {B}_{1}\right) P\left( {A \mid  {B}_{1}}\right) }{P\left( A\right) } =  \dfrac{\dfrac{3}{4} \times  {0.016}}{0.015} = \dfrac{4}{5}.$

11. (1)

 \ding{172}  提出假设 ${H}_{0}$ : 年龄与出游方式的选择无关;

 \ding{173}  确定显著性水平 $\alpha  = {0.05}$ ；

 \ding{174}  计算 ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{{170}{\left( {60} \times  {50} - {40} \times  {20}\right) }^{2}}{{100} \times  {70} \times  {80} \times  {90}} \approx  {16.325} > {3.841}$ ;

 \ding{175}  在显著性水平为 0.05 的前提下，拒绝原假设，有 95\% 的把握认为年龄与出游方式的选择有关；

(2)用分层抽样方式从跟团游中抽取 14 人，其中青壮年有 4 人，中老年有 10 人，则 $X$ 可能的值为 7、5、3、1.

$P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{3}{{C}}_{10}^{4} + {{C}}_{4}^{4}{{C}}_{10}^{3}}{{{C}}_{14}^{7}} = \dfrac{40}{143},P\left( {X = 3}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{2}{{C}}_{10}^{5}}{{{C}}_{14}^{7}} = \dfrac{63}{143},  P\left( {X = 5}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{1}{{C}}_{10}^{6}}{{{C}}_{14}^{7}} = \dfrac{35}{143},P\left( {X = 7}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{0}{{C}}_{10}^{7}}{{{C}}_{14}^{7}} = \dfrac{5}{143},$

$X$ 的分布 $\left( \begin{matrix} 1 & 3 & 5 & 7 \\  \dfrac{40}{143} & \dfrac{63}{143} & \dfrac{35}{143} & \dfrac{5}{143} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,则数学期望

$E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 1 \times  \dfrac{40}{143} + 3 \times  \dfrac{63}{143} + 5 \times  \dfrac{35}{143} + 7 \times  \dfrac{5}{143} = \dfrac{439}{143}.$

12.(1)由题可知 2 号盒子里有 2 个红球的概率为 $P = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{1} \times  {{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{5};$

(2)由题可知 ${X}_{3}$ 可取1、2、3，

$P\left( {{X}_{3} = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} \times  \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{4}^{2}} + \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} \times  \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{4}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{5},$

$P\left( {{X}_{3} = 3}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} \times  \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{4}^{2}} + \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} \times  \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{4}^{2}} = \dfrac{1}{5},$

$P\left( {{X}_{3} = 2}\right)  = 1 - P\left( {{X}_{3} = 1}\right)  - P\left( {{X}_{3} = 3}\right)  = \dfrac{3}{5}$ ,所以 3 号盒子里的红球的个数 ${X}_{3}$ 的分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} 1 & 2 & 3 \\  \dfrac{1}{5} & \dfrac{3}{5} & \dfrac{1}{5} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

$E\left\lbrack  {X}_{3}\right\rbrack   = 1 \times  \dfrac{1}{5} + 2 \times  \dfrac{3}{5} + 3 \times  \dfrac{1}{5} = 2$ . 猜想 $E\left\lbrack  {X}_{n}\right\rbrack   = 2$ .

13. (1) $P\left( B\right)  = \dfrac{2 + 3}{25} = \dfrac{1}{5}$ ,

$P\left( {B \mid  A}\right)  = \dfrac{P\left( {A \cap  B}\right) }{P\left( A\right) } = \dfrac{\left| A \cap  B\right| }{\left| A\right| } = \dfrac{2}{8 + 2} = \dfrac{1}{5},$

$P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{8 + 2}{25} = \dfrac{2}{5},$

$P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  = \dfrac{\left| A \cap  B\right| }{25} = \dfrac{2}{25} = P\left( A\right)  \cdot  P\left( B\right) ,$

所以事件 $A$ 和事件 $B$ 独立;

(2)外观和内饰均为同色的概率 $\dfrac{{{C}}_{8}^{2} + {{C}}_{12}^{2} + {{C}}_{2}^{2} + {{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{25}^{2}} = \dfrac{49}{150}$ ; 外观和内饰都异色的概率 $\dfrac{{{C}}_{8}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1} + {{C}}_{12}^{1}{{C}}_{2}^{1}}{{{C}}_{25}^{2}} = \dfrac{24}{150} = \dfrac{4}{25}$ ;

仅外观或仅内饰同色的概率

$\dfrac{{{C}}_{8}^{1}{{C}}_{2}^{1} + {{C}}_{12}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1} + {{C}}_{8}^{1}{{C}}_{12}^{1} + {{C}}_{2}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{25}^{2}} = \dfrac{77}{150};$

所以 $X$ 的分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} {600} & {300} & {150} \\  \dfrac{4}{25} & \dfrac{49}{150} & \dfrac{77}{150} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

期望 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {600} \times  \dfrac{4}{25} + {300} \times  \dfrac{49}{150} + {150} \times  \dfrac{77}{150} = {271}$ (元).

14.(1)设“计划依次派出甲乙丙进行闯关，该小组比赛胜利”为事件 $A$ ，因为甲乙丙各自闯关成功的概率分别为 ${p}_{1} =  \dfrac{3}{4},{p}_{2} = \dfrac{2}{3},{p}_{3} = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,每人能否闯关成功相互独立,解法 1 : 所以 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{3}{4} + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  \cdot  \dfrac{2}{3} + \left( {1 - \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  \cdot  \left( {1 - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)$ . $\dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{23}{24}$ . 解法 2: 所以 $P\left( A\right)  = 1 - \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{2}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{3}}\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{1}{4} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{23}{24}.$

(2)按甲在先，乙次之，丙最后的顺序派人，所需派出的人员数目 $X$ 的可能取值是 $1,2,3,P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = {p}_{1},P\left( {X = 2}\right)  =  \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) {p}_{2},P\left( {X = 3}\right)  = \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{2}}\right)$ ,所以 $X$ 的分布是: $\left( \begin{matrix} 1 & 2 & 3 \\  {p}_{1} & \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) {p}_{2} & \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{2}}\right)  \end{matrix}\right)$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {p}_{1} +  2 \times  \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) {p}_{2} + 3 \times  \left( {1 - {p}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{2}}\right)  = {p}_{1}{p}_{2} - 2{p}_{1} - {p}_{2} + 3;$

(3)若先派丙，再派乙，最后派甲，所需派出的人员数目 $Y$ 的分布是: $\left( \begin{matrix} 1 & 2 & 3 \\  {p}_{3} & \left( {1 - {p}_{3}}\right) {p}_{2} & \left( {1 - {p}_{3}}\right) \left( {1 - {p}_{2}}\right)  \end{matrix}\right)$ ,则 $E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   =  {p}_{3}{p}_{2} - 2{p}_{3} - {p}_{2} + 3$ ,所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   - E\left\lbrack  Y\right\rbrack   = \left( {{p}_{1}{p}_{2} - 2{p}_{1} - }\right.  \left. {{p}_{2} + 3}\right)  - \left( {{p}_{3}{p}_{2} - 2{p}_{3} - {p}_{2} + 3}\right)  = {p}_{2}\left( {{p}_{1} - {p}_{3}}\right)  - 2\left( {{p}_{1} - }\right.  \left. {p}_{3}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{2} - 2}\right) \left( {{p}_{1} - {p}_{3}}\right)  < 0$ ,所以先派甲,再派乙,最后派丙时, 派出的人员数目的数学期望较小.

15. (1)依题意得，事件 $B$ 的概率为 $P\left( B\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3}$ ，事件 $A$ 基于条件 $B$ 的概率为 $P\left( {A \mid  B}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,则 $P\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  = P\left( {A \mid  B}\right)  P\left( B\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{1}{6};$

(2)依题意得，事件 $A$ 发生的次数 $X$ 可取:0、、1、、2、、、4、、，则 $X$ 的分布为:

$\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 & 4 & 5 \\  {{C}}_{5}^{0}{\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right) }^{5} & {{C}}_{5}^{1}\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) {\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right) }^{4} & {{C}}_{5}^{2}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{2}{\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right) }^{3} & {{C}}_{5}^{3}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{3}{\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right) }^{2} & {{C}}_{5}^{4}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{4}\left( \dfrac{2}{3}\right) & {{C}}_{5}^{5}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{5} \end{matrix}\right) ,$

即 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 & 4 & 5 \\  \dfrac{32}{243} & \dfrac{80}{243} & \dfrac{80}{243} & \dfrac{40}{243} & \dfrac{10}{243} & \dfrac{1}{243} \end{matrix}\right)$ , 进行 3 局就结束的概率为 ${p}_{1} + {p}_{2} = \dfrac{2}{9} + \dfrac{1}{18} = \dfrac{5}{18}$ ;

则 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 1 \times  \dfrac{80}{243} + 2 \times  \dfrac{80}{243} + 3 \times  \dfrac{40}{243} + 4 \times  \dfrac{10}{243} + 5 \times  \dfrac{1}{243} =  \dfrac{5}{3}$ ,则所求的 $X$ 的期望 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{5}{3}$ .

16. (1)设“从 3 月 31 日至 4 月 13 日某天开始，连续统计三天，这三天中至少有两天是阵雨”为事件 $A$ ，连续统计三天共有 12 个样本点,事件 $A$ 共有 4 个样本点,所以 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{4}{12} =  \dfrac{1}{3};$

(2)4 月 1 日至 4 日这 4 天温差分别为 $9{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 、 $8{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 、 $9{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 、 $9{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 、 $9{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ ， 因此 ${s}_{1}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{4}{\left( {x}_{i} - \bar{x}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{3}{16}$ ,设 4 月 14 日的温差为 $x{}^{ \circ  }{C}$ ,则 4 月 11 日至 14 日这 4 天温差分别为 ${8}^{ \circ  }{C},{9}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 、 8 \textdegree C、 $x$  \textdegree C，因此 $\bar{x} = \dfrac{x + {25}}{4}$  \textdegree C、 ${s}_{2}^{2} = \dfrac{1}{4}\left\lbrack  {{\left( 8 - \dfrac{x + {25}}{4}\right) }^{2} \times  2 + }\right.  \left. {{\left( 9 - \dfrac{x + {25}}{4}\right) }^{2} + {\left( x - \dfrac{x + {25}}{4}\right) }^{2}}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{4}{3} \times  \dfrac{3}{16}$ ,解得 $x = 9$ ,因此,4 月 14 日这天最高气温是 ${18}^{ \circ  }{C}$ ;

(3)从3月31日至4月13日，一天温差不超过 ${9}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 的共有 11 天,高于 ${9}^{ \circ  }{C}$ 的共有 3 天, $X$ 可能取值的 $0,1,2,P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{3}^{2}}{{{C}}_{14}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{91},P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{11}^{1}{{C}}_{3}^{1}}{{{C}}_{14}^{2}} = \dfrac{33}{91},P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{11}^{2}}{{{C}}_{14}^{2}} = \dfrac{55}{91}$ , 随机变量 $X$ 的分布为: $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 \\  \dfrac{3}{91} & \dfrac{33}{91} & \dfrac{55}{91} \end{matrix}\right)$ , 随机变量 $X$ 的期望 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{3}{91} + 1 \times  \dfrac{33}{91} + 2 \times  \dfrac{55}{91} = \dfrac{11}{7}$ .

17.(1)提出原假设 ${H}_{0}$ :学生对垃圾分类的了解程度与性别无关,确定显著性水平 $\alpha  = {0.05}$ ,由题意得, $a = {40},b = c =  d = {20}$ ,可得 ${\chi }^{2} = \dfrac{n{\left( ad - bc\right) }^{2}}{\left( {a + b}\right) \left( {c + d}\right) \left( {a + c}\right) \left( {b + d}\right) } =  \dfrac{{100} \times  {\left( {40} \times  {20} - {20} \times  {20}\right) }^{2}}{{60} \times  {40} \times  {60} \times  {40}} = \dfrac{25}{9}$ ,由 $P\left( {{\chi }^{2} \geq  {3.841}}\right)  \approx  {0.05}$ ,且 $\dfrac{25}{9} < {3.841}$ ，所以接受原假设，学生对垃圾分类的了解程度与性别无关；

(2)(i)比赛只进行 3 局就结束，甲赢得比赛的概率为 ${p}_{1} =  \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} = \dfrac{2}{9}$ ,比赛只进行 3 局就结束,乙赢得比赛的概率为 ${p}_{2} = \left( {1 - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)  \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{1}{2}}\right)  \times  \left( {1 - \dfrac{2}{3}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{18}$ ,故比赛只

(ii) $X$ 的可能取值为 $0,1,2,3,X = 0$ ,即进行了 3 场比赛,且乙赢得比赛,故 $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{1}{18},X = 1$ ,即进行了 4 场比赛, 且乙赢得比赛, 前 3 场中, 甲赢得 1 场比赛, 乙第 4 场赢,故 $P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} + \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} = \dfrac{5}{36},X = 2$ ,即进行了 5 场比赛, 且乙赢得比赛, 前 4 场中, 甲赢得 2 场比赛, 乙第 5 场赢, 故 $P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{1}{2} \times  \dfrac{1}{3} = \dfrac{13}{108},X = 3$ ,即最后甲赢得比赛,由概率性质得 $P\left( {X = 3}\right)  = 1 - P\left( {X = 0}\right)  -  P\left( {X = 1}\right)  - P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = 1 - \dfrac{1}{18} - \dfrac{5}{36} - \dfrac{13}{108} = \dfrac{37}{54}$ ,所以分布为 $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 \\  \dfrac{1}{18} & \dfrac{5}{36} & \dfrac{13}{108} & \dfrac{37}{54} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

故数学期望为 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1}{18} + 1 \times  \dfrac{5}{36} + 2 \times  \dfrac{13}{108} + 3 \times  \dfrac{37}{54} = \dfrac{263}{108}.$

20. ( 1 )记 $A$ 、 $B$ 软件能正确解答数学问题的概率为 ${p}_{1}$ 和 ${p}_{2}$ ,则 ${p}_{1} = \dfrac{40}{50} = {0.8},{p}_{2} = \dfrac{38}{50} = \dfrac{19}{25}$ ;

(2)记“A 软件能正确解答这道题”为事件 $A,{}^{\prime \prime }B$ 软件能正确解答这道题”为事件 $B$ ，“该题为几何题”为事件 $C$ .

则 $P\left( A\right)  = P\left( C\right) P\left( {A \mid  C}\right)  + P\left( \bar{C}\right) P\left( {A \mid  \bar{C}}\right)$

$= \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{16}{20} + \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{24}{30} = \dfrac{4}{5},$

同理可得 $P\left( B\right)  = \dfrac{1}{3} \times  \dfrac{20}{30} + \dfrac{2}{3} \times  \dfrac{18}{20} = \dfrac{37}{45}$ .

因为 $P\left( B\right)  > P\left( A\right)$ ,所以 $B$ 软件能够正确解决这道试题的概率更大,小浦应该使用 $B$ 软件来解决这道试题;

(3)几何试题用 $A$ 软件解答,函数试题用 $B$ 软件解答.

因为 ${X}_{1} \sim  A\left( {3,\dfrac{4}{5}}\right) ,{X}_{2} \sim  B\left( {3,\dfrac{9}{10}}\right)$ ,

所以 $E\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   = 3 \times  \dfrac{4}{5} = \dfrac{12}{5},E\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   = 3 \times  \dfrac{9}{10} = \dfrac{27}{10}$ .

于是 $E\left\lbrack  {{X}_{1} + {X}_{2}}\right\rbrack   = E\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   + E\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack   = \dfrac{12}{5} + \dfrac{27}{10} = \dfrac{51}{10}$ .

显然 ${X}_{1},{X}_{2}$ 相互独立,

所以 $D\left\lbrack  {{X}_{1} + {X}_{2}}\right\rbrack   = D\left\lbrack  {X}_{1}\right\rbrack   + D\left\lbrack  {X}_{2}\right\rbrack$

$= \dfrac{12}{5} \times  \dfrac{1}{5} + \dfrac{27}{10} \times  \dfrac{1}{10} = \dfrac{3}{4}.$

21. ( 1 )因为 $A,B,C$ 三层题量之比为 $7 : 3 : 2$ ，所以在 $B$ 层选题的概率为 $\dfrac{3}{12} = \dfrac{1}{4}$ ，不在 $B$ 层选题的概率为 $1 - \dfrac{1}{4} =  \dfrac{3}{4}$ ，设至少 2 人的选题来自 $B$ 层的概率为 $P$ ，从 $B$ 层选题数量为 ${X}_{1}$ ,由题意得 ${X}_{1} \sim  B\left( {4,\dfrac{1}{4}}\right)$ ,而二项分布概率公式为 $P\left( {{X}_{1} = k}\right)  = {{C}}_{4}^{k}{\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{k}{\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{n - k}$ ,则至少 2 人的选题来自 $B$ 层的概率为 $P\left( {{X}_{1} = 2}\right)  + P\left( {{X}_{1} = 3}\right)  + P\left( {{X}_{1} = 4}\right)$ ,

故 $P = {{C}}_{4}^{2}{\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{2}{\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right) }^{2} + {{C}}_{4}^{3}{\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{3}\left( \dfrac{3}{4}\right)  + {{C}}_{4}^{4}{\left( \dfrac{1}{4}\right) }^{4}  = \dfrac{54}{256} + \dfrac{12}{256} + \dfrac{1}{256} = \dfrac{67}{256};$

(2)因为 $A$ 、 $B$ 、 $C$ 三层题量之比为 $7 : 3 : 2$ ，

所以在 $A$ 层最多抽到 7 道,且 $X$ 可取 $0,1,2,3$ ,

则 $P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{7}^{0}{{C}}_{5}^{3}}{{{C}}_{12}^{3}} = \dfrac{1}{22}$ ,

$P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{7}^{1}{{C}}_{5}^{2}}{{{C}}_{12}^{3}} = \dfrac{7}{22},$

$P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{7}^{2}{{C}}_{5}^{1}}{{{C}}_{12}^{3}} = \dfrac{21}{44},$

$P\left( {X = 3}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{7}^{3}{{C}}_{5}^{0}}{{{C}}_{12}^{3}} = \dfrac{7}{44},$

其分布为: $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 & 3 \\  \dfrac{1}{22} & \dfrac{7}{22} & \dfrac{21}{44} & \dfrac{7}{44} \end{matrix}\right)$ ,

所以期望 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = 0 \times  \dfrac{1}{22} + 1 \times  \dfrac{7}{22} + 2 \times  \dfrac{21}{44} + 3 \times  \dfrac{7}{44} = \dfrac{7}{4}$ .

22. (1)我们利用通过抽样获得的 100 名客户的样本信息来估计总体的分布情况可得: ${1350} \times  \dfrac{3}{10} = {405}$ 人; 当日消费金额在 $\lbrack {800},{1000})$ 和 $\left\lbrack  {{1000},{1200}}\right\rbrack$ (单位: 元)的人数所占比例为 ${0.00100} : {0.00050} = 2 : 1$ ,所以抽取的 6 人中有 2 人消费金额在 $\left\lbrack  {{1000},{1200}}\right\rbrack$ (单位: 元),有 4 人消费金额在 $\lbrack {800}$ , 1000) (单位: 元). 记“抽到的 2 人中至少 1 人消费额不少于 1 000 元”为事件 $A$ ，则 $P\left( A\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{4}^{1}{{C}}_{2}^{1}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} + \dfrac{{{C}}_{2}^{2}}{{{C}}_{6}^{2}} = \dfrac{3}{5}$ ，所以抽到的 2 人中至少 1 人消费金额不少于 1000 元的概率为 $\dfrac{3}{5}$ ;

(3)若选方案一，只需付款 1000-50×3=850 元；

若选方案二,设付款金额为 $X$ 元,

则 $X$ 可分别取 ${300},{600},{900},{1000}$ 元,其中

$P\left( {X = {300}}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{3}{\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{0}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{3} = \dfrac{1}{27}$ ,

$P\left( {X = {600}}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{2}{\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{1}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{2} = \dfrac{2}{9},$

$P\left( {X = {900}}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{1}{\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{2}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{1} = \dfrac{4}{9},$

$P\left( {X = {1000}}\right)  = {{C}}_{3}^{0}{\left( 1 - \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{3}{\left( \dfrac{1}{3}\right) }^{0} = \dfrac{8}{27},$

所以 $E\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   = {300} \times  \dfrac{1}{27} + {600} \times  \dfrac{2}{9} + {900} \times  \dfrac{4}{9} + {1000} \times  \dfrac{8}{27} \approx$ 840.7 元,因为 ${850} > {840.7}$ ,所以应选择第二种促销方案.

23. ( 1 )由频率分步直方图中小矩形的面积和为 1 可得:

${20} \times  \left( {{0.005} + b + {0.020} + {0.015}}\right)  = 1,$

解得 $b = {0.010}$ ;

该校这次初赛的平均分数为 ${20} \times  \left( {{30} \times  {0.005} + {50} \times  {0.010} + }\right)  {70} \times  {0.020} + {90} \times  {0.015}) = {68}$ ;

(2)初赛分数达到 80 及以上的同学为 ${0.015} \times  {20} \times  {40} = {12}$ 人，非优秀为 28 人，由题意可得 $X$ 的可能取值为 0、1、2，

$P\left( {X = 0}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{28}^{2}}{{{C}}_{40}^{2}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{28} \times  {27}}{2}}{\dfrac{{40} \times  {39}}{2}} = \dfrac{63}{130},$

$P\left( {X = 1}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{28}^{1}{{C}}_{12}^{1}}{{{C}}_{40}^{2}} = \dfrac{{28} \times  {12}}{\underset{2}{\underbrace{{40} \times  {39}}}} = \dfrac{56}{130} = \dfrac{28}{65},$

$P\left( {X = 2}\right)  = \dfrac{{{C}}_{12}^{2}}{{{C}}_{40}^{2}} = \dfrac{\dfrac{{12} \times  {11}}{2}}{\dfrac{{40} \times  {39}}{2}} = \dfrac{11}{130},$

所以 $X$ 的分布为: $\left( \begin{matrix} 0 & 1 & 2 \\  \dfrac{63}{130} & \dfrac{28}{65} & \dfrac{11}{130} \end{matrix}\right)$ ;

(3)按照不同题目顺序分类讨论:

 \ding{172}  填空-选择-简答:

得零分的概率: $1 - {0.8} = {0.2}$ ,

得一分的概率: ${0.8} \times  \left( {1 - {0.9}}\right)  = {0.08}$ ,

得两分的概率: ${0.8} \times  {0.9} \times  {0.2} = {0.144}$ ,

得三分的概率: ${0.8} \times  {0.9} \times  {0.8} = {0.576}$ ,

期望为 ${E}_{1} = 0 \times  {0.2} + 1 \times  {0.08} + 2 \times  {0.144} + 3 \times  {0.576} = {2.096}$ 分,

因为填空和简答的正确率相同,

所以“简答-选择-填空”的期望与之相同；

 \ding{173}  填空-简答-选择:

得零分的概率: $1 - {0.8} = {0.2}$ ,

得一分的概率: ${0.8} \times  {0.2} = {0.16}$ ,

得两分的概率: ${0.8} \times  {0.8} \times  {0.1} = {0.064}$ ,

得三分的概率: ${0.8} \times  {0.8} \times  {0.9} = {0.576}$ ,

期望为 ${E}_{2} = 0 \times  {0.2} + 1 \times  {0.16} + 2 \times  {0.064} + 3 \times  {0.576} = \mathbf{2.{016}}$ 分,

因为填空和简答的正确率相同，

所以“简答-填空-选择”的期望与之相同;

 \ding{174}  选择一填空-简答:

得零分的概率: $1 - {0.9} = {0.1}$ ,

得一分的概率: ${0.9} \times  \left( {1 - {0.8}}\right)  = {0.18}$ ,

得两分的概率: ${0.9} \times  {0.8} \times  {0.2} = {0.144}$ ,

得三分的概率: ${0.9} \times  {0.8} \times  {0.8} = {0.576}$ ,

期望为 ${E}_{3} = 0 \times  {0.1} + 1 \times  {0.18} + 2 \times  {0.144} + 3 \times  {0.576} = {2.196}$ 分,

因为填空和简答的正确率相同,

所以“选择-简答-填空”的期望与之相同；

所以 ${E}_{3} > {E}_{1} > {E}_{2}$ ,小杨应采用 “选择-填空-简答”或 “选择- 简答-填空”的顺序.

\section*{第 14 章 数学建模问题}

\section*{考点 1 圆锥曲线模型}

\section*{一、填空题}

1. 21

【解析】依题意,当滑块 $B$ 在两条空槽的交点处时, ${BC}$ 长为椭圆的短半轴长 $b$ ,当滑块 $A$ 在两条空槽的交点处时, ${AC}$ 长为椭圆的长半轴长 $a$ ,则 $a = {14} + b$ ,由椭圆的离心率为 $\dfrac{4}{5}$ ,得 $\dfrac{\sqrt{{a}^{2} - {b}^{2}}}{a} = \sqrt{1 - {\left( \dfrac{b}{a}\right) }^{2}} = \dfrac{4}{5}$ ,解得 $\dfrac{b}{a} = \dfrac{3}{5}$ ,即 $\dfrac{b}{{14} + b} = \dfrac{3}{5}$ , 解得 $b = {21}$ ,所以 $B,C$ 之间的距离为 21 厘米. 故答案为: 21 .

\section*{二、解答题}

2. (1) 由 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {x}^{2} + {y}^{2} \leq  1 \\  y = \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{3x}{4} \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + {\left( \dfrac{1}{x} - \dfrac{3x}{4}\right) }^{2} \leq  1 \Longrightarrow  {\left( \dfrac{5x}{4} - \dfrac{1}{x}\right) }^{2} \leq   \end{array}\right.  0 \Longrightarrow  \dfrac{5x}{4} - \dfrac{1}{x} = 0 \Longrightarrow  x =  \pm  \dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}$ . 当 $x = \dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}$ 时, $y = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2} - \dfrac{3}{4} \times  \dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5} = \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{5}$ ; 当 $x =  - \dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}$ 时, $y =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{2} + \dfrac{3}{4} \times  \dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5} =  - \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{5}$ . 所以 ${M}_{f}\left( {0,0}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5},\dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{5}}\right) ,\left( {-\dfrac{2\sqrt{5}}{5}, - \dfrac{\sqrt{5}}{5}}\right) }\right\}$ 有 2 个元素;

( 2 )将 $y = \sqrt{x + k}$ 代入圆 ${\left( x - \dfrac{1}{2}\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1 \Longrightarrow  {x}^{2} + k - \dfrac{3}{4} =$ 0,由相切 $\Delta  = 0 \Longrightarrow  4\left( {k - \dfrac{3}{4}}\right)  = 0 \Longrightarrow  k = \dfrac{3}{4}$ . 此时 ${x}_{0} = 0,{y}_{0} =  \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2}$ ,又 $f\left( x\right)  = \sqrt{x + \dfrac{3}{4}}$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{x + \dfrac{3}{4}}}$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( 0\right)  = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}$ ,切线方程 $y - \dfrac{\sqrt{3}}{2} = \dfrac{1}{\sqrt{3}}x$ ,即 ${2x} - 2\sqrt{3}y + 3 = 0$ ;

(3)对于任意的 $a$ ，皆有 ${M}_{f}\left( {a,0}\right)  = \varnothing$ 成立，即函数 $y = f\left( x\right)$ 的图像与圆系: ${\left( x - a\right) }^{2} + {y}^{2} = 1$ 无交点,所以 $f\left( x\right)  > 1$ 恒成立. 因为 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - \dfrac{\ln x}{x} - \dfrac{1}{x} + m,x > 0$ ,所以 ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} +  \dfrac{\ln x}{{x}^{2}},x > 0$ . 当 $x \geq  1$ 时, ${f}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  > 0$ 恒成立,所以函数在 $\lbrack 1, + \infty )$ 上严格增,且 $f\left( 1\right)  = {e} - 1 + m$ . 由 ${e} - 1 + m > 1 \Longrightarrow  m > 2 - {e}$ . 当 $0 < x < 1$ 时,设 $h\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - x - 1$ ,则 ${h}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  =  {{e}}^{x} - 1 > 0$ ,所以 $h\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,所以 $h\left( x\right)  > h\left( 0\right)  =$ 0 . 即当 $0 < x < 1$ 时， ${{e}}^{x} > x + 1$ ；又 $\ln x < 0$ ，所以 $\dfrac{\ln x + 1}{x} < \dfrac{1}{x}$ . 所以 $f\left( x\right)  = {{e}}^{x} - \dfrac{\ln x + 1}{x} + m \geq  x + 1 - \dfrac{1}{x} + m$ . 设 $g\left( x\right)  =  x + 1 - \dfrac{1}{x} + m,0 < x < 1$ ,则 ${g}^{\prime }\left( x\right)  = 1 + \dfrac{1}{{x}^{2}} > 0$ ,所以 $g\left( x\right)$ 在 $\left( {0,1}\right)$ 上严格增,所以 $g\left( x\right)  < g\left( 1\right)  = 1 + m$ . 由 $1 + m > 1 \Longrightarrow  m >$ 0 . 综上,实数 $m$ 的取值范围为: $\left( {0, + \infty }\right)$ .

3. ( 1 )如图建立平面直角坐标系 ${xOy}$ ，设抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $y = a{x}^{2},x \in  \left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ ,则抛物线 $\Gamma$ 过点 $\left( {2,2}\right)$ ,所以 $2 =  {4a}$ ,故 $a = \dfrac{1}{2}$ ,所以,抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的方程为 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2},x \in  \lbrack  - 2$ , 2]，线段 ${AB}$ 的方程为 $y = 2,x \in  \left\lbrack  {-2,2}\right\rbrack$ ；

\begin{center}
\includegraphics[width=0.2\textwidth]{bodyxb/src/181_1076_326_239_166_0.jpg}
\end{center}
 

(2)设 $E\left( {x,y}\right)$ ，则 ${DE} = 2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2}$ . 以 ${CF}$ 为母线的圆柱的底面半径 ${r}_{1}$ 满足 ${2x} = {2\pi }{r}_{1}$ ，所以 ${r}_{1} = \dfrac{x}{\pi }$ ，所以圆柱的体积 ${V}_{1} = \pi  \cdot  {\left( \dfrac{x}{\pi }\right) }^{2} \cdot  \left( {2 - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2}}\right)  = \dfrac{1}{\pi }\left( {2{x}^{2} - \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{4}}\right)$ . 所以 ${V}_{1} =  - \dfrac{1}{2\pi }{\left( {x}^{2} - 2\right) }^{2} + \dfrac{2}{\pi }$ ,所以,当 $x = \sqrt{2}$ 时,其体积取得最大值 $\dfrac{2}{\pi }$ ; (3)证明:因为函数 $y = \dfrac{1}{2}{x}^{2}$ 的导函数 ${y}^{\prime } = x$ ，所以，抛物线 $\Gamma$ 上任意一点 $\left( {x,y}\right)$ 的切线斜率为 $x$ ,设 ${l}_{1},{l}_{2}$ 是抛物线 $\Gamma$ 上两条相互垂直的切线,切点分别为 $\left( {{x}_{1},{y}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{x}_{2},{y}_{2}}\right)$ , 则其方程分别为 ${l}_{1} : y - {y}_{1} = {x}_{1}\left( {x - {x}_{1}}\right) ,{l}_{2} : y - {y}_{2} =  {x}_{2}\left( {x - {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,且 ${x}_{1}{x}_{2} =  - 1$ ,消去 $x$ ,解得 $\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right) y =  \dfrac{1}{2}{x}_{1}{x}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1} - {x}_{2}}\right)$ ,因为 ${x}_{1} \neq  {x}_{2}$ ,得 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ . 故抛物线 $\Gamma$ 的任何两条相互垂直的切线的交点都在直线 $y =  - \dfrac{1}{2}$ 上.

4. ( 1 )解法 1 :如图所示，以线段 ${AB}$ 的中点 $O$ 为坐标原点建立平面直角坐标系,则圆 $O$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {100}$ ,由 $\tan C = \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}},{OE} = {10}$ 得 ${CE} = {20}\sqrt{2},{CO} = {30}$ ,过点 $C$ 作圆 $O$ 的切线 ${CE}$ ,切点为 $E$ ,直线 ${CE}$ 的斜率为 $\dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}$ ,其方程为 $y =  \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + {30}}\right)$ ,所以直线 ${OE}$ 的斜率为 $- 2\sqrt{2}$ ,其方程为 $y =  - 2\sqrt{2}x$ ,将其代入 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {100}$ ,得点 $E$ 的坐标为 $\left( {-\dfrac{10}{3},\dfrac{{20}\sqrt{2}}{3}}\right)$ ,经过点 $D$ 作圆 $M$ 与圆 $O$ 切于点 $F$ (圆 $O$ 与 $y$ 轴的交点),设圆 $M$ 的半径为 $r$ ,则 $O{D}^{2} + O{M}^{2} = D{M}^{2}$ ,即 ${30}^{2} + {\left( r - {10}\right) }^{2} = {r}^{2}$ ,解得 $r = {50}$ ,所以,圆 $M$ 的方程为 ${x}^{2} +  {\left( y + {40}\right) }^{2} = {50}^{2}$ ,故用函数表示过桥道路为

$y = \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + {30}}\right) , - {30} \leq  x <  - \dfrac{10}{3} \\  \sqrt{{100} - {x}^{2}}, - \dfrac{10}{3} \leq  x < 0 \\  \sqrt{{2500} - {x}^{2}} - {40},0 \leq  x \leq  {30} \end{array}.}\right.$

解法 2: 如图所示,以线段 ${AB}$ 的中点 $O$ 为坐标原点建立平面直角坐标系,作圆 $N$ 与 $x$ 轴相切于点 $D$ ,并和圆 $O$ 切于点

$G$ ,设圆 $M$ 的半径为 $r$ ,则 $O{D}^{2} + D{N}^{2} = O{N}^{2}$ ,即 ${30}^{2} + {r}^{2} =  {\left( r + {10}\right) }^{2}$ ,解得 $r = {40}$ ,所以圆 $N$ 的方程为 ${\left( x - {30}\right) }^{2} +  {\left( y - {40}\right) }^{2} = {40}^{2}$ ,将直线 ${OG}$ 的方程代入 ${x}^{2} + {y}^{2} = {100}$ 得,点 $G$ 的坐标为 $\left( {6,8}\right)$ ,所以用函数表示过桥道路为

\[
y = \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} \dfrac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\left( {x + {30}}\right) , - {30} \leq  x <  - \dfrac{10}{3} \\  \sqrt{{100} - {x}^{2}}, - \dfrac{10}{3} \leq  x < 6 \\  \sqrt{{2.500} - {x}^{2}} - {40.6} \leq  x \leq  {30} \end{array}.}\right.
